1
ye triShaptAH pariyanti vishvA rUpANi bibhrataH /
vAchas patir balA teShAM tanvo adya dadhAtu me //1//
punar ehi vachas pate devena manasA saha /
vasoSh pate ni ramaya mayy evAstu mayi shrutam //2//
ihaivAbhi vi tanUbhe ArtnI iva jyayA /
vAchas patir ni yachhatu mayy evAstu mayi shrutam //3//
upahUto vAchas patir upAsmAn vAchaspatir hvayatAm /
saM shrutena gamemahi mA shrutena vi rAdhiShi //4//
2

vidmA sharasya pitaraM parjanyaM bhUridhAyasam /
vidmo Shv asya mAtaraM pR^ithivIM bhUrivarpasam //1//
jyAke pari No namAshmAnaM tanvaM kR^idhi /
vIDur varIyo 'rAtIr apa dveShAMsy A kR^idhi //2//
vR^ikShaM yad gAvaH pariShasvajAnA anusphuraM sharaM archanty R^ibhum /
sharum asmad yAvaya didyum indra //3//
yathA dyAM cha pR^ithivIM chAntas tiShThati tejanam /
evA rogaM chAsrAvaM chAntas tiShThatu mu~nja it //4//
3

vidmA sharasya pitaraM parjanyaM shatavR^iShNyaM /
tenA te tanve3 shaM karaM pR^ithivyAM te niShechanaM bahiSh Te astu bAl iti //1//
vidmA sharasya pitaraM mitraM shatavR^iShNyaM /
tenA te tanve3 shaM karaM pR^ithivyAM te niShechanaM bahiSh Te astu bAl iti //2//
vidmA sharasya pitaraM varuNaM shatavR^iShNyaM /
tenA te tanve3 shaM karaM pR^ithivyAM te niShechanaM bahiSh Te astu bAl iti //3//
vidmA sharasya pitaraM chandraM shatavR^iShNyaM /
tenA te tanve3 shaM karaM pR^ithivyAM te niShechanaM bahiSh Te astu bAl iti //4//
vidmA sharasya pitaraM sUryaM shatavR^iShNyaM /
tenA te tanve3 shaM karaM pR^ithivyAM te niShechanaM bahiSh Te astu bAl iti //5//
yad AntreShu gavInyor yad vastAv adhi saMshritam /
evA te mUtraM muchyatAM bahir bAl iti sarvakam //6//
pra te bhinadmi mehanaM vartraM veshantyA iva /
evA te mUtraM muchyatAM bahir bAl iti sarvakam //7//
viShitaM te vastibilaM samudrasyodadher iva /
evA te mUtraM muchyatAM bahir bAl iti sarvakam //8//
yatheShukA parApatad avasR^iShTAdhi dhanvanaH /
evA te mUtraM muchyatAM bahir bAl iti sarvakam //9//
4

ambayo yanty adhvabhir jAmayo adhvarIyatAm /
pR^i~nchatIr madhunA payaH //1//
amUr yA upa sUrye yAbhir vA sUryaH saha /
tA no hinvantv adhvaram //2//
apo devIr upa hvaye yatra gAvaH pibanti naH /
sindhubhyaH kartvaM haviH //3//
apsv a1ntar amR^itam apsu bheShajam /
apAm uta prashastibhir ashvA bhavatha vAjino gAvo bhavatha vAjinIH //4//
5

Apo hi ShThA mayobhuvas tA na Urje dadhAtana /
mahe raNAya chakShase //1//
yo vaH shivatamo rasas tasya bhAjayateha naH /
ushatIr iva mAtaraH //2//
tasmA araM gamAma vo yasya kShayAya jinvatha /
Apo janayathA cha naH //3//
IshAnA vAryANAM kShayantIsh charShaNInAm /
apo yAchAmi bheShajam //4//
6

shaM no devIr abhiShTaya Apo bhavantu pItaye /
shaM yor abhi sravantu naH //1//
apsu me somo abravId antar vishvAni bheShajA /
agniM cha vishvashaMbhuvam //2//
ApaH pR^iNIta bheShajaM varUthaM tanve3 mama /
jyok cha sUryaM dR^ishe //3//
shaM na Apo dhanvanyA3H sham u santv anUpyAH /
shaM naH khanitrimA ApaH sham u yAH kumbha AbhR^itAH /
shivA naH santu vArShikIH //4//
7

stuvAnam agna A vaha yAtudhAnaM kimIdinam /
tvaM hi deva vandito hantA dasyor babhUvitha //1//
Ajyasya parameShThin jAtavedas tanUvashin /
agne taulasya prAshAna yAtudhAnAn vi lApaya //2//
vi lapantu yAtudhAnA attriNo ye kimIdinaH /
athedam agne no havir indrash cha prati haryatam //3//
agniH pUrva A rabhatAM prendro nudatu bAhumAn /
bravItu sarvo yAtumAn ayam asmIty etya //4//
pashyAma te vIryaM jAtavedaH pra No brUhi yAtudhAnAn nR^ichakShaH /
tvayA sarve paritaptAH purastAt ta A yantu prabruvANA upedam //5//
A rabhasva jAtavedo 'smAkArthAya jaj~niShe /
dUto no agne bhUtvA yAtudhAnAn vi lApaya //6//
tvam agne yAtudhAnAn upabaddhAm? ihA vaha /
athaiShAm indro vajreNApi shIrShANi vR^ishchatu //7//
8

idaM havir yAtudhAnAn nAdI phenam iva A vahat /
ya idaM strI pumAn akar iha sa stuvatAM janaH //1//
ayaM stuvAna Agamad imaM sma prati haryata /
bR^ihaspate vashe labdhvAgnIShomA vi vidhyatam //2//
yAtudhAnasya somapa jahi prajAM nayasva cha /
ni stuvAnasya pAtaya param akShy utAvaram //3//
yatraiShAm agne janimAni vettha guhA satAm attriNAM jAtavedaH /
tAMs tvaM brahmaNA vAvR^idhAno jahy eShAM shatatarham agne //4//
9

asmin vasu vasavo dhArayantv indraH pUShA varuNo mitro agniH /
imam AdityA uta vishve cha devA uttarasmin jyotiShi dhArayantu //1//
asya devAH pradishi jyotir astu sUryo agnir uta vA hiraNyam /
sapatnA asmad adhare bhavantUttamaM nAkam adhi rohayemam //2//
yenendrAya samabharaH payAMsy uttamena brahmaNA jAtavedaH /
tena tvam agna iha vardhayemaM sajAtAnAM shraiShThya A dhehy enam //3//
aiShAM yaj~nam uta varcho dade 'haM rAyas poSham uta chittAny agne /
sapatnA asmad adhare bhavantUttamaM nAkam adhi rohayemam //4//
10

ayaM devAnAm asuro vi rAjati vashA hi satyA varuNasya rAj~naH /
tatas pari brahmaNA shAshadAna ugrasya manyor ud imaM nayAmi //1//
namas te rajan varuNAstu manyave vishvaM hy ùgra nichikeShi drugdham /
sahasram anyAn pra suvAmi sAkaM shataM jIvAti sharadas tavAyam //2//
yad uvakthAnR^itam jihvayA vR^ijinaM bahu /
rAj~nas tvA satyadharmaNo mu~nchAmi varuNAd aham //3//
mu~nchAmi tvA vaishvAnarAd arNavAn mahatas pari /
sajAtAn ugrehA vada brahma chApa chikIhi naH //4//
11

vaShaT te pUShann asmint sUtAv aryamA hotA kR^iNotu vedhAH /
sisratAM nAry R^itaprajAtA vi parvANi jihatAM sUtavA u //1//
chatasro divaH pradishash chatasro bhUmyA uta /
devA garbhaM sam airayan taM vy UrNuvantu sUtave //2//
sUShA vy UrNotu vi yoniM hApayAmasi /
shrathayA sUShaNe tvam ava tvaM biShkale sR^ija //3//
neva mAMse na pIvasi neva majjasv Ahatam /
avaitu pR^ishni shevalaM sune jarAyv attave 'va jarAyu padyatAm //4//
vi te bhinadmi mehanaM vi yoniM vi gavInike /
vi mAtaraM cha putraM cha vi kumAraM jarAyuNAva jarAyu padyatAm //5//
yathA vAto yathA mano yathA patanti pakShiNaH /
evA tvaM dashamAsya sAkaM jarAyuNA patAva jarAyu padyatAm //6//
12

jarAyujaH prathama usriyo vR^iShA vAtAbhrajA stanayann eti vR^iShTyA /
sa no mR^iDAti tanva R^ijugo rujan ya ekam ojas tredhA vichakrame //1//
a~Ngea~Nge shochiShA shishriyANaM namasyantas tvA haviShA vidhema /
a~NkAnt sama~NkAn haviShA vidhema yo agrabhIt parvAsyA grabhItA //2//
mu~ncha shIrShaktyA uta kAsa enaM paruShparur AviveshA yo asya /
yo abhrajA vAtajA yash cha shuShmo vanaspatInt sachatAM parvatAMsh cha //3//
shaM me parasmai gAtrAya sham astv avarAya me /
shaM me chaturbhyo a~NgebhyaH sham astu tanve3 mama //4//
13

namas te astu vidyute namas te stanayitnave /
namas te astv ashmane yenA dUDAshe asyasi //1//
namas te pravato napAd yatas tapaH samUhasi /
mR^iDayA nas tanUbhyo mayas tokebhyas kR^idhi //2//
pravato napAn nama evAstu tubhyaM namas te hetaye tapuShe cha kR^iNmaH /
vidma te dhAma paramaM guhA yat samudre antar nihitAsi nAbhiH //3//
yAM tvA devA asR^ijanta vishva iShuM kR^iNvAnA asanAya dhR^iShNum /
sA no mR^iDa vidathe gR^iNAnA tasyai te namo astu devi //4//
14

bhagam asyA varcha AdiShy adhi vR^ikShAd iva srajam /
mahAbudhna iva parvato jyok pitR^iShv AstAm //1//
eShA te rAjan kanyA vadhUr ni dhUyatAm yama /
sA mAtur badhyatAM gR^ihe 'tho bhrAtur atho pituH //2//
eShA te kulapA rAjan tAm u te pari dadmasi /
jyok pitR^iShv AsAtA A shIrShNaH shamopyAt //3//
asitasya te brahmaNA kashyapasya gayasya cha /
antaHkosham iva jAmayo 'pi nahyAmi te bhagam //4 //
15

saM saM sravantu sindhavaH saM vAtAH saM patatriNaH /
imaM yaj~naM pradivo me juShantAM saMsrAvyeNa haviShA juhomi //1//
ihaiva havam A yAta ma iha saMsrAvaNA utemaM vardhayatA giraH /
ihaitu sarvo yaH pashur asmin tiShThatu yA rayiH //2//
ye nadInAM saMsravanty utsAsaH sadam akShitAH /
tebhir me sarvaiH saMsrAvair dhanaM saM srAvayAmasi //3//
ye sarpiShaH saMsravanti kShIrasya chodakasya cha /
tebhir me sarvaiH saMsrAvair dhanaM saM srAvayAmasi //4//
16

ye 'mAvAsyA3M rAtrim udasthur vrAjam attriNaH /
agnis turIyo yAtuhA so asmabhyam adhi bravat //1//
sIsAyAdhy Aha varuNaH sIsAyAgnir upAvati /
sIsaM ma indraH prAyachhat tad a~Nga yAtuchAtanam //2//
idaM viShkandhaM sahata idaM bAdhate attriNaH /
anena vishvA sasahe yA jAtAni pishAchyAH //3//
yadi no gAM haMsi yady ashvaM yadi pUruSham /
taM tvA sIsena vidhyAmo yathA no 'so avIrahA //4//
17

amUr yA yanti yoShito hirA lohitavAsasaH /
abhrAtara iva jAmayas tiShThantu hatavarchasaH //1//
tiShThAvare tiShTha para uta tvaM tiShTha madhyame /
kaniShThikA cha tiShThati tiShThAd id dhamanir mahI //2//
shatasya dhamanInAM sahasrasya hirANAm /
asthur in madhyamA imAH sAkam antA araMsata //3//
pari vaH sikatAvatI dhanUr bR^ihaty akramIt /
tiShThatelayatA su kam //4//
18

nir lakShmyaM lalAmya1M nir arAtiM suvAmasi /
atha yA bhadrA tAni naH prajAyA arAtiM nayAmasi //1//
nir araNiM savitA sAviShak pador nir hastayor varuNo mitro aryamA /
nir asmabhyam anumatI rarANA premAM devA asAviShuH saubhagAya //2//
yat ta Atmani tanvAM ghoram asti yad vA kesheShu pratichakShaNe vA /
sarvaM tad vAchApa hanmo vayaM devas tvA savitA sUdayatu //3//
rishyapadIM vR^iShadatIM goShedhAM vidhamAm uta /
vilIDhyaM lalAmya1M tA asman nAshayAmasi //4//
19

mA no vidan vivyAdhino mo abhivyAdhino vidan /
ArAch chharavyA asmad viShUchIr indra pAtaya //1//
viShva~ncho asmach chharavaH patantu ye astA ye chAsyAH /
daivIr manuShyesavo mamAmitrAn vi vidhyata //2//
yo naH svo yo araNaH sajAta uta niShTyo yo asmA.N abhidAsati /
rudraH sharavyayaitAn mamAmitrAn vi vidhyatu //3//
yaH sapatno yo 'sapatno yash cha dviShan chhapAti naH /
devAs taM sarve dhUrvantu brahma varma mamAntaram //4//
20

adArasR^id bhavatu deva somAsmin yaj~ne maruto mR^iDatA naH /
mA no vidad abhibhA mo ashastir mA no vidad vR^ijinA dveShyA yA //1//
yo adya senyo vadho 'ghAyUnAm udIrate /
yuvaM taM mitrAvaruNAv asmad yAvayataM pari //2//
itash cha yad amutash cha yad vadhaM varuNa yAvaya /
vi mahach chharma yachha varIyo yAvayA vadham //3//
shAsa itthA mahA.N asy amitrasAho astR^itaH /
na yasya hanyate sakhA na jIyate kadA chana //4//
21

svastidA vishAM patir vR^itrahA vimR^idho vashI /
vR^iShendraH pura etu naH somapA abhayaMkaraH //1//
vi na indra mR^idho jahi nIchA yachha pR^itanyataH /
adhamaM gamayA tamo yo asmA.N abhidAsati //2//
vi rakSho vi mR^idho jahi vi vR^itrasya hanU ruja /
vi manyum indra vR^itrahann amitrasyAbhidAsataH //3//
apendra dviShato mano 'pa jijyAsato vadham /
vi mahach chharma yachha varIyo yAvayA vadham //4//
22

anu sUryam ud ayatAM hR^iddyoto harimA cha te /
go rohitasya varNena tena tvA pari dadhmasi //1//
pari tvA rohitair varNair dIrghAyutvAya dadhmasi /
yathAyam arapA asad atho aharito bhuvat //2//
yA rohiNIr devatyA3 gAvo yA uta rohiNIH /
rUpaMrUpaM vayovayas tAbhiSh TvA pari dadhmasi //3//
shukeShu te harimANaM ropaNAkAsu dadhmasi /
atho hAridraveShu te harimANaM ni dadhmasi //4//
23

naktaMjAtAsi oShadhe rAme kR^iShNe asikni cha /
idaM rajani rajaya kilAsaM palitaM cha yat //1//
kilAsaM cha palitaM cha nir ito nAshayA pR^iShat /
A tvA svo vishatAM varNaH parA shuklAni pAtaya //2//
asitaM te pralayanam AsthAnam asitaM tava /
asiknI asy oShadhe nir ito nAshayA pR^iShat //3//
asthijasya kilAsasya tanUjasya cha yat tvachi /
dUShyA kR^itasya brahmaNA lakShma shvetam anInasham //4//
24

suparNo jAtaH prathamas tasya tvaM pittam Asitha /
tad AsurI yudhA jitA rUpaM chakre vanaspatIn //1//
AsurI chakre prathamedaM kilAsabheShajam idaM kilAsanAshanam /
anInashat kilAsaM sarUpAm akarat tvacham //2//
sarUpA nAma te mAtA sarUpo nAma te pitA /
sarUpakR^it tvam oShadhe sA sarUpam idaM kR^idhi //3//
shyAmA sarUpaMkaraNI pR^ithivyA adhy udbhR^itA /
idam U Shu pra sAdhaya punA rUpANi kalpaya //4//
25

yad agnir Apo adahat pravishya yatrAkR^iNvan dharmadhR^ito namAMsi /
tatra ta AhuH paramaM janitraM sa naH saMvidvAn pari vR^i~Ngdhi takman //1//
yady archir yadi vAsi shochiH shakalyeShi yadi vA te janitram /
hrUDur nAmAsi haritasya deva sa naH saMvidvAn pari vR^i~Ngdhi takman //2//
yadi shoko yadi vAbhishoko yadi vA rAj~no varuNasyAsi putraH /
hrUDur nAmAsi haritasya deva sa naH saMvidvAn pari vR^i~Ngdhi takman //3//
namaH shItAya takmane namo rUrAya shochiShe kR^iNomi /
yo anyedyur ubhayadyur abhyeti tR^itIyakAya namo astu takmane //4//
26

Are3 'sAv asmad astu hetir devAso asat /
Are ashmA yam asyatha //1//
sakhAsAv asmabhyam astu rAtiH sakhendro bhagaH /
savitA chitrarAdhAH //2//
yUyam naH pravato napAn marutaH sUryatvachasaH /
sharma yachhatha saprathAH //3//
suShUdata mR^iData mR^iDayA nas tanUbhyo /
mayas tokebhyas kR^idhi //4//
27

amUH pAre pR^idAkvas triShaptA nirjarAyavaH /
tAsAm jarAyubhir vayam akShyAv api vyayAmasy aghAyoH paripanthinaH //1//
viShUchy etu kR^intatI pinAkam iva bibhratI /
viShvak punarbhuvA mano 'samR^iddhA aghAyavaH //2//
na bahavaH sam ashakan nArbhakA abhi dAdhR^iShuH /
veNor adgA ivAbhito 'samR^iddhA aghAyavaH //3//
pretaM pAdau pra sphurataM vahataM pR^iNato gR^ihAn /
indrAny etu prathamAjItAmuShitA puraH //4//
28

upa prAgAd devo agnI rakShohAmIvachAtanaH /
dahann apa dvayAvino yAtudhAnAn kimIdinaH //1//
prati daha yAtudhAnAn prati deva kimIdinaH /
pratIchIH kR^iShNavartane saM daha yAtudhAnyaH //2//
yA shashApa shapanena yAghaM mUram Adadhe /
yA rasasya haraNAya jAtam Arebhe tokam attu sA //3//
putram attu yAtudhAnIH svasAram uta naptyam /
adhA mitho vikeshyo3 vi ghnatAM yAtudhAnyo3 vi tR^ihyantAm arAyyaH //4//
29

abhIvartena maNinA yenendro abhivavR^idhe /
tenAsmAn brahmaNas pate 'bhi rAShTrAya vardhaya //1//
abhivR^itya sapatnAn abhi yA no arAtayaH /
abhi pR^itanyantaM tiShThAbhi yo no durasyati //2//
abhi tvA devaH savitAbhi Shomo avIvR^idhat /
abhi tvA vishvA bhUtAny abhIvarto yathAsasi //3//
abhIvarto abhibhavaH sapatnakShayaNo maNiH /
rAShTrAya mahyaM badhyatAM sapatnebhyaH parAbhuve //4//
ud asau sUryo agAd ud idaM mAmakaM vachaH /
yathAhaM shatruho 'sAny asapatnaH sapatnahA //5//
sapatnakShayaNo vR^iShAbhiraShTro viShAsahiH /
yathAham eShAM vIrANAM virAjAni janasya cha //6//
30

vishve devA vasavo rakShatemam utAdityA jAgR^ita yUyam asmin /
memaM sanAbhir uta vAnyanAbhir memaM prApat pauruSheyo vadho yaH //1//
ye vo devAH pitaro ye cha putrAH sachetaso me shR^iNutedam uktam /
sarvebhyo vaH pari dadAmy etaM svasty enaM jarase vahAtha //2//
ye devA divi ShTha ye pR^ithivyAM ye antarikSha oShadhIShu pashuShv apsv a1ntaH /
te kR^iNuta jarasam Ayur asmai shatam anyAn pari vR^iNaktu mR^ityUn //3//
yeShAM prayAjA uta vAnuyAjA hutabhAgA ahutAdash cha devAH /
yeShAM vaH pa~ncha pradisho vibhaktAs tAn vo asmai satrasadaH kR^iNomi //4//
31

AshAnAm AshApAlebhyash chaturbhyo amR^itebhyaH /
idaM bhUtasyAdhyakShebhyo vidhema haviShA vayam //1//
ya AshAnAm AshApAlAsh chatvAra sthana devAH /
te no nirR^ityAH pAshebhyo mu~nchatAMhasoaMhasaH //2//
asrAmas tvA haviShA yajAmy ashloNas tvA ghR^itena juhomi /
ya AshAnAm AshApAlas turIyo devaH sa naH subhUtam eha vakShat //3//
svasti mAtra uta pitre no astu svasti gobhyo jagate puruShebhyaH /
vishvam subhUtam suvidatraM no astu jyog eva dR^ishema sUryam //4//
32

idaM janAso vidatha mahad brahma vadiShyati /
na tat pR^ithivyAM no divi yena prANanti vIrudhaH //1//
antarikSha AsAM sthAma shrAntasadAm iva /
AsthAnam asya bhUtasya viduSh Tad vedhaso na vA //2//
yad rodasI rejamAne bhUmish cha niratakShatam /
ArdraM tad adya sarvadA samudrasyeva shrotyAH //3//
vishvam anyAm abhIvAra tad anyasyAm adhi shritam /
dive cha vishvavedase pR^ithivyai chAkaraM namaH //4//
33

hiraNyavarNAH shuchayaH pAvakA yAsu jAtaH savitA yAsv agniH /
yA agniM garbhaM dadhire suvarNAs tA na ApaH shaM syonA bhavantu //1//
yAsAM rAjA varuNo yAti madhye satyAnR^ite avapashyan janAnAm /
yA agniM garbhaM dadhire suvarNAs tA na ApaH shaM syonA bhavantu //2//
yAsAM devA divi kR^iNvanti bhakShaM yA antarikShe bahudhA bhavanti /
yA agniM garbhaM dadhire suvarNAs tA na ApaH shaM syonA bhavantu //3//
shivena mA chakShuShA pashyatApaH shivayA tanvopa spR^ishata tvachaM me /
ghR^itashchutaH shuchayo yAH pAvakAs tA na ApaH shaM syonA bhavantu //4//
34

iyaM vIrun madhujAtA madhunA tvA khanAmasi /
madhor adhi prajAtAsi sA no madhumatas kR^idhi //1//
jihvAyA agre madhu me jihvAmUle madhUlakam /
mamed aha kratAv aso mama chittam upAyasi //2//
madhuman me nikramaNaM madhuman me parAyaNam /
vAchA vadAmi madhumad bhUyAsaM madhusaMdR^ishaH //3//
madhor asmi madhutaro madughAn madhumattaraH /
mAm it kila tvaM vanAH shAkhAM madhumatIm iva //4//
pari tvA paritatnunekShuNAgAm avidviShe /
yathA mAM kaminy aso yathA man nApagA asaH //5//
35

yad Abadhnan dAkShAyaNA hiraNyaM shatAnIkAya sumanasyamAnAH /
tat te badnAmy AyuShe varchase balAya dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya //1//
nainaM rakShAMsi na pishAchAH sahante devAnAm ojaH prathamajam hy etat /
yo bibharti dAkShAyaNaM hiraNyaM sa jIveShu kR^iNute dIrgham AyuH //2//
apAM tejo jyotir ojo balaM cha vanaspatInAm uta vIryANi /
indra ivendriyANy adhi dhArayAmo asmin tad dakShamANo bibharad dhiraNyam //3//
samAnAM mAsAm R^itubhiSh TvA vayaM saMvatsarasya payasA piparmi /
indrAgnI vishve devAs te 'nu manyantAm ahR^iNIyamAnAH //4//
36
Book:
2

venas tat pashyat paramaM guhA yad yatra vishvaM bhavaty ekarUpam /
idaM pR^ishnir aduhaj jAyamAnAH svarvido abhy anUShata vrAH //1//
pra tad voched amR^itasya vidvAn gandharvo dhAma paramaM guhA yat /
trINi padAni nihitA guhAsya yas tAni veda sa pituSh pitAsat //2//
sa naH pitA janitA sa uta bandhur dhAmAni veda bhuvanAni vishvA /
yo devAnAM nAmadha eka eva taM saMprashnaM bhuvanA yanti sarvA //3//
pari dyAvApR^ithivI sadya Ayam upAtiShThe prathamajAm R^itasya /
vAcham iva vaktari bhuvaneShThA dhAsyur eSha nanv e3Sho agniH //4//
pari vishvA bhuvanAny Ayam R^itasya tantuM vitataM dR^ishe kam /
yatra devA amR^itam AnashAnAH samAne yonAv adhy airayanta //5//
37

divyo gandharvo bhuvanasya yas patir eka eva namasyo vikShv IDyaH /
taM tvA yaumi brahmaNA divya deva namas te astu divi te sadhastham //1//
divi spR^iShTo yajataH sUryatvag avayAtA haraso daivyasya /
mR^iDAt gandharvo bhuvanasya yas patir eka eva namasyaH sushevAH //2//
anavadyAbhiH sam u jagma Abhir apsarAsv api gandharva AsIt /
samudra AsAM sadanaM ma Ahur yataH sadya A cha parA cha yanti //3//
abhriye didyun nakShatriye yA vishvAvasuM gandharvaM sachadhve /
tAbhyo vo devIr nama it kR^iNomi //4//
yAH klandAs tamiShIchayo 'kShakAmA manomuhaH /
tAbhyo gandharvabhyo 'psarAbhyo 'karam namaH //5//
38

ado yad avadhAvaty avatkam adhi parvatAt /
tat te kR^iNomi bheShajaM subheShajaM yathAsasi //1//
Ad a~NgA kuvid a~Nga shataM yA bheShajAni te /
teShAm asi tvam uttamam anAsrAvam arogaNam //2//
nIchaiH khananty asurA arusrANam idaM mahat /
tad AsrAvasya bheShajaM tad u rogam anInashat //3//
upajIkA ud bharanti samudrAd adhi bheShajam /
tad AsrAvasya bheShajaM tad u rogam ashIshamat //4//
arusrANam idaM mahat pR^ithivyA adhy udbhR^itam /
tad AsrAvasya bheShajaM tad u rogam anInashat //5//
shaM no bhavantv apa oShadhayaH shivAH /
indrasya vajro apa hantu rakShasa ArAd visR^iShTA iShavaH patantu rakShasAm //6//
39

dIrghAyutvAya bR^ihatM raNAyAriShyanto dakShamANAH sadaiva /
maNiM viShkandhadUShaNaM ja~NgiDaM bibhR^imo vayam //1//
ja~NgiDo jambhAd visharAd viShkandhAd abhishochanAt /
maNiH sahasravIryaH pari NaH pAtu vishvataH //2//
ayaM viShkandhaM sahate 'yaM bAdhate attriNaH /
ayaM no vishvabheShajo ja~NgiDaH pAtv aMhasaH //3//
devair dattena maNinA ja~NgiDena mayobhuvA /
viShkandhaM sarvA rakShAMsi vyAyAme sahAmahe //4//
shaNash cha mA ja~NgiDash cha viShkandhAd abhi rakShatAm /
araNyAd anya AbhR^itaH kR^iShyA anyo rasebhyaH //5//
kR^ityAdUShir ayaM maNir atho arAtidUShiH /
atho sahasvAn ja~NgiDaH pra Na AyuMShi tAriShat //6//
40

indra juShasva pra vahA yAhi shUra haribhyAm /
pibA sutasya mater iha madhosh chakAnash chArur madAya //1//
indra jaTharaM navyo na pR^iNasva madhor divo na /
asya sutasya sva1r Nopa tvA madAH suvAcho aguH //2//
indras turAShAN mitro vR^itraM yo jaghAna yatIr na /
bibheda valaM bhR^igur na sasahe shatrUn made somasya //3//
A tvA vishantu sutAsa indra pR^iNasva kukShI viDDhi shakra dhiyehy A naH /
shrudhI havaM giro me juShasvendra svayugbhir matsveha mahe raNAya //4//
indrasya nu pra vochaM vIryANi yAni chakAra prathamAni vajrI /
ahann ahim anu apas tatarda pra vakShaNA abhinat parvatAnAm //5//
ahann ahiM parvate shishriyANaM tvaShTAsmai vajraM svaryaM tatakSha /
vAshrA iva dhenavaH syandamAnA a~njaH samudram ava jagmur ApaH //6//
vR^iShAyamANo avR^iNIta somaM trikadrukeShu apibat sutasya /
A sAyakaM maghavAdatta vajram ahann enaM prathamajAm ahInAm //7//
41

samAs tvAgna R^itavo vardhayantu saMvatsarA R^iShayo yAni satyA /
saM divyena dIdihi rochanena vishvA A mAhi pradishash chatasraH //1//
saM chedhyasvAgne pra cha vardhayemam uch cha tiShTha mahate saubhagAya /
mA te riShann upasattAro agne brahmANas te yashasaH santu mAnye //2//
tvAm agne vR^iNate brAhmaNA ime shivo agne saMvaraNe bhavA naH /
sapatnahAgne abhimAtijid bhava sve gaye jAgR^ihy aprayuchhan //3//
kShatreNAgne svena saM rabhasva mitreNAgne mitradhA yatasva /
sajAtAnAM madhyameShThA rAj~nAm agne vihavyo dIdihIha //4//
ati niho ati sR^idho 'ty achittIr ati dviShaH /
vishvA hy agne duritA tara tvam athAsmabhyaM sahavIraM rayiM dAH //5//
42

aghadviShTA devajAtA vIruch chhapathayopanI /
Apo malam iva prANaikShIt sarvAn mach chhapathAm? adhi //1//
yash cha sApatnaH shapatho jAmyAH shapathash cha yaH /
brahmA yan manyutaH shapAt sarvaM tan no adhaspadam //2//
divo mUlam avatataM pR^ithivyA adhy uttatam /
tena sahasrakANDena pari NaH pAhi vishvataH //3//
pari mAM pari me prajAM pari NaH pAhi yad dhanam /
arAtir no mA tArIn mA nas tArishur abhimAtayaH //4//
shaptAram etu shapatho yaH suhArt tena naH saha /
chakShurmantrasya durhArdaH pR^iShTIr api shR^iNImasi //5//
43

ud agAtAM bhagavatI vichR^itau nAma tArake /
vi kShetriyasya mu~nchatAm adhamaM pAsham uttamam //1//
apeyaM rAtry uchhatv apochhantv abhikR^itvarIH /
vIrut kShetriyanAshany apa kShetriyam uchhatu //2//
babhror arjunakANDasya yavasya te palAlyA tilasya tilapi~njyA /
vIrut kShetriyanAshany apa kShetriyam uchhatu //3//
namas te lA~Ngalebhyo nama IShAyugebhyaH /
vIrut kShetriyanAshany apa kShetriyam uchhatu //4//
namaH sanisrasAkShebhyo namaH saMdeshyebhyaH /
namaH kShetrasya pataye vIrut kShetriyanAshany apa kShetriyam uchhatu //5//
44

dashavR^ikSha mu~nchemaM rakShaso grAhyA adhi yainaM jagrAha parvasu /
atho enam vanaspate jIvAnAM lokam un naya //1//
AgAd ud agAd ayaM jIvAnAM vrAtam apy agAt /
abhUd u putrANAM pitA nR^iNAM cha bhagavattamaH //2//
adhItIr adhy agAd ayam adhi jIvapurA agAn /
shataM hy asya bhiShajaH sahasram uta vIrudhaH //3//
devAs te chItim avidan brahmANa uta vIrudhaH /
chItiM te vishve devA avidan bhUmyAm adhi //4//
yash chakAra sa niSh karat sa eva subhiShaktamaH /
sa eva tubhyaM bheShajAni kR^iNavad bhiShajA shuchiH //5//
45

kShetriyAt tvA nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //1//
shaM te agniH sahAdbhir astu shaM somaH sahauShadhIbhiH /
evAhaM tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //2//
sham te vAto antarikShe vayo dhAch chhaM te bhavantu pradishash chatasraH /
evAhaM tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //3//
imA yA devIH pradishash chatasro vAtapatnIr abhi sUryo vichaShTe /
evAhaM tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^inomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //4//
tAsu tvAntar jarasy A dadhAmi pra yakShma etu nirR^itiH parAchaiH /
evAhaM tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //5//
amukthA yakShmAd duritAd avadyAd druhaH pAshAd grAhyAsh chod amukthAH /
evAhaM tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //6//
ahA arAtim avidaH syonam apy abhUr bhadre sukR^itasya loke /
evAhaM tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasaM brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //7//
sUryam R^itaM tamaso grAhyA adhi devA mu~nchanto asR^ijan nir enasaH /
evAham tvAM kShetriyAn nirR^ityA jAmishaMsAd druho mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAt /
anAgasam brahmaNA tvA kR^iNomi shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //8//
46

dUShyA dUShir asi hetyA hetir asi menyA menir asi /
Apnuhi shreyAMsam ati samaM krAma //1//
sraktyo 'si pratiSharo 'si pratyabhicharaNo 'si /
Apnuhi shreyAMsam ati samaM krAma //2//
prati tam abhi chara yo 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH /
Apnuhi shreyAMsam ati samaM krAma //3//
sUrir asi varchodhA asi tanUpAno 'si /
Apnuhi shreyAMsam ati samaM krAma //4//
shukro 'si bhrAjo 'si svar asi jyotir asi /
Apnuhi shreyAMsam ati samaM krAma //5//
47

dyAvApR^ithivI urv a1ntarikShaM kShetrasya patny urugAyo 'dbhutaH /
utAntarikSham uru vAtagopaM ta iha tapyantAM mayi tapyamAne //1//
idaM devAH shR^iNuta ye yaj~niyA stha bharadvAjo mahyam ukthAni shaMsati /
pAshe sa baddho durite ni yujyatAM yo asmAkaM mana idaM hinasti //2//
idam indra shR^iNuhi somapa yat tvA hR^idA shochatA johavImi /
vR^ishchAmi taM kulisheneva vR^ikShaM yo asmAkaM mana idaM hinasti //3//
ashItibhis tisR^ibhiH sAmagebhir Adityebhir vasubhir a~NgirobhiH /
iShTApUrtam avatu naH pitR^INAm AmuM dade harasA daivyena //4//
dyAvApR^ithivI anu mA dIdhIthAM vishve devAso anu mA rabhadhvam /
a~NgirasaH pitaraH somyAsaH pApam A R^ichhatv apakAmasya kartA //5//
atIva yo maruto manyate no brahma vA yo nindiShat kriyamANam /
tapUMShi tasmai vR^ijinAni santu brahmadviShaM dyaur abhisaMtapAti //6//
sapta prANAn aShTau manyas tAMs te vR^ishchAmi brahmaNA /
ayA yamasya sAdanam agnidUto araMkR^itaH //7//
A dadhAmi te padaM samiddhe jAtavedasi /
agniH sharIraM veveShTv asuM vAg api gachhatu //8//
48

AyurdA agne jarasaM vR^iNAno ghR^itapratIko ghR^itapR^iShTho agne /
ghR^itaM pItvA madhu chAru gavyaM piteva putrAn abhi rakShatAd imam //1//
pari dhatta dhatta no varchasemam jarAmR^ityuM kR^iNuta dIrgham AyuH /
bR^ihaspatiH prAyachhad vAsa etat somAya rAj~ne paridhAtavA u //2//
parIdaM vAso adhithAH svastaye 'bhUr gR^iShTInAm abhishastipA u /
shataM cha jIva sharadaH purUchI rAyash cha poSham upasaMvyayasva //3//
ehy ashmAnam A tiShThAshmA bhavatu te tanUH /
kR^iNvantu vishve devA AyuShTe sharadaH shatam //4//
yasya te vAsaH prathamavAsya1M harAmas taM tvA vishve 'vantu devAH /
taM tvA bhrAtaraH suvR^idhA vardhamAnam anu jAyantAM bahavaH sujAtam //5//
49

niHsAlAM dhR^iShNuM dhiShaNam ekavAdyAm jighatsvam /
sarvAsh chaNDasya naptyo nAshayAmaH sadAnvAH //1//
nir vo goShThAd ajAmasi nir akShAn nir upAnashAt /
nir vo magundyA duhitaro gR^ihebhyash chAtayAmahe //2//
asau yo adharAd gR^ihas tatra santv arAyyaH /
tatra sedir ny ùchyatu sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyaH //3//
bhUtapatir nir ajatv indrash chetaH sadAnvAH /
gR^ihasya budhna AsInAs tA indro vajreNAdhi tiShThatu //4//
yadi stha kShetriyANAM yadi vA puruSheShitAH /
yadi stha dasyubhyo jAtA nashyatetaH sadAnvAH //5//
pari dhAmAny AsAm Ashur gAShThAm ivAsaram /
ajaiShaM sarvAn AjIn vo nashyatetaH sadAnvAH //6//
50

yathA dyaush cha pR^ithivI cha na bibhIto na riShyataH /
evA me prANa mA bibheH //1//
yathAhash cha rAtrI cha na bibhIto na riShyataH /
evA me prANa mA bibheH //2//
yathA sUryash cha chandrash cha na bibhIto na riShyataH /
evA me prANa mA bibheH //3//
yathA brahma cha kShatraM cha na bibhIto na riShyataH /
evA me prANa mA bibheH //4//
yathA satyaM chAnR^itaM cha na bibhIto na riShyataH /
evA me prANa mA bibheH //5//
yathA bhUtaM cha bhavyaM cha na bibhIto na riShyataH /
evA me prANa mA bibheH //6//
51

prANApAnau mR^ityor mA pAtaM svAhA //1//
dyAvApR^ithivI upashrutyA mA pAtaM svAhA //2//
sUrya chakShuShA mA pAhi svAhA //3//
agne vaishvAnara vishvair mA devaiH pAhi svAhA //4//
vishvambhara vishvena mA bharasA pAhi svAhA //5//
52

ojo 'sy ojo me dAH svAhA /1//
saho 'si saho me dAH svAhA //2//
balam asi balaM dAH svAhA //3//
Ayur asy Ayur me dAH svAha //4//
shrotram asi shrotraM me dAH svAha //5//
chakShur asi chakShur me dAH svAha //6//
paripANam asi paripANaM me dAH svAha //7//
53

bhrAtR^ivyakShayaNam asi bhrAtR^ivyachAtanaM me dAH svAha //1//
sapatnakShayaNam asi sapatnachAtanaM me dAH svAha //2//
arAyakShayaNam asy arAyachAtanaM me dAH svAha //3//
pishAchakShayaNam asi pishAchachAtanaM me dAH svAha //4//
sadAnvAkShayaNam asi sadAnvAchAtanaM me dAH svAha //5//
54

agne yat te tapas tena taM prati tapa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //1//
agne yat te haras tena taM prati hara yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //2//
agne yat te 'rchis tena taM praty archa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //3//
agne yat te shochis tena taM prati shocha yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //4//
agne yat te tejas tena tam atejasaM kR^iNu yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //5//
55

vAyo yat te tapas tena taM prati tapa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //1//
vAyo yat te haras tena taM prati hara yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //2//
vAyo yat te 'rchis tena taM praty archa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //3//
vAyo yat te shochis tena taM prati shocha yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //4//
vAyo yat te tejas tena tam atejasaM kR^iNu yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //5//
56

sUrya yat te tapas tena taM prati tapa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //1//
sUrya yat te haras tena taM prati hara yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //2//
sUrya yat te 'rchis tena taM praty archa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //3//
sUrya yat te shochis tena taM prati shocha yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //4//
sUrya yat te tejas tena tam atejasaM kR^iNu yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //5//
57

chandra yat te tapas tena taM prati tapa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //1//
chandra yat te haras tena taM prati hara yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //2//
chandra yat te 'rchis tena taM praty archa yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //3//
chandra yat te shochis tena taM prati shocha yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //4//
chandra yat te tejas tena tam atejasaM kR^iNu yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //5//
58

Apo yad vas tapas tena taM prati tapata yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //1//
Apo yad vas haras tena taM prati harata yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //2//
Apo yad vas 'rchis tena taM prati archata yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //3//
Apo yad vas shochis tena taM prati shochata yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //4//
Apo yad vas tejas tena tam atejasaM kR^iNuta yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //5//
59

sherabhaka sherabha punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //1//
shevR^idhaka shevR^idha punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //2//
mrokAnumroka punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //3//
sarpAnusarpa punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //4//
jUrNi punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //5//
upabde punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //6//
arjuni punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //7//
bharUji punar vo yantu yAtavaH punar hetiH kimIdinaH /
yasya stha tam atta yo vo prAhait tam atta svA mAMsAny atta //8//
60

shaM no devI pR^ishniparNy ashaM nirR^ityA akaH /
ugrA hi kaNvajambhanI tAm abhakShi sahasvatIm //1//
sahamAneyaM prathamA pR^ishniparNy ajAyata /
tayAhaM durNAmnAM shiro vR^ishchAmi shakuner iva //2//
arAyam asR^ikpAvAnaM yash cha sphAtiM jihIrShati /
garbhAdaM kaNvaM nAshaya pR^ishniparNi sahasva cha //3//
girim enAm? A veshaya kaNvAn jIvitayopanAn /
tAMs tvaM devi pR^ishniparNy agnir ivAnudahann ihi //4//
parAcha enAn pra Nuda kaNvAn jIvitayopanAn /
tamAMsi yatra gachhanti tat kravyAdo ajIgamam //5//
61

eha yantu pashavo ye pareyur vAyur yeShAM sahachAraM jujoSha /
tvaShTA yeShAM rUpadheyAni vedAsmin tAn goShThe savitA ni yachhatu //1//
imaM goShThaM pashavaH saM sravantu bR^ihaspatir A nayatu prajAnan /
sinIvAlI nayatv Agram eShAm AjagmuSho anumate ni yachha //2//
saM saM sravantu pashavaH sam ashvAH sam u pUruShAH /
saM dhAnyasya yA sphAtiH saMsrAvyeNa haviShA juhomi //3//
saM si~nchAmi gavAM kShIram sam Ajyena balam rasam /
saMsiktA asmAkaM vIrA dhruvA gAvo mayi gopatau //4//
A harAmi gavAM kShIram AhArShaM dhAnya1M rasam /
AhR^itA asmAkaM vIrA A patnIr idam astakam //5//
62

nech chhatruH prAshaM jayAti sahamAnAbhibhUr asi /
prAshaM pratiprAsho jahy arasAn kR^inv oShadhe //1//
suparNas tvAnv avindat sUkaras tvAkhanan nasA /
prAshaM pratiprAsho jahy arasAn kR^iNv oShadhe //2//
indro ha chakre tvA bAhAv asurebhya starItave /
prAshaM pratiprAsho jahy arasAn kR^iNv oShadhe //3//
pATAm indro vy AshnAd asurebhya starItave /
prAshaM pratiprAsho jahy arasAn kR^iNv oShadhe //4//
tayAhaM shatrUnt sAkShe indraH sAlAvR^ikA.N iva /
prAshaM pratiprAsho jahy arasAn kR^iNv oShadhe //5//
rudra jalAShabheShaja nIlashikhaNDa karmakR^it /
prAshaM pratiprAsho jahy arasAn kR^iNv oShadhe //6//
tasya prAshaM tvaM jahi yo na indrAbhidAsati /
adhi no brUhi shaktibhiH prAshi mAm uttaraM kR^idhi //7//
63

tubhyam eva jariman vardhatAm ayam memam anye mR^ityavo hiMsiShuH shataM ye /
mAteva putraM pramanA upasthe mitra enaM mitriyAt pAtv aMhasaH //1//
mitra enaM varuNo vA rishAdA jarAmR^ityuM kR^iNutAM saMvidAnau /
tad agnir hotA vayunAni vidvAn vishvA devAnAM janimA vivakti //2//
tvam IshiShe pashUnAm pArthivAnAM ye jAtA uta vA ye janitrAH /
memaM prANo hAsIn mo apAno memaM mitrA vadhiShur mo amitrAH //3//
dyauSh TvA pitA pR^ithivI mAtA jarAmR^ityuM kR^iNutAM saMvidAne /
yathA jIvA aditer upasthe prANApAnAbhyAM gupitaH shataM himAH //4//
imam agne AyuShe varchase naya priyaM reto varuNa mitra rAjan /
mAtevAsmA adite sharma yachha vishve devA jaradaShTir yathAsat //5//
64

pArthivasya rase devA bhagasya tanvo3 bale /
AyuShyam asmA agniH sUryo varcha A dhAd bR^ihaspatiH //1//
Ayur asmai dhehi jAtavedaH prajAM tvaShTar adhinidhehi asmai /
rAyas poShaM savitar A suvAsmai shataM jIvAti sharadas tavAyam //2//
AshIr Na Urjam uta sauprajAstvaM dakShaM dhattaM draviNaM sachetasau /
jayam kShetrANi sahasAyam indra kR^iNvAno anyAn adharAnt sapatnAn //3//
indreNa datto varuNena shiShTo marudbhir ugraH prahito no Agan /
eSha vAM dyAvApR^ithivI upasthe mA kShudhan mA tR^iShat //4//
Urjam asmA UrjasvatI dhattaM payo asmai payasvatI dhattam /
Urjam asmai dyAvapR^ithivI adhAtAM vishve devA maruta Urjam ApaH //5//
shivAbhiSh Te hR^idayaM tarpayAmy anamIvo modiShIShThAH suvarchAH /
savAsinau pibatAM mantham etam ashvino rUpaM paridhAya mAyAm //6//
indra etAM sasR^ije viddho agra UrjAM svadhAm ajarAM sA ta eShA /
tayA tvaM jIva sharadah suvarchA mA ta A susrod bhiShajas te akran //7//
65

yathedaM bhUmyA adhi tR^iNaM vAto mathAyati /
evA mathnAmi te mano yathA mAM kAminy aso yathA man nApagA asaH //1//
saM chen nayAtho ashvinA kAminA saM cha vakShathaH /
saM vAM bhagAso agmata saM chittAni sam u vratA //2//
yat suparNA vivakShavo anamIvA vivakShavaH /
tatra me gachhatAd dhavaM shalya iva kulmalaM yathA //3//
yad antaraM tad bAhyaM yad bAhyaM tad antaram /
kanyAnAM vishvarUpANAM mano gR^ibhAyauShadhe //4//
eyam agan patikAmA janikAmo 'ham Agamam /
ashvaH kanikradad yathA bhagenAhaM sahAgamam //5//
66

indrasya yA mahI dR^iShat krimer vishvasya tarhaNI /
tayA pinaShmi saM krimIn dR^iShadA khalvAm? iva //1//
dR^iShTam adR^iShTam atR^iham atho kurUrum atR^iham /
algaNDUnt sarvAn chhalunAn krimIn vachasA jambhayAmasi //2//
algaNDUn hanmi mahatA vadhena dUnA adUnA arasA abhUvan /
shiShTAn ashiShTAn ni tirAmi vAchA yathA krimINAM nakir uchhiShAtai //3//
anvAntryaM shIrShaNya1m atho pArShTeyaM krimIn /
avaskavaM vyadhvaraM krimIn vachasA jambhayAmasi //4//
ye krimayaH parvateshu vaneShv oShadhIShu pashuShv apsv a1ntaH /
ye asmAkaM tanvam AvivishuH sarvaM tad dhanmi janima krimINAm //5//
67

udyann AdityaH krimIn hantu nimrochan hantu rashmibhiH /
ye antaH krimayo gavi //1//
vishvarUpaM chaturakShaM krimiM sAra~Ngam arjunam /
shR^iNAmy asya pR^iShTIr api vR^ishchAmi yach chhiraH //2//
atrivad vaH krimayo hanmi kaNvavaj jamadagnivat /
agastyasya brahmaNA saM pinaShmy ahaM krimIn //3//
hato rAjA krimINAm utaiShAM sthapatir hataH /
hato hatamAtA krimir hatabhrAtA hatasvasA //4//
hatAso asya veshaso hatAsaH pariveshasaH /
atho ye kShullakA iva sarve te krimayo hatAH //5//
pra te shR^iNAmi shR^i~Nge yAbhyAM vitudAyasi /
bhinAdmi te kuShumbhaM yas te viShadhAnaH //6//
68

akShIbhyAM te nAsikAbhyAM karNAbhyAM chhubukAd adhi /
yakShmaM shIrShaNyaM mastiShkAj jihvAyA vi vR^ihAmi te //1//
grIvAbhyas ta uShNihAbhyaH kIkasAbhyo anUkyAt /
yakShmaM doShanya1m aMsAbhyAM bAhubhyAM vi vR^ihAmi te //2//
hR^idayAt te pari klomno halIkShNAt pArshvAbhyAm /
yakShmaM matasnAbhyAM plIhno yaknas te vi vR^ihAmasi //3//
Antrebhyas te gudAbhyo vaniShThor udarAd adhi /
yakShmaM kukShibhyAm plAsher nAbhyA vi vR^ihAmi te //4//
UrubhyAM te aShThIvadbhyAM pArShNibhyAM prapadAbhyAm /
yakShmaM bhasadya1M shroNibhyAM bhAsadaM bhaMsaso vi vR^ihAmi te //5//
asthibhyas te majjabhyaH snAvabhyo dhamanibhyaH /
yakShmam pANibhyAm a~Ngulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vR^ihAmi te //6//
a~Ngea~Nge lomnilomni yas te parvaNiparvaNi /
yakShmaM tvachasyaM te vayaM kashyapasya vIbarheNa viShva~nchaM vi vR^ihAmasi //7//
69

ya Ishe pashupatiH pashUnAm chatuShpadAm uta yo dvipadAm /
niShkrItaH sa yaj~niyaM bhAgam etu rAyas poShA yajamAnaM sachantAm //1//
pramu~nchanto bhuvanasya reto gAtuM dhatta yajamAnAya devAH /
upAkR^itaM shashamAnaM yad asthAt priyam devAnAm apy etu pAthaH //2//
ye badhyamAnam anu dIdhyAnA anvaikShanta manasA chakShuShA cha /
agniSh TAn agre pra mumoktu devo vishvakarmA prajayA saMrarANaH //3//
ye grAmyAH pashavo vishvarUpA virUpAH santo bahudhaikarUpAH /
vAyuSh TAn agre pra mumoktu devaH prajApatiH prajayA saMrarANaH //4//
prajAnantaH prati gR^ihNantu pUrve prANam a~NgebhyaH pary Acharantam /
divaM gachha prati tiShThA sharIraiH svargaM yAhi pathibhir devayAnaiH //5//
70

ye bhakShayanto na vasUny AnR^idhur yAn agnayo anvatapyanta dhiShNyAH /
yA teShAm avayA duriShTiH svìShTiM nas tAM kR^iNavad vishvakarmA //1//
yaj~napatim R^iShayaH enasAhur nirbhaktaM prajA anutapyamAnam /
mathavyAnt stokAn apa yAn rarAdha saM naSh TebhiH sR^ijatu vishvakarmA //2//
adAnyAnt somapAn manyamAno yaj~nasya vidvAnt samaye na dhIraH /
yad enash chakR^ivAn baddha eSha taM vishvakarman pra mu~nchA svastaye //3//
ghorA R^iShayo namo astv ebhyash chakShur yad eShAM manasash cha satyam /
bR^ihaspataye mahiSha dyumann namo vishvakarman namas te pAhy a1smAn //4//
yaj~nasya chakShuH prabhR^itir mukhaM cha vAchA shrotreNa manasA juhomi /
imaM yaj~naM vitataM vishvakarmaNA devA yantu sumanasyamAnAH //5//
71

A no agne sumatiM saMbhalo gamed imAM kumArIM saha no bhagena /
juShTA vareShu samaneShu valgur oShaM patyA saubhagam astu asyai //1//
somajuShTaM brahmajuShTam aryamnA saMbhR^itaM bhagam /
dhAtur devasya satyena kR^iNomi pativedanam //2//
iyam agne nArI patim videShTa somo hi rAjA subhagAM kR^iNoti /
suvAnA putrAn mahiShI bhavAti gatvA patiM subhagA vi rAjatu //3//
yathAkharo maghavaMsh chArur eSha priyo mR^igANAM suShadA babhUva /
evA bhagasya juShTeyam astu nArI saMpriyA patyAvirAdhayantI //4//
bhagasya nAvam A roha pUrNAm anupadasvatIm /
tayopapratAraya yo varaH pratikAmyaH //5//
A krandaya dhanapate varam AmanasaM kR^iNu /
sarvaM pradakShiNaM kR^iNu yo varaH pratikAmyaH //6//
idaM hiraNyaM gulgulv ayam aukSho atho bhagaH /
ete patibhyas tvAm aduH pratikAmAya vettave //7
A te nayatu savitA nayatu patir yaH pratikAmyaH /
tvam asyai dhehi oShadhe //8//
72
Book:
3

agnir naH shatrUn praty etu vidvAn pratidahann abhishastim arAtim /
sa senAM mohayatu pareShAM nirhastAMsh cha kR^iNavaj jAtavedAH //1//
yUyam ugrA maruta IdR^ishe sthAbhi preta mR^iNata sahadhvam /
amImR^iNan vasavo nAthitA ime agnir hy eShAM dUtaH pratyetu vidvAn //2//
amitrasenAM maghavann asmAn chhatrUyatIm abhi /
yuvaM tAm indra vR^itrahann agnish cha dahataM prati //3//
prasUta indra pravatA haribhyAM pra te vajraH pramR^iNann etu shatrUn /
jahi pratIcho anUchaH parAcho viShvak satyaM kR^iNuhi chittam eShAm //4//
indra senAM mohayAmitrANAm /
agner vAtasya dhrAjyA tAn viShUcho vi nAshaya //5//
indraH senAM mohayatu maruto ghnantv ojasA /
chakShUMsy agnir A dattAM punar etu parAjitA //6//
73

agnir no dUtaH pratyetu vidvAn pratidahann abhishastim arAtim /
sa chittAni mohayatu pareShAM nirhastAMsh cha kR^iNavaj jAtavedAH //1//
ayam agnir amUmuhad yAni chittAni vo hR^idi /
vi vo dhamatv okasaH pra vo dhamatu sarvataH //2//
indra chittAni mohayann arvA~N AkUtyA chara /
agner vAtasya dhrAjyA tAn viShUcho vi nAshaya //3//
vy AkUtaya eShAm itAtho chittAni muhyata /
atho yad adyaiShAM hR^idi tad eShAM pari nir jahi //4//
amIShAM chittAni pratimohayantI gR^ihANA~NgAny apve parehi /
abhi prehi nir daha hR^itsu shokair grAhyAmitrAMs tamasA vidhya shatrUn //5//
asau yA senA marutaH pareShAm asmAn aity abhy ojasA spardhamAnA /
tAm vidhyata tamasApavratena yathaiShAm anyo anyaM na jAnAt //6//
74

achikradat svapA iha bhuvad agne vy achasva rodasI urUchI /
yu~njantu tvA maruto vishvavedasa AmuM naya namasA rAtahavyam //1//
dUre chit santam aruShAsa indram A chyAvayantu sakhyAya vipram /
yad gAyatrIM bR^ihatIm arkam asmai sautrAmaNyA dadhR^iShanta devAH //2//
adbhyas tvA rAja varuNo hvayatu somas tvA hvayatu parvatebhyaH /
indras tvA hvayatu viDbhya AbhyaH shyeno bhUtvA visha A patemAH //3// {4}

shyeno havyaM nayatv A parasmAd anyakShetre aparuddhaM charantam /
ashvinA panthAM kR^iNutAM sugaM ta imaM sajAtA abhisaMvishadhvam //4// {5}

hvayantu tvA pratijanAH prati mitrA avR^iShata /
indrAgnI vishve devAs te vishi kShemam adIdharan //5// {6}

yas te havaM vivadat sajAto yash cha niShTyaH /
apA~ncham indra taM kR^itvAthemam ihAva gamaya //6// {7}

75

A tvA gan rAShtraM saha varchasod ihi prA~N vishAM patir ekarAT tvaM vi rAja /
sarvAs tvA rAjan pradisho hvayantUpasadyo namasyo bhaveha //1//
tvAM visho vR^iNatAM rAjyAya tvAm imAH pradishaH pa~ncha devIH /
varShman rAShTrasya kakudi shrayasva tato na ugro vi bhajA vasUni //2//
achha tvA yantu havinaH sajAtA agnir dUto ajiraH saM charAtai /
jAyAH putrAH sumanaso bhavantu bahuM baliM prati pashyAsA ugraH //3//
ashvinA tvAgre mitrAvaruNobhA vishve devA marutas tvA hvayantu /
adhA mano vasudeyAya kR^iNuShva tato na ugro vi bhajA vasUni //4//
A pra drava paramasyAH parAvataH shive te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm /
tad ayaM rAjA varuNas tathAha sa tvAyam ahvat sa upedam ehi //5//
indrendra manuShyA3H parehi saM hy aj~nAsthA varuNaiH saMvidAnaH /
sa tvAyam ahvat sve sadhasthe sa devAn yakShat sa u kalpayad vishaH //6//
pathyA revatIr bahudhA virUpAH sarvAH saMgatya varIyas te akran /
tAs tvA sarvAH saMvidAnA hvayantu dashamIm ugraH sumanA vasheha //7//
76

Ayam agan parNamaNir balI balena pramR^iNant sapatnAn /
ojo devAnAM paya oShadhInAM varchasA mA jinvantv aprayAvan //1//
mayi kShatraM parNamaNe mayi dhArayatAd rayim /
ahaM rAShTrasyAbhIvarge nijo bhUyAsam uttamaH //2//
yaM nidadhur vanaspatau guhyaM devAH priyaM maNim /
tam asmabhyaM sahAyuShA devA dadatu bhartave //3//
somasya parNaH saha ugram Agann indreNa datto varuNena shiShTaH /
taM priyAsaM bahu rochamAno dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya //4//
A mArukShat parNamaNir mahyA ariShTatAtaye /
yathAham uttaro 'sAny aryamNa uta saMvidaH //5//
ye dhIvAno rathakArAH karmArA ye manIShiNaH /
upastIn parNa mahyaM tvaM sarvAn kR^iNv abhito janAn //6//
ye rAjAno rAjakR^itaH sUtA grAmaNyash cha ye /
upastIn parNa mahyaM tvaM sarvAn kR^iNv abhito janAn //7//
parNo 'si tanUpAnaH sayonir vIro vIreNa mayA /
saMvatsarasya tejasA tena badhnAmi tvA maNe //8//
77

pumAn puMsaH parijAto 'shvatthaH khadirAd adhi /
sa hantu shatrUn mAmakAn yAn ahaM dveShmi ye cha mAm //1//
tAn ashvattha niH shR^iNIhi shatrUn vaibAdhadodhataH /
indreNa vR^itraghnA medI mitreNa varuNena cha //2//
yathAshvattha nirabhano 'ntar mahaty arNave /
evA tAnt sarvAn nir bha~Ngdhi yAn ahaM dveShmi ye cha mAm //3//
yaH sahamAnash charasi sAsahAna iva R^iShabhaH /
tenAshvattha tvayA vayaM sapatnAnt sahiShImahi //4//
sinAtv enAn nirR^itir mR^ityoH pAshair amokyaiH /
ashvattha shatrUn mAmakAn yAn ahaM dveShmi ye cha mAm //5//
yathAshvattha vAnaspatyAn Arohan kR^iNuShe 'dharAn /
evA me shatror mUrdhAnaM viShvag bhinddhi sahasva cha //6//
te 'dharA~nchaH pra plavantAM chhinnA naur iva bandhanAt /
na vaibAdhapraNuttAnAM punar asti nivartanam //7//
praiNAn nude manasA pra chittenota brahmaNA /
praiNAn vR^ikShasya shAkhayAshvatthasya nudAmahe //8//
78

hariNasya raghuShyado 'dhi shIrShaNi bheShajam /
sa kShetriyaM viShANayA viShUchInam anInashat //1//
anu tvA hariNo vR^iShA padbhish chaturbhir akramIt /
viShANe vi Shya guShpitaM yad asya kShetriyaM hR^idi //2//
ado yad avarochate chatuShpakSham iva chhadiH /
tenA te sarvaM kShetriyam a~Ngebhyo nAshayAmasi //3//
amU ye divi subhage vichR^itau nAma tArake /
vi kShetriyasya mu~nchatAm adhamaM pAsham uttamam //4//
Apa id vA u bheShajIr Apo amIvachAtanIH /
Apo vishvasya bheShajIs tAs tvA mu~nchantu kShetriyAt //5//
yad AsuteH kriyamAnAyAH kShetriyaM tvA vyAnashe /
vedAhaM tasya bheShajaM kShetriyaM nAshayAmi tvat //6//
apavAse nakShatrANAm apavAsa uShasAm uta /
apAsmat sarvaM durbhUtam apa kShetriyam uchhatu //7//
79

A yAtu mitra R^itubhiH kalpamAnaH saMveshayan pR^ithivIm usriyAbhiH /
athAsmabhyam varuNo vAyur agnir bR^ihad rAShTraM saMveshyam dadhAtu //1//
dhAtA rAtiH savitedaM jushantAm indras tvaShTA prati haryantu me vachaH /
huve devIm aditiM shUraputrAM sajAtAnAM madhyameShThA yathAsAni //2//
huve somaM savitAraM namobhir vishvAn AdityA.N aham uttaratve /
ayam agnir dIdAyad dIrgham eva sajAtair iddho 'pratibruvadbhiH //3//
ihed asAtha na paro gamAtheryo gopAH puShTapatir va Ajat /
asmai kAmAyopa kAminIr vishve vo devA upasaMyantu //4//
saM vo manAMsi saM vratA sam AkUtIr namAmasi /
amI ye vivratA sthana tAn vaH saM namayAmasi //5//
ahaM gR^ibhNAmi manasA manAMsi mama chittam anu chittebhir eta /
mama vasheShu hR^idayAni vaH kR^iNomi mama yAtam anuvartmAna eta //6//
80

karshaphasya vishaphasya dyauH pitA pR^ithivI mAtA /
yathAbhichakra devAs tathApa kR^iNutA punaH //1//
ashreShmANo adhArayan tathA tan manunA kR^itam /
kR^iNomi vadhri viShkandhaM muShkAbarho gavAm iva //2//
pisha~Nge sUtre khR^igalaM tad A badhnanti vedhasaH /
shravasyuM shuShmaM kAbavaM vadhriM kR^iNvantu bandhuraH //3//
yenA shravasyavash charatha devA ivAsuramAyayA /
shunAM kapir iva dUShaNo bandhurA kAbavasya cha //4//
duShTyai hi tvA bhatsyAmi dUShayiShyAmi kAbavam /
ud Ashavo rathA iva shapathebhiH sariShyatha //5//
ekashataM viShkandhAni viShThitA pR^ithivIm anu /
teShAM tvAm agre uj jaharur maNiM viShkandhadUShaNam //6//
81

prathamA ha vy ùvAsa sA dhenur abhavad yame /
sA naH payasvatI duhAm uttarAmuttarAm samAm //1//
yAM devAH pratinandanti rAtrim dhenum upAyatIm /
saMvatsarasya yA patnI sA no astu suma~NgalI //2//
saMvatsarasya pratimAM yAM tvA rAtry upAsmahe /
sA na AyuShmatIM prajAM rAyas poSheNa saM sR^ija //3//
iyam eva sA yA prathamA vyauchhad Asv itarAsu charati praviShTA /
mahAnto asyAM mahimAno antar vadhUr jigAya navagaj janitrI //4//
vAnaspatyA grAvANo ghoSham akrata haviSh kR^iNvantaH parivatsarINam /
ekAShTake suprajasaH suvIrA vayaM syAma patayo rayINAm //5//
iDAyAs padaM ghR^itavat sarIsR^ipaM jAtavedaH prati havyA gR^ibhAya /
ye grAmyAH pashavo vishvarUpAs teShAM saptAnAM mayi rantir astu //6//
A mA puShTe cha poShe cha rAtri devAnAM sumatau syAma /
pUrNA darve parA pata supUrNA punar A pata /
sarvAn yaj~nAnt saMbhu~njatISham UrjaM na A bhara //7//
Ayam agant saMvatsaraH patir ekAShTake tava /
sA na AyuShmatIM prajAM rAyas poSheNa saM sR^ija //8//
R^itUn yaja R^itupatIn ArtavAn uta hAyanAn /
samAH saMvatsarAn mAsAn bhUtasya pataye yaje //9//
R^itubhyaSh TvArtavebhyo mAdbhyaH saMvatsarebhyaH /
dhAtre vidhAtre samR^idhe bhUtasya pataye yaje //10//
iDayA juhvato vayaM devAn ghR^itavatA yaje /
gR^ihAn alubhyato vayaM saM vishemopa gomataH //11//
ekAShTakA tapasA tapyamAnA jajAna garbhaM mahimAnam indram /
tena devA vy asahanta shatrUn hantA dasyUnAm abhavach chhachIpatiH //12//
indraputre somaputre duhitAsi prajApateH /
kAmAn asmAkaM pUraya prati gR^ihNAhi no haviH //13//
82

mu~nchAmi tvA haviShA jIvanAya kam aj~nAtayakShmAd uta rAjayakShmAt /
grAhir jagrAha yady etad enaM tasyA indrAgnI pra mumuktam enam //1//
yadi kShitAyur yadi vA pareto yadi mR^ityor antikaM eva /
tam A harAmi nirR^iter upasthAd aspArsham enaM shatashAradAya //2//
sahasrAkSheNa shatavIryeNa shatAyuShA haviShAhArSham enam /
indro yathainaM sharado nayAty ati vishvasya duritasya pAram //3//
shataM jIva sharado vardhamAnaH shataM hemantAn chhatam u vasantAn /
shataM te indro agniH savitA bR^ihaspatiH shatAyuShA haviShAhArSham enam //4//
pra vishataM prANApAnAv anaDvAhAv iva vrajam /
vy anye yantu mR^ityavo yAn Ahur itarAn chhatam //5//
ihaiva staM prANApAnau mApa gAtam ito yuvam /
sharIram asyA~NgAni jarase vahataM punaH //6//
jarAyai tvA pari dadAmi jarAyai ni dhuvAmi tvA /
jarA tvA bhadrA neShTa vy anye yantu mR^ityavo yAn Ahur itarAn chhatam //7//
abhi tvA jarimAhita gAm ukShaNam iva rajjvA /
yas tvA mR^ityur abhyadhatta jAyamAnaM supAshayA /
taM te satyasya hastAbhyAm ud amu~nchad bR^ihaspatiH //8//
83

ihaiva dhruvAM ni minomi shAlAM kSheme tiShThAti ghR^itam ukShamANA /
tAM tvA shAle sarvavIrAH suvIrA ariShTavIrA upa saM charema //1//
ihaiva dhruvA prati tiShTha shAle 'shvAvatI gomatI sUnR^itAvatI /
UrjasvatI ghR^itavatI payasvaty uch chhrayasva mahate saubhagAya //2//
dharuNy asi shAle bR^ihachhandAH pUtidhAnyA /
A tvA vatso gamed A kumAra A dhenavaH sAyam AspandamAnAH //3//
imAM shAlAM savitA vAyur indro bR^ihaspatir ni minotu prajAnan /
ukShantUdrA maruto ghR^itena bhago no rAjA ni kR^iShiM tanotu //4//
mAnasya patni sharaNA syonA devI devebhir nimitAsy agre /
tR^iNaM vasAnA sumanA asas tvam athAsmabhyaM sahavIraM rayiM dAH //5//
R^itena sthUNAm adhi roha vaMshogro virAjann apa vR^i~NkShva shatrUn /
mA te riShann upasattAro gR^ihANAM shAle shataM jIvema sharadaH sarvavIrAH //6//
emAm kumAras taruNa A vatso jagatA saha /
emAm parisrutaH kumbha A dadhnaH kalashair aguH //7//
pUrNaM nAri pra bhara kumbham etaM ghR^itasya dhArAm amR^itena saMbhR^itAm /
imAM pAtR^In amR^itena sam a~NgdhIShTApUrtam abhi rakShAty enAm //8//
imA ApaH pra bharAmy ayakShmA yakShmanAshanIH /
gR^ihAn upa pra sIdAmy amR^itena sahAgninA //9//
84

yad adaH saMprayatIr ahAv anadatA hate /
tasmAd A nadyo3 nAma stha tA vo nAmAni sindhavaH //1//
yat preShitA varuNenAch chhIbham samavalgata /
tad Apnod indro vo yatIs tasmAd Apo anu ShThana //2//
apakAmaM syandamAnA avIvarata vo hi kam /
indro vaH shaktibhir devIs tasmAd vAr nAma vo hitam //3//
ekaH vo devo 'py atiShThat syandamAnA yathAvasham /
ud AniShur mahIr iti tasmAd udakam uchyate //4//
Apo bhadrA ghR^itam id Apa Asann agnIShomau bibhraty Apa it tAH /
tIvro raso madhupR^ichAm araMgama A mA prANena saha varchasA gamet //5//
Ad it pashyAmy uta vA shR^iNomy A mA ghoSho gachhati vA~N mAsAm /
manye bhejAno amR^itasya tarhi hiraNyavarNA atR^ipaM yadA vaH //6//
idaM va Apo hR^idayam ayaM vatsa R^itAvarIH /
ihettham eta shakvarIr yatredaM veshayAmi vaH //7//
85

saM vo goShThena suShadA saM rayyA saM subhUtyA /
aharjAtasya yan nAma tenA vaH saM sR^ijAmasi //1//
saM vaH sR^ijatv aryamA saM pUShA saM bR^ihaspatiH /
sam indro yo dhanaMjayo mayi puShyata yad vasu //2//
saMjagmAnA abibhyuShIr asmin goShThe karIShiNIH /
bibhratIH somyaM madhv anamIvA upetana //3//
ihaiva gAva etaneho shakeva puShyata /
ihaivota pra jAyadhvaM mayi saMj~nAnam astu vaH //4//
shivo vo goShTho bhavatu shArishAkeva puShyata /
ihaivota pra jAyadhvaM mayA vaH saM sR^ijAmasi //5//
mayA gAvo gopatinA sachadhvam ayaM vo goShTha iha poShayiShNuH /
rAyas poSheNa bahulA bhavantIr jIvA jIvantIr upa vaH sadema //6//
86

indram ahaM vaNijaM chodayAmi sa na aitu puraetA no astu /
nudann arAtiM paripanthinaM mR^igaM sa IshAno dhanadA astu mahyam //1//
ye panthAno bahavo devayAnA antarA dyAvApR^ithivI saMcharanti /
te mA juShantAM payasA ghR^itena yathA krItvA dhanam AharANi //2//
idhmenAgna ichhamAno ghR^itena juhomi havyaM tarase balAya /
yAvad Ishe brahmaNA vandamAna imAM dhiyaM shataseyAya devIm //3//
imAm agne sharaNiM mImR^iSho no yam adhvAnam agAma dUram /
shunaM no astu prapaNo vikrayash cha pratipaNaH phalinaM mA kR^iNotu /
idaM havyaM saMvidAnau juShethAM shunaM no astu charitam utthitaM cha //4//
yena dhanena prapaNaM charAmi dhanena devA dhanam ichhamAnaH /
tan me bhUyo bhavatu mA kanIyo 'gne sAtaghno devAn haviShA ni Shedha //5//
yena dhanena prapaNaM charAmi dhanena devA dhanam ichhamAnaH /
tasmin ma indro ruchim A dadhAtu prajApatiH savitA somo agniH //6//
upa tvA namasA vayaM hotar vaishvAnara stumaH /
sa naH prajAsv Atmasu goShu prANeShu jAgR^ihi //7//
vishvAhA te sadam id bharemAshvAyeva tiShThate jAtavedaH /
rAyas poSheNa sam iShA madanto mA te agne prativeshA riShAma //8//
87

prAtar agniM prAtar indram havAmahe prAtar mitrAvaruNA prAtar ashvinA /
prAtar bhagaM pUShaNaM brahmaNas patiM prAtaH somam uta rudraM havAmahe //1//
prAtarjitam bhagam ugram havAmahe vayaM putram aditer yo vidhartA /
Adhrash chid yaM manyamAnas turash chid rAjA chid yaM bhagaM bhakShIty Aha //2//
bhaga praNetar bhaga satyarAdho bhagemAM dhiyam ud avA dadan naH /
bhaga pra No janaya gobhir ashvair bhaga pra nR^ibhir nR^ivantaH syAma //3//
utedAnIM bhagavantaH syAmota prapitva uta madhye ahnAm /
utoditau maghavant sUryasya vayaM devAnAM sumatau syAma //4//
bhaga eva bhagavAm? astu devas tenA vayaM bhagavantaH syAma /
taM tvA bhaga sarva ij johavImi sa no bhaga puraetA bhaveha //5//
sam adhvarAyoShaso namanta dadhikrAveva shuchaye padAya /
arvAchInaM vasuvidaM bhagaM me ratham ivAshvA vAjina A vahantu //6//
ashvAvatIr gomatIr na uShAso vIravatIH sadam uchhantu bhadrAH /
ghR^itaM duhAnA vishvataH prapItA yUyaM pAta svastibhiH sadA naH //7//
88

sIrA yu~njanti kavayo yugA vi tanvate pR^ithak /
dhIrA deveShu sumnayau //1//
yunakta sIrA vi yugA tanota kR^ite yonau vapateha bIjam /
virAjaH shnuShTiH sabharA asan no nedIya it sR^iNyaH pakvam A yavan //2//
lA~NgalaM pavIravat sushImaM somasatsaru /
ud id vapatu gAm aviM prasthAvad rathavAhanaM pIbarIM cha prapharvyam //3//
indraH sItAM ni gR^ihNAtu tAM pUShAbhi rakShatu /
sA naH payasvatI duhAm uttarAmuttarAM samAm //4//
shunaM suphAlA vi tudantu bhUmiM shunaM kInAshA anu yantu vAhAn /
shunAsIrA haviShA toshamAnA supippalA oShadhIH kartam asmai //5//
shunaM vAhAH shunaM naraH shunaM kR^iShatu lA~Ngalam /
shunaM varatrA badhyantAM shunam aShTrAm ud i~Ngaya //6//
shunAsIreha sma me juShethAm /
yad divi chakrathuH payas tenemAm upa si~nchatam //7//
sIte vandAmahe tvArvAchI subhage bhava /
yathA naH sumanA aso yathA naH suphalA bhuvaH //8//
ghR^itena sItA madhunA samaktA vishvair devair anumatA marudbhiH /
sA naH sIte payasAbhyAvavR^itsvorjasvatI ghR^itavat pinvamAnA //9//
89

imAM khanAmy oShadhiM vIrudhAM balavattamAm /
yayA sapatnIM bAdhate yayA saMvindate patim //1//
uttAnaparNe subhage devajUte sahasvati /
sapatnIM me parA Nuda patiM me kevalaM kR^idhi //2//
nahi te nAma jagrAha no asmin ramase patau /
parAm eva parAvataM sapatnIM gamayAmasi //3//
uttarAham uttara uttared uttarAbhyaH /
adhaH sapatnI yA mamAdharA sAdharAbhyaH //4//
aham asmi sahamAnAtho tvam asi sAsahiH /
ubhe sahasvatI bhUtvA sapatnIM me sahAvahai //5//
abhi te 'dhAM sahamAnAm upa te 'dhAM sahIyasIm /
mAm anu pra te mano vatsaM gaur iva dhAvatu pathA vAr iva dhAvatu //6//
90

saMshitaM ma idaM brahma saMshitaM vIrya1M balam /
saMshitaM kShatram ajaram astu jiShNur yeShAm asmi purohitaH //1//
sam aham eShAM rAShTraM syAmi sam ojo vIrya1M balam /
vR^ishchAmi shatrUNAM bAhUn anena haviShA aham //2//
nIchaiH padyantAm adhare bhavantu ye naH sUriM maghavAnaM pR^itanyAn /
kShiNAmi brahmaNAmitrAn un nayAmi svAn aham //3//
tIkShNIyAMsaH parashor agnes tIkShNatarA uta /
indrasya vajrAt tIkShNIyAMso yeShAm asmi purohitaH //4//
eShAm aham AyudhA saM syAmy eShAM rAShTraM suvIraM vardhayAmi /
eShAm kShatram ajaram astu jiShNv eShAM chittaM vishve 'vantu devAH //5//
ud dharShantAM maghavan vAjinAny ud vIrANAM jayatAm etu ghoShaH /
pR^ithag ghoShA ululayaH ketumanta ud IratAm /
devA indrajyeShThA maruto yantu senayA //6//
pretA jayatA nara ugrA vaH santu bAhavaH /
tIkShNeShavo 'baladhanvano hatogrAyudhA abalAn ugrabAhavaH //7//
avasR^iShTA parA pata sharavye brahmasaMshite /
jaya amitrAn pra padyasva jahy eShAM varaMvaraM mAmIShAM mochi kash chana //8//
91

ayaM te yonir R^itviyo yato jAto arochathAH /
taM jAnann agna A rohAdhA no vardhaya rayim //1//
agne achhA vadeha naH pratya~N naH sumanA bhava /
pra No yachha vishAM pate dhanadA asi nas tvam //2//
pra No yachhatv aryamA pra bhagaH pra bR^ihaspatiH /
pra devIH prota sUnR^itA rayiM devI dadhAtu me //3//
somaM rAjAnam avase 'gniM gIrbhir havAmahe /
Adityam viShNum sUryaM brahmANaM cha bR^ihaspatim //4//
tvaM no agne agnibhir brahma yaj~naM vardhaya /
tvaM no deva dAtave rayiM dAnAya chodaya //5//
indravAyU ubhAv iha suhaveha havAmahe /
yathA naH sarva ij janaH saMgatyAM sumanA asad dAnakAmash cha no bhuvat //6//
aryamaNaM bR^ihaspatim indraM dAnAya chodaya /
vAtaM viShNuM sarasvatIM savitAraM cha vAjinam //7//
vAjasya nu prasave saM babhUvimemA cha vishvA bhuvanAni antaH /
utAditsantaM dApayatu prajAnan rayiM cha naH sarvavIraM ni yachha //8//
duhrAm me pa~ncha pradiSho duhrAm urvIr yathAbalam /
prApeyaM sarvA AkUtIr manasA hR^idayena cha //9//
gosaniM vAcham udeyaM varchasA mAbhyudihi /
A rundhAM sarvato vAyus tvaShTA poShaM dadhAtu me //10//
92

ye agnayo apsv a1ntar ye vR^itre ye puruShe ye ashmasu /
ya AviveshoShadhIr yo vanaspatIMs tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //1//
yaH some antar yo goShv antar ya AviShTo vayaHsu yo mR^igeShu /
ya Avivesha dvipado yas chatuShpadas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //2//
ya indreNa sarathaM yAti devo vaishvAnara uta vishvadAvyaH /
yaM johavImi pR^itanAsu sAsahiM tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //3//
yo devo vishvAd yam u kAmam Ahur yaM dAtAraM pratigR^ihNantam AhuH /
yo dhIraH shakraH paribhUr adAbhyas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //4//
yaM tvA hotAraM manasAbhi saMvidus trayodasha bhauvanAH pa~ncha mAnavAH /
varchodhase yashase sUnR^itAvate tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //5//
ukShAnnAya vashAnnAya somapR^iShThAya vedhase /
vaishvAnarajyeShThebhyas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //6//
divaM pR^ithivIm anv antarikSham ye vidyutam anusaMcharanti /
ye dikShv a1ntar ye vAte antas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat //7//
hiraNyapANiM savitAram indraM bR^ihaspatiM varuNaM mitram agnim /
vishvAn devAn a~Ngiraso havAmahe imaM kravyAdaM shamayantv agnim //8//
shAnto agniH kravyAch chhAntaH puruShareShaNaH /
atho yo vishvadAvya1s taM kravyAdam ashIshamam //9//
ye parvatAH somapR^iShThA Apa uttAnashIvarIH /
vAtaH parjanya Ad agnis te kravyAdam ashIshaman //10//
93

hastivarchasaM prathatAM bR^ihad yasho adityA yat tanvaH saMbabhUva /
tat sarve sam adur mahyam etad vishve devA aditiH sajoShAH //1//
mitrash cha varuNash chendro rudrash cha chetatu /
devAso vishvadhAyasas te mA~njantu varchasA //2//
yena hastI varchasA saMbabhUva yena rAjA manuShyesv apsv a1ntaH /
yena devA devatAm agra Ayan tena mAm adya varchasAgne varchasvinaM kR^iNu //3//
yat te varcho jAtavedo bR^ihad bhavaty AhuteH /
yAvat sUryasya varcha Asurasya cha hastinaH /
tAvan me ashvinA varcha A dhattAM puShkarasrajA //4//
yAvach chatasraH pradishash chakShur yAvat samashnute /
tAvat samaitv indriyaM mayi tad dhastivarchasam //5//
hastI mR^igANAM suShadAm atiShThAvAn babhUva hi /
tasya bhagena varchasAbhi Shi~nchAmi mAm aham //6//
94

yena vehad babhUvitha nAshayAmasi tat tvat /
idaM tad anyatra tvad apa dUre ni dadhmasi //1//
A te yoniM garbha etu pumAn bANa iveShudhim /
A vIro 'tra jAyatAM putras te dashamAsyaH //2//
pumAMsaM putraM janaya taM pumAn anu jAyatAm /
bhavAsi putrANAM mAtA jAtAnAM janayAsh cha yAn //3//
yAni badrANi bIjAny R^iShabhA janayanti cha /
tais tvaM putraM vindasva sA prasUr dhenukA bhava //4//
kR^iNomi te prAjApatyam A yoniM garbha etu te /
vindasva tvaM putraM nAri yas tubhyaM sham asach chham u tasmai tvam bhava //5//
yAsAm dyauH pitA pR^ithivI mAtA samudro mUlaM vIrudhAM babhUva /
tAs tvA putravidyAya daivIH prAvantv oShadhayaH //6//
95

payasvatIr oShadhayaH payasvan mAmakaM vachaH /
atho payasvatInAm A bhare 'haM sahasrashaH //1//
vedAhaM payasvantaM chakAra dhAnyam bahu /
saMbhR^itvA nAma yo devas taM vayaM havAmahe yoyo ayajvano gR^ihe //2//
imA yAH pa~ncha pradisho mAnavIH pa~ncha kR^iShTayaH /
vR^iShTe shApaM nadIr iveha sphAtiM samAvahAn //3//
ud utsaM shatadhAraM sahasradhAram akShitam /
evAsmAkedaM dhAnyaM sahasradhAram akShitam //4//
shatahasta samAhara sahasrahasta saM kira /
kR^itasya kAryasya cheha sphAtiM samAvaha //5//
tisro mAtrA gandharvANAM chatasro gR^ihapatnyAH /
tAsAM yA sphAtimattamA tayA tvAbhi mR^ishAmasi //6//
upohash cha samUhash cha kShattArau te prajApate /
tAv ihA vahatAM sphAtiM bahuM bhUmAnam akShitam //7//
96

uttudas tvot tudatu mA dhR^ithAH shayane sve /
iShuH kAmasya yA bhImA tayA vidhyAmi tvA hR^idi //1//
AdhIparNAM kAmashalyAm iShuM saMkalpakulmalAm /
tAM susaMnatAM kR^itvA kAmo vidhyatu tvA hR^idi //2//
yA plIhAnaM shoShayati kAmasyeShuH susaMnatA /
prAchInapakShA vyoShA tayA vidhyAmi tvA hR^idi //3//
shuchA viddhA vyoShayA shuShkAsyAbhi sarpa mA /
mR^idur nimanyuH kevalI priyavAdiny anuvratA //4//
AjAmi tvAjanyA pari mAtur atho pituH /
yathA mama kratAv aso mama chittam upAyasi //5//
vy asyai mitrAvaruNau hR^idash chittAny asyatam /
athainAm akratuM kR^itvA mamaiva kR^iNutaM vashe //6//
97

ye3 'syAM stha prAchyAM dishi hetayo nAma devAs teShAM vo agnir iShavaH /
te no mR^iData te no 'dhi brUta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaH svAhA //1//
ye3 'syAM stha dakShiNAyAM dishy aviShyavo nAma devAs teShAM vaH kAma iShavaH /
te no mR^iData te no 'dhi brUta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaH svAhA //2//
ye3 'syAM stha pratIchyAM dishi vairAjA nAma devAs teShAM va Apa iShavaH /
te no mR^iData te no 'dhi brUta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaH svAhA //3//
ye3 'syAM sthodIchyAM dishi pravidhyanto nAma devAs teShAM vo vAta iShavaH /
te no mR^iData te no 'dhi brUta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaH svAhA //4//
ye3 'syAM stha dhruvAyAM dishi nilimpA nAma devAs teShAM va oShadhIr iShavaH /
te no mR^iData te no 'dhi brUta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaH svAhA //5//
ye3 'syAM sthordhvAyAM dishy avasvanto nAma devAs teShAM vo bR^ihaspatir iShavaH /
te no mR^iData te no 'dhi brUta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaH svAhA //6//
98

prAchI dig agnir adhipatir asito rakShitAdityA iShavaH /
tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakShitR^ibhyo nama iShubhyo nama ebhyo astu /
yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmas taM vo jambhe dadhmaH //1//
dakShiNA dig indro 'dhipatis tirashchirAjI rakShitA pitara iShavaH /
tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakShitR^ibhyo nama iShubhyo nama ebhyo astu /
yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmas taM vo jambhe dadhmaH //2//
pratIchI dig varuNo 'dhipatiH pR^idAkU rakShitAnnam iShavaH /
tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakShitR^ibhyo nama iShubhyo nama ebhyo astu /
yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmas taM vo jambhe dadhmaH //3//
udIchI dik somo 'dhipatiH svajo rakShitAshanir iShavaH /
tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakShitR^ibhyo nama iShubhyo nama ebhyo astu /
yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmas taM vo jambhe dadhmaH //4//
dhruvA dig viShNur adhipatiH kalmAShagrIvo rakShitA vIrudha iShavaH /
tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakShitR^ibhyo nama iShubhyo nama ebhyo astu /
yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayam dviShmas taM vo jambhe dadhmaH //5//
UrdhvA dig bR^ihaspatir adhipatiH shvitro rakShitA varSham iShavaH /
tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakShitR^ibhyo nama iShubhyo nama ebhyo astu /
yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmas taM vo jambhe dadhmaH //6//
99

ekaikayaiShA sR^iShTyA saM babhUva yatra gA asR^ijanta bhUtakR^ito vishvarUpAH /
yatra vijAyate yaminy apartuH sA pashUn kShiNAti riphatI rushatI //1//
eShA pashUnt saM kShiNAti kravyAd bhUtvA vyadvarI /
utainAM brahmaNe dadyAt tathA syonA shivA syAt //2//
shivA bhava puruShebhyo gobhyo ashvebhyaH shivA /
shivAsmai sarvasmai kShetrAya shivA na ihaidhi //3//
iha puShTir iha rasa iha sahasrasAtamA bhava /
pashUn yamini poShaya //4//
yatrA suhArdaH sukR^ito madanti vihAya rogaM tanva1H svAyAh /
taM lokaM yaminy abhisaMbabhUva sA no mA hiMsIt puruShAn pashUMsh cha //5//
yatrA suhArdAM sukR^itAm agnihotrahutAm yatra lokaH /
taM lokaM yaminy abhisaMbabhUva sA no mA hiMsIt puruShAn pashUMsh cha //6//
100

yad rAjAno vibhajanta iShTApUrtasya ShoDasham yamasyAmI sabhAsadaH /
avis tasmAt pra mu~nchati dattaH shitipAt svadhA //1//
sarvAn kAmAn pUrayaty Abhavan prabhavan bhavan /
AkUtipro 'vir dattaH shitipAnn nopa dasyati //2//
yo dadAti shitipAdam aviM lokena saMmitam /
sa nAkam abhyArohati yatra shulko na kriyate abalena balIyase //3//
pa~nchApUpaM shitipAdam aviM lokena saMmitam /
pradAtopa jIvati pitR^INAM loke 'kShitam //4//
pa~nchApUpaM shitipAdam aviM lokena saMmitam /
pradAtopa jIvati sUryAmAsayor akShitam //5//
ireva nopa dasyati samudra iva payo mahat /
devau savAsinAv iva shitipAn nopa dasyati //6//
ka idaM kasmA adAt kAmaH kAmAyAdAt /
kAmo dAtA kAmaH pratigrahItA kAmaH samudram A vivesha /
kAmena tvA prati gR^ihnAmi kAmaitat te //7//
bhUmiSh TvA prati gR^ihNAtv antarikSham idaM mahat /
mAhaM prANena mAtmanA mA prajayA pratigR^ihya vi rAdhiShi //8//
101

sahR^idayaM sAMmanasyam avidveShaM kR^iNomi vaH /
anyo anyam abhi haryata vatsaM jAtam ivAghnyA //1//
anuvrataH pituH putro mAtrA bhavatu saMmanAH /
jAyA patye madhumatIM vAchaM vadatu shantivAm //2//
mA bhrAtA bhrAtaraM dvikShan mA svasAram uta svasA /
samya~nchaH savratA bhUtvA vAchaM vadata bhadrayA //3//
yena devA na viyanti no cha vidviShate mithaH /
tat kR^iNmo brahma vo gR^ihe saMj~nAnaM puruShebhyaH //4//
jyAyasvantash chittino mA vi yauShTa saMrAdhayantaH sadhurAsh charantaH /
anyo anyasmai valgu vadanta eta sadhrIchInAn vaH saMmanasas krNomi //5//
samAnI prapA saha vo 'nnabhAgaH samAne yoktre saha vo yunajmi /
samya~ncho 'gniM saparyatArA nAbhim ivAbhitaH //6//
sadhrIchInAn vaH saMmanasas kR^iNomy ekashnuShTInt saMvananena sarvAn /
devA ivAmR^itaM rakShamANAH sAyaMprAtaH saumanaso vo astu //7//
102

vi devA jarasAvR^itan vi tvam agne arAtyA /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //1//
vy ArtyA pavamAno vi shakraH pApakR^ityayA /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //2//
vi grAmyAH pashava AraNyair vy Apas tR^iShNayAsaran /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //3//
vI3 me dyAvApR^ithivI ito vi panthAno dishaMdisham /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //4//
tvaShTA duhitre vahatuM yunaktItIdaM vishvaM bhuvanaM vi yAti /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //5//
agniH prANAnt saM dadhAti chandraH prANena saMhitaH /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //6//
prANena vishvatovIryaM devAH sUryaM sam airayan /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //7//
AyuShmatAm AyuShkR^itAM prANena jIva mA mR^ithAH /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //8//
prANena prANatAM prANehaiva bhava mA mR^ithAH /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //9//
ud AyuShA sam AyuShod oShadhInAM rasena /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //10//
A parjanyasya vR^iShTyod asthAmAmR^itA vayam /
vy a1haM sarveNa pApmanA vi yakShmeNa sam AyuShA //11//
103
Book:
4

brahma jaj~nAnaM prathamaM purastAd vi sImataH surucho vena AvaH /
sa budhnyA upamA asya viShThAH satash cha yonim asatash cha vi vaH //1//
iyaM pitryA rAShTry etv agre prathamAya januShe bhuvaneShThAH /
tasmA etaM suruchaM hvAram ahyaM gharmaM shrINantu prathamAya dhAsyave //2//
pra yo jaj~ne vidvAn asya bandhur vishvA devAnAM janimA vivakti /
brahma brahmaNa uj jabhAra madhyAn nichair uchchaiH svadhA abhi pra tasthau //3//
sa hi vidaH sa pR^ithivyA R^itasthA mahI kShemaM rodasI askabhAyat /
mahAn mahI askabhAyad vi jAto dyAM sadma pArthivaM cha rajaH //4//
sa bhudhnyAd AShTra januSho 'bhy agram bR^ihaspatir devatA tasya samrAT /
ahar yach chhukraM jyotiSho janiShTAtha dyumanto vi vasantu viprAH //5//
nUnaM tad asya kAvyo hinoti maho devasya pUrvyasya dhAma /
eSha jaj~ne bahubhiH sAkam itthA pUrve ardhe viShite sasan nu //6//
yo 'tharvANaM pitaraM devabandhuM bR^ihaspatiM namasAva cha gachhAt /
tvaM vishveShAM janitA yathAsaH kavir devo na dabhAyat svadhAvAn //7//
104

ya AtmadA baladA yasya vishva upAsate prashiShaM yasya devAH /
yo3 'syeshe dvipado yash chatuShpadaH kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //1//
yaH prANato nimiShato mahitvaiko rAjA jagato babhUva /
yasya chhAyAmR^itaM yasya mR^ityuH kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //2//
yaM krandasI avatash chaskabhAne bhiyasAne rodasI ahvayethAm /
yasyAsau panthA rajaso vimAnaH kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //3//
yasya dyaur urvI pR^ithivI cha mahI yasyAda urv antarikSham /
yasyAsau sUro vitato mahitvA kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //4//
yasya vishve himavanto mahitvA samudre yasya rasAm id AhuH /
imAsh cha pradisho yasya bAhU kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //5//
Apo agre vishvam Avan garbhaM dadhAnA amR^itA R^itaj~nAH /
yAsu devIShv adhi deva AsIt kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //6//
hiraNyagarbhaH sam avartatAgre bhUtasya jAtaH patir eka AsIt /
sa dAdhAra pR^ithivIm uta dyAM kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //7//
Apo vatsaM janayantIr garbham agre sam airayan /
tasyota jAyamAnasyolba AsId dhiraNyayaH kasmai devAya haviShA vidhema //8//
105

ud itas trayo akraman vyAghraH puruSho vR^ikaH /
hirug ghi yanti sindhavo hirug devo vanaspatir hiru~N namantu shatravaH //1//
pareNaitu pathA vR^ikaH parameNota taskaraH /
pareNa datvatI rajjuH pareNAghAyur arShatu //2//
akShyaù cha te mukhaM cha te vyAghra jambhayAmasi /
At sarvAn viMshatiM nakhAn //3//
vyAghraM datvatAM vayaM prathamaM jambhayAmasi /
Ad u ShTenam atho ahiM yAtudhAnam atho vR^ikam //4//
yo adya stena Ayati sa saMpiShTo apAyati /
pathAm apadhvaMsenaitv indro vajreNa hantu tam //5//
mUrNA mR^igasya dantA apishIrNA u pR^iShTayaH /
nimruk te godhA bhavatu nIchAyach chhashayur mR^igaH //6//
yat saMyamo na vi yamo vi yamo yan na saMyamaH /
indrajAh somajA AtharvaNam asi vyAghrajambhanam //7//
106

yAM tvA gandharvo akhanad varuNAya mR^itabhraje /
tAM tvA vayaM khanAmasy oShadhiM shepaharShaNIm //1//
ud uShA ud u sUrya ud idaM mAmakaM vachaH /
ud ejatu prajApatir vR^iShA shuShmeNa vAjinA //2//
yathA sma te virohato 'bhitaptam ivAnati /
tatas te shuShmavattaram iyaM kR^iNotv oShadhiH //3//
uch chhuShmauShadhInAM sArA R^iShabhANAm /
saM puMsAm indra vR^iShNyam asmin dhehi tanUvashin //4//
apAM rasaH prathamajo 'tho vanaspatInAm /
uta somasya bhrAtAsy utArsham asi vR^iShNyam //5//
adyAgne adya savitar adya devi sarasvati /
adyAsya brahmaNas pate dhanur ivA tAnayA pasaH //6//
AhaM tanomi te paso adhi jyAm iva dhanvani /
kramasvarsha iva rohitam anavaglAyatA sadA //7//
ashvasyAshvatarasyAjasya petvasya cha /
atha R^iShabhasya ye vAjAs tAn asmin dhehi tanUvashin //8//
107

sahasrashR^i~Ngo vR^iShabho yaH samudrAd udAcharat /
tenA sahasyenA vayaM ni janAnt svApayAmasi //1//
na bhUmiM vAto ati vAti nAti pashyati kash chana /
striyash cha sarvAH svApaya shunash chendrasakhA charan //2//
proShTheshayAs talpeshayA nArIr yA vahyashIvarIH /
striyo yAH puNyagandhayas tAH sarvAH svApayAmasi //3//
ejadejad ajagrabhaM chakShuH prANam ajagrabham /
a~NgAny ajagrabhaM sarvA rAtrINAm atisharvare //4//
ya Aste yash charati yash cha tiShThan vipashyati /
teShAM saM dadhmo akShINi yathedaM harmyaM tathA //5//
svaptu mAtA svaptu pitA svaptu shvA svaptu vishpatiH /
svapantv asyai j~nAtayaH svaptv ayam abhito janaH //6//
svapna svapnAbhikaraNena sarvaM ni svApayA janam /
otsUryam anyAnt svApayAvyuShaM jAgR^itAd aham indra ivAriShTo akShitaH //7//
108

brAhmaNo jaj~ne prathamo dashashIrSho dashAsyaH /
sa somaM prathamaH papau sa chakArArasaM viSham //1//
yAvatI dyAvApR^ithivI varimNA yAvat sapta sindhavo vitaShThire /
vAchaM viShasya dUShaNIM tAm ito nir avAdiSham //2//
suparNas tvA garutmAn viSha prathamam Avayat /
nAmImado nArUrupa utAsmA abhavaH pituH //3//
yas ta Asyat pa~nchA~Ngurir vakrAch chid adhi dhanvanaH /
apaskambhasya shalyAn nir avocham ahaM viSham //4//
shalyAd viShaM nir avocham prA~njanAd uta parNadheH /
apAShThAch chhR^i~NgAt kulmalAn nir avocham aham viSham //5//
arasas ta iSho shalyo 'tho te arasaM viSham /
utArasasya vR^ikShasya dhanuSh Te arasArasam //6//
ye apIShan ye adihan ya Asyan ye avAsR^ijan /
sarve te vadhrayaH kR^itA vadhrir viShagiriH kR^itaH //7//
vadhrayas te khanitAro vadhris tvam asy oShadhe /
vadhriH sa parvato girir yato jAtam idaM viSham //8//
109

vAr idam vArayAtai varaNAvatyAm adhi /
tatrAmR^itasyAsiktaM tenA te vAraye viSham //1//
arasaM prAchyaM viSham arasaM yad udIchyam /
athedam adharAchyaM karambheNa vi kalpate //2//
karambhaM kR^itvA tiryaM pIbaspAkam udArathim /
kShudhA kila tvA duShTano jakShivAnt sa na rUrupaH //3//
vi te madaM madAvati sharam iva pAtayAmasi /
pra tvA charum iva yeShantaM vachasA sthApayAmasi //4//
pari grAmam ivAchitaM vachasA sthApayAmasi /
tiShThA vR^ikSha iva sthAmny abhrikhAte na rUrupaH //5//
pavastais tvA pary akrINan dUrshebhir ajinair uta /
prakrIr asi tvam oShadhe 'bhrikhAte na rUrupaH //6//
anAptA ye vaH prathamA yAni karmANi chakrire /
vIrAn no atra mA dabhan tad va etat puro dadhe //7//
110

bhUto bhUteShu paya A dadhAti sa bhUtAnAm adhipatir babhUva /
tasya mR^ityush charati rAjasUyaM sa rAjA anu manyatAm idaM //1//
abhi prehi mApa vena ugrash chettA sapatnahA /
A tiShTha mitravardhana tubhyam devA adhi bruvan //2//
AtiShThantaM pari vishve abhUShaM chhriyaM vasAnash charati svarochiH /
mahat tad vR^iShNo asurasya nAmA vishvarUpo amR^itAni tasthau //3//
vyAghro adhi vaiyAghre vi kramasva disho mahIH /
vishas tvA sarvA vA~nchhantv Apo divyAH payasvatIH //4//
yA Apo divyAH payasA madanty antarikSha uta vA pR^ithivyAm /
tAsAM tvA sarvAsAm apAm abhi Shi~nchAmi varchasA //5//
abhi tvA varchasAsichann Apo divyAH payasvatIh /
yathAso mitravardhanas tathA tvA savitA karat //6//
enA vyAghraM pariShasvajAnAH siMhaM hinvanti mahate saubhagAya /
samudraM na subhuvas tasthivAMsaM marmR^ijyante dvIpinam apsv a1ntaH //7//
111

ehi jIvaM trAyamANaM parvatasyAsy akShyam /
vishvebhir devair dattaM paridhir jIvanAya kam //1//
paripANaM puruShANAM paripANaM gavAM asi /
ashvAnAm arvatAm paripANAya tasthiShe //2//
utAsi paripANam yAtujambhanam A~njana /
utAmR^itasya tvaM vetthAtho asi jIvabhojanam atho haritabheShajam //3//
yasyA~njana prasarpasy a~Ngama~Ngam paruShparuH /
tato yakShmaM vi bAdhasa ugro madhyamashIr iva //4//
nainaM prApnoti shapatho na kR^ityA nAbhishochanam /
nainaM viShkandham ashnute yas tvA bibharty A~njana //5//
asanmantrAd duShvapnyAd duShkR^itAch chhamalAd uta /
durhArdash chakShuSho ghorAt tasmAn naH pAhy A~njana //6//
idaM vidvAn A~njana satyaM vakShyAmi nAnR^itam /
saneyam ashvaM gAm aham AtmAnaM tava pUruSha //7//
trayo dAsA A~njanasya takmA balAsa Ad ahiH /
varShiShThaH parvatAnAM trikakun nAma te pitA //8//
yad A~njanaM traikakudam jAtaM himavatas pari /
yAtUMsh cha sarvA~n jambhayat sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyaH //9//
yadi vAsi traikakudaM yadi yAmunam uchyase /
ubhe te bhadre nAmnI tAbhyAM naH pAhy A~njana //10//
112

vAtAj jAto antarikShAd vidyuto jyotiShas pari /
sa no hiraNyajAH sha~NkhaH kR^ishanaH pAtv aMhasaH //1//
yo agrato rochanAnAM samudrAd adhi jaj~niShe /
sha~Nkhena hatvA rakShAMsy attriNo vi ShahAmahe //2//
sha~NkhenAmIvAm amatiM sha~Nkhenota sadAnvAH /
sha~Nkho no vishvabheShajaH kR^ishanaH pAtv aMhasaH //3//
divi jAtaH samudrajaH sindhutas pary AbhR^itaH /
sa no hiraNyajAH sha~Nkha AyuShprataraNo maNiH //4//
samudrAj jAto maNir vR^itrAj jAto divAkaraH /
so asmAnt sarvataH pAtu hetyA devAsurebhyaH //5//
hiraNyAnAm eko 'si somAt tvam adhi jaj~niShe /
rathe tvam asi darshata iShudhau rochanas tvaM pra Na AyUMShi tAriShat //6//
devAnAm asthi kR^ishanaM babhUva tad Atmanvach charaty apsv a1ntaH /
tat te badhnAmy AyuShe varchase balAya dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya kArshanas tvAbhi rakShatu //7//
113

anaDvAn dAdhAra pR^ithivIm uta dyAm anaDvAn dAdhArorv a1ntarikSham /
anaDvAn dAdhAra pradishaH ShaD urvIr anaDvAn vishvaM bhuvanam A vivesha //1//
anaDvAn indraH sa pashubhyo vi chaShTe trayAM chhakro vi mimIte adhvanaH /
bhUtaM bhaviShyad bhuvanA duhAnaH sarvA devAnAm charati vratAni //2//
indro jAto manuShyeShv antar gharmas taptash charati shoshuchAnaH /
suprajAH sant sa udAre na sarShad yo nAshnIyAd anaDuho vijAnan //3//
anaDvAn duhe sukR^itasya loka ainaM pyAyayati pavamAnaH purastAt /
parjanyo dhArA maruta Udho asya yaj~naH payo dakShiNA doho asya //4//
yasya neshe yaj~napatir na yaj~no nAsya dAteshe na pratigrahItA /
yo vishvajid vishvabhR^id vishvakarmA gharmaM no brUta yatamash chatuShpAt //5//
yena devAH svar Aruruhur hitvA sharIram amR^itasya nAbhim /
tena geShma sukR^itasya lokaM gharmasya vratena tapasA yashasyavaH //6//
indro rUpeNAgnir vahena prajApatiH parameShThI virAT /
vishvAnare akramata vaishvAnare akramatAnaduhy akramata /
so 'dR^iMhayata so 'dhArayata //7//
madhyam etad anaDuho yatraiSha vaha AhitaH /
etAvad asya prAchInaM yAvAn pratya~N samAhitaH //8//
yo vedAnaduho dohAn saptAnupadasvataH /
prajAM cha lokaM chApnoti tathA saptaR^iShayo viduH //9//
padbhiH sedim avakrAmann irAM ja~NghAbhir utkhidan /
srameNAnaDvAn kIlAlaM kInAshash chAbhi gachhataH //10//
dvAdasha vA etA rAtrIr vratyA AhuH prajApateH /
tatropa brahma yo veda tad vA anaDuho vratam //11//
duhe sAyaM duhe prAtar duhe madhyaMdinaM pari /
dohA ye asya saMyanti tAn vidmAnupadasvataH //12//
114

rohaNy asi rohaNy asthnash chhinnasya rohaNI /
rohayedam arundhati //1//
yat te riShTaM yat te dyuttam asti peShTraM ta Atmani /
dhAtA tad bhadrayA punaH saM dadhat paruShA paruH //2//
saM te majjA maj~nA bhavatu sam u te paruShA paruH /
saM te mAMsasya visrastaM sam asthy api rohatu //3//
majjA maj~nA saM dhIyatAM charmaNA charma rohatu /
asR^ik te asthi rohatu mAMsaM mAMsena rohatu //4//
loma lomnA saM kalpayA tvachA saM kalpayA tvacham /
asR^ik te asthi rohatu chhinnaM saM dhehy oShadhe //5//
sa ut tiShTha prehi pra drava rathaH suchakraH /
supaviH sunAbhiH prati tiShThordhvaH //6//
yadi kartaM patitvA saMshashre yadi vAsmA prahR^ito jaghAna /
R^ibhU rathasyevA~NgAni saM dadhat paruShA paruH //7//
115

uta devA avahitaM devA un nayathA punaH /
utAgash chakruShaM devA devA jIvayathA punaH //1//
dvAv imau vAtau vAta A sindhor A parAvataH /
dakShaM te anya AvAtu vy anyo vAtu yad rapaH //2//
A vAta vAhi bheShajaM vi vAta vAhi yad rapaH /
tvaM hi vishvabheShaja devAnAM dUta Iyase //3//
trAyantAm imaM devAs trAyantAM marutAM gaNAH /
trAyantAM vishvA bhUtAni yathAyam arapA asat //4//
A tvAgamaM shaMtAtibhir atho ariShTatAtibhiH /
dakShaM ta ugram AbhAriShaM parA yakShmaM suvAmi te //5//
ayaM me hasto bhagavAn ayaM me bhagavattaraH /
ayaM me vishvabheShajo 'yaM shivAbhimarshanaH //6//
hastAbhyAM dashashAkhAbhyAM jihvA vAchaH purogavI /
anAmayitnubhyAM hastAbhyAM tAbhyAM tvAbhi mR^ishAmasi //7//
116

ajo hy agner ajaniShTa shokAt so apashyaj janitAram agre /
tena devA devatAm agrA Ayan tena rohAn ruruhur medhyAsaH //1//
kramadhvam agninA nAkam ukhyAn hasteShu bibhrataH /
divas pR^iShThaM svar gatvA mishrA devebhir Adhvam //2//
