pR^iShThAt pR^ithivyA aham antarikSham Aruham antarikShAd divam Aruham /
divo nAkasya pR^iShThAt sva1r jyotir agAm aham //3//
sva1r yanto nApekShanta A dyAM rohanti rodasI /
yaj~naM ye vishvatodhAraM suvidvAMso vitenire //4//
agne prehi prathamo devatAnAM chakShur devAnAm uta mAnuShAnAm /
iyakShamANA bhR^igubhiH sajoShAH svar yantu yajamAnAH svasti //5//
ajam anajmi payasA ghR^itena divyaM suparnaM payasaM bR^ihantam /
tena geShma sukR^itasya lokaM svar Arohanto abhi nAkam uttamam //6//
pa~nchaudanaM pa~nchabhir a~Ngulibhir darvyoddhara pa~nchadhaitam odanam /
prAchyAM dishi shiro ajasya dhehi dakShiNAyAM dishi dakShiNaM dhehi pArshvam //7//
pratIchyAM dishi bhasadam asya dhehy uttarasyAM dishy uttaraM dhehi pArshvam /
UrdhvAyAM dishy a1jasyAnUkaM dhehi dishi dhruvAyAM dhehi pAjasyam antarikShe madhyato madhyam asya //8//
shR^itam ajaM shR^itayA prorNuhi tvachA sarvair a~NgaiH saMbhR^itaM vishvarUpam /
sa ut tisTheto abhi nAkam uttamaM padbhish chaturbhiH prati tiShTha dikShu //9//
117

samutpatantu pradisho nabhasvatIH sam abhrANi vAtajUtAni yantu /
mahaR^iShabhasya nadato nabhasvato vAshrA ApaH pR^ithivIM tarpayantu //1//
sam IkShayantu taviShAH sudAnavo 'pAM rasA oShadhIbhiH sachantAm /
varShasya sargA mahayantu bhUmiM pR^ithag jAyantAm oShadhayo vishvarUpAH //2//
sam IkShayasva gAyato nabhAMsy apAm vegAsah pR^ithag ud vijantAm /
varShasya sargA mahayantu bhUmiM pR^ithag jAyantAm vIrudho vishvarUpAH //3//
gaNAs tvopa gAyantu mArutAH parjanya ghoShiNaH pR^ithak /
sargA varShasya varShato varShantu pR^ithivIm anu //4//
ud Irayata marutaH samudratas tveSho arko nabha ut pAtayAtha /
mahaR^iShabhasya nadato nabhasvato vAshrA ApaH pR^ithivIM tarpayantu //5//
abhi kranda stanayArdayodadhiM bhUmiM parjanya payasA sam a~Ndhi /
tvayA sR^iShTaM bahulam aitu varSham AshAraiShI kR^ishagur etv astam //6//
saM vo 'vantu sudAnava utsA ajagarA uta /
marudbhiH prachyutA meghA varShantu pR^ithivIm anu //7//
AshAmAshAM vi dyotatAM vAtA vAntu dishodishaH /
marudbhiH prachyutA meghAH saM yantu pR^ithivIm anu //8//
Apo vidyud abhraM varShaM saM vo 'vantu sudAnava utsA ajagarA uta /
marudbhiH prachyutA meghAH prAvantu pR^ithivIm anu //9//
apAm agnis tanUbhiH saMvidAno ya oShadhInAm adhipA babhUva /
sa no varShaM vanutAM jAtavedAH prANaM prajAbhyo amR^itaM divas pari //10//
prajApatiH salilAd A samudrAd Apa Irayann udadhim ardayAti /
pra pyAyatAM vR^iShNo ashvasya reto 'rvAn etena stanayitnunehi //11//
apo niShi~nchann asuraH pitA naH shvasantu gargarA apAM varuNAva nIchIr apaH sR^ija /
vadantu pR^ishnibAhavo maNDUkA iriNAnu //12//
saMvatsaraM shashayAnA brAhmaNA vratachAriNaH /
vAcham parjanyajinvitAM pra maNdUkA avAdiShuH //13//
upapravada maNDUki varShaM A vada tAduri /
madhye hradasya plavasva vigR^ihya chaturaH padaH //14//
khaNvakhA3i khaimakhA3i madhye taduri /
varShaM vanudhvaM pitaro marutAM mana ichhata //15//
mahAntaM kosham ud achAbhi Shi~ncha savidyutaM bhavatu vAtu vAtaH /
tanvatAM yaj~naM bahudhA visR^iShTA AnandinIr oShadhayo bhavantu //16//
118

bR^ihan eShAm adhiShThAtA antikAd iva pashyati /
ya stAyan manyate charant sarvaM devA idaM viduH //1//
yas tiShThati charati yash cha va~nchati yo nilAyaM charati yaH prata~Nkam /
dvau saMniShadya yan mantrayete rAjA tad veda varuNas tR^itIyaH //2//
uteyaM bhUmir varuNasya rAj~na utAsau dyaur bR^ihatI dUreantA /
uto samudrau varuNasya kukShI utAsminn alpa udake nilInaH //3//
uta yo dyAm atisarpAt parastAn na sa muchyAtai varuNasya rAj~naH /
diva spashaH pra charantIdam asya sahasrAkShA ati pashyanti bhUmim //4//
sarvaM tad rAjA varuNo vi chaShTe yad antarA rodasI yat parastAt /
saMkhyAtA asya nimiSho janAnAm akShAn iva shvaghnI ni minoti tAni //5//
ye te pAshA varuNa saptasapta tredhA tiShThanti viShitA ruShantaH /
chhinantu sarve anR^itaM vadantaM yaH satyavAdy ati taM sR^ijantu //6//
shatena pAshair abhi dhehi varuNainaM mA te mochy anR^itavA~N nR^ichakShaH /
AstAM jAlma udaraM shraMshayitvA kosha ivAbandhaH parikR^ityamAnaH //7//
yaH samAbhyo3 varuNo yo vyAbhyo3 yaH saMdeshyo3 varuNo yo videshyo /
yo daivo varuNo yash cha mAnuShaH //8//
tais tvA sarvair abhi ShyAmi pAshair asAv AmuShyAyaNAmuShyAH putra /
tAn u te sarvAn anusaMdishAmi //9//
119

IshANAM tvA bheShajAnAm ujjeSha A rabhAmahe /
chakre sahasravIryam sarvasmA oShadhe tvA //1//
satyajitaM shapathayAvanIM sahamAnAM punaHsarAm /
sarvAH sam ahvy oShadhIr ito naH pArayAd iti //2//
yA shashApa shapanena yAghaM mUram Adadhe /
yA rasasya haraNAya jAtam Arebhe tokam attu sA //3//
yAM te chakrur Ame pAtre yAM chakrur nIlalohite /
Ame mAMse kR^ityAM yAM chakrus tayA kR^ityAkR^ito jahi //4//
dauShvapnyaM daurjIvityaM rakSho abhvam arAyyaH /
durNAmnIH sarvA durvAchas tA asman nAshayAmasi //5//
kShudhAmAraM tR^iShNAmAram agotAm anapatyatAm /
apAmArga tvayA vayaM sarvaM tad apa mR^ijmahe //6//
tR^iShNAmAraM kShudhAmAraM atho akShaparAjayam /
apAmArga tvayA vayaM sarvaM tad apa mR^ijmahe //7//
apAmArga oShadhInAM sarvAsAm eka id vashI /
tena te mR^ijma Asthitam atha tvam agadash chara //8//
120

samaM jyotiH sUryeNAhnA rAtrI samAvatI /
kR^iNomi satyam Utaye 'rasAH santu kR^itvarIH //1//
yo devAH kR^ityAM kR^itvA harAd aviduSho gR^iham /
vatso dhArur iva mAtaraM taM pratyag upa padyatAm //2//
amA kR^itvA pApmAnaM yas tenAnyaM jighAMsati /
ashmAnas tasyAM dagdhAyAM bahulAH phaT karikrati //3//
sahasradhAman vishikhAn vigrIvAM chhAyayA tvam /
prati sma chakruShe kR^ityAM priyAM priyAvate hara //4//
anayAham oShadhyA sarvAH kR^ityA adUduSham /
yAM kShetre chakrur yAM goShu yAM vA te puruSheShu //5//
yash chakAra na shashAka kartuM shashre pAdam a~Ngurim /
chakAra bhadram asmabhyam Atmane tapanam tu saH //6//
apAmArgo 'pa mArShTu kShetriyaM shapathash cha yaH /
apAha yAtudhAnIr apa sarvA arAyyaH //7//
apamR^ijya yAtudhAnAn apa sarvA arAyyaH /
apAmArga tvayA vayaM sarvaM tad apa mR^ijmahe //8//
121

uto asy abandhukR^id uto asi nu jAmikR^it /
uto kR^ityAkR^itaH prajAM nadam ivA chhindhi vArShikam //1//
brAhmaNena paryuktAsi kaNvena nArShadena /
senevaiShi tviShImatI na tatra bhayam asti yatra prApnoShy oShadhe //2//
agram eShy oShadhInAM jyotiShevAbhidIpayan /
uta trAtAsi pAkasyAtho hantAsi rakShasaH //3//
yad ado devA asurAMs tvayAgre nirakurvata /
tatas tvam adhy oShadhe 'pAmArgo ajAyathAH //4//
vibhindatI shatashAkhA vibhindan nAma te pitA /
pratyag vi bhindhi tvaM taM yo asmAM abhidAsati //5//
asad bhUmyAH sam abhavat tad yAm eti mahad vyachaH /
tad vai tato vidhUpAyat pratyak kartAram R^ichhatu //6//
pratya~N hi saMbabhUvitha pratIchInaphalas tvam /
sarvAn mach chhapathAm adhi varIyo yAvayA vadham //7//
shatena mA pari pAhi sahasreNAbhi rakShA mA /
indras te vIrudhAM pata ugra ojmAnam A dadhat //8//
122

A pashyati prati pashyati parA pashyati pashyati /
divam antarikSham Ad bhUmiM sarvaM tad devi pashyati //1//
tisro divas tisraH pR^ithivIH ShaT chemAH pradishAH pR^ithak /
tvayAhaM sarvA bhUtAni pashyAni devy oShadhe //2//
divyasya suparNasya tasya hAsi kanInikA /
sA bhUmim A rurohitha vahyaM shrAntA vadhUr iva //3//
tAM me sahasrAkSho devo dakShiNe hasta A dadhat /
tayAhaM sarvaM pashyAmi yash cha shUdra utAryaH //4//
AviSh kR^iNuShva rUpAni mAtmAnam apa gUhathAH /
atho sahasrachakSho tvaM prati pashyAH kimIdinaH //5//
darshaya mA yAtudhAnAn darshaya yAtudhAnyaH /
pishAchAnt sarvAn darshayeti tvA rabha oShadhe //6//
kashyapasya chakShur asi shunyAsh cha chaturakShyAH /
vIdhre sUryam iva sarpantaM mA pishAchaM tiras karaH //7//
ud agrabhaM paripANAd yAtudhAnaM kimIdinam /
tenAhaM sarvaM pashyAmy uta shUdram utAryam //8//
yo antarikSheNa patati divam yash cha atisarpati /
bhUmiM yo manyate nAthaM taM pishAcham pra darshaya //9//
123

A gAvo agmann uta bhadram akrant sIdantu goShThe raNayantv asme /
prajAvatIH pururUpA iha syur indrAya pUrvIr uShaso duhAnAH //1//
indro yajvane gR^iNate cha shikShata uped dadAti na svaM muShAyati /
bhUyobhUyo rayim id asya vardhayann abhinne khilye ni dadhAti devayum //2//
na tA nashanti na dabhAti taskaro nAsAm Amitro vyathir A dadharShati /
devAMsh cha yAbhir yajate dadAti cha jyog it tAbhiH sachate gopatiH saha //3//
na tA arvA reNukakATo 'shnute na saMskR^itatram upa yanti tA abhi /
urugAyam abhayaM tasya tA anu gAvo martasya vi charanti yajvanaH //4//
gAvo bhago gAva indro ma ichhAd gAva somasya prathamasya bhakShaH /
imA yA gAvaH sa janAsa indra ichhAmi hR^idA manasA chid indram //5//
yUyaM gAvo medayatha kR^ishaM chid ashrIraM chit kR^iNuthA supratIkam /
bhadraM gR^ihaM kR^iNutha bhadravAcho bR^ihad vo vaya uchyate sabhAsu //6//
prajAvatIH sUyavase rushantIH shuddhA apaH suprapANe pibantIH /
mA va stena Ishata mAghashaMsaH pari vo rudrasya hetir vR^iNaktu //7//
124

imam indra vardhaya kShatriyaM me imaM vishAm ekavR^iShaM kR^iNu tvam /
nir amitrAn akShNuhy asya sarvAMs tAn randhayAsmA ahamuttareShu //1//
emaM bhaja grAme ashveShu goShu niSh TaM bhaja yo amitro asya /
varShma kShatrANAm ayam astu rAjendra shatruM randhaya sarvam asmai //2//
ayam astu dhanapatir dhanAnAm ayaM vishAM vishpatir astu rAjA /
asminn indra mahi varchAMsi dhehy avarchasaM kR^iNuhi shatrum asya //3//
asmai dyAvApR^ithivI bhUri vAmaM duhAthAM gharmadughe iva dhenU /
ayaM rAjA priya indrasya bhUyAt priyo gavAm oShadhInAM pashUnAm //4//
yunajmi ta uttarAvantam indraM yena jayanti na parAjayante /
yas tvA karad ekavR^iShaM janAnAm uta rAj~nAm uttamaM mAnavAnAm //5//
uttaras tvam adhare te sapatnA ye ke cha rAjan pratishatravas te /
ekavR^iSha indrasakhA jigIvAM chhatrUyatAm A bharA bhojanAni //6//
siMhapratIko visho addhi sarvA vyAghrapratIko 'va bAdhasva shatrUn /
ekavR^iSha indrasakhA jigIvAM chhatrUyatAm A khidA bhojanAni //7//
125

agner manve prathamasya prachetasaH pA~nchajanyasya bahudhA yam indhate /
vishovishaH pravishivAMsam Imahe sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //1//
yathA havyaM vahasi jAtavedo yathA yaj~naM kalpayasi prajAnan /
evA devebhyaH sumatiM na A vaha sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //2//
yAmanyAmann upayuktaM vahiShThaM karma~Nkarmann Abhagam agnim IDe /
rakShohaNaM yaj~navR^idhaM ghR^itAhutaM sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //3//
sujAtaM jAtavedasam agniM vaishvAnaraM vibhum /
havyavAhaM havAmahe sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //4//
yena R^iShayo balam adyotayan yujA yenAsurANAm ayuvanta mAyAH /
yenAgninA paNIn indro jigAya sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //5//
yena devA amR^itam anvavindan yenauShadhIr madhumatIr akR^iNvan /
yena devAH sva1r Abharant sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //6//
yasyedaM pradishi yad virochate yaj jAtaM janitavyaM cha kevalam /
staumy agniM nAthito johavImi sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //7//
126

indrasya manmahe shashvad id asya manmahe vR^itraghna stomA upa mema AguH /
yo dAshuShaH sukR^ito havam eti sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //1//
ya ugrINAm ugrabAhur yayur yo dAnavAnAM balam Aruroja /
yena jitAH sindhavo yena gAvaH sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //2//
yash charShaNipro vR^iShabhaH svarvid yasmai grAvANaH pravadanti nR^imNam /
yasyAdhvaraH saptahotA madiShThaH sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //3//
yasya vashAsa R^iShabhAsa ukShaNo yasmai mIyante svaravaH svarvide /
yasmai shukraH pavate brahmashumbhitaH sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //4//
yasya juShTiM sominaH kAmayante yaM havanta iShumantaM gaviShTau /
yasminn arkaH shishriye yasminn ojaH sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //5//
yaH prathamaH karmakR^ityAya jaj~ne yasya vIryam prathamasyAnubuddham /
yenodyato vajro 'bhyAyatAhiM sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //6//
yaH saMgrAmAn nayati saM yudhe vashI yaH puShTAni saMsR^ijati dUyAni /
staumIndraM nAthito johavImi sa no mu~nchatv aMhasaH //7//
127

vAyoH savitur vidathAni manmahe yAv Atmanvad vishatho yau cha rakShathaH /
yau vishvasya paribhU babhUvathus tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //1//
yayoH saMkhyAtA varimA pArhivAni yAbhyAM rajo yupitam antarikShe /
yayoH prAyaM nAnv Anashe kash chana tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //2//
tava vrate ni vishante janAsas tvayy udite prerate chitrabhAno /
yuvaM vAyo savitA cha bhuvanAni rakShathas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //3//
apeto vAyo savitA cha duShkR^itam apa rakShAMsi shimidAM cha sedhatam /
saM hy UrjayA sR^ijathaH saM balena tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //4//
rayiM me poShaM savitota vAyus tanU dakSham A suvatAM sushevam /
ayakShmatAtiM maha iha dhattaM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //5//
pra sumatiM savitar vAya Utaye mahasvantaM matsaraM mAdayAthaH /
arvAg vAmasya pravato ni yachhataM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //6//
upa shreShThA na AshiSho devayor dhAmann asthiran /
staumi devaM savitAraM cha vAyuM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //7//
128

manve vAM dyAvApR^ithivI subhojasau sachetasau ye aprathethAm amitA yojanAni /
pratiShThe hy abhavataM vasUnAM te no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //1//
pratiShThe hy abhavataM vasUnAM pravR^iddhe devI subhage urUchI /
dyAvApR^ithivI bhavataM me syone te no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //2//
asantApe sutapasau huve 'ham urvI gambhIre kavibhir namasye /
dyAvApR^ithivI bhavataM me syone te no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //3//
ye amR^itaM bibhR^itho ye havIMShi ye srotyA bibhR^itho ye manuShyAn /
dyAvApR^ithivI bhavataM me syone te no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //4//
ye usriyA bibhR^itho ye vanaspatIn yayor vAM vishvA bhuvanAny antaH /
dyAvApR^ithivI bhavataM me syone te no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //5//
ye kIlAlena tarpayatho ye ghR^itena yAbhyAm R^ite na kiM chana shaknuvanti /
dyAvApR^ithivI bhavataM me syone te no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //6//
yan medam abhishochati yenayena vA kR^itaM pauruSheyAn na daivAt /
staumi dyAvApR^ithivI nAthito johavImi te no mu~nchatam amhasaH //7//
129

marutAM manve adhi me bruvantu premaM vAjaM vAjasAte avantu /
AshUn iva suyamAn ahva Utaye te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //1//
utsam akShitaM vyachanti ye sadA ya Asi~nchanti rasam oShadhIShu /
puro dadhe marutaH pR^ishnimAtR^IMs te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //2//
payo dhenUnAM rasam oShadhInAM javam arvatAM kavayo ya invatha /
shagmA bhavantu maruto naH syonAs te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //3//
apaH samudrAd divam ud vahanti divas pR^ithivIm abhi ye sR^ijanti /
ye adbhir IshAnA marutash charanti te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //4//
ye kIlAlena tarpayanti ye ghR^itena ye vA vayo medasA saMsR^ijanti /
ye adbhir IshAnA maruto varShayanti te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //5//
yadId idaM maruto mArutena yadi devA daivyenedR^ig Ara /
yUyam Ishidhve vasavas tasya niShkR^ites te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //6//
tigmam anIkam viditaM sahasvan mArutaM shardhaH pR^itanAsUgram /
staumi maruto nAthito johavImi te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //7//
130

bhavAsharvau manve vAM tasya vittaM yayor vAm idaM pradishi yad virochate /
yAv asyeshAthe dvipado yau chatuShpadas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //1//
yayor abhyabhva uta yad dUre chid yau viditAv iShubhR^itAm asiShThau /
yAv asyeshathe dvipado yau chatuShpadas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //2//
sahasrAkShau vR^itrahanA huvehaM dUregavyUtI stuvann emy ugrau /
yAv asyeshathe dvipado yau chatuShpadas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //3//
yAv ArebhAthe bahu sAkam agre pra ched asrAShTram abhibhAM janeShu /
yAv asyeshathe dvipado yau chatuShpadas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //4//
yayor vadhAn nApapadyate kash chanAntar deveShUta mAnuSheShu /
yAv asyeshathe dvipado yau chatuShpadas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //5//
yaH kR^ityAkR^in mUlakR^id yAtudhAno ni tasmin dhattaM vajram ugrau /
yAv asyeshathe dvipado yau chatuShpadas tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //6//
adhi no brUtaM pR^itanAsUgrau saM vajreNa sR^ijataM yaH kimIdI /
staumi bhavAsharvau nAthito johavImi tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //7//
131

manve vAM mitrAvaruNAv R^itAvR^idhau sachetasau druhvaNo yau nudethe /
pra satyAvAnam avatho bhareShu tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //1//
sachetasau druhvaNo yau nudethe pra satyAvAnam avatho bhareShu /
yau gachhatho nR^ichakShasau babhruNA sutaM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //2//
yAv a~Ngirasam avatho yAv agastiM mitrAvaruNA jamadagnim attrim /
yau kashyapam avatho yau vasiShThaM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //3//
yau shyAvAshvam avatho vAdhryashvaM mitrAvaruNA purumIDham attrim /
yau vimadam avatho saptavadhriM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //4//
yau bharadvAjam avatho yau gaviShThiraM vishvAmitraM varuNa mitra kutsam /
yau kakShIvantam avatho prota kaNvaM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //5//
yau medhAtithim avatho yau trishokaM mitrAvaruNAv ushanAm kAvyaM yau /
yau gotamam avatho prota mugdalaM tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //6//
yayo rathaH satyavartma rjurashmir mithuyA charantam abhiyAti dUShayan /
staumi mitrAvaruNau nAthito johavImi tau no mu~nchatam aMhasaH //7//
132

ahaM rudrebhir vasubhish charAmy aham Adityair uta vishvadevaiH /
ahaM mitrAvarunobhA bibharmy aham indrAgnI aham ashvinobhA //1//
ahaM rAShTrI saMgamanI vasUnAM chikituShI prathamA yaj~niyAnAm /
tAM mA devA vy adadhuH purutrA bhUristhAtrAM bhUry AveshayantaH //2//
aham eva svayam idaM vadAmi juShTaM devAnAm uta mAnuShANAm /
yaM kAmaye tantam ugraM kR^iNomi taM brahmANaM tam R^iShiM taM sumedhAm //3//
mayA so 'nnam atti yo vipashyati yaH prANati ya Im shR^iNoty uktam /
amantavo mAM ta upa kShiyanti shrudhi shruta shruddheyaM te vadAmi //4//
ahaM rudrAya dhanur A tanomi brahmadviShe sharave hantavA u /
ahaM janAya samadaM kR^iNomi aham dyAvApR^ithivI A vivesha //5//
ahaM somam AhanasaM bibharmy ahaM tvaShTAram uta pUShaNaM bhagam /
ahaM dadhAmi draviNA haviShmate suprAvyA3 yajamAnAya sunvate //6//
ahaM suve pitaram asya mUrdhan mama yonir apsv a1ntaH samudre /
tato vi tiShThe bhuvanAni vishvotAmUM dyAM varShmaNopa spR^ishAmi //7//
aham eva vAtaiva pra vAmy ArabhamANA bhuvanAni vishvA /
paro divA para enA pR^ithivyaitAvatI mahimnA saM babhUva //8//
133

tvayA manyo saratham Arujanto harShamANA hR^iShitAso marutvan /
tigmeShava AyudhA saMshishAnA upa pra yantu naro agnirUpAH //1//
agnir iva manyo tviShitaH sahasva senAnIr naH sahure hUta edhi /
hatvAya shatrUn vi bhajasva veda ojo mimAno vi mR^idho nudasva //2//
sahasva manyo abhimAtim asmai rujan mR^iNan pramR^iNan prehi shatrUn /
ugraM te pAjo nanv A rurudhre vashI vashaM nayAsA ekaja tvam //3//
eko bahUnAm asi manya IDitA vishaMvisham yuddhAya saM shishAdhi /
akR^ittaruk tvayA yujA vayaM dyumantaM ghoSham vijayAya kR^iNmasi //4//
vijeShakR^id indra ivAnavabravo 'smAkaM manyo adhipA bhaveha /
priyaM te nAma sahure gR^iNImasi vidmA tam utsaM yata AbabhUtha //5//
AbhUtyA sahajA vajra sAyaka saho bibharShi sahabhUte uttaram /
kratvA no manyo saha medy edhi mahAdhanasya puruhUta saMsR^iji //6//
saMsR^iShTaM dhanam ubhayaM samAkR^itam asmabhyaM dhattAM varuNash cha manyuH /
bhiyo dadhAnA hR^idayeShu shatravah parAjitAso apa ni layantAm //7//
134

yas te manyo 'vidhad vajra sAyaka saha ojaH puShyati vishvam AnuShak /
sAhyAma dAsam AryaM tvayA yujA vayaM sahaskR^itena sahasA sahasvatA //1//
manyur indro manyur evAsa devo manyur hotA varuNo jAtavedAH /
manyuM visha IDate mAnuShIr yAH pahi no manyo tapasA sajoShAH //2//
abhIhi manyo tavasas tavIyAn tapasA yujA vi jahi shatrUn /
amitrahA vR^itrahA dasyuhA cha vishvA vasUny A bharA tvaM naH //3//
tvaM hi manyo abhibhUtyojAH svayaMbhUr bhAmo abhimAtiShAhaH /
vishvacharShaNiH sahuriH sahIyAn asmAsv ojaH pR^itanAsu dhehi //4//
abhAgaH sann apa pareto asmi tava kratvA taviShasya prachetaH /
taM tvA manyo akratur jihIDAhaM svA tanUr baladAvA na ehi //5//
ayaM te asmy upa na ehy arvA~N pratIchInaH sahure vishvadAvan /
manyo vajrinn abhi na A vavR^itsva hanAva dasyUMr uta bodhy ApeH //6//
abhi prehi dakShiNato bhavA no 'dhA vR^itrANi ja~NghanAva bhUri /
juhomi te dharuNaM madhvo agram ubhAv upAMshu prathamA pibAva //7//
135

apa naH shoshuchad agham agne shushugdhy A rayim /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //1//
sukShetriyA sugAtuyA vasUyA cha yajAmahe /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //2//
pra yad bhandiShTha eShAM prAsmAkAsash cha sUrayaH /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //3//
pra yat te agne sUrayo jAyemahi pra te vayam /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //4//
pra yad agneH sahasvato vishvato yanti bhAnavaH /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //5//
tvaM hi vishvatomukha vishvataH paribhUr asi /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //6//
dviSho no vishvatomukhAti nAveva pAraya /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //7//
sa naH sindhum iva nAvAti parSha svastaye /
apa naH shoshuchad agham //8//
136

brahmAsya shIrSham bR^ihad asya pR^iShThaM vAmadevyam udaram odanasya /
chhandAMsi pakShau mukham asya satyaM viShTArI jAtas tapaso'dhi yaj~naH //1//
anasthAH pUtAH pavanena shuddhAH shuchayaH shuchim api yanti lokam /
naiShAM shishnaM pra dahati jAtavedAH svarge loke bahu straiNam eShAm //2//
viShTAriNam odanaM ye pachanti nainAn avartiH sachate kadA chana /
Aste yama upa yAti devAnt sam gandharvair madate somyebhiH //3//
viShTAriNam odanam ye pachanti nainAn yamaH pari muShNAti retaH /
rathI ha bhUtvA rathayAna Iyate pakShI ha bhUtvAti divaH sam eti //4//
eSha yaj~nAnAM vitato vahiShTho viShTAriNaM paktvA divam A vivesha /
ANDIkaM kumudaM saM tanoti bisaM shAlUkaM shaphako mulAlI /
etAs tvA dhArA upa yantu sarvAH svarge loke madhumat pinvamAnA upa tvA tiShThantu puShkariNIH samantAH //5//
ghR^itahradA madhukUlAH surodakAH kShIreNa pUrNA udakena dadhnA /
etAs tvA dhArA upa yantu sarvAH svarge loke madhumat pinvamAnA upa tvA tiShThantu puShkariNIH samantAH //6//
chaturaH kumbhAMsh chaturdhA dadAmi kShIreNa pUrnA.N udakena dadhnA /
etAs tvA dhArA upa yantu sarvAH svarge loke madhumat pinvamAnA upa tvA tiShThantu puShkariNIH samantAH //7//
imam odanaM ni dadhe brAhmaNeShu viShTAriNaM lokajitaM svargam /
sa me mA kSheShTa svadhayA pinvamAno vishvarUpA dhenuH kAmadughA me astu //8//
137

yam odanaM prathamajA R^itasya prajApatis tapasA brahmaNe 'pachat /
yo lokAnAM vidhR^itir nAbhireShAt tenaudanenAti tarANi mR^ityum //1//
yenAtaran bhUtakR^ito 'ti mR^ityuM yam anvavindan tapasA shrameNa /
yaM papAcha brahmaNe brahma pUrvaM tenaudanenAti tarANi mR^ityum //2//
yo dAdhAra pR^ithivIM vishvabhojasaM yo antarikSham ApR^iNAd rasena /
yo astabhnAd divam Urdhvo mahimnA tenaudanenAti tarANi mR^ityum //3//
yasmAn mAsA nirmitAs triMshadarAH saMvatsaro yasmAn nirmito dvAdashAraH /
ahorAtrA yaM pariyanto nApus tenaudanenAti tarANi mR^ityum //4//
yaH prANadaH prANadavAn babhUva yasmai lokA ghR^itavantaH kSharanti /
jyotiShmatIH pradisho yasya sarvAs tenaudanenAti tarANi mR^ityum //5//
yasmAt pakvAd amR^itaM saMbabhUva yo gAyatryA adhipatir babhUva /
yasmin vedA nihitA vishvarUpAs tenaudanenAti tarANi mR^ityum //6//
ava bAdhe dviShantaM devapIyuM sapatnA ye me 'pa te bhavantu /
brahmaudanaM vishvajitaM pachAmi shR^iNvantu me shraddadhAnasya devAH //7//
138

tAnt satyaujAH pra dahatv agnir vaishvAnaro vR^iShA /
yo no durasyAd dipsAch chAtho yo no arAtiyAt //1//
yo no dipsAd adipsato dipsato yash cha dipsati /
vaishvAnarasya daMShTrayor agner api dadhAmi tam //2//
ya Agare mR^igayante pratikroshe 'mAvAsye /
kravyAdo anyAn dipsataH sarvAMs tAnt sahasA sahe //3//
sahe pishAchAnt sahasaiShAM draviNaM dade /
sarvAn durasyato hanmi saM ma AkUtir R^idyatAm //4//
ye devAs tena hAsante sUryeNa mimate javam /
nadIShu parvateShu ye saM taiH pashubhir vide //5//
tapano asmi pishAchAnAm vyAghro gomatAm iva /
shvAnaH siMham iva dR^iShTvA te na vindante nya~nchanam //6//
na pishAchaiH saM shaknomi na stenaiH na vanargubhiH /
pishAchAs tasmAn nashyanti yam ahaM grAmam Avishe //7//
yaM grAmam Avishata idam ugraM saho mama /
pishAchAs tasmAn nashyanti na pApam upa jAnate //8//
ye mA krodhayanti lapitA hastinaM mashakA iva /
tAn ahaM manye durhitAn jane alpashayUn iva //9//
abhi taM nirR^itir dhattAm ashvam iva ashvAbhidhAnyA /
malvo yo mahyaM krudhyati sa u pAshAn na muchyate //10//
139

tvayA pUrvam atharvANo jaghnU rakShAMsy oShadhe /
tvayA jaghAna kashyapas tvayA kaNvo agastyaH //1//
tvayA vayam apsaraso gandharvAMs chAtayAmahe /
ajashR^i~Ngy aja rakShaH sarvAn gandhena nAshaya //2//
nadIM yantv apsaraso 'pAM tAram avashvasam /
gulgulUH pIlA nalady aù3kShagandhiH pramandanI /
tat paretApsarasaH pratibuddhA abhUtana //3//
yatrAshvatthA nyagrodhA mahAvR^ikShAH shikhaNDinaH /
tat paretApsarasaH pratibuddhA abhUtana //4//
yatra vaH pre~NkhA haritA arjunA uta yatrAghAtAH karkaryaH saMvadanti /
tat paretApsarasaH pratibuddhA abhUtana //5//
eyam agann oShadhInAM vIrudhAm vIryAvatI /
ajashR^i~Ngy arATakI tIkShNashR^i~NgI vyr?Shatu //6//
AnR^ityataH shikhaNDino gandharvasyApsarApateH /
bhinadmi muShkAv api yAmi shepaH //7//
bhImA indrasya hetayaH shataM R^iShTIr ayasmayIH /
tAbhir haviradAn gandharvAn avakAdAn vyr?Shatu //8//
bhImA indrasya hetayaH shataM R^iShTIr hiraNyayIH /
tAbhir haviradAn gandharvAn avakAdAn vyr?Shatu //9//
avakAdAn abhishochAn apsu jyotaya mAmakAn /
pishAchAnt sarvAn oShadhe pra mR^iNIhi sahasva cha //10//
shvevaikaH kapir ivaikaH kumAraH sarvakeshakaH /
priyo dR^isha iva bhUtvA gandharvaH sachate striyas /
tam ito nAshayAmasi brahmaNA vIryAvatA //11//
jAyA id vo apsaraso gandharvAH patayo yuyam /
apa dhAvatAmartyA martyAn mA sachadhvam //12//
140

udbhindatIM saMjayantIm apsarAM sAdhudevinIm /
glahe kR^itAni kR^iNvAnAm apsarAM tAm iha huve //1//
vichinvatIm AkirantIm apsarAM sAdhudevinIm /
glahe kR^itAni gR^ihNAnAm apsarAM tAm iha huve //2//
yAyaiH parinR^ityaty AdadAnA kR^itaM glahAt /
sA naH kR^itAni sIShatI prahAm Apnotu mAyayA /
sA naH payasvaty aitu mA no jaiShur idaM dhanam //3//
yA akSheShu pramodante shuchaM krodhaM cha bibhratI /
AnandinIM pramodinIm apsarAM tAm iha huve //4//
sUryasya rashmIn anu yAH sa~ncharanti marIchIr vA yA anusa~ncharanti /
yAsAm R^iShabho dUrato vAjinIvAnt sadyaH sarvAn lokAn paryeti rakShan /
sa na aitu homam imaM jusANo3 'ntarikSheNa saha vAjinIvAn //5//
antarikShena saha vAjinIvan karkIM vatsAm iha rakSha vAjin /
ime te stokA bahulA ehy arvA~N iyaM te karkIha te mano 'stu //6//
antarikSheNa saha vAjinIvan karkIM vatsAm iha rakSha vAjin /
ayaM ghAso ayaM vraja iha vatsAM ni badhnImaH /
yathAnAma va Ishmahe svAhA //7//
141

pR^ithivyAm agnaye sam anamant sa Ardhnot /
yathA pR^ithivyAm agnaye samanamann evA mahyaM saMnamaH saM namantu //1//
pR^ithivI dhenus tasyA agnir vatsaH /
sA me 'gninA vatseneSham UrjaM kAmaM duhAm /
AyuH prathamaM prajAM poShaM rayiM svAhA //2//
antarikShe vAyave sam anamant sa Ardhnot /
yathAntarikShe vAyave samanamann evA mahyaM saMnamaH saM namantu //3//
antarikShaM dhenus tasyA vatsaH /
sA me vAyunA vatseneSham UrjaM kAmaM duhAm /
AyuH prathamaM prajAM poShaM rayiM svAhA //4//
divyAdityAya sam anamant sa Ardhnot /
yathA divyAdityAya samanamann evA mahyaM saMnamaH saM namantu //5//
dyaur dhenus tasyA Adityo vatsaH /
sA ma Adityena vatseneSham UrjaM kAmaM duhAm /
AyuH prathamaM prajAM poShaM rayiM svAhA //6//
dikShu chandrAya sam anamant sa Ardhnot /
yathA dikShu chandrAya samanamann evA mahyaM saMnamaH saM namantu //7//
disho dhenavas tAsAM chandro vatsaH /
tA me chandreNa vatseneSham UrjaM kAmaM duhAm AyuH prathamaM prajAM posaM rayiM svAhA //8//
agnAv agnish charati praviShTa R^iShINAm putro abhishastipA u /
namaskAreNa namasA te juhomi mA devAnAM mithuyA karma bhAgam //9//
hR^idA pUtam manasA jAtavedo vishvAni deva vayunAni vidvAn /
saptAsyAni tava jAtavedas tebhyo juhomi sa juShasva havyam //10//
142

ye purastAj juhvati jAtavedaH prAchyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
agnim R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //1//
ye dakShiNato juhvati jAtavedo dakShiNAyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
yamaM R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enA pratisareNa hanmi //2//
ye pashchAj juhvati jAtavedaH pratIchyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
varuNam R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //3//
ya uttarato juhvati jAtaveda udIchyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
somam R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //4//
ye 'dhastAj juhvati jAtaveda udIchyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
bhUmim R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //5//
ye3 'ntarikShAj juhvati jAtavedo vyadhvAyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
vAyum R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //6//
ya upariShTAj juhvati jAtaveda UrdhvAyA disho 'bhidAsanty asmAn /
sUryam R^itvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //7//
ye dishAm antardeshebhyo juhvati jAtavedaH sarvAbhyo digbhyo 'bhidAsanti asmAn /
brahma rtvA te parA~ncho vyathantAM pratyag enAn pratisareNa hanmi //8//
143
Book:
5

R^idha~Nmantro yoniM ya AbabhUvamR^itAsur vardhamAnaH sujanmA /
adabdhAsur bhrAjamAno 'heva trito dhartA dAdhAra trINi //1//
A yo dharmANi prathamaH sasAda tato vapUMShi kR^iNuShe purUNi /
dhAsyur yoniM prathama A viveshA yo vAcham anuditAM chiketa //2//
yas te shokAya tanvaM rirecha kSharad dhiraNyaM shuchayo 'nu svAH /
atrA dadhete amR^itAni nAmAsme vastrANi visha erayantAm //3//
pra yad ete prataraM pUrvyaM guH sadaHsada AtiShThanto ajuryam /
kaviH shuShasya mAtarA rihANe jAmyai dhuryaM patim erayethAm //4//
tad U Shu te mahat pR^ithujman namaH kaviH kAvyena kR^iNomi /
yat samya~nchAv abhiyantAv abhi kShAm atrA mahI rodhachakre vAvR^idhete //5//
sapta maryAdAH kavayas tatakShus tAsAm id ekAm abhy aMhuro gAt /
Ayor ha skambha upamasya nIDe pathAM visarge dharuNeShu tasthau //6//
utAmR^itAsur vrata emi kR^invann asur AtmA tanva1s tat sumadguH /
uta vA shakro ratnaM dadhAty UrjayA vA yat sachate havirdAH //7//
uta putraH pitaraM kShatram IDe jyeShThaM maryAdam ahvayant svastaye /
darshan nu tA varuNa yAs te viShThA AvarvratataH kR^iNavo vapUMShi //8//
ardham ardhena payasA pR^iNakShy ardhena shuShma vardhase amura /
aviM vR^idhAma shagmiyaM sakhAyaM varuNaM putram adityA iShiram /
kavishastAny asmai vapUMShy avochAma rodasI satyavAchA //9//
144

tad id Asa bhuvaneShu jyeShTham yato yaj~na ugras tveShanR^imNaH /
sadyo jaj~nAno ni riNAti shatrUn anu yad enaM madanti vishva UmAH //1//
vavR^idhAnaH shavasA bhUryojAH shatrur dAsAya bhiyasaM dadhAti /
avyanach cha vyanach cha sasni saM te navanta prabhR^itA madeShu //2//
tve kratum api pR^i~nchanti bhUri dvir yad ete trir bhavanty UmAH /
svadoH svAdIyaH svAdunA sR^ijA sam adaH su madhu madhunAbhi yodhIH //3//
yadi chin nu tvA dhanA jayantaM raNeraNe anumadanti viprAH /
ojIyaH shuShmint sthiram A tanuShva mA tvA dabhan durevAsaH kashokAH //4//
tvayA vayaM shAshadmahe raNeShu prapashyanto yudhenyAni bhUri /
chodayAmi ta AyudhA vachobhiH saM te shishAmi brahmaNA vayAMsi //5//
ni tad dadhiShe 'vare pare cha yasminn AvithAvasA duroNe /
A sthApayata mAtaraM jigatnum ata invata karvarANi bhUri //6//
stuShva varShman puruvartmAnaM sam R^ibhvANam inatamam Aptam AptyAnAm /
A darshati shavasA bhUryojAH pra sakShati pratimAnaM pR^ithivyAH //7//
imA brahma bR^ihaddivaH kR^iNavad indrAya shUSham agriyaH svarShAH /
maho gotrasya kShayati svarAjA turash chid vishvam arNavat tapasvAn //8//
evA mahAn bR^ihaddivo atharvAvochat svAM tanva1m indram eva /
svasArau mAtaribhvarI aripre hinvanti chaine shavasA vardhayanti cha //9//
145

mamAgne varcho vihaveShv astu vayaM tvendhAnAs tanvam puShema /
mahyaM namantAM pradishash chatasras tvayAdhyakSheNa pR^itanA jayema //1//
agne manyuM pratinudan pareShAM tvaM no gopAH pari pAhi vishvataH /
apA~ncho yantu nivatA durasyavo 'maiShAM chittaM prabudhAM vi neshat //2//
mama devA vihave santu sarva indravanto maruto viShNur agniH /
mamAntarikSham urulokam astu mahyaM vAtaH pavatAM kAmAyAsmai //3//
mahyaM yajantAM mama yAnIShTAkUtiH satyA manaso me astu /
eno mA ni gAM katamach chanAhaM vishve devA abhi rakShantu meha //4//
mayi devA draviNam A yajantAM mayi AshIr astu mayi devahUtiH /
daivAH hotAraH saniShan na etad ariShTAH syAma tanvA suvIrAH //5//
daivIH ShaD urvIr uru naH kR^iNota vishve devAsa iha mAdayadhvam /
mA no vidad abhibhA mo ashastir mA no vidad vR^ijinA dveShyA yA //6//
tisro devIr mahi naH sharma yachhata prajAyai nas tanve3 yach cha puShTam /
mA hAsmahi prajayA mA tanUbhir mA radhAma dviShate soma rAjan //7//
uruvyachA no mahiShaH sharma yachhatv asmin have puruhUtaH purukShu /
sa naH prajAyai haryashva mR^iDendra mA no rIriSho mA parA dAH //8//
dhAtA vidhAtA bhuvanasya yas patir devaH savitAbhimAtiShAhaH /
AdityA rudrA ashvinobhA devAH pAntu yajamAnaM nirR^ithAt //9//
ye naH sapatnA apa te bhavantv indrAgnibhyAm ava bAdhAmaha enAn /
AdityA rudrA uparispR^isho no ugraM chettAram adhirAjam akrata //10//
arvA~ncham indram amuto havAmahe yo gojid dhanajid ashvajid yaH /
imaM no yaj~naM vihave shR^iNotv asmAkam abhUr haryashva medI //11//
146

yo giriShv ajAyathA vIrudhAM balavattamaH /
kuShThehi takmanAshana takmAnaM nAshayann itaH //1//
suparNasuvane girau jAtaM himavatas pari /
dhanair abhi shrutvA yanti vidur hi takmanAshanam //2//
ashvattho devasadanas tR^itIyasyAm ito divi /
tatrAmR^itasya chakShaNaM devAH kuShTham avanvata //3//
hiraNyayI naur acharad dhiraNyabandhanA divi /
tatrAmR^itasya puShpaM devAH kuShTham avanvata //4//
hiraNyayAH panthAna Asann aritrANi hiraNyayA /
nAvo hiraNyayIr Asan yAbhiH kuShThaM nirAvahan //5//
imaM me kuShTha pUruShaM tam A vaha taM niSh kuru /
tam u me agadaM kR^idhi //6//
devebhyo adhi jAto 'si somasyAsi sakhA hitaH /
sa prANAya vyAnAya chakShuShe me asmai mR^iDa //7//
uda~N jAto himavataH sa prAchyAM nIyase janam /
tatra kuShThasya nAmAny uttamAni vi bhejire //8//
uttamo nAma kuShThasy uttamo nAma te pitA /
yakShmaM cha sarvaM nAshaya takmAnaM chArasaM kR^idhi //9//
shIrShAmayaM upahatyAm akShyos tanvo3 rapaH /
kuShThas tat sarvaM niSh karad daivaM samaha vR^iShNyam //10//
147

rAtrI mAtA nabhaH pitAryamA te pitAmahaH /
silAchI nAma vA asi sA devAnAm asi svasA //1//
yas tvA pibati jIvati trAyase puruShaM tvam /
bhartrI hi shashvatAm asi janAnAM cha nya~nchanI //2//
vR^ikShaMvR^ikSham A rohasi vR^iShaNyantIva kanyalA /
jayantI pratyAtiShThantI sparaNI nAma vA asi //3//
yad daNDena yad iShvA yad vArur harasA kR^itam /
tasya tvam asi niShkR^itiH semaM niSh kR^idhi pUruSham //4//
bhadrAt plakShAn nis tiShThasy ashvatthAt khadirAd dhavAt /
bhadrAn nyagrodhAt parNAt sA na ehy arundhati //5//
hiraNyavarNe subhage sUryavarNe vapuShTame /
rutaM gachhAsi niShkR^ite niShkR^itir nAma vA asi //6//
hiraNyavarNe subhage shuShme lomashavakShane /
apAm asi svasA lAkShe vAto hAtmA babhUva te //7//
silAchI nAma kAnIno 'jababhru pitA tava /
ashvo yamasya yaH shyAvas tasya hAsnAsy ukShitA //8//
ashvasyAsnaH saMpatitA sA vR^ikShA.N abhi siShyade /
sarA patatriNI bhUtvA sA na ehy arundhati //9//
148

brahma jaj~nAnaM prathamaM purastAd vi sImataH surucho vena AvaH /
sa budhnyA upamA asya viShThAH satash cha yonim asatash cha vi vaH //1//
anAptA ye vaH prathamA yAni karmANi chakrire /
vIrAn no atra mA dabhan tad va etat puro dadhe //2//
sahasradhAra eva te sam asvaran divo nAke madhujihvA asashchataH /
tasya spasho na ni miShanti bhUrNayaH padepade pAshinaH santi setave //3//
pary U Shu pra dhanvA vAjasAtaye pari vR^itrANi sakShaNiH /
dviShas tad adhy arNaveneyase sanisraso nAmAsi trayodasho mAsa indrasya gR^ihaH //4//
nv e3tenArAtsIr asau svAhA /
tigmAyudhau tigmahetI sushevau somArudrAv iha su mR^iDataM naH //5//
avaitenArAtsIr asau svAhA /
tigmAyudhau tigmahetI sushevau somArudrAv iha su mR^iDataM naH //6//
apaitenArAtsIr asau svAhA /
tigmAyudhau tigmahetI sushevau somArudrAv iha su mR^iDataM naH //7//
mumuktam asmAn duritAd avadyAj juShethAm yaj~nam amR^itam asmAsu dhattam //8//
chakShuSho hete manaso hete brahmaNo hete tapasash cha hete /
menyA menir asy amenayas te santu ye 'smA.N abhyaghAyanti //9//
yo3 'smAMsh chakShuShA manasA chittyAkUtyA cha yo aghAyur abhidAsAt /
tvaM tAn agne menyAmenIn kR^iNu svAhA //10//
indrasya gR^iho 'si /
taM tvA pra padye taM tvA pra vishAmi sarvaguH sarvapUruShaH sarvAtmA sarvatanUH saha yan me 'sti tena //11//
indrasya sharmAsi /
taM tvA pra padye taM tvA pra vishAmi sarvaguH sarvapUruShaH sarvAtmA sarvatanUH saha yan me 'sti tena //12//
indrasya varmAsi /
taM tvA pra padye taM tvA pra vishAmi sarvaguH sarvapUruShaH sarvAtmA sarvatanUH saha yan me 'sti tena //13//
indrasya varUtham asi /
taM tvA pra padye taM tvA pra vishAmi sarvaguH sarvapUruShaH sarvAtmA sarvatanUH saha yan me 'sti tena //14//
149

A no bhara mA pari ShThA arAte mA no rakShIr dakShiNAM nIyamAnAm /
namo vIrtsAyA asamR^iddhaye namo astv arAtaye //1//
yam arAte purodhatse puruShaM parirApiNam /
namas te tasmai kR^iNmo mA vaniM vyathayIr mama //2//
pra No vanir devakR^itA divA naktaM cha kalpatAm /
arAtim anupremo vayaM namo astv arAtaye //3//
sarasvatIm anumatim bhagam yanto havAmahe /
vAcham juShTAM madhumatIm avAdiShaM devAnAM devahUtiShu //4//
yaM yAchAmy ahaM vAchA sarasvatyA manoyujA /
shraddhA tam adya vindatu dattA somena babhruNA //5//
mA vaniM mA vAchaM no vIrtsIr ubhAv indrAgnI A bharatAM no vasUni /
sarve no adya ditsanto 'rAtiM prati haryata //6//
paro 'pehy asamR^iddhe vi te hetim nayAmasi /
veda tvAhaM nimIvantIM nitudantIm arAte //7//
uta nagnA bobhuvatI svapnayA sachase janam /
arAte chittam vIrtsanty AkUtim puruShasya cha //8//
yA mahatI mahonmAnA vishvA AshA vyAnashe /
tasyai hiraNyakeshyai nirR^ityA akaraM namaH //9//
hiraNyavarNA subhagA hiraNyakashipur mahI /
tasyai hiraNyadrApaye 'rAtyA akaraM namaH //10//
150

vaika~Nkatenedhmena devebhya AjyaM vaha /
agne tAm iha mAdaya sarva A yantu me havam //1//
indrA yAhi me havam idaM kariShyAmi tach chhR^iNu /
ima aindrA atisarA AkUtiM saM namantu me /
tebhiH shakema vIrya1M jAtavedas tanUvashin //2//
yad asAv amuto devA adevaH saMsh chikIrShati /
mA tasyAgnir havyaM vAkShId dhavaM devA asya mopa gur mamaiva havam etana //3//
ati dhAvatAtisarA indrasya vachasA hata /
aviM vR^ika iva mathnIta sa vo jIvan mA mochi prAnam asyApi nahyata //4//
yam amI purodadhire brahmANam apabhUtaye /
indra sa te adhaspadaM taM praty asyAmi mR^ityave //5//
yadi preyur devapurA brahma varmANi chakrire /
tanUpAnaM paripANaM kR^iNvAnA yad upochire sarvaM tad arasaM kR^idhi //6//
yAn asAv atisarAMsh chakAra kR^iNavach cha yAn /
tvaM tAn indra vR^itrahan pratIchaH punar A kR^idhi yathAmuM tR^iNahAM janam //7//
yathendra udvAchanaM labdhvA chakre adhaspadam /
kR^iNve 'ham adharAn tathA amU~n chhashvatIbhyaH samAbhyaH //8//
atrainAn indra vR^itrahann ugro marmaNi vidhya /
atraivainAn abhi tiShThendra medy a1haM tava /
anu tvendrA rabhAmahe syAma sumatau tava //9//
151

dive svAhA //1//
pR^ithivyai svAhA //2//
antarikShAya svAhA //3//
antarikShAya svAhA //4//
dive svAhA //5//
pR^ithivyai svAhA //6//
sUryo me chakShur vAtaH prANo 'ntarikSham AtmA pR^ithivI sharIram /
astR^ito nAmAham ayam asmi sa AtmAnaM ni dadhe dyAvApR^ithivIbhyAM gopIthAya //7//
ud Ayur ud balam ut kR^itam ut kR^ityAm un manIShAm ud indriyam /
AyuShkR^id AyuShpatnI svadhAvantau gopA me staM gopAyataM mA /
Atmasadau me staM mA mA hiMsiShTam //8//
152

ashmavarma me 'si yo mA prAchyA disho 'ghAyur abhidAsAt /
etat sa R^ichhAt //1//
ashmavarma me 'si yo mA dakShiNAyA disho 'ghAyur abhidAsAt /
etat sa R^ichhAt //2//

bR^ihatA mana upa hvaye mAtarishvanA prANApAnau /

sarasvatyA vAcham upa hvayAmahe manoyujA //8//
153

kathaM mahe asurAyAbravIr iha kathaM pitre haraye tveShanR^imNaH /
pR^ishniM varuNa dakShiNAM dadAvAn punarmagha tvaM manasAchikitsIH //1//
na kAmena punarmagho bhavAmi saM chakShe kaM pR^ishnim etAm upAje /
kena nu tvam atharvan kAvyena kena jAtenAsi jAtavedAH //2//
satyam ahaM gabhIraH kAvyena satyaM jAtenAsmi jAtavedAH /
na me dAso nAryo mahitvA vrataM mImAya yad ahaM dhariShye //3//
na tvad anyaH kavitaro na medhayA dhIrataro varuNa svadhAvan /
tvaM tA vishvA bhuvanAni vettha sa chin nu tvaj jano mAyI bibhAya //4//
tvaM hy a~Nga varuNa svadhAvan vishvA vettha janima supraNIte /
kiM rajasa enA paro anyad asty enA kiM pareNAvaram amura //5//
ekaM rajasa enA paro anyad asty enA para ekena durNashaM chid arvAk /
tat te vidvAn varuNa pra bravImy adhovachasaH paNayo bhavantu nIchair dAsA upa sarpantu bhUmim //6//
tvaM hy a~Nga varuNa bravIShi punarmagheShv avadyAni bhUri /
mo Shu paNI.Nr abhy etAvato bhUn mA tvA vochann arAdhasaM janAsaH //7//
mA mA vochann arAdhasaM janAsaH punas te pR^ishniM jaritar dadAmi /
stotraM me vishvam A yAhi shachIbhir antar vishvAsu mAnuShIShu dikShu //8//
A te stotrANy udyatAni yantv antar vishvAsu mAnuShIShu dikShu /
dehi nu me yan me adatto asi yujyo me saptapadaH sakhAsi //9//
samA nau bandhur varuNa samA jA vedAhaM tad yan nAv eShA samA jA /
dadAmi tad yat te adatto asmi yujyas te saptapadaH sakhAsmi //10//
devo devAya gR^iNate vayodhA vipro viprAya stuvate sumedhAH /
ajIjano hi varuNa svadhAvann atharvANaM pitaraM devabandhum /
tasmA u rAdhaH kR^iNuhi suprashastaM sakhA no asi paramaM cha bandhuH //11//
154

samiddho adya manuSho duroNe devo devAn yajasi jAtavedaH /
A cha vaha mitramahash chikitvAn tvaM dUtaH kavir asi prachetAH //1//
tanUnapAt patha R^itasya yAnAn madhvA sama~njant svadayA sujihva /
manmAni dhIbhir uta yaj~nam R^indhan devatrA cha kR^iNuhy adhvaram naH //2//
AjuhvAna IDyo bandyash chA yAhy agne vasubhiH sajoShAH /
tvaM devAnAm asi yahva hotA sa enAn yakShIShito yajIyAn //3//
prAchInaM barhiH pradishA pR^ithivyA vastor asyA vR^ijyate agre ahnAm /
vy ù prathate vitaraM varIyo devebhyo aditaye syonam //4//
vyachasvatIr urviyA vi shrayantAM patibhyo na janayaH shumbhamAnAH /
devIr dvAro bR^ihatIr vishvaminvA devebhyo bhavata suprAyaNAH //5//
A suShvayantI yajate upAke uShAsAnaktA sadatAM ni yonau /
divye yoShaNe bR^ihatI surukme adhi shriyaM shukrapishaM dadhAne //6//
daivyA hotArA prathamA suvAchA mimAnA yaj~naM manuSho yajadhyai /
prachodayantA vidatheShu kArU prAchInaM jyotiH pradishA dishantA //7//
A no yaj~naM bhAratI tUyam etv iDA manuShvad iha chetayantI /
tisro devIr barhir edaM syonaM sarasvatIH svapasaH sadantAm //8//
ya ime dyAvApR^ithivI janitrI rUpair apiMshad bhuvanAni vishvA /
tam adya hotar iShito yajIyAn devaM tvaShTAram iha yakShi vidvAn //9//
upAvasR^ija tmanyA sama~njan devAnAM pAtha R^ituthA havIMShi /
vanaspatiH shamitA devo agniH svadantu havyaM madhunA ghR^itena //10//
sadyo jAto vy amimIta yaj~nam agnir devAnAm abhavat purogAH /
asya hotuH prashiShy R^itasya vAchi svAhAkR^itaM havir adantu devAH //11//
155

dadir hi mahyaM varuNo divaH kavir vachobhir ugrair ni riNAmi te viSham /
khAtam akhAtam uta saktam agrabham ireva dhanvan ni jajAsa te viSham //1//
yat te apodakaM viShaM tat ta etAsv agrabham /
gR^ihNAmi te madhyamam uttamaM rasam utAvamam bhiyasA neshad Ad u te //2//
vR^iShA me ravo nabhasA na tanyatur ugreNa te vachasA bAdha Ad u te /
ahaM tam asya nR^ibhir agrabham rasaM tamasa iva jyotir ud etu sUryaH //3//
chakShuShA te chakShur hanmi viSheNa hanmi te viSham /
ahe mriyasva mA jIvIH pratyag abhy etu tvA viSham //4//
kairAta pR^ishna upatR^iNya babhra A me shR^iNutAsitA alIkAH /
mA me sakhyuH stAmAnam api ShThAtAshrAvayanto ni viShe ramadhvam //5//
asitasya taimAtasya babhror apodakasya cha /
sAtrAsAhasyAhaM manyor ava jyAm iva dhanvano vi mu~nchAmi rathAm? iva //6//
AligI cha viligI cha pitA cha matA cha /
vidma vaH sarvato bandhv arasAH kiM kariShyatha //7//
urugUlAyA duhitA jAtA dAsy asiknyA /
prata~NkaM dadruShINAM sarvAsAm arasam viSham //8//
karNA shvAvit tad abravId girer avacharantikA /
yAH kAsh chemAH khanitrimAs tAsAm arasatamaM viSham //9//
tAbuvaM na tAbuvaM na ghet tvam asi tAbuvam /
tAbuvenArasaM viSham //10//
tastuvaM na tastuvaM na ghet tvam asi tastuvam /
tastuvenArasaM viSham //11//
156

suparNas tvAnv avindat sUkaras tvAkhanan nasA /
dipsauShadhe tvaM dipsantam ava kR^ityAkR^itaM jahi //1//
ava jahi yAtudhAnAn ava kR^ityAkR^itaM jahi /
atho yo asmAn dipsati tam u tvaM jahy oShadhe //2//
rishyasyeva parIshAsaM parikR^itya pari tvachaH /
kR^ityAM kR^ityAkR^ite devA niShkam iva prati mu~nchata //3//
punaH kR^ityAM kR^ityAkR^ite hastagR^ihya parA Naya /
samakSham asma A dhehi yathA kR^ityAkR^itam hanat //4//
kR^ityAH santu kR^ityAkR^ite shapathaH shapathIyate /
sukho ratha iva vartatAM kR^ityA kR^ityAkR^itaM punaH //5//
yadi strI yadi vA pumAn kR^ityAM chakAra pApmane /
tAm u tasmai nayAmasy ashvam ivAshvAbhidhAnyA //6//
yadi vAsi devakR^itA yadi vA puruShaiH kR^itA /
tAM tvA punar NayAmasIndreNa sayujA vayam //7//
agne pR^itanAShAT pR^itanAH sahasva /
punaH kR^ityAM kR^ityAkR^ite pratiharaNena harAmasi //8//
kR^itavyadhani vidya taM yash chakAra tam ij jahi /
na tvAm achakruShe vayaM vadhAya saM shishImahi //9//
putra iva pitaraM gachha svaja ivAbhiShThito dasha /
bandham ivAvakrAmI gachha kR^itye kR^ityAkR^itaM punaH //10//
ud eNIva vAraNy abhiskandhaM mR^igIva /
kR^ityA kartAram R^ichhatu //11//
iShvA R^ijIyaH patatu dyAvApR^ithivI taM prati /
sA taM mR^igam iva gR^ihNatu kR^ityA kR^ityAkR^itaM punaH //12//
agnir ivaitu pratikUlam anukUlam ivodakam /
sukho ratha iva vartatAM kR^ityA kR^ityAkR^itaM punaH //13//
157

ekA cha me dasha cha me 'pavaktAra oShadhe /
R^itajAta R^itAvari madhu me madhulA karaH //1//

yady ekavR^iSho 'si sR^ijAraso 'si //1//
