159

te 'vadan prathamA brahmakilbiShe 'kUpAraH salilo mAtarishvA /
vIDuharAs tapa ugraM mayobhUr Apo devIH prathamajA R^itasya //1//
somo rAjA prathamo brahmajAyAM punaH prAyachhad ahR^iNIyamAnaH /
anvartitA varuNo mitra AsId agnir hotA hastagR^ihyA ninAya //2//
hastenaiva grAhya Adhir asyA brahmajAyeti ched avochat /
na dUtAya praheyA tastha eShA tathA rAShTraM gupitaM kShatriyasya //3//
yAm Ahus tArakaiShA vikeshIti duchhunAM grAmam avapadyamAnAm /
sA brahmajAyA vi dunoti rAShTraM yatra prApAdi shasha ulkuShImAn //4//
brahmachArI charati veviShad viShaH sa devAnAM bhavaty ekam a~Ngam /
tena jAyAm anv avindad bR^ihaspatiH somena nItAM juhva1M na devAH //5//
devA vA etasyAm avadanta pUrve saptaR^iShayas tapasA ye niSheduH /
bhImA jAyA brAhmaNasyApanItA durdhAM dadhAti parame vyoman //6//
ye garbhA avapadyante jagad yach chApalupyate /
vIrA ye tR^ihyante mitho brahmajAyA hinasti tAn //7//
uta yat patayo dasha striyAH pUrve abrAhmaNAH /
brahmA ched dhastam agrahIt sa eva patir ekadhA //8//
brAhmaNa eva patir na rAjanyo3 na vaishyaH /
tat sUryaH prabruvann eti pa~nchabhyo mAnavebhyaH //9//
punar vai devA adaduH punar manuShyA adaduH /
rAjAnaH satyaM gR^ihNAnA brahmajAyAM punar daduH //10//
punardAya brahmajAyAM kR^itvA devair nikilbiSham /
UrjaM pR^ithivyA bhaktvorugAyam upAsate //11//
nAsya jAyA shatavAhI kalyANI talpam A shaye /
yasmin rAShTre nirudhyate brahmajAyAchittyA //12//
na vikarNaH pR^ithushirAs tasmin veshmani jAyate /
yasmin rAShTre nirudhyate brahmajAyAchittyA //13//
nAsya ksattA niShkagrIvaH sUnAnAm ety agrataH /
yasmin rAShTre nirudhyate brahmajAyAchittyA //14//
nAsya shvetaH kR^iShNakarNo dhuri yukto mahIyate /
yasmin rAShTre nirudhyate brahmajAyAchittyA //15//
nAsya kShetre puShkariNI nANDIkam jAyate bisam /
yasmin rAShTre nirudhyate brahmajAyAchittyA //16//
nAsmai pR^ishniM vi duhanti ye 'syA doham upAsate /
yasmin rAShTre nirudhyate brahmajAyAchittyA //17//
nAsya dhenuH kalyANI nAnaDvAnt sahate dhuram /
vijAnir yatra brahmaNo rAtriM vasati pApayA //18//
160

naitAM te devA adadus tubhyaM nR^ipate attave /
mA brAhmaNasya rAjanya gAM jighatso anAdyAm //1//
akShadrugdho rAjanyaH pApa AtmaparAjitaH /
sa brAhmaNasya gAm adyAd adya jIvAni mA shvaH //2//
AviShTitAghaviShA pR^idAkUr iva charmaNA /
sA brAhmaNasya rAjanya tR^iShTaiShA gaur anAdyA //3//
nir vai kShatraM nayati hanti varcho 'gnir ivArabdho vi dunoti sarvam /
yo brAhmaNaM manyate annam eva sa viShasya pibati taimAtasya //4//
ya enaM hanti mR^iduM manyamAno devapIyur dhanakAmo na chittAt /
saM tasyendro hR^idaye 'gnim indhe ubhe enaM dviShTo nabhasI charantam //5//
na brAhmaNo hiMsitavyo3 'gniH priyatanor iva /
somo hy asya dAyAda indro asyAbhishastipAH //6//
shatApAShThAM ni girati tAM na shaknoti niHkhidam /
annaM yo brahmaNAm malvaH svAdv a1dmIti manyate //7//
jihvA jyA bhavati kulmalaM vA~N nADIkA dantAs tapasAbhidigdhAH /
tebhir brahmA vidhyati devapIyUn hR^idbalair dhanurbhir devajUtaiH //8//
tIkShNeShavo brAhmaNA hetimanto yAm asyanti sharavyA3M na sA mR^iShA /
anuhAya tapasA manyunA chota durAd ava bhindanty enam //9//
ye sahasram arAjann Asan dashashatA uta /
te brAhmaNasya gAM jagdhvA vaitahavyAH parAbhavan //10//
gaur eva tAn hanyamAnA vaitahavyA.N avAtirat /
ye kesaraprAbandhAyAsh charamAjAm apechiran //11//
ekashataM tA janatA yA bhUmir vyadhUnuta /
prajAM hiMsitvA brAhmaNIm asaMbhavyaM parAbhavan //12//
devapIyush charati martyeShu garagIrNo bhavaty asthibhUyAn /
yo brAhmaNaM devabandhuM hinasti na sa pitR^iyANam apy eti lokam //13//
agnir vai naH padavAyaH somo dAyAda uchyate /
hantAbhishastendras tathA tad vedhaso viduH //14//
iShur iva digdhA nR^ipate pR^idAkUr iva gopate /
sA brAhmaNasyeShur ghorA tayA vidhyati pIyataH //15//
161

atimAtram avardhanta nod iva divam aspR^ishan /
bhR^iguM hiMsitvA sR^i~njayA vaitahavyAH parAbhavan //1//
ye bR^ihatsAmAnam A~Ngirasam Arpayan brAhmaNaM janAH /
petvas teShAm ubhayAdam avis tokAny Avayat //2//
ye brAhmaNaM pratyaShThIvan ye vAsmi~n chhulkam IShire /
asnas te madhye kulyAyAH keshAn khAdanta Asate //3//
brahmagavI pachyamAnA yAvat sAbhi vija~Ngahe /
tejo rAShTrasya nir hanti na vIro jAyate vR^iShA //4//
krUram asyA AshasanaM tR^iShTaM pishitam asyate /
kShIraM yad asyAH pIyate tad vai pitR^iShu kilbiSham //5//
ugro rAjA manyamAno brAhmaNaM yo jighatsati /
parA tat sichyate rAShTraM brAhmaNo yatra jIyate //6//
aShTApadI chaturakShI chatuHshrotrA chaturhanuH /
dvyAsyA dvijihvA bhUtvA sA rAShTram ava dhUnute brahmajyasya //7//
tad vai rAShTram A sravati nAvaM bhinnAm ivodakam /
brahmANaM yatra hiMsanti tad rAShTraM hanti duchhunA //8//
taM vR^ikShA apa sedhanti chhAyAM no mopa gA iti /
yo brAhmaNasya sad dhanam abhi nArada manyate //9//
viSham etad devakR^itaM rAjA varuNo 'bravIt /
na brAhmaNasya gAM jagdhvA rAstre jAgAra kash chana //10//
navaiva tA navatayo yA bhUmir vyadhUnuta /
prajAM hiMsitvA brAhmaNIm asaMbhavyaM parAbhavan //11//
yAm mR^itAyAnubadhnanti kUdyaM padayopanIm /
tad vai brahmajya te devA upastaraNam abruvan //12//
ashrUNi kR^ipamAnasya yAni jItasya vAvR^ituH /
taM vai brahmajya te devA apAM bhAgam adhArayan //13//
yena mR^itaM snapayanti shmashrUNi yenondate /
taM vai brahmajya te devA apAM bhAgam adhArayan //14//
na varShaM maitrAvaruNaM brahmajyam abhi varShati /
nAsmai samitiH kalpate na mitraM nayate vasham //15//
162

uchchairghoSho dundubhiH satvanAyan vAnaspatyaH saMbhR^ita usR^iiyAbhiH /
vAchaM kShuNuvAno damayant sapatnAnt siMha iva jeShyann abhi taMstanIhi //1//
siMha ivAstAnId druvayo vibaddho 'bhikrandann R^iShabho vAsitAm iva /
vR^iShA tvaM vadhrayas te sapatnA aindras te shuShmo abhimAtiShAhaH //2//
vR^iSheva yUthe sahasA vidAno gavyann abhi ruva saMdhanAjit /
shuchA vidhya hR^idayaM pareShAM hitvA grAmAn prachyutA yantu shatravaH //3//
saMjayan pR^itanA UrdhvamAyur gR^ihyA gR^ihNAno bahudhA vi chakShva /
daivIM vAchaM dundubha A gurasva vedhAH shatrUNAm upa bharasva vedaH //4//
dundubher vAchaM prayatAM vadantIm AshR^iNvatI nAthitA ghoShabuddhA /
nArI putraM dhAvatu hastagR^ihyAmitrI bhItA samare vadhAnAm //5//
pUrvo dundubhe pra vadAsi vAchaM bhUmyAH pR^iShThe vada rochamAnaH /
amitrasenAm abhija~njabhAno dyumad vada dundubhe sUnR^itAvat //6//
antareme nabhasI ghoSho astu pR^ithak te dhvanayo yantu shIbham /
abhi kranda stanayotpipAnaH shlokakR^in mitratUryAya svardhI //7//
dhIbhiH kR^itaH pra vadAti vAcham ud dharShaya satvanAm AyudhAni /
indramedI satvano ni hvayasva mitrair amitrAm? ava ja~NghanIhi //8//
saMkrandanaH pravado dhR^iShNuSheNaH pravedakR^id bahudhA grAmaghoShI /
shriyo vanvano vayunAni vidvAn kIrtim bahubhyo vi hara dvirAje //9//
shreyaHketo vasujit sahIyAnt saMgrAmajit saMshito brahmaNAsi /
aMshUn iva grAvAdhiShavaNe adrir gavyan dundubhe'dhi nR^itya vedaH //10//
shatrUShAN nIShAd abhimAtiShAho gaveShaNaH sahamAna udbhit /
vAgvIva mantraM pra bharasva vAcham sAMgrAmajityAyeSham ud vadeha //11//
achyutachyut samado gamiShTho mR^idho jetA puraetAyodhyaH /
indreNa gupto vidathA nichikyad dhR^iddyotano dviShatAM yAhi shIbham //12//
163

vihR^idayaM vaimanasyaM vadAmitreShu dundubhe /
vidveShaM kashmashaM bhayam amitreShu ni dadhmasy ava enAn dundubhe jahi //1//
udvepamAnA manasA chakShuShA hR^idayena cha /
dhAvantu bibhyato 'mitrAH pratrAsenAjye hute //2//
vAnaspatyaH saMbhR^ita usriyAbhir vishvagotryaH /
pratrAsam amitrebhyo vadAjyenAbhighAritaH //3//
yathA mR^igAH saMvijanta AraNyAH puruShAd adhi /
eva tvaM dundubhe 'mitrAn abhi kranda pra trAsayAtho chittAni mohaya //4//
yathA vR^ikAd ajAvayo dhAvanti bahu bibhyatIH /
eva tvaM dundubhe 'mitrAn abhi kranda pra trAsayAtho chittAni mohaya //5//
yathA shyenAt patatriNaH saMvijante ahardivi siMhasya stanathor yathA /
eva tvaM dundubhe 'mitrAn abhi kranda pra trAsayAtho chittAni mohaya //6//
parAmitrAn dundubhinA hariNasyAjinena cha /
sarve devA atitrasan ye saMgrAmasyeShate //7//
yair indraH prakrIDate padghoShaish chhAyayA saha /
tair amitrAs trasantu no 'mI ye yanty anIkashaH //8//
jyAghoShA dundubhayo 'bhi kroshantu yA dishaH /
senAH parAjitA yatIr amitrANAm anIkashaH //9//
Aditya chakShur A datsva marIchayo 'nu dhAvata /
patsa~NginIr A sajantu vigate bAhuvIrye //10//
yUyam ugrA marutaH pR^ishnimAtara indreNa yujA pra mR^inIta shatrUn /
somo rAjA varuNo rAjA mahAdeva uta mR^ityur indraH //11//
etA devasenAH sUryaketavaH sachetasaH /
amitrAn no jayantu svAhA //12//
164

agnis takmAnam apa bAdhatAm itaH somo grAvA varuNaH pUtadakShAH /
vedir barhiH samidhaH shoshuchAnA apa dveShAMsy amuyA bhavantu //1//
ayaM yo vishvAn haritAn kR^iNoShy uchchhochayann agnir ivAbhidunvan /
adhA hi takmann araso hi bhUyA adhA nya~N~N adharAn vA parehi //2//
yaH paruShaH pAruSheyo 'vadhvaMsa ivAruNaH /
takmAnaM vishvadhAvIryAdharA~nchaM parA suvA //3//
adharA~ncham pra hiNomi namaH kR^itvA takmane /
shakambharasya muShTihA punar etu mahAvR^iShAn //4//
oko asya mUjavanta oko asya mahAvR^iShAH /
yAvaj jAtas takmaMs tAvAn asi balhikeShu nyocharaH //5//
takman vyAla vi gada vya~Nga bhUri yAvaya /
dAsIM niShTakvarIm ichha tAm vajreNa sam arpaya //6//
takman mUjavato gachha balhikAn vA parastarAm /
shUdrAm ichha prapharvya1M tAM takman vIva dhUnuhi //7//
mahAvR^iShAn mUjavato bandhv addhi paretya /
praitAni takmane brUmo anyakShetrANi vA imA //8//
anyakShetre na ramase vashI san mR^iDayAsi naH /
abhUd u prArthas takmA sa gamiShyati balhikAn //9//
yat tvaM shIto 'tho rUraH saha kAsAvepayaH /
bhImAs te takman hetayas tAbhiH sma pari vR^i~Ngdhi naH //10//
mA smaitAnt sakhIn kuruthA balAsaM kAsam udyugam /
mA smAto'rvA~N aiH punas tat tvA takmann upa bruve //11//
takman bhrAtrA balAsena svasrA kAsikayA saha /
pApmA bhrAtR^ivyeNa saha gachhAmum araNaM janam //12//
tR^itIyakaM vitR^itIyaM sadandim uta shAradam /
takmAnaM shItaM rUraM graiShmaM nAshaya vArShikam //13//
gandhAribhyo mUjavadbhyo '~Ngebhyo magadhebhyaH /
praiShyan janam iva shevadhiM takmAnaM pari dadmasi //14//
165

ote me dyAvApR^ithivI otA devI sarasvatI /
otau ma indrash chAgnish cha krimiM jambhayatAm iti //1//
asyendra kumArasya krimIn dhanapate jahi /
hatA vishvA arAtaya ugreNa vachasA mama //2//
yo akShyaù parisarpati yo nAse parisarpati /
datAM yo madyaM gachhati taM krimiM jambhayAmasi //3//
sarUpau dvau virUpau dvau kR^iShNau dvau rohitau dvau /
babhrush cha babhrukarNash cha gR^idhraH kokash cha te hatAH //4//
ye krimayaH shitikakShA ye kR^iShNAH shitibAhavaH /
ye ke cha vishvarUpAs tAn krimIn jambhayAmasi //5//
ut purastAt sUrya eti vishvadR^iShTo adR^iShTahA /
dR^iShTAMsh cha ghnann adR^iShTAMsh cha sarvAMsh cha pramR^iNan krimIn //6//
yevAShAsaH kaShkaShAsa ejatkAH shipavitnukAH /
dR^iShTAMsh cha hanyatAM krimir utAdR^iShTash cha hanyatAm //7//
hato yevAShaH krimINAM hato nadanimota /
sarvAn ni maShmaShAkaraM dR^iShadA khalvAm? iva //8//
trishIrShANaM trikakudaM krimiM sAra~Ngam arjunam /
shR^iNAmy asya pR^iShTIr api vR^ishchAmi yach chhiraH //9//
atrivad vaH krimayo hanmi kaNvavaj jamadagnivat /
agastyasya brahmaNA saM pinaShmy ahaM krimIn //10//
hato rAjA krimINAm utaiShAM sthapatir hataH /
hato hatamAtA krimir hatabhrAtA hatasvasA //11//
hatAso asya veshaso hatAsaH pariveshasaH /
atho ye kShullakA iva sarve te krimayo hatAH //12//
sarveShAM cha krimINAM sarvAsAM cha krimInAm /
bhinadmy ashmanA shira dahAmy agninA mukham //13//
166

savitA prasavAnAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAm pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //1//
agnir vanaspatInAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //2//
dyAvApR^ithivI dAtR^INAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //3//
varuNo 'pAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //4//
mitrAvaruNau vR^iShTyAdhipatI tau mAvatAm /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //5//
marutaH parvatAnAm adhipatayas te mAvantu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //6//
somo vIrudhAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //7//
vAyur antarikShasyAdhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //8//
sUryash chakShuShAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //9//
chandramA nakShatrANAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //10//
indro divo 'dhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //11//
marutAM pitA pashUnAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAm purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //12//
mR^ityuH prajAnAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAm purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //13//
yamaH pitR^INAm adhipatiH sa mAvatu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //14//
pitaraH pare te mAvantu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //15//
tatA avare te mAvantu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratisThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //16//
tatas tatAmahAs te mAvantu /
asmin brahmaNy asmin karmaNy asyAM purodhAyAm asyAM pratiShThAyAm asyAM /
chittyAm asyAm AkUtyAm asyAm AshiShy asyAM devahUtyAM svAhA //17//
167

parvatAd divo yoner a~NgAda~NgAt samAbhR^itam /
shepo garbhasya retodhAH sarau parNam ivA dadhat //1//
yatheyaM pR^ithivI mahI bhUtAnAM garbham Adadhe /
evA dadhAmi te garbhaM tasmai tvAm avase huve //2//
garbhaM dhehi sinIvAli garbhaM dhehi sarasvati /
garbhaM te ashvinobhA dhattAM puShkarasrajA //3//
garbhaM te mitrAvaruNau garbham devo bR^ihaspatiH /
garbhaM ta indrash chAgnish cha garbhaM dhAtA dadhAtu te //4//
viShNur yoniM kalpayatu tvaShTA rUpANi piMshatu /
A si~nchatu prajApatir dhAtA garbhaM dadhAtu te //5//
yad veda rAjA varuNo yad vA devI sarasvatI /
yad indro vR^itrahA veda tad garbhakaraNaM piba //6//
garbho asy oShadhInAM garbho vanaspatInAm /
garbho vishvasya bhUtasya so agne garbham eha dhAH //7//
adhi skanda vIrayasva garbham A dhehi yonyAm /
vR^iShAsi vR^iShNyAvan prajAyai tvA nayAmasi //8//
vi jihIShva bArhatsAme garbhas te yonim A shayAm /
aduSh Te devAH putraM somapA ubhayAvinam //9//
dhAtaH shreShThena rUpeNAsyA nAryA gavInyoH /
pumAMsaM putram A dhehi dashame mAsi sUtave //10//
tvaShTaH shreShThena rUpeNAsyA nAryA gavInyoH /
pumAMsaM putram A dhehi dashame mAsi sUtave //11//
savitaH shreShThena rUpeNAsyA nAryA gavInyoH /
pumAMsaM putram A dhehi dashame mAsi sUtave //12//
prajApate shreShThena rUpeNAsyA nAryA gavInyoH /
pumAMsaM putram A dhehi dashame mAsi sUtave //13//
168

yajUMShi yaj~ne samidhaH svAhAgniH pravidvAn iha vo yunaktu //1//
yunaktu devaH savitA prajAnann asmin yaj~ne mahiShaH svAhA //2//
indra ukthAmadAny asmin yaj~ne pravidvAn yunaktu suyujaH svAhA //3//
praiShA yaj~ne nividaH svAhA shiShTAH patnIbhir vahateha yuktAH //4//
chhandAMsi yaj~ne marutaH svAhA mAteva putraM pipR^iteha yuktAH //5//
eyam agan barhiShA prokShaNIbhir yaj~naM tanvAnAditiH svAhA //6//
viShNur yunaktu bahudhA tapAMsy asmin yaj~ne suyujaH svAhA //7//
tvaShTA yunaktu bahudhA nu rUpA asmin yaj~ne yunaktu suyujaH svAhA //8//
bhago yunaktvAshiSho nv asmA asmin yaj~ne pravidvAn yunaktu suyujaH svAhA //9//
somo yunaktu bahudhA payAMsy asmin yaj~ne suyujaH svAhA //10//
indro yunaktu bahudhA payAMsy asmin yaj~ne suyujaH svAhA //11//
ashvinA brahmaNA yAtam arvA~nchau vaShaTkAreNa yaj~naM vardhayantau /
bR^ihaspate brahmaNA yAhy arvA~N yaj~no ayaM svar idaM yajamAnAya svAhA //12//
169

UrdhvA asya samidho bhavanty UrdhvA shukrA shochIShy agneH /
dyumattamA supratIkaH sasUnus tanUnapAd asuro bhUripANiH //1//
devo deveShu devaH patho anakti madhvA ghR^itena //2//
madhvA yaj~nam nakShati praiNAno narAshaMso agniH sukR^id devaH savitA vishvavAraH //3//
achhAyam eti shavasA ghR^itA chid IdAno vahnir namasA //4//
agniH srucho adhvareShu prayakShu sa yakShad asya mahimAnam agneH //5//
tarI mandrAsu prayakShu vasavash chAtiShThan vasudhAtarash cha //6//
dvAro devIr anv asya vishve vrataM rakShanti vishvahA //7//
uruvyachasAgner dhAmnA patyamAne /
A suShvayantI yajate upAke uShAsAnaktemaM yaj~nam avatAm adhvaram naH //8//
daivA hotAra Urdhvam adhvaraM no 'gner jihvayAbhi gR^inata gR^inatA naH svìShTaye /
tisro devIr barhir edaM sadantAm iDA sarasvatI mahI bhAratI gR^iNAnA //9//
tan nas turIpam adbhutaM purukShu /
deva tvaShTA rAyas poShaM vi Shya nAbhim asya //10//
vanaspate 'va sR^ijA rarANaH /
tmanA devebhyo agnir havyaM shamitA svadayatu //11//
agne svAhA kR^iNuhi jAtavedaH /
indrAya yaj~naM vishve devA havir idaM juShantAm //12//
170

nava prANAn navabhiH saM mimIte dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya /
harite trINi rajate trINy ayasi trINi tapasAviShThitAni //1//
agniH sUryash chandramA bhUmir Apo dyaur antarikShaM pradisho dishash cha /
ArtavA R^itubhiH saMvidAnA anena mA trivR^itA pArayantu //2//
trayaH poShas trivR^iti shrayantAm anaktu pUShA payasA ghR^itena /
annasya bhUmA puruShasya bhUmA bhUmA pashUnAM ta iha shrayantAm //3//
imam AdityA vasunA sam ukShatemam agne vardhaya vavR^idhAnaH /
imam indra saM sR^ija vIryeNAsmin trivR^ich chhrayatAM poShayiShNu //4//
bhUmiSh TvA pAtu haritena vishvabhR^id agniH pipartv ayasA sajoShAH /
vIrudbhiSh Te arjunaM saMvidAnaM dakShaM dadhAtu sumanasyamAnam //5//
tredhA jAtam janmanedaM hiraNyam agner ekaM priyatamaM babhUva somasyaikaM hiMsitasya parApatat /
apAm ekaM vedhasAM reta Ahus tat te hiraNyaM trivR^id astv AyuShe //6//
tryAyuShaM jamadagneH kashyapasya tryAyuSham /
tredhAmR^itasya chakShaNaM trINy AyUMShi te 'karam //7//
trayah suparNAs trivR^itA yad Ayann ekAkSharam abhisaMbhUya shakrAH /
praty auhan mR^ityum amR^itena sAkam antardadhAnA duritAni vishvA //8//
divas tvA pAtu haritaM madhyAt tvA pAtv arjunam /
bhUmyA ayasmayaM pAtu prAgAd devapurA ayam //9//
imAs tisro devapurAs tAs tvA rakShantu sarvataH /
tAs tvaM bibhrad varchasvy uttaro dviShatAM bhava //10//
puraM devAnAm amR^itaM hiraNyam ya Abedhe prathamo devo agre /
tasmai namo dasha prAchIH kR^iNomy anu manyatAM trivR^id Abadhe me //11//
A tvA chR^itatv aryamA pUShA bR^ihaspatiH /
aharjAtasya yan nAma tena tvAti chR^itAmasi //12//
R^itubhiSh TvArtavair AyuShe varchase tvA /
saMvatsarasya tejasA tena samhanu kR^iNmasi //13//
ghR^itAd ulluptam madhunA samaktaM bhUmidR^iMham achyutam pArayiShNu /
bhindat sapatnAn adharAMsh cha kR^iNvad A mA roha mahate saubhagAya //14//
171

purastAd yukto vaha jAtavedo 'gne viddhi kriyamANam yathedam /
tvaM bhiShag bheShajasyAsi kartA tvayA gAm ashvaM puruShaM sanema //1//
tathA tad agne kR^iNu jAtavedo vishvebhir devaiH saha saMvidAnaH /
yo no dideva yatamo jaghAsa yathA so asya paridhiSh patAti //2//
yathA so asya paridhiSh patAti tathA tad agne kR^iNu jAtavedaH /
vishvebhir devair saha saMvidAnaH //3//
akShyaù3 ni vidhya hR^idayaM ni vidhya jihvAM ni tR^inddhi pra dato mR^iNIhi /
pishAcho asya yatamo jaghAsAgne yaviShTha prati shR^iNIhi //4//
yad asya hR^itaM vihR^itaM yat parAbhR^itam Atmano jagdhaM yatamat pishAchaiH /
tad agne vidvAn punar A bhara tvaM sharIre mAMsam asum erayAmaH //5//
Ame supakve shabale vipakve yo mA pishAcho ashane dadambha /
tad AtmanA prajayA pishAchA vi yAtayantAm agado 'yam astu //6//
kShire mA manthe yatamo dadambhAkR^iShTapachye ashane dhAnye3 yaH /
tad AtmanA prajayA pishAchA vi yAtayantAm agado 'yam astu //7//
apAM mA pAne yatamo dadambha kravyAd yAtUnAm shayane shayAnam /
tad AtmanA prajayA pishAchA vi yAtayantAm agado 'yam astu //8//
divA mA naktaM yatamo dadambha kravyAd yAtUnAm shayane shayAnam /
tad AtmanA prajayA pishAchA vi yAtayantAm agado 'yam astu //9//
kravyAdam agne rudhiraM pishAchaM manohanaM jahi jAtavedaH /
tam indro vAjI vajreNa hantu chhinattu somaH shiro asya dhR^iShNuH //10//
sanAd agne mR^iNasi yAtudhAnAn na tvA rakShAMsi pR^itanAsu jigyuH /
sahamUrAn anu daha kravyAdo mA te hetyA mukShata daivyAyAH //11//
samAhara jAtavedo yad dhR^itaM yat parAbhR^itam /
gAtrANy asya vardhantAm aMshur ivA pyAyatAm ayam //12//
somasyeva jAtavedo aMshur A pyAyatAm ayam /
agne virapshinaM medhyam ayakShmaM kR^iNu jIvatu //13//
etAs te agne samidhaH pishAchajambhanIH /
tAs tvaM juShasva prati chainA gR^ihANa jAtavedaH //14//
tArShTAghIr agne samidhaH prati gR^ihNAhy archiShA /
jahAtu kravyAd rUpaM yo asya mAMsaM jihIrShati //15//
172

Avatas ta AvataH parAvatas ta AvataH /
ihaiva bhava mA nu gA mA pUrvAn anu gAH pitR^In asuM badhnAmi te dR^iDham //1//
yat tvAbhicheruH puruShaH svo yad araNo janaH /
unmochanapramochane ubhe vAchA vadAmi te //2//
yad dudrohitha shepiShe striyai puMse achittyA /
unmochanapramochane ubhe vAchA vadAmi te //3//
yat enaso mAtR^ikR^itAch chheShe pitR^ikR^itAch cha yat /
unmochanapramochane ubhe vAchA vadAmi te //4//
yat te mAtA yat te pitA jamir bhrAtA cha sarjataH /
pratyak sevasva bheShajaM jaradaShTiM kR^iNomi tvA //5//
ihaidhi puruSha sarveNa manasA saha /
dUtau yamasya mAnu gA adhi jIvapurA ihi //6//
anuhUtaH punar ehi vidvAn udayanaM pathaH /
ArohaNam AkramaNaM jIvatojIvato 'yanam //7//
mA bibher na mariShyasi jaradaShTiM kR^iNomi tvA /
nir avocham ahaM yakShmam a~Ngebhyo a~NgajvaraM tava //8//
a~Ngabhedo a~Ngajvaro yash cha te hR^idayAmayaH /
yakShmaH shyena iva prApaptad vachA sADhaH parastarAm //9//
R^iShI bodhapratIbodhAv asvapno yash cha jAgR^iviH /
tau te prANasya goptArau divA naktaM cha jAgR^itAm //10//
ayam agnir upasadya iha sUrya ud etu te /
udehi mR^ityor gambhIrAt kR^iShNAch chit tamasas pari //11//
namo yamAya namo astu mR^ityave namaH pitR^ibhya uta ye nayanti /
utpAraNasya yo veda tam agniM puro dadhe 'smA ariShTatAtaye //12//
aitu prANa aitu mana aitu chakShur atho balam /
sharIram asya sam vidAM tat padbhyAM prati tiShThatu //13//
prANenAgne chakShuShA saM sR^ijemaM sam Iraya tanvA3 saM balena /
vetthAmR^itasya mA nu gAn mA nu bhUmigR^iho bhuvat //14//
mA te prANa upa dasan mo apAno 'pi dhAyi te /
sUryas tvAdhipatir mR^ityor udAyachhatu rashmibhiH //15//
ivam antar vadati jihvA baddhA paniShpadA /
tvayA yakShmam nir avochaM shataM ropIsh cha takmanaH //16//
ayaM lokaH priyatamo devAnAm aparAjitaH /
yasmai tvam iha mR^ityave diShTaH puruSha jaj~niShe /
sa cha tvAnu hvayAmasi mA purA jaraso mR^ithAH //17//
173

yAM te chakrur Ame pAtre yAM chakrur mishradhAnye /
Ame mAMse kR^ityAM yAM chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //1//
yAM te chakruH kR^ikavAkAv aje vA yAM kurIriNi /
avyAM te kR^ityAM yAm chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //2//
yAM te chakrur ekashaphe pashUnAm ubhayAdati /
gardabhe kR^ityAM yAM chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //3//
yAM te chakrur amUlAyAM valagaM vA narAchyAm /
kShetre te kR^ityAM yAM chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //4//
yAM te chakrur gArhapatye pUrvAgnAv uta dushchitah /
shAlAyAM kR^ityAM yAm chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //5//
yAM te chakruH sabhAyAM yAm chakrur adhidevane /
akSheShu kR^ityAM yAM chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //6//
yAM te chakruH senAyAM yAM chakrur iShvAyudhe /
dundubhau kR^ityAM yAM chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //7//
yAM te kR^ityAm kUpe 'vadadhuH shmashAne vA nichakhnuH /
sadmani kR^ityAm yAM chakruH punaH prati harAmi tAm //8//
yAM te chakruH puruShAsthe agnau saMkasuke cha yAm /
mrokaM nirdAhaM kravyAdaM punaH prati harAmi tAm //9//
apathenA jabhArainAM tAM pathetaH pra hiNmasi /
adhIro maryAdhIrebhyaH saM jabhArAchittyA //10//
yash chakAra na shashAka kartuM shashre pAdam a~Ngurim /
chakAra bhadram asmabhyam abhago bhagavadbhyaH //11//
kR^ityAkR^itam valaginaM mUlinaM shapatheyyam /
indras taM hantu mahatA vadhenAgnir vidhyatv astayA //12//
174
Book:
6

doSho gAya bR^ihad gAya dyumad dhehi /
AtharvaNa stuhi devaM savitAram //1//
tam u ShTuhi yo antaH sindhau sUnuH /
satyasya yuvAnam adroghavAchaM sushevam //2//
sa ghA no devaH savitA sAviShad amR^itAni bhUri /
ubhe suShTutI sugAtave //3//
175

indrAya somam R^itvijaH sunotA cha dhAvata /
stotur yo vachaH shR^iNavad dhavaM cha me //1//
A yaM vishantIndavo vayo na vR^ikSham andhasaH /
virapshin vi mR^idho jahi rakShasvinIH //2//
sunotA somapAvne somam indrAya vajriNe /
yuvA jeteshAnah sa puruShTutaH //3//
176

pAtaM na indrApUShaNAditiH pAntu marutaH /
apAM napAt sindhavaH sapta pAtana pAtu no viShNur uta dyauH //1//
pAtAM no dyAvApR^ithivI abhiShTaye pAtu grAvA pAtu somo no aMhasaH /
pAtu no devI subhagA sarasvatI pAtv agniH shivA ye asya pAyavaH //2//
pAtAm no devAshvinA shubhas patI uShAsAnaktota na uruShyatAm /
apAM napAd abhihrutI gayasya chid deva tvaShTar vardhaya sarvatAtaye //3//
177

tvaShTA me daivyaM vachaH parjanyo brahmaNas patiH /
putrair bhrAtR^ibhir aditir nu pAtu no duShTaraM trAyamANaM sahaH //1//
aMsho bhago varuNo mitro aryamAditiH pAntu marutaH /
apa tasya dveSho gamed abhihruto yAvayach chhatrum antitam //2//
dhiye sam ashvinA prAvataM na uruShyA Na urujmann aprayuchhan /
dyaù3Sh pitar yAvaya duchhunA yA //3//
178

ud enam uttaraM nayAgne ghR^itenAhuta /
sam enaM varchasA sR^ija prajayA cha bahuM kR^idhi //1//
indremaM prataraM kR^idhi sajAtAnAm asad vashI /
rAyas poSheNa saM sR^ija jIvAtave jarase naya //2//
yasya kR^iNmo havir gR^ihe tam agne vardhayA tvam /
tasmai somo adhi bravad ayaM cha brahmaNas patiH //3//
179

yo 'smAn brahmaNas pate 'devo abhimanyate /
sarvaM tam randhayAsi me yajamAnAya sunvate //1//
yo naH soma sushaMsino duHshaMsa Adideshati /
vajreNAsya mukhe jahi sa saMpiShTo apAyati //2//
yo naH somAbhidAsati sanAbhir yash cha niShTyaH /
apa tasya balaM tira mahIva dyaur vadhatmanA //3//
180

yena somAditiH pathA mitrA vA yanty adruhaH /
tenA no 'vasA gahi //1//
yena soma sAhantyAsurAn randhayAsi naH /
tenA no adhi vochata //2//
yena devA asurANAm ojAMsy avR^iNIdhvam /
tenA naH sharma yachhata //3//
181

yathA vR^ikShaM libujA samantaM pariShasvaje /
evA pari Shvajasva mAM yathA mAM kAminy aso yathA man nApagA asaH //1//
yathA suparNaH prapatan pakShau nihanti bhUmyAm /
evA ni hanmi te mano yathA mAM kAminy aso yathA man nApagA asaH //2//
yatheme dyAvApR^ithivI sadyaH paryeti sUryaH /
evA pary emi te mano yathA mAm kAminy aso yathA man nApagA asaH //3//
182

vA~nchha me tanva1M pAdau vA~nchhAkShyaù3 vA~nchha sakthyaù /
akShyaù vR^iShaNyantyAH keshA mAM te kAmena shuShyantu //1//
mama tvA doShaNishriShaM kR^iNomi hR^idayashriSham /
yathA mama kratAv aso mama chittam upAyasi //2//
yAsAM nAbhir ArehaNaM hR^idi saMvananaM kR^itam gAvo ghR^itasya mAtaro 'mUM saM vAnayantu me //3//
183

pR^ithivyai shrotrAya vanaspatibhyo 'gnaye 'dhipataye svAhA //1//
prANAyAntarikShAya vayobhyo vAyave 'dhipataye svAhA //2//
dive chakShuShe nakShatrebhyaH sUryAyAdhipataye svAhA //3//
184

samIm ashvattha ArUDhas tatra puMsuvanaM kR^itam /
tad vai putrasya vedanaM tat strIShv A bharAmasi //1//
puMsi vai reto bhavati tat striyAm anu Shichyate /
tad vai putrasya vedanaM tat prajApatir abravIt //2//
prajApatir anumatiH sinIvAly achIk?pat /
straiShUyam anyatra dadhat pumAMsam u dadhat iha //3//
185

pari dyAm iva sUryo 'hInAM janimAgamam /
rAtrI jagad ivAnyad dhaMsAt tenA te vAraye viSham //1//
yad brahmabhir yad R^iShibhir yad devair viditaM purA /
yad bhUtaM bhavyam Asanvat tenA te vAraye viSham //2//
madhvA pR^i~nche nadya1H parvatA girayo madhu /
madhu paruShNI shIpAlA sham Asne astu shaM hR^ide //3//
186

namo devavadhebhyo namo rAjavadhebhyaH /
atho ye vishyAnAM vadhAs tebhyo mR^ityo namo 'stu te //1//
namas te adhivAkAya parAvAkAya te namaH /
sumatyai mR^ityo te namo durmatyai te idaM namaH //2//
namas te yAtudhAnebhyo namas te bheShajebhyaH /
namas te mR^ityo mUlebhyo brAhmaNebhya idaM namaH //3//
187

asthisraMsaM parusraMsam Asthitam hR^idayAmayam /
balAsaM sarvaM nAshayA~NgeShThA yash cha parvasu //1//
nir balAsaM balAsinaH kShiNomi muShkaraM yathA /
chhinadmy asya bandhanaM mUlam urvArvA iva //2//
nir balAsetaH pra patAshu~NgaH shishuko yathA /
atho ita iva hAyano 'pa drAhy avIrahA //3//
188

uttamo asy oShadhInAM tava vR^ikShA upastayaH /
upastir astu so 'smAkaM yo asmA.N abhidAsati //1//
sabandhush chAsabandhush cha yo asmA.N abhidAsati /
teShAM sA vR^ikShANAm ivAhaM bhUyAsam uttamaH //2//
yathA soma oShadhInAm uttamo haviShAM kR^itaH /
talAshA vR^ikShAnAm ivAhaM bhUyAsam uttamaH //3//
189

Abayo anAbayo rasas ta ugra Abayo /
A te karambham admasi //1//
vihahlo nAma te pitA madAvatI nAma te mAtA /
sa hina tvam asi yas tvam AtmAnam AvayaH //2//
tauvilike 'velayAvAyam ailaba ailayIt /
babhrush cha babhrukarNash chApehi nir Ala //3//
alasAlAsi pUrva silA~njAlAsy uttarA /
nIlAgalasAla //4//
190

yatheyam pR^ithivI mahI bhUtAnAM garbham Adadhe /
evA te dhriyatAM garbho anu sUtuM savitave //1//
yatheyaM pR^ithivI mahI dAdhAremAn vanaspatIn /
evA te dhriyatAM garbho anu sUtuM savitave //2//
yatheyaM pR^ithivI mahI dAdhAra parvatAn girIn /
evA te dhriyatAM garbho anu sUtuM savitave //3//
yatheyaM pR^ithivI mahI dAdhAra viShThitaM jagat /
evA te dhriyatAM garbho anu sUtuM savitave //4//
191

IrShyAyA dhrAjiM prathamAM prathamasyA utAparAm /
agniM hR^idayya1M shokaM taM te nir vApayAmasi //1//
yathA bhUmir mR^itamanA mR^itAn mR^itamanastarA /
yathota mamruSho mana everShyor mR^itaM manaH //2//
ado yat te hR^idi shritaM manaskaM patayiShNukam /
tatas ta IrShyAM mu~nchAmi nir UShmANaM dR^iter iva //3//
192

punantu mA devajanAH punantu manavo dhiyA /
punantu vishvA bhUtAni pavamAnaH punAtu mA //1//
pavamAnaH punAtu mA kratve dakShAya jIvase /
atho ariShTatAtaye //2//
ubhAbhyAM deva savitaH pavitreNa savena cha /
asmAn punIhi chakShase //3//
193

agner ivAsya dahata eti shuShmiNa uteva matto vilapann apAyati /
anyam asmad ichhatu kaM chid avratas tapurvadhAya namo astu takmane //1//
namo rudrAya namo astu takmane namo rAj~ne varuNAya tviShImate /
namo dive namaH pR^ithivyai nama oShadhIbhyaH //2//
ayaM yo abhishochayiShNur vishvA rUpANi haritA kR^iNoShi /
tasmai te 'ruNAya babhrave namaH kR^iNomi vanyAya takmane //3//
194

imA yAs tisraH pR^ithivIs tAsAM ha bhUmir uttamA /
tAsAm adhi tvacho ahaM bheShajaM sam u jagrabham //1//
shreShTham asi bheShajAnAM vasiShThaM vIrudhAnAm /
somo bhaga iva yAmeShu deveShu varuNo yathA //2//
revatIr anAdhR^iShaH siShAsavaH siShAsatha /
uta stha keshadR^imhaNIr atho ha keshavardhanIH //3//
195

kR^iShNaM niyAnaM harayaH suparNA apo vasAnA divam ut patanti /
ta AvavR^itrant sadanAd R^itasyAd id ghR^itena pR^ithivIM vy UduH //1//
payasvatIH kR^iNuthApa oShadhIH shivA yad ejathA maruto rukmavakShasaH /
UrjaM cha tatra sumatiM cha pinvata yatrA naro marutaH si~nchathA madhu //2//
udapruto marutas tA.N iyarta vR^iShTir yA vishvA nivatas pR^iNAti /
ejAti glahA kanyeva tunnairuM tundAnA patyeva jAyA //3//
196

sasruShIs tad apaso divA naktaM cha sasruShIH /
vareNyakratur aham apo devIr upa hvaye //1//
otA ApaH karmaNyA mu~nchantv itaH praNItaye /
sadyaH kR^iNvantv etave //2//
devasya savituH save karma kR^iNvantu mAnuShAH /
shaM no bhavantv apa oShadhIH shivAH //3//
197

himavataH pra sravanti sindhau samaha sa~NgamaH /
Apo ha mahyaM tad devIr dadan hR^iddyotabheShajam //1//
yan me akShyor Adidyota pArShNyoH prapadosh cha yat /
Apas tat sarvaM niSh karan bhiShajAM subhiShaktamAH //2//
sindhupatnIH sindhurAj~nIH sarvA yA nadya1 sthana /
datta nas tasya bheShajaM tenA vo bhunajAmahai //3//
198

pa~ncha cha yAH pa~nchAshach cha saMyanti manyA abhi /
itas tAH sarvA nashyantu vAkA apachitAm iva //1//
sapta cha yAH saptatish cha saMyanti graivyA abhi /
itas tAH sarvA nashyantu vAkA apachitAm iva //2//
nava cha yA navatish cha saMyanti skandhyA abhi /
itas tAH sarvA nashyantu vAkA apachitAm iva //3//
199

ava mA pApman sR^ija vashI san mR^idayAsi naH /
A mA bhadrasya loke pApman dhehy avihrutam //1//
yo naH pApman na jahAsi tam u tvA jahimo vayam /
pathAm anu vyAvartane 'nyaM pApmAnu padyatAm //2//
anyatrAsman ny ùchyatu sahasrAkSho amartyaH /
yaM dveShAma tam R^ichhatu yam u dviShmas tam ij jahi //3//
200

devAH kapota iShito yad ichhan dUto nirR^ityA idam AjagAma /
tasmA archAma kR^iNavAma niShkR^itiM shaM no astu dvipade shaM chatuShpade //1//
shivaH kapota iShito no astv anAgA devAH shakuno gR^ihaM naH /
agnir hi vipro juShatAm havir naH pari hetiH pakShiNI no vR^iNaktu //2//
hetiH pakShiNI na dabhAty asmAn AShTrI padaM kR^iNute agnidhAne /
shivo gobhya uta puruShebhyo no astu mA no devA iha hiMsIt kapota //3//
201

R^ichA kapotaM nudata praNodam iShaM madantaH pari gAM nayAmaH /
saMlobhayanto duritA padAni hitvA na UrjaM pra padAt pathiShThaH //1//
parIme 'gnim arShata parIme gAm aneShata /
deveShv akrata shravaH ka imA.N A dadharShati //2//
yaH prathamaH pravatam AsasAda bahubhyaH panthAm anupaspashAnaH /
yo 'syeshe dvipado yash chatuShpadas tasmai yamAya namo astu mR^ityave //3//
202

amUn hetiH patatriNI ny etu yad ulUko vadati mogham etat /
yad vA kapota padam agnau kR^iNoti //1//
yau te dUtau nirR^ita idam eto 'prahitau prahitau vA gR^ihaM naH /
kapotolUkAbhyAm apadaM tad astu //2//
avairahatyAyedam A papatyAt suvIratAyA idam A sasadyAt /
parA~N eva parA vada parAchIm anu saMvatam /
yathA yamasya tvA gR^ihe 'rasaM pratichAkashAn AbhUkaM pratichAkashAn //3//
203

devA imaM madhunA saMyutaM yavaM sarasvatyAm adhi maNAv acharkR^iShuH /
indra AsIt sIrapatiH shatakratuH kInAshA Asan marutaH sudAnavaH //1//
yas te mado 'vakesho vikesho yenAbhihasyaM puruShaM kR^iNoShi /
ArAt tvad anyA vanAni vR^ikShi tvaM shami shatavalshA vi roha //2//
bR^ihatpalAshe subhage varShavR^iddha R^itAvari /
mAteva putrebhyo mR^iDa keshebhyaH shami //3//
204

AyaM gauH pR^ishnir akramId asadan mAtaram puraH /
pitaram cha prayant svaH //1//
antash charati rochanA asya prANAd apAnataH /
vy akhyan mahiShaH svaH //2//
triMshad dhAmA vi rAjati vAk pata~Ngo ashishriyat /
prati vastor ahar dyubhiH //3//
205

antardAve juhuta sv etad yAtudhAnakShayaNaM ghR^itena /
ArAd rakShAMsi prati daha tvam agne na no gR^ihANAm upa tItapAsi //1//
rudro vo grIvA asharait pishAchAh pR^iShTIr vo 'pi shR^iNAtu yAtudhAnAH /
vIrud vo vishvatovIryA yamena sam ajIgamat //2//
abhayaM mitrAvaruNAv ihAstu no 'rchiShAttriNo nudataM pratIchaH /
mA j~nAtAraM mA pratiShThAM vidanta mitho vighnAnA upa yantu mR^ityum //3//
206

yasyedam A rajo yujas tuje janA navaM svaH /
indrasya rantyaM bR^ihat //1//
nAdhR^iSha A dadhR^iShate dhR^iShANo dhR^iShitaH shavaH /
purA yathA vyathiH shrava indrasya nAdhR^iShe shavaH //2//
sa no dadAtu tAM rayim uruM pisha~NgasaMdR^isham /
indraH patis tuviShTamo janeShv A //3//
207

prAgnaye vAcham Iraya vR^iShabhAya kShitInAm /
sa naH parShad ati dviShaH //1//
yo rakShAMsi nijUrvaty agnis tigmena shochiShA /
sa naH parShad ati dviShaH //2//
yaH parasyAH parAvatas tiro dhanvAtirochate /
sa naH parShad ati dviShaH //3//
yo vishvAbhi vipashyati bhuvanA saM cha pashyati /
sa naH parShad ati dviShaH //4//
yo asya pAre rajasaH shukro agnir ajAyata /
sa naH parShad ati dviShaH //5//
208

vaishvAnaro na Utaya A pra yAtu parAvataH /
agnir naH suShTutIr upa //1//
vaishvAnaro na Agamad imaM yaj~naM sajUr upa /
agnir uktheShv aMhasu //2//
vaishvAnaro '~NgirasAM stomam ukthaM cha chAk?pat /
aiShu dyumnaM svar yamat //3//
209

R^itAvAnaM vaishvAnaram R^itasya jyotiShas patim /
ajasraM gharmam Imahe //1//
sa vishvA prati chAk?pa R^itUMr ut sR^ijate vashI /
yaj~nasya vaya uttiran //2//
agniH pareShu dhAmasu kAmo bhUtasya bhavyasya /
samrAd eko vi rAjati //3//
210

upa prAgAt sahasrAkSho yuktvA shapatho ratham /
shaptAram anvichhan mama vR^ika ivAvimato gR^iham //1//
pari No vR^i~Ngdhi shapatha hradam agnir ivA dahan /
shaptAram atra no jahi divo vR^ikSham ivAshaniH //2//
yo naH shapAd ashapataH shapato yash cha naH shapAt /
shune peShTram ivAvakShAmaM taM praty asyAmi mR^ityave //3//
211

simhe vyAghra uta yA pR^idAkau tviShir agnau brAhmaNe sUrye yA /
indraM yA devI subhagA jajAna sA na aitu varchasA saMvidAnA //1//
yA hastini dvIpini yA hiraNye tviShir apsu goShu yA puruSheShu /
indraM yA devI subhagA jajAna sA na aitu varchasA saMvidAnA //2//
rathe akSheShv R^iShabhasya vAje vAte parjanye varuNasya shuShme /
indram yA devI subhagA jajAna sA na aitu varchasA saMvidAnA //3//
rAjanye dundubhAv AyatAyAm ashvasya vAje puruShasya mAyau /
indram yA devI subhagA jajAna sA na aitu varchasA samvidAnA //4//
212

yasho havir vardhatAm indrajUtaM sahasravIryaM subhR^itaM sahaskR^itam /
prasarsrANam anu dIrghAya chakShase haviShmantaM mA vardhaya jyeShThatAtaye //1//
achhA na indraM yashasaM yashobhir yashasvinaM namasAnA vidhema /
sa no rAsva rAShTram indrajUtaM tasya te rAtau yashasaH syAma //2//
yashA indro yashA agnir yashAH somo ajAyata /
yashA vishvasya bhUtasya aham asmi yashastamaH //3//
213

abhayaM dyAvApR^ithivI ihAstu no 'bhayaM somaH savitA naH kR^iNotu /
abhayaM no 'stUrv antarikShaM saptaR^iShINAM cha haviShAbhayaM no astu //1//
asmai grAmAya pradishash chatasra UrjaM subhUtaM svasti savitA naH kR^iNotu /
ashatrv indro abhayaM naH kR^iNotv anyatra rAj~nAm abhi yAtu manyuH //2//
anamitraM no adharAd anamitraM na uttarAt /
indrAnamitraM naH pashchAd anamitraM puras kR^idhi //3//
214

manase chetase dhiya AkUtaya uta chittaye /
matyai shrutAya chakShase vidhema haviShA vayam //1//
apAnAya vyAnAya prANAya bhUridhAyase /
sarasvatyA uruvyache vidhema haviShA vayam //2//
mA no hAsiShur R^iShayo daivyA ye tanUpA ye nas tanvas tanUjAH /
amartyA martyAM abhi naH sachadhvam Ayur dhatta prataraM jIvase naH //3//
215

ava jyAm iva dhanvano manyuM tanomi te hR^idaH /
yathA saMmanasau bhUtvA sakhAyAv iva sachAvahai //1//
sakhAyAv iva sachAvahA ava manyuM tanomi te /
adhas te ashmano manyum upAsyAmasi yo guruH //2//
abhi tiShThAmi te manyuM pArShNyA prapadena cha /
yathAvasho na vAdiSho mama chittam upAyasi //3//
216

ayaM darbho vimanyukaH svAya chAraNAya cha /
manyor vimanyukasyAyaM manyushamana uchyate //1//
ayaM yo bhUrimUlaH samudram avatiShThati /
darbhaH pR^ithivyA utthito manyushamana uchyate //2//
vi te hanavyAM sharaNiM vi te mukhyAM nayAmasi /
yathAvasho na vAdiSho mama chittam upAyasi //3//
217

asthAd dyaur asthAt pR^ithivy asthAd vishvam idaM jagat /
asthur vR^ikShA UrdhvasvapnAs tiShThAd rogo ayaM tava //1//
shataM yA bheShajAni te sahasraM saMgatAni cha /
shreShTham AsrAvabheShajaM vasiShThaM roganAshanam //2//
rudrasya mUtram asy amR^itasya nAbhiH /
viShANakA nAma vA asi pitR^INAM mUlAd utthitA vAtIkR^itanAshanI //3//
218

paro 'pehi manaspApa kim ashastAni shaMsasi /
parehi na tvA kAmaye vR^ikShAM vanAni saM chara gR^iheShu goShu me manaH //1//
avashasA niHshasA yat parAshasopArima jAgrato yat svapantaH /
agnir vishvAny apa duShkR^itAny ajuShTAny Are asmad dadhAtu //2//
yad indra brahmaNas pate 'pi mR^iShA charAmasi /
prachetA na A~Ngiraso duritAt pAtv aMhasaH //3//
219

yo na jIvo 'si na mR^ito devAnAm amR^itagarbho 'si svapna /
varuNAnI te mAtA yamaH pitArarur nAmAsi //1//
vidma te svapna janitraM devajAmInAM putro 'si yamasya karaNaH /
antako 'si mR^ityur asi taM tvA svapna tathA saM vidma sa naH svapna duShvapnyAt pAhi //2//
yathA kalAM yathA shaphaM yatharNaM saMnayanti /
evA duShvapnyaM sarvaM dviShate saM nayAmasi //3//
220

agniH prAtaHsavane pAtv asmAn vaishvAnaro vishvakR^id vishvashaMbhUH /
sa naH pAvako draviNe dadhAtv AyuShmantaH sahabhakShAH syAma //1//
vishve devA maruta indro asmAn asmin dvitIye savane na jahyuH /
AyuShmantaH priyam eShAM vadanto vayaM devAnAM sumatau syAma //2//
idaM tR^itIyaM savanaM kavInAm R^itena ye chamasam airayanta /
te saudhanvanAH svar AnashAnAH svìShTiM no abhi vasyo nayantu //3//
221

shyeno 'si gAyatrachhandA anu tvA rabhe /
svasti mA saM vahAsya yaj~nasyodR^ichi svAhA //1//
R^ibhur asi jagachhandA anu tvA rabhe /
svasti mA saM vahAsya yaj~nasyodR^ichi svAhA //2//
vR^iShAsi triShTupchhandA anu tvA rabhe /
svasti mA saM vahAsya yaj~nasyodR^ichi svAhA //3//
222

nahi te agne tanvaH krUram AnaMsha martyaH /
kapir babhasti tejanaM svaM jarAyu gaur iva //1//
meSha iva vai saM cha vi chorv achyase yad uttaradrAv uparash cha khAdataH /
shIrShNA shiro 'psasApso ardayann aMshUn babhasti haritebhir AsabhiH //2//
suparNA vAcham akratopa dyavy Akhare kR^iShNA iShirA anartiShuH /
ni yan niyanti uparasya niShkR^itiM purU reto dadhire sUryashritaH //3//
223

hataM tardaM sama~Nkam Akhum ashvinA chhintaM shiro api pR^iShTIH shR^iNItam /
yavAn ned adAn api nahyataM mukham athAbhayaM kR^iNutaM dhAnyAya //1//
tarda hai pata~Nga hai jabhya hA upakvasa /
brahmevAsaMsthitaM havir anadanta imAn yavAn ahiMsanto apodita //2//
tardApate vaghApate tR^iShTajambhA A shR^iNota me /
ya AraNyA vyadvarA ye ke cha stha vyadvarAs tAnt sarvAn jambhayAmasi //3//
224

vAyoH pUtaH pavitreNa pratya~N somo ati drutaH /
indrasya yujaH sakhA //1//
Apo asmAn mAtaraH sUdayantu ghR^itena no ghR^itapvah punantu /
vishvaM hi ripraM pravahanti devIr ud id AbhyaH shuchir A pUta emi //2//
yat kiM chedaM varuNa daivye jane 'bhidrohaM manuShyA3sh charanti /
achittyA chet tava dharma yuyopima mA nas tasmAd enaso deva rIriShaH //3//
225

ut sUryo diva eti puro raksAMsi nijUrvan /
AdityaH parvatebhyo vishvadR^iShTo adR^iShTahA //1//
ni gAvo goShThe asadan ni mR^igAso avikShata /
ny U3rmayo nadInaM ny adR^iShTA alipsata //2//
AyurdadaM vipashchitaM shrutAM kaNvasya vIrudham /
AbhAriShaM vishvabheShajIm asyAdR^iShTAn ni shamayat //3//
226

dyaush cha ma idaM pR^ithivI cha prachetasau shukro bR^ihan dakShiNayA pipartu /
anu svadhA chikitAM somo agnir vAyur naH pAtu savitA bhagash cha //1//
punaH prANaH punar AtmA na aitu punash chakShuH punar asur na aitu /
vaishvAnaro no adabdhas tanUpA antas tiShThAti duritAni vishvA //2//
saM varchasA payasA saM tanUbhir aganmahi manasA saM shivena /
tvaShTA no atra varIyaH kR^iNotv anu no mArShTu tanvo3 yad viriShTam //3//
227

idaM tad yuja uttaram indraM shumbhAmy aShTaye /
asya kShatraM shriyaM mahIM vR^iShTir iva vardhayA tR^iNam //1//
asmai kShatram agnIShomAv asmai dhArayataM rayim /
imaM rAShTrasyAbhIvarge kR^iNutam yuja uttaram //2//
sabandhush chAsabandhush cha yo asmA.N abhidAsati /
sarvaM taM randhayAsi me yajamAnAya sunvate //3//
228

ye panthAno bahavo devayAnA antarA dyAvApR^ithivI saMcharanti /
teShAm ajyAniM yatamo vahAti tasmai mA devAH pari datteha sarve //1//
grIShmo hemantaH shishiro vasantaH sharad varShAH svite no dadhAta /
A no goShu bhajatA prajAyAM nivAta id vaH sharaNe syAma //2//
idAvatsarAya parivatsarAya saMvatsarAya kR^iNutA bR^ihan namaH /
teShAM vayaM sumatau yaj~niyAnAm api bhadre sau manase syAma //3//
229

mA no devA ahir vadhIt satokAnt sahapuruShAn /
samyataM na vi Shparad vyAttaM na saM yaman namo devajanebhyaH /
namo 'stv asitAya namas tirashchirAjaye /
svajAya babhrave namo namo devajanebhyaH //2//
saM te hanmi datA dataH sam u te hanvA hanU /
saM te jihvayA jihvAM sam v AsnAha Asyam //3//
230

idam id vA u bheShajam idaM rudrasya bheShajam /
yeneShum ekatejanAM shatashalyAm apabravat //1//
jAlASheNAbhi Shi~nchata jAlASheNopa si~nchata /
jAlASham ugraM bheShajaM tena no mR^iDa jIvase //2//
shaM cha no mayash cha no mA cha naH kiM chanAmamat /
kShamA rapo vishvaM no astu bheShajaM sarvaM no astu bheShajam //3//
231

yashasaM mendro maghavAn kR^iNotu yashasaM dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe ime /
yashasaM mA devaH savitA kR^iNotu priyo dAtur dakShiNAyA iha syAm //1//
yathendro dyAvApR^ithivyor yashasvAn yathApa oShadhIShu yashasvatIH /
evA vishveShu deveShu vayaM sarveShu yashasaH syAma //2//
yashA indro yashA agnir yashAH somo ajAyata /
yashA vishvasya bhUtasyAham asmi yashastamaH //3//
232

anaDudbhyas tvaM prathamaM dhenubhyas tvam arundhati /
adhenave vayase sharma yachha chatuShpade //1//
sharma yachhatv oShadhiH saha devIr arundhatI /
karat payasvantaM goShTham ayakShmA.N uta pUruShAn //2//
vishvarUpAM subhagAm achhAvadAmi jIvalAm /
sA no rudrasyAstAM hetiM dUraM nayatu gobhyaH //3//
233

ayam A yAty aryamA purastAd viShitastupaH /
asyA ichhann agruvai patim uta jAyAm ajAnaye //1//
ashramad iyam aryamann anyAsAM samanaM yatI /
a~Ngo nv aryamann asyA anyAH samanam Ayati //2//
dhAtA dAdhAra pR^ithivIm dhAtA dyAm uta sUryam /
dhAtAsyA agruvai patim dadhAtu pratikAmyam //3//
234

mahyam Apo madhumad erayantAM mahyaM sUro abharaj jyotiShe kam /
mahyaM devA uta vishve tapojA mahyaM devaH savitA vyacho dhAt //1//
ahaM vivecha pR^ithivIm uta dyAm aham R^itUMr ajanayaM sapta sAkam /
ahaM satyam anR^itaM yad vadAmy ahaM daivIM pari vAcham vishash cha //2//
ahaM jajAna pR^ithivIm uta dyAm aham R^itUMr ajanayaM sapta sindhUn /
ahaM satvam anR^itaM yad vadAmi yo agnIShomAv ajuShe sakhAyA //3//
235

vaishvAnaro rashmibhir naH punAtu vAtaH prANeneShiro nabhobhiH /
dyAvApR^ithivI payasA payasvatI R^itAvarI yaj~niye na punItAm //1//
vaishvAnarIM sUnR^itAm A rabhadhvaM yasyA AshAs tanvo vItapR^iShThAH /
tayA gR^iNantaH sadhamAdeShu vayaM syAma patayo rayInAm //2//
vaishvAnarIM varchasa A rabhadhvaM shuddhA bhavantaH shuchayaH pAvakAH /
iheDayA sadhamAdaM madanto jyok pashyema sUryam uchcharantam //3//
236

yat te devI nirR^itir Ababandha dAma grIvAsv avimokyaM yat /
tat te vi ShyAmy AyuShe varchase balAyAdomadam annam addhi prasUtaH //1//
namo 'stu te nirR^ite tigmatejo 'yasmayAn vi chR^itA bandhapAshAn /
yamo mahyam punar it tvAm dadAti tasmai yamAya namo astu mR^ityave //2//
ayasmaye drupade bedhiShe ihAbhihito mR^ityubhir ye sahasram /
yamena tvaM pitR^ibhiH saMvidAna uttamaM nAkam adhi rohayemam //3//
saMsam id yuvase vR^iShann agne vishvAny arya A /
iDas pade sam idhyase sa no vasUny A bhara //4//
237

saM jAnIdhvaM saM pR^ichyadhvaM saM vo manAMsi jAnatAm /
devA bhAgaM yathA pUrve samjAnAnA upAsate //1//
samAno mantraH samitiH samAnI samAnaM vrataM saha chittam eShAm /
samAnena vo haviShA juhomi samAnaM cheto abhisaMvishadhvam //2//
samAnI va AkUtiH samAnA hR^idayAni vaH /
samAnam astu vo manaH yathA vaH susahAsati //3//
238

ava manyur avAyatAva bAhU manoyujA /
parAshara tvaM teShAm parA~nchaM shuShmam ardayAdhA no rayim A kR^idhi //1//
nirhastebhyo nairhastam yaM devAH sharum asyatha /
vR^ishchAmi shatrUNAM bAhUn anena haviShA 'ham //2//
indrash chakAra prathamaM nairhastam asurebhyaH /
jayantu satvAno mama sthireNendreNa medinA //3//
239

nirhastaH shatrur abhidAsann astu ye senAbhir yudham Ayanty asmAn /
sam arpayendra mahatA vadhena drAtv eShAm aghahAro vividdhaH //1//
AtanvAnA Ayachhanto 'syanto ye cha dhAvatha /
nirhastAH shatravaH sthanendro vo 'dya parAsharIt //2//
nirhastAH santu shatravo '~NgaiShAM mlApayAmasi /
athaiShAm indra vedAMsi shatasho vi bhajAmahai //3//
240

pari vartmAni sarvata indraH pUShA cha sasratuH /
muhyantv adyAmUH senA amitrANAM parastarAm //1//
mUDhA amitrAsh charatAshIrShANa ivAhayaH /
teShAM vo agnimUDhAnAm indro hantu varaMvaram //2//
aiShu nahya vR^iShAjinaM hariNasya bhiyaM kR^idhi /
parA~N amitra eShatv arvAchI gaur upeShatu //3//
241

Ayam agant savitA kShureNoShNena vAya udakenehi /
AdityA rudrA vasava undantu sachetasaH somasya rAj~no vapata prachetasaH //1//
aditiH shmashru vapatv Apa undantu varchasA /
chikitsatu prajApatir dIrghAyutvAya chakShase //2//
yenAvapat savitA kShureNa somasya rAj~no varuNasya vidvAn /
tena brahmANo vapatedam asya gomAn ashvavAn ayam astu prajAvAn //3//
242

girAv aragarATeShu hiranye goShu yad yashaH /
surAyAM sichyamAnAyAM kIlAle madhu tan mayi //1//
ashvinA sAragheNa mA madhunA~NktaM shubhas patI /
yathA bhargasvatIM vAcham AvadAni janAm? anu //2//
mayi varcho atho yasho 'tho yaj~nasya yat payaH /
tan mayi prajApatir divi dyAm iva dR^iMhatu //3//
243

yathA mAMsam yathA surA yathAkShA adhidevane /
yathA puMso vR^iShaNyata striyAM nihanyate manaH /
evA te aghnye mano 'dhi vatse ni hanyatAm //1//
yathA hastI hastinyAH padena padam udyuje /
yathA puMso vR^iShaNyata striyAM nihanyate manaH /
evA te aghnye mano 'dhi vatse ni hanyatAm //2//
yathA pradhir yathopadhir yathA nabhyaM pradhAv adhi /
yathA puMso vR^iShaNyata striyAM nihanyate manaH /
evA te aghnye mano 'dhi vatse ni hanyatAm //3//
244

yad annam admi bahudhA virUpaM hiraNyam ashvam uta gAm ajAm avim /
yad eva kiM cha pratijagrahAham agniSh Tad dhotA suhutaM kR^inotu //1//
yan mA hutam ahutam AjagAma dattaM pitR^ibhir anumataM manuShyaiH /
yasmAn me mana ud iva rArajIty agniSh Tad dhotA suhutaM kR^iNotu //2//
yad annam admy anR^itena devA dAsyann adAsyann uta saMgR^iNAmi /
vaishvAnarasya mahato mahimnA shivaM mahyaM madhumad astv annam //3//
245

yathAsitaH prathayate vashAm anu vapUMShi kR^iNvann asurasya mAyayA /
evA te shepaH sahasAyam arko '~NgenA~NgaM saMsamakaM kR^iNotu //1//
yathA pasas tAyAdaraM vAtena sthUlabhaM kR^itam /
yAvat parasvataH pasas tAvat te vardhatAM pasaH //2//
yAvad a~NgInaM pArasvataM hAstinaM gArdabham cha yat /
yAvad ashvasya vajinas tAvat te vardhatAM pasaH //3//
246

eha yAtu varuNaH somo agnir bR^ihaspatir vasubhir eha yAtu /
asya shriyam upasaMyAta sarva ugrasya chettuH saMmanasaH sajAtAH //1//
yo vaH shuShmo hR^idayeShv antar AkUtir yA vo manasi praviShTA /
tAnt sIvayAmi haviShA ghR^itena mayi sajAtA ramatir vo astu //2//
ihaiva sta mApa yAtAdhy asmat pUShA parastAd apatham vaH kR^iNotu /
vAstoSh patir anu vo johavItu mayi sajAtA ramatiH vo astu //3//
247

saM vaH pR^ichyantAM tanva1H saM manAMsi sam u vratA /
sam vo 'yam brahmaNas patir bhagaH saM vo ajIgamat //1//
samj~napanaM vo manaso 'tho samj~napanam hR^idaH /
atho bhagasya yach chhrAntaM tena saMj~napayAmi vaH //2//
yathAdityA vasubhiH sambabhUvur marudbhir ugrA ahR^iNIyamAnAH /
evA triNAmann ahR^iNIyamAna imAn janAnt saMmanasas kR^idhIha //3//
248

nir amuM nuda okasaH sapatno yaH pR^itanyati /
nairbAdhyena haviShendra enaM parAsharIt //1//
paramAM taM parAvatam indro nudatu vR^itrahA /
yato na punar Ayati shashvatIbhyaH samAbhyaH //2//
etu tisraH parAvata etu pa~ncha janAm? ati /
etu tisro 'ti rochanA yato na punar Ayati /
shashvatIbhyaH samAbhyo yAvat sUryo asad divi //3//
249

ya enaM pariShIdanti samAdadhati chakShase /
saMpreddho agnir jihvAbhir ud etu hR^idayAd adhi //1//
agneH sAmtapanasyAham AyuShe padam A rabhe /
addhAtir yasya pashyati dhUmam udyantam AsyataH //2//
yo asya samidhaM veda kShatriyeNa samAhitAm /
nAbhihvAre padaM ni dadhAti sa mR^ityave //3//
nainaM ghnanti paryAyino na sannA.N ava gachhati /
agner yaH kShatriyo vidvAn nAma gR^ihnAti AyuShe //4//
250

asthAd dyaur asthAt pR^ithivy asthAd vishvam idaM jagat /
AsthAne parvatA asthu sthAmny ashvAm? atiShThipam //1//
ya udAnat parAyaNaM ya udAnaN nyAyanam /
Avartanam nivartanaM yo gopA api taM huve //2//
jAtavedo ni vartaya shataM te santv AvR^itaH /
sahasraM ta upAvR^itas tabhir naH punar A kR^idhi //3//
251

tena bhUtena haviShAyam A pyAyatAM punaH /
jAyAm yAm asmA AvAkShus tAm rasenAbhi vardhatAm //1//
abhi vardhatAM payasAbhi rAShTreNa vardhatAm /
rayyA sahasravarchasemau stAm anupakShitau //2//
tvaShTA jAyAm ajanayat tvaShTAsyai tvAM patim /
tvaShTA sahasram AyuMShi dIrgham AyuH kR^iNotu vAm //3//
252

ayaM no nabhasas patiH saMsphAno abhi rakShatu /
asamAtim gR^iheShu naH //1//
tvaM no nabhasas pate UrjaM gR^ihesu dhAraya /
A puShTam etv A vasu //2//
deva saMsphAna sahasrApoShasyeshiShe /
tasya no rAsva tasya no dhehi tasya te bhaktivAmsaH syAma //3//
253

antarikseNa patati vishvA bhUtAvachAkashat /
shuno divyasya yan mahas tenA te haviShA vidhema //1//
ye trayaH kAlakA~njA divi devA iva shritAH /
tAn sarvAn ahva Utaye 'smA ariShTatAtaye //2//
apsu te janma divi te sadhasthaM samudre antar mahimA te pR^ithivyAm /
shuno divyasya yan mahas tenA te haviShA vidhema //3//
254

yantAsi yachhase hastAv apa rakShAMsi sedhasi /
prajAM dhanaM cha gR^ihNAnaH parihasto abhUd ayam //1//
parihasta vi dhAraya yoniM garbhAya dhAtave /
maryAde putram A dhehi taM tvam A gamayAgame //2//
yaM parihastam abibhar aditiH putrakAmyA /
tvaShTA tam asyA A badhnAd yathA putraM janAd //3//
255

Agachhata Agatasya nAma gR^ihNAmy AyataH /
indrasya vR^itraghno vanve vAsavasya shatakratoH //1//
yena sUryAM sAvitrIm ashvinohatuH pathA /
tena mAm abravId bhago jayAm A vahatAd iti //2//
yas te '~Nkusho vasudAno bR^ihann indra hiraNyayaH /
tenA janIyate jAyAM mahyaM dhehi shachIpate //3//
256

apachitaH pra patata suparNo vasater iva /
sUryaH kR^iNotu bheShajaM chandramA vo 'pochhatu //1//
eny ekA shyeny ekA kR^iShNaikA rohiNI dve /
sarvAsAm agrabhaM nAmAvIraghnIr apetana //2//
asUtikA rAmAyaNy apachit pra patiShyati /
glaur itaH pra patiShyati sa galunto nashiShyati //3//
vIhi svAm AhutiM juShAno manasA svAhA manasA yad idaM juhomi //4//
257

yasyAs ta Asani ghore juhomy eShAM baddhAnAm avasarjanAya kam /
bhUmir iti tvAbhipramanvate janA nirR^itir iti tvAhaM pari veda sarvataH //1//
bhUte haviShmatI bhavaiSha te bhAgo yo asmAsu /
mu~nchemAn amUn enasaH svAhA //2//
evo Shv asman nirR^ite 'nehA tvam ayasmayAn vi chR^itA bandhapAshAn /
yamo mahyaM punar it tvAM dadAti tasmai yamAya namo astu mR^ityave //3//
ayasmaye drupade bedhiSha ihAbhihito mR^ityubhir ye sahasram /
yamena tvaM pitR^ibhiH saMvidAna uttamaM nAkam adhi rohayemam //4//
258

varaNo vArayAtA ayaM devo vanaspatiH /
yakShmo yo asminn AviShTas tam u devA avIvaran //1//
indrasya vachasA vayaM mitrasya varuNasya cha /
devAnAM sarveShAM vAchA yakShmaM te vArayAmahe //2//
yathA vR^itra imA Apas tastambha vishvadhA yatIH /
evA te agninA yakShmaM vaishvAnareNa vAraye //3//
259

vR^iShendrasya vR^iShA divo vR^isA pR^ithivyA ayam /
vR^iShA vishvasya bhUtasya tvam ekavR^iSho bhava //1//
samudra Ishe sravatAm agniH pR^ithivyA vashI /
chandramA nakShatrANAm Ishe tvam ekavR^iSho bhava //2//
samrAD asy asurANAM kakun manuShyAnAm /
devAnAm ardhabhAg asi tvam ekavR^iSho bhava //3//
260

A tvAhArSham antar abhUr dhruvas tiShThAvichAchalat /
vishas tvA sarvA vA~nchhantu mA tvad rAShTram adhi bhrashat //1//
ihaivaidhi mApa chyoShThAH parvata ivAvichAchalat /
indra iveha dhruvas tiShTheha rAShTram u dhAraya //2//
indra etam adIdharat dhruvaM dhruveNa haviShA /
tasmai somo adhi bravad ayaM cha brahmaNas patiH //3//
261

dhruvA dyaur dhruvA pR^ithivI dhruvaM vishvam idaM jagat /
dhruvAsaH parvatA ime dhruvo rAjA vishAm ayam //1//
dhruvaM te rAjA varuNo dhruvam devo bR^ihaspatiH /
dhruvaM ta indrash chAgnish cha rAShTraM dhArayatAM dhruvam //2//
dhruvo 'chyutaH pra mR^iNIhi shatrUn chhatrUyato 'dharAn pAdayasva /
sarvA dishaH saMmanasaH sadhrIchIr dhruvAya te samitiH kalpatAm iha //3//
262

idaM yat preNyaH shiro dattaM somena vR^iShNyam /
tataH pari prajAtena hArdiM te shochayAmasi //1//
shochayAmasi te hArdiM shochayAmasi te manaH /
vAtaM dhUma iva sadhrya1~N mAm evAnv etu ye manaH //2//
mahyaM tvA mitrAvaruNau mahyaM devI sarasvatI /
mahyaM tvA madhyaM bhUmyA ubhAv antau sam asyatAm //3//
263

yAM te rudra iShum Asyad a~Ngebhyo hR^idayAya cha /
idaM tAm adya tvad vayaM viShUchIM vi vR^ihAmasi //1//
yAs te shataM dhamanayo '~NgAny anu viShThitAH /
tAsAM te sarvAsAm vayaM nir viShANi hvayAmasi //2//
namas te rudrAsyate namaH pratihitAyai /
namo visR^ijyamAnAyai namo nipatitAyai //3//
264

imaM yavam aShTAyogaiH Shadyogebhir acharkR^iShuH /
tenA te tanvo3 rapo 'pAchInam apa vyaye //1//
nya1g vAto vAti nyak tapati sUryaH /
nIchInam aghnyA duhe nyag bhavatu te rapaH //2//
Apa id vA u bheShajIr Apo amIvachAtanIH /
Apo vishvasya bheShajIs tAs te kR^iNvantu bheShajam //3//
265

vAtaramhA bhava vAjin yujamAna indrasya yAhi prasave manojavAH /
yu~njantu tvA maruto vishvavedasa A te tvastA patsu javaM dadhAtu //1//
javas te arvan nihito guhA yaH shyene vAte uta yo 'charat parIttaH /
tena tvaM vAjin balavAn balenAjiM jaya samane parayiShNuH //2//
tanUSh Te vAjin tanva1M nayantI vAmam asmabhyaM dhAvatu sharma tubhyam /
ahruto maho dharuNAya devo divIva jyotih svam A mimIyAt //3//
266

yamo mR^ityur aghamAro nirR^itho babhruH sharvo 'stA nIlashikhaNDaH /
devajanAH senayottasthivAMsas te asmAkaM pari vR^i~njantu vIrAn //1//
manasA homair harasA ghR^itena sharvAyAstra uta rAj~ne bhavAya /
namasyebhyo nama ebhyaH kR^iNomy anyatrAsmad aghaviShA nayantu //2//
trAyadhvaM no aghaviShAbhyo vadhAd vishve devA maruto vishvavedasaH /
agnIShomA varuNaH pUtadakShA vAtAparjanyayoH sumatau syAma //3//
267

saM vo manAMsi saM vratA sam AkUtIr namAmasi /
amI ye vivratA sthana tAn vaH saM namayAmasi //1//
ahaM gR^ibhNAmi manasA manAMsi mama chittam anu chittebhir eta /
mama vasheShu hR^idayAni vaH kR^iNomi mama yAtam anuvartmAna eta //2//
ote me dyAvApR^ithivI otA devI sarasvatI /
otau ma indrash chAgnish cha rdhyAsmedaM sarasvati //3//
268

ashvattho devasadanas tR^itIyasyAm ito divi /
tatrAmR^itasya chakShaNam devAH kuShTham avanvata //1//
hiraNyayI naur acharad dhiraNyabandhanA divi /
tatrAmR^itasya puShpaM devAH kuShTham avanvata //2//
garbho asy oShadhInAM garbho himavatAm uta /
garbho vishvasya bhUtasyemaM me agadaM kR^idhi //3//
269

yA oShadhayaH somarAj~nIr bahvIH shatavichakShaNAH /
bR^ihaspatiprasUtAs tA no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //1//
mu~nchantu mA shapathyA3d atho varuNyAd uta /
atho yamasya paDvIShAd vishvasmAd devakilbiShAt //2//
yach chakShuShA manasA yach cha vAchopArima jAgrato yat svapantaH /
somas tAni svadhayA naH punAtu //3//
270

abhibhUr yaj~no abhibhUr agnir abhibhUH somo abhibhUr indraH /
abhy ahaM vishvAH pR^itanA yathAsAny evA vidhemAgnihotrA idaM haviH //1//
svadhAstu mitrAvaruNA vipashchitA prajAvat kShatraM madhuneha pinvatam /
bAdhethAM dUraM nirR^itiM parAchaiH kR^itaM chid enaH pra mumuktam asmat //2//
imaM vIram anu harShadhvam ugram indraM sakhAyo anu saM rabhadhvam /
grAmajitaM gojitaM vajrabAhuM jayantam ajma pramR^iNantam ojasA //3//
271

indro jayAti na parA jayAtA adhirAjo rAjasu rAjayAtai /
charkR^itya IDyo vandyash chopasadyo namasysh bhaveha //1//
tvam indrAdhirAjaH shravasyus tvaM bhUr abhibhUtir janAnAm /
tvaM daivIr visha imA vi rAjAyuShmat kShatram ajaraM te astu //2//
prAchyA dishas tvam indrAsi rAjotodIchyA disho vR^itrahan chhatruho 'si /
yatra yanti srotyAs taj jitaM te dakShiNato vR^iShabha eShi havyaH //3//
272

abhi tvendra varimataH purA tvAMhUraNAd dhuve /
hvayAmy ugraM chettAraM puruNAmAnam ekajam //1//
yo adya senyo vadho jighAMsan na udIrate /
indrasya tatra bAhU samantaM pari dadmaH //2//
pari dadma indrasya bAhU samantaM trAtus trAyatAM naH /
deva savitaH soma rAjan sumanasaM mA kR^iNu svastaye //3//
273

devA aduH sUryo dyaur adAt pR^ithivy adAt /
tisraH sarasvatir aduH sachittA viShadUShaNam //1//
yad vo devA upajIkA Asi~nchan dhanvany udakam /
tena devaprasUtenedaM dUShayatA viSham //2//
asurANAM duhitAsi sA devAnAm asi svasA /
divas pR^ithivyAH saMbhUtA sA chakarthArasaM viSham //3//
274

A vR^iShAyasva shvasihi vardhasva prathayasva cha /
yathA~NgaM vardhatAM shepas tena yoShitam ij jahi //1//
yena kR^iShaM vAjayanti yena hinvanty Aturam /
tenAsya brahmaNas pate dhanur ivA tAnayA pasaH //2//
AhaM tanomi te paso adhi jyAm iva dhanvani /
kramasva R^iSha iva rohitam anavaglAyatA sadA //3//
275

yathayaM vAho ashvinA samaiti saM cha vartate /
evA mAm abhi te manaH samaitu saM cha vartatAm //1//
AhaM khidAmi te mano rAjAshvaH pR^iShTyAm iva /
reShmachhinnam yathA tR^iNaM mayi te veShTatAM manaH //2//
A~njanasya madughasya kuShThasya naladasya cha /
turo bhagasya hastAbhyAm anurodhanam ud bhare //3//
276

saMdAnaM vo bR^ihaspatiH saMdAnaM savitA karat /
saMdAnaM mitro aryamA saMdAnaM bhago ashvinA //1//
sam paramAnt sam avamAn atho saM dyAmi madhyamAn /
indras tAn pary ahAr dAmnA tAn agne saM dyA tvam //2//
amI ye yudham Ayanti ketUn kR^itvAnIkashaH /
indras tAn pary ahAr dAmna tAn agne saM dyA tvam //3//
277

AdAnena saMdAnenAmitrAn A dyAmasi /
apAnA ye chaiShAM prANA asunAsUnt sam achhidan //1//
idam AdAnam akaraM tapasendreNa saMshitam /
amitrA ye 'tra naH santi tAn agna A dyA tvam //2//
ainAn dyatAm indrAgnI somo rAjA cha medinau /
indro marutvAn AdAnam amitrebhyaH kR^iNotu naH //3//
278

yathA mano manasketaiH parApataty Ashumat /
evA tvaM kAse pra pata manaso 'nu pravAyyam //1//
yathA bANaH susaMshitaH parApataty Ashumat /
evA tvaM kAse pra pata pR^ithivyA anu saMvatam //2//
yathA sUryasya rashmayaH parApatanty Ashumat /
evA tvaM kAse pra pata samudrasyAnu vikSharam //3//
279

Ayane te parAyane dUrvA rohantu puShpiNIH /
utso vA tatra jAyatAm hrado vA puNDarIkavAn //1//
apAm idaM nyayanaM samudrasya niveshanam /
madhye hradasya no gR^ihAH parAchInA mukhA kR^idhi //2//
himasya tvA jarAyuNA shAle pari vyayAmasi /
shItahradA hi no bhuvo 'gniSh kR^iNotu bheShajam //3//
280

vishvajit trAyamANAyai mA pari dehi /
trAyamANe dvipAch cha sarvaM no rakSha chatuShpAD yach cha naH svam //1//
trAyamANe vishvajite mA pari dehi /
vishvajid dvipAch cha sarvaM no rakSha chatuShpAD yach cha naH svam //2//
vishvajit kalyANyai mA pari dehi /
kalyANi dvipAch cha sarvaM no rakSha chatuShpAD yach cha naH svam //3//
kalyANi sarvavide mA pari dehi /
sarvavid dvipAch cha sarvaM no rakSha chatuShpAD yach cha naH svam //4//
281

tvaM no medhe prathamA gobhir ashvebhir A gahi /
tvaM sUryasya rashmibhis tvam no asi yaj~niyA //1//
medhAm ahaM prathamAM brahmaNvatIM brahmajUtAm R^iShiShTutAm /
prapItAM brahmachAribhir devAnAm avase huve //2//
yAM medhAm R^ibhavo vidur yAM medhAm asurA viduH /
R^iShayo bhadrAM medhAM yAM vidus tAM mayy A veshayAmasi //3//
yAm R^iShayo bhUtakR^ito medhAM medhAvino viduH /
tayA mAm adya medhayAgne medhAvinaM kR^iNu //4//
medhAM sAyaM medhAM prAtar medhAM madhyandinaM pari /
medhAM sUryasya rashmibhir vachasA veshayAmahe //5//
282

pippalI kShiptabheShajy ù3tAtividdhabheShajI /
tAM devAH sam akalpayann iyaM jIvitavA alam //1//
pippalya1H sam avadantAyatIr jananAd adhi /
yaM jIvam ashnavAmahai na sa riShyAti pUruShaH //2//
asurAs tvA ny akhanan devAs tvod avapan punaH /
vAtIkR^itasya bheShajIm atho kShiptasya bheShajIm //3//
283

pratno hi kam IDyo adhvareShu sanAch cha hotA navyash cha satsi /
svAm chAgne tanvaM piprAyasvAsmabhyaM cha saubhagam A yajasva //1//
jyeShThaghnyAM jAto vichR^itor yamasya mUlabarhaNAt pari pAhy enam /
aty enam neShad duritAni vishvA dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya //2//
vyAghre 'hny ajaniShTa vIro nakShatrajA jAyamAnaH suvIraH /
sa mA vadhIt pitaraM vardhamAno mA mAtaraM pra minIj janitrIm //3//
284

imam me agne puruSham mumugdhy ayaM yo baddhaH suyato lAlapIti /
ato 'dhi te kR^iNavad bhAgadheyaM yadAnunmadito 'sati //1//
agniSh Te ni shamayatu yadi te mana udyatam /
kR^iNomi vidvAn bheShajaM yathAnunmadito 'sasi //2//
devainasAd unmaditam unmattam rakShasas pari /
kR^iNomi vidvAn bheShajaM yadAnunmadito 'sati //3//
punas tvA dur apsarasaH punar indraH punar bhagaH /
punas tvA dur vishve devA yathAnunmadito 'sasi //4//
285

mA jyeShThaM vadhId ayam agna eShAm mUlabarhaNAt pari pAhy enam /
sa grAhyAH pAshAn vi chR^ita prajAnan tubhyaM devA anu jAnantu vishve //1//
un mu~ncha pAshAMs tvam agna eShAM trayas tribhir utsitA yebhir Asan /
sa grAhyAH pAshAn vi chR^ita prajAnan pitAputrau mAtaraM mu~ncha sarvAn //2//
yebhiH pAshaiH parivitto vibaddho '~Ngea~Nga Arpita utsitash cha /
vi te muchyantaM vimucho hi santi bhrUNaghni pUShan duritAni mR^ikShva //3//
286

trite devA amR^ijataitad enas trita enan manuShyeShu mamR^ije /
tato yadi tvA grAhir Anashe tAM te devA brahmaNA nAshayantu //1//
marIchIr dhUmAn pra vishAnu pApmann udArAn gachhota vA nIhArAn /
nadInaM phenAm anu tAn vi nashya bhrUNaghni pUShan duritAni mR^ikShva //2//
dvAdashadhA nihitaM tritasyApamR^iShTam manuShyainasAni /
tato yadi tvA grAhir Anashe tAM te devA brahmaNA nAshayantu //3//
287

yad devA devahedanaM devAsash chakR^ima vayam /
AdityAs tasmAn no yuyam R^itasya R^itena mu~nchata //1//
R^itasya R^itenAdityA yajatrA mu~nchateha naH /
yaj~naM yad yaj~navAhasaH shikShanto nopashekima //2//
medasvatA yajamAnAH sruchAjyAni juhvataH /
akAmA vishve vo devAH shikShanto nopa shekima //3//
288

yad vidvAmso yad avidvAMsa enAMsi chakR^imA vayam /
yUyaM nas tasmAn mu~nchata vishve devAH sajoShasah //1//
yadi jAgrad yadi svapann ena enasyo 'karam /
bhUtaM mA tasmAd bhavyaM cha drupadAd iva mu~nchatAm //2//
drupadAd iva mumuchAnaH svinnaH snAtvA malAd iva /
pUtaM pavitreNevAjyaM vishve shumbhantu mainasaH //3//
289

yad yAmam chakrur nikhananto agre kArShIvaNA annavido na vidyayA /
vaivasvate rAjani taj juhomy atha yaj~niyaM madhumad astu no 'nnam //1//
vaivasvataH kR^iNavad bhAgadheyaM madhubhAgo madhunA saM sR^ijAti /
mAtur yad ena iShitaM na Agan yad vA pitA 'parAddho jihIde //2//
yadIdaM mAtur yadi pitur naH pari bhrAtuH putrAch chetasa ena Agan /
yAvanto asmAn pitaraH sachante teShAM sarveShAM shivo astu manyuH //3//
290

apamityam apratIttaM yad asmi yamasya yena balinA charAmi /
idaM tad agne anR^iNo bhavAmi tvaM pAshAn vichR^itaM vettha sarvAn //1//
ihaiva santaH prati dadma enaj jIvA jIvebhyo ni harAma enat /
apamitya dhAnya1M yaj jaghasAham idaM tad agne anR^iNo bhavAmi //2//
anR^iNA asminn anR^iNAH parasmin tR^itIye loke anR^iNAH syAma /
ye devayAnAH pitR^iyANash cha lokAH sarvAn patho anR^iNA A kShiyema //3//
291

yad dhastAbhyAM chakR^ima kilbiShAny akShAnAM gatnum upalipsamAnAH /
ugraMpashye ugrajitau tad adyApsarasAv anu dattAm R^iNaM naH //1//
ugraMpashye rAShTrabhR^it kilbiShANi yad akShavR^ittam anu dattam na etat /
R^iNAn no na R^iNam ertsamAno yamasya loke adhirajjur Ayat //2//
yasmA R^iNaM yasya jAyAm upaimi yaM yAchamAno abhyaimi devAH /
te vAchaM vAdiShur mottarAM mad devapatnI apsarasAv adhItam //3//
292

yad adIvyann R^iNam ahaM kR^iNomy adAsyann agne uta saMgR^iNAmi /
vaishvAnaro no adhipA vasiShTha ud in nayAti sukR^itasya lokam //1//
vaishvAnarAya prati vedayAmi yadi R^iNaM saMgaro devatAsu /
sa etAn pAshAn vichR^itam veda sarvAn atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //2//
vaishvAnaraH pavitA mA punAtu yat saMgaram abhidhAvAmy AshAm /
anAjAnan manasA yAchamAno yat tatraino apa tat suvAmi //3//
293

yad antarikShaM pR^ithivIm uta dyAm yan mAtaraM pitaraM vA jihiMsima /
ayaM tasmAd gArhapatyo no agnir ud in nayAti sukR^itasya lokam //1//
bhUmir mAtAditir no janitraM bhrAtAntarikSham abhishastyA naH /
dyaur naH pitA pitryAch chhaM bhavAti jAmim R^itvA mAva patsi lokAt //2//
yatrA suhArdaH sukR^ito madanti vihAya rogaM tanva1H svAyAH /
ashlonA a~Ngair ahrutAH svarge tatra pashyema pitarau cha putrAn //3//
294

viShANA pAshAn vi ShyAdhy asmad ya uttamA adhamA vAruNA ye /
duShvapnyaM duritaM ni ShvAsmad atha gachhema sukR^itasya lokam //1//
yad dAruNi badhyase yach cha rajjvAM yad bhUmyAM badhyase yach cha vAchA /
ayaM tasmAd gArhapatyo no agnir ud in nayAti sukR^itasya lokam //2//
ud agAtAM bhagavatI vichR^itau nAma tArake /
prehAmR^itasya yachhatAM praitu baddhakamochanam //3//
vi jihIShva lokam kR^iNu bandhAn mu~nchAsi baddhakam /
yonyA iva prachyuto garbhaH pathaH sarvAm? anu kShiya //4//
295

etaM bhAgaM pari dadAmi vidvAn vishvakarman prathamajA R^itasya /
asmAbhir dattaM jarasaH parastAd achhinnaM tantum anu saM tarema //1//
tataM tantum anv eke taranti yeShAM dattaM pitryam Ayanena /
abandhv eke dadataH prayachhanto dAtuM chech chhikShAnt sa svarga eva //2//
anvArabhethAm anusaMrabhethAm etaM lokaM shraddadhAnAH sachante /
yad vAM pakvaM pariviShTam agnau tasya guptaye dampatI saM shrayethAm //3//
yaj~nam yantaM manasA bR^ihantam anvArohAmi tapasA sayoniH /
upahUtA agne jarasaH parastAt tR^itIye nAke sadhamAdaM madema //4//
shuddhAH pUtA yoShito yaj~niyA imA brahmaNAM hasteShu prapR^ithak sAdayAmi /
yatkAma idaM abhiShi~nchAmi vo 'haM indro marutvAnt sa dadAtu tan me //5//
296

etaM sadhasthAH pari vo dadAmi yaM shevadhim AvahAj jAtavedaH /
anvAgantA yajamAnaH svasti taM sma jAnIta parame vyoman //1//
jAnIta smainaM parame vyoman devAH sadhasthA vida lokam atra /
anvAgantA yajamAnaH svastIShTApUrtaM sma kR^iNutAvir asmai //2//
devAH pitaraH pitaro devAH /
yo asmi so asmi //3//
sa pachAmi sa dadAmi /
sa yaje sa dattAn mA yUSham //4//
nAke rAjan prati tiShTha tatraitat prati tiShThatu /
viddhi pUrtasya no rAjant sa deva sumanA bhava //5//
297

divo nu mAm bR^ihato antarikShAd apAM stoko abhy apaptad rasena /
sam indriyena payasAham agne chhandobhir yaj~naiH sukR^itAM kR^itena //1//
yadi vR^ikShAd abhyapaptat phalaM tad yady antarikShAt sa u vAyur eva /
yatrAspR^ikShat tanvo3 yach cha vAsasa Apo nudantu nirR^itiM parAchaiH //2//
abhya~njanaM surabhi sA samR^iddhir hiraNyaM varchas tad u pUtrimam eva /
sarvA pavitrA vitatAdhy asmat tan mA tArIn nirR^itir mo arAtiH //3//
298

vanaspate vIDva~Ngo hi bhUyA asmatsakhA prataraNaH suvIraH /
gobhiH saMnaddho asi vIDayasvAsthAtA te jayatu jetvAni //1//
divas pR^ithivyAH pary oja udbhR^itaM vanaspatibhyaH pary AbhR^itaM sahaH /
apAm ojmAnaM pari gobhir AvR^itam indrasya vajraM haviShA rathaM yaja //2//
indrasyaujo marutAm anIkaM mitrasya garbho varuNasya nAbhiH /
sa imAM no havyadAtiM juShANo deva ratha prati havyA gR^ibhAya //3//
299

upa shvAsaya pR^ithivIm uta dyaM purutrA te vanvatAM viShThitam jagat /
sa dundubhe sajUr indreNa devair dUrAd davIyo apa sedha shatrUn //1//
A krandaya balam ojo na A dhA abhi ShTana duritA bAdhamAnaH /
apa sedha dundubhe duchhunAm ita indrasya muShTir asi vIDayasva //2//
prAmUM jayAbhIme jayantu ketumad dundubhir vAvadItu /
sam ashvaparNAH patantu no naro 'smAkam indra rathino jayantu //3//
300

vidradhasya balAsasya lohitasya vanaspate /
visalpakasyoShadhe moch chhiShaH pishitaM chana //1//
yau te balAsa tiShThataH kakShe muShkAv apashritau /
vedAhaM tasya bheShajaM chIpudrur abhichakShaNam //2//
yo a~Ngyo yaH karNyo yo akShyor visalpakaH /
vi vR^ihAmo visalpakaM vidradhaM hR^idayAmayam /
parA tam aj~nAtam yakShmam adharA~nchaM suvAmasi //3//
301

shakadhUmaM nakShatrANi yad rAjAnam akurvata /
bhadrAham asmai prAyachhan idaM rAShTram asAd iti //1//
bhadrAhaM no madhyaMdine bhadrAhaM sAyam astu naH /
bhadrAhaM no ahnAM prAtA rAtrI bhadrAham astu naH //2//
ahorAtrAbhyAM nakShatrebhyaH suryAchandramasAbhyAm /
bhadrAham asmabhyaM rAjan chhakadhUma tvaM kR^idhi //3//
yo no bhadrAham akaraH sAyaM naktam atho divA /
tasmai te nakShatrarAja shakadhUma sadA namaH //4//
302

bhagena mA shAMshapena sAkam indreNa medinA /
kR^iNomi bhaginaM mApa drAntv arAtayaH //1//
yena vR^ikShA.N abhyabhavo bhagena varchasA saha /
tena mA bhaginaM kR^iNv apa drAntv arAtayaH //2//
yo andho yaH punaHsaro bhago vR^ikSheShv AhitaH /
tena mA bhaginaM kR^iNv apa drAntv arAtayaH //3//
303

rathajitAM rAthajiteyInAm apsarasAm ayaM smaraH /
devAH pra hiNuta smaram asau mAm anu shochatu //1//
asau me smaratAd iti priyo me smaratAd iti /
devAh pra hiNuta smaram asau mAm anu shochatu //2//
yathA mama smarAd asau nAmuShyAhaM kadA chana /
devAH pra hiNuta smaram asau mAm anu shochatu //3//
un mAdayata maruta ud antarikSha mAdaya /
agna un mAdayA tvam asau mAm anu shochatu //4//
304

ni shIrShato ni pattata Adhyo3 ni tirAmi te /
devAH pra hiNuta smaram asau mAm anu shochatu //1//
anumate 'nv idaM manyasvAkute sam idaM namaH /
devAH pra hiNuta smaram asau mAm anu shochatu //2//
yad dhAvasi triyojanaM pa~nchayojanam Ashvinam /
tatas tvaM punar Ayasi putrANAM no asaH pitA //3//
305

yaM devAH smaram asi~nchann apsv antaH shoshuchAnaM sahAdhyA /
taM te tapAmi varuNasya dharmaNA //1//
yaM vishve devAH smaram asi~nchann apsv antaH shoshuchAnaM sahAdhyA /
taM te tapAmi varuNasya dharmaNA //2//
yam indrANI smaram asi~nchad apsv antaH shoshuchAnaM sahAdhyA /
taM te tapAmi varuNasya dharmaNA //3//
yam indrAgnI smaram asi~nchatAm apsv antaH shoshuchAnaM sahAdhyA /
taM te tapAmi varuNasya dharmaNA //4//
yam mitrAvaruNau smaram asi~nchatAm apsv antaH shoshuchAnaM sahAdhyA /
taM te tapAmi varuNasya dharmaNA //5//
306

ya imAM devo mekhalAm Ababandha yaH saMnanAha ya u no yuyoja /
yasya devasya prashiShA charAmaH sa pAram ichhAt sa u no vi mu~nchAt //1//
AhutAsy abhihuta R^iShINAm asy Ayudham /
pUrvA vratasya prAshnatI vIraghnI bhava mekhale //2//
mR^ityor ahaM brahmachArI yad asmi niryAchan bhUtAt puruShaM yamAya /
tam ahaM brahmaNA tapasA shrameNAnayainaM mekhalayA sinAmi //3//
shraddhAyA duhitA tapaso 'dhi jAtA svasA R^iShINAM bhUtakR^itAM babhUva /
sA no mekhale matim A dhehi medhAm atho no dhehi tapa indriyaM cha //4//
yAM tvA pUrve bhUtakR^ita R^iShayaH paribedhire /
sA tvaM pari Shvajasva mAM dIrghAyutvAya mekhale //5//
307

ayaM vajras tarpayatAm R^itasyAvAsya R^iAShTram apa hantu jIvitam /
shR^iNAtu grIvAH pra shR^iNAtUShNihA vR^itrasyeva shachIpatiH //1//
adharo'dhara uttarebhyo gUDhaH pR^ithivyA mot sR^ipat /
vajreNAvahataH shayAm //2//
yo jinAti tam anvichha yo jinAti tam ij jahi /
jinato vajra tvaM sImantam anva~ncham anu pAtaya //3//
308

yad ashnAmi balaM kurva itthaM vajram A dade /
skandhAn amuShya shAtayan vR^itrasyeva shachIpatiH //1//
yat pibAmi saM pibAmi samudra iva saMpibaH /
prANAn amuShya saMpAya saM pibAmo amuM vayam //2//
yad girAmi saM girAmi samudra iva saMgiraH /
prANAn amuShya saMgIrya saM girAmo amum vayam //3//
309

devI devyAm adhi jAtA pR^ithivyAm asy oShadhe /
tAM tvA nitatni keshebhyo dR^iMhaNAya khanAmasi //1//
dR^iMha pratnAn janayAjAtAn jAtAn u varShIyasas kR^idhi //2//
yas te kesho 'vapadyate samUlo yash cha vR^ishchate /
idaM taM vishvabheShajyAbhi Shi~nchAmi vIrudhA //3//
310

yAM jamadagnir akhanad duhitre keshavardhanIm /
tAM vItahavya Abharad asitasya gR^ihebhyaH //1//
abhIshunA meyA Asan vyAmenAnumeyAH /
keshA naDA iva vardhantAM shIrShNas te asitAH pari //2//
dR^iMha mUlam AgraM yachha vi madhyaM yAmayauShadhe /
keshA naDA iva vardhantAm shIrShNas te asitAH pari //3//
311

tvaM vIrudhAM shreShThatamAbhishrutAsy oShadhe /
imaM me adya puruShaM klIbam opashinaM kR^idhi //1//
klIbaM kR^idhy opashinam atho kurIriNaM kR^idhi /
athAsyendro grAvabhyAm ubhe bhinattv ANDyaù //2//
klIba klIbaM tvAkaraM vadhre vadhriM tvAkaram arasArasaM tvAkaram /
kurIram asya shIrShaNi kumbaM chAdhinidadhmasi //3//
ye te nAdyaù devakR^ite yayos tiShThati vR^iShNyam /
te te bhinadmi shamyayAmuShyA adhi muShkayoH //4//
yathA naDam kashipune striyo bhindanty ashmanA /
evA bhinadmi te shepo 'muShyA adhi muShkayoH //5//
312

nyastikA rurohitha subhagaMkaraNI mama /
shataM tava pratAnAs trayastriMshan nitAnAH //
tayA sahasraparNyA hR^idayaM shoShayAmi te //1//
shuShyatu mayi te hR^idayam atho shuShyatv Asyam /
atho ni shuShya mAM kAmenAtho shuShkAsyA chara //2//
saMvananI samuShpalA babhru kalyANi saM nuda /
amUM cha mAM cha saM nuda samAnaM hR^idayaM kR^idhi //3//
yathodakam apapuSho 'pashuShyaty Asyam /
evA ni shuShya mAM kAmenAtho shuShkAsyA chara //4//
yathA nakulo vichhidya saMdadhAty ahiM punaH /
evA kAmasya vichhinnaM saM dhehi vIryAvati //5//
313

yau vyAghrAv avarUdhau jighatsataH pitaraM mAtaraM cha /
tau dantaM brahmaNas pate shivau kR^iNu jAtavedaH //1//
vrIhim attaM yavam attam atho mASham atho tilam /
eSha vAM bhAgo nihito ratnadheyAya dantau mA hiMsiShTaM pitaram mAtaraM cha //2//
upahUtau sayujau syonau dantau suma~Ngalau /
anyatra vAM ghoraM tanvaH paraitu dantau mA hiMsiShTaM pitaraM mAtaraM cha //3//
314

vAyur enAH samAkarat tvaShTA poShAya dhriyatAm /
indra Abhyo adhi bravad rudro bhUmne chikitsatu //1//
lohitena svadhitinA mithunaM karNayoH kR^idhi /
akartAm ashvinA lakShma tad astu prajayA bahu //2//
yathA chakrur devAsurA yathA manuShyA uta /
evA sahasrapoShAya kR^iNutaM lakShmAshvinA //3//
315

uch chhrayasva bahur bhava svena mahasA yava /
mR^iNIhi vishvA pAtrANi mA tvA divyAshanir vadhIt //1//
AshR^iNvantaM yavaM devaM yatra tvAchhAvadAmasi /
tad uch chhrayasva dyaur iva samudra ivaidhy akShitaH //2//
akShitAs ta upasado 'kShitAH santu rAshayaH /
pR^iNanto akShitAH santv attAraH santv akShitAH //3//

Book:
7

dhItI vA ye anayan vAcho agraM manasA vA ye 'vadann R^itAni /
tR^itIyena brahmaNA vAvR^idhAnAs turIyeNAmanvata nAma dhenoH //1//
