sa dyAm aurNod antarikShaM svaH sa idaM vishvam abhavat sa Abharat //2//
317

atharvANaM pitaraM devabandhuM mAtur garbhaM pitur asuM yuvAnam /
ya imaM yaj~nam manasA chiketa pra No vochas tam iheha bravaH //1//
318

ayA viShThA janayan karvarANi sa hi ghR^iNir urur varAya gAtuH /
sa pratyudaid dharuNaM madhvo agraM svayA tanvA tanvam airayata //1//
319

ekayA cha dashabhish cha suhute dvAbhyAm iShTaye viMshatyA cha /
tisR^ibhish cha vahase triMshatA cha viyugbhir vAya iha tA vi mu~ncha //1//
320

yaj~nena yaj~nam ayajanta devAs tAni dharmANi prathamAny Asan /
te ha nAkaM mahimAnaH sachanta yatra pUrve sAdhyAH santi devAH //1//
yaj~no babhUva sa A babhUva sa pra jaj~ne sa u vAvR^idhe punaH /
sa devAnAm adhipatir babhUva so asmAsu draviNam A dadhAtu //2//
yad devA devAn haviShA 'yajantAmartyAn manasA martyena /
madema tatra parame vyoman pashyema tad uditau sUryasya //3//
yat puruSheNa haviShA yaj~naM devA atanvata /
asti nu tasmAd ojIyo yad vihavyenejire //4//
mugdhA devA uta shunA 'yajantota gor a~NgaiH purudhA 'yajanta /
ya imaM yaj~naM manasA chiketa pra No vochas tam iheha bravaH //5//
321

aditir dyaur aditir antarikSham aditir mAtA sa pitA sa putraH /
vishve devA aditir pa~ncha janA aditir jAtam aditir janitvam //1//
mahIm U Shu mAtaraM suvratAnAm R^itasya patnIm avase havAmahe /
tuvikShatrAm ajarantIm urUchIM susharmANam aditiM supraNItim //2//
{7}
 {1}
sutrAmANaM pR^ithivIM dyAm anehasaM susharmANam aditiM supraNItim /
daivIM nAvaM svaritrAm anAgaso asravantIm A ruhemA svastaye //1//
 {2}
vAjasya nu prasave mAtaraM mahIm aditiM nAma vachasA karAmahe /
yasyA upastha urv antarikShaM sA naH sharma trivarUthaM ni yachhAt //2//
322{8}

diteH putrANAm aditer akAriSham ava devAnAM bR^ihatAm anarmaNAm /
teShAM hi dhAma gabhiShak samudriyaM nainAn namasA paro asti kashchana //1//
323{9}

bhadrAd adhi shreyaH prehi bR^ihaspatiH puraetA te astu /
athemam asyA vara A pR^ithivyA AreshatruM kR^iNuhi sarvavIram //1//
324{10}

prapathe pathAm ajaniShTa pUShA prapathe divaH prapathe pR^ithivyAH /
ubhe abhi priyatame sadhasthe A cha parA cha charati prajAnan //1//
pUShemA AshA anu veda sarvAH so asmA.N abhayatamena neShat /
svastidA AghR^iNiH sarvavIro 'prayuchhan pura etu prajAnan //2//
pUShan tava vrate vayaM na riShyema kadA chana /
stotAras ta iha smasi //3//
pari pUShA parastAd dhastaM dadhAtu dakShiNam /
punar no naShTam Ajatu saM naShTena gamemahi //4//
325{11}

yas te stanaH shashayur yo mayobhUr yaH sumnayuH suhavo yaH sudatraH /
yena vishvA puShyasi vAryANi sarasvati tam iha dhAtave kaH //1//
326{12}

yas te pR^ithu stanayitnur ya R^iShvo daivaH ketur vishvam AbhUShatIdam /
mA no vadhIr vidyutA deva sasyaM mota vadhI rashmibhiH sUryasya //1//
327{13}

sabhA cha mA samitish chAvatAM prajApater duhitarau saMvidAne /
yenA saMgachhA upa mA sa shikShAch chAru vadAni pitarah saMgateShu //1//
vidma te sabhe nAma nariShTA nAma vA asi /
ye te ke cha sabhAsadas te me santu savAchasaH //2//
eShAm ahaM samAsInAnAM varcho vij~nAnam A dade /
asyAH sarvasyAH saMsado mAm indra bhaginaM kR^iNu //3//
yad vo manaH parAgataM yad baddham iha veha vA /
tad va A vartayAmasi mayi vo ramatAM manaH //4//
328{14}

yathA sUryo nakShatrANAm udyaMs tejAMsy Adade /
evA strINAM cha puMsAM cha dviShatAM varcha A dade //1//
yAvanto mA sapatnAnAm AyantaM pratipashyatha /
udyant sUrya iva suptAnAM dviShatAm varcha A dade //2//
329{15}

abhi tyaM devaM savitAram oNyoH kavikratum /
archAmi satyasavaM ratnadhAm abhi priyaM matim //1//
urdhvA yasyAmatir bhA adidyutat savImani /
hiraNyapANir amimIta sukratuH kR^ipAt svaH //2//

athAsmabhyaM savitar vAryANi divodiva A suvA bhUri pashvaH //3//
damUnA devaH savitA vareNyo dadhad ratnaM pitR^ibhya AyUMShi /
pibAt somaM mamadad enam iShTe parijmA chit kramate asya dharmaNi //4//
330{16}

tAM savitaH satyasavAM suchitrAm AhaM vR^iNe sumatiM vishvavArAm /
yAm asya kaNvo aduhat prapInAM sahasradhArAM mahiSho bhagAya //1//
331{17}

bR^ihaspate savitar vardhayainaM jyotayainaM mahate saubhagAya /
saMshitaM chit saMtaraM saM shishAdhi vishva enam anu madantu devAH //1//
332{18}

dhAtA dadhAtu no rayim IshAno jagatas patiH /
sa naH pUrNena yachhatu //1//
dhAtA dadhAtu dAshuShe prAchIM jIvAtum akShitAm /
vayam devasya dhImahi sumatiM vishvarAdhasaH //2//
dhAtA vishvA vAryA dadhAtu prajAkAmAya dAshuShe duroNe /
tasmai devA amR^itaM saM vyayantu vishve devA aditiH sajoShAH //3//
dhAtA rAtiH savitedaM juShantAM prajApatir nidhipatir no agniH /
tvaShTA viShNuH prajayA saMrarANo yajamAnAya draviNaM dadhAtu //4//
333{19}

pra nabhasva pR^ithivi bhinddhI3daM divyaM nabhaH /
udno divyasya no dhAtar IshAno vi ShyA dR^itim //1//
na ghraMs tatApa na himo jaghAna pra nabhatAM pR^ithivI jIradAnuH /
Apash chid asmai ghR^itam it kSharanti yatra somaH sadam it tatra bhadram //2//
334{20}

prajApatir janayati prajA imA dhAtA dadhAtu sumanasyamAnaH /
saMjAnAnAH saMmanasaH sayonayo mayi puShTaM puShTapatir dadhAtu //1//
335{21}

anv adya no 'numatir yaj~naM deveShu manyatAm /
agnish cha havyavAhano bhavatAM dAshuShe mama //1//
anv id anumate tvaM maMsase shaM cha nas kR^idhi /
juShasva havyam AhutaM prajAM devi rarAsva naH //2//
anu manyatAm anumanyamAnaH prajAvantaM rayim akShIyamANam /
tasya vayaM heDasi mApi bhUma sumR^iDIke asya sumatau syAma //3//
yat te nAma suhavaM supraNIte 'numate anumataM sudAnu /
tenA no yaj~naM pipR^ihi vishvavAre rayiM no dhehi subhage suvIram //4//
emaM yaj~nam anumatir jagAma sukShetratAyai suvIratAyai sujAtam /
bhadrA hy asyAH pramatir babhUva semam yaj~nam avatu devagopA //5//
anumatiH sarvam idaM babhUva yat tiShThati charati yad u cha vishvam ejati /
tasyAs te devi sumatau syAmAnumate anu hi maMsase naH //6//
336{22}

sameta vishve vachasA patiM diva eko vibhUr atithir janAnAm /
sa pUrvyo nUtanam AvivAsat taM vartanir anu vAvR^ita ekam it puru //1//
337{23}

ayaM sahasram A no dR^ishe kavInAM matir jyotir vidharmaNi //1//
bradhnaH samIchIr uShasaH sam airayan /
arepasaH sachetasaH svasare manyumattamAsh chite goH //2//
338{24}

dauShvapnyam daurjIvityaM rakSho abhvam arAyyaH /
durNAmnIH sarvA durvAchas tA asman nAshayAmasi //1//
339{25}

yan na indro akhanad yad agnir vishve devA maruto yat svarkAH /
tad asmabhyaM savitA satyadharmA prajApatir anumatir ni yachhAt //1//
340{26}

yayor ojasA skabhitA rajAMsi yau vIryair vIratamA shaviShThA /
yau patyete apratItau sahobhir viShNum agan varuNaM pUrvahUtiH //1//
yasyedaM pradishi yad virochate pra chAnati vi cha chaShTe shachIbhiH /
purA devasya dharmaNA sahobhir viShNum agan varuNaM pUrvahUtiH //2//
341{27}

viShNor nu kaM prA vochaM vIryANi yaH pArthivAni vimame rajAMsi /
yo askabhAyad uttaraM sadhasthaM vichakramANas tredhorugAyaH //1//
pra tad viShNu stavate vIryANi mR^igo na bhImaH kucharo giriShThAH /
parAvata A jagamyAt parasyAH //2//

samUDham asya paMsure //4//

ito dharmANi dhArayan //5//

indrasya yujyaH sakhA //6//

342{28}

343{29}

344{30}

agnAviShNU mahi tad vAM mahitvam pAtho ghR^itasya guhyasya nAma /

349{35}

350{36}

351{37}

352{38}

353{39}

354{40}

355{41}

356{42}

357{43}

somArudrA vi vR^ihataM viShUchIm amIvA yA no gayam Avivesha /

358{44}

360{46}

 {1}

361{48}

362{49}

363{50}

rAkAm ahaM suhavA suShTutI huve shR^iNotu naH subhagA bodhatu tmanA /
sIvyatv apaH sUchyAchhidyamAnayA dadAtu vIraM shatadAyam ukthyam //1//
yAs te rAke sumatayaH supeshaso yAbhir dadAsi dAshuShe vasUni /
tAbhir no adya sumanA upAgahi sahasrApoSham subhage rarANA //2//
364{51}

devAnAM patnIr ushatIr avantu naH prAvantu nas tujaye vAjasAtaye /

uta gnA vyantu devapatnIr indrANy agnAyy ashvinI rAT /
A rodasI varunAnI shR^iNotu vyantu devIr ya R^itur janInAm //2//
365{52}

yathA vR^ikShaM ashanir vishvAhA hanty aprati /
evAham adya kitavAn akShair badhyAsam aprati //1//
turANAm aturANAM vishAm avarjuShINAm /
samaitu vishvato bhago antarhastaM kR^itaM mama //2//
IDe agniM svAvasuM namobhir iha prasakto vi chayat kR^itaM naH /
rathair iva pra bhare vAjayadbhiH pradakShiNaM marutAM stomam R^idhyAm //3//
vayaM jayema tvayA yujA vR^itam asmAkam aMsham ud ava bharebhare /
asmabhyam indra varIyaH sugaM kR^idhi pra shatrUNAM maghavan vR^iShNyA ruja //4//
ajaiShaM tvA saMlikhitam ajaiSham uta saMrudham /
aviM vR^iko yathA mathad evA mathnAmi te kR^itam //5//
uta prahAm atidIvA jayati kR^itam iva shvaghnI vi chinoti kAle /
yo devakAmo na dhanam ruNaddhi sam it taM rAyaH sR^ijati svadhAbhiH //6//
gobhiSh TaremAmatiM durevAM yavena vA kShudhaM puruhUta vishve /
vayaM rAjasu prathamA dhanAny ariShTAso vR^ijanIbhir jayema //7//
kR^itaM me dakShiNe haste jayo me savya AhitaH /
gojid bhUyAsam ashvajid dhanaMjayo hiraNyajit //8//
akShAH phalavatIm dyuvaM datta gAM kShIriNIm iva /
saM mA kR^itasya dhArayA dhanuH snAvneva nahyata //9//
366{53}

bR^ihaspatir naH pari pAtu pashchAd utottarasmAd adharAd aghayoH /
indraH purastAd uta madhyato naH sakhA sakhibhyo varIyaH kR^iNotu //1//
367{54}

saMj~nAnaM naH svebhiH saMj~nAnam araNebhiH /

mA ghoShA ut sthur bahule vinirhate meShuH paptad indrasyAhany Agate //2//
368{55}

amutrabhUyAd adhi yad yamasya bR^ihaspate abhishaster amu~nchaH /
praty auhatAm ashvinA mR^ityum asmad devAnAm agne bhiShajA shachIbhiH //1//
saM krAmataM mA jahItaM sharIraM prANApAnau te sayujAv iha stAm /
shataM jIva sharado vardhamAno 'gniSh Te gopA adhipA vasiShThaH //2//
Ayur yat te atihitaM parAchair apAnaH prANaH punar A tAv itAm /
agniSh Tad AhAr nirR^iter upasthAt tad Atmani punar A veshayAmi te //3//
memaM prANo hAsIn mo apAno 'vahAya parA gAt /

ayaM jarimnaH shevadhir ariShTa iha vardhatAm //5//

ud vayaM tamasas pari rohanto nAkam uttamam /
devaM devatrA sUryam aganma jyotir uttamam //7//
369{56}

ete sadasi rAjato yaj~naM deveShu yachhataH //1//

371{58}

tirashchirAjer asitAt pR^idAkoH pari saMbhR^itam /
tat ka~NkaparvaNo viSham iyaM vIrud anInashat //1//
iyaM vIrun madhujAtA madhushchun madhulA madhUH /
sA vihrutasya bheShajy atho mashakajambhanI //2//
yato daShTaM yato dhItaM tatas te nir hvayAmasi /
arbhasya tR^ipradaMshino mashakasyArasaM viSham //3//

arasasya sharkoTasya nIchInasyopasarpataH /
viShaM hy asyAdiShy atho enam ajIjabham //5//

atha kiM pApayA 'muyA puchhe bibharShy arbhakam //6//

sarve bhala bravAtha shArkoTam arasaM viSham //7//
ya ubhAbhyAM praharasi puchhena chAsyena cha /
Asye3 na te viShaM kim u te puchhadhAv asat //8//
372{59}

ubhe id asyobhe asya rAjata ubhe yatete ubhe asya puShyataH //2//
373{60}

indrAvaruNA sutapAv imaM sutaM somaM pibataM madyaM dhR^itavratau /

374{61}

yo naH shapAd ashapataH shapato yash cha naH shapAt /

375{62}

UrjaM bibhrad vasuvanih sumedhA aghoreNa chakShuShA mitriyeNa /
gR^ihAn aimi sumanA vandamAno ramadhvam mA bibhIta mat //1//
ime gR^ihA mayobhuva UrjasvantaH payasvantaH /

yeShAm adhyeti pravasan yeShu saumanaso bahuH /

atho annasya kIlAla upahUto gR^iheShu //5//

376{63}

377{64}

378{65}

380{67}

385{73}

386{74}

387{75}

{76}
 {1}

388{77}

samiddho agnir vR^iShaNA rathI divas tapto gharmo duhyate vAm iShe madhu /
vayaM hi vAM purudamAso ashvinA havAmahe sadhamAdeShu kAravaH //1//

duhyante nUnaM vR^iShaNeha dhenavo dasrA madanti vedhasaH //2//
ivAhAkR^itaH shuchir deveShu yaj~no yo ashvinosh chamaso devapAnaH /
tam u vishve amR^itAso juShANA gandharvasya praty AsnA rihanti //3//
yad usriyAsv AhutaM ghR^itaM payo 'yaM sa vAm ashvinA bhAga A gatam /
mAdhvI dhartArA vidathasya satpatI taptaM gharmaM pibatam divaH //4//
tapto vAM gharmo nakShatu svahotA pra vAm adhvaryush charatu payasvAn /
madhor dugdhasyAshvinA tanAyA vItaM pAtaM payasa usriyAyAH //5//

hi~NkR^iNvatI vasupatnI vasUnAM vatsam ichhantI manasA nyAgan /
duhAm ashvibhyAM payo aghnyeyaM sA vardhatAM mahate saubhagAya //8//
juShTo damUnA atithir duroNa imaM no yaj~nam upa yAhi vidvAn /
vishvA agne abhiyujo vihatya shatrUyatAm A bharA bhojanAni //9//

addhi tR^iNam aghnye vishvadAnIM piba shuddham udakam AcharantI //11//
389{78}

apachitAM lohinInAM kR^iShNA mAteti shushruma /
muner devasya mUlena sarvA vidhyAmi tA aham //1//

390{79}

prajAvatIH sUyavase rushantIH shuddhA apaH suprapANe pibantIH /

391{80}

A susrasaH susraso asatIbhyo asattarAH /

{81}
 {1}

394{84}

395{85}

paurNamAsI jigAya /

396{86}

397{87}

abhy archata suShTutiM gavyam Ajim asmAsu bhadrA draviNAni dhatta /

398{88}

405{95}

412{102}

413{103}

423{113}

424{114}

idam ugrAya babhrave namo yo akSheShu tanUvashI /
ghR^itena kaliM shikShAmi sa no mR^iDAtIdR^ishe //1//
ghR^itam apsarAbhyo vaha tvam agne pAMsUn akShebhyaH sikatA apash cha /
yathAbhagaM havyadAtiM juShANA madanti devA ubhayAni havyA //2//

tA me hastau saM sR^ijantu ghR^itena sapatnaM me kitavam randhayantu //3//
AdinavaM pratidIvne ghR^itenAsmA.N abhi kShara /
vR^ikSham ivAshanyA jahi yo asmAn pratidIvyati //4//

sa no devo havir idaM juShANo gandharvebhiH sadhamAdaM madema //5//

tebhyo va indavo haviShA vidhema vayaM syAma patayo rayINAm //6//

425{115}

agna indrash cha dAshuShe hato vR^itrANy aprati /

426{116}

427{117}

430{120}

432{122}

433{123}
