uror varIyo varuNas te kR^iNotu jayantaM tvAnu devA madantu //1//
434
Book:
8

antakAya mR^ityave namaH prAnA apAnA iha te ramantAm /
ihAyam astu puruShaH sahAsunA sUryasya bhAge amR^itasya loke //1//
ud enaM bhago agrabhId ud enaM somo aMshumAn /
ud enaM maruto devA ud indrAgnI svastaye //2//
iha te 'sur iha prANa ihAyur iha te manaH /
ut tvA nirR^ityAH pAshebhyo daivyA vachA bharAmasi //3//
ut krAmAtaH puruSha mAva patthA mR^ityoH paDvISham avamu~nchamAnaH /
mA chhitthA asmAl lokAd agneH sUryasya saMdR^ishaH //4//
tubhyaM vAtaH pavatAM mAtarishvA tubhyaM varShantv amR^itAny ApaH /
sUryas te tanve3 shaM tapAti tvAm mR^ityur dayatAM mA pra meShThAH //5//
udyAnaM te puruSha nAvayAnaM jIvAtuM te dakShatAtiM kR^inomi /
A hi rohemam amR^itaM sukhaM ratham atha jirvir vidatham A vadAsi //6//
mA te manas tatra gAn mA tiro bhUn mA jIvebhyaH pra mado mAnu gAH pitR^In /
vishve devA abhi rakShantu tveha //7//
mA gatAnAm A dIdhIthA ye nayanti parAvatam /
A roha tamaso jyotir ehy A te hastau rabhAmahe //8//
shyAmash cha tvA mA shabalash cha preShitau yamasya yau pathirakShI shvAnau /
arvA~N ehi mA vi dIdhyo mAtra tiShThaH parA~NmanAH //9//
maitaM panthAm anu gA bhIma eSha yena pUrvaM neyatha taM bravImi /
tama etat puruSha mA pra patthA bhayaM parastAd abhayaM te arvAk //10// {1}

rakShantu tvAgnayo ye apsv a1ntA rakShatu tvA manuShyA3 yam indhate /
vaishvAnaro rakShatu jAtavedA divyas tvA mA pra dhAg vidyutA saha //11//
mA tvA kravyAd abhi maMstArAt saMkasukAch chara rakShatu tvA dyau rakShatu /
pR^ithivI sUryash cha tvA rakShatAM chandramAsh cha /
antarikShaM rakShatu devahetyAH //12//
bodhash cha tvA pratibodhash cha rakShatAm asvapnash cha tvAnavadrANash cha rakShatAm /
gopAyaMsh cha tvA jAgR^ivish cha rakShatAm //13//
te tvA rakShantu te tvA gopAyantu tebhyo namas tebhyaH svAhA //14//
jIvebhyas tvA samude vAyur indro dhAtA dadhAtu savitA trAyamANaH /
mA tvA prANo balaM hAsId asuM te 'nu hvayAmasi //15//
mA tvA jambhaH saMhanur mA tamo vidan mA jihvA barhis pramayuH kathA syAH /
ut tvAdityA vasavo bharantUd indrAgnI svastaye //16//
ut tvA dyaur ut pR^ithivy ut prajApatir agrabhIt /
ut tvA mR^ityor oShadhayaH somarAj~nIr apIparan //17//
ayaM devA ihaivAstv ayaM mAmutra gAd itaH /
imaM sahasravIryeNa mR^ityor ut pArayAmasi //18//
ut tvA mR^ityor apIparaM saM dhamantu vayodhasaH /
mA tvA vyastakeshyo3 mA tvAgharudo rudan //19//
AhArSham avidaM tvA punar AgAH punarNavaH /
sarvA~Nga sarvaM te chakShuH sarvam Ayush cha te 'vidam //20//
vy avAt te jyotir abhUd apa tvat tamo akramIt /
apa tvan mR^ityuM nirR^itim apa yakShmaM ni dadhmasi //21// {2}

435

A rabhasvemAm amR^itasya shnuShTim achhidyamAnA jaradaShTir astu te /
asuM ta AyuH punar A bharAmi rajas tamo mopa gA mA pra meShThAh //1//
jIvatAM jyotir abhyehy arvA~N A tvA harAmi shatashAradAya /
avamu~nchan mR^ityupAshAn ashastiM drAghIya AyuH prataraM te dadhAmi //2//
vAtAt te prAnam avidaM sUryAch chakShur ahaM tava /
yat te manas tvayi tad dhArayAmi saM vitsvA~Ngair vada jihvayAlapan //3//
prANena tvA dvipadAM chatuShpadAm agnim iva jAtam abhi saM dhamAmi /
namas te mR^ityo chakShuShe namaH prANAya te 'karam //4//
ayaM jIvatu mA mR^itemaM sam IrayAmasi /
kR^iNomy asmai bheShajaM mR^ityo mA puruShaM vadhIH //5//
jIvalAM naghAriShAM jIvantIm oShadhIm aham /
trAyamANAM sahamAnAM sahasvatIm iha huve 'smA ariShTatAtaye //6//
adhi brUhi mA rabhathAh sR^ijemaM tavaiva sant sarvahAyAH ihAstu /
bhavAsharvau mR^iDataM sharma yachhatam apasidhya duritaM dhattam AyuH //7//
asmai mR^ityo adhi brUhImaM dayasvod ito 'yam etu /
ariShTaH sarvA~NgaH sushruj jarasA shatahAyana AtmanA bhujam ashnutAm //8//
devAnAM hetih pari tvA vR^iNaktu pArayAmi tvA rajasa ut tvA mR^ityor apIparam /
ArAd agniM kravyAdaM nirUhaM jIvAtave te paridhiM dadhAmi //9//
yat te niyAnaM rajasaM mR^ityo anavadharShyam /
patha imaM tasmAd rakShanto brahmAsmai varma kR^iNmasi //10// {3}

kR^iNomi te prANApAnau jarAM mR^ityuM dIrgham AyuH svasti /
vaivasvatena prahitAn yamadUtAMsh charato 'pa sedhAmi sarvAn //11//
ArAd arAtiM nirR^itiM paro grAhiM kravyAdaH pishAchAn /
rakSho yat sarvaM durbhUtaM tat tama ivApa hanmasi //12//
agneSh Ta prAnam amR^itAd AyuShmato vanve jAtavedasaH /
yathA na riShyA amR^itaH sajUr asas tat te kR^iNomi tad u te sam R^idhyatAm //13//
shive te stAM dyAvApR^ithivI asaMtApe abhishriyau /
shaM te sUrya A tapatu shaM vAto vAtu te hR^ide /
shivA abhi kSharantu tvApo divyAH payasvatIH //14//
shivAs te santv oShadhaya ut tvAhArSham adharasyA uttarAM pR^ithivIm abhi /
tatra tvAdityau rakShatAM sUryAchandramasAv ubhA //15//
yat te vAsaH paridhAnaM yAM nIviM kR^iNuShe tvam /
shivaM te tanve3 tat kR^iNmaH saMsparshe 'drUkShNam astu te //16//
yat kShureNa marchayatA sutejasA vaptA vapasi keshashmashru /
shubhaM mukhaM mA na AyuH pra moShIH //17//
shivau te stAM vrIhiyavAv abalAsAv adomadhau /
etau yakShmaM vi bAdhete etau mu~nchato amhasaH //18//
yad ashnAsi yat pibasi dhAnyaM kR^iShyAH payaH /
yad Adya1M yad anAdyaM sarvaM te annam aviShaM kR^iNomi //19//
ahne cha tvA rAtraye chobhAbhyAM pari dadmasi /
arAyebhyo jighatsubhya imaM me pari rakShata //20// {4}

shataM te 'yutaM hAyanAn dve yuge trINi chatvAri kR^iNmaH /
indrAgnI vishve devAs te 'nu manyantAm ahR^iNIyamAnAH //21//
sharade tvA hemantAya vasantAya grIShmAya pari dadmasi /
varShANi tubhyaM syonAni yeShu vardhanta oShadhIH //22//
mR^ityur Ishe dvipadAM mR^ityur Ishe chatuShpadAm /
tasmAt tvAM mR^ityor gopater ud bharAmi sa mA bibheH //23//
so 'riShTa na mariShyasi na mariShyasi mA bibheH /
na vai tatra mriyante no yanti adhamaM tamaH //24//
sarvo vai tatra jIvati gaur ashvaH puruShaH pashuH /
yatredaM brahma kriyate paridhir jIvanAya kam //25//
pari tvA pAtu samAnebhyo 'bhichArAt sabandhubhyaH /
amamrir bhavAmR^ito 'tijIvo mA te hAsiShur asavaH sharIram //26//
ye mR^ityava ekashataM yA nAShTrA atitAryAH /
mu~nchantu tasmAt tvAM devA agner vaishvAnarAd adhi //27//
agneH sharIram asi pArayiShNu rakShohAsi sapatnahA /
atho amIvachAtanaH pUtudrur nAma bheShajam //28// {5}

436

rakShohaNaM vAjinam A jigharmi mitraM prathiShTham upa yAmi sharma /
shishAno agniH kratubhiH samiddhaH sa no divA sa riShaH pAtu naktam //1//
ayodaMShTro archiShA yAtudhAnAn upa spR^isha jAtavedaH samiddhaH /
A jihvayA mUradevAn rabhasva kravyAdo vR^iShTvApi dhatsvAsan //2//
ubhobhayAvinn upa dhehi daMShTrau hiMsraH shishAno 'varaM paraM cha /
utAntarikShe pari yAhy agne jambhaiH saM dhehy abhi yAtudhAnAn //3//
agne tvachaM yAtudhAnasya bhindhi hiMsrAshanir harasA hantv enam /
pra parvANi jAtavedaH shR^iNIhi kravyAt kraviShNur vi chinotv enam //4//
yatredAnIM pashyasi jAtavedas tiShThantam agna uta vA charantam /
utAntarikShe patantaM yAtudhAnaM tam astA vidhya sharvA shishAnaH //5//
yaj~nair iShUH saMnamamAno agne ivAchA shalyA.N ashanibhir dihAnaH /
tAbhir vidhya hR^idaye yAtudhAnAn pratIcho bAhUn prati bha~Ngdhy eShAm //6//
utArabdhAnt spR^inuhi jAtaveda utArebhANA.N R^iShTibhir yAtudhAnAn /
agne pUrvo ni jahi shoshuchAna AmAdaH kShvi~NkAs tam adantv enIH //7//
iha pra brUhi yatamaH so agne yAtudhAno ya idaM kR^iNoti /
tam A rabhasva samidhA yaviShTha nR^ichakShasash chakShuShe randhayainam //8//
tIkShNenAgne chakShuShA rakSha yaj~naM prA~nchaM vasubhyaH pra Naya prachetaH /
hiMsraM rakShAMsy abhi shoshuchAnaM mA tvA dabhan yAtudhAnA nR^ichakShaH //9//
nR^ichakShA rakShaH pari pashya vikShu tasya trINi prati shR^iNIhy agrA /
tasyAgne pR^iShTIr harasA shR^iNIhi tredhA mUlaM yAtudhAnasya vR^ishcha //10// {6}

trir yAtudhAnaH prasitiM ta etv R^itaM yo agne anR^itena hanti /
tam archiShA sphUrjayan jAtavedaH samakSham enam gR^iNate ni yu~Ngdhi //11//
yad agne adya mithunA shapAto yad vAchas tR^iShTaM janayanta rebhAH /
manyor manasaH sharavyA3 jAyate yA tayA vidhya hR^idaye yAtudhAnAn //12//
parA shR^iNIhi tapasA yAtudhAnAn parAgne rakSho harasA shR^iNIhi /
parArchiShA mUradevAn chhR^iNIhi parAsutR^ipaH shoshuchataH shR^iNIhi //13//
parAdya devA vR^ijinaM shR^iNantu pratyag enaM shapathA yantu sR^iShTAH /
vAchAstenaM sharava R^ichhantu marman vishvasyaitu prasitiM yAtudhAnaH //14//
yaH pauruSheyeNa kraviShA sama~Nkte yo ashvyena pashunA yAtudhAnaH /
yo aghnyAyA bharati kShIram agne teShAM shIrShANi harasApi vR^ishcha //15//
viShaM gavAM yAtudhAnA bharantAm A vR^ishchantAm aditaye durevAH /
paraiNAn devaH savitA dadAtu parA bhAgam oShadhInAM jayantAm //16//
saMvatsarINaM paya usriyAyAs tasya mAshId yAtudhAno nR^ichakShaH /
pIyUSham agne yatamas titR^ipsAt taM pratya~ncham archiShA vidhya marmaNi //17//
sanAd agne mR^iNasi yAtudhAnAn na tvA rakShAMsi pR^itanAsu jigyuH /
sahamUrAn anu daha kravyAdo mA te hetyA mukShata daivyAyAH //18//
tvaM no agne adharAd udaktas tvaM pashchAd uta rakShA purastAt /
prati tye te ajarAsas tapiShThA aghashaMsaM shoshuchato dahantu //19//
pashchAt purastAd adharAd utottarAt kaviH kAvyena pari pAhy agne /
sakhA sakhAyam ajaro jarimne agne martAm? amartyas tvaM naH //20// {7}

tad agne chakShuH prati dhehi rebhe shaphArujo yena pashyasi yAtudhAnAn /
atharvavaj jyotiShA daivyena satyaM dhUrvantam achitaM nyoSha //21//
pari tvAgne puraM vayaM vipraM sahasya dhImahi /
dhR^iShadvarNaM divedive hantAraM bha~NgurAvataH //22//
viSheNa bha~NgurAvataH prati sma rakShaso jahi /
agne tigmena shochiShA tapuragrAbhir archibhiH //23//
vi jyotiShA bR^ihatA bhAty agnir Avir vishvAni kR^iNute mahitvA /
prAdevIr mAyAH sahate durevAH shishIte shR^i~Nge rakShobhyo vinikShe //24//
ye te shR^i~Nge ajare jAtavedas tigmahetI brahmasaMshite /
tAbhyAM durhArdam abhidAsantaM kimIdinaM /
pratya~ncham archiShA jAtavedo vi nikShva //25//
agnI rakShAMsi sedhati shukrashochir amartyaH /
shuchiH pAvaka IDyaH //26// {8}

437

indrAsomA tapataM rakSha ubjataM ny arpayataM vR^iShaNA tamovR^idhaH /
parA shR^iNItam achito ny oShataM hataM nudethAM ni shishItam attriNaH //1//
indrAsomA sam aghashaMsam abhy aghaM tapur yayastu charur agnimA.N iva /
brahmadviShe kravyAde ghorachakShase dveSho dhattam anavAyaM kimIdine //2//
indrAsomA duShkR^ito vavre antar anArambhaNe tamasi pra vidhyatam /
yato naiShAM punar ekash chanodayat tad vAm astu sahase manyumach chhavaH //3//
indrAsomA vartayataM divo vadhaM saM pR^ithivyA aghashaMsAya tarhaNam /
ut takShataM svaryaM1 parvatebhyo yena rakSho vAvR^idhAnaM nijUrvathaH //4//
indrAsomA vartayataM divas pary agnitaptebhir yuvam ashmahanmabhiH /
tapurvadhebhir ajarebhir attriNo ni parshAne vidhyataM yantu nisvaram //5//
indrAsomA pari vAM bhUtu vishvata iyaM matiH kakShyAshveva vAjinA /
yAM vAM hotrAM parihinomi medhayemA brahmANi nR^ipatI iva jinvatam //6//
prati smarethAM tujayadbhir evair hataM druho rakShaso bha~NgurAvataH /
indrAsomA duShkR^ite mA sugaM bhUd yo mA kadA chid abhidAsati druhuH //7//
yo mA pAkena manasA charantam abhichaShTe anR^itebhir vachobhiH /
Apa iva kAshinA samgR^ibhItA asann astv asataH indra vaktA //8//
ye pAkashaMsaM viharanta evair ye vA bhadraM dUShayanti svadhAbhiH /
ahaye vA tAn pradadAtu soma A vA dadhAtu nirR^iter upaShThe //9//
yo no rasaM dipsati pitvo agne ashvAnAM gavAM yas tanUnAm /
ripu stena steyakR^id dabhram etu ni Sha hIyatAM tanvA3 tanA cha //10// {9}

paraH so astu tanvA3 tanA cha tisraH pR^ithivIr adho astu vishvAH /
prati shuShyatu yasho asya devA yo mA divA dipsati yash cha naktam //11//
suvij~nAnaM chikituShe janAya sach chAsach cha vachasI paspR^idhAte /
tasyor yat satyaM yatarad R^ijIyas tad it somo 'vati hanty asat //12//
na vA u somo vR^ijinaM hinoti na kShatriyam mithuyA dhArayantam /
hanti rakSho hanty Asad vadantam ubhAv indrasya prasitau shayAte //13//
yadi vAham anR^itadevo asmi moghaM vA devA.N apyUhe agne /
kim asmabhyaM jAtavedo hR^iNIShe droghavAchas te nirR^ithaM sachantAm //14//
adyA murIya yadi yAtudhAno asmi yadi vAyus tatapa puruShasya /
adhA sa vIrair dashabhir vi yUyA yo mA moghaM yAtudhAnety Aha //15//
yo mAyAtuM yAtudhAnety Aha yo vA rakShAH shichir asmIty Aha /
indras taM hantu mahatA vadhena vishvasya jantor adhamas padIShTa //16//
pra yA jigAti khargaleva naktam apa druhus tanvaM1 gUhamAnA /
vavram anantam ava sA padIShTia grAvANo ghnantu rakShasa upabdaiH //17//
vi tiShThadhvam maruto vikShv ì3chhata gR^ibhAyata rakShasaH saM pinaShTan /
vayo ye bhUtvA patayanti naktabhir ye vA ripo dadhire deve adhvare //18//
pra vartaya divo 'shmAnam indra somashitaM maghavant saM shishAdhi /
prAkto apAkto adharAd udakto 'bhi jahi rakShasaH parvatena //19//
eta u tye patayanti shvayAtava indraM dipsanti dipsavo 'dAbhyam /
shishIte shakraH pishunebhyo vadhaM nunaM sR^ijad ashaniM yAtumadbhyaH //20// {10}

indro yAtUnAm abhavat parAsharo havirmathInAm abhy A3vivAsatAm /
abhId u shakraH parashur yathA vanaM pAtreva bhindant sata etu rakShasaH //21//
ulUkayAtuM shushulUkayAtuM jahi shvayAtum uta kokayAtum /
suparNayAtum uta gR^idhrayAtuM dR^iShadeva pra mR^iNa rakSha indra //22//
mA no rakSho abhi naD yAtumAvad apochhantu mithunA ye kimIdinaH /
pR^ithivI naH pArthivAt pAtv aMhaso 'ntarikShaM divyAt pAtv asmAn //23//
indra jahi pumAMsaM yAtudhAnam uta striyaM mAyayA shAshadAnAm /
vigrIvAso mUradevA R^idantu mA te dR^ishant sUryam uchcharantam //24//
prati chakShva vi chakShvendrash cha soma jAgR^itam /
rakShobhyo vadham asyatam ashaniM yAtumadbhyaH //25// {11}

438

ayaM pratisaro maNir vIro vIrAya badhyate /
vIryavAnt sapatnahA shUravIraH paripANaH suma~NgalaH //1//
ayaM maNiH sapatnahA suvIraH sahasvAn vAjI sahamAna ugraH /
pratyak kR^ityA dUShayann eti vIraH //2//
anenendro maNinA vR^itram ahann anenAsurAn parAbhAvayan manIShI /
anenAjayad dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe ime anenAjayat pradishash chatasraH //3//
ayaM srAktyo maNiH pratIvartaH pratisaraH /
ojasvAn vimR^idho vashI so asmAn pAtu sarvataH //4//
tad agnir Aha tad u soma Aha bR^ihaspatiH savitA tad indraH /
te me devAH purohitAH pratIchIH kR^ityAH pratisarair ajantu //5//
antar dadhe dyAvApR^ithivI utAhar uta sUryam /
te me devAH purohitAH pratIchIH kR^ityAH pratisarair ajantu //6//
ye srAktyaM maNiM janA varmANi kR^iNvate /
sUrya iva divam Aruhya vi kR^ityA bAdhate vashI //7//
srAktyena maNinA R^iShiNeva manIShiNA /
ajaiShaM sarvAH pR^itanA vi mR^idho hanmi rakShasaH //8//
yAH kR^ityA A~NgirasIr yAH kR^ityA AsurIr yAH /
kR^ityAH svayaMkR^itA yA u chAnyebhir AbhR^itAH /
ubhayIs tAH parA yantu parAvato navatiM nAvyA3 ati //9//
asmai maNiM varma badhnantu devA indro viShNuH savitA rudro agniH /
prajApatiH parameShThI virAD vaishvAnara R^iShayash cha sarve //10// {12}

uttamo asy oShadhInAm anaDvAn jagatAm iva vyAghraH shvapadAm iva /
yam aichhAmAvidAma taM pratispAshanam antitam //11//
sa id vyAghro bhavaty atho siMho atho vR^iShA /
atho sapatnakarshano yo bibhartImaM maNim //12//
nainaM ghnanty apsaraso na gandharvA na martyAH /
sarvA disho vi rAjati yo bibhartImaM maNim //13//
kashyapas tvAm asR^ijata kashyapas tvA sam airayat /
abibhas tvendro mAnuShe bibhrat saMshreShiNe 'jayat /
maNiM sahasravIryaM varma devA akR^iNvata //14//
yas tvA kR^ityAbhir yas tvA dIkShAbhir yaj~nair yas tvA jighAMsati /
pratyak tvam indra taM jahi vajreNa shataparvaNA //15//
ayam id vai pratIvarta ojasvAn saMjayo maNiH /
prajAM dhanaM cha rakShatu paripANaH suma~NgalaH //16//
asapatnaM no adharAd asapatnaM na uttarAt /
indrAsapatnaM naH pashchAj jyotiH shUra puras kR^idhi //17//
varma me dyAvApR^ithivI varmAhar varma sUryaH /
varma ma indrash chAgnish cha varma dhAtA dadhAtu me //18//
aindrAgnaM varma bahulaM yad ugraM vishve devA nAtividhyanti sarve /
tan me tanvaM trAyatAM sarvato bR^ihad AyuShmAM jaradaShTir yathAsAni //19//
A mArukShad devamaNir mahyA ariShTatAtaye /
imaM methim abhisaMvishadhvaM tanUpAnaM trivarUtham ojase //20//
asminn indro ni dadhAtu nR^imNam imaM devAso abhisaMvishadhvam /
dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAyAyuShmAn jaradaShTir yathAsat //21//
svastidA vishAM patir vR^itrahA vimR^idho vashI /
indro badhnAtu te maNiM jigIvA.N aparAjitaH /
somapA abhaya~Nkaro vR^iShA /
sa tvA rakShatu sarvato divA naktaM cha vishvataH //22// {13}

439

yau te mAtonmamArja jAtAyAH pativedanau /
durNAmA tatra mA gR^idhad aliMsha uta vatsapaH //1//
palAlAnupalAlau sharkuM kokaM malimluchaM palIjakam /
AshreShaM vavrivAsasam R^ikShagrIvaM pramIlinam //2//
mA saM vR^ito mopa sR^ipa UrU mAva sR^ipo 'ntarA /
kR^iNomy asyai bheShajaM bajaM durNAmachAtanam //3//
durNAmA cha sunAmA chobhA samvR^itam ichhataH /
arAyAn apa hanmaH sunAmA straiNam ichhatAm //4//
yaH kR^iShNaH keshy asura stambaja uta tuNDikaH /
arAyAn asyA muShkAbhyAM bhaMsaso 'pa hanmasi //5//
anujighraM pramR^ishantaM kravyAdam uta reriham /
arAyAM chhvakiShkiNo bajaH pi~Ngo anInashat //6//
yas tvA svapne nipadyate bhrAtA bhUtvA piteva cha /
bajas tAnt sahatAm itaH klIbarUpAMs tirItinaH //7//
yas tvA svapantIM tsarati yas tvA dipsati jAgratIm /
chhAyAm iva pra tAnt sUryaH parikrAmann anInashat //8//
yaH kR^iNoti mR^itavatsAm avatokAm imAM striyam /
tam oShadhe tvaM nAshayAsyAH kamalam a~njivam //9//
ye shAlAH parinR^ityanti sAyaM gardabhanAdinaH /
kusUlA ye cha kukShilAH kakubhAH karumAH srimAH /
tAn oShadhe tvaM gandhena viShUchInAn vi nAshaya //10// {14}

ye kukundhAH kukirabhAH kR^ittIr dUrshAni bibhrati /
klIbA iva pranR^ityanto vane ye kurvate ghoShaM tAn ito nAshayAmasi //11//
ye sUryaM na titikShanta Atapantam amuM divaH /
arAyAn bastavAsino durgandhIMl lohitAsyAn makakAn nAshayAmasi //12//
ya AtmAnam atimAtram aMsa AdhAya bibhrati /
strINAM shroNipratodina indra rakShAMsi nAshaya //13//
ye pUrve badhvo3 yanti haste shR^i~NgAni bibhrataH /
ApAkesthAH prahAsina stambe ye kurvate jyotis tAn ito nAshayAmasi //14//
yeShAm pashchAt prapadAni puraH pArShNIH puro mukhA /
khalajAH shakadhUmajA uruNDA ye cha maTmaTAH kumbhamuShkA ayAshavaH /
tAn asyA brahmaNas pate pratIbodhena nAshaya //15//
paryastAkShA apracha~NkashA astraiNAH santu paNDagAH /
ava bheShaja pAdaya ya imAM saMvivR^itsaty apatiH svapatiM striyam //16//
uddharShiNaM munikeshaM jambhayantaM marImR^isham /
upeShantam udumbalaM tuNDelam uta shAluDam /
padA pra vidhya pArShNyA sthAlIM gaur iva spandanA //17//
yas te garbhaM pratimR^ishAj jAtaM vA mArayAti te /
pi~Ngas tam ugradhanvA kR^iNotu hR^idayAvidham //18//
ye amno jatAn mArayanti sUtikA anusherate /
strIbhAgAn pi~Ngo gandharvAn vAto abhram ivAjatu //19//
parisR^iShTaM dharayatu yad dhitaM mAva pAdi tat /
garbhaM ta ugrau rakShatAm bheShajau nIvibhAryaù //20// {15}

pavInasAt ta~NgalvA3ch chhAyakAd uta nagnakAt /
prajAyai patye tvA pi~NgaH pari pAtu kimIdinaH //21//
dvyAsyAch chaturakShAt pa~nchapadAd ana~NgureH /
vR^intAd abhi prasarpataH pari pAhi varIvR^itAt //22//
ya AmaM mAmsam adanti pauruSheyaM cha ye kraviH /
garbhAn khAdanti keshavAs tAn ito nAshayAmasi //23//
ye sUryAt parisarpanti snuSheva shvashurAd adhi /
bajash cha teShAM pi~Ngash cha hR^idaye 'dhi ni vidhyatAm //24//
pi~Nga rakSha jAyamAnaM mA pumAMsaM striyaM kran /
ANDAdo garbhAn mA dabhan bAdhasvetaH kimIdinaH //25//
aprajAstvaM mArtavatsam Ad rodam agham Avayam /
vR^ikShAd iva srajam kR^itvApriye prati mu~ncha tat //26// {16}

440

yA babhravo yAsh cha shukrA rohiNIr uta pR^ishnayaH /
asiknIH kR^iShNA oShadhIH sarvA achhAvadAmasi //1//
trAyantAm imaM purusaM yakShmAd deveShitAd adhi /
yAsAm dyauH pitA pR^ithivI mAtA samudro mUlaM vIrudhAM babhUva //2//
Apo agraM divyA oShadhayaH /
tAs te yakShmam enasya1m a~NgAda~NgAd anInashan //3//
prastR^iNatI stambinIr ekashu~NgAH pratanvatIr oShadhIr A vadAmi /
aMshumatIH kaNDinIr yA vishAkhA hvayAmi te vIrudho vaishvadevIr ugrAH puruShajIvanIH //4//
yad vaH sahaH sahamAnA vIryaM1 yach cha vo balam /
tenemam asmAd yakShmAt puruShaM mu~nchatauShadhIr atho kR^iNomi bheShajam //5//
jIvalAM naghAriShAM jIvantIm oShadhIm aham /
arundhatIm unnayantIM puShpAm madhumatIm iha huve 'smA ariShTatAtaye //6//
ihA yantu prachetaso medinIr vachaso mama /
yathemaM pArayAmasi puruShaM duritAd adhi //7//
agner ghAso apAM garbho yA rohanti punarNavAH /
dhruvAH sahasranAmnIr bheShajIH santv AbhR^itAH //8//
avakolbA udakAtmAna oShadhayaH /
vyr?Shantu duritaM tIkShNashR^i~NgyaH //9//
unmu~nchantIr vivaruNA ugrA yA viShadUShanIH /
atho balAsanAshanIH kR^ityAdUShaNIsh cha yAs tA ihA yantv oShadhIh //10// {17}

apakrItAH sahIyasIr vIrudho yA abhiShTutAH /
trAyantAm asmin grAme gAm ashvaM puruShaM pashum //11//
madhuman mUlaM madhumad agram AsAm madhuman madhyaM vIrudhAM babhUva /
madhumat parNaM madhumat puShpam AsAM madhoH sambhaktA amR^itasya bhakSho ghR^itam annaM duhratAM gopurogavam //12//
yAvatIH kiyatIsh chemAH pR^ithivyAm adhy oShadhIH /
tA mA sahasraparNyo mR^ityor mu~nchantv aMhasaH //13//
vaiyAghro maNir vIrudhAM trAyamAno 'bhishastipAh /
amIvAH sarvA rakShAMsy apa hantv adhi dUram asmat //14//
siMhasyeva stanathoH saM vijante 'gner iva vijante AbhR^itAbhyaH /
gavAM yakShmaH puruShANAM vIrudbhir atinutto nAvyA etu srotyAH //15//
mumuchAnA oShadhayo 'gner vaishvAnarAd adhi /
bhUmiM saMtavatIr ita yAsAM rAjA vanaspatiH //16//
yA rohanty A~NgirasIH parvateShu sameShu cha /
tA naH payasvatIH shivA oShadhIH santu shaM hR^ide //17//
yAsh chAhaM veda vIrudho yAsh cha pashyAmi chakShuShA /
aj~nAtA jAnImash cha yA yAsu vidma cha saMbhR^itam //18//
sarvAH samagrA oShadhIr bodhantu vachaso mama /
yathemaM pArayAmasi puruSham duritAd adhi //19//
ashvattho darbho vIrudhAM somo rAjAmR^itaM haviH /
vrIhir yavash cha bheShajau divasi putrAv amartyau //20// {18}

uj jihIdhve stanayaty abhikrandaty oShadhIH /
yadA vaH pR^ishnimAtaraH parjanyo retasAvati //21//
tasyAmR^itasyemaM balaM puruShaM payayAmasi /
atho kR^iNomi bheShajaM yathAsach chhatahAyanaH //22//
varAho veda vIrudhaM nakulo veda bheShajIm /
sarpA gandharvA yA vidus tA asmA avase huve //23//
yAH suparNA A~NgirasIr divyA yA raghato viduH /
vayAMsi haMsA yA vidur yAs cha sarve patatriNaH /
mR^igA yA vidur oShadhIs tA asmA avase huve //24//
yAvatInAm oShadhInAM gAvaH prAshnanty aghnyA yavatInAm ajAvayaH /
tAvatIs tubhyam oShadhIH sharma yachhantv AbhR^itAH //25//
yAvatIShu manuShyA bheShajaM bhiShajo viduH /
tAvatIr vishvabheShajIr A bharAmi tvAm abhi //26//
puShpavatIh prasUmatIH phalinIr aphalA uta /
saMmAtara iva duhrAm asmA ariShTatAtaye //27//
ut tvAhArShaM pa~nchashalAd atho dashashalAd uta /
atho yamasya paDvIshAd vishvasmAd devakilbiShAt //28// {19}

441

indro manthatu manthitA shakraH shUraH puraMdaraH /
yathA hanAma senA amitrANAM sahasrashaH //1//
pUtirajjur upadhmAnI pUtiM senAM kR^iNotv amUm /
dhUmam agnim parAdR^ishyA 'mitrA hR^itsv A dadhatAM bhayam //2//
amUn ashvattha niH shR^iNIhi khAdAmUn khadirAjiram /
tAjadbha~Nga iva bhajantAM hantv enAn vadhako vadhaiH //3//
paruShAn amUn paruShAhvaH kR^iNotu hantv enAn vadhako vadhaiH /
kShipraM shara iva bhajantAM bR^ihajjAlena saMditAH //4//
antarikShaM jAlam AsIj jAladaNDA disho mahIH /
tenAbhidhAya dasyUnAM shakraH senAm apAvapat //5//
bR^ihad dhi jAlaM bR^ihataH shakrasya vAjinIvataH /
tena shatrUn abhi sarvAn ny ùbja yathA na muchyAtai katamash chanaiShAm //6//
bR^ihat te jAlaM bR^ihata indra shUra sahasrArghasya shatavIryasya /
tena shataM sahasram ayutaM nyarbudaM jaghAna shakro dasyUnAm abhidhAya senayA //7//
ayaM loko jAlam AsIch chhakrasya mahato mahAn /
tenAham indrajAlenAmUMs tamasAbhi dadhAmi sarvAn //8//
sedir ugrA vyr?ddhir Artish chAnapavAchanA /
shramas tandrIsh cha mohash cha tair amUn abhi dadhAmi sarvAn //9//
mR^ityave 'mUn pra yachhAmi mR^ityupAshair amI sitAH /
mR^ityor ye aghalA dUtAs tebhya enAn prati nayAmi baddhvA //10// {20}

nayatAmUn mR^ityudUtA yamadUtA apombhata /
paraHsahasrA hanyantAM tR^iNedv enAn matyaM bhavasya //11//
sAdhyA ekaM jAladaNDam udyatya yanty ojasA /
rudrA ekaM vasava ekam Adityair eka udyataH //12//
vishve devAH upariShTAd ubjanto yantv ojasA /
madhyena ghnanto yantu senAm a~Ngiraso mahIm //13//
vanaspatIn vAnaspatyAn oShadhIr uta vIrudhaH /
dvipAch chatuShpAd iShNAmi yathA senAm amUM hanan //14//
gandharvApsarasaH sarpAn devAn puNyajanAn pitR^In /
dR^iShTAn adR^iShTAn iShNAmi yathA senAm amUM hanan //15//
ima uptA mR^ityupAshA yAn Akramya na muchyase /
amuShyA hantu senAyA idaM kUtaM sahasrashaH //16//
gharmaH samiddho agninAyaM homaH sahasrahaH /
bhavash cha pR^ishnibAhush cha sharva senAm amUM hatam //17//
mR^ityor ASham A padyantAM kShudhaM sediM vadham bhayam /
indrash chAkShujAlAbhyAM sharva senAm amUM hatam //18//
parAjitAH pra trasatAmitrA nuttA dhAvata brahmaNA /
bR^ihaspatipranuttAnAM mAmIShAM mochi kash chana //19//
ava padyantAm eShAm AyudhAni mA shakan pratidhAm iShum /
athaiShAM bahu bibhyatAm iShavaH ghnantu marmaNi //20//
saM kroshatAm enAn dyAvApR^ithivI sam antarikShaM saha devatAbhiH /
mA j~nAtAraM mA pratiShThAM vidanta mitho vighnAnA upa yantu mR^ityum //21//
dishash chatasro 'shvataryo devarathasya purodAshAH shaphA antarikSham uddhiH /
dyAvApR^ithivI pakShasI R^itavo 'bhIshavo 'ntardeshAH kimkarA vAk parirathyam //22//
saMvatsaro rathaH parivatsaro rathopastho virAD IShAgnI rathamukham /
indraH savyaShThAsh chandramAH sArathiH //23//
ito jayeto vi jaya saM jaya jaya svAhA /
ime jayantu parAmI jayantAM svAhaibhyo durAhAmIbhyaH /
nIlalohitenAmUn abhyavatanomi //24// {21}

442

kutas tau jAtau katamaH so ardhaH kasmAl lokAt katamasyAH pR^ithivyAH /
vatsau virAjaH salilAd ud aitAM tau tvA pR^ichhAmi katareNa dugdhA //1//
yo akrandayat salilaM mahitvA yoniM kR^itvA tribhujaM shayAnaH /
vatsaH kAmadugho virAjaH sa guhA chakre tanvaH parAchaiH //2//
yAni trINi bR^ihanti yeShAM chaturthaM viyunakti vAcham /
brahmainad vidyAt tapasA vipashchid yasminn ekaM yujyate yasminn ekam //3//
bR^ihataH pari sAmAni ShaShThAt pa~nchAdhi nirmitA /
bR^ihad bR^ihatyA nirmitaM kuto 'dhi bR^ihatI mitA //4//
bR^ihatI pari mAtrAyA mAtur mAtrAdhi nirmitA /
mAyA ha jaj~ne mAyAyA mAyAyA mAtalI pari //5//
vaishvAnarasya pratimopari dyaur yAvad rodasI vibabAdhe agniH /
tataH ShaShThAd Amuto yanti stomA ud ito yanty abhi ShaShTham ahnaH //6//
ShaT tvA pR^ichhAma R^iShayaH kashyapeme tvaM hi yuktaM yuyukShe yogyaM cha /
virAjam Ahur brahmaNaH pitaraM tAM no vi dhehi yatidhA sakhibhyaH //7//
yAM prachyutAm anu yaj~nAh prachyavanta upatiShThanta upatiShThamAnAm /
yasyA vrate prasave yakSham ejati sA virAT R^iShayaH parame vyoman //8//
aprANaiti prANena prANatInAM virAT svarAjam abhy eti pashchAt /
vishvaM mR^ishantIm abhirUpAM virAjaM pashyanti tve na tve pashyanty enAm //9//
ko virAjo mithunatvaM pra veda ka R^itUn ka u kalpam asyAH /
kramAn ko asyAH katidhA vidugdhAn ko asyA dhAma katidhA vyùShTIH //10// {22}

iyam eva sA yA prathamA vyauchhad Asv itarAsu charati praviShTA /
mahAnto asyAM mahimAno antar vadhUr jigAya navagaj janitrI //11//
chhandaHpakShe uShasA pepishAne samAnaM yonim anu saM chareme /
sUryapatnI saM charataH prajAnatI ketumatI ajare bhUriretasA //12//
R^itasya panthAm anu tisra Agus trayo gharmA anu reta AguH /
prajAm ekA jinvaty Urjam ekA rAShTram ekA rakShati devayUnAm //13//
agnIShomAv adadhur yA turIyAsId yaj~nasya pakShAv R^iShayaH kalpayantaH /
gAyatrIM triShTubhaM jagatIm anuShTubhaM bR^ihadarkIM yajamAnAya svar AbharantIm //14//
pa~ncha vyùShTIr anu pa~ncha dohA gAM pa~nchanAmnIm R^itavo 'nu pa~ncha /
pa~ncha dishaH pa~nchadashena k?ptAs tA ekamUrdhnIr abhi lokam ekam //15//
ShaT jAtA bhUtA prathamajA R^itasya ShaT u sAmAni ShaTahaM vahanti /
ShaTyogaM sIram anu sAmasAma ShaT Ahur dyAvApR^ithivIH ShaT urvIH //16//
ShaD AhuH shItAn ShaD u mAsa uShNAn R^ituM no brUta yatamo 'tiriktaH /
sapta suparNAH kavayo ni SheduH sapta chhandAMsy anu sapta dIkShAH //17//
sapta homAH samidho ha sapta madhUni sapta R^itavo ha sapta /
saptAjyAni pari bhUtam Ayan tAH saptagR^idhrA iti shushrumA vayam //18//
sapta chhandAMsi chaturuttarANy anyo anyasminn adhy ArpitAni /
kathaM stomAH prati tiShThanti teShu tAni stomeShu katham ArpitAni //19//
kathaM gAyatrI trivR^itaM vy Apa kathaM triShTup pa~nchadashena kalpate /
trayastriMshena jagatI katham anuShTup katham ekaviMshaH //20// {23}

aShTa jAtA bhUtA prathamajA R^itasyAShTendra R^itvijo daivyA ye /
aShTayonir aditir aShTaputrAstamIM rAtrim abhi havyam eti //21//
itthaM shreyo manyamAnedam AgamaM yuShmAkaM sakhye aham asmi shevA /
samAnajanmA kratur asti vaH shivaH sa vaH sarvAH saM charati prajAnan //22//
aShTendrasya ShaD yamasya R^iShINAM sapta saptadhA /
apo manuShyA3n oShadhIs tA.N u pa~nchAnu sechire //23//
kevalIndrAya duduhe hi gR^iShTir vasham pIyUShaM prathamaM duhAnA /
athAtarpayach chaturash chaturdhA devAn manuShyA3m? asurAn uta R^iShIn //24//
ko nu gauH ka ekaR^iShiH kim u dhAma kA AshiShaH /
yakSham pR^ithivyAm ekavR^id ekartuH katamo nu saH //25//
eko gaur eka ekaR^iShir ekaM dhAmaikadhAshiShaH /
yakShaM pR^ithivyAm ekavR^id ekartur nAti richyate //26// {24}

443
 {1}
virAD vA idam agra AsIt tasyA jAtAyAH sarvam abibhed iyam evedaM bhaviShyatIti //1//
sod akrAmat sA gArhapatye ny akrAmat /
gR^ihamedhI gR^ihapatir bhavati ya evaM veda //2//
sod akrAmat sAhavanIye ny akrAmat /
yanty asya devA devahUtiM priyo devAnAM bhavati ya evaM veda //3//
sod akrAmat sA dakShiNAgnau ny akrAmat /
yaj~narto dakShiNIyo vAsateyo bhavati ya evaM veda //4//
sod akrAmat sA sabhAyAM ny akrAmat /
yanty asya sabhAM sabhyo bhavati ya evaM veda //5//
sod akrAmat sA samitau ny akrAmat /
yanty asya samitiM sAmityo bhavati ya evaM veda //6//
sod akrAmat sAmantraNe ny akrAmat /
yanty asyAmantraNam AmantraNIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //7// {25}

 {2}
sod akrAmat sAntarikShe chaturdhA vikrAntAtiShThat //8//
tAM devamanuShyA abruvann iyam eva tad veda yad ubhaya upajIvememAm upa hvayAmahA iti //9//
tAm upAhvayanta //10//
Urja ehi svadha ehi sUnR^ita ehIrAvaty ehIti //10//
tasyA indro vatsa AsId gAyatry abhidhAny abhram UdhaH //11//
bR^ihach cha rathaMtaraM cha dvau stanAv AstAM yaj~nAyaj~niyaM cha vAmadevyaM cha dvau //12//
oShadhIr eva rathaMtareNa devA aduhran vyacho bR^ihat //13//
apo vAmadevyena yaj~naM yaj~nAyaj~niyena //14//
oShadhIr evAsmai rathaMtaraM duhe vyacho bR^ihat //16//
apo vAmadevyaM yaj~naM yaj~nAyaj~niyaM ya veda //17// {26}

 {3}
sod akrAmat sA vanaspatIn Agachhat tAM vanaspatayo 'ghnata sA saMvatsare sam abhavat /
tasmAd vanaspatInAM saMvatsare vR^ikNam api rohati vR^ishchate 'syApriyo bhrAtR^ivyo ya evaM veda //18//
sod akrAmat sA pitR^In Agachhat tAM pitaro 'ghnata sA mAsi sam abhavat /
tasmAt pitR^ibhyo mAsy upamAsyaM dadati pra pitR^iyANaM panthAM jAnAti ya evaM veda //19//
sod akrAmat sA devAn Agachhat tAM devA aghnata sArdhamAse sam abhavat /
tasmAd devebhyo 'rdhamAse vaShaT kurvanti pra devayAnaM panthAM jAnAti ya evaM veda //20//
sod akrAmat sA manuShyA3n Agachhat tAM manuShyA aghnata sA sadyaH sam abhavat /
tasmAn manuShyebhya ubhayadyur upa haranty upAsya gR^ihe haranti ya evaM veda //21// {27}

 {4}
sod akrAmat sAsurAn Agachhat tAm asurA upAhvayanta mAya ehIti /
tasyA virochanaH prAhrAdir vatsa AsId ayaspAtraM pAtram /
tAM dvimUrdhArtvyo 'dhok tAM mAyAm evAdhok /
tAM mAyAm asurA upa jIvanty upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //22//
 sod akrAmat sA pitR^In Agachhat tAM pitara upAhvayanta svadha ehIti /
tasyA yamo rAjA vatsa AsId rajatapAtraM pAtram /
tAm antako mArtyavo 'dhok tAM svadhAm evAdhok /
tAM svadhAM pitara upa jIvanty upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //23//
sod akrAmat sA manuShyA3n Agachhat tAM manuShyA3 upAhvayanterAvaty ehIti /
tasyA manur vaivasvato vatsa AsIt pR^ithivI pAtram /
tAM pR^ithI vainyo 'dhok tAM kR^iShiM cha sasyaM chAdhok /
te kR^iShiM cha sasyaM cha manuShyA3 upa jIvanti kR^iShTarAdhir upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //24//
 sod akrAmat sA saptaR^iShIn Agachhat tAM saptaR^iShaya upAhvayanta brahmaNvaty ehIti /
tasyAH somo rAjA vatsa AsIch chhandaH pAtram /
tAM bR^ihaspatir A~Ngiraso 'dhok tAM brahma cha tapash chAdhok /
tad brahma cha tapash cha saptaR^iShaya upa jIvanti brahmavarchasy ùpajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //25// {28}

 {5}
sod akrAmat sA devAn Agachhat tAM devA upAhvayantorja ehIti /
tasyA indro vatsa AsIch chamasaH pAtram /
tAM devaH savitAdhok tAm UrjAm evAdhok /
tAm UrjAM devA upa jIvanty upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //26//
sod akrAmat sA gandharvApsarasa Agachhat tAM gandharvApsarasa upAhvayanta puNyagandha ehIti /
tasyAsh chitrarathaH sauryavarchaso vatsa AsIt puShkaraparNaM pAtram /
tAM vasuruchiH sauryavarchaso 'dhok tAM puNyam eva gandham adhok /
tAM puNyam gandhaM gandharvApsarasa upa jIvanti puNyagandhir upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //27//
sod akrAmat setarajanAn Agachhat tAm itarajanA upAhvayanta tirodha ehIti /
tasyAH kubero vaishravaNo vatsa AsId AmapAtraM pAtram /
tAM rajatanAbhiH kaberako 'dhok tAM tirodhAm evAdhok /
tAM tirodhAm itarajanA upa jIvanti tiro dhatte sarvaM pApmAnam upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //28//
sod akrAmat sA sarpAn Agachhat tAM sarpA upAhvayanta viShavaty ehIti /
tasyAs takShako vaishaleyo vatsa AsId alAbupAtraM pAtraM /
tAM dhR^itarAShTra airAvato 'dhok tAM viSham evAdhok /
tad viShaM sarpA upa jIvanty upajIvanIyo bhavati ya evaM veda //29// {29}

 {6}
tad yasmA evaM viduShe 'lAbunAbhiShi~nchet pratyAhanyAt //30//
na cha pratyAhanyAn manasA tvA pratyAhanmIti pratyAhanyAt //31//
yat pratyAhanti viSham eva tat pratyAhanti //32//
viSham evAsyApriyaM bhrAtR^ivyam anuviShichyate ya evaM veda //33// {30}

444
Book:
9

divas pR^ithivyA antarikShAt samudrAd agner vAtAn madhukashA hi jaj~ne /
tAM chAyitvAmR^itaM vasAnAM hR^idbhiH prajAH prati nandanti sarvAH //1//
mahat payo vishvarUpam asyAH samudrasya tvota reta AhuH /
yata aiti madhukashA rarANA tat prANas tad amR^itaM niviShTam //2//
pashyanty asyAsh charitaM pR^ithivyAM pR^itha~N naro bahudhA mImAMsamAnAH /
agner vAtAn madhukashA hi jaj~ne marutAm ugrA naptiH //3//
mAtAdityAnAM duhitA vasUnAM prANaH prajAnAm amR^itasya nAbhiH /
hiraNyavarNA madhukashA ghR^itAchI mahAn bhargash charati martyeShu //4//
madhoH kashAm ajanayanta devAs tasyA garbho abhavad vishvarUpaH /
taM jAtaM taruNaM piparti mAtA sa jAto vishvA bhuvanA vi chaShTe //5//
kas taM pra veda ka u taM chiketa yo asyA hR^idaH kalashaH somadhAno akShitaH /
brahmA sumedhAH so asmin madeta //6//
sa tau pra veda sa u tau chiketa yAv asyAH stanau sahasradhArAv akShitau /
UrjaM duhAte anapasphurantau //7//
hi~NkarikratI bR^ihatI vayodhA uchchairghoShAbhyeti yA vratam /
trIn gharmAn abhi vAvashAnA mimAti mAyuM payate payobhiH //8//
yAm ApInAm upasIdanty ApaH shAkvarA vR^iShabhA ye svarAjaH /
te varShanti te varShayanti tadvide kAmam Urjam ApaH //9//
stanayitnus te vAk prajApate vR^iShA shuShmaM kShipasi bhUmyAm adhi /
agner vAtAn madhukashA hi jaj~ne marutAm ugrA naptiH //10// {1}

yathA somaH prAtaHsavane ashvinor bhavati priyaH /
evA me ashvinA varcha Atmani dhriyatAm //11//
yathA somo dvitIye savana indrAgnyor bhavati priyaH /
evA ma indrAgnI varcha Atmani dhriyatAm //12//
yathA somas tR^itIye savana R^ibhUNAM bhavati priyaH /
evA ma R^ibhavo varcha Atmani dhriyatAm //13//
madhu janiShIya madhu vaMsiShIya /
payasvAn agna AgamaM taM mA saM sR^ija varchasA //14//
saM mAgne varchasA sR^ija saM prajayA sam AyuShA /
vidyur me asya devA indro vidyAt saha R^iShibhiH //15//
yathA madhu madhukR^itaH saMbharanti madhAv adhi /
evA me ashvinA varcha Atmani dhriyatAm //16//
yathA makShAH idaM madhu nya~njanti madhAv adhi /
evA me ashvinA varchas tejo balam ojash cha dhriyatAm //17//
yad giriShu parvateShu goShv ashveShu yan madhu /
surAyAM sichyamAnAyAM yat tatra madhu tan mayi //18//
ashvinA sAragheNa mA madhunA~NktaM shubhas patI /
yathA varchasvatIM vAcham AvadAni janAm? anu //19//
stanayitnus te vAk prajApate vR^iShA shuShmaM kShipasi bhUmyAM divi /
tAM pashava upa jIvanti sarve teno seSham UrjaM piparti //20//
pR^ithivI daNDo 'ntarikShaM garbho dyauH kashA vidyut prakasho hiraNyayo binduH //21//
yo vai kashAyAH sapta madhUni veda madhumAn bhavati /
brAhmaNash cha rAjA cha dhenush chAnaDvAMsh cha vrIhish cha yavash cha madhu saptamam //22//
madhumAn bhavati madhumad asyAhAryaM bhavati /
madhumato lokAn jayati ya evaM veda //23//
yad vIdhre stanayati prajApatir eva tat prajAbhyaH prAdur bhavati /
tasmAt prAchInopavItas tiShThe prajApate 'nu mA budhyasveti /
anv enaM prajA anu prajApatir budhyate ya evaM veda //24// {2}

445

sapatnahanam R^iShabhaM ghR^itena kAmaM shikShAmi haviShAjyena /
nIchaiH sapatnAn mama padaya tvam abhiShTuto mahatA vIryeNa //1//
yan me manaso na priyaM chakShuSho yan me babhasti nAbhinandati /
tad duShvapnyaM prati mu~nchAmi sapatne kAmaM stutvod ahaM bhideyam //2//
duShvapnyaM kAma duritaM cha kamAprajastAm asvagatAm avartim /
ugra IshAnaH prati mu~ncha tasmin yo asmabhyam aMhUraNA chikitsAt //3//
nudasva kAma pra Nudasva kAmAvartiM yantu mama ye sapatnAH /
teShAM nuttAnAm adhamA tamAMsy agne vAstUni nir daha tvam //4//
sA te kAma duhitA dhenur uchyate yAm Ahur vAchaM kavayo virAjam /
tayA sapatnAn pari vR^i~Ngdhi ye mama pary enAn prANaH pashavo jIvanaM vR^iNaktu //5//
kAmasyendrasya varuNasya rAj~no viShNor balena savituH savena /
agner hotreNa pra Nude sapatnAM chhambIva nAvam udakeShu dhIraH //6//
adhyakSho vAjI mama kAma ugraH kR^iNotu mahyam asapatnam eva /
vishve devA mama nAthaM bhavantu sarve devA havam A yantu ma imam //7//
idam AjyaM ghR^itavaj juShANAH kAmajyeShThA iha mAdayadhvam /
kR^iNvanto mahyam asapatnam eva //8//
indrAgnI kAma sarathaM hi bhUtvA nIchaiH sapatnAn mama pAdayAthaH /
teShAM pannAnAm adhamA tamAMsy agne vAstUny anunirdaha tvam //9//
jahi tvam kAma mama ye sapatnA andhA tamAMsy ava pAdayainAn /
nirindriyA arasAH santu sarve mA te jIviShuH katamach chanAhaH //10// {3}

avadhIt kAmo mama ye sapatnA uruM lokam akaran mahyam edhatum /
mahyaM namantAM pradishash chatasro mahyaM ShaD urvIr ghR^itam A vahantu //11//
te 'dharA~nchaH pra plavantAM chhinnA naur iva bandhanAt /
na sAyakapraNuttAnAM punar asti nivartanam //12//
agnir yava indro yavaH somo yavaH /
yavayAvAno devA yavayantv enam //13//
asarvavIrash charatu praNutto dveShyo mitrAnAM parivargya1H svAnAm /
uta pR^ithivyAm ava syanti vidyuta ugro vo devaH pra mR^iNat sapatnAn //14//
chyutA cheyaM bR^ihaty achyutA cha vidyud bibharti stanayitnUMsh cha sarvAn /
udyann Adityo draviNena tejasA nIchaiH sapatnAn nudatAM me sahasvAn //15//
yat te kAma sharma trivarUtham udbhu brahma varma vitatam anativyAdhyaM kR^itam /
tena sapatnAn pari vR^i~Ngdhi ye mama pary enAn prANaH pashAvo jIvanaM vR^iNaktu //16//
yena devA asurAn prANudanta yenendro dasyUn adhamaM tamo ninAya /
tena tvaM kAma mama ye sapatnAs tAn asmAl lokAt pra Nudasva dUram //17//
yathA devA asurAn prANudanta yathendro dasyUn adhamaM tamo babAdhe /
tathA tvaM kAma mama ye sapatnAs tAn asmAl lokAt pra Nudasva dUram //18//
kAmo jaj~ne prathamo nainaM devA ApuH pitaro na martyAH /
tatas tvam asi jyAyAn vishvahA mahAMs tasmai te kAma nama it kR^inomi //19//
yAvatI dyAvApR^ithivI varimNA yAvad ApaH siShyadur yAvad agniH /
tatas tvam asi jyAyAn vishvahA mahAMs tasmai te kAma nama it kR^iNomi //20// {4}

yAvatIr dishaH pradisho viShUchIr yAvatIr AshA abhichakShaNA divaH /
tatas tvam asi jyAyAn vishvahA mahAMs tasmai te kAma nama it kR^iNomi //21//
yAvatIr bhR^i~NgA jatvaH kurUravo yAvatIr vaghA vR^ikShasarpyo babhUvuH /
tatas tvam asi jyAyAn vishvahA mahAMs tasmai te kAma nama it kR^iNomi //22//
jyAyAn nimiShato 'si tiShThato jyAyAnt samudrAd asi kAma manyo /
tatas tvam asi jyAyAn vishvahA mahAMs tasmai te kAma nama it kR^inomi //23//
na vai vAtash chana kAmam Apnoti nAgniH sUryo nota chandramAH /
tatas tvam asi jyAyAn vishvahA mahAMs tasmai te kAma nama it kR^iNomi //24//
yAs te shivAs tanvaH kAma bhadrA yAbhiH satyaM bhavati yad vR^iNiShe /
tAbhiSh Tvam asmA.N abhisaMvishasvAnyatra pApIr apa veshayA dhiyaH //25// {5}

446

upamitAM pratimitAm atho parimitAm uta /
shAlAyA vishvavArAyA naddhAni vi chR^itAmasi //1//
yat te naddhaM vishvavAre pAsho granthish cha yaH kR^itaH /
bR^ihaspatir ivAhaM balaM vAchA vi sraMsayAmi tat //2//
A yayAma saM babarha granthIMsh chakAra te dR^iDhAn /
parUMShi vidvAM chhastevendreNa vi chR^itAmasi //3//
vaMshAnAM te nahanAnAM prANAhasya tR^iNasya cha /
pakShANAM vishvavAre te naddhAni vi chR^itAmasi //4//
saMdaMshAnAM paladAnAM pariShva~njalyasya cha /
idaM mAnasya patnyA naddhAni vi chR^itAmasi //5//
yAni te 'ntaH shikyAny AbedhU raNyAya kam /
pra te tAni chR^itAmasi shivA mAnasya patni na uddhitA tanve bhava //6//
havirdhAnam agnishAlaM patnInAM sadanaM sadaH /
sado devAnAm asi devi shAle //7//
akShum opashaM vitataM sahasrAkShaM viShUvati /
avanaddham abhihitaM brahmaNA vi chR^itAmasi //8//
yas tvA shAle pratigR^ihNAti yena chAsi mitA tvam /
ubhau mAnasya patni tau jIvatAM jaradaShTI //9//
amutrainam A gachhatAd dR^iDhA naddhA pariShkR^itA /
yasyAs te vichR^itAmasy a~Ngama~NgaM paruShparuH //10// {6}

yas tvA shAle nimimAya saMjabhAra vanaspatIn /
prajAyai chakre tvA shAle parameShThI prajApatiH //11//
namas tasmai namo dAtre shAlApataye cha kR^iNmaH /
namo 'gnaye pracharate puruShAya cha te namaH //12//
gobhyo ashvebhyo namo yach chhAlAyAM vijAyate /
vijAvati prajAvati vi te pAshAMsh chR^itAmasi //13//
agnim antash chhAdayasi puruShAn pashubhiH saha /
vijAvati prajAvati vi te pAshAMsh chR^itAmasi //14//
antarA dyAM cha pR^ithivIM cha yad vyachas tena shAlAM prati gR^ihNAmi ta imAm /
yad antarikShaM rajaso vimAnaM tat kR^iNve 'ham udaraM shevadhibhyaH /
tena shAlAM prati gR^ihNAmi tasmai //15//
UrjasvatI payasvatI pR^ithivyAM nimitA mitA /
vishvAnnaM bibhratI shAle mA hiMsIH pratigR^ihNataH //16//
tR^iNair AvR^itA paladAn vasAnA rAtrIva shAlA jagato niveshanI /
mitA pR^ithivyAM tiShThasi hastinIva padvatI //17//
itasya te vi chR^itAmy apinaddham aporNuvan /
varuNena samubjitAM mitraH prAtar vy ùbjatu //18//
brahmaNA shAlAM nimitAM kavibhir nimitAM mitAm /
indrAgnI rakShatAM shAlAm amR^itau somyaM sadah //19//
kulAye 'dhi kulAyaM koshe koshaH samubjitaH /
tatra marto vi jAyate yasmAd vishvaM prajAyate //20// {7}

yA dvipakShA chatuShpakShA ShaTpakShA yA nimIyate /
aShTApakShAM dashapakShAM shAlAM mAnasya patnIm agnir garbha ivA shaye //21//
pratIchIM tvA pratIchInaH shAle praimy ahiMsatIm /
agnir hy antar Apash cha R^itasya prathamA dvAH //22//
imA ApaH pra bharAmy ayakShmA yakShmanAshanIH /
gR^ihAn upa pra sIdAmy amR^itena sahAgninA //23//
mA naH pAshaM prati mucho gurur bhAro laghur bhava /
vadhUm iva tvA shAle yatrakAmaM bharAmasi //24//
prAchyA dishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //25//
dakShiNAyA dishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //26//
pratIchyA dishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //27//
udIchyA dishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //28//
dhruvAyA dishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //29//
UrdhvAyA dishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //30//
dishodishaH shAlAyA namo mahimne svAhA devebhyaH svAhyebhyaH //31// {8}

447

sAhasras tveSha R^iShabhaH payasvAn vishvA rUpANi vakShaNAsu bibhrat /
bhadraM dAtre yajamAnAya shIkShan bArhaspatya usriyas tantum AtAn //1//
apAM yo agne pratimA babhUva prabhUH sarvasmai pR^ithivIva devI /
pitA vatsAnAM patir aghnyAnAM sAhasre poShe api naH kR^iNotu //2//
pumAn antarvAnt sthaviraH payasvAn vasoH kabandhaM R^iShabho bibharti /
tam indrAya pathibhir devayAnair hutam agnir vahatu jAtavedAH //3//
pitA vatsAnAM patir aghnyAnAM atho pitA mahatAM gargarANAm /
vatso jarAyu pratidhuk pIyUSha AmikShA ghR^itaM tad v asya retaH //4//
devAnAM bhAga upanAha eSho 'pAM rasa oShadhInAM ghR^itasya /
somasya bhakSham avR^iNIta shakro bR^ihann adrir abhavad yach chharIram //5//
somena pUrNaM kalashaM bibharShi tvastA rupANAM janitA pashUnAm /
shivAs te santu prajanva iha yA imA ny asmabhyaM svadhite yachha yA amUH //6//
AjaM bibharti ghR^itam asya retaH sAhasraH poShas tam u yaj~nam AhuH /
indrasya rUpam R^iShabho vasAnaH so asmAn devAH shiva aitu dattaH //7//
indrasyaujo varuNasya bAhU ashvinor aMsau marutAm iyaM kakut /
bR^ihaspatiM saMbhR^itam etam Ahur ye dhIrAsaH kavayo ye manIShiNaH //8//
daivIr vishaH payasvAn A tanoShi tvAm indraM tvAM sarasvantam AhuH /
sahasraM sa ekamukhA dadAti yo brAhmaNa R^iShabham Ajuhoti //9//
bR^ihaspatiH savitA te vayo dadhau tvaShTur vAyoH pary AtmA ta AbhR^itaH /
antarikShe manasA tvA juhomi barhiSh Te dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe stAm //10// {9}

ya indra iva deveShu goShv eti vivAvadat /
tasya R^iShabhasyA~NgAni brahmA saM stautu bhadrayA //11//
pArshve AstAm anumatyA bhagasyAstAm anUvR^ijau /
aShThIvantAv abravIn mitro mamaitau kevalAv iti //12//
bhasad AsId AdityAnAM shroNI AstAM bR^ihaspateH /
puchhaM vAtasya devasya tena dhUnoty oShadhIH //13//
gudA Asant sinIvAlyAH sUryAyAs tvacham abruvan /
utthAtur abruvan pada R^iShabhaM yad akalpayan //14//
kroDa AsIj jAmishaMsasya somasya klasho dhR^itaH /
devAH saMgatya yat sarva R^iShabhaM vyakalpayan //15//
te kuShThikAH saramAyai kurmebhyo adadhuH shaphAn /
Ubadhyam asya kItebhyaH shvavartebhyo adhArayan //16//
shR^i~NgAbhyAM rakSha R^iShaty avartim hanti chakShuShA /
shR^iNoti bhadraM karNAbhyAM gavAM yaH patir aghnyaH //17//
shatayAjaM sa yajate nainaM dunvanty agnayaH /
jinvanti vishve taM devA yo brAhmaNa R^iShabham Ajuhoti //18//
brAhmaNebhya R^iShabhaM dattvA varIyaH kR^iNute manaH /
puShTiM so aghnyAnAM sve goShThe 'va pashyate //19//
gAvaH santu prajAH santv atho astu tanUbalam /
tat sarvam anu manyantAM devA R^iShabhadAyine //20//
ayaM pipAna indra id rayiM dadhAtu chetanIm /
ayaM dhenuM sudughAM nityavatsAM vashaM duhAM vipashchitaM paro divaH //21//
pisha~NgarUpo nabhaso vayodhA aindraH shuShmo vishvarUpo na Agan /
Ayur asmabhyaM dadhat prajAM cha rAyash cha poShair abhi naH sachatAm //22//
upehopaparchanAsmin goShTha upa pR^i~ncha naH /
upa R^iShabhasya yad reta upendra tava vIryam //23//
etaM vo yuvAnaM prati dadhmo atra tena krIDantIsh charata vashAm? anu /
mA no hAsiShTa januShA subhAgA rAyash cha poShair abhi naH sachadhvam //24// {10}

448

A nayaitam A rabhasva sukR^itAM lokam api gachhatu prajAnan /
tIrtvA tamAMsi bahudhA mahAnty ajo nAkam A kramatAM tR^itIyam //1//
indrAya bhAgaM pari tvA nayAmy asmin yaj~ne yajamAnAya sUrim /
ye no dviShanty anu tAn rabhasvAnAgaso yajamAnasya vIrAH //2//
pra pado 'va nenigdhi dushcharitaM yach chachAra shuddhaiH shaphair A kramatAM prajAnan /
tIrtvA tamAMsi bahudhA vipashyann ajo nAkam A kramatAM tR^itIyam //3//
anuchhya shyAmena tvacham etAM vishastar yathAparv a1sinA mAbhi maMsthAH /
mAbhi druhaH parushaH kalpayainaM tR^itIye nAke adhi vi shrayainam //4//
R^ichA kumbhIm adhy agnau shrayAmy A si~nchodakam ava dhehy enam /
paryAdhattAgninA shamitAraH sR^ito gachhatu sukR^itAM yatra lokaH //5//
ut kAmAtaH pari ched ataptas taptAch charor adhi nAkaM tR^itIyam /
agner agnir adhi saM babhUvitha jyotiShmantam abhi lokaM jayaitam //6//
ajo agnir ajam u jyotir Ahur ajaM jIvatA brahmaNe deyam AhuH /
ajas tamAMsy apa hanti dUram asmiMl loke shraddadhAnena dattaH //7//
pa~nchaudanaH pa~nchadhA vi kramatAm AkraMsyamAnas trINi jyotIMShi /
IjAnAnAM sukR^itAM prehi madhyaM tR^itIye nAke adhi vi shrayasva //8//
ajA roha sukR^itAM yatra lokaH sharabho na chatto 'ti durgAny eShaH /
pa~nchaudano brahmaNe dIyamAnaH sa dAtAraM tR^iptyA tarpayAti //9//
ajas trinAke tridive tripR^iShThe nAkasya pR^iShThe dadivAMsaM dadhAti /
pa~nchaudano brahmaNe dIyamAno vishvarUpA dhenuH kAmadughAsy ekA //10// {11}

etad vo jyotiH pitaras tR^itIyaM pa~nchaudanaM brahmaNe 'jaM dadAti /
ajas tamAMsy apa hanti dUram asmiMl loke shraddadhAnena dattaH //11//
IjAnAnAM sukR^itAM lokam Ipsan pa~nchaudanaM brahmaNe 'jaM dadAti /
sa vyAptim abhi lokaM jayaitaM shivo3 'smabhyaM pratigR^ihIto astu //12//
ajo hy a1gner ajaniShTa shokAd vipro viprasya sahaso vipashchit /
iShTaM pUrtam abhipUrtaM vaShaTkR^itaM tad devA R^itushaH kalpayantu //13//
amotaM vAso dadyAd dhiraNyam api dakShiNAm /
tathA lokAnt sam Apnoti ye divyA ye cha pArthivAH //14//
etAs tvAjopa yantu dhArAH somyA devIr ghR^itapR^iShThA madhushchutaH /
stabhAn pR^ithivIm uta dyAM nAkasya pR^iShThe 'dhi saptarashmau //15//
ajo 'sy aja svargo 'si tvayA lokam a~NgirasaH prAjAnan /
taM lokaM puNyaM pra j~neSham //16//
yenA sahasraM vahasi yenAgne sarvavedasam /
tenemaM yaj~naM no vaha svar deveShu gantave //17//
ajaH pakvaH svarge loke dadhAti pa~nchaudano nirR^itiM bAdhamAnaH /
tena lokAnt sUryavato jayema //18//
yaM brAhmaNe nidadhe yaM cha vikShu yA vipruSha odanAnAm ajasya /
sarvaM tad agne sukR^itasya loke jAnItAn naH saMgamane pathInAm //19//
ajo vA idam agne vy akramata tasyora iyam abhavad dyauH pR^iShTiham /
antarikShaM madhyam dishaH pArshve samudrau kukShI //20// {12}

satyaM cha rtaM cha chakShuShI vishvaM satyaM shraddhA prANo virAT shiraH /
eSha vA aparimito yaj~no yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH //21//
aparimitam eva yaj~nam Apnoty aparimitaM lokam ava runddhe /
yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShiNAjyotiShaM dadAti //22//
nAsyAsthIni bhindyAn na majj~no nir dhayet /
sarvam enaM samAdAyedamidaM pra veshayet //23//
idamidam evAsya rUpaM bhavati tenainaM saM gamayati /
iShaM maha Urjam asmai duhe yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanam dakShiNAjyotiShaM dadAti //24//
pa~ncha rukmA pa~ncha navAni vastrA pa~nchAsmai dhenavaH kAmadughA bhavanti /
yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShiNAjyotiShaM dadAti //25//
pa~ncha rukmA jyotir asmai bhavanti varma vAsAMsi tanve bhavanti /
svargaM lokam ashnute yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShiNAjyotiSham dadAti //26//
yA pUrvaM patiM vittvA 'thAnyaM vindate 'param /
pa~nchaudanaM cha tAv ajaM dadAto na vi yoShataH //27//
samAnaloko bhavati punarbhuvAparaH patiH /
yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanam dakShiNAjyotiShaM dadAti //28//
anupUrvavatsAM dhenum anaDvAham upabarhaNam /
vAso hiraNyaM dattvA te yanti divam uttamAm //29//
AtmAnaM pitaraM putraM pautraM pitAmaham /
jAyAM janitrIM mAtaraM ye priyAs tAn upa hvaye //30// {13}

yo vai naidAghaM nAma rtuM veda /
eSha vai naidAgho nAma rtur yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH /
nir evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM dahati bhavaty AtmanA /
yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShiNAjyotiShaM dadAti //31//
yo vai kurvantaM nAma rtuM veda /
kurvatIMkurvatIm evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM A datte /
eSha vai kurvan nAma rtur yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH /
nir evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM dahati bhavaty AtmanA yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShinAjyotiShaM dadAti //32//
yo vai saMyantaM nAma rtuM veda /
saMyatIMsaMyatIm evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM A datte /
eSha vai saMyan nAma rtur yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH /
nir evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM dahati bhavaty AtmanA yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShiNAjyotiShaM dadAti //33//
yo vai pinvantaM nAma rtum veda /
pinvatIMpinvatIm evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM A datte /
eSha vai pinvan nAma rtur yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH /
nir evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM dahati bhavaty AtmanA yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShinAjyotiShaM dadAti //34//
yo vA udyantaM nAma rtuM veda /
udyatIMudyatIm evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM A datte /
eSha vA udyann nAma rtur yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH /
nir evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM dahati bhavaty AtmanA yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM daksiNAjyotiSham dadAti //35//
yo vA abhibhuvaM nAma rtuM veda /
abhibhavantImabhibhavantIm evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM A datte /
eSha vA abhibhUr nAma rtur yad ajaH pa~nchaudanaH /
nir evApriyasya bhrAtR^ivyasya shriyaM dahati bhavaty AtmanA /
yo3 'jaM pa~nchaudanaM dakShinAjyotiShaM dadAti //36//
ajaM cha pachata pa~ncha chaudanAn /
sarvA dishaH saMmanasaH sadhrIchIH sAntardeshAH prati gR^ihnantu ta etam //37//
tAs te rakShantu tava tubhyam etaM tAbhya AjyaM havir idaM juhomi //38// {14}

449
 {1}
yo vidyAd brahma pratyakShaM parUMShi yasya saMbhArA R^icho yasyAnUkyam //1//
sAmAni yasya lomAni yajur hR^idayam uchyate paristaraNam id dhaviH //2//
yad vA atithipatir atithIn pratipashyati devayajanaM prekShate //3//
yad abhivadati dIkShAm upaiti yad udakaM yAchaty apaH pra Nayati //4//
yA eva yaj~na ApaH praNIyante tA eva tAH //5//
yat tarpaNam Aharanti ya evAgnIShomIyaH pashur badhyate sa eva saH //6//
yad AvasathAn kalpayanti sadohavirdhAnAny eva tat kalpayanti //7//
yad upastR^iNanti barhir eva tat //8//
yad uparishayanam Aharanti svargam eva tena lokam ava runddhe //9//
yat kashipUpabarhaNam Aharanti paridhaya eva te //10//
yad A~njanAbhya~njanam Aharanty Ajyam eva tat //11//
yat purA pariveShAt svAdam Aharanti purodAshAv eva tau //12//
yad ashanakR^itaM hvayanti haviShkR^itam eva tad dhvayanti //13//
ye vrIhayo yavA nirupyante 'Mshava eva te //14//
yAny ulUkhalamusalAni grAvANa eva te //15//
shUrpaM pavitraM tuShA R^ijIShAbhiShavaNIr ApaH //16//
srug darvir nekShaNam AyavanaM droNakalashAH kumbhyo vAyavyAni pAtrANIyam eva kR^iShNAjinam //17// {15}

 {2}
yajamAnabrAhmaNaM vA etad atithipatiH kurute yad AhAryANi prekShata idaM bhUyA3 idA3m iti //18//
yad Aha bhUya ud dhareti prANam eva tena varShIyAMsaM kurute //19//
upa harati havIMShy A sAdayati //20//
teShAm AsannAnAm atithir Atman juhoti //21//
sruchA hastena prANe yUpe srukkAreNa vaShaTkAreNa //22//
ete vai priyAsh chApriyAsh cha rtvijaH svargaM lokaM gamayanti yad atithayaH //23//
sa ya evaM vidvAn na dviShann ashnIyAn na dviShato 'nnam ashnIyAn na mImAMsitasya na mImAMsamAnasya //24//
sarvo vA eSha jagdhapApmA yasyAnnam ashnanti //25//
sarvo vA eso 'jagdhapApmA yasyAnnam nAshnanti //26//
sarvadA vA eSha yuktagrAvArdrapavitro vitatAdhvara AhR^itayaj~nakratur ya upaharati //27//
prAjApatyo vA etasya yaj~no vitato ya upaharati //28//
prajApater vA eSha vikramAn anuvikramate ya upaharati //29//
yo 'tithInAM sa AhavanIyo yo veshmani sa gArhapatyo yasmin pachanti sa dakShiNAgnih //30// {16}

 {3}
iShTaM cha vA eSha pUrtaM cha gR^ihANAm ashnAti yaH pUrvo 'tither ashnAti //31//
payash cha vA eSha rasaM cha gR^ihANAm ashnAti yaH pUrvo 'tither ashnAti //32//
UrjAM cha vA eSha sphAtiM cha gR^ihANAm ashnAti yaH pUrvo 'tither ashnAti //33//
prajAM vA eSha pashUMsh cha gR^ihANAm ashnAti yaH pUrvo 'tither ashnAti //34//
kIrtiM vA eSha yashash cha gR^ihANAm ashnAti yaH pUrvo 'tither ashnAti //35//
shriyaM vA eSha saMvidaM cha gR^ihANAm ashnAti yaH pUrvo 'tither ashnAti //36//
eSha vA atithir yach chhrotriyas tasmAt pUrvo nAshnIyAt //37//
ashitAvaty atithAv ashnIyAd yaj~nasya sAtmatvAya yaj~nasyAvichhedAya tad vratam //38//
etad vA u svAdIyo yad adhigavaM kShIraM vA mAMsaM vA tad eva nAshnIyAt //39// {17}

 {4}
sa ya evaM vidvAn kShIram upasichyopaharati /
yAvad agniShTomeneShTvA susamR^iddhenAvarunddhe tAvad enenAva runddhe //40//
sa ya evaM vidvAnt sarpir upasichyopaharati /
yAvad atirAtreNeShTvA susamR^iddhenAvarunddhe tAvad enenAva runddhe //41//
sa ya evaM vidvAn madhUpasichyopaharati /
yAvad sattrasadyeneShTvA susamR^iddhenAvarunddhe tAvad enenAva runddhe //42//
sa ya evaM vidvAn mAMsam upasichyopaharati /
yAvad dvAdashAheneShTvA susamR^iddhenAvarunddhe tAvad enenAva runddhe //43//
sa ya evaM vidvAn udakam upasichyopaharati /
prajAnAM prajananAya gachhati pratiShThAM priyah prajAnAM bhavati ya evaM vidvAn upasichyopaharati //44// {18}
{5}tasmA uShA hi~N kR^iNoti savitA pra stauti /
bR^ihaspatir Urjayod gAyati tvaShTA puShTyA prati harati vishve devA nidhanam //2//
nidhanaM bhUtyAH prajAyAH pashUnAM bhavati ya evaM veda //45//
tasmA udyant sUryo hi~N kR^iNoti saMgavaH pra stauti /
madhyandina ud gAyaty aparAhNaH prati haraty astaMyan nidhanam /
nidhanaM bhUtyAH prajAyAH pashUnAM bhavati ya evaM veda //46//
tasmA abhro bhavan hi~N kR^iNoti stanayan pra stauti /
vidyotamAnaH prati harati varShann ud gAyaty udgR^ihNan nidhanam /
nidhanaM bhUtyAH prajAyAH pashUnAM bhavati ya evaM veda //47//
atithIn prati pashyati hi~N kR^iNoty abhi vadati pra stauty udakam yAchaty ud gAyati /
upa harati prati haraty uchchhiShTaM nidhanam /
nidhanaM bhUtyAH prajAyAH pashUnAM bhavati ya evam veda //48// {19}

 {6}
yat kShattAraM hvayaty A shrAvayaty eva tat //49//
yat pratishR^iNoti pratyAshrAvayaty eva tat //50//
yat pariveShTAraH pAtrahastAH pUrve chApare cha prapadyante chamasAdhvaryava eva te //51//
teShAM na kash chanAhotA //52//
yad vA atithipatir atithIn pariviShya gR^ihAn upodaity avabhR^itham eva tad upAvaiti //53//
yat sabhAgayati dakShiNAH sabhAgayati yad anutiShThata udavasyaty eva tat //54//
sa upahUtaH pR^ithivyAM bhakShayaty upahUtas tasmin yat pR^ithivyAM vishvarUpam //55//
sa upahUto 'ntarikShe bhakShayaty upahUtas tasmin yad divi vishvarUpam //56//
sa upahUto divi bhakShayaty upahUtas tasmin yad divi vishvarUpam //57//
sa upahUto deveShu bhakShayaty upahUtas tasmin yad divi vishvarUpam //58//
sa upahUto lokeShu bhakShayaty upahUtas tasmin yad divi vishvarUpam //59//
sa upahUta upahUtaH //60//
ApnotImaM lokam Apnoty amum //61//
jyotiShmato lokAn jayati ya evaM veda //62// {20}

450

prajApatish cha parameShThI cha shR^i~Nge indraH shiro agnir lalATaM yamaH kR^ikATam //1//
somo rAjA mastiShko dyaur uttarahanuH pR^ithivy adharahanuH //2//
vidyuj jihvA maruto dantA revatir grIvAH kR^ittikA skandhA gharmo vahaH //3//
vishvaM vAyuH svargo lokaH kR^iShNadraM vidharaNI niveShyaH //4//
shyenaH kroto 'ntarikShaM pAjasya1M bR^ihaspatiH kakud bR^ihatIH kIkasAH //5//
devAnAM patnIH pR^iShTaya upasadaH parshavaH //6//
mitrash cha varuNash chAMsau tvaShTA chAryamA cha doShaNI mahAdevo bAhU //7//
indrANI bhasad vAyuH puchhaM pavamAno bAlAH //8//
brahma cha kShatraM cha shroNI balam UrU //9//
dhAtA cha savitA chAShThIvantau ja~NghA gandharvA apsarasaH kuShThikA aditiH shaphAH //10//
cheto hR^idayaM yakR^in medhA vrataM purItat //11//
kShut kukShir irA vaniShThuH parvatAH plAshayaH //12//
krodho vR^ikkau manyur ANDau prajA shepaH //13//
nadI sUtrI varShasya pataya stanA stanayitnur UdhaH //14//
vishvavyachAs charmauShadhayo lomAni nakShatrANi rUpam //15//
devajanA gudA manuShyA AntrANy atrA udaram //16//
rakShAMsi lohitam itarajanA Ubadhyam //17//
abhraM pIbo majjA nidhanam //18//
agnir AsIna utthito 'shvinA //19//
indraH prA~N tiShThan dakShiNA tiShThan yamaH //20//
pratya~N tiShThan dhAtoda~N tiShThant savitA //21//
tR^iNAmi prAptaH somo rAjA //22//
mitra IkShamANa AvR^itta AnandaH //23//
yujyamAno vaishvadevo yuktaH prajApatir vimuktaH sarvam //24//
etad vai vishvarUpaM sarvarUpaM gorUpam //25//
upainaM vishvarUpAH sarvarUpAH pashavas tiShThanti ya evaM veda //26// {21}

451

shIrShaktiM shIrShAmayaM karNashUlaM vilohitam /
sarvaM shIrShanyaM te rogaM bahir nir mantrayAmahe //1//
karNAbhyAM te ka~NkUShebhyaH karNashUlaM visalpakam /
sarvaM shIrShanyaM te rogaM bahir nir mantrayAmahe //2//
yasya hetoH prachyavate yakShmaH karNato AsyataH /
sarvaM shIrShanyaM te rogaM bahir nir mantrayAmahe //3//
yaH kR^iNoti pramotam andhaM kR^iNoti pUruSham /
sarvaM shIrShanyaM te rogaM bahir nir mantrayAmahe //4//
a~Ngabhedam a~Ngajvaram vishvA~NgyaM visalpakam /
sarvaM shIrShanyaM te rogaM bahir nir mantrayAmahe //5//
yasya bhImaH pratIkAsha udvepayati pUruSham /
takmAnaM vishvashAradaM bahir nir mantrayAmahe //6//
ya UrU anusarpaty atho eti gavInike /
yakShmaM te antar a~Ngebhyo bahir nir mantrayAmahe //7//
yadi kAmAd apakAmAd dhR^idayAj jAyate pari /
hR^ido balAsam a~Ngebhyo bahir nir mantrayAmahe //8//
harimANaM te a~Ngebhyo 'pvAm antarodarAt /
yakShmodhAm antar Atmano bahir nir mantrayAmahe //9//
Aso balAso bhavatu mUtraM bhavatv Amayat /
yakShmANAM sarveShAM viShaM nir avocham ahaM tvat //10// {22}

bahir bilaM nir dravatu kAhAbAhaM tavodarAt /
yakShmANAM sarveShAM viShaM nir avocham ahaM tvat //11//
udarAt te klomno nAbhyA hR^idayAd adhi /
yakShmANAM sarveShAM viShaM nir avocham ahaM tvat //12//
yAH sImAnaM virujanti mUrdhAnaM praty arShanIH /
ahiMsantIr anAmayA nir dravantu bahir bilam //13//
yA hR^idayam uparShanty anutanvanti kIkasAH /
ahiMsantIr anAmayA nir dravantu bahir bilam //14//
yAH pArshve uparShanty anunikShanti pR^iShTIH /
ahiMsantIr anAmayA nir dravantu bahir bilam //15//
yAs tirashchIH uparShanty arShaNIr vakShaNAsu te /
ahiMsantIr anAmayA nir dravantu bahir bilam //16//
yA gudA anusarpanty AntrANi mohayanti cha /
ahiMsantIr anAmayA nir dravantu bahir bilam //17//
yA majj~no nirdhayanti parUMShi virujanti cha /
ahiMsantIr anAmayA nir dravantu bahir bilam //18//
ye a~NgAni madayanti yakShmAso ropaNAs tava /
yakShmANAM sarveShAM viShaM nir avocham ahaM tvat //19//
visalpasya vidradhasya vAtIkArasya vAlajeH /
yakShmANAM sarveShAM viShaM nir avocham ahaM tvat //20//
pAdAbhyAM te jAnubhyAM shroNibhyAM pari bhaMsasaH /
anUkAd arShaNIr uShNihAbhyaH shIrShNo rogam anInasham //21//
saM te shIrShNaH kapAlAni hR^idayasya cha yo vidhuH /
udyann Aditya rashmibhiH shIrShNo rogam anInasho '~Ngabhedam ashIshamaH //22// {23}

452

asya vAmasya palitasya hotus tasya bhrAtA madhyamo asty ashnaH /
tR^itIyo bhrAtA ghR^itapR^iShTho asyAtrApashyaM vishpatiM saptaputram //1//
sapta yu~njanti ratham ekachakram eko ashvo vahati saptanAmA /
trinAbhi chakram ajaram anarvaM yatremA vishvA bhuvanAdhi tasthuH //2//
imaM ratham adhi ye sapta tasthuH saptachakraM sapta vahanty ashvAH /
sapta svasAro abhi saM navanta yatra gavAm nihitA sapta nAma //3//
ko dadarsha prathamaM jAyamAnam asthanvantaM yad anasthA bibharti /
bhUmyA asur asR^ig AtmA kva svit ko vidvAMsam upa gAt praShTum etat //4//
iha bravItu ya Im a~Nga vedAsya vAmasya nihitaM padaM veH /
shIrShNaH kShIraM duhrate gAvo asya vavriM vasAnA udakaM padA 'puH //5//
pAkaH pR^ichhAmi manasA 'vijAnan devAnAm enA nihitA padAni /
vatse baShkaye 'dhi sapta tantUn vi tatnire kavaya otavA u //6//
achikitvAMs chikituShash chid atra kavIn pR^ichhAmi vidvano na vidvAn /
vi yas tastambha ShaT imA rajAMsy ajasya rUpe kiM api svid ekam //7//
mAtA pitaram R^ita A babhAja 'dhIty agre manasA saM hi jagme /
sA bIbhatsur garbharasA nividdhA namasvanta id upavAkam IyuH //8//
yuktA mAtAsid dhuri dakShiNAyA atiShThad garbho vR^ijanIShv antaH /
amImed vatso anu gAm apashyad vishvarUpyaM triShu yoganeShu //9//
tisro matR^Is trIn pitR^In bibhrad eka urdhvas tasthau nem ava glApayanta /
mantrayante divo amuShya pR^iShThe vishvavido vAcham avishvavinnAm //10// {24}

pa~nchAre chakre parivartamAne yasminn Atasthur bhuvanAni vishvA /
tasya nAkShas tapyate bhUribhAraH sanAd eva na chhidyate sanAbhiH //11//
pa~nchapAdaM pitaraM dvAdashAkR^itiM diva AhuH pare ardhe purIShiNam /
atheme anya upare vichakShaNe saptachakre ShaDara Ahur arpitam //12//
dvAdashAraM nahi taj jarAya varvarti chakraM pari dyAm R^itasya /
A putrA agne mithunAso atra sapta shatAni viMshatish cha tasthuH //13//
sanemi chakram ajaraM vi vavR^ita uttAnAyAM dasha yuktA vahanti /
sUryasya chakShU rajasaity AvR^itaM yasminn Atasthur bhuvanAni vishvA //14//
striyaH satIs tAm u me puMsaH AhuH pashyad akShaNvAnn vi chetad andhaH /
kavir yaH putraH sa Im A chiketa yas tA vijAnAt sa pituSh pitAsat //15//
sAkaMjAnAM saptatham Ahur ekajaM ShaD id yamA R^iShayo devajA iti /
teShAm iShTAni vihitAni dhAmasha sthAtre rejante vikR^itAni rUpashaH //16//
avaH pareNa para enA avareNa padA vatsaM bibhratI gaur ud asthAt /
sA kadrIchI kaM svid ardhaM parAgAt kva svit sUte nahi yUthe asmin //17//
avaH pareNa pitaraM yo asya vedAvaH pareNa para enAvareNa /
kavIyamAnaH ka iha pra vochad devaM manaH kuto adhi prajAtam //18//
ye arvA~nchas tAm u parAcha Ahur ye parA~nchas tA.N u arvAcha AhuH /
indrash cha yA chakrathuH soma tAni dhurA na yuktA rajaso vahanti //19//
dvA suparNA sayujA sakhAyA samAnaM vR^ikShaM pari ShasvajAte /
tayor anyaH pippalaM svAdv atty anashnann anyo abhi chAkashIti //20//
yasmin vR^ikShe madhvadaH suparNA nivishante suvate chAdhi vishve /
tasya yad AhuH pippalaM svAdv agre tan non nashad yaH pitaraM na veda //21//
yatrA suparNA amR^itasya bhakSham animeShaM vidathAbhisvaranti /
enA vishvasya bhuvanasya gopAH sa mA dhIraH pAkam atrA vivesha //22// {25}

453

yad gAyatre adhi gAyatram AhitaM traiShTubhaM vA traiShTubhAn niratakShata /
yad vA jagaj jagaty AhitaM padaM ya it tad vidus te amR^itatvam AnushuH //1//
gAyatreNa prati mimIte arkam arkeNa sAma traiShTubhena vAkam /
vAkena vAkaM dvipadA chatuShpadAkShareNa mimate sapta vANIH //2//
jagatA sindhuM divy askabhAyad rathaMtare sUryaM pary apashyat /
gAyatrasya samidhas tisra Ahus tato mahnA pra ririche mahitvA //3//
upa hvaye sudughAM dhenum etAM suhasto godhug uta dohad enAm /
shreShThaM savaM savitA sAviShan no 'bhIddho gharmas tad u Shu pra vochat //4//
hi~NkR^iNvatI vasupatnI vasUnAM vatsam ichhantI manasAbhyAgAt /
duhAm ashvibhyAM payo aghnyeyaM sA vardhatAM mahate saubhagAya //5//
gaur amImed abhi vatsaM miShantaM mUrdhAnaM hi~N~N akR^iNon mAtavA u /
sR^ikvANaM gharmam abhi vAvashAnA mimAti mAyuM payate payobhiH //6//
ayaM sa shi~Nkte yena gaur abhivR^itA mimAti mayuM dhvasanAv adhi shritA /
sA chittibhir ni hi chakAra martyAn vidyud bhavantI prati vavrim auhata //7//
anach chhaye turagAtu jIvam ejad dhruvaM madhya A pastyAnAm /
jIvo mR^itasya charati svadhAbhir amartyo martyenA sayoniH //8//
vidhuM dadrANaM salilasya pR^iShThe yuvAnaM santaM palito jagAra /
devasya pashya kAvyaM mahitvAdya mamAra sa hyaH sam Ana //9//
ya IM chakAra na so asya veda ya IM dadarsha hirug in nu tasmAt /
sa mAtur yonA parivIto antar bahuprajA nirR^itir A vivesha //10// {26}

apashyaM gopAm anipadyamAnam A cha parA cha pathibhish charantam /
sa sadhrIchIH sa viShUchIr vasAna A varIvarti bhuvaneShv antaH //11//
dyaur naH pitA janitA nAbhir atra bandhur no mAtA pR^ithivI mahIyam /
uttAnayosh chamvo3r yonir antar atrA pitA duhitur garbham AdhAt //12//
pR^ichhAmi tvA param antaM pR^ithivyAH pR^ichhAmi vR^iShNo ashvasya retaH /
pR^ichhAmi vishvasya bhuvanasya nAbhiM pR^ichhAmi vAchaH paramaM vyoma //13//
iyaM vediH paro antaH pR^ithivyA ayaM somo vR^iShNo ashvasya retaH /
ayaM yaj~no vishvasya bhuvanasya nAbhir brahmAyaM vAchaH paramaM vyoma //14//
na vi jAnAmi yad ivedam asmi niNyaH saMnaddho manasA charAmi /
yadA mAgan prathamajA R^itasyAd id vAcho ashnuve bhAgam asyAH //15//
apA~N prA~N eti svadhayA gR^ibhIto 'martyo martyenA sayoniH /
tA shashvantA viShUchInA viyantA ny anyaM chikyur na ni chikyur anyam //16//
saptArdhagarbhA bhuvanasya reto viShNos tiShThanti pradishA vidharmaNi /
te dhItibhir manasA te vipashchitaH paribhuvaH pari bhavanti vishvataH //17//
R^icho akShare parame vyoman yasmin devA adhi vishve niSheduH /
yas tan na veda kim R^ichA kariShyati ya it tad vidus te amI sam Asate //18//
R^ichaH padaM mAtrayA kalpayanto 'rdharchena chak?pur vishvam ejat /
tripAd brahma pururUpaM vi taShThe tena jIvanti pradishash chatasraH //19//
sUyavasAd bhagavatI hi bhUyA adhA vayaM bhagavantaH syAma /
addhi tR^iNam aghnye vishvadAnIM piba shuddham udakam AcharantI //20// {27}

gaur in mimAya salilAni takShatI ekapadI dvipadI sA chatuShpadI /
aShTApadI navapadI babhUvuShI sahasrAkSharA bhuvanasya pa~Nktis tasyAH samudrA adhi vi kSharanti //21//
kR^iShNaM niyAnaM harayaH suparNA apo vasAnA divam ut patanti /
taM AvavR^itrant sadanAd R^itasyAd id ghR^itena pR^ithivIM vy UduH //22//
apAd eti prathamA padvatInAM kas tad vAM mitrAvaruNA chiketa /
garbho bhAraM bharaty A chid asyA R^itaM piparti anR^itaM ni pAti //23//
virAD vAg virAT pR^ithivI virAD antarikShaM virAT prajApatiH /
virAN mR^ityuH sAdhyAnAm adhirAjo babhUva tasya bhUtaM bhavyaM vashe sa me bhUtaM bhavyaM vashe kR^iNotu //24//
shakamayaM dhUmam ArAd apashyaM viShUvatA para enAvareNa /
ukShANaM pR^ishnim apachanta vIrAs tAni dharmANi prathamAny Asan //25//
trayaH keshina R^ituthA vi chakShate saMvatsare vapata eka eShAm /
vishvam anyo abhichaShTe shachIbhir dhrAjir ekasya dadR^ishe na rUpam //26//
chatvAri vAk parimitA padAni tAni vidur brAhmaNA ye manIShiNaH /
guhA trINi nihitA ne~Ngayanti turIyaM vAcho manuShyA vadanti //27//
indraM mitraM varuNam agnim Ahur atho divyaH sa suparNo garutmAn /
ekaM sad viprA bahudhA vadanty agniM yamaM mAtarishvAnam AhuH //28// {28}

454
Book:
10

yAM kalpayanti vahatau vadhUm iva vishvarUpAM hastakR^itAM chikitsavaH /
sArAd etv apa nudAma enAm //1//
shIrShaNvatI nasvatI karNiNI kR^ityAkR^itA saMbhR^itA vishvarUpA /
sArAd etv apa nudAma enAm //2//
shUdrakR^itA rAjakR^itA strIkR^itA brahmabhiH kR^itA /
jAyA patyA nutteva kartAraM bandhv R^ichhatu //3//
anayAham oShadhyA sarvAH kR^ityA adUduSham /
yAM kShetre chakrur yAM goShu yAM vA te puruSheShu //4//
agham astv aghakR^ite shapathaH shapathIyate /
pratyak pratiprahiNmo yathA kR^ityAkR^itaM hanat //5//
pratIchIna A~Ngiraso 'dhyakSho naH purohitaH /
pratIchIH kR^ityA AkR^ityAmUn kR^ityAkR^ito jahi //6//
yas tvovAcha parehIti pratikUlam udAyyam /
taM kR^itye 'bhinivartasva mAsmAn ichho anAgasaH //7//
yas te parUMShi saMdadhau rathasyeva rbhur dhiyA /
taM gachha tatra te 'yanam aj~nAtas te 'yaM janaH //8//
ye tvA kR^itvAlebhire vidvalA abhichAriNaH /
shaMbhv ì3daM kR^ityAdUShaNaM prativartma punaHsaraM tena tvA snapayAmasi //9//
yad durbhagAM prasnapitAM mR^itavatsAm upeyima /
apaitu sarvaM mat pApaM draviNaM mopa tiShThatu //10// {1}

yat te pitR^ibhyo dadato yaj~ne vA nAma jagR^ihuH /
saMdeshyA3t sarvasmAt pApAd imA mu~nchantu tvauShadhIH //11//
devainasAt pitryAn nAmagrAhAt saMdeshyAd abhiniShkR^itAt /
mu~nchantu tvA vIrudho vIryeNa brahmaNA R^igbhiH payasA R^iShINAm //12//
yathA vAtash chyAvayati bhUmyA reNum antarikShAch chAbhram /
evA mat sarvaM durbhUtaM brahmanuttam apAyati //13//
apa krAma nAnadatI vinaddhA gardabhIva /
kartR^In nakShasveto nuttA brahmaNA vIryAvatA //14//
ayaM panthAH kR^ityeti tvA nayAmo 'bhiprahitAM prati tvA pra hiNmaH /
tenAbhi yAhi bha~njaty anasvatIva vAhinI vishvarUpA kurUtinI //15//
parAk te jyotir apathaM te arvAg anyatrAsmad ayanA kR^iNuShva /
pareNehi navatiM nAvyA3 ati durgAH srotyA mA kShaNiShThAH parehi //16//
vAta iva vR^ikShAn ni mR^iNIhi pAdaya mA gAm ashvaM puruSham uch chhiSha eShAm /
kartR^In nivR^ityetaH kR^itye 'prajAstvAya bodhaya //17//
yAM te barhiShi yAM shmashAne kShetre kR^ityAM valagaM vA nichakhnuH /
agnau vA tvA gArhapatye 'bhicheruH pAkaM santaM dhIratarA anAgasam //18//
upAhR^itam anubuddhaM nikhAtaM vairaM tsAry anv avidAma kartram /
tad etu yata AbhR^itaM tatrAshva iva vi vartatAM hantu kR^ityAkR^itaH prajAm //19//
svAyasA asayaH santi no gR^ihe vidmA te kR^itye yatidhA parUMShi /
ut tiShThaiva parehIto 'j~nAte kim ihechhasi //20// {2}

grIvAs te kR^itye pAdau chApi kartsyAmi nir drava /
indrAgnI asmAn rakShatAM yau prajAnAM prajAvatI //21//
somo rAjAdhipA mR^iDitA cha bhUtasya naH patayo mR^iDayantu //22//
bhavAsharvAv asyatAM pApakR^ite kR^ityAkR^ite /
duShkR^ite vidyutaM devahetim //23//
yady eyatha dvipadI chatuShpadI kR^ityAkR^itA saMbhR^itA vishvarUpA /
seto 'ShTApadI bhUtvA punaH parehi duchhune //24//
abhyaktAktA svaraMkR^itA sarvaM bharantI duritaM parehi /
jAnIhi kR^itye kartAraM duhiteva pitaraM svam //25//
parehi kR^itye mA tiShTho viddhasyeva padaM naya /
mR^igaH sa mR^igayus tvaM na tvA nikartum arhati //26//
uta hanti pUrvAsinaM pratyAdAyApara iShvA /
uta pUrvasya nighnato ni hanty aparaH prati //27//
etad dhi shR^iNu me vacho 'thehi yata eyatha /
yas tvA chakAra taM prati //28//
anAgohatyA vai bhImA kR^itye mA no gAm ashvaM puruShaM vadhIH /
yatrayatrAsi nihitA tatas tvot thApayAmasi parNAl laghIyasI bhava //29//
yadi stha tamasAvR^itA jAlenabhihitA iva /
sarvAH saMlupyetaH kR^ityAH punaH kartre pra hiNmasi //30//
kR^ityAkR^ito valagino 'bhiniShkAriNaH prajAm /
mR^iNIhi kR^itye moch chhiSho 'mUn kR^ityAkR^ito jahi //31//
yathA sUryo muchyate tamasas pari rAtriM jahAty uShasashcha ketUn /
evAhaM sarvaM durbhUtaM kartraM kR^ityAkR^itA kR^itaM hastIva rajo duritaM jahAmi //32// {3}

455

kena pArShNI AbhR^ite pUruShasya kena mAMsaM saMbhR^itaM kena gulphau /
kenA~NgulIH peshanIH kena khAni kenochchhla~Nkhau madhyataH kaH pratiShThAm //1//
kasmAn nu gulphAv adharAv akR^iNvann aShThIvantAv uttarau puruShasya /
ja~Nghe nirR^itya ny adadhuH kva svij jAnunoH saMdhI ka u tach chiketa //2//
chatuShTayaM yujate saMhitAntaM jAnubhyAm UrdhvaM shithiraM kabandham /
shroNI yad UrU ka u taj jajAna yAbhyAM kusindhaM sudR^iDhaM babhUva //3//
kati devAH katame ta Asan ya uro grIvAsh chikyuH puruShasya /
kati stanau vy adadhuH kaH kaphodau kati skandhAn kati pR^iShTIr achinvan //4//
ko asya bAhU sam abharad vIryAM karavAd iti /
aMsau ko asya tad devaH kusindhe adhy A dadhau //5//
kaH sapta khAni vi tatarda shIrShaNi karNAv imau nAsike chakShaNI mukham /
yeShAM purutrA vijayasya mahnani chatuShpAdo dvipado yanti yAmam //6//
hanvor hi jihvAm adadhAt purUchIm adhA mahIm adhi shishrAya vAcham /
sa A varIvarti bhuvaneShv antar apo vasAnaH ka u tach chiketa //7//
mastiShkam asya yatamo lalAtaM kakATikAM prathamo yaH kapAlam /
chitvA chityaM hanvoH pUruShasya divaM ruroha katamaH sa devaH //8//
priyApriyANi bahulA svapnaM saMbAdhatandyaH /
AnandAn ugro nandAMsh cha kasmAd vahati pUruShaH //9//
Artir avartir nirR^itiH kuto nu puruShe 'matiH /
rAddhiH samR^iddhir avyR^iddhir matir uditayaH kutaH //10// {4}

ko asminn Apo vy adadhAt viShUvR^itaH purUvR^itaH sindhusR^ityAya jAtAH /
tIvrA aruNA lohinIs tAmradhUmrA UrdhvA avAchIH puruShe tirashchIH //11//
ko asmin rUpam adadhAt ko mahmAnaM cha nAma cha /
gAtuM ko asmin kaH ketuM kash charitrAni puruShe //12//
ko asmin prANaM avayat ko apAnaM vyAnam u /
samAnam asmin ko devo 'dhi shishrAya puruShe //13//
ko asmin yaj~nam adadhAd eko devo 'dhi puruShe /
ko asmint satyaM ko 'nR^itaM kuto mR^ityuH kuto 'mR^itam //14//
ko asmai vAsaH pary adadhAt ko asyAyur akalpayat /
balaM ko asmai prAyachhat ko asyAkalpayaj javam //15//
kenApo anv atanuta kenAhar akarod ruche /
uShasaM kenAnv ainddha kena sAyaMbhavaM dade //16//
ko asmin reto ny adadhAt tantur A tAyatAm iti /
medhAM ko asminn adhy auhat ko bANaM ko nR^ito dadhau //17//
kenemAM bhUmim aurNot kena pary abhavad divam /
kenAbhi mahnA parvatAn kena karmANi puruShaH //18//
kena parjanyam anv eti kena somaM vichakShaNam /
kena yaj~nam cha shraddhAM cha kenAsmin nihitaM manaH //19//
kena shrotriyam Apnoti kenemaM parameShThinam /
kenemam agniM pUruShaH kena saMvatsaraM mame //20// {5}

brahma shrotriyam Apnoti brahmemaM parameShThinam /
brahmemam agniM pUruSho brahma saMvatsaraM mame //21//
kena devA.N anu kShiyati kena daivajanIr vishaH /
kenedam anyan nakShatraM kena sat kShatram uchyate //22//
brahma devA.N anu kShiyati brahma daivajanIr vishaH /
brahmedam anyan nakShatraM brahma sat kShatram uchyate //23//
keneyaM bhUmir vihitA kena dyaur uttarA hitA /
kenedam UrdhvaM tiryak chAntarikSham vyacho hitam //24//
brahmaNA bhUmir vihitA brahma dyaur uttarA hitA /
brahmedam UrdhvaM tiryak chAntarikShaM vyacho hitam //25//
mUrdhAnam asya saMsIvyAtharvA hR^idayaM cha yat /
mastiShkAd UrdhvaH prairayat pavamAno 'dhi shIrShataH //26//
tad vA atharvaNaH shiro devakoshaH samubjitaH /
tat prANo abhi rakShati shiro annam atho manaH //27//
Urdhvo nu sR^iShTA3s tirya~N nu sR^iShTA3s sarvA dishaH puruSha A babhUvA3m? /
puraM yo brahmaNo veda yasyAH puruSha uchyate //28//
yo vai tAM brahmaNo vedAmR^itenAvR^itAM puram /
tasmai brahma cha brAhmAsh cha chakShuH prANaM prajAM daduH //29//
na vai tam chakShur jahAti na prANo jarasaH purA /
puraM yo brahmaNo veda yasyAH puruSha uchyate //30//
aShTAchakrA navadvArA devAnAM pUr ayodhyA /
tasyAM hiraNyayaH koshaH svargo jyotiShAvR^itaH //31//
tasmin hiraNyaye koshe tryare tripratiShThite /
tasmin yad yakSham Atmanvat tad vai brahmavido viduH //32//
prabhrAjamAnAM hariNIM yashasA saMparIvR^itAm /
puraM hiraNyayIM brahmA viveshAparAjitAm //33// {6}

456

ayaM me varaNo maNiH sapatnakShayaNo vR^iShA /
tenA rabhasva tvaM shatrUn pra mR^iNIhi durasyataH //1//
praiNAn chhR^iNIhi pra mR^iNA rabhasva maNis te astu puraetA purastAt /
avArayanta varaNena devA abhyAchAram asurANAM shvaHshvaH //2//
ayaM maNir varaNo vishvabheShajaH sahasrAkSho harito hiraNyayaH /
sa te shatrUn adharAn pAdayAti pUrvas tAn dabhnuhi ye tvA dviShanti //3//
ayaM te kR^ityAM vitatAm pauruSheyAd ayaM bhayAt /
ayaM tvA sarvasmAt pApAd varaNo vArayiShyate //4//
varaNo vArayAtA ayaM devo vanaspatiH /
yakShmo yo asminn AviShTas tam u devA avIvaran //5//
svapnaM suptvA yadi pashyAsi pApaM mR^igaH sR^itiM yati dhAvAd ajuShTAm /
parikShavAch chhakuneH pApavAdAd ayaM maNir varaNo vArayiShyate //6//
arAtyAs tvA nirR^ityA abhichArAd atho bhayAt /
mR^ityor ojIyaso vadhAd varaNo vArayiShyate //7//
yan me mAtA yan me pitA bhrAtaro yach cha me svA yad enash chakR^imA vayam /
tato no vArayiShyate 'yaM devo vanaspatiH //8//
varaNena pravyathitA bhrAtR^ivyA me sabandhavaH /
asUrtaM rajo apy agus te yantv adhamaM tamaH //9//
ariShTo 'ham ariShTagur AyuShmAnt sarvapUruShaH /
tam mAyaM varaNo maNiH pari pAtu dishodishaH //10// {7}

ayaM me varaNa urasi rAjA devo vanaspatiH /
sa me shatrUn vi bAdhatAm indro dasyUn ivAsurAn //11//
imaM bibharmi varaNam AyuShmAn chhatashAradaH /
sa me rAShTraM cha kShatraM cha pashUn ojash cha me dadhat //12//

evA sapatnAn me bha~Ngdhi pUrvAn jAtA.N utAparAn varaNas tvAbhi rakShatu //13//
yathA vAtash chAgnish cha vR^ikShAn psAto vanaspatIn /
evA sapatnAn me psAhi pUrvAn jAtA.N utAparAn varaNas tvAbhi rakShatu //14//
yathA vAtena prakShINA vR^ikShAH shere nyarpitAH /
evA sapatnAMs tvaM mama pra kShiNIhi ny arpaya /
pUrvAn jAtA.N utAparAn varaNas tvAbhi rakShatu //15//
tAMs tvaM pra chhinddhi varaNa purA diShTAt purAyuShaH /
ya enaM pashuShu dipsanti ye chAsya rAShTradipsavaH //16//
yathA sUryo atibhAti yathAsmin teja Ahitam /
evA me varaNo maNiH kIrtiM bhUtiM ni yachhatu /
tejasA mA sam ukShatu yashasA sam anaktu mA //17//
yathA yashash chandramasy Aditye cha nR^ichakShasi /
evA me varaNo maNiH kIrtiM bhUtiM ni yachhatu /
tejasA mA sam ukShatu yashasA sam anaktu mA //18//

evA me varaNo maNiH kIrtiM bhUtiM ni yachhatu /
tejasA mA sam ukShatu yashasA sam anaktu mA //19//

evA me varaNo maNiH kIrtiM bhUtiM ni yachhatu /
tejasA mA sam ukShatu yashasA sam anaktu mA //20// {8}

yathA yashaH somapIthe madhuparke yathA yashaH /
evA me varaNo maNiH kIrtiM bhUtiM ni yachhatu /
tejasA mA sam ukShatu yashasA sam anaktu mA //21//

evA me varaNo maNiH kIrtiM bhUtiM ni yachhatu /
tejasA mA sam ukShatu yashasA sam anaktu mA //25// {9}

457

indrasya prathamo ratho devAnAm aparo ratho varuNasya tR^itIya it /
ahInAm apamA ratha sthAnum Arad athArShat //1//
darbhaH shochis tarUNakam ashvasya vAraH paruShasya vAraH /
rathasya bandhuram //2//
ava shveta padA jahi pUrveNa chApareNa cha /
udaplutam iva dArv ahInAm arasaM viShaM vAr ugram //3//
araMghuSho nimajyonmaja punar abravIt /
udaplutam iva dArv ahInAm arasaM viShaM vAr ugram //4//
paidvo hanti kasarNIlaM paidvaH shvitram utAsitam /
paidvo ratharvyAH shiraH saM bibheda pR^idAkvAH //5//

ahIn vy asyatAt patho yena smA vayam emasi //6//

imAny arvataH padAhighnyo vAjinIvataH //7//

aghAshvasyedaM bheShajam ubhayo svajasya cha /
indro me 'him aghAyantam ahiM paidvo arandhayat //10// {10}

paidvasya manmahe vayaM sthirasya sthiradhAmnaH /
ime pashchA pR^idAkavaH pradIdhyata Asate //11//
naShTAsavo naShTaviShA hatA indreNa vajriNA /

darviM karikrataM shvitraM darbheShv asitaM jahi //13//

hiraNyayIbhir abhribhir girInAm upa sAnuShu //14//
Ayam agan yuvA bhiShak pR^ishnihAparAjitaH /
sa vai svajasya jambhana ubhayor vR^ishchikasya cha //15//
indro me 'him arandhayan mitrash cha varuNash cha /
vAtAparjanyobhA //16//
indro me 'him arandhayat pR^idAkuM cha pR^idAkvam /
svajaM tirashchirAjiM kasarNIlaM dashonasim //17//
indro jaghAna prathamaM janitAram ahe tava /
teShAm u tR^ihyamANAnAM kaH svit teShAm asad rasaH //18//
saM hi shIrShANy agrabhaM pau~njiShTha iva karvaram /
sindhor madhyaM paretya vy anijam aher viSham //19//

hatAs tirashchirAjayo nipiShTAsaH pR^idAkavaH //20// {11}

oShadhInAm ahaM vR^iNa urvarIr iva sAdhuyA /

kAndAviShaM kanaknakaM niraitv aitu te viSham //22//

yeShAM jAtAni bahudhA mahAnti tebhyaH sarpebhyo namasA vidhema //23//
taudI nAmAsi kanyA ghR^itAchI nAma vA asi /
adhaspadena te padam A dade viShadUShaNam //24//
a~NgAda~NgAt pra chyAvaya hR^idayam pari varjaya /
adhA viShasya yat tejo 'vAchInaM tad etu te //25//
Are abhUd viSham araud viShe viSham aprAg api /
agnir viSham aher nir adhAt somo nir aNayIt /
daMShTAram anv agAd viSham ahir amR^ita //26// {12}

indrasyauja sthendrasya saha sthendrasya balaM sthendrasya vIrya1M sthendrasya nR^imNaM stha /

brAhmaNA.N abhyAvarte /
