459

arAtIyor bhrAtR^ivyasya durhArdo dviShataH shiraH /
api vR^ishchAmy ojasA //1//

taM devA bibhrato maNiM sarvAMl lokAn yudhAjayan /
sa ebhyo jitim id duhe bhUyobhUyaH shvaHshvas tena tvaM dviShato jahi //16//
yam abadhnAd bR^ihaspatir vAtAya maNim Ashave /

taM dhAtA praty amu~nchata sa bhUtaM vy akalpayat /
tena tvaM dviShato jahi //21//
yam abadhnAd bR^ihaspatir devebhyo asurakShitim /

sa mAyaM maNir Agamat tejasA tviShyA saha yashasA kIrtyA saha //27//
yam abadhnAd bR^ihaspatir devebhyo asurakShitim /
sa mAyaM maNir Agamat sarvAbhir bhUtibhiH saha //28//
tam imaM devatA maNiM mahyaM dadatu puShTaye /
abhibhuM kShatravardhanaM sapatnadambhanaM maNim //29//
brahmaNA tejasA saha prati mu~nchAmi me shivam /
asapatnaH sapatnahA sapatnAn me 'dharAm? akaH //30// {20}

uttaraM dviShato mAm ayaM maNiH kR^iNotu devajAH /
yasya lokA ime trayaH payo dugdham upAsate /
sa mAyam adhi rohatu maNiH shraiShThyAya mUrdhataH //31//
yaM devAH pitaro manuShyA upajIvanti sarvadA /
sa mAyam adhi rohatu maNiH shraiShThyAya mUrdhataH //32//
yathA bIjam urvarAyAM kR^iShTe phAlena rohati /
evA mayi prajA pashavo 'nnamannaM vi rohatu //33//
yasmai tvA yaj~navardhana maNe pratyamuchaM shivam /
taM tvaM shatadakShiNa maNe shraiShThyAya jinvatAt //34//
etam idhmaM samAhitaM juShaNo agne prati harya homaiH /
tasmin vidhema sumatiM svasti prajAm chakShuH pashUnt samiddhe jAtavedasi brahmaNA //35// {21}

460

kasminn a~Nge tapo asyAdhi tiShThati kasminn a~Nga R^itam asyAdhy Ahitam /
kva vrataM kva shraddhAsya tiShThati kasminn a~Nge satyam asya pratiShThitam //1//
kasmAd a~NgAd dIpyate agnir asya kasmAd a~NgAt pavate mAtarishva /
kasmAd a~NgAd vi mimIte 'dhi chandramA maha skambhasya mimAno a~Ngam //2//
kasminn a~Nge tiShThati bhUmir asya kasminn a~Nge tiShThaty antarikSham /
kasminn a~Nge tiShThaty AhitA dyauH kasminn a~Nge tiShThaty uttaraM divaH //3//
kva1 prepsan dIpyata Urdhvo agniH kva1 prepsan pavate mAtarishvA /
yatra prepsantIr abhiyanty AvR^itaH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //4//
kvArdhamAsAH kva yanti mAsAH saMvatsareNa saha saMvidAnAH /
yatra yanty R^itavo yatrArtavAH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //5//
kva1 prepsantI yuvatI virUpe ahorAtre dravataH saMvidAne /
yatra prepsantIr abhiyanty ApaH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //6//
yasmint stabdhvA prajApatir lokAnt sarvAm? adhArayat /
skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //7//
yat paramam avamam yach cha madhyamaM prajApatiH sasR^ije vishvarUpam /
kiyatA skambhaH pra vivesha tatra yan na prAvishat kiyat tad babhUva //8//
kiyatA skambhaH pra vivesha bhUtam kiyad bhaviShyad anvAshaye 'sya /
ekaM yad a~Ngam akR^iNot sahasradhA kiyatA skambhaH pra vivesha tatra //9//
yatra lokAmsh cha koshAMsh chApo brahma janA viduH /
asach cha yatra sach chAnta skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //10// {22}

yatra tapaH parAkramya vrataM dhArayaty uttaram /
R^itaM cha yatra shraddhA chApo brahma samAhitAH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //11//
yasmin bhUmir antarikShaM dyaur yasminn adhy AhitA /
yatrAgnish chandramAH sUryo vAtas tiShThanty ArpitAH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //12//
yasya trayastriMshad devA a~Nge sarve samAhitAH /
skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //13//
yatra R^iShayaH prathamajA R^ichaH sAma yajur mahI /
ekarShir yasminn ArpitaH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //14//
yatrAmR^itaM cha mR^ityush cha puruShe 'dhi samAhite /
samudro yasya nADya1H puruShe 'dhi samAhitAH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //15//
yasya chatasraH pradisho nADya1s tiShThanti prathamAH /
yaj~no yatra parAkrAntaH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //16//
ye puruShe brahma vidus te viduH parameShThinam /
yo veda parameShThinaM yash cha veda prajApatim /
jyeShThaM ye brAhmaNaM vidus te skambham anusaMviduH //17//
yasya shiro vaishvAnarash chakShur a~Ngiraso 'bhavan /
a~NgAni yasya yAtavaH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //18//
yasya brahma mukham Ahur jihvAM madhukashAm uta /
virAjam Udho yasyAhuH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //19//
yasmAd R^icho apAtakShan yajur yasmAd apAkaShan /
sAmAni yasya lomAny atharvA~Ngiraso mukhaM skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //20// {23}

asachchAkhAM pratiShThantIM paramam iva janA viduH /
uto san manyante 'vare ye te shAkhAm upAsate //21//
yatrAdityAsh cha rudrAsh cha vasavash cha samAhitAH /
bhUtaM cha yatra bhavyaM cha sarve lokAH pratiShThitAH skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //22//
yasya trayastriMshad devA nidhiM rakShanti sarvadA /
nidhiM tam adya ko veda yaM devA abhirakShatha //23//
yatra devA brahmavido brahma jyeShTham upAsate /
yo vai tAn vidyAt pratyakShaM sa brahmA veditA syAt //24//
bR^ihanto nAma te devA ye 'sataH pari jaj~nire /
ekaM tad a~NgaM skambhasyAsad AhuH paro janAH //25//
yatra skambhaH prajanayan purANaM vyavartayat /
ekaM tad a~NgaM skambhasya purANam anusaMviduH //26//
yasya trayastriMshad devA a~Nge gAtrA vibhejire /
tAn vai trayastriMshad devAn eke brahamvido viduH //27//
hiraNyagarbham paramam anatyudyaM janA viduH /
skambhas tad agre prAsi~nchad dhiraNyaM loke antarA //28//
skambhe lokAH skambhe tapaH skambhe 'dhy R^itam Ahitam /
skambha tvA veda pratyakSham indre sarvaM samAhitam //29//
indre lokA indre tapa indre 'dhy R^itam Ahitam /
indraM tvA veda pratyakShaM skambhe sarvaM pratiShThitam //30// {24}

nAma nAmnA johavIti purA sUryAt puroShasaH /
yad ajaH prathamaM saMbabhUva sa ha tat svarAjyam iyAya yasmAn nAnyat param asti bhUtam //31//
yasya bhUmiH pramAntarikSham utodaram /
divaM yash chakre mUrdhAnaM tasmai jyeShThAya brahmaNe namaH //32//
yasya sUryash chakShush chandramAsh cha punarNavaH /
agniM yash chakra Asya1M tasmai jyeShThAya brahmaNe namaH //33//
yasya vAtaH prANApAnau chakShur a~Ngiraso 'bhavan /
disho yash chakre praj~nAnIs tasmai jyeShThAya brahmaNe namaH //34//
skambho dAdhAra dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe ime skambho dAdhArorv antarikSham /
skambho dAdhAra pradishaH ShaD urvIH skambha idaM vishvaM bhuvanam A vivesha //35//
yaH shramAt tapaso jAto lokAnt sarvAnt samAnashe /
somaM yash chakre kevalaM tasmai jyeShThAya brahmaNe namaH //36//
kathaM vAto nelayati kathaM na ramate manaH /
kim ApaH satyaM prepsantIr nelayanti kadA chana //37//
mahad yakShaM bhuvanasya madhye tapasi krAntaM salilasya pR^iShThe /
tasmin chhrayante ya u ke cha devA vR^ikShasya skandhaH parita iva shAkhAH //38//
yasmai hastAbhyAM pAdAbhyAM vAchA shrotreNa chakShuShA /
yasmai devAH sadA baliM prayachhanti vimite 'mitaM skambhaM taM brUhi katamaH svid eva saH //39//
apa tasya hataM tamo vyAvR^ittaH sa pApmanA /
sarvANi tasmin jyotIMShi yAni trINi prajApatau //40//
yo vetasaM hiraNyayaM tiShThantaM salile veda /
sa vai guhyaH prajApatiH //41//
tantram eke yuvatI virUpe abhyAkrAmaM vayataH ShaNmayUkham /
prAnyA tantUMs tirate dhatte anyA nApa vR^i~njAte na gamAto antam //42//
tayor ahaM parinR^ityantyor iva na vi jAnAmi yatarA parastAt /
pumAn enad vayaty ud gR^iNanti pumAn enad vi jabhArAdhi nAke //43//
ime mayUkhA upa tastabhur divaM sAmAni chakrus tasarANi vAtave //44// {25}

461

yo bhUtaM cha bhavyaM cha sarvaM yash chAdhitiShThati /
sv a1ryasya cha kevalaM tasmai jyeShThAya brahmaNe namaH //1//
skambheneme viShTabhite dyaush cha bhUmish cha tiShThataH /
skambha idaM sarvam Atmanvad yat prANan nimiShach cha yat //2//
tisro ha prajA atyAyam Ayan ny anyA arkam abhito 'vishanta /
bR^ihan ha tasthau rajaso vimAno harito hariNIr A vivesha //3//
dvAdasha pradhayash chakram ekaM trINi nabhyAni ka u tach chiketa /
tatrAhatAs trINi shatAni sha~NkavaH ShaShTish cha khIlA avichAchalA ye //4//
idaM savitar vi jAnIhi ShaD yamA eka ekajaH /
tasmin hApitvam ichhante ya eShAm eka ekajaH //5//
AviH san nihitaM guhA jaran nAma mahat padam /
tatredaM sarvam Arpitam ejat prANat pratiShThitam //6//
ekachakraM vartata ekanemi sahasrAkSharaM pra puro ni pashchA /
ardhena vishvaM bhuvanaM jajAna yad asyArdhaM kva1 tad babhUva //7//
pa~nchavAhI vahatyagram eShAM praShTayo yuktA anusaMvahanti /
ayAtam asya dadR^ishe na yAtaM paraM nedIyo 'varaM davIyaH //8//
tiryagbilash chamasa Urdhvabudhnas tasmin yasho nihitaM vishvarUpam /
tad Asata R^iShayaH sapta sAkaM ye asya gopA mahato babhUvuH //9//
yA purastAd yujyate yA cha pashchAd yA vishvato yujyate yA cha sarvataH /
yayA yaj~naH prA~N tAyate tAM tvA pR^ichhAmi katamA sA R^ichAm //10// {26}

yad ejati patati yach cha tiShThati prANad aprANan nimiShach cha yad bhuvat /
tad dAdhAra pR^ithivIM vishvarUpaM tat saMbhUya bhavaty ekam eva //11//
anantaM vitataM purutrAnantam antavach chA samante /
te nAkapAlash charati vichinvan vidvAn bhUtam uta bhavyam asya //12//
prajApatish charati garbhe antar adR^ishyamAno bahudhA vi jAyate /
ardhena vishvaM bhuvanaM jajAna yad asyArdhaM katamaH sa ketuH //13//
UrdhvaM bharantam udakaM kumbhenevodahAryam /
pashyanti sarve chakShuShA na sarve manasA viduH //14//
dUre pUrNena vasati dUra Unena hIyate /
mahad yakShaM bhuvanasya madhye tasmai baliM rAShTrabhR^ito bharanti //15//
yataH sUryaH udety astaM yatra cha gachhati /
tad eva manye 'haM jyeShThaM tad u nAty eti kiM chana //16//
ye arvA~N madhya uta vA purANaM vedaM vidvAMsam abhito vadanti /
Adityam eva te pari vadanti sarve agniM dvitIyaM trivR^itaM cha haMsam //17//
sahasrAhNyaM viyatAv asya pakShau harer haMsasya patataH svargam /
sa devAnt sarvAn urasy upadadya saMpashyan yAti bhuvanAni vishvA //18//
satyenordhvas tapati brahmaNArvA~N vi pashyati /
prANena tirya~N prANati yasmin jyeShTham adhi shritam //19//
yo vai te vidyAd araNI yAbhyAM nirmathyate vasu /
sa vidvAn jyeShThaM manyeta sa vidyAd brAhmaNaM mahat //20// {27}

apAd agre sam abhavat so agre sva1r Abharat /
chatuShpAd bhUtvA bhogyaH sarvam Adatta bhojanam //21//
bhogyo bhavad atho annam adad bahu /
yo devam uttarAvantam upAsAtai sanAtanam //22//
sanAtanam enam Ahur utAdya syAt punarNavaH /
ahorAtre pra jAyete anyo anyasya rUpayoH //23//
shataM sahasram ayutaM nyarbudam asaMkhyeyaM svam asmin niviShTam /
tad asya ghnanty abhipashyata eva tasmAd devo rochat eSha etat //24//
bAlAd ekam aNIyaskam utaikaM neva dR^ishyate /
tataH pariShvajIyasI devatA sA mama priyA //25//
iyaM kalyANy a1jarA martyasyAmR^itA gR^ihe /
yasmai kR^itA shaye sa yash chakAra jajAra saH //26//
tvaM strI tvaM pumAn asi tvaM kumAra uta vA kumArI//
tvaM jIrNo daNDena va~nchasi tvaM jAto bhavasi vishvatomukhaH //27//
utaiShAM pitota vA putra eShAm utaiShAM jyeShTha uta vA kaniShThah /
eko ha devo manasi praviShTaH prathamo jAtaH sa u garbhe antah //28//
pUrNAt pUrNam ud achati pUrNaM pUrNena sichyate /
uto tad adya vidyAma yatas tat pariShichyate //29//
eShA sanatnI sanam eva jAtaiShA purANI pari sarvaM babhUva /
mahI devy ù1Shaso vibhAtI saikenaikena miShatA vi chaShTe //30// {28}

avir vai nAma devatartenAste parIvR^itA /
tasyA rUpeNeme vR^ikShA haritA haritasrajaH //31//
anti santaM na jahAty anti santaM na pashyati /
devasya pashya kAvyaM na mamAra na jIryati //32//
apUrveNeShitA vAchas tA vadanti yathAyatham /
vadantIr yatra gachhanti tad Ahur brAhmaNaM mahat //33//
yatra devAsh cha manuShyAsh chArA nAbhAv iva shritAH /
apAM tvA puShpaM pR^ichhAmi yatra tan mAyayA hitam //34//
yebhir vAta iShitaH pravAti ye dadante pa~ncha dishaH sadhrIchIH /
ya Ahutim atyamanyanta devA apAM netAraH katame ta Asan //35//
imAm eShAM pR^ithivIM vasta eko 'ntarikShaM pary eko babhUva /
divam eShAM dadate yo vidhartA vishvA AshAH prati rakShanty eke //36//
yo vidyAt sUtraM vitataM yasminn otAH prajA imAH /
sUtraM sUtrasya yo vidyAd sa vidyAd brAhmaNaM mahat //37//
vedAhaM sUtraM vitataM yasminn otAH prajA imAH /
sUtraM sUtrasyAhaM vedAtho yad brAhmaNaM mahad //38//
yad antarA dyAvApR^ithivI agnir ait pradahan vishvadAvyaH /
yatrAtiShThann ekapatnIH parastAt kvevAsIn mAtarishvA tadAnIm //39//
apsv AsIn mAtarishvA praviShTaH praviShTA devAH salilAny Asan//
bR^ihan ha tasthau rajaso vimAnaH pavamAno harita A vivesha //40//
uttareNeva gayatrIm amR^ite 'dhi vi chakrame /
sAmnA ye sAma saMvidur ajas tad dadR^ishe kva //41//
niveshanaH saMgamano vasUnAM deva iva savitA satyadharmA /
indro na tasthau samare dhanAnAm //42//
puNDarIkaM navadvAraM tribhir guNebhir AvR^itam /
tasmin yad yakSham Atmanvat tad vai brahmavido viduh //43//
akAmo dhIro amR^itaH svayaMbhU rasena tR^ipto na kutash chanonaH /
tam eva vidvAn na bibhAya mR^ityor AtmAnaM dhIram ajaraM yuvAnam //44// {29}

462

aghAyatAm api nahyA mukhAni sapatneShu vajram arpayaitam /
indreNa dattA prathamA shataudanA bhrAtR^ivyaghnI yajamAnasya gAtuH //1//
vediSh Te charma bhavatu barhir lomAni yAni te /
eShA tvA rashanAgrabhId grAvA tvaiSho 'dhi nR^ityatu //2//
bAlAs te prokShaNIH santu jIhvA saM mArShTu aghnye /
shuddhA tvaM yaj~niyA bhUtvA divaM prehi shataudane //3//
yaH shataudanAM pachati kAmapreNa sa kalpate /
prItA hy asya R^itvijaH sarve yanti yathAyatham //4//
sa svargam A rohati yatrAdas tridivaM divaH /
apUpanAbhiM kR^itvA yo dadAti shataudanAm //5//
sa tAMl lokAnt sam Apnoti ye divyA ye cha pArthivAH /
hiraNyajyotiShaM kR^itvA yo dadAti shataudanAm //6//
ye te devi shamitAraH paktAro ye cha te janAH /
te tvA sarve gopsyanti maibhyo bhaiShIH shataudane //7//
vasavas tvA dakShiNata uttarAn marutas tvA /
AdityAH pashchAd gopsyanti sAgniShTomam ati drava //8//
devAH pitaro manuShyA gandharvApsarasash cha ye /
te tvA sarve gopsyanti sAtirAtram ati drava //9//
antarikShaM divaM bhUmim AdityAn maruto dishaH /
lokAnt sa sarvAn Apnoti yo dadAti shataudanAm //10// {30}

ghR^itaM prokShantI subhagA devI devAn gamiShyati /
paktAram aghnye mA hiMsIr divaM prehi shataudane //11//
ye devA diviShado antarikShasadash cha ye ye cheme bhUmyAm adhi /
tebhyas tvaM dhukShva sarvadA kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //12//
yat te shiro yat te mukhaM yau karNau ye cha te hanU /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //13//
yau ta oShThau ye nAsike ye shR^i~Nge ye cha te 'kShiNI /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //14//
yat te klomA yad dhR^idayaM purItat sahakaNThikA /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //15//
yat te yakR^id ye matasne yad Antram yAsh cha te gudAH /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //16//
yas te plAshir yo vaniShThur yau kukShI yach cha charma te /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre ksIraM sarpir atho madhu //17//
yat te majjA yad asthi yan maMsaM yach cha lohitam /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre ksIraM sarpir atho madhu //18//
yau te bAhU ye doShaNI yAv aMsau yA cha te kakut /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre ksIraM sarpir atho madhu //19//
yAs te grIvA ye skandhA yAH pR^iShTIr yAsh cha parshavaH /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //20// {31}

yau ta urU aShThIvantau ye shroNI yA cha te bhasat /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //21//
yat te puchhaM ye te bAlA yad Udho ye cha te stanAH /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //22//
yAs te ja~NghAH yAH kuShThikA R^ichharA ye cha te shaphAH /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //23//
yat te charma shataudane yAni lomAny aghnye /
AmikShAM duhratAM dAtre kShIraM sarpir atho madhu //24//
kroDau te stAM purodAshAv AjyenAbhighAritau /
tau pakShau devi kR^itvA sA paktAraM divaM vaha //25//
ulUkhale musale yash cha charmaNi yo vA shUrpe taNDulaH kaNaH /
yaM vA vAto mAtarishvA pavamAno mamAthAgniSh Tad dhotA suhutaM kR^iNotu //26//
apo devIr madhumatIr ghR^itashchuto brahmaNAM hasteShu prapR^ithak sAdayAmi /
yatkAma idam abhiShi~nchAmi vo 'haM tan me sarvaM saM padyatAM vayaM syAma patayo rayINAm //27// {32}

463

namas te jAyamAnAyai jAtAyA uta te namaH /
bAlebhyaH shaphebhyo rUpAyAghnye te namaH //1//
yo vidyAt sapta pravataH sapta vidyAt parAvataH /
shiro yaj~nasya yo vidyAt sa vashAM prati gR^ihNIyAt //2//
vedAhaM sapta pravataH sapta veda parAvataH /
shiro yaj~nasyAhaM veda somaM chAsyAM vichakShaNam //3//
yayA dyaur yayA pR^ithivI yayApo gupitA imAH /
vashAM sahasradhArAM brahmaNAchhAvadAmasi //4//
shataM kaMsAH shataM dogdhAraH shataM goptAro adhi pR^iShThe asyAH /
ye devAs tasyAM prANanti te vashAM vidur ekadhA //5//
yaj~napadIrAkShIrA svadhAprANA mahIlukA /
vashA parjanyapatnI devA.N apy eti brahmaNA //6//
anu tvAgniH prAvishad anu somo vashe tvA /
Udhas te bhadre parjanyo vidyutas te stanA vashe //7//
apas tvaM dhukShe prathamA urvarA aparA vashe /
tR^itIyaM rAShTraM dhukShe 'nnaM kShIraM vashe tvam //8//
yad Adityair hUyamAnopAtiShTha R^itavari /
indraH sahasraM pAtrAnt somaM tvApAyayad vashe //9//
yad anUchIndram air At tvA R^iShabho 'hvayat /
tasmAt te vR^itrahA payaH kShIraM kruddho 'harad vashe //10// {33}

yat te kruddho dhanapatir A kShIram aharad vashe /
idaM tad adya nAkas triShu pAtreShu rakShati //11//
triShu pAtreShu taM somam A devy aharad vashA /
atharvA yatra dIkShito barhiShy Asta hiraNyaye //12//
saM hi somenAgata sam u sarveNa padvatA /
vashA samudram adhy aShThad gandharvaiH kalibhiH saha //13//
saM hi vAtenAgata sam u sarvaiH patatribhiH /
vashA samudre prAnR^ityad R^ichaH sAmAni bibhratI //14//
saM hi sUryeNAgata sam u sarveNa chakShuShA /
vashA samudram aty akhyad bhadrA jyotIMShi bibhratI //15//
abhIvR^itA hiraNyena yad atiShTha R^itAvari /
ashvaH samudro bhUtvAdhy askandad vashe tvA //16//
tad bhadrAH sam agachhanta vashA deShTry atho svadhA /
atharvA yatra dIkShito barhiShy Asta hiraNyaye //17//
vashA mAtA rAjanyasya vashA mAtA svadhe tava /
vashAyA yaj~na AyudhaM tatash chittam ajAyata //18//
Urdhvo bindur ud acharad brahmaNaH kakudAd adhi /
tatas tvaM jaj~niShe vashe tato hotAjAyata //19//
Asnas te gAthA abhavann uShNihAbhyo balaM vashe /
pAjasyAj jaj~ne yaj~na stanebhyo rashmayas tava //20// {34}

IrmAbhyAm ayanaM jAtaM sakthibhyAM cha vashe tava /
Antrebhyo jaj~nire atrA udarAd adhi vIrudhaH //21//
yad udaraM varuNasyAnuprAvishathA vashe /
tatas tvA brahmod ahvayat sa hi netram avet tava //22//
sarve garbhAd avepanta jAyamAnAd asUsvaH /
sasUva hi tAm Ahu vasheti brahmabhiH k?ptaH sa hy asyA bandhuH //23//
yudha ekaH saM sR^ijati yo asyA eka id vashI /
tarAMsi yaj~nA abhavan tarasAM chakShur abhavad vashA //24//
vashA yaj~naM praty agR^ihNAd vashA sUryam adhArayat /
vashAyAm antar avishad odano brahmaNA saha //25//
vashAm evAmR^itam Ahur vashAM mR^ityum upAsate /
vashedaM sarvam abhavad devA manuShyA3 asurAH pitara R^iShayaH //26//
ya evaM vidyAt sa vashAM prati gR^ihNIyAt /
tathA hi yaj~naH sarvapAd duhe dAtre 'napasphuran //27//
tisro jihvA varuNasyAntar dIdyaty Asani /
tAsAM yA madhye rAjati sA vashA duShpratigrahA //28//
chaturdhA reto abhavad vashAyAH /
Apas turIyam amR^itaM turIyaM yaj~nas turIyaM pashavas turIyam //29//
vashA dyaur vashA pR^ithivI vashA viShNuH prajApatiH /
vashAyA dugdham apibant sAdhyA vasavash cha ye //30//
vashAyA dugdhaM pItvA sAdhyA vasavash cha ye /
te vai bradhnasya viShTapi payo asyA upAsate //31//
somam enAm eke duhre ghR^itam eka upAsate /
ya evaM viduShe vashAM dadus te gatAs tridivaM divaH //32//
brAhmaNebhyo vashAM dattvA sarvAMl lokAnt sam ashnute /
R^itaM hy asyAm Arpitam api brahmAtho tapaH //33//
vashAM devA upa jIvanti vashAM manuShyA uta /
vashedaM sarvam abhavad yAvat sUryo vipashyati //34// {35}

464
Book:

agne jAyasvAditir nAthiteyaM brahmaudanaM pachati putrakAmA /
saptaR^iShayo bhUtakR^itas te tvA manthantu prajayA saheha //
kR^iNuta dhUmaM vR^iShaNaH sakhAyo 'droghAvitA vAcham achha /
ayam agniH pR^itanAShAt suvIro yena devA asahanta dasyUn //
agne 'janiShThA mahate vIryAya brahmaudanAya paktave jAtavedaH /
saptaR^iShayo bhUtakR^itas te tvAjIjanann asyai rayiM sarvavIraM ni yachha //
samiddho agne samidhA sam idhyasva vidvAn devAn yaj~niyAm? eha vakShaH /
tebhyo haviH shrapayaM jAtaveda uttamaM nAkam adhi rohayemam //
tredhA bhAgo nihito yaH purA vo devAnAM pitR^INAM martyAnAm /
aMshAn jAnIdhvaM vi bhajAmi tAn vo yo devAnAM sa imAM pArayAti //
agne sahasvAn abhibhUr abhId asi nIcho ny ùbja dviShataH sapatnAn /
iyaM mAtrA mIyamAnA mitA cha sajAtAMs te balihR^itaH kR^iNotu //
sAkaM sajAtaiH payasA sahaidhy ud ubjainAM mahate vIryAya /
Urdhvo nAkasyAdhi roha viShTapaM svargo loka iti yaM vadanti//
iyaM mahI prati gR^ihNAtu charma pR^ithivI devI sumanasyamAnA /
atha gachhema sukR^itasya lokam //
etau grAvANau sayujA yu~Ngdhi charmaNi nirbindhy aMshUn yajamAnAya sAdhu /
nir avaghnatI ni jahi ya imAM pR^itanyava UrdhvaM prajAm udbharanty ud Uha //
gR^ihANa grAvANau sakR^itau vIra hasta A te devA yaj~niyA yaj~nam aguH /
trayo varA yatamAMs tvaM vR^iNIShe tAs te samR^iddhIr iha rAdhayAmi // {1}

iyaM te dhItir idam u te janitraM gR^ihNAtu tvAm aditiH shUraputrA /
parA punIhi ya imAM pR^itanyavo 'syai rayiM sarvavIraM ni yachha //
upashvase druvaye sIdatA yUyaM vi vichyadhvaM yaj~niyAsas tuShaiH /
shriyA samAnAn ati sarvAnt syAmAdhaspadaM dviShatas pAdayAmi //
parehi nAri punar ehi kShipram apAM tvA goShTho 'dhy arukShad bharAya /
tAsAM gR^ihNItAd yatamA yaj~niyA asan vibhAjya dhIrItarA jahItAt //
emA agur yoShitaH shumbhamAnA ut tiShTha nAri tavasaM rabhasva /
supatnI patyA prajayA prajAvaty A tvAgan yaj~naH prati kumbhaM gR^ibhAya //
Urjo bhAgo nihito yaH purA va R^iShiprashiShTApa A bharaitAH /
ayaM yajo gAtuvin nAthavit prajAvid ugraH pashuvid vIravid vo astu //
agne charur yaj~niyas tvAdhy arukShach chhuchis tapiShThas tapasA tapainam /
ArSheyA daivA abhisaMgatya bhAgam emaM tapiShThA R^itubhis tapantu //
shuddhAH putA yoShito yaj~niyA imA Apash charum ava sarpantu shubhrAH /
aduH prajAM bahulAn pashUn naH paktaudanasya sukR^itAm etu lokam //
brahmaNA shuddhA uta pUtA ghR^itena somasyAMshavas taNDulA yaj~niyA ime /
apaH pra vishata prati gR^ihNAtu vash charur imaM paktvA sukR^itAm eta lokam //
uruH prathasva mahatA mahimnA sahasrapR^iShThaH sukR^itasya loke /
pitAmahAH pitaraH prajopajAhaM paktA pa~nchadashas te asmi //
sahasrapR^iShThaH shatadhAro akShito brahmaudano devayAnaH svargaH /
amUMs ta A dadhAmi prajayA reShayainAn balihArAya mR^iDatAn mahyam eva // {2}

udehi vediM prajayA vardhayainAM nudasva rakShaH prataraM dhehy enAm /
shriyA samAnAn ati sarvAnt syAmAdhaspadaM dviShatas pAdayAmi //
abhyAvartasva pashubhiH sahainAM pratya~N enAM devatAbhiH sahaidhi /
mA tvA prApach chhapatho mAbhichAraH sve kShetre anamIvA vi rAja //
R^itena taShTA manasA hitaiShA brahmaudanasya vihitA vedir agre /
aMsadrIM shuddhAm upa dhehi nAri tatraudanaM sAdaya daivAnAm //
aditer hastAM srucham etAM dvitIyAM saptaR^iShayo bhUtakR^ito yAm akR^iNvan /
sA gAtrANi viduShy odanasya darvir vedyAm adhy enaM chinotu //
shR^itaM tvA havyam upa sIdantu daivA niHsR^ipyAgneH punar enAn pra sIda /
somena pUto jathare sIda brahmaNAm ArSheyAs te mA riShan prAshitAraH //
soma rAjant saMj~nAnam A vapaibhyaH subrAhmaNA yatame tvopasIdAn /
R^iShIn ArSheyAMs tapaso 'dhi jAtAn brahmaudane suhavA johavImi //
shuddhAH pUtA yosito yaj~niyA imA brahmaNAM hasteShu prapR^ithaH sAdayAmi /
yatkAma idam abhiShi~nchAmi vo 'ham indro marutvAnt sa dadAt idam me //
idaM me jyotir amR^itaM hiraNyaM pakvaM kShetrAt kAmadughA ma eShA /
idaM dhanaM ni dadhe brAhmaNeShu kR^iNve panthAM pitR^iShu yaH svargaH //
agnau tuShAn A vapa jAtavedasi paraH kambUkAm? apa mR^iDDhi dUram /
etaM shushruma gR^iharAjasya bhAgam atho vidma nirR^iter bhAgadheyam //
shrAmyataH pachato viddhi sunvataH panthAM svargam adhi rohayainam /
yena rohAt param Apadya yad vaya uttamaM nAkaM paramaM vyoma // {3}

babhrer adhvaryo mukham etad vi mR^iDDhy AjyAya lokaM kR^iNuhi pravidvAn /
ghR^itena gAtrAnu sarvA vi mR^iDDhi kR^iNve panthAM pitR^iShu yaH svargaH //
babhre rakShaH samadam A vapaibhyo 'brAhmaNA yatame tvopasIdAn /
purIShiNaH prathamAnAH purastAd ArSheyAs te mA riShan prAshitAraH //
ArSheyeShu ni dadha odana tvA nAnArSheyANAm apy asty atra /
agnir me goptA marutash cha sarve vishve devA abhi rakShantu pakvam //
yaj~naM duhAnaM sadam it prapInaM pumAMsaM dhenuM sadanaM rayINAm /
prajAmR^itatvam uta dIrgham Ayu rAyash cha poShair upa tvA sadema //
vR^iShabho 'si svarga R^iShIn ArSheyAn gachha /
sukR^itAM loke sIda tatra nau saMskR^itam //
samAchinuShvAnusamprayAhy agne pathaH kalpaya devayAnAn /
etaiH sukR^itair anu gachhema yaj~naM nAke tiShThantam adhi saptarashmau //
yena devA jyotiShA dyAm udAyan brahmaudanaM paktvA sukR^itasya lokam /
tena geShma sukR^itasya lokaM svar Arohanto abhi nAkam uttamam // {4}


465

bhavAsharvau mR^iDataM mAbhi yAtaM bhUtapatI pashupatI namo vAm /
pratihitAm AyatAM mA vi srAShTam mA no hiMsiShTaM dvipado mA chatuShpadaH //
shune kroShTre mA sharIrANi kartam aliklavebhyo gR^idhrebhyo ye cha kR^iShNA aviShyavaH /
makShikAs te pashupate vayAMsi te vighase mA vidanta //
krandAya te prANAya yAsh cha te bhava ropayaH /
namas te rudra kR^iNmaH sahasrAkShAyAmartya //
purastAt te namaH kR^iNma uttarAd adharAd uta /
abhIvargAd divas pary antarikShAya te namaH //
mukhAya te pashupate yAni chakShUMShi te bhava /
tvache rUpAya saMdR^ishe pratIchInAya te namaH //
a~Ngebhyas ta udarAya jihvAyA AsyAya te /
dadbhyo gandhAya te namaH //
astrA nIlashikhaNDena sahasrAkSheNa vAjinA /
rudreNArdhakaghAtinA tena mA sam arAmahi //
sa no bhavaH pari vR^iNaktu vishvata Apa ivAgniH pari vR^iNaktu no bhavaH /
mA no 'bhi mAMsta namo astv asmai //
chatur namo aShTakR^itvo bhavAya dasha kR^itvaH pashupate namas te /
taveme pa~ncha pashavo vibhaktA gAvo ashvAH puruShA ajAvayaH //
tava chatasraH pradishas tava dyaus tava pR^ithivI tavedam ugrorv antarikSham /
tavedaM sarvam Atmanvad yat prANat pR^ithivIm anu // {5}

uruH kosho vasudhAnas tavAyaM yasminn imA vishvA bhuvanAny antaH /
sa no mR^iDa pashupate namas te paraH kroShTAro abhibhAH shvAnaH paro yantv agharudo vikeshyaH //
dhanur bibharShi haritaM hiraNyayaM sahasraghni shatavadhaM shikhaNDin /
rudrasyeShush charati devahetis tasyai namo yatamasyAM dishItaH //
yo 'bhiyAto nilayate tvAM rudra nichikIrShati /
pashchAd anuprayu~NkShe taM viddhasya padanIr iva //
bhavArudrau sayujA saMvidAnAv ubhAv ugrau charato vIryAya /
tabhyAM namo yatamasyAm dishItaH //
namas te 'stv Ayate namo astu parAyate /
namas te rudra tiShThata AsInAyota te namaH //
namaH sAyaM namaH prAtar namo rAtryA namo divA /
bhavAya cha sharvAya chobhAbhyAm akaraM namaH //
sahasrAkSham atipashyaM purastAd rudram asyantaM bahudhA vipashchitam /
mopArAma jihvayeyamAnam //
shyAvAshvaM kR^iShNam asitaM mR^iNantaM bhImaM rathaM keshinaH pAdayantam /
pUrve pratImo namo astv asmai //
mA no 'bhi srA matyaM devahetiM mA naH krudhaH pashupate namas te /
anyatrAsmad divyAM shAkhAM vi dhUnu //
mA no hiMsIr adhi no brUhi pari No vR^i~Ngdhi mA krudhaH /
mA tvayA sam arAmahi // {6}

mA no goShu puruSheShu mA gR^idho no ajAviShu /
anyatrogra vi vartaya piyArUNAM prajAM jahi //
yasya takmA kAsikA hetir ekam ashvasyeva vR^iShaNaH kranda eti /
abhipUrvaM nirNayate namo astv asmai //
yo 'ntarikShe tiShThati viShTabhito 'yajvanaH pramR^iNan devapIyUn /
tasmai namo dashabhiH shakvarIbhiH //
tubhyam AraNyAH pashavo mR^igA vane hitA haMsAH suparNAH shakunA vayAMsi /
tava yakShaM pashupate apsv antas tubhyaM kSharanti divyA Apo vR^idhe //
shishumArA ajagarAH purIkayA jaShA matsyA rajasA yebhyo asyasi /
na te dUraM na pariShThAsti te bhava sadyaH sarvAn pari pashyasi bhUmiM pUrvasmAd dhaMsy uttarasmin samudre //
mA no rudra takmanA mA viseNa mA naH saM srA divyenAgninA /
anyatrAsmad vidyutaM pAtayaitAm //
bhavo divo bhava Ishe pR^ithivyA bhava A papra urv antarikSham /
tasmai namo yatamasyAM dishItaH //
bhava rajan yajamAnAya mR^iDa pashUnAM hi pashupatir babhUtha /
yaH shraddadhAti santi devA iti chatuShpade dvipade 'sya mR^iDa //
mA no mahAntam uta mA no arbhakaM mA no vahantam uta mA no vakShyataH /
mA no hiMsIH pitaraM mAtaraM cha svAM tanvaM rudra mA rIriso naH //
rudrasyailabakArebhyo 'saMsUktagilebhyaH /
idaM mahAsyebhyaH shvabhyo akaraM namaH //
namas te ghoShiNIbhyo namas te keshinIbhyaH /
namo namaskR^itAbhyo namaH saMbhu~njatIbhyaH /
namas te deva senAbhyaH svasti no abhayaM cha naH // {7}

tasyaudanasya bR^ihaspatiH shiro brahma mukham /
dyAvApR^ithivI shrotre sUryAchandramasAv akShiNI saptaR^iShayaH prANApAnAH /
chakShur musalaM kAma ulUkhalam /
ditiH shUrpam aditiH shUrpagrAhI vAto 'pAvinak /
ashvAH kaNA gAvas taNDulA mashakAs tuShAH /
kabru phalIkaraNAH sharo 'bhram /
shyAmam ayo 'sya mAMsAni lohitam asya lohitam /
trapu bhasma haritaM varNaH puShkaram asya gandhaH /
khalaH pAtraM sphyAv aMsAv IShe anUkye /
AntrANi jatravo gudA varatrAH /
iyam eva pR^ithivI kumbhI bhavati rAdhyamAnasyaudanasya dyaur apidhAnam /
sItAH parshavaH sikatA Ubadhyam /
R^itaM hastAvanejanaM kulyopasechanam /
R^ichA kumbhy adhihitArtvijyena preShitA /
brahmaNA parigR^ihItA sAmnA paryUDhA /
bR^ihad AyavanaM rathantaraM darviH /
R^itavaH paktAra ArtavAH sam indhate /
charuM pa~nchabilam ukhaM gharmo 'bhIndhe /

odanena yaj~navachaH sarve lokAH samApyAH /

yasya devA akalpantochchhiShTe ShaD ashItayaH /
taM tvaudanasya pR^ichhAmi yo asya mahimA mahAn /
taM tvaudanasya mahimAnaM vidyAt /
nAlpa iti brUyAn nAnupasechana iti nedaM cha kiM cheti /
yAvad dAtAbhimanasyeta tan nAti vadet /
brahmavAdino vadanti parA~ncham odanaM prAshI3H pratya~nchA3m iti /

naivAham odanaM na mAm odanaH /
