sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 12]

44_4_13]
tatash chainam anyAbhyAm UrubhyAM prAshIr yAbhyAM chaitaM pUrva R^iShayaH prAshnan /
UrU te mariShyata ity enam Aha /
taM vA ahaM nArvA~nchaM na parA~nchaM na pratya~ncham /
mitrAvaruNayor urubhyAm /
tAbhyAm enaM prAshiShaM tAbhyAm enaM ajIgamam /
eSha vA odanaH sarvA~NgaH sarvaparuH sarvatanUH /
sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 13]

45_4_14]
tatash chainam anyAbhyAm aShThIvadbhyAM prAshIr yAbhyAM chaitaM pUrva R^iShayaH prAshnan /
srAmo bhaviShyasIti enam Aha /
taM vA ahaM nArvA~nchaM na parA~nchaM na pratya~ncham /
tvaShTur aShThIvadbhyAm /
tAbhyAm enaM prAshiShaM tAbhyAm enaM ajIgamam /
eSha vA odanaH sarvA~NgaH sarvaparuH sarvatanUH /
sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 14]

46_4_15]
tatash chainam anyAbhyAM pAdAbhyAM prAshIr yAbhyAM chaitaM pUrva R^iShayaH prAshnan /
bahuchArI bhaviShyasIty enam Aha /
taM vA ahaM nArvA~nchaM na parA~nchaM na pratya~ncham /
ashvinoH pAdAbhyAm /
tAbhyAm enaM prAshiShaM tAbhyAm enaM ajIgamam /
eSha vA odanaH sarvA~NgaH sarvaparuH sarvatanUH /
sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 15]

47_4_16]
tatash chainam anyAbhyAM prapadAbhyAM prAshIr yAbhyAM chaitaM pUrva R^iShayaH prAshnan /
sarpas tvA haniShyatIty enam Aha /
taM vA ahaM nArvA~nchaM na parA~nchaM na pratya~ncham /
savituH prapadAbhyAm /
tAbhyAm enaM prAshiShaM tAbhyAm enaM ajIgamam /
eSha vA odanaH sarvA~NgaH sarvaparuH sarvatanUH /
sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 16]

48_4_17]
tatash chainam anyAbhyAm hastAbhyAM prAshIr yAbhyAM chaitam pUrva R^iShayaH prAshnan /
brAhmaNaM haniShyasIty enam Aha /
taM vA ahaM nArvA~nchaM na parA~nchaM na pratya~ncham /
savituH prapadAbhyAm /
tAbhyAm enaM prAshiShaM tAbhyAm enaM ajIgamam /
eSha vA odanaH sarvA~NgaH sarvaparuH sarvatanUH /
sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 17]

49_4_18]
tatash chainam anyayA pratiShThayA prAshIr yayA chaitaM pUrva R^iShayaH prAshnan /
apratiShThAno 'nAyatano mariShyasIty enam Aha /
taM vA ahaM nArvA~nchaM na parA~nchaM na pratya~ncham /
satye pratiShThAya /
tayainaM prAshiShaM tayainaM ajIgamam /
eSha vA odanaH sarvA~NgaH sarvaparuH sarvatanUH /
sarvA~Nga eva sarvaparuH sarvatanUH saM bhavati ya evaM veda / 18] {9}

50_5_1]
etad vai bradhnasya viShTapaM yad odanaH / 1]

51_5_2]
bradhnaloko bhavati bradhnasya viShTapi shrayate ya evaM veda / 2]

52_5_3]
etasmAd vA odanAt trayastriMshataM lokAn nir amimIta prajApatiH / 3]

53_5_4]
teShAM praj~nAnAya yaj~nam asR^ijata / 4]

54_5_5]
sa ya evaM viduSha upadraShTA bhavati prANaM ruNaddhi / 5]

55_5_6]
na cha prAnaM ruNaddhi sarvajyAniM jIyate / 6]

56_5_7]
na cha sarvajyAniM jIyate purainaM jarasaH prANo jahAti / 7] {10}


467
_6]
prANAya namo yasya sarvam idaM vashe /
yo bhUtaH sarvasyeshvaro yasmint sarvaM pratiShThitam //
namas te prANa krandAya namas te stanayitnave /
namas te prANa vidyute namas te prANa varShate //
yat prANa stanayitnunAbhikrandaty oShadhIH /
pra vIyante garbhAn dadhate 'tho bahvIr vi jAyante //
yat prANa R^itAv Agate 'bhikrandaty oShadhIH /
sarvaM tadA pra modate yat kiM cha bhUmyAm adhi //
yadA prANo abhyavarShId varSheNa pR^ithivIM mahIm /
pashavas tat pra modante maho vai no bhaviShyati //
abhivR^iShTA oShadhayaH prANena sam avAdiran /
Ayur vai naH prAtItaraH sarvA naH surabhIr akaH //
namas te astv Ayate namo astu parAyate /
namas te prANa tiShThata AsInAyota te namaH //
namas te prANa prANate namo astv apAnate /
parAchInAya te namaH pratIchInAya te namaH sarvasmai ta idaM namaH //
yA te prANa priyA tanUr yo te prANa preyasI /
atho yad bheShajaM tava tasya no dhehi jIvase //
prANaH prajA anu vaste pitA putram iva priyam /
prANo ha sarvasyeshvaro yach cha prANati yach cha na // {11}

prANo mR^ityuH prANas takmA prANaM devA upAsate /
prANo ha satyavAdinam uttame loka A dadhat //
prANo virAT prANo deShTrI prAnaM sarva upAsate /
prANo ha sUryash chandramAH prANam AhuH prajApatim //
prANApAnau vrIhiyavAv anaDvAn prANa uchyate /
yave ha prANa Ahito 'pAno vrIhir uchyate //
apAnati prANati puruSho garbhe antarA /
yadA tvaM prANa jinvasy atha sa jAyate punaH //
prANaM Ahur mAtarishvAnaM vAto ha prANa uchyate /
prANe ha bhUtaM bhavyaM cha prANe sarvaM pratiShThitam //
AtharvaNIr A~NgirasIr daivIr manuShyajA uta /
oShadhayaH pra jAyante yadA tvaM prANa jinvasi //
yadA prANo abhyavarShId varSheNa pR^ithivIm mahIm /
oShadhayaH pra jAyante 'tho yAH kAsh cha vIrudhaH //
yas te prANedaM veda yasmiMsh chAsi pratiShThitaH /
sarve tasmai baliM harAn amuShmiMl loka uttame //
yathA prANa balihR^itas tubhyaM sarvAH prajA imAH /
evA tasmai baliM harAn yas tvA shR^iNavat sushravaH //
antar garbhash charati devatAsv AbhUto bhUtaH sa u jAyate punaH /
sa bhUto bhavyaM bhaviShyat pitA putraM pra viveshA shachIbhiH // {12}

ekaM pAdaM not khidati salilAd dhaMsa uchcharan /
yad a~Nga sa tam utkhiden naivAdya na shvaH syAt /
na rAtrI nAhaH syAn na vy ùchhet kadA chana //
aShTAchakraM vartata ekanemi sahasrAkSharaM pra puro ni pashchA /
ardhena vishvaM bhuvanaM jajAna yad asyArdhaM katamaH sa ketuH //
yo asya vishvajanmana Ishe vishvasya cheShTataH /
anyeShu kShipradhanvane tasmai prANa namo 'stu te //
yo asya sarvajanmana Ishe sarvasya cheShTataH /
atandro brahmaNA dhIraH prANo mAnu tiShThAtu //
UrdhvaH supteShu jAgAra nanu tirya~N ni padyate /
na suptam asya supteShv anu shushrAva kash chana //
prANa mA mat paryAvR^ito na mad anyo bhaviShyasi /
apAM garbham iva jIvase prANa badhnAmi tvA mayi // {13}


468
_7]
brahmachArIShNaMsh charati rodasI ubhe tasmin devAH saMmanaso bhavanti /
sa dAdhAra pR^ithivIM divaM cha sa AchAryaM tapasA piparti //
brahmachAriNaM pitaro devajanAH pR^ithag devA anusaMyanti sarve /
gandharvA enam anv Ayan trayastriMshat trishatAh ShaTsahasrAH sarvAnt sa devAMs tapasA piparti //
AchArya upanayamAno brahmachAriNaM kR^iNute garbham antaH /
tam rAtrIs tisra udare bibharti taM jAtaM draShTum abhisaMyanti devAH //
iyaM samit pR^ithivI dyaur dvitIyotAntarikShaM samidhA pR^iNAti /
brahmachArI samidhA mekhalayA shrameNa lokAMs tapasA piparti //
pUrvo jAto brahmaNo brahmachArI gharmaM vasAnas tapasod atiShThat /
tasmAj jAtaM brAhmaNaM brahma jyeShThaM devAsh cha sarve amR^itena sAkam //
brahmachAry eti samidhA samiddhaH kArShNaM vasAno dIkShito dIrghashmashruH /
sa sadya eti pUrvasmAd uttaraM samudraM lokAnt samgR^ibhya muhur Acharikrat //
brahmachArI janayan brahmApo lokaM prajApatiM parameShThinam virAjam /
garbho bhUtvAmR^itasya yonAv indro ha bhUtvAsurAMs tatarha //
AchAryas tatakSha nabhasI ubhe ime urvI gambhIre pR^ithivIM divaM cha /
te rakShati tapasA brahmachArI tasmin devAH saMmanaso bhavanti //
imAM bhUmiM pR^ithivIM brahmachArI bhikShAm A jabhAra prathamo divaM cha /
te kR^itvA samidhAv upAste tayor ArpitA bhuvanAni vishvA //
arvAg anyaH paro anyo divas pR^iShThAd guhA nidhI nihitau brAhmaNasya /
tau rakShati tapasA brahmachArI tat kevalaM kR^iNute brahma vidvAn // {14}

arvAg anya ito anyaH pR^ithivyA agnI sameto nabhasI antareme /
tayoH shrayante rashmayo 'dhi dR^iDhAs tAn A tiShThati tapasA brahmachArI //
abhikrandan stanayann aruNaH shiti~Ngo bR^ihach chhepo 'nu bhUmau jabhAra /
brahmachArI si~nchati sAnau retaH pR^ithivyAM tena jIvanti pradishash chatasraH //
agnau sUrye chandramasi mAtarishvan brahmachAry apsu samidham A dadhAti /
tAsAm archIShi pR^ithag abhre charanti tAsAm AjyaM puruSho varSham ApaH //
AchAryo mR^ityur varuNaH somo oShadhayaH payaH /
jIbhUtA Asant satvAnas tair idaM svar AbhR^itam //
amA ghR^itaM kR^iNute kevalam AchAryo bhUtvA varuNo yadyad aichhat prajApatau /
tad brahmachArI prAyachhat svAn mitro adhy AtmanaH //
AchAryo brahmachArI brahmacharI prajApatiH /
prajApatir vi rAjati virAD indro 'bhavad vashI //
brahmacharyeNa tapasA rAjA rAShTraM vi rakShati /
AchAryo brahmacharyeNa brahmachAriNam ichhate //
brahmacharyeNa kanyA yuvAnam vindate patim /
anaDvAn brahmacharyeNAshvo ghAsaM jigIrShati //
brahmacharyeNa tapasA devA mR^ityum apAghnata /
indro ha brahmacharyeNa devebhyaH svar Abharat //
oShadhayo bhUtabhavyam ahorAtre vanaspatiH /
saMvatsaraH saha rtubhis te jAtA brahmachAriNaH // {15}

pArthivA divyAH pashava AraNyA grAmyAsh cha ye /
apakShAH pakShiNash cha ye te jAtA brahmachAriNaH //
pR^ithak sarve prAjApatyAH prANAn Atmasu bibhrati /
tAnt sarvAn brahma rakShati brahmachAriNy AbhR^itam //
devAnAm etat pariShUtam anabhyArUDhaM charati rochamAnam /
tasmAj jAtaM brAhmaNaM brahma jyeShThaM devAsh cha sarve amR^itena sAkam //
brahmachArI brahma bhrAjad bibharti tasmin devA adhi vishve samotAH /
prANApAnau janayann Ad vyAnaM vAchaM mano hR^idayaM brahma medhAm //
chakShuH shrotraM yasho asmAsu dhehy annaM reto lohitam udaram /
tAni kalpan brahmachArI salilasya pR^iShThe tapo 'tiShThat tapyamAnaH samudre /
sa snAto babhruH pi~NgalaH pR^ithivyAM bahu rochate // {16}


469
_8]
agnim brUmo vanaspatIn oShadhIr uta vIrudhaH /
indraM bR^ihaspatiM sUryaM te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
brUmo rAjAnaM varuNaM mitraM viShNum atho bhagam /
aMshaM vivasvantaM brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasah //
brUmo devaM savitAraM dhAtAram uta pUShaNam /
tvaShTAram agriyaM brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasah //
gandharvApsaraso brUmo ashvinA brahmaNas patim /
aryamA nAma yo devas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
ahorAtre idaM brUmaH sUryAchandramasAv ubhA /
vishvAn AdityAn brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
vAtaM brUmaH parjanyam antarikSham atho dishaH /
AshAsh cha sarvA brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
mu~nchantu mA shapathyAd ahorAtre atho uShAH /
somo mA devo mu~nchatu yam Ahush chandramA iti //
pArthivA divyaH pashava AraNyA uta ye mR^igAH /
shakuntAn pakShino brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
bhavAsharvAv idaM brUmo rudraM pashupatish cha yaH /
iShUr yA eShAM saMvidma tA naH santu sadA shivAH //
divaM brUmo nakShatrANi bhUmiM yakShANi parvatAn /
samudrA nadyo veshantAs te no mu~nchantv amhasaH // {17}

saptarShIn vA idaM brUmo 'po devIH prajApatim /
pitR^In yamashreShThAn brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
ye devA diviShado antarikShasadash cha ye /
pR^ithivyAM shakrA ye shritAs te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
AdityA rudrA vasavo divi devA atharvAnaH /
a~Ngiraso manIShiNas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
yaj~naM brUmo yajamAnam R^ichaH sAmAni bheShajA /
yajUMShi hotrA brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
pa~ncha rAjyAni vIrudhAM somashreShThAni brUmaH /
darbho bha~Ngo yavaH sahas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
arAyAn brUmo rakShAMsi sarpAn puNyajanAn pitR^In /
mR^ityUn ekashataM brUmas te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
R^itUn brUma R^itupatIn ArtavAn uta hAyanAn /
samAH saMvatsarAn mAsAMs te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
eta devA dakShiNataH pashchAt prA~ncha udeta /
purastAd uttarAch chhakrA vishve devAH sametya te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
vishvAn devAn idaM brUmaH satyasaMdhAn R^itavR^idhaH /
vishvAbhiH patnIbhiH saha te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
sarvAn devAn idaM brUmaH satyasaMdhAn R^itAvR^idhaH /
sarvAbhiH patnIbhiH saha te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
bhUtaM brUmo bhUtapatiM bhUtAnAm uta yo vashI /
bhUtAni sarvA saMgatya te no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //
yA devIH pa~ncha pradisho ye devA dvAdasha rtavah /
saMvatsarasya ye daMShTrAs te naH santu sadA shivAH //
yan mAtalI rathakrItam amR^itaM veda bheShajam /
tad indro apsu prAveshayat tad Apo datta bheShajam // {18}


470
_9]
uchchhiShTe nAma rUpaM chochchhiShTe loka AhitaH /
uchchhiShTa indrash chAgnish cha vishvam antaH samAhitam //
uchchhiShTe dyAvApR^ithivI vishvaM bhUtaM samAhitam /
ApaH samudra uchchhiShTe chandramA vAta AhitaH //
sann uchchhiShTe asaMsh cha ubhau mR^ityur vAjaH prajApatiH /
laikyA uchchhiShTa AyattA vrash cha drash chApi shrIr mayi //
dR^iDho dR^iMhasthiro nyo brahma vishvasR^ijaH dasha /
nAbhim iva sarvataH chakram uchchhiShTe devatAs shritAH //
R^ik sAma yajur uchchhiShTa udgIthaH prastutam stutam /
hi~NkAra uchchhiShTe svaraH sAmno medish cha tan mayi //
aindrAgnaM pAvamAnaM mahAnAmnIr mahAvratam /
uchchhiShTe yaj~nasyA~NgAny antar garbha iva mAtari //
rAjasUyaM vAjapeyam agniShTomaH tad adhvaraH /
arkAshvamedhAv uchchhiShTe jIvabarhir madintamaH //
agnyAdheyam atho dIkShA kAmaprash chhandasA saha /
utsannA yaj~nAH satrANy uchchhiShTe 'dhi samAhitAH //
agnihotraM cha shraddhA cha vaShaTkAro vrataM tapaH /
dakShiNeShTaM pUrtaM chochchhiShTe 'dhi samAhitAH //
ekarAtro dvirAtraH sadyaHkrIH prakrIr ukthyaH /
otaM nihitam uchchhiShTe yaj~nasyANUni vidyayA // {19}

chatUrAtraH pa~ncharAtraH ShaDrAtrash chobhayaH saha /
ShoDashI saptarAtrash chochchhiShTAj jaj~nire sarve ye yaj~nA amR^ite hitAH //
pratIhAro nidhanaM vishvajich chAbhijich cha yaH /
sAhnAtirAtrAv uchchhiShTe dvAdashAho 'pi tan mayi //
sUnR^itA samnatiH kShemaH svadhorjAmR^itaM sahaH /
uchchhiShTe sarve pratya~nchaH kAmAH kAmena tatR^ipuh //
nava bhUmIH samudrA uchchhisTe adhi shritA divaH /
A sUryo bhAty uchchhiShTe 'horAtre api tan mayi //
upahavyaM viShUvantaM ye cha yaj~nA guhA hitAH /
bibharti bhartA vishvasyochchhiShTo janituH pitA //
pitA janitur uchchhisTo 'soH pautraH pitAmahaH /
sa kShiyati vishvasyeshAno vR^iShA bhUmyAm atighnyaH //
R^itaM satyaM tapo rAsTraM shramo dharmash cha karma cha /
bhUtaM bhaviShyad uchchhiShTe vIryaM lakShmIr balaM bale //
samR^iddhir oja AkutiH kShatraM rAShTraM ShaD urvyaH /
saMvatsaro 'dhy uchchhista iDA praiShA grahA haviH //
chaturhotAra Apriyash chAturmAsyAni nIvidaH /
uchchhiShTe yaj~nAH hotrAH pashubandhAs tad iShTayaH //
ardhamAsAsh cha mAsAsh chArtavA R^itubhiH saha /
uchchhiShTe ghoShiNIr ApaH stanayitnuH shrutir mahI // {20}

sharkarAH sikatA ashmAna oShadhayo vIrudhas tR^iNA /
abhrANi vidyuto varSham uchchhiShTe saMshritA shritA //
rAddhiH prAptiH samAptir vyAptir maha edhatuH /
atyAptir uchchhiShTe bhUtish chAhitA nihitA hitA //
yach cha prANati prANena yach cha pashyati chakShuShA /
uchchhiShTAj jaj~nire sarve divi devA divishritaH //
R^ichaH sAmAni chhandAMsi purANaM yajuShA saha /
uchchhiShTAj jaj~nire sarve divi devA divishritaH //
prANApAnau chakShuH shrotram akShitish cha kShitish cha yA /
uchchhiShTAj jaj~nire sarve divi devA divishritaH //
AnandA modAH pramudo 'bhimodamudash cha ye /
uchchhiShTAj jaj~nire sarve divi devA divishritaH //
devAH pitaro manuShyA gandharvApsarasash cha ye /
uchchhiShTAj jaj~nire sarve divi devA divishritaH // {21}


471
_10]
yan manyur jAyAm Avahat saMkalpasya gR^ihAd adhi /
ka AsaM janyAH ke varAH ka u jyeShThavaro 'bhavat //
tapash chaivAstAM karma chAntar mahaty arNave /
ta AsaM janyAs te varA brahma jyeShThavaro 'bhavat //
dasha sAkam ajAyanta devA devebhyaH purA /
yo vai tAn vidyAt pratyakShaM sa vA adya mahad vadet //
prANApAnau chakShuH shrotram akShitish cha kShitish cha yA /
vyAnodAnau vA~N manas te vA AkUtim Avahan //
ajAtA Asann R^itavo 'tho dhAtA bR^ihaspatiH /
indrAgnI ashvinA tarhi kaM te jyeShTham upAsata //
tapash chaivAstAM karma chAntar mahaty arNave /
tapo ha jaj~ne karmaNas tat te jyeShTham upAsata //
yeta AsId bhUmiH pUrvA yAm addhAtaya id viduH /
yaH vai tAM vidyAn nAmathA sa manyeta purANavit //
kuta indraH kutaH somaH kuto agnir ajAyata /
kutas tvaShTA sam abhavat kuto dhAtAjAyata //
indrAd indraH somAt somo agner agnir ajAyata /
tvaShTA ha jaj~ne tvaShTur dhAtur dhAtAjAyata //
ye ta Asan dasha jAtA devA devebhyaH purA /
putrebhyo lokaM dattvA kasmiMs te loka Asate // {22}

yadA keshAn asthi snAva mAMsaM majjAnam Abharat /
sharIraM kR^itvA pAdavat kaM lokam anu prAvishat //
kutaH keshAn kutaH snAva kuto asthIny Abharat /
a~NgA parvANi majjAnaM ko mAMsaM kuta Abharat //
saMsicho nAma te devA ye saMbhArAnt samabharan /
sarvaM saMsichya martyaM devAH puruSham Avishan //
UrU pAdAv aShThIvantau shiro hastAv atho mukham /
pR^iShTIr barjahye pArshve kas tat sam adadhAd R^iShiH //
shiro hastAv atho mukhaM jihvAM grIvAsh cha kIkasAH /
tvachA prAvR^itya sarvaM tat saMdhA sam adadhAn mahI //
yat tach chharIram ashayat saMdhayA saMhitaM mahat /
yenedam adya rochate ko asmin varNam Abharat //
sarve devA upAshikShan tad ajAnAd vadhUH satI /
IshA vashasya yA jAyA sAsmin varNam Abharat //
yadA tvaShTA vyatR^iNat pitA tvaShTur ya uttaraH /
gR^ihaM kR^itvA martyaM devAH puruSham Avishan //
svapno vai tandrIr nirR^itiH pApmAno nAma devatAH /
jarA khAlatyaM pAlityaM sharIram anu prAvishan //
steyaM duShkR^itaM vR^ijinaM satyaM yaj~no yasho bR^ihat /
balaM cha kShatram ojash cha sharIram anu prAvishan // {23}

bhUtish cha vA abhUtish cha rAtayo 'rAtayash cha yAH /
kShudhash cha sarvAs tR^iShNAsh cha sharIram anu prAvishan //
nindAsh cha vA anindAsh cha yach cha hanteti neti cha /
sharIraM shraddhA dakShiNAshraddhA chAnu prAvishan //
vidyAsh cha vA avidyAsh cha yach chAnyad upadeshyam /
sharIraM brahma prAvishad R^ichaH sAmAtho yajuH //
AnandA modAH pramudo 'bhimodamudash cha ye /
haso nariShTA nR^ittAni sharIram anu prAvishan //
AlApAsh cha pralApAsh chAbhilApalapash cha ye /
sharIraM sarvaM prAvishann AyujaH prayujo yujaH //
prANApAnau chakShuH shrotram akShitish cha kShitish cha yA /
vyAnodAnau vAn manaH sharIreNa ta Iyante //
AshiShash cha prashiShash cha saMshiSho vishiShash cha yAH /
chittAni sarve saMkalpAH sharIram anu prAvishan //
AsteyIsh cha vAsteyIsh cha tvaraNAH kR^ipaNAsh cha yAH /
guhAH shukrA sthUlA apas tA bIbhatsAv asAdayan //
asthi kR^itvA ShamidhaM tad aShTa Apo asAdayan /
retaH kR^itivAjyaM devAH puruSham Avishan //
yA Apo yAsh cha devatA yA virAT brahmaNA saha /
sharIraM brahma prAvishach chharIre 'dhi prajApatiH //
sUryash chakShur vAtaH prANaM puruShasya vi bhejire /
athAsyetaram AtmAnaM devAH prAyachhann agnaye //
tasmAd vai vidvAn puruSham idaM brahmeti manyate /
sarvA hy asmin devatA gAvo goShTha ivAsate //
prathamena pramAreNa tredhA viShva~N vi gachhati /
ada ekena gachhaty ada ekena gachhaty ihaikena ni Shevate //
apsu stImAsu vR^iddhAsu sharIram antarA hitam /
tasmiM chhavo 'dhi antarA tasmAch chhavo 'dhy uchyate // {24}


472
_11]
ye bAhavo yA iShavo dhanvanAM vIryANi cha /
asIn parashUn AyudhaM chittAkUtaM cha yad dhR^idi /
sarvaM tad arbude tvam amitrebhyo dR^ishe kurUdArAMsh cha pra darshaya //
ut tiShThata saM nahyadhvaM mitrA devajanA yUyam /
saMdR^iShTA guptA vaH santu yA no mitrANy arbude //
ut tiShThatam A rabhetAm AdAnasaMdAnAbhyAm /
amitrANAM senA abhi dhattam arbude //
arbudir nAma yo deva IshAnash cha nyarbudiH /
yAbhyAm antarikSham AvR^itam iyaM cha pR^ithivI mahI /
tAbhyAm indramedibhyAm ahaM jitam anv emi senayA //
ut tiShTha tvaM devajanArbude senayA saha /
bha~njann amitrANAM senAm bhogebhiH pari vAraya //
sapta jAtAn nyarbuda udArANAM samIkShayan /
tebhiSh Tvam Ajye hute sarvair ut tiShTha senayA //
pratighnAnAshrumukhI kR^idhukarNI cha kroshatu /
vikeshI puruShe hate radite arbude tava //
saMkarShantI karUkaraM manasA putram ichhantI /
patiM bhrAtaram At svAn radite arbude tava //
aliklavA jAShkamadA gR^idhrAH shyenAH patatriNaH /
dhvA~NkShAH shakunayas tR^ipyantv amitreShu samIkShayan radite arbude tava //
atho sarvaM shvApadaM makShikA tR^ipyatu krimiH /
pauruSheye 'dhi kuNape radite arbude tava // {25}

A gR^ihNItaM saM bR^ihataM prAnApAnAn nyarbude /
nivAshA ghoShAH saM yantv amitreShu samIkShayan radite arbude tava //
ud vepaya saM vijantAM bhiyAmitrAnt saM sR^ija /
urugrAhair bAhva~Nkair vidhyAmitrAn nyarbude //
muhyantv eShAM bAhavash chittAkUtaM cha yad dhR^idi /
maiShAm uch chheShi kiM chana radite arbude tava //
pratighnAnAH saM dhAvantUraH paTUrAv AghnAnAH /
aghAriNIr vikeshyo rudatyaH puruShe hate radite arbude tava //
shvanvatIr apsaraso rUpakA utArbude /
antaHpAtre rerihatIM rishAM durNihitaiShiNIm /
sarvAs tA arbude tvam amitrebhyo dR^ishe kurUdArAMsh cha pra darshaya //
khadUre 'dhicha~NkramAM kharvikAM kharvavAsinIm /
ya udArA antarhitA gandharvApsarasash cha ye sarpA itarajanA rakShAMsi //
chaturdamShTrAM chhyAvadataH kumbhamuShkAm? asR^i~NmukhAn /
svabhyasA ye chodbhyasAH //
ud vepaya tvam arbude 'mitrANAm amUH sichaH /
jayAMsh cha jiShNush chAmitrAm? jayatAm indramedinau //
prablIno mR^iditaH shayAM hato 'mitro nyarbude /
agnijihvA dhUmashikhA jayantIr yantu senayA //
tayArbude praNuttAnAm indro hantu varaMvaram /
amitrANAM shachIpatir mAmIShAM mochi kash chana // {26}

ut kasantu hR^idayAny UrdhvaH prANa ud IShatu /
shauShkAsyam anu vartatAm amitrAn mota mitriNaH //
ye cha dhIrA ye chAdhIrAH parA~ncho badhirAsh cha ye /
tamasA ye cha tUparA atho bastAbhivAsinaH /
sarvAMs tAm arbude tvam amitrebhyo dR^ishe kurUdArAMsh cha pra darshaya //
arbudish cha triShandhish chAmitrAn no vi vidhyatAm /
yathaiShAm indra vR^itrahan hanAma shachIpate 'mitrANAM sahasrashaH //
vanaspatIn vAnaspatyAn oShadhIr uta vIrudhaH /
gandharvApsarasaH sarpAn devAn puNyajanAn pitR^In /
sarvAMs tA.N arbude tvam amitrebhyo dR^ishe kurUdArAMsh cha pra darshaya //
IshAM vo maruto deva Adityo brahmaNas patiH /
IshAM va indrash chAgnish cha dhAtA mitraH prajApatiH /
IshAM va R^iShayash chakrur amitreShu samIkShayan radite arbude tava //
teShAM sarveShAm IshAnA ut tiShThata saM nahyadhvaM mitrA devajanA yUyam /
imaM saMgrAmaM saMjitya yathAlokaM vi tiShThadhvam // {27}


473
_12]
ut tiShThata saM nahyadhvam udArAH ketubhiH saha /
sarpA itarajanA rakShAMsy amitrAn anu dhAvata //
IshAM vo veda rAjyaM triShandhe aruNaiH ketubhiH saha /
ye antarikShe ye divi pR^ithivyAM ye cha manavAH /
triShandhes te chetasi durNAmAna upAsatAm //
ayomukhAH sUchImukhA atho vika~NkatImukhAH /
kravyAdo vAtaraMhasaH A sajantv amitrAn vajreNa triShandhinA //
antar dhehi jAtaveda Aditya kuNapam bahu /
trisandher iyaM senA suhitAstu me vashe //
ut tiShTha tvaM devajanArbude senayA saha /
ayaM balir va Ahutas triShandher AhutiH priyA //
shitipadI saM dyatu sharavyeyaM chatuShpadI /
kR^itye 'mitrebhyo bhava triShandheH saha senayA //
dhUmAkShI saM patatu kR^idhukarnI cha kroshatu /
triShandheH senayA jite aruNAH santu ketavaH //
avAyantAM pakShiNo ye vayAMsy antarikShe divi ye charanti /
shvApado makShikAH saM rabhantAm AmAdo gR^idhrAH kuNape radantAm //
yAm indreNa saMdhAM samadhatthA brahmaNA cha bR^ihaspate /
tayAham indrasaMdhayA sarvAn devAn iha huva ito jayata mAmutaH //
bR^ihaspatir A~Ngirasa R^iShayo brahmasaMshitAH /
asurakShayaNaM vadhaM triShandhim divy Asrayan // {28}

yenAsau gupta Aditya ubhAv indrash cha tiShThataH /
triShandhiM devA abhajantaujase cha balAya cha //
sarvAMl lokAnt sam ajayan devA AhutyAnayA /
bR^ihaspatir A~Ngiraso vajraM yam asi~nchatAsuraksayaNaM vadham //
bR^ihaspatir A~Ngiraso vajraM yam asi~nchatAsurakShayaNaM vadham /
tenAham amUM senAM ni limpAmi bR^ihaspate 'mitrAn hanmy ojasA //
sarve devA atyAyanti ye ashnanti vaShaTkR^itam /
imAM juShadhvam Ahutim ito jayata mAmutaH //
sarve devA atyAyantu triShandher AhutiH priyA /
saMdhAM mahatIM rakShata yayAgre asurA jitAh //
vAyur amitrANAm iShvagrANy A~nchatu /
indra eShAM bahUn prati bhanaktu mA shakan pratidhAm iShum /
Aditya eShAm astraM vi nAshayatu chandramA yutAm agatasya panthAm //
yadi preyur devapurA brahma varmANi chakrire /
tanUpAnaM paripANaM kR^iNvAnA yad upochire sarvaM tad arasaM kR^idhi //
kravyAdAnuvartayan mR^ityunA cha purohitam /
triShandhe prehi senayA jayAmitrAn pra padyasva //
triShandhe tamasA tvam amitrAn pari vAraya /
pR^iShadAjyapraNuttAnAM mAmIShAM mochi kash chana //
shitipadI saM patatv amitrAnAm amUH shichaH /
muhyantv adyAmUH senAH amitrAnAM nyarbude // {29}

mUDhA amitrA nyarbude jahy eShAM varaMvaram /
anayA jahi senayA //
yash cha kavachI yash chAkavacho 'mitro yash chAjmani /
jyApAshaiH kavachapAshair ajmanAbhihataH shayAm //
ye varmiNo ye 'varmANo amitrA ye cha varmiNaH /
sarvAMs tA.N arbude hatAM chhvAno 'dantu bhUmyAm //
ye rathino ye arathA asAdA ye cha sAdinaH /
sarvAn adantu tAn hatAn gR^idhrAH shyenAH patatriNaH //
sahasrakuNapA shetAm AmitrI senA samare vadhAnAm /
vividdhA kakajAkR^itA //
marmAvidhaM roruvataM suparNair adantu dushchitaM mR^iditam shayAnam /
ya imAM pratIchIm Ahutim amitro no yuyutsati //
yAM devA anutisThanti yasyA nAsti virAdhanam /
tayendro hantu vR^itrahA vajreNa triShaMdhinA //
474
Book:
12

satyaM bR^ihad R^itam ugraM dIkShA tapo brahma yaj~naH pR^ithivIM dhArayanti /
sA no bhUtasya bhavyasya patny uruM lokaM pR^ithivI naH kR^iNotu //1//
asaMbAdhaM madhyato mAnavAnAM yasyA udvataH pravataH samaM bahu /
nAnAvIryA oShadhIr yA bibharti pR^ithivI naH prathatAM rAdhyatAM naH //2//
yasyAM samudra uta sindhur Apo yasyAm annaM kR^iShTayaH saMbabhUvuH /
yasyAm idaM jinvati prANad ejat sA no bhUmiH pUrvapeye dadhAtu //3//
yasyAsh chatasraH pradishaH pR^ithivyA yasyAm annam kR^iShTayaH saMbabhUvuH /
yA bibharti bahudhA prANad ejat sA no bhUmir goShv apy anne dadhAtu //4//
yasyAM pUrve pUrvajanA vichakrire yasyAM devA asurAn abhyavartayan /
gavAm ashvAnAM vayasash cha viShThA bhagaM varchaH pR^ithivI no dadhAtu //5//
vishvaMbharA vasudhAnI pratiShThA hiraNyavakShA jagato niveshanI /
vaishvAnaraM bibhratI bhUmir agnim indraR^iShabhA draviNe no dadhAtu //6//
yAM rakShanty asvapnA vishvadAnIM devA bhUmiM pR^ithivIm apramAdam /
sA no madhu priyaM duhAm atho ukShatu varchasA //7//
yArNave 'dhi salilam agra AsIt yAM mAyAbhir anvacharan manIShiNaH /
yasyA hR^idayaM parame vyomant satyenAvR^itam amR^itaM pR^ithivyAH /
sA no bhUmis tviShiM balaM rAShTre dadhAtUttame //8//
yasyAm ApaH paricharAH samAnIr ahorAtre apramAdaM kSharanti /
sA no bhUmir bhUridhArA payo duhAm atho ukShatu varchasA //9//
yAm ashvinAv amimAtAM viShNur yasyAM vichakrame /
indro yAM chakra Atmane 'namitrAM shachIpatiH /
sA no bhUmir vi sR^ijatAM mAtA putrAya me payaH //10// {1}

girayas te parvatA himavanto 'raNyaM te pR^ithivi syonam astu /
babhruM kR^iShNAm rohiNIM vishvarUpAM dhruvAM bhUmiM pR^ithivIm indraguptAm /
ajIto 'hato akShato 'dhy aShThAm pR^ithivIm aham //11//
yat te madhyaM pR^ithivi yach cha nabhyaM yAs ta Urjas tanvaH saMbabhUvuH /
tAsu no dhehy abhi naH pavasva mAtA bhUmiH putro ahaM pR^ithivyAH parjanyaH pitA sa u naH pipartu //12//
yasyAM vediM parigR^ihNanti bhUmyAM yasyAM yaj~naM tanvate vishvakarmANaH /
yasyAM mIyante svaravaH pR^ithivyAm UrdhvAH shukrA AhutyAH purastAt /
sA no bhUmir vardhayad vardhamAnA //13//
yo no dveShat pR^ithivi yaH pR^itanyAd yo 'bhidAsAn manasA yo vadhena /
taM no bhUme randhaya pUrvakR^itvari //14//
tvaj jAtAs tvayi charanti martyAs tvaM bibharShi dvipadas tvaM chatuShpadaH /
taveme pR^ithivi pa~ncha mAnavA yebhyo jyotir amR^itaM martyebhya udyant sUryo rashmibhir Atanoti //15//
tA naH prajAH saM duhratAM samagrA vAcho madhu pR^ithivi dhehi mahyam //16//
vishvasvaM mAtaram oShadhInAM dhruvAM bhUmiM pR^ithivIM dharmaNA dhR^itAm /
shivAM syonAm anu charema vishvahA //17//
mahat sadhasthaM mahatI babhUvitha mahAn vega ejathur vepathuSh Te /
mahAMs tvendro rakShaty apramAdam /
sA no bhUme pra rochaya hiraNyasyeva saMdR^ishi mA no dvikShata kash chana //18//
agnir bhUmyAm oShadhIShv agnim Apo bibhraty agnir ashmasu /
agnir antaH puruSheShu goShv ashveShv agnayaH //19//
agnir diva A tapaty agner devasyorv a1ntarikSham /
agniM martAsa indhate havyavAhaM ghR^itapriyam //20// {2}

agnivAsAH pR^ithivy asitaj~nUs tviShImantaM saMshitaM mA kR^iNotu //21//
bhUmyAM devebhyo dadati yaj~naM havyam araMkR^itam /
bhUmyAM manuShyA jIvanti svadhayAnnena martyAH /
sA no bhUmiH prANam Ayur dadhAtu jaradaShTiM mA pR^ithivI kR^iNotu //22//
yas te gandhaH pR^ithivi saMbabhUva yaM bibhraty oShadhayo yam ApaH /
yaM gandharvA apsarasash cha bhejire tena mA surabhiM kR^iNu mA no dvikShata kash chana //23//
yas te gandhaH puShkaram Avivesha yaM saMjabhruH sUryAyA vivAhe /
amartyAH pR^ithivi gandham agre tena mA surabhiM kR^iNu mA no dvikShata kash chana //24//
yas te gandhaH puruSheShu strIShu puMsu bhago ruchiH /
yo ashveShu vIreShu yo mR^igeShUta hastiShu /
kanyAyAM varcho yad bhUme tenAsmA.N api saM sR^ija mA no dvikShata kash chana //25//
shilA bhUmir ashmA pAMsuH sA bhUmiH saMdhR^itA dhR^itA /
tasyai hiraNyavakShase pR^ithivyA akaraM namaH //26//
yasyAM vR^ikShA vAnaspatyA dhruvAs tiShThanti vishvahA /
pR^ithivIM vishvadhAyasaM dhR^itAm achhAvadAmasi //27//
udIrANA utAsInAs tiShThantaH prakrAmantaH /
padbhyAM dakShiNasavyAbhyAM mA vyathiShmahi bhUmyAm //28//
vimR^igvarIM pR^ithivIm A vadAmi kShamAM bhUmiM brahmaNA vAvR^idhAnAm /
UrjaM puShTaM bibhratIm annabhAgaM ghR^itaM tvAbhi ni ShIdema bhUme //29//
shuddhA na Apas tanve kSharantu yo naH sedur apriye taM ni dadhmaH /
pavitreNa pR^ithivi mot punAmi //30// {3}

yAs te prAchIH pradisho yA udIchIr yAs te bhUme adharAd yAsh cha pashchAt /
syonAs tA mahyaM charate bhavantu mA ni paptaM bhuvane shishriyANaH //31//
mA naH pashchAn mA purastAn nudiShThA mottarAd adharAd uta /
svasti bhUme no bhava mA vidan paripanthino varIyo yAvayA vadham //32//
yAvat te 'bhi vipashyAmi bhUme sUryeNa medinA /
tAvan me chakShur mA meShTottarAmuttarAM samAm //33//
yach chhayAnaH paryAvarte dakShiNaM sakhyam abhi bhUme pArshvam uttAnAs tvA pratIchIM yat pR^iShTIbhir adhishemahe /
mA hiMsIs tatra no bhUme sarvasya pratishIvari //34//
yat te bhUme vikhanAmi kShipraM tad api rohatu /
mA te marma vimR^igvari mA te hR^idayam arpipam //35//
grIShmas te bhUme varShANi sharad dhemantaH shishiro vasantaH /
R^itavas te vihitA hAyanIr ahorAtre pR^ithivi no duhAtAm //36//
yApa sarpaM vijamAnA vimR^igvarI yasyAm Asann agnayo ye apsv antaH /
parA dasyUn dadatI devapIyUn indraM vR^iNAnA pR^ithivI na vR^itram shakrAya dadhre vR^iShabhAya vR^iShNe //37//
yasyAM sadohavirdhAne yUpo yasyAM nimIyate /
brahmANo yasyAm archanty R^igbhiH sAmnA yajurvidaH yujyante yasyAm R^itvijaH somam indrAya pAtave //38//
yasyAM pUrve bhUtakR^ita R^iShayo gA udAnR^ichuH /
sapta satreNa vedhaso yaj~nena tapasA saha //39//
sA no bhUmir A dishatu yad dhanaM kAmayAmahe /
bhago anuprayu~NktAm indra etu purogavaH //40// {4}

yasyAM gAyanti nR^ityanti bhUmyAM martyA vyaìlabAH /
yudhyante yasyAm Akrando yasyAm vadati dundubhiH /
sA no bhUmiH pra NudatAM sapatnAn asapatnaM mA pR^ithivI kR^iNotu //41//
yasyAm annaM vrIhiyavau yasyA imAH pa~ncha kR^iShTayaH /
bhUmyai parjanyapatnyai namo 'stu varShamedase //42//
yasyAH puro devakR^itAH kShetre yasyA vikurvate /
prajApatiH pR^ithivIM vishvagarbhAm AshAmAshAM raNyAM naH kR^iNotu //43//
nidhiM bibhratI bahudhA guhA vasu maNiM hiraNyaM pR^ithivI dadAtu me /
vasUni no vasudA rAsamAnA devI dadhAtu sumanasyamAnA //44//
janaM bibhratI bahudhA vivAchasaM nAnAdharmANaM pR^ithivI yathaukasam /
sahasraM dhArA draviNasya me duhAM dhruveva dhenur anapasphurantI //45//
yas te sarpo vR^ishchikas tR^iShTadaMshmA hemantajabdho bhR^imalo guhA shaye /
krimir jinvat pR^ithivi yadyad ejati prAvR^iShi tan naH sarpan mopa sR^ipad yach chhivaM tena no mR^iDa //46//
ye te panthAno bahavo janAyanA rathasya vartmAnasash cha yAtave /
yaiH saMcharanty ubhaye bhadrapApAs taM panthAnaM jayemAnamitram ataskaraM yach chhivaM tena no mR^iDa //47//
malvaM bibhratI gurubhR^id bhadrapApasya nidhanaM titikShuH /
varAheNa pR^ithivI saMvidAnA sUkarAya vi jihIte mR^igAya //48//
ye ta AraNyAH pashavo mR^igA vane hitAH siMhA vyAghrAH puruShAdash charanti /
ulaM vR^ikaM pR^ithivi duchhunAm ita R^ikShIkAM rakSho apa bAdhayAsmat //49//
ye gandharvA apsaraso ye chArAyAH kimIdinaH /
pishAchAnt sarvA rakShAMsi tAn asmad bhUme yAvaya //50// {5}

yAM dvipAdaH pakShiNaH saMpatanti haMsAH suparNAH shakunA vayAMsi /
yasyAM vAto mAtarishveyate rajAMsi kR^iNvaMsh chyAvayaMsh cha vR^ikShAn /
vAtasya pravAm upavAm anu vAty archiH //51//
yasyAM kR^iShNam aruNaM cha saMhite ahorAtre vihite bhUmyAm adhi /
varSheNa bhUmiH pR^ithivI vR^itAvR^itA sA no dadhAtu bhadrayA priye dhAmanidhAmani //52//
dyaush cha ma idaM pR^ithivI chAntarikShaM cha me vyachaH /
agniH sUrya Apo medhAM vishve devAsh cha saM daduH //53//
aham asmi sahamAna uttaro nAma bhUmyAm /
abhIShAD asmi vishvAShAD AshAmAshAM viShAsahiH //54//
ado yad devi prathamAnA purastAd devair uktA vyasarpo mahitvam /
A tvA subhUtam avishat tadAnIm akalpayathAH pradishash chatasraH //55//
ye grAmA yad araNyaM yAH sabhA adhi bhUmyAm /
ye saMgrAmAH samitayas teShu chAru vadema te //56//
ashva iva rajo dudhuve vi tAn janAn ya AkShiyan pR^ithivIM yAd ajAyata /
mandrAgretvarI bhuvanasya gopA vanaspatInAM gR^ibhir oShadhInAm //57//
yad vadAmi madhumat tad vadAmi yad IkShe tad vananti mA /
tviShImAn asmi jUtimAn avAnyAn hanmi dodhataH //58//
shantivA surabhiH syonA kIlAlodhnI payasvatI /
bhUmir adhi bravItu me pR^ithivI payasA saha //59//
yAm anvaichhad dhaviShA vishvakarmAntar arNave rajasi praviShTAm /
bhujiShya1M pAtraM nihitaM guhA yad Avir bhoge abhavan mAtR^imadbhyaH //60//
tvam asy AvapanI janAnAm aditiH kAmadughA paprathAnA /
yat ta UnaM tat ta A pUrayAti prajApatiH prathamajA R^itasya //61//
upasthAs te anamIvA ayakShmA asmabhyaM santu pR^ithivi prasUtAH /
dIrghaM na AyuH pratibudhyamAnA vayaM tubhyaM balihR^itaH syAma //62//
bhUme mAtar ni dhehi mA bhadrayA supratiShThitam /
saMvidAnA divA kave shriyAM mA dhehi bhUtyAm //63// {6}

475

naDam A roha na te atra loka idaM sIsaM bhAgadheyaM ta ehi /
yo goShu yakShmaH puruSheShu yakShmas tena tvaM sAkam adharA~N parehi //1//
aghashaMsaduHshaMsAbhyAM kareNAnukareNa cha /
yakShmaM cha sarvaM teneto mR^ityuM cha nir ajAmasi //2//
nir ito mR^ityuM nirR^itiM nir arAtim ajAmasi /
yo no dveShTi tam addhy agne akravyAd yam u dviShmas tam u te pra suvAmasi //3//
yady agniH kravyAd yadi vA vyAghra imaM goShThaM praviveshAnyokAH /
taM mAShAjyaM kR^itvA pra hiNomi dUraM sa gachhatv apsuShado 'py agnIn //4//
yat tvA kruddhAH prachakrur manyunA puruShe mR^ite /
sukalpam agne tat tvayA punas tvod dIpayAmasi //5//
punas tvAdityA rudrA vasavaH punar brahmA vasunItir agne /
punas tvA brahmaNas patir AdhAd dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya //6//
yo agniH kravyAt pravivesha no gR^iham imaM pashyann itaraM jAtavedasam /
taM harAmi pitR^iyaj~nAya dUraM sa gharmam indhAM parame sadhasthe //7//
kravyAdam agniM pra hiNomi dUram yamarAj~no gachhatu ripravAhaH /
ihAyam itaro jAtavedA devo devebhyo havyaM vahatu prajAnan //8//
kravyAdam agnim iShito harAmi janAn dR^iMhantaM vajreNa mR^ityum /
ni taM shAsmi gArhapatyena vidvAn pitR^INAM loke 'pi bhAgo astu //9//
kravyAdam agniM shashamAnam ukthya1M pra hiNomi pathibhiH pitR^iyANaiH /
mA devayAnaiH punar A gA atraivaidhi pitR^iShu jAgR^ihi tvam //10// {7}

sam indhate saMkasukaM svastaye shuddhA bhavantaH shuchayaH pAvakAH /
jahAti ripram aty ena eti samiddho agniH supunA punAti //11//
devo agniH saMkasuko divas pR^iShThAny Aruhat /
muchyamAno nir eNaso 'mog asmA.N ashastyAH //12//
asmin vayaM saMkasuke agnau riprANi mR^ijmahe /
abhUma yaj~niyAH shuddhAH pra Na AyUMShi tAriShat //13//
saMkasuko vikasuko nirR^itho yash cha nisvaraH /
te te yakShmaM savedaso dUrAd dUram anInashan //14//
yo no ashveShu vIreShu yo no goShv ajAviShu /
kravyAdaM nir nudAmasi yo agnir janayopanaH //15//
anyebhyas tvA puruShebhyo gobhyo ashvebhyas tvA /
niH kravyAdaM nudAmasi yo agnir jIvitayopanaH //16//
yasmin devA amR^ijata yasmin manuShyA uta /
tasmin ghR^itastAvo mR^iShTvA tvam agne divaM ruha //17//
samiddho agna Ahuta sa no mAbhyapakramIH /
atraiva dIdihi dyavi jyok cha sUryaM dR^ishe //18//
sIse mR^iDDhvaM nade mR^iDDhvam agnau saMkasuke cha yat /
atho avyAM rAmAyAM shIrShaktim upabarhaNe //19//
sIse malaM sAdayitvA shIrShaktim upabarhaNe /
avyAm asiknyAM mR^iShTvA shuddhA bhavata yaj~niyAH //20// {8}

paraM mR^ityo anu parehi panthAM yas ta eSha itaro devayAnAt /
chakShuShmate shR^iNvate te bravImIheme vIrA bahavo bhavantu //21//
ime jIvA vi mR^itair AvavR^itrann abhUd bhadrA devahutir no adya /
prA~ncho agAma nR^itaye hasAya suvIrAso vidatham A vadema //22//
imaM jIvebhyaH paridhiM dadhAmi maiShAM nu gAd aparo artham etam /
shataM jIvantaH sharadaH purUchIs tiro mR^ityuM dadhatAm parvatena //23//
A rohatAyur jarasaM vR^iNAnA anupUrvaM yatamAnA yati stha /
tAn vas tvaShTA sujanimA sajoShAH sarvam Ayur nayatu jIvanAya //24//
yathAhAny anupUrvaM bhavanti yatha rtava R^itubhir yanti sAkam /
yathA na pUrvam aparo jahAty evA dhAtar AyUMShi kalpayaiShAm //25//
ashmanvatI rIyate saM rabhadhvaM vIrayadhvaM pra taratA sakhAyaH /
atrA jahIta ye asan durevA anamIvAn ut taremAbhi vAjAn //26//
ut tiShThatA pra taratA sakhAyo 'shmanvatI nadI syandata iyam /
atrA jahIta ye asann ashivAH shivAnt syonAn ut taremAbhi vAjAn //27//
vaishvadevIM varchasA A rabhadhvaM shuddhA bhavantaH shuchayaH pAvakAH /
atikrAmanto duritA padAni shataM himAH sarvavIrA madema //28//
udIchInaiH pathibhir vAyumadbhir atikrAmanto 'varAn parebhiH /
triH sapta kR^itva R^iShayaH paretA mR^ityuM praty auhan padayopanena //29//
mR^ityoH padaM yopayanta eta drAghIya AyuH prataraM dadhAnAH /
AsInA mR^ityuM nudatA sadhasthe 'tha jIvAso vidatham A vadema //30// {9}

imA nArIr avidhavAH supatnIr A~njanena sarpiShA saM spR^ishantAm /
anashravo anamIvAH suratnA A rohantu janayo yonim agre //31//
vyAkaromi haviShAham etau tau brahmaNA vy ahaM kalpayAmi /
svadhAM pitR^ibhyo ajarAM kR^iNomi dIrgheNAyuShA sam imAnt sR^ijAmi //32//
yo no agniH pitaro hR^itsv antar AviveshAmR^ito martyeShu /
mayy ahaM taM pari gR^ihNAmi devaM mA so asmAn dvikShata mA vayaM tam //33//
apAvR^itya gArhapatyAt kravyAdA preta dakShiNA /
priyaM pitR^ibhya Atmane brahmabhyaH kR^iNutA priyam //34//
dvibhAgadhanam AdAya pra kShiNAty avartyA /
agniH putrasya jyeShThasya yaH kravyAd anirAhitah //35//
yat kR^iShate yad vanute yach cha vasnena vindate /
sarvaM martyasya tan nAsti kravyAch ched anirAhitaH //36//
ayaj~niyo hatavarchA bhavati nainena havir attave /
chhinatti kR^iShyA gor dhanAd yaM kravyAd anuvartate //37//
muhur gR^idhyaiH pra vadaty Artim martyo nItya /
kravyAd yAn agnir antikAd anuvidvAn vitAvati //38//
grAhyA gR^ihAH saM sR^ijyante striyA yan mriyate patiH /
brahmaiva vidvAn eShyo3 yaH kravyAdaM nirAdadhat //39//
yad ripraM shamalaM chakR^ima yach cha duShkR^itam /
Apo mA tasmAch chhumbhantv agneH saMkasukAch cha yat //40// {10}

tA adharAd udIchIr AvavR^itran prajAnaitIH pathibhir devayAnaiH /
parvatasya vR^iShabhasyAdhi pR^iShThe navAsh charanti saritaH purANIH //41//
agne akravyAn niH kravyAdaM nudA devayajanaM vaha //42//
imaM kravyAd A viveshAyaM kravyAdam anv agAt /
vyAgrau kR^itvA nAnAnaM taM harAmi shivAparam //43//
antardhir devAnAM paridhir manuShyANAm agnir gArhpatya ubhayAn antarA shritaH //44//
jIvAnAm AyuH pra tira tvam agne pitR^INAM lokam api gachhantu ye mR^itAH /
sugArhapatyo vitapann arAtim uShAmuShAM shreyasIM dhehy asmai //45//
sarvAn agne sahamAnaH sapatnAn aiShAm UrjaM rayim asmAsu dhehi //46//
imam indraM vahniM paprim anvArabhadhvaM sa vo nir vakShad duritAd avadyAt /
tenApa hata sharum ApatantaM tena rudrasya pari pAtAstAm //47//
anaDvAhaM plavam anvArabhadhvaM sa vo nir vakShad duritAd avadyAt /
A rohata savitur nAvam etAM ShaDbhir urvIbhir amatiM tarema //48//
ahorAtre anv eShi bibhrat kShemyas tiShThan prataraNaH suvIraH /
anAturAnt sumanasas talpa bibhraj jyog eva naH puruShagandhir edhi //49//
te devebhya A vR^ishchante pApaM jIvanti sarvadA /
kravyAd yAn agnir antikAd ashva ivAnuvapate naDam //50// {11}

ye 'shraddhA dhanakAmyA kravyAdA samAsate /
te vA anyeShAM kumbhIM paryAdadhati sarvadA //51//
preva pipatiShati manasA muhur A vartate punaH /
kravyAd yAn agnir antikAd anuvidvAn vitAvati //52//
aviH kR^iShNA bhAgadheyaM pashUnAM sIsaM kravyAd api chandraM ta AhuH /
mAShAH piShTA bhAgadheyaM te havyam araNyAnyA gahvaraM sachasva //53//
iShIkAM jaratIm iShTvA tilpi~njaM daNDanaM naDam /
tam indra idhmam kR^itvA yamasyAgniM nirAdadhau //54//
pratya~ncham arkaM pratyarpayitvA pravidvAn panthAM vi hy Avivesha /
parAmIShAm asUn didesha dIrgheNAyuShA sam imAnt sR^ijAmi //55// {12}

476

pumAn puMso 'dhi tiShTha charmehi tatra hvayasva yatamA priyA te /
yAvantAv agre prathamaM sameyathus tad vAM vayo yamarAjye samAnam //1//
tAvad vAM chakShus tati vIryANi tAvat tejas tatidhA vAjinAni /
agniH sharIraM sachate yadaidho 'dhA pakvAn mithunA saM bhavAthaH //2//
sam asmiMl loke sam u devayAne saM smA sametaM yamarAjyeShu /
pUtau pavitrair upa tad dhvayethAM yadyad reto adhi vAM saMbabhUva //3//
Apas putrAso abhi saM vishadhvam imaM jIvaM jIvadhanyAH sametya /
tAsAM bhajadhvam amR^itaM yam Ahur odanaM pachati vAM janitrI //4//
yaM vAM pitA pachati yaM cha mAtA riprAn nirmuktyai shamalAch cha vAchaH /
sa odanaH shatadhAraH svarga ubhe vy Apa nabhasI mahitvA //5//
ubhe nabhasI ubhayAMsh cha lokAn ye yajvanAm abhijitAH svargAH /
teShAM jyotiShmAn madhumAn yo agre tasmin putrair jarasi saM shrayethAm //6//
prAchIMprAchIM pradisham A rabhethAm etaM lokaM shraddadhAnAH sachante /
yad vAM pakvaM pariviShTam agnau tasya guptaye daMpatI saM shrayethAm //7//
dakShiNAM disham abhi nakShamANau paryAvartethAm abhi pAtram etat /
tasmin vAM yamaH pitR^ibhiH saMvidAnaH pakvAya sharma bahulaM ni yachhAt //8//
pratIchI dishAm iyam id varaM yasyAM somo adhipA mR^iDitA cha /
tasyAM shrayethAM sukR^itaH sachethAm adhA pakvAn mithunA saM bhavAthaH //9//
uttaraM rAShTraM prajayottarAvad dishAm udIchI kR^iNavan no agram /
pA~NktaM chhandaH puruSho babhUva vishvair vishvA~NgaiH saha saM bhavema //10// {13}

dhruveyaM virAN namo astv asyai shivA putrebhya uta mahyam astu /
sA no devy adite vishvavAra irya iva gopA abhi rakSha pakvam //11//
piteva putrAn abhi saM svajasva naH shivA no vAtA iha vAntu bhUmau /
yam odanaM pachato devate iha taM nas tapa uta satyaM cha vettu //12//
yadyad kR^iShNaH shakuna eha gatvA tsaran viShaktaM bila AsasAda /
yad vA dAsy ArdrahastA sama~Nkta ulUkhalaM musalaM shumbhatApaH //13//
ayaM grAvA pR^ithubudhno vayodhAH pUtaH pavitrair apa hantu rakShaH /
A roha charma mahi sharma yachha mA daMpatI pautram aghaM ni gAtAm //14//
vanaspatiH saha devair na Agan rakShaH pishAchA.N apabAdhamAnaH /
sa uch chhrayAtai pra vadAti vAchaM tena lokA.N abhi sarvAn jayema //15//
sapta medhAn pashavaH pary agR^ihNan ya eShAM jyotiShmAm? uta yash chakarsha /
trayastriMshad devatAs tAn sachante sa naH svargam abhi neSha lokam //16//
svargaM lokam abhi no nayAsi saM jAyayA saha putraiH syAma /
gR^ihNAmi hastam anu maitv atra mA nas tArIn nirR^itir mo arAtiH //17//
grAhiM pApmAnam ati tA.N ayAma tamo vy asya pra vadAsi valgu /
vAnaspatya udyato mA jihiMsIr mA taNDulaM vi sharIr devayantam //18//
vishvavyachA ghR^itapR^iShTho bhaviShyant sayonir lokam upa yAhy etam /
varShavR^iddham upa yachha shUrpaM tuShaM palAvAn apa tad vinaktu //19//
trayo lokAH saMmitA brAhmaNena dyaur evAsau pR^ithivy a1ntarikSham /
aMshUn gR^ibhItvAnvArabhethAm A pyAyantAM punar A yantu shUrpam //20// {14}

pR^ithag rUpANi bahudhA pashUnAm ekarUpo bhavasi saM samR^iddhyA /
etAM tvachaM lohinIM tAM nudasva grAvA shumbhAti malaga iva vastrA //21//
pR^ithivIM tvA pR^ithivyAm A veshayAmi tanUH samAnI vikR^itA ta eShA /
yadyad dyuttaM likhitam arpaNena tena mA susror brahmaNApi tad vapAmi //22//
janitrIva prati haryAsi sUnuM saM tvA dadhAmi pR^ithivIM pR^ithivyA /
ukhA kumbhI vedyAM mA vyathiShThA yaj~nAyudhair AjyenAtiShaktA //23//
agniH pachan rakShatu tvA purastAd indro rakShatu dakShiNato marutvAn /
varuNas tvA dR^iMhAd dharuNe pratIchyA uttarAt tvA somaH saM dadAtai //24//
pUtAH pavitraiH pavante abhrAd divaM cha yanti pR^ithivIM cha lokAn /
tA jIvalA jIvadhanyAH pratiShThAH pAtra AsiktAH pary agnir indhAm //25//
A yanti divaH pR^ithivIM sachante bhUmyAH sachante adhy antarikSham /
shuddhAH satIs tA u shumbhanta eva tA naH svargam abhi lokaM nayantu //26//
uteva prabhvIr uta saMmitAsa uta shukrAH shuchayash chAmR^itAsaH /
tA odanaM daMpatibhyAM prashiShTA ApaH shikShantIH pachatA sunAthAH //27//
saMkhyAtA stokAH pR^ithivIM sachante prANApAnaiH saMmitA oShadhIbhiH /
asaMkhyAtA opyamAnAH suvarNAH sarvaM vyApuH shuchayaH shuchitvam //28//
ud yodhanty abhi valganti taptAH phenam asyanti bahulAMsh cha bindUn /
yoSheva dR^iShTvA patim R^itviyAyaitais taNDulair bhavatA sam ApaH //29//
ut thApaya sIdato budhna enAn adbhir AtmAnam abhi saM spR^ishantAm /
amAsi pAtrair udakaM yad etan mitAs taNDulAH pradisho yadImAH //30// {15}

pra yachha parshuM tvarayA harausam ahiMsanta oShadhIr dAntu parvan /
yAsAM somaH pari rAjyaM babhUvAmanyutA no vIrudho bhavantu //31//
navaM barhir odanAya stR^iNIta priyaM hR^idash chakShuSho valgv astu /
tasmin devAH saha daivIr vishantv imaM prAshnantv R^itubhir niShadya //32//
vanaspate stIrNam A sIda barhir agniShTobhaiH saMmito devatAbhiH /
tvaShTreva rUpaM sukR^itaM svadhityainA ehAH pari pAtre dadR^ishrAm //33//
ShaShTyAM sharatsu nidhipA abhIchhAt svaH pakvenAbhy ashnavAtai /
upainaM jIvAn pitarash cha putrA etaM svargaM gamayAntam agneH //34//
dhartA dhriyasva dharuNe pR^ithivyA achyutaM tvA devatAsh chyAvayantu /
taM tvA daMpatI jIvantau jIvaputrAv ud vAsayAtaH pary agnidhAnAt //35//
sarvAnt samAgA abhijitya lokAn yAvantaH kAmAH sam atItR^ipas tAn /
vi gAhethAm AyavanaM cha darvir ekasmin pAtre adhy ud dharainam //36//
upa stR^iNIhi prathaya purastAd ghR^itena pAtram abhi ghArayaitat /
vAshrevosrA taruNaM stanasyum imaM devAso abhihi~NkR^iNota //37//
upAstarIr akaro lokam etam uruH prathatAm asamaH svargaH /
tasmiM chhrayAtai mahiShaH suparNo devA enaM devatAbhyaH pra yachhAn //38//
yadyaj jAyA pachati tvat paraHparaH patir vA jAye tvat tiraH /
saM tat sR^ijethAM saha vAM tad astu saMpAdayantau saha lokam ekam //39//
yAvanto asyAH pR^ithivIM sachante asmat putrAH pari ye saMbabhUvuH /
sarvAMs tA.N upa pAtre hvayethAM nAbhiM jAnAnAH shishavaH samAyAn //40// {16}

vasor yA dhArA madhunA prapInA ghR^itena mishrA amR^itasya nAbhayaH /
sarvAs tA ava rundhe svargaH ShaShTyAM sharatsu nidhipA abhIchhAt //41//
nidhiM nidhipA abhy enam ichhAd anIshvarA abhitaH santu ye 'nye /
asmAbhir datto nihitaH svargas tribhiH kANDais trInt svargAn arukShat //42//
agnI rakShas tapatu yad videvaM kravyAd pishAcha iha mA pra pAsta /
nudAma enam apa rudhmo asmad AdityA enam a~NgirasaH sachantAm //43//
Adityebhyo a~Ngirobhyo madhv idaM ghR^itena mishraM prati vedayAmi /
shuddhahastau brAhmaNasyAnihatyaitaM svargaM sukR^itAv apItam //44//
idaM prApam uttamaM kANDam asya yasmAl lokAt parameShThI samApa /
A si~ncha sarpir ghR^itavat sam a~Ngdhy eSha bhAgo a~Ngiraso no atra //45//
satyAya cha tapase devatAbhyo nidhiM shevadhiM pari dadma etam /
mA no dyUte 'va gAn mA samityAM mA smAnyasmA ut sR^ijatA purA mat //46//
ahaM pachAmy ahaM dadAmi mamed u karman karuNe 'dhi jAyA /
kaumAro loko ajaniShTa putro3 'nvArabhethAM vaya uttarAvat //47//
na kilbiSham atra nAdhAro asti na yan mitraiH samamamAna eti /
anUnaM pAtraM nihitaM na etat paktAraM pakvaH punar A vishAti //48//
priyaM priyANAM kR^iNavAma tamas te yantu yatame dviShanti /
dhenur anaDvAn vayovaya Ayad eva pauruSheyam apa mR^ityuM nudantu //49//
sam agnayaH vidur anyo anyaM ya oShadhIH sachate yash cha sindhUn /
yAvanto devA divy A3tapanti hiraNyaM jyotiH pachato babhUva //50// {17}

eShA tvachAM puruShe saM babhUvAnagnAH sarve pashavo ye anye /
kShatreNAtmAnaM pari dhApayAtho 'motaM vAso mukham odanasya //51//
yad akSheShu vadA yat samityAM yad vA vadA anR^itaM vittakAmyA /
samAnaM tantum abhi samvasAnau tasmint sarvaM shamalaM sAdayAthaH //52//
varShaM vanuShvApi gachha devAMs tvacho dhUmaM pary ut pAtayAsi /
vishvavyachA ghR^itapR^iShTho bhaviShyant sayonir lokam upa yAhy etam //53//
tanvaM svargo bahudhA vi chakre yathA vida Atmann anyavarNAm /
apAjait kR^iShNAM rushatIM punAno yA lohinI tAM te agnau juhomi //54//
prAchyai tvA dishe3 'gnaye 'dhipataye 'sitAya rakShitra AdityAyeShumate /
etaM pari dadmas taM no gopAyatAsmAkam aitoH /
diShTaM no atra jarase ni neShaj jarA mR^ityave pari No dadAtv atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //55//
dakShiNAyai tvA disha indrAyAdhipataye tirashchirAjaye rakShitre yamAyeShumate /
etaM pari dadmas taM no gopAyatAsmAkam aitoH /
diShTaM no atra jarase ni neShaj jarA mR^ityave pari No dadAtv atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //56//
pratIchyai tvA dishe varuNAyAdhipataye pR^idAkave rakShitre 'nnAyeShumate /
etaM pari dadmas taM no gopAyatAsmAkam aitoH /
diShTaM no atra jarase ni neShaj jarA mR^ityave pari No dadAtv atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //57//
udIchyai tvA dishe somAyAdhipataye svajAya rakShitre 'shanyA iShumatyai /
etaM pari dadmas taM no gopAyatAsmAkam aitoH /
diShTaM no atra jarase ni neShaj jarA mR^ityave pari No dadAtv atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //58//
dhruvAyai tvA dishe viShNave 'dhipataye kalmAShagrIvAya rakShitra oShadhIbhya iShumatIbhyaH /
etaM pari dadmas taM no gopAyatAsmAkam aitoH /
diShTaM no atra jarase ni neShaj jarA mR^ityave pari No dadAtv atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //59//
UrdhvAyai tvA dishe bR^ihaspataye 'dhipataye shvitrAya rakShitre varShAyeShumate /
etaM pari dadmas taM no gopAyatAsmAkam aitoH /
diShTaM no atra jarase ni neShaj jarA mR^ityave pari No dadAtv atha pakvena saha saM bhavema //60// {18}

477

dadAmIty eva brUyAd anu chainAm abhutsata /
vashAM brahmabhyo yAchadbhyas tat prajAvad apatyavat //1//
prajayA sa vi krINIte pashubhish chopa dasyati /
ya ArSheyebhyo yAchadbhyo devAnAM gAM na ditsati //2//
kUTayAsya saM shIryante shloNayA kAtam ardati /
baNDayA dahyante gR^ihAH kANayA dIyate svam //3//
vilohito adhiShThAnAch chhakno vindati gopatim /
tathA vashAyAH saMvidyaM duradabhnA hy ùchyase //4//
pador asyA adhiShThAnAd viklindur nAma vindati /
anAmanAt saM shIryante yA mukhenopajighrati //5//
yo asyAH karNAv Askunoty A sa deveShu vR^ishchate /
lakShma kurva iti manyate kanIyaH kR^iNute svam //6//
yad asyAH kasmai chid bhogAya bAlAn kash chit prakR^intati /
tataH kishorA mriyante vatsAMsh cha ghAtuko vR^ikaH //7//
yad asyA gopatau satyA loma dhvA~NkSho ajIhiDat /
tataH kumArA mriyante yakShmo vindaty anAmanAt //8//
yad asyAH palpUlanaM shakR^id dAsI samasyati /
tato 'parUpaM jAyate tasmAd avyeShyad enasaH //9//
jAyamAnAbhi jAyate devAnt sabrAhmaNAn vashA /
tasmAd brahmabhyo deyaiShA tad AhuH svasya gopanam //10// {19}

ya enAM vanim Ayanti teShAM devakR^itA vashA /
brahmajyeyaM tad abruvan ya enAM nipriyAyate //11//
ya ArSheyebhyo yAchadbhyo devAnAM gAM na ditsati /
A sa deveShu vR^ishchate brAhmaNAnAM cha manyave //12//
yo asya syAd vashAbhogo anyAm ichheta tarhi saH /
hiMste adattA puruShaM yAchitAM cha na ditsati //13//
yathA shevadhir nihito brAhmaNAnAM tathA vashA /
tAm etad achhAyanti yasmin kasmiMsh cha jAyate //14//
svam etad achhAyanti yad vashAM brAhmaNA abhi /
yathainAn anyasmin jinIyAd evAsyA nirodhanam //15//
chared evA traihAyaNAd avij~nAtagadA satI /
vashAM cha vidyAn nArada brAhmaNAs tarhy eShyAH //16//
ya enAm avashAm Aha devAnAM nihitaM nidhim /
ubhau tasmai bhavAsharvau parikramyeShum asyatah //17//
yo asyA Udho na vedAtho asyA stanAn uta /
ubhayenaivAsmai duhe dAtuM ched ashakad vashAm //18//
duradabhnainam A shaye yAchitAM cha na ditsati /
nAsmai kAmAH sam R^idhyante yAm adattvA chikIrShati //19//
devA vashAm ayAchan mukhaM kR^itvA brAhmaNam /
teShAM sarveShAm adadad dheDaM ny eti mAnuShaH //20// {20}

heDaM pashUnAM ny eti brAhmaNebhyo 'dadad vashAm /
devAnAM nihitaM bhAgaM martyash chen nipriyAyate //21//
yad anye shataM yAcheyur brAhmaNA gopatiM vashAm /
athainAM devA abruvann evaM ha viduSho vashA //22//
ya evaM viduShe 'dattvAthAnyebhyo dadad vashAm /
durgA tasmA adhiShThAne pR^ithivI sahadevatA //23//
devA vashAm ayAchan yasminn agre ajAyata /
tAm etAM vidyAn nAradaH saha devair ud Ajata //24//
anapatyam alpapashuM vashA kR^iNoti pUruSham /
brAhmaNaish cha yAchitAm athainAM nipriyAyate //25//
agnIShomAbhyAM kAmAya mitrAya varuNAya cha /
tebhyo yAchanti brAhmaNAs teShv A vR^ishchate 'dadat //26//
yAvad asyA gopatir nopashR^iNuyAd R^ichaH svayam /
chared asya tAvad goShu nAsya shrutvA gR^ihe vaset //27//
yo asyA R^icha upashrutyAtha goShv achIcharat /
Ayush cha tasya bhUtiM cha devA vR^ishchanti hIDitAH //28//
vashA charantI bahudhA devAnAM nihito nidhiH /
AviSh kR^iNuShva rUpANi yadA sthAma jighAMsati //29//
Avir AtmAnaM kR^iNute yadA sthAma jighAMsati /
atho ha brahmabhyo vashA yA~nchyAya kR^iNute manaH //30// {21}

manasA saM kalpayati tad devA.N api gachhati /
tato ha brahmANo vashAm upaprayanti yAchitum //31//
svadhAkAreNa pitR^ibhyo yaj~nena devatAbhyaH /
dAnena rAjanyo vashAyA mAtur heDam na gachhati //32//
vashA mAtA rAjanyasya tathA saMbhUtam agrashaH /
tasyA Ahur anarpaNaM yad brahmabhyaH pradIyate //33//
yathAjyaM pragR^ihItam Alumpet srucho agnaye /
evA ha brahmabhyo vashAm agnaya A vR^ishchate 'dadat //34//
puroDAshavatsA sudughA loke 'smA upa tiShThati /
sAsmai sarvAn kAmAn vashA pradaduShe duhe //35//
sarvAn kAmAn yamarAjye vashA pradaduShe duhe /
athAhur nArakaM lokaM nirundhAnasya yAchitAm //36//
pravIyamAnA charati kruddhA gopataye vashA /
vehataM mA manyamAno mR^ityoH pAsheShu badhyatAm //37//
yo vehataM manyamAno 'mA cha pachate vashAm /
apy asya putrAn pautrAMsh cha yAchayate bR^ihaspatiH //38//
mahad eShAva tapati charantI goShu gaur api /
atho ha gopataye vashAdaduShe viShaM duhe //39//
priyaM pashUnAM bhavati yad brahmabhyaH pradIyate /
atho vashAyAs tat priyaM yad devatrA haviH syAt //40// {22}

yA vashA udakalpayan devA yaj~nAd udetya /
tAsAM viliptyaM bhImAm udAkuruta nAradaH //41//
tAM devA amImAMsanta vasheyA3m avasheti /
tAm abravIn nArada eShA vashAnAM vashatameti //42//
kati nu vashA nArada yAs tvaM vettha manuShyajAH /
tAs tvA pR^ichhAmi vidvAMsaM kasyA nAshnIyAd abrAhmaNaH //43//
viliptyA bR^ihaspate yA cha sUtavashA vashA /
tasyA nAshnIyAd abrAhmaNo ya AshaMseta bhUtyAm //44//
namas te astu nAradAnuShThu viduShe vashA /
katamAsAM bhImatamA yAm adattvA parAbhavet //45//
viliptI yA bR^ihaspate 'tho sUtavashA vashA /
tasyA nAshnIyAd abrAhmaNo ya AshaMseta bhUtyAm //46//
trINi vai vashAjAtAni viliptI sUtavashA vashA /
tAH pra yachhed brahmabhyaH so 'nAvraskaH prajApatau //47//
etad vo brAhmaNA havir iti manvIta yAchitaH /
vashAM ched enaM yAcheyur yA bhImAdaduSho gR^ihe //48//
devA vashAM pary avadan na no 'dAd iti hIDitAH /
etAbhir R^igbhir bhedaM tasmAd vai sa parAbhavat //49//
utainAM bhedo nAdadAd vashAm indreNa yAchitaH /
tasmAt taM devA Agaso 'vR^ishchann ahamuttare //50//
ye vashAyA adAnAya vadanti parirApiNah /
indrasya manyave jAlmA A vR^ishchante achittyA //51//
ye gopatiM parANIyAthAhur mA dadA iti /
rudrasyAstAM te hetIM pari yanty achittyA //52//
yadi hutaM yady ahutAm amA cha pachate vashAm /
devAnt sabrAhmaNAn R^itvA jihmo lokAn nir R^ichhati //53// {23}

 {1}
shrameNa tapasA sR^iShTA brahmaNA vittA rte shritA //1//
satyenAvR^itA shriyA prAvR^itA yashasA parIvR^itA //2//
svadhayA parihitA shraddhayA paryUDhA dIkShayA guptA yaj~ne pratiShThitA loko nidhanam //3//

 {2}
ojash cha tejash cha sahash cha balaM cha vAk chendriyaM cha shrIsh cha dharmash cha //7//

 {3}
saiShA bhImA brahmagavy a1ghaviShA sAkShAt kR^ityA kUlbajam AvR^itA //12//

sarvANy asyAM krUrANi sarve puruShavadhAH //14//
sA brahmajyaM devapIyuM brahmagavy AdIyamAnA mR^ityoH padvISha A dyati //15//

menir duhyamAnA shIrShaktir dugdhA //23//
sedir upatiShThantI mithoyodhaH parAmR^iShTA //24//
sharavyA3 mukhe 'pinahyamAna R^itir hanyamAnA //25//
aghaviShA nipatantI tamo nipatitA //26//
anugachhantI prANAn upa dAsayati brahmagavI brahmajyasya //27// {26}

 {4}
vairaM vikR^ityamAnA pautrAdyaM vibhAjyamAnA //28//
devahetir hriyamANA vyr?ddhir hR^itA //29//
pApmAdhidhIyamAnA pAruShyam avadhIyamAnA //30//
viShaM prayasyantI takmA prayastA //31//
aghaM prachyamAnA duShvapnyaM pakvA //32//
mUlabarhaNI paryAkriyamANA kShitiH paryAkR^itA //33//
asaMj~nA gandhena shug uddhriyamANAshIviSha uddhR^itA //34//

sharvaH kruddhaH pishyamAnA shimidA pishitA //36//
avartir ashyamAnA nirR^itir ashitA //37//
ashitA lokAch chhinatti brahmagavI brahmajyam asmAch chAmuShmAch cha //38// {27}
 {5}
tasyA AhananaM kR^ityA menir AshasanaM valaga Ubadhyam //39//
asvagatA parihNutA //40//

avAstum enam asvagam aprajasaM karoty aparAparaNo bhavati kShIyate //45//

 {6}
kShipraM vai tasyAhanane gR^idhrAH kurvata ailabam //47//

A datse jinatAM varcha iShTaM pUrtaM chAshiShaH //56//
AdAya jItaM jItAya loke3 'muShmin pra yachhasi //57//

 {7}

pra skandhAn pra shiro jahi //67//
lomAny asya saM chhindhi tvacham asya vi veShTaya //68//

asthIny asya pIDaya majjAnam asya nir jahi //70//

agnir enaM kravyAt pR^ithivyA nudatAm ud oShatu vAyur antarikShAn mahato varimNaH //72//
sUrya enaM divaH pra NudatAM ny oShatu //73// {30}

udehi vAjin yo apsv a1ntar idaM rAShTraM pra visha sUnR^itAvat /
yo rohito vishvam idaM jajAna sa tvA rAShTrAya subhR^itaM bibhartu //1//
ud vAja A gan yo apsv a1ntar visha A roha tvadyonayo yAH /
somaM dadhAno 'pa oShadhIr gAsh chatuShpado dvipada A veshayeha //2//
yUyam ugrA marutaH pR^ishnimAtara indreNa yujA pra mR^iNIta shatrUn /
A vo rohitaH shR^iNavat sudAnavas triShaptAso marutaH svAdusaMmudaH //3//
ruho ruroha rohita A ruroha garbho janInAM januShAm upastham /
tAbhiH saMrabdham anv avindan ShaD urvIr gAtuM prapashyann iha rAShTram AhAH //4//
A te rAShTram iha rohito 'hArShId vy Asthan mR^idho abhayaM te abhUt /
tasmai te dyAvApR^ithivI revatIbhiH kAmaM duhAtAm iha shakvarIbhiH //5//
rohito dyAvApR^ithivI jajAna tatra tantuM parameShThI tatAna /
tatra shishriye 'ja ekapAdo 'dR^iMhad dyAvApR^ithivI balena //6//
rohito dyAvApR^ithivI adR^iMhat tena sva stabhitaM tena nAkaH /
tenAntarikShaM vimitA rajAMsi tena devA amR^itam anv avindan //7//
vi rohito amR^ishad vishvarUpaM samAkurvANaH praruho ruhash cha /
divaM rUDhvA mahatA mahimnA saM te rAShTram anaktu payasA ghR^itena //8//
yAs te ruhaH praruho yAs ta Aruho yAbhir ApR^iNAsi divam antarikSham /
tAsAM brahmaNA payasA vavR^idhAno vishi rAShTre jAgR^ihi rohitasya //9//
yas te vishas tapasaH saMbabhUvur vatsaM gAyatrIm anu tA ihAguH /
tAs tvA vishantu manasA shivena saMmAtA vatso abhy etu rohitaH //10// {1}

Urdhvo rohito adhi nAke asthAd vishvA rUpANi janayan yuvA kaviH /
tigmenAgnir jyotiShA vi bhAti tR^itIye chakre rajasi priyANi //11//
sahasrashR^i~Ngo vR^iShabho jAtavedA ghR^itAhutaH somapR^iShThaH suvIraH /
mA mA hAsIn nAthito net tvA jahAni gopoShaM cha me vIrapoShaM cha dhehi //12//
rohito yaj~nasya janitA mukhaM cha rohitAya vAchA shrotreNa manasA juhomi /
rohitaM devA yanti sumanasyamAnAH sa mA rohaiH sAmityai rohayatu //13//
rohito yaj~naM vy adadhAd vishvakarmaNe tasmAt tejAMsy upa memAny AguH /
vocheyaM te nAbhiM bhuvanasyAdhi majmani //14//
A tvA ruroha bR^ihaty ùta pa~Nktir A kakub varchasA jAtavedaH /
A tvA rurohoShNihAkSharo vaShaTkAra A tvA ruroha rohito retasA saha //15//
ayaM vaste garbhaM pR^ithivyA divaM vaste 'yam antarikSham /
ayaM bradhnasya viShTapi svar lokAn vy Anashe //16//
vAchas pate pR^ithivI naH syonA syonA yonis talpA naH sushevA /
ihaiva prANaH sakhye no astu taM tvA parameShThin pary agnir AyuShA varchasA dadhAtu //17//
vAchas pata R^itavaH pa~ncha ye nau vaishvakarmaNAH pari ye saMbabhUvuH /
ihaiva prANaH sakhye no astu taM tvA parameShThin pari rohita AyuShA varchasA dadhAtu //18//
vAchas pate saumanasaM manash cha goShThe no gA janaya yoniShu prajAH /
ihaiva prANaH sakhye no astu taM tvA parameShThin pary aham AyuShA varchasA dadhAtu //19//
pari tvA dhAt savitA devo agnir varchasA mitrAvaruNAv abhi tvA /
sarvA arAtIr avakrAmann ehIdaM rAShTram akaraH sunR^itAvat //20// {2}

yaM tvA pR^iShatI rathe praShTir vahati rohita /
shubhA yAsi riNann apaH //21//
anuvratA rohiNI rohitasya sUriH suvarNA bR^ihatI suvarchAH /
tayA vAjAn vishvarUpAM jayema tayA vishvAH pR^itanA abhi ShyAma //22//
idaM sado rohiNI rohitasyAsau panthAH pR^iShatI yena yAti /
tAM gandharvAH kashyapA un nayanti tAM rakShanti kavayo 'pramAdam //23//
sUryasyAshvA harayaH ketumantaH sadA vahanty amR^itAH sukhaM ratham /

adhA sapatnAn mAmakAn agnes tejobhir AdiShi //30// {3}

agne sapatnAn adharAn pAdayAsmad vyathayA sajAtam utpipAnaM bR^ihaspate /
indrAgnI mitrAvaruNAv adhare padyantAm apratimanyUyamAnAH //31//

avainAn ashmanA jahi te yantv adhamaM tamaH //32//

ye devA rAShTrabhR^ito 'bhito yanti sUryam /
taiSh Te rohitaH samvidAno rAShTraM dadhAtu sumanasyamAnaH //35//
ut tvA yaj~nA brahmapUtA vahanty adhvagato harayas tvA vahanti /
tiraH samudram ati rochase 'rNavam //36//
rohite dyAvApR^ithivI adhi shrite vasujiti gojiti saMdhanAjiti /
sahasraM yasya janimAni sapta cha vocheyaM te nAbhiM bhuvanasyAdhi majmani //37//
yashA yAsi pradisho dishash cha yashAH pashUnAm uta charShaNInAm /
yashAH pR^ithivyA adityA upasthe 'haM bhUyAsaM saviteva chAruH //38//
amutra sann iha vetthetaH saMs tAni pashyasi /
itaH pashyanti rochanaM divi sUryaM vipashchitam //39//
devo devAn marchayasy antash charasy arNave /
samAnam agnim indhate taM viduH kavayaH pare //40// {4}

avaH pareNa para enAvareNa padA vatsaM bibratI gaur ud asthAt /
sA kadrIchI kaM svid ardhaM parAgAt kva svit sUte nahi yUthe asmin //41//
ekapadI dvipadI sA chatuShpady aShTApadI navapadI babhUvuShI /
sahasrAkSharA bhuvanasya pa~Nktis tasyAH samudrA adhi vi kSharanti //42//
Arohan dyAm amR^itaH prAva me vachaH /
ut tvA yaj~nA brahmapUtA vahanty adhvagato harayas tvA vahanti //43//
veda tat te amartya yat ta AkramaNaM divi /
yat te sadhasthaM parame vyoman //44//
sUryo dyAM sUryaH pR^iThivIM sUrya Apo'ti pashyati /
sUryo bhUtasyaikaM chakShur A ruroha divaM mahIm //45//
urvIr Asan paridhayo vedir bhUmir akalpata /
tatraitAv agnI Adhatta himaM ghraMsaM cha rohitaH //46//
himaM ghraMsaM chAdhAya yUpAn kR^itvA parvatAn /
varShAjyAv agnI IjAte rohitasya svarvidaH //47//
svarvido rohitasya brahmaNAgniH sam idhyate /
tasmAd ghraMsas tasmAd dhimas tasmAd yaj~no 'jAyata //48//
brahmaNAgnI vAvR^idhAnau brahmavR^iddhau brahmAhutau /
brahmeddhAv agnI IjAte rohitasya svarvidaH //49//
satye anyaH samAhito 'psv a1nyaH sam idhyate /
brahmeddhAv agnI IjAte rohitasya svarvidaH //50// {5}

yaM vAtaH parishumbhati yaM vendro brahmaNaspatiH /
brahmeddhAvagnI IjAte rohitasya svarvidaH //51//
vediM bhUmiM kalpayitvA divaM kR^itvA dakShiNAm /
ghraMsaM tad agniM kR^itvA chakAra vishvam Atmanvad varSheNAjyena rohitaH //52//
varSham Ajam ghraMso agnir vedir bhUmir akalpata /
tatraitAn parvatAn agnir gIrbhir UrdhvA.N akalpayat //53//
gIrbhir UrdhvAn kalpayitvA rohito bhUmim abravIt /
tvayIdaM sarvaM jAyatAM yad bhUtaM yach cha bhAvyam //54//
sa yaj~naH prathamo bhUto bhavyo ajAyata /
tasmAd dha jaj~na idaM sarvaM yat kiM chedaM virochate rohitena R^iShiNAbhR^itam //55//
yash cha gAM padA sphurati pratya~N sUryaM cha mehati /
tasya vR^ishchAmi te mUlaM na chhAyAM karavo 'param //56//
yo mAbhichhAyam atyeShi mAM chAgniM chAntarA /
tasya vR^ishchAmi te mUlaM na chhAyAM karavo 'param //57//
yo adya deva sUrya tvAM cha mAM chAntarAyati /
duShvapnyaM tasmiM chhamalaM duritAni cha mR^ijmahe //58//
mA pra gAma patho vayaM mA yaj~nAd indra sominaH /
mAnta sthur no arAtayaH //59//
yo yaj~nasya prasAdhanas tantur deveShv AtataH /
tam Ahutam ashImahi //60// {6}
