ud asya ketavo divi shukrA bhrAjanta Irate /
Adityasya nR^ichakShaso mahivratasya mIDhuShaH //1//
dishAM praj~nAnAM svarayantam archiShA supakSham AshuM patayantam arNave /
stavAma sUryaM bhuvanasya gopAM yo rashmibhir disha AbhAti sarvAH //2//
yat prA~N pratya~N svadhayA yAsi shIbhaM nAnArUpe ahanI karShi mAyayA /
tad Aditya mahi tat te mahi shravo yad eko vishvaM pari bhUma jAyase //3//
vipashchitaM taraNiM bhrAjamAnaM vahanti yaM haritaH sapta bahvIH /
srutAd yam attrir divam unninAya taM tvA pashyanti pariyAntam Ajim //4//
mA tvA dabhan pariyAntam AjiM svasti durgA.N ati yAhi shIbham /
divaM cha sUrya pR^ithivIM cha devIm ahorAtre vimimAno yad eShi //5//
svasti te sUrya charase rathAya yenobhAv antau pariyAsi sadyaH /
yaM te vahanti harito vahiShThAH shatam ashvA yadi vA sapta bahvIH //6//
sukhaM sUrya ratham aMshumantaM syonaM suvahnim adhi tiShTha vAjinam /
yaM te vahanti harito vahiShThAH shatam ashvA yadi vA sapta bahvIH //7//
sapta sUryo harito yAtave rathe hiraNyatvachaso bR^ihatIr ayukta /
amochi shukro rajasaH parastAd vidhUya devas tamo divam Aruhat //8//
ut ketunA bR^ihatA deva Agann apAvR^ik tamo 'bhi jyotir ashrait /
divyaH suparNaH sa vIro vy akhyad aditeH putro bhuvanAni vishvA //9//
udyan rashmIn A tanuShe vishvA rupANi puShyasi /
ubhA samudrau kratunA vi bhAsi sarvAMl lokAn paribhUr bhrAjamAnaH //10// {7}

pUrvAparaM charato mAyayaitau shishU krIDantau pari yAto 'rNavam /
vishvAnyo bhuvanA vichaShTe hairaNyair anyaM harito vahanti //11//
divi tvAttrir adhArayat sUryA mAsAya kartave /
sa eShi sudhR^itas tapan vishvA bhUtAvachAkashat //12//
ubhAv antau sam arShasi vatsaH saMmAtarAv iva /
nanv e3tad itaH purA brahma devA amI viduH //13//
yat samudram anu shritaM tat siShAsati sUryaH /
adhvAsya vitato mahAn pUrvash chAparash cha yaH //14//
taM sam Apnoti jUtibhis tato nApa chikitsati /
tenAmR^itasya bhakShaM devAnAM nAva rundhate //15//
ud u tyaM jAtavedasaM devaM vahanti ketavaH /
dR^ishe vishvAya sUryam //16//
apa tye tAyavo yathA nakShatrA yanty aktubhiH /
sUrAya vishvachakShase //17//
adR^ishrann asya ketavo vi rashmayo janAm? anu /
bhrAjanto agnayo yathA //18//
taraNir vishvadarshato jyotiShkR^id asi sUrya /
vishvam A bhAsi rochana //19//
pratya~N devAnAM vishaH pratya~N~N ud eShi mAnuShIH /
pratya~N vishvaM svar dR^ishe //20// {8}

yenA pAvaka chakShasA bhuraNyantaM janAm? anu /
tvaM varuNa pashyasi //21//
vi dyAm eShi rajas pR^ithv ahar mimAno aktubhiH /
pashyan janmAni sUrya //22//
sapta tvA harito rathe vahanti deva sUrya /
shochiShkeshaM vichakShaNam //23//
ayukta sapta shundhyuvaH sUro rathasya naptyaH /
tAbhir yAti svayuktibhiH //24//
rohito divam Aruhat tapasA tapasvI /
sa yonim aiti sa u jAyate punaH sa devAnAm adhipatir babhUva //25//
yo vishvacharShaNir uta vishvatomukho yo vishvataspANir uta vishvataspR^ithaH /
saM bAhubhyAM bharati saM patatrair dyAvApR^ithivI janayan deva ekaH //26//
ekapAd dvipado bhUyo vi chakrame dvipAt tripAdam abhy eti pashchAt /
dvipAd dha ShaTpado bhUyo vi chakrame ta ekapadas tanva1M sam Asate //27//
atandro yAsyan harito yad AsthAd dve rUpe kR^iNute rochamAnaH /
ketumAn udyant sahamAno rajAMsi vishvA Aditya pravato vi bhAsi //28//
baN mahA.N asi sUrya baD Aditya mahA.N asi /
mahAMs te mahato mahimA tvam Aditya mahA.N asi //29//
rochase divi rochase antarikShe pata~Nga pR^ithivyAM rochase rochase apsv a1ntaH /
ubhA samudrau ruchyA vy Apitha devo devAsi mahiShaH svarjit //30// {9}

arvA~N parastAt prayato vyadhva Ashur vipashchit patayan pata~NgaH /
viShNur vichittaH shavasAdhitiShThan pra ketunA sahate vishvam ejat //31//
chitrAsh chikitvAn mahiShaH suparNa Arochayan rodasI antarikSham /
ahorAtre pari sUryaM vasAne prAsya vishvA tirato vIryANi //32//
tigmo vibhrAjan tanva1M shishAno 'raMgamAsaH pravato rarANaH /
jyotiShmAn pakShI mahiSho vayodhA vishvA AsthAt pradishaH kalpamAnaH //33//
chitraM devAnAm ketur anIkaM jyotiShmAn pradishaH sUrya udyan /
divAkaro 'ti dyumnais tamAMsi vishvAtArId duritAni shukraH //34//
chitraM devAnAm ud agAd anIkaM chakShur mitrasya varuNasyAgneH /
AprAd dyAvApR^ithivI antarikShaM sUrya AtmA jagatas tasthuShash cha //35//
uchchA patantam aruNaM suparNaM madhye divas taraNiM bhrAjamAnam /
pashyama tvA savitAraM yam Ahur ajasraM jyotir yad avindad attriH //36//
divas pR^iShThe dhAvamAnaM suparNam adityAH putraM nAthakAma upa yAmi bhItaH /
sa naH sUrya pra tira dIrgham Ayur mA riShAma sumatau te syAma //37//
sahasrAhNyaM viyatAv asya pakShau harer haMsasya patataH svargam /
sa devAnt sarvAn urasy upadadya saMpashyan yAti bhuvanAni vishvA //38//
rohitaH kAlo abhavad rohito 'gre prajApatiH /
rohito yaj~nAnAM mukhaM rohitaH sva1r Abharat //39//
rohito loko abhavad rohito 'ty atapad divam /
rohito rashmibhir bhUmiM samudram anu saM charat //40// {10}

sarvA dishaH sam acharad rohito 'dhipatir divaH /
divaM samudram Ad bhUmiM sarvaM bhUtaM vi rakShati //41//
Arohan chhukro bR^ihatIr atandro dve rUpe kR^iNute rochamAnaH /
chitrash chikitvAn mahiSho vAtamAyA yAvato lokAn abhi yad vibhAti //42//
abhy a1nyad eti pary anyad asyate 'horAtrAbhyAM mahiShaH kalpamAnaH /
sUryaM vayaM rajasi kShiyantaM gAtuvidaM havAmahe nAdhamAnAH //43//
pR^ithivIpro mahiSho nAdhamAnasya gAtur adabdhachakShuH pari vishvaM babhUva /
vishvaM saMpashyant suvidatro yajatra idaM shR^iNotu yad ahaM bravImi //44//
pary asya mahimA pR^ithivIM samudraM jyotiShA vibhrAjan pari dyAm antarikSham /
sarvaM saMpashyant suvidatro yajatra idaM shR^iNotu yad ahaM bravImi //45//
abodhy agniH samidhA janAnAM prati dhenum ivAyatIm uShasam /
yahvA iva pra vayAm ujjihAnAH pra bhAnavaH sisrate nAkam achha //46// {11}

481

ya ime dyAvApR^ithivI jajAna yo drApim kR^itvA bhuvanAni vaste /
yasmin kShiyanti pradishaH ShaD urvIr yAH pata~Ngo anu vichAkashIti /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //1//
yasmAd vAtA R^ituthA pavante yasmAt samudrA adhi vikSharanti /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //2//
yo mArayati prANayati yasmAt prANanti bhuvanAni vishvA /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //3//
yaH prANena dyAvApR^ithivI tarpayaty apAnena samudrasya jaTharaM yaH piparti /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //4//
yasmin virAT parameShThI prajApatir agnir vaishvAnaraH saha pa~NktyA shritaH /
yaH parasya prANaM paramasya teja Adade /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //5//
yasmin ShaD urvIH pa~ncha disho adhishritAsh chatasra Apo yaj~nasya trayo 'kSharAH /
yo antarA rodasI kruddhash chakShuShaikShata /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNam jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //6//
yo annAdo annapatir babhUva brahmaNas patir uta yaH /
bhUto bhaviShyat bhuvanasya yas patiH /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //7//
ahorAtrair vimitaM triMshada~NgaM trayodashaM mAsaM yo nirmimIte /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //8//
kR^isNaM niyAnaM harayaH suparNA apo vasAnA divam ut patanti /
ta AvavR^itrant sadanAd R^itasya /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //9//
yat te chandraM kashyapa rochanAvad yat saMhitaM puShkalaM chitrabhAnu yasmint sUryA ArpitAH sapta sAkam /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //10// {12}

bR^ihad enam anu vaste purastAd rathaMtaraM prati gR^ihNAti pashchAt /
jyotir vasAne sadam apramAdam /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //11//
bR^ihad anyataH pakSha AsId rathaMtaram anyataH sabale sadhrIchI /
yad rohitam ajanayanta devAH /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //12//
sa varuNaH sAyam agnir bhavati sa mitro bhavati prAtar udyan /
sa savitA bhUtvAntarikSheNa yAti sa indro bhUtvA tapati madhyato divam /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //13//
sahasrAhNyaM viyatAv asya pakShau harer haMsasya patataH svargam /
sa devAnt sarvAn urasy upadadya saMpashyan yAti bhuvanAni vishvA /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //14//
ayaM sa devo apsv a1ntaH sahasramUlaH parushAko attriH /
ya idaM vishvaM bhuvanaM jajAna /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //15//
shukraM vahanti harayo raghuShyado devaM divi varchasA bhrAjamAnam /
yasyordhvA divaM tanva1s tapanty arvA~N suvarNaiH paTarair vi bhAti /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //16//
yenAdityAn haritaH samvahanti yena yaj~nena bahavo yanti prajAnantaH /
yad ekaM jyotir bahudhA vibhAti /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //17//
sapta yu~njanti ratham ekachakram eko ashvo vahati saptanAmA /
trinAbhi chakram ajaram anarvaM yatremA vishvA bhuvanAdhi tasthuH /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //18//
aShTadhA yukto vahati vahnir ugraH pitA devAnAM janitA matInAm /
R^itasya tantuM manasA mimAnaH sarvA dishaH pavate mAtarishvA /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //19//
saMya~nchaM tantuM pradisho 'nu sarvA antar gAyatryAm amR^itasya garbhe /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //20// {13}

nimruchas tisro vyuSho ha tisras trINi rajAMsi divo a~Nga tisrah /
vidmA te agne tredhA janitraM tredhA devAnAM janimAni vidma /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //21//
vi ya aurNot pR^ithivIM jAyamAna A samudram adadhAt antarikShe /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //22//
tvam agne kratubhiH ketubhir hito3 'rkaH samiddha ud arochathA divi /
kim abhy Archan marutaH pR^ishnimAtaro yad rohitam ajanayanta devAH /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //23//
ya AtmadA baladA yasya vishva upAsate prashiShaM yasya devAH /
yo3 'syeshe dvipado yash chatuShpadaH /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //24//
ekapAd dvipado bhUyo vi chakrame dvipAt tripAdam abhy eti pashchAt /
chatuShpAch chakre dvipadAm abhisvare saMpashyan pa~Nktim upatiShThamAnaH /
tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad Ago ya evaM vidvAMsaM brAhmaNaM jinAti /
ud vepaya rohita pra kShiNIhi brahmajyasya prati mu~ncha pAshAn //25//
kR^iShNAyaH putro arjuno rAtryA vatso 'jAyata /
sa ha dyAm adhi rohati ruho ruroha rohitaH //26// {14}

482
 {1}
sa eti savitA svar divas pR^iShThe 'vachAkashat //1//
rashmibhir nabha AbhR^itaM mahendra ety AvR^itaH //2//
sa dhAtA sa vidhartA sa vAyur nabha uchchhritam //3//
so 'ryamA sa varuNaH sa rudraH sa mahAdevaH //4//
so agniH sa u sUryaH sa u eva mahAyamaH //5//
taM vatsA upa tiShThanty ekashIrShANo yutA dasha //6//
pashchAt prA~ncha A tanvanti yad udeti vi bhAsati //7//
tasyaiSha mAruto gaNaH sa eti shikyAkR^itaH //8//
rashmibhir nabha AbhR^itaM mahendra ety AvR^itaH //9//
tasyeme nava koshA viShTambhA navadhA hitAH //10//
sa prajAbhyo vi pashyati yach cha prANati yach cha na //11//
tam idaM nigataM sahaH sa eSha eka ekavR^id eka eva //12//
ete asmin devA ekavR^ito bhavanti //13// {15}

 {2}
kIrtish cha yashash chAmbhash cha nabhash cha brAhmaNavarchasaM chAnnaM chAnnAdyaM cha //14//
ya etaM devam ekavR^itaM veda //15//
na dvitIyo na tR^itIyash chaturtho nApy uchyate //16//
na pa~nchamo na ShaShThaH saptamo nApy uchyate //17//
nAShTamo na navamo dashamo nApy uchyate //18//
sa sarvasmai vi pashyati yach cha prANati yach cha na //19//
tam idaM nigataM sahaH sa eSha eka ekavR^id eka eva //20//
sarve asmin devA ekavR^ito bhavanti //21// {16}

 {3}
brahma cha tapash cha kIrtish cha yashash chAmbhash cha nabhash cha brAhmaNavarchasaM chAnnaM chAnnAdyaM cha //2//
bhUtaM cha bhavyaM cha shraddhA cha ruchish cha svargash cha svadhA cha //23//
ya etaM devam ekavR^itaM veda //24//
sa eva mR^ityuH so3 'mR^itaM so3 'bhva1M sa rakShaH //25//
sa rudro vasuvanir vasudeye namovAke vaShaTkAro 'nu saMhitaH //26//
tasyeme sarve yAtava upa prashiSham Asate //27//
tasyAmU sarvA nakShatrA vashe chandramasA saha //28// {17}

 {4}
sa vA ahno 'jAyata tasmAd ahar ajAyata //29//
sa vai rAtryA ajAyata tasmAd rAtrir ajAyata //30//
sa vA antarikShAd ajAyata tasmAd antarikSham ajAyata //31//
sa vai vAyor ajAyata tasmAd vAyur ajAyata //32//
sa vai divo 'jAyata tasmAd dyaur adhi ajAyata //33//
sa vai digbhyo 'jAyata tasmAd disho 'jAyanta //34//
sa vai bhUmer ajAyata tasmAd bhUmir ajAyata //35//
sa vA agner ajAyata tasmAd agnir ajAyata //36//
sa vA adbhyo 'jAyata tasmAd Apo 'jAyanta //37//
sa vA R^igbhyo 'jAyata tasmAd R^icho 'jAyanta //38//
sa vai yaj~nAd ajAyata tasmAd yaj~no 'jAyata //39//
sa yaj~nas tasya yaj~naH sa yaj~nasya shiras kR^itam //40//
sa stanayati sa vi dyotate sa u ashmAnam asyati //41//
pApAya vA bhadrAya vA puruShAyAsurAya vA //42//
yad vA kR^iNoShy oShadhIr yad vA varShasi bhadrayA yad vA janyam avIvR^idhaH //43//
tAvAMs te maghavan mahimopo te tanvaH shatam //44//
upo te badhve baddhAni yadi vAsi nyarbudam //45// {18}

 {5}
bhUyAn indro namurAd bhUyAn indrAsi mR^ityubhyaH //46//
bhUyAn arAtyAH shachyAH patis tvam indrAsi vibhUH prabhUr iti tvopAsmahe vayam //47//
namas te astu pashyata pashya mA pashyata //48//
annAdyena yashasA tejasA brAhmaNavarchasena //49//
ambho amo mahaH saha iti tvopAsmahe vayam //50//
ambho aruNaM rajataM rajaH saha iti tvopAsmahe vayam //51// {19}

 {6}
uruH pR^ithuH subhUr bhuva iti tvopAsmahe vayam //52//
pratho varo vyacho loka iti tvopAsmahe vayam //53//
bhavadvasur idadvasuH saMyadvasur Ayadvasur iti tvopAsmahe vayam //54//
namas te astu pashyata pashya mA pashyata //55//
annAdyena yashasA tejasA brAhmaNavarchasena //56// {20}

483
Book:
14

satyenottabhitA bhUmiH sUryeNottabhitA dyauH /
R^itenAdityAs tiShThanti divi somo adhi shritaH //1//
somenAdityA balinaH somena pR^ithivI mahI /
atho nakShatrANAm eShAm upasthe soma AhitaH //2//
somaM manyate papivAn yat saMpiMShanty oShadhim /
somaM yaM brahmANo vidur na tasyAshnAti pArthivaH //3//
yat tvA soma prapibanti tata A pyAyase punaH /
vAyuH somasya rakShitA samAnAM mAsa AkR^itiH //4//
AchhadvidhAnair gupito bArhataiH soma rakShitaH /
grAvNAm ich chhR^iNvan tiShThasi na te ashnAti pArthivaH //5//
chittir A upabarhaNaM chakShur A abhya~njanam /
dyaur bhUmiH kosha AsId yad ayAt sUryA patim //6//
raibhy AsId anudeyI nArAshaMsI nyochanI /
suryAyA bhadram id vAso gAthayati pariShkR^itA //7//
stomA Asan pratidhayaH kurIraM chhanda opashaH /
sUryAyA ashvinA varAgnir AsIt purogavaH //8//
somo vadhUyur abhavad ashvinAstAm ubhA varA /
sUryAM yat patye shaMsantIM manasA savitAdadAt //9//
mano asyA ana AsId dyaur AsId uta chhadiH /
shukrAv anaDvAhAv AstAM yad ayAt sUryA patim //10// {1}

R^iksAmAbhyAm abhihitau gAvau te sAmanAv aitAm /
shrotre te chakre AstAM divi panthAsh charAcharaH //11//
shuchI te chakre yAtyA vyAno akSha AhataH /
ano manasmayaM sUryArohat prayatI patim //12//
sUryAyA vahatuH prAgAt savitA yam avAsR^ijat /
maghAsu hanyante gAvaH phalgunIShu vy ùhyate //13//
yad ashvinA pR^ichhamAnAv ayAtaM trichakreNa vahatuM sUryAyAH /
kvaikaM chakraM vAm AsIt kva deShTrAya tasthathuH //14//
yad ayAtaM shubhas patI vareyaM sUryAm upa /
vishve devA anu tad vAm ajAnan putraH pitaram avR^iNIta pUShA //15//
dve te chakre sUrye brahmANa R^ituthA viduH /
athaikaM chakraM yad guhA tad addhAtaya id viduH //16//
aryamaNaM yajAmahe subandhuM pativedanam /
urvArukam iva bandhanAt preto mu~nchAmi nAmutaH //17//
preto mu~nchAmi nAmutaH subaddhAm amutas karam /
yatheyam indra mIDhvaH suputrA subhagAsati //18//
pra tvA mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAd yena tvAbadhnAt savitA sushevAH /
R^itasya yonau sukR^itasya loke syonaM te astu sahasaMbhalAyai //19//
bhagas tveto nayatu hastagR^ihyAshvinA tvA pra vahatAM rathena /
gR^ihAn gachha gR^ihapatnI yathAso vashinI tvaM vidatham A vadAsi //20// {2}

iha priyaM prajAyai te sam R^idhyatAm asmin gR^ihe gArhapatyAya jAgR^ihi /
enA patyA tanva1M saM spR^ishasvAtha jirvir vidatham A vadAsi //21//
ihaiva staM mA vi yauShTaM vishvam Ayur vy ashnutam /
krIDantau putrair naptR^ibhir modamAnau svastakau //22//
pUrvAparaM charato mAyaitau shishU krIDantau pari yAto 'rNavam /
vishvAnyo bhuvanA vichaShTa R^itUMr anyo vidadhaj jAyase navaH //23//
navonavo bhavasi jAyamAno 'hnAM ketur uShasAm eShy agram /
bhAgaM devebhyo vi dadhAsy Ayan pra chandramas tirase dIrgham AyuH //24//
parA dehi shAmulyaM brahmabhyo vi bhajA vasu /
kR^ityaiShA padvatI bhUtvA jAyA vishate patim //25//
nIlalohitaM bhavati kR^ityAsaktir vy ajyate /
edhante asyA j~nAtayaH patir bandheShu badhyate //26//
ashlIlA tanUr bhavati rushatI pApayAmuyA /
patir yad vadhvo3 vAsasaH svam a~Ngam abhyUrNute //27//
AshasanaM vishasanam atho adhivikartanam /
sUryAyAH pashya rUpANi tAni brahmota shumbhati //28//
tR^iShTam etat kaTukam apAShThavad viShavan naitad attave /
sUryAM yo brahmA veda sa id vAdhUyam arhati //29//
sa it tat syonaM harati brahmA vAsaH suma~Ngalam /
prAyashchittiM yo adhyeti yena jAyA na riShyati //30// {3}

yuvaM bhagaM saM bharataM samR^iddham R^itaM vadantAv R^itodyeShu /
brahmaNas pate patim asyai rochaya chAru saMbhalo vadatu vAcham etAm //31//
ihed asAtha na paro gamAthemaM gAvaH prajayA vardhayAtha /
shubhaM yatIr usriyAH somavarchaso vishve devAH krann iha vo manAMsi //32//
imaM gAvaH prajayA saM vishAthAyaM devAnAM na minAti bhAgam /
asmai vaH pUShA marutash cha sarve asmai vo dhAtA savitA suvAti //33//
anR^ikSharA R^ijavaH santu panthano yebhiH sakhAyo yanti no vareyam /
saM bhagena sam aryamNA saM dhAtA sR^ijatu varchasA //34//
yach cha varcho akSheShu surAyAM cha yad Ahitam /
yad goShv ashvinA varchas tenemAM varchasAvatam //35//
yena mahAnaghnyA jaghanam ashvinA yena vA surA /
yenAkShA abhyaShichyanta tenemAM varchasAvatam //36//
yo anidhmo dIdayad apsv a1ntar yaM viprAsa IDate adhvareShu /
apAM napAn madhumatIr apo dA yAbhir indro vAvR^idhe vIryAvAn //37//
idam ahaM rushantaM grAbhaM tanUdUShim apohAmi /
yo bhadro rochanas tam ud achAmi //38//
Asyai brAhmaNAH snapanIr harantv avIraghnIr ud ajantv ApaH /
aryamNo agniM pary etu pUShan pratIkShante shvashuro devarash cha //39//
shaM te hiraNyaM sham u santv ApaH shaM methir bhavatu shaM yugasya tardma /
shaM ta ApaH shatapavitrA bhavantu sham u patyA tanva1M saM spR^ishasva //40// {4}

khe rathasya khe 'nasaH khe yugasya shatakrato /
apAlAm indra triSh pUtvAkR^iNoH sUryatvacham //41//
AshAsAnA saumanasaM prajAM saubhAgyaM rayim /
patyur anuvratA bhUtvA saM nahyasvAmR^itAya kam //42//
yathA sindhur nadInAM sAmrAjyaM suShuve vR^iShA /
evA tvaM samrAj~ny edhi patyur astaM paretya //43//
samrAj~ny edhi shvashureShu samrAj~ny uta devR^iShu /
nanAnduH samrAj~ny edhi samrAj~ny uta shvashrvAH //44//
yA akR^intann avayan yAsh cha tatnire yA devIr antAm? abhito 'dadanta /
tAs tvA jarase saM vyayantv AyuShmatIdaM pari dhatsva vAsaH //45//
jIvaM rudanti vi nayanty adhvaraM dIrghAm anu prasitiM dIdhyur naraH /
vAmaM pitR^ibhyo ya idaM samIrire mayaH patibhyo janaye pariShvaje //46//
syonaM dhruvaM prajAyai dhArayAmi te 'shmAnaM devyAH pR^ithivyA upasthe /
tam A tiShThAnumAdyA suvarchA dIrghaM ta AyuH savitA kR^iNotu //47//
yenAgnir asyA bhUmyA hastaM jagrAha dakShiNam /
tena gR^ihNAmi te hastaM mA vyathiShThA mayA saha prajayA cha dhanena cha //48//
devas te savitA hastaM gR^ihNAtu somo rAjA suprajasaM kR^iNotu /
agniH subhagAM jatavedAH patye patnIM jaradaShTim kR^iNotu //49//
gR^ihNAmi te saubhagatvAya hastaM mayA patyA jaradaShTir yathAsaH /
bhago aryamA savitA puraMdhir mahyaM tvAdur gArhapatyAya devAH //50// {5}

bhagas te hastam agrahIt savitA hastam agrahIt /
patnI tvam asi dharmaNAhaM gR^ihapatis tava //51//
mameyam astu poShyA mahyaM tvAdAd bR^ihaspatiH /
mayA patyA prajAvati saM jIva sharadaH shatam //52//
tvaShTA vAso vy adadhAch chhubhe kaM bR^ihaspateH prashiShA kavInAm /
tenemAM nArIM savitA bhagash cha sUryAm iva pari dhattAM prajayA //53//
indrAgnI dyAvApR^ithivI mAtarishvA mitrAvaruNA bhago ashvinobhA /
bR^ihaspatir maruto brahma soma imAM nAriM prajayA vardhayantu //54//
bR^ihaspatiH prathamaH sUryAyAH shIrShe keshAm? akalpayat /
tenemAm ashvinA nArIM patye saM shobhayAmasi //55//
idaM tad rUpaM yad avasta yoShA jAyAM jij~nAse manasA charantIm /
tAm anv artiShye sakhibhir navagvaiH ka imAn vidvAn vi chacharta pAshAn //56//
ahaM vi ShyAmi mayi rUpam asyA vedad it pashyan manasaH kulAyam /
na steyam admi manasod amuchye svayaM shrathnAno varuNasya pAshAn //57//
pra tvA mu~nchAmi varuNasya pAshAd yena tvAbadhnAt savitA sushevAH /
uruM lokaM sugam atra panthAM kR^iNomi tubhyaM sahapatnyai vadhu //58//
ud yachhadhvam apa rakSho hanAthemaM nArIM sukR^ite dadhAta /
dhAtA vipashchit patim asyai viveda bhago rAjA pura etu prajAnan //59//
bhagas tatakSha chaturaH pAdAn bhagas tatakSha chatvAry uShpalAni /
tvaShTA pipesha madhyato 'nu vardhrAnt sA no astu suma~NgalI //60//
sukiMshukaM vahatuM vishvarUpaM hiraNyavarNaM suvR^itaM suchakram /
A roha sUrye amR^itasya lokaM syonaM patibhyo vahatuM kR^iNu tvam //61//
abhrAtR^ighnIM varuNApashughnIM bR^ihaspate /
indrApatighnIm putriNIm AsmabhyaM savitar vaha //62//
mA hiMsiShTaM kumArya1M sthUNe devakR^ite pathi /
shAlAyA devyA dvAraM syonaM kR^iNmo vadhUpatham //63//
brahmAparaM yujyatAM brahma pUrvaM brahmAntato madhyato brahma sarvataH /
anAvyAdhAM devapurAM prapadya shivA syonA patiloke vi rAja //64// {6}

484

tubhyam agre pary avahant sUryAM vahatunA saha /
sa naH patibhyo jAyAM dA agne prajayA saha //1//
punaH patnIm agnir adAd AyuShA saha varchasA /
dIrghAyur asyA yaH patir jIvAti sharadaH shatam //2//
somasya jAyA prathamaM gandharvas te 'paraH patiH /
tR^itIyo agniSh Te patis turIyas te manuShyajAH //3//
somo dadad gandharvAya gandharvo dadad agnaye /
rayiM cha putrAMs chAdAd agnir mahyam atho imAm //4//
A vAm agant sumatir vAjinIvasU ny ashvinA hR^itsu kAmA araMsata /
abhUtaM gopA mithunA shubhas patI priyA aryamNo duryAm? ashImahi //5//
sA mandasAnA manasA shivena rayiM dhehi sarvavIraM vachasyam /
sugaM tIrthaM suprapANaM shubhas patI sthANuM pathiShThAm apa durmatiM hatam //6//
yA oShadhayo yA nadyo3 yAni kShetrANi yA vanA /
tAs tvA vadhu prajAvatIM patye rakShantu rakShasaH //7//
emaM panthAm arukShAma sugaM svastivAhanam /
yasmin vIro na riShyaty anyeShAM vindate vasu //8//
idaM su me naraH shR^iNuta yayAshiShA daMpatI vAmam ashnutaH /
ye gandharvA apsarasash cha devIr eShu vAnaspatyeShu ye 'dhi tasthuH /
syonAs te asyai vadhvaì bhavantu mA hiMsiShur vahatum uhyamAnam //9//
ye vadhvash chandraM vahatuM yakShmA yanti janAm? anu /
punas tAn yaj~niyA devA nayantu yata AgatAH //10// {7}

mA vidan paripanthino ya AsIdanti daMpatI /
sugena durgam atItAm apa drAntv arAtayaH //11//
saM kAshayAmi vahatuM brahmaNA gR^ihair aghoreNa chakShuShA mitriyeNa /
paryANaddhaM vishvarUpaM yad asti syonaM patibhyaH savitA tat kR^iNotu //12//
shivA nArIyam astam Agann imaM dhAtA lokam asyai didesha /
tAm aryamA bhago ashvinobhA prajApatiH prajayA vardhayantu //13//
Atmanvaty urvarA nArIyam Agan tasyAM naro vapata bIjam asyAm /
sA vaH prajAM janayad vakShaNAbhyo bibhratI dugdham R^iShabhasya retaH //14//
prati tiShTha virAD asi viShNur iveha sarasvati /
sinIvAli pra jAyatAM bhagasya sumatAv asat //15//
ud va UrmiH shamyA hantv Apo yoktrANi mu~nchata /
mAduShkR^itau vyenasAv aghnyAv ashunam AratAm //16//
aghorachakShur apatighnI syonA shagmA sushevA suyamA gR^ihebhyaH /
vIrasUr devR^ikAmA saM tvayaidhiShImahi sumasyamAnA //17//
adevR^ighny apatighnIhaidhi shivA pashubhyaH suyamA suvarchAh /
prajAvatI vIrasUr devR^ikAmA syonemam agniM gArhapatyaM saparya //18//
ut tiShThetaH kim ichhantIdam AgA ahaM tveDe abhibhUH svAd gR^ihAt /
shUnyaiShI nirR^ite yAjaganthot tiShThArAte pra pata meha raMsthAH //19//
yadA gArhapatyam asaparyait pUrvam agniM vadhUr iyam /
adhA sarasvatyai nAri pitR^ibhyash cha namas kuru //20// {8}

sharma varmaitad A harAsyai nAryA upastire /
sinIvAli pra jAyatAM bhagasya sumatAv asat //21//
yaM balbajaM nyasyatha charma chopastR^iNIthana /
tad A rohatu suprajA yA kanyA vindate patim //22//
upa stR^iNIhi balbajam adhi charmaNi rohite /
tatropavishya suprajA imam agniM saparyatu //23//
A roha charmopa sIdAgnim eSha devo hanti rakShAMsi sarvA /
iha prajAM janaya patye asmai sujyaiShThyo bhavat putras ta eShaH //24//
vi tiShThantAM mAtur asyA upasthAn nAnArUpAH pashavo jAyamAnAH /
suma~Ngaly upa sIdemam agniM saMpatnI prati bhUSheha devAn //25//
suma~NgalI prataraNI gR^ihANAM sushevA patye shvashurAya shaMbhUH /
syonA shvashrvai pra gR^ihAn vishemAn //26//
syonA bhava shvashurebhyaH syonA patye gR^ihebhyaH /
syonAsyai sarvasyai vishe syonA puShTAyaiShAM bhava //27//
suma~NgalIr iyaM vadhUr imAM sameta pashyata /
saubhAgyam asyai dattvA daurbhAgyair viparetana //28//
yA durhArdo yuvatayo yAsh cheha jaratIr api /
varcho nv asyai saM dattAthAstaM viparetana //29//
rukmaprastaraNaM vahyaM vishvA rUpANi bibhratam /
Arohat sUryA sAvitrI bR^ihate saubhagAya kam //30// {9}

A roha talpaM sumanasyamAneha prajAM janaya patye asmai /
indrANIva subudhA budhyamAnA jyotiragrA uShasaH prati jAgarAsi //31//
devA agre ny apadyanta patnIH sam aspR^ishanta tanvas tanUbhiH /
sUryeva nAri vishvarUpA mahitvA prajAvatI patyA saM bhaveha //32//
ut tiShTheto vishvAvaso namaseDAmahe tvA /
jAmim ichha pitR^iShadaM nyaktAM sa te bhAgo januShA tasya viddhi //33//
apsarasaH sadhamAdaM madanti havirdhAnam antarA sUryaM cha /
tAs te janitram abhi tAH parehi namas te gandharvartunA kR^iNomi //34//
namo gandharvasya namase namo bhAmAya chakShuShe cha kR^iNmaH /
vishvAvaso brahmaNA te namo 'bhi jAyA apsarasaH parehi //35//
rAyA vayaM sumanasaH syAmod ito gandharvam AvIvR^itAma /
agant sa devaH paramaM sadhastham aganma yatra pratiranta AyuH //36//
saM pitarAv R^itviye sR^ijethAM mAtA pitA cha retaso bhavAthaH /
marya iva yoShAm adhi rohayainAM prajAM kR^iNvAthAm iha puShyataM rayim //37//
tAM pUShaM chhivatamAm erayasva yasyAM bIjaM manuShyA3 vapanti /
yA na UrU ushatI vishrayAti yasyAm ushantaH praharema shepaH //38//
A rohorum upa dhatsva hastaM pari Shvajasva jAyAM sumanasyamAnaH /
prajAM kR^iNvAthAm iha modamAnau dIrghaM vAm AyuH savitA kR^iNotu //39//
A vAM prajAM janayatu prajApatir ahorAtrAbhyAM sam anaktv aryamA /
adurma~NgalI patilokam A vishemaM shaM no bhava dvipade shaM chatuShpade //40// {10}

devair dattaM manunA sAkam etad vAdhUyaM vAso vadhvash cha vastram /
yo brahmaNe chikituShe dadAti sa id rakShAMsi talpAni hanti //41//
yaM me datto brahmabhAgaM vadhUyor vAdhUyaM vAso vadhvash cha vastram /
yuvaM brahmaNe 'numanyamAnau bR^ihaspate sAkam indrash cha dattam //42//
syonAd yoner adhi badhyamAnau hasAmudau mahasA modamAnau /
sugU suputrau sugR^ihau tarAtho jIvAv uShaso vibhAtIH //43//
navaM vasAnaH surabhiH suvAsA udAgAM jIva uShaso vibhAtIH /
ANDAt patatrIvAmukShi vishvasmAd enasas pari //44//
shumbhanI dyAvApR^ithivI antisumne mahivrate /
ApaH sapta susruvur devIs tA no mu~nchantv aMhasaH //45//
sUryAyai devebhyo mitrAya varuNAya cha /
ye bhUtasya prachetasas tebhya idam akaraM namaH //46//
ya R^ite chid abhishriShaH purA jatrubhya AtR^idaH /
saMdhAtA saMdhiM maghavA purUvasur niShkartA vihrutaM punaH //47//
apAsmat tama uchhatu nIlaM pisha~Ngam uta lohitaM yat /
nirdahanI yA pR^iShAtaky asmin tAM sthANAv adhy A sajAmi //48//
yAvatIH kR^ityA upavAsane yAvanto rAj~no varuNasya pAshAH /
vyr?ddhayo yA asamR^iddhayo yA asmin tA sthANAv adhi sAdayAmi //49//
yA me priyatamA tanUH sA me bibhAya vAsasaH /
tasyAgre tvaM vanaspate nIviM kR^iNuShva mA vayaM riShAma //50// {11}

ye antA yAvatIH sicho ya otavo ye cha tantavaH /
vAso yat patnIbhir utaM tan na syonam upa spR^ishAt //51//
ushatIH kanyalA imAH pitR^ilokAt patiM yatIH /
ava dIkShAm asR^ikShata svAhA //52//
bR^ihaspatinAvasR^iShTAM vishve devA adhArayan /
varcho goShu praviShTaM yat tenemAM saM sR^ijAmasi //53//
bR^ihaspatinAvasR^iShTAM vishve devA adhArayan /
tejo goShu praviShTaM yat tenemAM saM sR^ijAmasi //54//
bR^ihaspatinAvasR^iShTAM vishve devA adhArayan /
bhajo goShu praviShTo yas tenemAM saM sR^ijAmasi //55//
bR^ihaspatinAvasR^iShTAM vishve devA adhArayan /
yasho goShu praviShTaM yat tenemAM saM sR^ijAmasi //56//
bR^ihaspatinAvasR^iShTAM vishve devA adhArayan /
payo goShu praviShTaM yat tenemAM saM sR^ijAmasi //57//
bR^ihaspatinAvasR^iShTAM vishve devA adhArayan /
raso goShu praviShTo yas tenemAM saM sR^ijAmasi //58//
yadIme keshino janA gR^ihe te samanartiShU rodena kR^iNvanto 'gham /
agniSh TvA tasmAd enasaH savitA cha pra mu~nchatAm //59//
yadIyaM duhitA tava vikeshy arudad gR^ihe rodena kR^iNvaty agham /
agniSh TvA tasmAd enasaH savitA cha pra mu~nchatAm //60// {12}

yaj jAmayo yad yuvatayo gR^ihe te samanartiShU rodena kR^iNvatIr agham /
agniSh TvA tasmAd enasaH savitA cha pra mu~nchatAm //61//
yat te prajAyAM pashuShu yad vA gR^iheShu niShThitam aghakR^idbhir aghaM kR^itam /
agniSh TvA tasmAd enasaH savitA cha pra mu~nchatAm //62//
iyaM nAry upa brUte pUlyAny AvapantikA /
dIrghAyur astu me patir jIvAti sharadaH shatam //63//
ihemAv indra saM nuda chakravAkeva daMpatI /
prajayainau svastakau vishvam Ayur vy ashnutAm //64//
yad AsandyAm upadhAne yad vopavAsane kR^itam /
vivAhe kR^ityAM yAM chakrur AsnAne tAM ni dadhmasi //65//
yad duShkR^itaM yach chhamalaM vivAhe vahatau cha yat /
tat saMbhalasya kambale mR^ijmahe duritaM vayam //66//
saMbhale malaM sAdayitvA kambale duritaM vayam /
abhUma yaj~niyAH shuddhAH pra Na AyUMShi tAriShat //67//
kR^itrimaH kaNTakaH shatadan ya eShaH /
apAsyAH keshyaM malam apa shIrShaNyaM likhAt //68//
a~NgAda~NgAd vayam asyA apa yakShmaM ni dadhmasi /
tan mA prApat pR^ithivIM mota devAn divaM mA prApad urv antarikSham /
apo mA prApan malam etad agne yamam mA prApat pitR^IMsh cha sarvAn //69//
saM tvA nahyAmi payasA pR^ithivyAH saM tvA nahyAmi payasauShadhInAm /
saM tvA nahyAmi prajayA dhanena sA saMnaddhA sanuhi vAjam emam //70// {13}

amo 'ham asmi sA tvaM sAmAham asmy R^ik tvaM dyaur ahaM pR^ithivI tvam /
tAv iha saM bhavAva prajAm A janayAvahai //71//
janiyanti nAv agravaH putriyanti sudAnavaH /
ariShTAsU sachevahi bR^ihate vAjasAtaye //72//
ye pitaro vadhUdarshA imaM vahatum Agaman /
te asyai vadhvaì saMpatnyai prajAvach chharma yachhantu //73//
yedaM pUrvAgan rashanAyamAnA prajAm asyai draviNaM cheha dattvA /
tAM vahantv agatasyAnu panthAM virAD iyaM suprajA aty ajaiShIt //74//
pra budhyasva subudhA budhyamAnA dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya /
gR^ihAn gachha gR^ihapatnI yathAso dIrghaM ta AyuH savitA kR^iNotu //75// {14}

485
Book:
15

vrAtya AsId IyamAna eva sa prajApatiM sam airayat //1//
sa prajApatiH suvarNam Atmann apashyat tat prAjanayat //2//
tad ekam abhavat tal lalAmam abhavat tan mahad abhavat taj jyeShTham abhavat tad /
brahmAbhavat tat tapo 'bhavat tat satyam abhavat tena prAjAyata //3//
so 'vardhata sa mahAn abhavat sa mahAdevo 'bhavat //4//
sa devAnAm IshAM pary ait sa IshAno 'bhavat //5//
sa ekavrAtyo bhavat sa dhanur Adatta tad evendradhanuH //6//
nIlam asyodaraM lohitaM pR^iShTham //7//
nIlenaivApriyaM bhrAtR^ivyaM prorNoti lohitena dviShantaM vidhyatIti brahmavAdino vadanti //8//
486 :

sa ud atiShThat sa prAchIM disham anu vy achalat //1//
taM bR^ihach cha rathaMtaraM chAdityAsh cha vishve cha devA anuvyachalan //2//
bR^ihate cha vai sa rathaMtarAya chAdityebhyash cha vishvebhyash cha devebhya A vR^ishchate ya evaM vidvAMsaM vrAtyam upavadati //3//
bR^ihatash cha vai sa rathaMtarasya chAdityAnAM cha vishveShAM cha devAnAM priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //4//
tasya prAchyAM dishi shraddhA puMshchalI mitro mAgadho vij~nAnaM vaso 'har uShNIShaM rAtrI keshA haritau pravartau kalmalir maNiH //5//
bhUtaM cha bhaviShyach cha pariShkandau mano vipatham //6//
mAtarishvA cha pavamAnash cha vipathavAhau vAtaH sArathI reShmA pratodaH kIrtish cha yashash cha puraHsarau //7//
ainaM kIrtir gachhaty A yasho gachhati ya evaM veda //8// 1]

487 :

sa ud atiShThat sa dakShiNAM disham anu vy achalat //9//
taM yaj~nAyaj~niyaM cha vai sa vAmadevyaM cha yaj~nash cha yajamAnash cha pashavash chAnuvyachalan //10//
yaj~nAyaj~niyAya cha vai sa vAmadevyAya cha yaj~nAya cha yajamAnAya cha pashubhyash chA vR^ishchate ya evaM vidvAMsaM vrAtyam upavadati //11//
yaj~nAyaj~niyasya cha vai sa vAmadevyasya cha yaj~nasya cha yajamAnasya cha pashUnAM cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //12//
tasya dakShiNAyAM dishy ùShAH puMshchalI mantro mAgadho vij~nAnaM vAso 'har uShNIShaM rAtrI keshA haritau pravartau kalmalir maNiH //13//
amAvAsyA cha paurNamAsI cha pariShkandau mano vipatham /
mAtarishvA cha pavamAnash cha vipathavAhau vAtaH sArathI reShmA pratodaH kIrtish cha yashash cha puraHsarau /
ainaM kIrtir gachhaty A yasho gachhati ya evaM veda //14// 2]

488 :

sa ud atiShThat sa pratIchIM disham anu vy achalat //15//
taM vairUpaM cha vairAjaM chApash cha varuNash cha rAjAnuvyachalan //16//
vairUpAya cha vai sa vairAjAya chAdbhyash cha varuNAya cha rAj~na A vR^ishchate ya evaM vidvAMsaM vrAtyam upavadati //17//
vairUpasya cha vai sa vairAjasya chApAM cha varuNasya cha rAj~naH priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //18//
tasya pratIchyAM dishIrA puMshchalI haso mAgadho vij~nAnaM vAso 'har uShNIShaM rAtrI keshA haritau pravartau kalmalir maNiH //19//
ahash cha rAtrI cha pariShkandau mano vipatham /
mAtarishvA cha pavamAnash cha vipathavAhau vAtaH sArathI reShmA pratodaH kIrtish cha yashash cha puraHsarau /
ainaM kIrtir gachhaty A yasho gachhati ya evaM veda //20// 3]

489 :

sa ud atiShThat sa udIchIM disham anu vy achalat //21//
taM shyaitam cha naudhasaM cha saptarShayash cha somash cha rAjAnuvyachalan //22//
shyaitAya cha vai sa naudhasAya cha saptarShibhyash cha somAya cha rAj~na A vR^ishchate ya evaM vidvAMsaM vrAtyam upavadati //23//
shyaitasya cha vai sa naudhasasya cha saptarShINAM cha somasya cha rAj~naH priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //24//
tasyodIchyAM dishi vidyut puMshchalI stanayitnur mAgadho vij~nAnaM vAso 'har uShNIShaM rAtrI keshA haritau pravartau kalmalir maNiH //25//
shrutaM cha vishrutaM cha pariShkandau mano vipatham //26//
mAtarishvA cha pavamAnash cha vipathavAhau vAtaH sArathI reShmA pratodaH /
kIrtish cha yashash cha puraHsarau //27//
ainaM kIrtir gachhaty A yasho gachhati ya evaM veda //28// 4]

490

sa saMvatsaram Urdhvo 'tiShThat taM devA abruvan vrAtya kiM nu tiShThasIti //1//
so 'bravId AsandIM me saM bharantv iti //2//
tasmai vrAtyAyAsandIM sam abharan //3//
tasyA grIShmash cha vasantash cha dvau pAdAv AstAM sharach cha varShAsh cha dvau //4//
bR^ihach cha rathaMtaraM chAnUchye3 AstAM yaj~nAyaj~niyaM cha vAmadevyaM cha tirashchye //5//
R^ichaH prA~nchas tantavo yajUMShi tirya~nchaH //6//
veda AstaraNaM brahmopabarhaNam //7//
sAmAsAda udgItho 'pashrayaH //8//
tAm AsandIM vrAtya Arohat //9//
tasya devajanAH pariShkandA Asant saMkalpAH prahAyyA3 vishvAni bhUtAny upasadaH //10//
vishvAny evAsya bhUtAny upasado bhavanti ya evaM veda //11//
491 :

tasmai prAchyA dishaH //1//
vAsantau mAsau goptArAv akurvan bR^ihach cha rathaMtaraM chAnuShThAtArau //2//
vAsantAv enaM mAsau prAchyA disho gopAyato bR^ihach cha rathaMtaraM chAnu tiShThato ya evaM veda //3// 1]

492 :

tasmai dakShiNAyA dishaH //4//
graiShmau mAsau goptArAv akurvan yaj~nAyaj~niyaM cha vAmadevyaM chAnuShThAtArau //5//
graiShmAv enaM mAsau dakShiNAyA disho gopAyato yaj~nAyaj~niyaM cha vAmadevyaM chAnu tiShThato ya evaM veda //6// 2]

493 :

tasmai pratIchyA dishaH //7//
vArShikau mAsau goptArAv akurvan vairUpaM cha vairAjaM chAnuShThAtArau //8//
vArShikAv enaM mAsau pratIchyA disho gopAyato vairUpaM cha vairAjaM chAnu tiShThato ya evaM veda //9// 3]

494 :

tasmA udIchyA dishaH //10//
shAradau mAsau goptArAv akurvaM chhyaitaM cha naudhasaM chAnuShThAtArau //11//
shAradAv enaM mAsAv udIchyA disho gopAyataH shyaitaM cha naudhasaM chAnu tiShThato ya evaM veda //12// 4]

495 :

tasmai dhruvAyA dishaH //13//
haimanau mAsau goptArAv akurvan bhUmiM chAgniM chAnuShThAtArau //14//
haimanAv enaM mAsau dhruvAyA disho gopAyato bhUmish chAgnish chAnu tiShThato ya evaM veda //15// 5]

496 :

tasmA UrdhvAyA dishaH //16//
shaishirau mAsau goptArAv akurvan divaM chAdityaM chAnuShThAtArau //17//
shaishirAv enaM mAsAv UrdhvAyA disho gopAyato dyaush chAdityash chAnu tiShThato ya evaM veda //18// 6]

497 :

tasmai prAchyA disho antardeshAd bhavam iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //1// 1]

498 :

bhava enam iShvAsaH prAchyA disho antardeshAd anuShThAtAnu tiShThati //2//
nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshAno nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda //3// 1]

499 :

tasmai dakShiNAyA disho antardeshAch chharvam iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //4//
sharva enam ishvAso dakShiNAyA disho antardeshAd anuShThAtAnu tiShThati nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshAno nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda //5// 2]

500 :

tasmai pratIchyA disho antardeshAt pashupatim iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //6//
pashupatir enam iShvAsaH pratIchyA disho antardeshAd anuShThAtAnu tiShThati nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshano nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda //7//
3]

501 :

tasmA udIchyA disho antardeshAd ugraM devam iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //8//
ugra enaM deva iShvAsa udIchyA disho antardeshAd anuShThAtAnu tiShThati nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshAno nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda //9// 4]


502 :

tasmai dhruvAyA disho antardeshAd rudram iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //10//
rudra enam iShvAso dhruvAyA disho antardeshAd anuShThAtAnu tiShThati nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshAno nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda //11// 5]

503 :

tasmA UrdhvAyA disho antardeshAn mahAdevam iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //12//
mahAdeva enam iShvAsa UrdhvAyA disho antardeshAd anuShThAtAnu tiShThati nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshAno nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda
//13// 6]

504 :

tasmai sarvebhyo antardeshebhya IshAnam iShvAsam anuShThAtAram akurvan //14//

nainaM sharvo na bhavo neshAno nAsya pashUn na samAnAn hinasti ya evaM veda //16// 7]

505 :

sa dhruvAM disham anu vy achalat //1//
taM bhUmish chAgnish chauShadhayash cha vanaspatayash cha vAnaspatyAsh cha vIrudhash chAnuvyachalan //2//
bhUmesh cha vai so3 'gnesh chauShadhInAM cha vanaspatInAM cha vAnaspatyAnAM /
cha vIrudhAM cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //3// 1]

506 :

sa UrdhvAM disham anu vy achalat //4//
tam R^itaM cha satyaM cha sUryash cha chandrash cha nakShatrANi chAnuvyachalan //5//
R^itasya cha vai sa satyasya cha sUryasya cha chandrasya cha nakShatrANAM cha /
priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //6// 2]

507 :

sa uttamAM disham anu vy achalat //7//
tam R^ichash cha sAmAni cha yajUMShi cha brahma chAnuvyachalan //8//
R^ichAM cha vai sa sAmnAM cha yajuShAM cha brahmaNash cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //9// 3]

508 :

sa bR^ihatIM disham anu vy achalat //10//
tam itihAsash cha purANaM cha gAthAsh cha nArAshaMsIsh chAnuvyachalan //11//
itihAsasya cha vai sa purANasya cha gAthAnAM cha nArAshaMsInAM cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //12// 4]

509 :

sa paramAM disham anu vy achalat //13//
tam AhavanIyash cha gArhapatyash cha dakShiNAgnish cha yaj~nash cha yajamAnash cha pashavash chAnuvyachalan //14//
AhavanIyasya cha vai sa gArhapatyasya cha dakShiNAgnesh cha yaj~nasya cha yajamAnasya cha pashUnAM cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //15// 5]

510 :

so 'nAdiShTAM disham anu vy achalat //16//
tam R^itavash chArtavAsh cha lokAsh cha laukyAsh cha mAsAsh chArdhamAsAsh chAhorAtre chAnuvyachalan //17//
R^itUnAM cha vai sa ArtavAnAM cha lokAnAM cha laukyAnAM cha mAsAnAM chArdhamAsAnAM chAhorAtrayosh cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //18// 6]

511 :

so 'nAvR^ittAM disham anu vy achalat tato nAvartsyann amanyata //19//
tam ditish chAditish cheDA chendrANI chAnuvyachalan //20//
ditesh cha vai so 'ditesh cheDAyAsh chendrANyAsh cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //21// 7]

512 :

sa disho 'nu vy achalat taM virAD anu vy achalat sarve cha devAH sarvAsh cha devatAH //22//
virAjash cha vai sa sarveShAM cha devAnAM sarvAsAM cha devatAnAM priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //23// 8]

513 :

sa sarvAn antardeshAn anu vy achalat //24//
taM prajApatish cha parameShThI cha pitA cha pitAmahash chAnuvyachalan //25//
prajApatesh cha vai parameShThinash cha pitush cha pitAmahasya cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //26// 9]

514

sa mahimA sadrur bhUtvAntaM pR^ithivyA agachhat sa samudro 'bhavat //1//
taM prajApatish cha parameShThI cha pitA cha pitAmahash chApash cha shraddhA cha varShaM bhUtvAnuvyavartayanta //2//
ainam Apo gachhaty ainaM shraddhA gachhaty ainaM varShaM gachhati ya evaM veda //3//
taM shraddhA cha yaj~nash cha lokash chAnnaM chAnnAdyaM cha bhUtvAbhiparyAvartanta //4//
ainaM shraddhA gachhaty ainaM yaj~no gachhaty ainaM loko gachhaty ainam annaM gachhaty ainam annAdyaM gachhati ya evaM veda //5//
515

so 'rajyata tato rAjanyo 'jAyata //1//
sa vishaH sabandhUn annam annAdyam abhyudatiShThat //2//
vishAM cha vai sa sabandhUnAM chAnnasya chAnnAdyasya cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //3//
516

sa visho 'nu vy achalat //1//
taM sabhA cha samitish cha senA cha shurA chAnuvyachalan //2//
sabhAyAsh cha vai sa samitesh cha senAyAsh cha surAyAsh cha priyaM dhAma bhavati ya evaM veda //3//
517

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtyo rAj~no 'tithir gR^ihAn Agachhet //1//
shreyAMsam enam Atmano mAnayet tathA kShatrAya nA vR^ishchate tathA rAShTrAya nA vR^ishchate //2//
ato vai brahma cha kShatraM chod atiShThatAM te abrUtAM kaM pra vishAveti //3//
ato vai bR^ihaspatim eva brahma pra vishatv indraM kShatraM tathA vA iti //4//
ato vai bR^ihaspatim eva brahma prAvishad indraM kShatraM //5//
iyaM vA u pR^ithivI bR^ihaspatir dyaur evendraH //6//
ayaM vA u agnir brahmAsAv AdityaH kShatram //7//
ainaM brahma gachhati brahmavarchasI bhavati //8//
yaH pR^ithivIM bR^ihaspatim agniM brahma veda //9//
ainam indriyaM gachhatIndriyavAn bhavati //10//
ya AdityaM kShatraM divam indraM veda //11//
518

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtyo 'tithir gR^ihAn Agachhet //1//
svayam enam abhyudetya brUyAd vrAtya kvAvAtsIr vrAtyodakaM vrAtya tarpayantu vrAtya yathA te priyaM tathAstu vrAtya yathA te vashas tathAstu vrAtya
yathA te nikAmas tathAstv iti //2//
yad enam Aha vrAtya kvAvAtsIr iti patha eva tena devayAnAn ava runddhe //3//
yad enam Aha vrAtyodakam ity apa eva tenAva runddhe //4//
yad enam Aha vrAtya tarpayantv iti prANam eva tena varShIyAMsaM kurute //5//
yad enam Aha vrAtya yathA te priyaM tathAstv iti priyam eva tenAva runddhe //6//
ainaM priyaM gachhati priyaH priyasya bhavati ya evaM veda //7//
yad enam Aha vrAtya yathA te vashas tathAstv iti vasham eva tenAva runddhe //8//
ainaM vasho gachhati vashI vashinAM bhavati ya evaM veda //9//
yad enam Aha vrAtya yathA te nikAmas tathAstv iti nikAmam eva tenAva runddhe //10//
ainaM nikAmo gachhati nikAme nikAmasya bhavati ya evaM veda //11//
519

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtya uddhR^iteShv agniShu adhishrite 'gnihotre 'tithir gR^ihAn Agachhet //1//
svayam enam abhyudetya brUyAd vrAtyAti sR^ija hoShyAmIti //2//
sa chAtisR^ijej juhuyAn na chAtisR^ijen na juhuyAt //3//
sa ya evaM viduShA vrAtyenAtisR^isTo juhoti //4//
pra pitR^iyANaM panthAM jAnAti pra devayAnam //5//
na deveShv A vR^ishchate hutam asya bhavati //6//
pary asyAsmiMl loka AyatanaM shiShyate ya evaM viduShA vrAtyenAtisR^iShTo juhoti //7//
atha ya evaM viduShA vrAtyenAnatisR^iShTo juhoti //8//
na pitR^iyANaM panthAM jAnAti na devayAnam //9//
A deveShu vR^ishchate ahutam asya bhavati //10//
nAsyAsmiMl loka AyatanaM shiShyate ya evaM viduShA vrAtyenAnatisR^iShTo juhoti //11//
520 :

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtya ekAM rAtrim atithir gR^ihe vasati //1//
ye pR^ithivyAM punyA lokAs tAn eva tenAva runddhe //2// 1]

521 :

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtyo dvitIyAM rAtrim atithir gR^ihe vasati //3//
ye 'ntarikShe punyA lokAs tAn eva tenAva runddhe //4// 2]

522 :

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtyas tR^itIyAM rAtrim atithir gR^ihe vasati //5//
ye divi punyA lokAs tAn eva tenAva runddhe //6// 3]

523 :

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtyash chaturthIM rAtrim atithir gR^ihe vasati //7//
ye puNyAnAM punyA lokAs tAn eva tenAva runddhe //8// 4]

524 :

tad yasyaivaM vidvAn vrAtyo 'parimitA rAtrIr atithir gR^ihe vasati //9//
ya evAparimitAH punyA lokAs tAn eva tenAva runddhe //10// 5]

525 :

atha yasyAvrAtyo vrAtyabruvo nAmabibhraty atithir gR^ihAn Agachhet //11// 6]

540 :

tasya vrAtyasya //1//
sapta prANAH saptApAnAH sapta vyAnAH //2//
tasya vrAtyasya /
yo 'sya prathamaH prANa Urdhvo nAmAyaM so agniH //3//

yo 'sya dvitIyaH prANaH prauDho nAmAsau sa AdityaH //4//

yo 'sya ShaShThaH prANaH priyo nAma ta ime pashavaH //8//

yo 'sya saptamaH prANo 'parimito nAma tA prajAH //9//
542

tasya vrAtyasya /
yo 'sya prathamo 'pAnaH sA paurNamAsI //1//

543

tasya vrAtyasya /
yo 'sya prathamo vyAnaH seyaM bhUmiH //1//
tasya vrAtyasya /
yo 'sya dvitIyo vyAnas tad antarikSham //2//
tasya vrAtyasya /
yo 'sya tR^itIyo vyAnaH sA dyauH //3//
tasya vrAtyasya /
yo 'sya chaturtho vyAnas tAni nakShatrANi //4//

samAnam arthaM pari yanti devAH saMvatsaraM vA etad R^itavo 'nupariyanti vrAtyaM cha //8//

544

tasya vrAtyasya //1//
yad asya dakShiNam akShy asau sa Adityo yad asya savyam akShy asau sa chandramAH //2//
yo 'sya dakShiNaH karNo 'yaM so agnir yo 'sya savyaH karNo 'yaM sa pavamAnaH //3//
ahorAtre nAsike ditish chAditish cha shIrShakapAle saMvatsaraH shiraH //4//
ahnA pratya~N vrAtyo rAtryA prA~N namo vrAtyAya //5//
545
Book:
16

atisR^iShTo apAM vR^iShabho 'tisR^iShTA agnayo divyAH //1//
rujan parirujan mR^iNan pramR^iNan //2//
mroko manohA khano nirdAha AtmadUShis tanUdUShiH //3//
idaM tam ati sR^ijAmi taM mAbhyavanikShi //4//
tena tam abhyatisR^ijAmo yo3 'smAn dveShTi yaM vayaM dviShmaH //5//
apAm agram asi samudraM vo 'bhyavasR^ijAmi //6//

viShAsahiM sahamAnaM sAsahAnaM sahIyAMsam /
sahamAnaM sahojitaM svarjitaM gojitaM saMdhanAjitam /
IDyaM nAma hva indram AyuShmAn bhUyAsam //1//
viShAsahiM sahamAnaM sAsahAnaM sahIyAMsam /
sahamAnaM sahojitaM svarjitaM gojitaM saMdhanAjitam /
IDyaM nAma hva indram priyo devAnAM bhUyAsam //2//
viShAsahiM sahamAnaM sAsahAnaM sahIyAMsam /
sahamAnaM sahojitaM svarjitaM gojitaM saMdhanAjitam /
IDyaM nAma hva indram priyaH prajAnAM bhUyAsam //3//
viShAsahiM sahamAnaM sAsahAnaM sahIyAMsam /
sahamAnaM sahojitaM svarjitaM gojitaM saMdhanAjitam /
IDyaM nAma hva indram priyah pashUnAM bhUyAsam //4//
viShAsahiM sahamAnaM sAsahAnaM sahIyAMsam /
sahamAnaM sahojitaM svarjitaM gojitaM saMdhanAjitam /
IDyaM nAma hva indram priyaH samAnAnAM bhUyAsam //5//
ud ihy ud ihi sUrya varchasA mAbhyudihi /
dviShaMsh cha mahyaM radhyatu mA chAhaM dviShate radhaM taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryAni /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //6//
ud ihy ud ihi sUrya varchasA mAbhyudihi /
AMsh cha pashyAmi yAMsh cha na teShu mA sumatiM kR^idhi taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryAni /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //7//
mA tvA dabhant salile apsv a1ntar ye pAshina upatiShThanty atra /
hitvAshastiM divam ArukSha etAM sa no mR^iDa sumatau te syAma taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryAni /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //8//
tvaM na indra mahate saubhagAyAdabdhebhiH pari pAhy aktubhis taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryAni /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //9//
tvaM na indrotibhiH shivAbhiH shaMtamo bhava /
ArohaMs tridivaM divo gR^iNAnaH somapItaye priyadhAmA svastaye taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryAni /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //10// {1}

tvam indrAsi vishvajit sarvavit puruhUtas tvam indra /
tvam indremaM suhavaM stomam erayasva sa no mR^iDa sumatau te syAma taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //11//
adabdho divi pR^ithivyAm utAsi na ta Apur mahimAnam antarikShe /
adabdhena brahmaNA vAvR^idhAnaH sa tvaM na indra divi saM chharma yachha taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //12//
yA ta indra tanUr apsu yA pR^ithivyAM yAntar agnau yA te indra pavamAne svarvidi /
yayendra tanvA3 'ntarikShaM vyApitha tayA na indra tanvA3 sharma yachha taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //13//
tvAm indra brahmaNA vardhayantaH sattraM ni Shedur R^iShayo nAdhamAnAs taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //14//
tvaM tR^itaM tvaM pary eShy utsaM sahasradhAraM vidathaM svarvidaM taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //15//
tvaM rakShase pradishash chatasras tvaM shochiShA nabhasI vi bhAsi /
tvam imA vishvA bhuvanAnu tiShThasa R^itasya panthAm anv eShi vidvAMs taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //16//
pa~nchabhiH parA~N tapasy ekayArvA~N ashastim eShi sudine bAdhamAnas taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //17//
tvam indras tvam mahendras tvaM lokas tvaM prajApatiH /
tubhyaM yaj~no vi tAyate tubhyaM juhvati juhvatas taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //18//
asati sat pratiShThitaM sati bhUtaM pratiShThitam /
bhUtam ha bhavya AhitaM bhavyaM bhUte pratiShThitaM taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //19//
shukro 'si bhrAjo 'si /
sa yathA tvaM bhrAjatA bhrAjo 'sy evAhaM bhrAjatA bhrAjyAsam //20// {2}

ruchir asi rocho 'si /
sa yathA tvaM ruchyA rocho 'sy evAhaM pashubhish cha brAhmaNavarchasena cha ruchiShIya //21//
udyate nama udAyate nama uditAya namaH /
virAje namaH svarAje namaH samrAje namaH //22//
astaMyate namo 'stameShyate namo 'stamitAya namaH /
virAje namaH svarAje namah samrAje namaH //23//
ud agAd ayam Adityo vishvena tapasA saha /
sapatnAn mahyaM randhayan mA chAhaM dviShate radhaM taved viShNo bahUdhA vIryANi /
tvaM naH pR^iNIhi pashubhir vishvarUpaiH sudhAyAM mA dhehi parame vyoman //24//
Aditya nAvam ArukShaH shatAritrAM svastaye /
ahar mAty apIparo rAtriM satrAti pAraya //25//
sUrya nAvam ArukShaH shatAritrAM svastaye /
rAtriM mAty apIparo 'haH satrAti pAraya //26//
prajApater AvR^ito brahmaNA varmaNAhaM kashyapasya jyotiShA varchasA cha /
jaradaShTiH kR^itavIryo vihAyAH sahasrAyuH sukR^itash chareyam //27//
parivR^ito brahmaNA varmaNAham kashyapasya jyotiShA varchasA cha /
mA mA prApann iShavo daivyA yA mA mAnuShIr avasR^iShTAH vadhAya //28//
R^itena gupta R^itubhish cha sarvair bhUtena gupto bhavyena chAham /
mA mA prApat pApmA mota mR^ityur antar dadhe 'haM salilena vAchaH //29//
agnir mA goptA pari pAtu vishvataH udyant sUryo nudatAM mR^ityupAshAn /
vyuchhantIr uShasaH parvatA dhruvAH sahasraM prANA mayy A yatantAm //30// {3}

Book:

o chit sakhAyaM sakhyA vavR^ityAM tiraH puru chid arNavaM jaganvAn /
pitur napAtam A dadhIta vedhA adhi kShami prataraM dIdhyAnaH //1//
na te sakhA sakhyaM vaShTy etat salakShmA yad viShurUpA bhavati /
mahas putrAso asurasya vIrA divo dhartAra urviyA pari khyan //2//
ushanti ghA te amR^itAsa etad ekasya chit tyajasaM martyasya /
ni te mano manasi dhAyy asme janyuH patis tanva1m A viviShyAH //3//
na yat purA chakR^imA kad dha nUnam R^itaM vadanto anR^itaM rapema /
gandharvo apsv apyA cha yoShA sA nau nAbhiH paramaM jAmi tan nau //4//
garbhe nu nau janitA dampatI kar devas tvaShTA savitA vishvarUpaH /
nakir asya pra minanti vratAni veda nAv asya pR^ithivI uta dyauH //5//
ko adya yu~Nkte dhuri gA R^itasya shimIvato bhAmino durhR^iNAyUn /
AsanniShUn hR^itsvaso mayobhUn ya eShAM bhR^ityAm R^iNadhat sa jIvAt //6//
ko asya veda prathamasyAhnaH ka IM dadarsha ka iha pra vochat /
bR^ihan mitrasya varuNasya dhAma kad u brava Ahano vIchyA nR^In //7//
yamasya mA yamya1M kAma Agant samAne yonau sahasheyyAya /
jAyeva patye tanvaM ririchyAM vi chid vR^iheva rathyeva chakrA //8//
na tiShThanti na ni miShanty ete devAnAM spasha iha ye charanti /
anyena mad Ahano yAhi tUyaM tena vi vR^iha rathyeva chakrA //9//
rAtrIbhir asmA ahabhir dashasyet sUryasya chakShur muhur un mimIyAt /
divA pR^ithivyA mithunA sabandhU yamIr yamasya vivR^ihAd ajAmi //10// {1}

A ghA tA gachhAn uttarA yugAni yatra jAmayaH kR^iNavann ajAmi /
upa barbR^ihi vR^iShabhAya bAhum anyam ichhasva subhage patiM mat //11//
kiM bhrAtAsad yad anAthaM bhavAti kim u svasA yan nirR^itir nigachhAt /
kAmamUtA bahv e3tad rapAmi tanvA me tanva1M saM pipR^igdhi //12//
na te nAthaM yamy atrAham asmi na te tanUM tanvA3 sam papR^ichyAm /
anyena mat pramudaH kalpayasva na te bhrAtA subhage vaShTy etat //13//
na vA u te tanUM tanvA3 saM pipR^ichyAM pApam Ahur yaH svasAraM nigachhAt /
asaMyad etan manaso hR^ido me bhrAtA svasuH shayane yach chhayIya //14//
bato batAsi yama naiva te mano hR^idayaM chAvidAm A /
anyA kila tvAM kakShyeva yuktaM pari ShvajAtau libujeva vR^ikSham //15//
anyam U Shu yamy anya u tvAM pari ShvajAtau libujeva vR^ikSham /
tasya vA tvaM mana ichhA sa vA tavAdhA kR^iNuShva saMvidaM subhadrAm //16//
trINi chhandAMsi kavayo vi yetire pururUpaM darshataM vishvachakShaNam /
Apo vAtA oShadhayas tAny ekasmin bhuvana ArpitAni //17//
vR^iShA vR^iShNe duduhe dohasA divaH payAMsi yahvo aditer adAbhyaH /
vishvaM sa veda varuNo yathA dhiyA sa yaj~niyo yajati yaj~niyAm? R^itUn //18//
rapad gandharvIr apyA cha yoShaNA nadasya nAde pari pAtu no manaH /
iShTasya madhye aditir ni dhAtu no bhrAtA no jyeShThaH prathamo vi vochati //19//
so chit nu bhadrA kShumatI yashasvaty uShA uvAsa manave svarvatI /
yad Im ushantam ushatAm anu kratum agniM hotAraM vidathAya jIjanan //20// {2}

adha tyaM drapsaM vibhvaM vichakShanaM vir Abharad iShiraH shyeno adhvare /
yadI visho vR^iNate dasmam AryA agniM hotAram adha dhIr ajAyata //21//
sadAsi raNvo yavaseva puShyate hotrAbhir agne manuShaH svadhvaraH /
viprasya vA yach chhashamAna ukthyo3 vAjaM sasavA.N upayAsi bhUribhiH //22//
ud Iraya pitarA jAra A bhagam iyakShati haryato hR^itta iShyati /
vivakti vahniH svapasyate makhas taviShyate asuro vepate matI //23//
yas te agne sumatiM marto akhyat sahasaH sUno ati sa pra shR^iNve /
iShaM dadhAno vahamAno ashvair A sa dyumA.N amavAn bhUShati dyUn //24//
shrudhI no agne sadane sadhasthe yukShvA ratham amR^itasya dravitnum /
A no vaha rodasI devaputre mAkir devAnAm apa bhUr iha syAH //25//
yad agna eShA samitir bhavAti devI deveShu yajatA yajatra /
ratnA cha yad vibhajAsi svadhAvo bhAgaM no atra vasumantaM vItAt //26//
anv agnir uShasAm agram akhyad anv ahAni prathamo jAtavedAH /
anu sUrya uShaso anu rashmIn dyAvApR^ithivI A vivesha //27//

prati sUryasya purudhA cha rashmIn prati dyAvApR^ithivI A tatAna //28//
dyAvA ha kShAmA prathame R^itenAbhishrAve bhavataH satyavAchA /
devo yan martAn yajathAya kR^iNvant sIdad dhotA pratya~N svam asuM yan //29//
devo devAn paribhUr R^itena vahA no havyaM prathamash chikitvAn /
dhUmaketuH samidhA bhAR^ijIko mandro hotA nityo vAchA yajIyAn //30// {3}

archAmi vAM vardhAyApo ghR^itasnU dyAvAbhUmI shR^iNutaM rodasI me /
ahA yad devA asunItim Ayan madhvA no atra pitarA shishItAm //31//
svAvR^ig devasyAmR^itaM yadI gor ato jAtAso dhArayanta urvI /
vishve devA anu tat te yajur gur duhe yad enI divyaM ghR^itam vAH //32//
kiM svin no rAjA jagR^ihe kad asyAti vrataM chakR^imA ko vi veda /
mitras chid dhi ShmA juhurANo devAM chhloko na yAtAm api vAjo asti //33//
durmantv atrAmR^itasya nAma salakShmA yad viShurUpA bhavAti /
yamasya yo manavate sumantv agne tam R^iShva pAhy aprayuchhan //34//
yasmin devA vidathe mAdayante vivasvataH sadane dhArayante /
sUrye jyotir adadhur mAsy a1ktUn pari dyotaniM charato ajasrA //35//
yasmin devA manmani saMcharanty apIchye3 na vayam asya vidma /
mitro no atrAditir anAgAnt savitA devo varuNAya vochat //36//
sakhAya A shiShAmahe brahmendrAya vajriNe /
stuSha U Shu nR^itamAya dhR^iShNave //37//
shavasA hy asi shruto vR^itrahatyena vR^itrahA /
maghair maghono ati shUra dAshasi //38//
stego na ksAm aty eShi pR^ithivIM mahI no vAtA iha vAntu bhUmau /
mitro no atra varuNo yujamAno agnir vane na vy asR^iShTa shokam //39//
stuhi shrutaM gartasadaM janAnAM rAjAnaM bhImam upahatnum ugram /
mR^iDA jaritre rudra stavAno anyam asmat te ni vapantu senyam //40// {4}

sarasvatIM devayanto havante sarasvatIm adhvare tAyamAne /
sarasvatIM sukR^ito havante sarasvatI dAshuShe vAryaM dAt //41//
sarasvatIM pitaro havante dakShinA yaj~nam abhinakShamANAH /
AsadyAsmin barhiShi mAdayadhvam anamIvA iSha A dhehy asme //42//
sarasvati yA sarathaM yayAthokthaiH svadhAbhir devi pitR^ibhir madantI /
sahasrArgham iDo atra bhAgaM rAyas poShaM yajamAnAya dhehi //43//
ud IrathAm avara ut parAsa un madhyamAH pitaraH somyAsaH /
asuM ya Iyur avR^ikA R^itaj~nAs te no 'vantu pitaro haveShu //44//
AhaM pitR^Int suvidatrAm? avitsi napAtaM cha vikramaNaM cha viShNoH /
barhiShado ye svadhayA sutasya bhajanta pitvas ta ihAgamiShThAH //45//
idaM pitR^ibhyo namo astv adya ye pUrvAso ye aparAsa IyuH /
ye pArthive rajasy A niShaktA ye vA nUnaM suvR^ijanAsu dikShu //46//
mAtalI kavyair yamo a~Ngirobhir bR^ihaspatir R^ikvabhir vAvR^idhAnaH /
yAMsh cha devA vAvR^idhur ye cha devAMs te no 'vantu pitaro haveShu //47//
svAduSh kilAyaM madhumAm? utAyaM tIvraH kilAyaM rasavAm? utAyam /
uto nv asya papivAMsam indraM na kash chana sahata AhaveShu //48//
pareyivAMsaM pravato mahIr iti bahubhyaH panthAm anupaspashAnam /
vaivasvataM saMgamanaM janAnAM yamaM rAjAnaM haviShA saparyata //49//
yamo no gAtuM prathamo viveda naiShA gavyUtir apabhartavA u /
yatrA naH pUrve pitaraH paretA enA jaj~nAnAH pathyA3 anu svAH //50// {5}

barhiShadaH pitara Uty arvAg imA vo havyA chakR^imA juShadhvam /
ta A gatAvasA shaMtamenAdhA naH shaM yor arapo dadhAta //51//
AchyA jAnu dakShiNato niShadyedaM no havir abhi gR^iNantu vishve /
mA hiMsiShTa pitaraH kena chin no yad va AgaH puruShatA karAma //52//
tvaShTA duhitre vahatuM kR^iNoti tenedaM vishvaM bhuvanaM sam eti /
yamasya mAtA paryuhyamAnA maho jAyA vivasvato nanAsha //53//
prehi prehi pathibhiH pUryANair yenA te pUrve pitaraH paretAH /
ubhA rAjAnau svadhayA madantau yamaM pashyAsi varuNaM cha devam //54//
apeta vIta vi cha sarpatAto 'smA etaM pitaro lokam akran /
ahobhir adbhir aktubhir vyaktaM yamo dadAty avasAnam asmai //55//
ushantas tvedhImahy ushantaH sam idhImahi /
ushann ushata A vaha pitR^In haviShe attave //56//
dyumantas tvedhImahi dyumantaH sam idhImahi /
dyumAn dyumata A vaha pitR^In haviShe attave //57//
a~Ngiraso naH pitaro navagvA atharvANo bhR^igavaH somyAsaH /
teShAM vayaM sumatau yaj~niyAnAm api bhadre saumanase syAma //58//
a~Ngirobhir yaj~niyair A gahIha yama vairUpair iha mAdayasva /
vivasvantaM huve yaH pitA te 'smin barhiShy A niShadya //59//
imaM yama prastaram A hi rohA~NgirobhiH pitR^ibhiH saMvidAnaH /
A tvA mantrAH kavishastA vahantv enA rAjan haviSho mAdayasva //60//
ita eta udAruhan divas pR^iShThAnv Aruhan /
pra bhUrjayo yathA pathA dyAm a~Ngiraso yayuH //61// {6}

556

yamAya somaH pavate yamAya kriyate haviH /
yamaM ha yaj~no gachhaty agnidUto araMkR^itaH //1//
yamAya madhumattamaM juhotA pra cha tiShThata /
idaM nama R^iShibhyaH pUrvajebhyaH pUrvebhyaH pathikR^idbhyaH //2//
yamAya ghR^itavat payo rAj~ne havir juhotana /
sa no jIveShv A yamed dIrgham AyuH pra jIvase //3//
mainam agne vi daho mAbhi shUshucho mAsya tvachaM chikShipo mA sharIram /

Ayur vasAna upa yAtu sheShaH saM gachhatAM tanvA suvarchAH //10// {7}

ati drava shvAnau sArameyau chaturakShau shabalau sAdhunA pathA /

yau te shvAnau yama rakShitArau chaturakShau pathiShadI nR^ichakShasA /
tAbhyAM rAjan pari dhehy enaM svasty asmA anamIvaM cha dhehi //12//
urUNasAv asutR^ipAv udumbalau yamasya dUtau charato janAm? anu /
tAv asmabhyaM dR^ishaye sUryAya punar dAtAm asum adyeha bhadram //13//
soma ekebhyaH pavate ghR^itam eka upAsate /
yebhyo madhu pradhAvati tAMsh chid evApi gachhatAt //14//
ye chit pUrva R^itasAtA R^itajAtA R^itAvR^idhaH /
R^iShIn tapasvato yama tapojA.N api gachhatAt //15//
tapasA ye anAdhR^iShyAs tapasA ye svar yayuH /
tapo ye chakrire mahas tAMsh chid evApi gachhatAt //16//
ye yudhyante pradhaneShu shUrAso ye svar tanUtyajaH /
ye vA sahasradakShiNAs tAM chid evApi gachhatAt //17//
sahasraNIthAH kavayo ye gopAyanti sUryam /
R^iShIn tapasvato yama tapojA.N api gachhatAt //18//
syonAsmai bhava pR^ithivy anR^ikSharA niveshanI /
yachhAsmai sharma saprathAH //19//
asaMbAdhe pR^ithivyA urau loke ni dhIyasva /
svadhA yAsh chakR^iShe jIvan tAs te santu madhushchutaH //20// {8}

hvayAmi te manasA mana ihemAn gR^ihAm upa jujuShANa ehi /
saM gachhasva pitR^ibhiH saM yamena syonAs tvA vAtA upa vAntu shagmAH //21//
ut tvA vahantu maruta udavAhA udaprutaH /
ajena kR^iNvantaH shItaM varSheNokShantu bAl iti //22//
ud ahvam Ayur AyuShe kratve dakShAya jIvase /
svAn gachhatu te mano adhA pitR^Im?r upa drava //23//

mA te hAsta tanva1H kiM chaneha //24//
mA tvA vR^ikShaH saM bAdhiShTa mA devI pR^ithivI mahI /
lokaM pitR^iShu vittvaidhasva yamarAjasu //25//
yat te a~Ngam atihitaM parAchair apAnaH prANo ya u vA te paretaH /
tat te saMgatya pitaraH sanIDA ghAsAd ghAsaM punar A veshayantu //26//
apemaM jIvA arudhan gR^ihebhyas taM nir vahata pari grAmAd itaH /
mR^ityur yamasyAsId dUtaH prachetA asUn pitR^ibhyo gamayAM chakAra //27//
ye dasyavaH pitR^iShu praviShTA j~nAtimukhA ahutAdash charanti /
parApuro nipuro ye bharanty agniSh TAn asmAt pra dhamAti yaj~nAt //28//
saM vishantv iha pitaraH svA naH syonaM kR^iNvantah pratiranta AyuH /
tebhyaH shakema haviShA nakShamANA jyog jIvantaH sharadaH purUchIH //29//
yAM te dhenuM nipR^iNAmi yam u kShIra odanam /
tenA janasyAso bhartA yo 'trAsad ajIvanaH //30// {9}

ashvAvatIM pra tara yA sushevA rkShAkaM vA prataraM navIyaH /
yas tvA jaghAna vadhyaH so astu mA so anyad vidata bhAgadheyam //31//
yamaH paro 'varo vivasvAn tataH paraM nAti pashyAmi kiM chana /
yame adhvaro adhi me niviShTo bhuvo vivasvAn anvAtatAna //32//
apAgUhann amR^itAM martyebhyaH kR^itvA savarNAm adadhur vivasvate /
utAshvinAv abharad yat tad AsId ajahAd u dvA mithunA saraNyUH //33//
ye nikhAtA ye paroptA ye dagdhA ye choddhitAH /
sarvAMs tAn agna A vaha pitR^In haviShe attave //34//
ye agnidagdhA ye anagnidagdhA madhye divaH svadhayA mAdayante /
tvaM tAn vettha yadi te jAtavedaH svadhayA yaj~naM svadhitiM juShantAm //35//
shaM tapa mAti tapo agne mA tanva1M tapaH /
vaNeShu shuShmo astu te pR^ithivyAm astu yad dharaH //36//
dadAmy asmA avasAnam etad ya eSha Agan mama ched abhUd iha /
yamash chikitvAn praty etad Aha mamaiSha rAya upa tiShThatAm iha //37//
imAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //38//
premAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //39//
apemAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //40// {10}

 vImAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //41//
nir imAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //42//
ud imAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //43//
sam imAM mAtrAM mimImahe yathAparaM na mAsAtai /
shate sharatsu no purA //44//
amAsi mAtrAM svar agAm AyuShmAn bhUyAsam /
yathAparaM na mAsAtai shate sharatsu no purA //45//
prANo apAno vyAna Ayush chakShur dR^ishaye sUryAya /
aparipareNa pathA yamarAj~naH pitR^In gachha //46//
ye agravaH shashamAnAh pareyur hitvA dveShAMsy anapatyavantaH /
te dyAm udityAvidanta lokaM nAkasya pR^iShThe adhi dIdhyAnAH //47//
udanvatI dyaur avamA pIlumatIti madhyamA /
tR^itIyA ha pradyaur iti yasyAM pitara Asate //48//
ye na pituH pitaro ye pitAmahA ya Avivishur urv antarikSham /
ya AkShiyanti pR^ithivIm uta dyAM tebhyaH pitR^ibhyo namasA vidhema //49//
idam id vA u nAparaM divi pashyasi sUryam /
mAtA putraM yathA sichAbhy enaM bhUma UrNuhi //50// {11}

idam id vA u nAparaM jarasy anyad ito 'param /
jAyA patim iva vAsasAbhy enaM bhUma UrNuhi //51//
abhi tvorNomi pR^ithivyA mAtur vastreNa bhadrayA /
jIveShu bhadraM tan mayi svadhA pitR^iShu sA tvayi //52//
agnIShomA pathikR^itA syonaM devebhyo ratnaM dadhathur vi lokam /
upa preShyantaM pUShaNaM yo vahAty a~njoyAnaiH pathibhis tatra gachhatam //53//
pUShA tvetash chyAvayatu pra vidvAn anaShTapashur bhuvanasya gopAH /
sa tvaitebhyaH pari dadat pitR^ibhyo 'gnir devebhyaH suvidatriyebhyaH //54//
Ayur vishvAyuH pari pAtu tvA pUShA tvA pAtu prapathe purastAt /
yatrAsate sukR^ito yatra ta Iyus tatra tvA devaH savitA dadhAtu //55//
imau yunajmi te vahnI asunItAya voDhave /
tAbhyAM yamasya sAdanaM samitish chAva gachhatAt //56//
etat tvA vAsaH prathamaM nv Agann apaitad Uha yad ihAbibhaH purA /
iShTApUrtam anusaMkrAma vidvAn yatra te dattaM bahudhA vibandhuShu //57//
agner varma pari gobhir vyayasva saM prorNuShva medasA pIvasA cha /
net tvA dhR^iShNur harasA jarhR^iShANo dadhR^ig vidhakShan parI~NkhayAtai //58//
daNDaM hastAd AdadAno gatAsoH saha shrotreNa varchasA balena /
atraiva tvam iha vayaM suvIrA vishvA mR^idho abhimAtIr jayema //59//
dhanur hastAd AdadAno mR^itasya saha kShatreNa varchasA balena /
samAgR^ibhAya vasu bhUri puShTam arvA~N tvam ehy upa jIvalokam //60// {12}

557

iyaM nArI patilokaM vR^iNAnA ni padyata upa tvA martya pretam /
dharmaM purANam anupAlayantI tasyai prajAM draviNaM cheha dhehi //1//
ud IrShva nAry abhi jIvalokaM gatAsum etam upa sheSha ehi /
hastagrAbhasya dadhiShos tavedaM patyur janitvam abhi saM babhUtha //2//
apashyaM yuvatiM nIyamAnAM jIvAM mR^itebhyaH pariNIyamAnAm /
andhena yat tamasA prAvR^itAsIt prAkto apAchIm anayaM tad enAm //3//
prajAnaty aghnye jIvalokaM devAnAM panthAm anusaMcharantI /
ayaM te gopatis taM juShasva svargaM lokam adhi rohayainam //4//
upa dyAm upa vetasam avattaro nadInAm /
agne pittam apAm asi //5//
yaM tvam agne samadahas tam u nir vApaya punaH /
kyAmbUr atra rohatu shANDadUrvA vyalkashA //6//
idaM ta ekam pura U ta ekaM tR^itIyena jyotiShA saM vishasva /
saMveshane tanvA3 chArur edhi priyo devAnAM parame sadhasthe //7//
ut tiShTha prehi pra dravaukaH kR^iNuShva salile sadhasthe /
tatra tvaM pitR^ibhiH saMvidAnaH saM somena madasva saM svadhAbhiH //8//
pra chyavasva tanva1M saM bharasva mA te gAtrA vi hAyi mo sharIram /
mano niviShTam anusaMvishasva yatra bhUmer juShase tatra gachha //9//
varchasA mAM pitaraH somyAso a~njantu devA madhunA ghR^itena /
chakShuShe mA prataraM tArayanto jarase mA jaradaShTiM vardhantu //10// {13}

varchasA mAM sam anaktv agnir medhAM me viShNur ny anaktv Asan /
rayiM me vishve ni yachhantu devAH syonA mApaH pavanaiH punantu //11//
mitrAvaruNA pari mAm adhAtAm AdityA mA svaravo vardhayantu /
varcho ma indro ny anaktu hastayor jaradaShTiM mA savitA kR^iNotu //12//
yo mamAra prathamo martyAnAM yaH preyAya prathamo lokam etam /
vaivasvataM saMgamanaM janAnAM yamaM rAjAnaM haviShA saparyata //13//
parA yAta pitara A cha yAtAyaM vo yaj~no madhunA samaktaH /
datto asmabhyaM draviNeha bhadraM rayiM cha naH sarvavIraM dadhAta //14//
kaNvaH kakShIvAn purumIDho agastyaH shyAvAshvaH sobhary archanAnAH /
vishvAmitro 'yaM jamadagnir atrir avantu naH kashyapo vAmadevaH //15//
vishvAmitra jamadagne vasiShTha bharadvAja gotama vAmadeva /
shardir no atrir agrabhIn namobhiH susaMshAsaH pitaro mR^iDatA naH //16//
kasye mR^ijAnA ati yanti ripram Ayur dadhAnAH prataraM navIyaH /
ApyAyamAnAH prajayA dhanenAdha syAma surabhayo gR^iheShu //17//
a~njate vy a~njate sam a~njate kratuM rihanti madhunAbhy a~njate /
sindhor uchchhvAse patayantam ukShaNaM hiraNyapAvAH pashum Asu gR^ihnate //18//
yad vo mudraM pitaraH somyaM cha teno sachadhvaM svayashaso hi bhUta /
te arvANaH kavaya A shR^iNota suvidatrA vidathe huyamAnAH //19//
ye atrayo a~Ngiraso navagvA iShTAvanto rAtiShAcho dadhAnAH /
dakShiNAvantaH sukR^ito ya u sthAsadyAsmin barhiShi mAdayadhvam //20// {14}

adhA yathA naH pitaraH parAsaH pratnAso agna R^itam AshashAnAH /
shuchId ayan dIdhyata ukthashasaH kShAmA bhindanto aruNIr apa vran //21//
sukarmAnaH surucho devayanto ayo na devA janimA dhamantaH /
shuchanto agniM vAvR^idhanta indram urvIm gavyAM pariShadaM no akran //22//
A yUtheva kShumati pashvo akhyad devAnAM janimAnty ugraH /
martAsash chid urvashIr akR^ipran vR^idhe chid arya uparasyAyoH //23//
akarma te svapaso abhUma R^itam avasrann uShaso vibhAtIH /
vishvaM tad bhadraM yad avanti devA bR^ihad vadema vidathe suvIrAH //24//
indro mA marutvAn prAchyA dishaH pAtu bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //25//
dhAtA mA nirR^ityA dakShiNAyA dishaH pAtu bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAM ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //26//
aditir mAdityaiH pratIchyA dishaH pAtu bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //27//
somo mA vishvair devair udIchyA dishaH pAtu bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //28//
dhartA ha tvA dharuNo dhArayAtA UrdhvaM bhAnuM savitA dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //29//
prAchyAM tvA dishi purA samvR^itaH svadhAyAm A dadhAmi bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //30// {15}

dakShiNAyAM tvA dishi purA samvR^itaH svadhAyAm A dadhAmi bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //31//
pratIchyAM tvA dishi purA saMvR^itaH svadhAyAm A dadhAmi bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //32//
udIchyAM tvA dishi purA samvR^itaH svadhAyAm A dadhAmi bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //33//
dhruvAyAM tvA dishi purA saMvR^itaH svadhAyAm A dadhAmi bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //34//
UrdhvAyAM tvA dishi purA samvR^itaH svadhAyAm A dadhAmi bAhuchyutA pR^ithivI dyAm ivopari /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //35//
dhartAsi dharuno 'si vaMsago 'si //36//
udapUr asi madhupUr asi vAtapUr asi //37//
itash cha mAmutash chAvatAM yame iva yatamAne yad aitam /
pra vAM bharan mAnuShA devayanto A sIdatAM svam u lokaM vidAne //38//
svAsasthe bhavatam indave no yuje vAM brahma pUrvyaM namobhiH /
vi shloka eti pathyeva sUriH shR^iNvantu vishve amR^itAsa etat //39//
trINi padAni rupo anv arohach chatuShpadIm anv etad vratena /
akShareNa prati mimIte arkam R^itasya nAbhAv abhi saM punAti //40// {16}

devebhyaH kam avR^iNIta mR^ityuM prajAyai kim amR^itaM nAvR^iNIta /
bR^ihaspatir yaj~nam atanuta R^iShiH priyAM yamas tanva1m A rirecha //41//
tvam agna IDito jAtavedo 'vAD DhavyAni surabhINi kR^itvA /
prAdAH pitR^ibhyaH svadhayA te akShann addhi tvaM deva prayatA havIMShi //42//
AsInAso aruNInAm upasthe rayiM dhatta dAshuShe martyAya /
putrebhyaH pitaras tasya vasvaH pra yachhata ta ihorjaM dadhAta //43//
agniShvAttAH pitara eha gachhata sadaHsadaH sadata supraNItayaH /
atto havIMShi prayatAni barhiShi rayiM cha naH sarvavIraM dadhAta //44//
upahUtA naH pitaraH somyAso barhiShyeShu nidhiShu priyeShu /
ta A gamantu ta iha shruvantv adhi bruvantu te 'vantv asmAn //45//
ye naH pituH pitaro ye pitAmahA anUjahire somapIthaM vasiShThAH /
tebhir yamaH samrarANo havIMShy ushann ushadbhiH pratikAmam attu //46//
ye tAtR^iShur devatrA jehamAnA hotrAvidaH stomataShTAso arkaiH /
Agne yAhi sahasraM devavandaiH satyaiH kavibhir R^iShibhir gharmasadbhiH //47//
ye satyAso havirado haviShpA indreNa devaiH sarathaM tureNa /
Agne yAhi suvidatrebhir arvA~N paraiH pUrvair R^iShibhir gharmasadbhiH //48//
upa sarpa mAtaraM bhUmim etAm uruvyachasaM pR^ithivIM sushevAm /
UrNamradAH pR^ithivI dakShiNAvata eShA tvA pAtu prapathe purastAt //49//
uch chhva~nchasva pR^ithivi mA ni bAdhathAH sUpAyanAsmai bhava sUpasarpaNA /
mAtA putraM yathA sichAbhy enaM bhUma UrNuhi //50// {17}

uchchhva~nchamAnA pR^ithivI su tiShThatu sahasraM mita upa hi shrayantAm /
te gR^ihAso ghR^itashchutaH syonA vishvAhAsmai sharaNAH santv atra //51//
ut te stabhnAmi pR^ithivIM tvat parImaM logaM nidadhan mo aham riSham /
etAM sthUNAM pitaro dhArayanti te tatra yamaH sAdanA te kR^iNotu //52//
imam agne chamasaM mA vi jihvaraH priyo devAnAm uta somyAnAm /
ayaM yash chamaso devapAnas tasmin devA amR^itA mAdayantAm //53//
atharvA pUrNam chamasam yam indrAyAbibhar vAjinIvate /
tasmin kR^iNoti sukR^itasya bhakShaM tasmin induH pavate vishvadAnim //54//
yat te kR^iShNaH shakuna Atutoda pipIlaH sarpa uta vA shvApadaH /
agniSh Tad vishvAd agadaM kR^iNotu somash cha yo brAhmaNA.N Avivesha //55//
payasvatIr oShadhayaH payasvan mAmakaM payaH /
apAM payaso yat payas tena mA saha shumbhatu //56//
imA nArIr avidhavAH supatnIr A~njanena sarpiShA saM spR^ishantAm /
anashravo anamIvAH suratnA A rohantu janayo yonim agre //57//
saM gachhasva pitR^ibhiH saM yameneShTApUrtena parame vyoman /
hitvAvadyaM punar astam ehi saM gachhatAM tanvA suvarchAH //58//
ye naH pituH pitaro ye pitAmahA ya Avivishur urv a1ntarikSham /
tebhyaH svarAd asunItir no adya vathAvashaM tanvaH kalpayAti //59//
shaM te nIhAro bhavatu shaM te pruShvAva shIyatAm /
shItike shItikAvati hlAdike hlAdikAvati /
maNDUky a1psu shaM bhuva imaM sv agniM shamaya //60// {18}

vivasvAn no abhayaM kR^iNotu yaH sutrAmA jIradAnuH sudAnuH /
iheme vIrA bahavo bhavantu gomad ashvavan mayy astu puShTam //61//
vivasvAn no amR^itatve dadhAtu paraitu mR^ityur amR^itaM na aitu /
imAn rakShatu puruShAn A jarimNo mo sv eShAm asavo yamaM guH //62//
yo dadhre antarikShe na mahnA pitR^INAM kaviH pramatir matInAm /
tam archata vishvamitrA havirbhiH sa no yamaH prataraM jIvase dhAt //63//
A rohata divam uttamAm R^iShayo mA bibhItana /
somapAH somapAyina idaM vaH kriyate havir aganma jvotir uttamam //64//
pra ketunA bR^ihatA bhAty agnir A rodasI vR^iShabho roravIti /
divash chid antAd upamAm ud AnaD apAm upasthe mahiSho vavardha //65//
nAke suparNam upa yat patantaM hrdA venanto abhyachakShata tvA /
hiraNyapakShaM varuNasya dUtaM yamasya yonau shakunaM bhuranyum //66//
indra kratuM na A bhara pitA putrebhyo yathA /
shikShA No asmin puruhUta yAmani jIvA jyotir ashImahi //67//
apUpApihitAn kumbhAn yAMs te devA adhArayan /
te te santu svadhAvanto madhumanto ghR^itashchutaH //68//
yAs te dhAnA anukirAmi tilamishrA svadhAvatIH /
tAs te santu vibhvIH prabhvIs tAs te yamo rAjAnu manyatAm //69//
punar dehi vanaspate ya eSha nihitas tvayi /
yathA yamasya sAdana AsAtau vidathA vadan //70//
A rabhasva jAtavedas tejasvad dharo astu te /
sharIram asya saM dahAthainaM dehi sukR^itAm u loke //71//
ye te pUrve parAgatA apare pitarash cha ye /
tebhyo ghR^itasya kulyaitu shatadhArA vyundatI //72//
etad A roha vaya unmR^ijAnaH svA iha bR^ihad u dIdayante /
abhi prehi madhyato mApa hAsthAH pitR^InAM lokaM prathamo yo atra //73// {19}

558

A rohata janitrIM jAtavedasaH pitR^iyAnaiH saM va A rohayAmi /
avAD DhavyeShito havyavAha IjAnaM yuktAH sukR^itAM dhatta loke //1//
devA yaj~nam R^itavaH kalpayanti haviH puroDAshaM srucho yaj~nAyudhAni /
tebhir yAhi pathibhir devayAnair yair IjAnAH svargaM yanti lokam //2//
R^itasya panthAm anu pashya sAdhv a~NgirasaH sukR^ito yena yanti /
tebhir yAhi pathibhiH svargaM yatrAdityA madhu bhakShayanti tR^itIye nAke adhi vi shrayasva //3//
trayaH suparNA uparasya mAyU nAkasya pR^iShThe adhi viShTapi shritAH /
svargA lokA amR^itena viShThA iSham UrjaM yajamAnAya duhrAm //4//
juhUr dAdhAra dyAm upabhR^id antarikShaM dhruvA dAdhAra pR^ithivIM pratiShThAm /
pratImAM lokA ghR^itapR^iShThAH svargAH kAmaMkAmaM yajamAnAya duhrAm //5//
dhruva A roha pR^ithivIM vishvabhojasam antarikSham upabhR^id A kramasva /
juhu dyAM gachha yajamAnena sAkaM sruveNa vatsena dishaH prapInAH sarvA dhukShvAhR^iNyamAnaH //6//
tIrthais taranti pravato mahIr iti yaj~nakR^itaH sukR^ito yena yanti /
atrAdadhur yajamAnAya lokaM disho bhUtAni yad akalpayanta //7//
a~NgirasAm ayanaM pUrvo agnir AdityAnAm ayanaM gArhapatyo dakShiNAnAm ayanaM dakShiNAgniH /
mahimAnam agner vihitasya brahmaNA sama~NgaH sarva upa yAhi shagmaH //8//
pUrvo agniSh TvA tapatu shaM purastAch chhaM pashchAt tapatu gArhapatyaH /
dakShiNAgniSh Te tapatu sharma varmottarato madhyato antarikShAd dishodisho agne pari pAhi ghorAt //9//
yUyam agne shaMtamAbhis tanUbhir IjAnam abhi lokaM svargam /
ashvA bhUtvA pR^iShTivAho vahAtha yatra devaiH sadhamAdaM madanti //10// {20}

sham agne pashchAt tapa shaM purastAch chham uttarAch chham adharAt tapainam /
ekas tredhA vihito jAtavedaH samyag enaM dhehi sukR^itAm u loke //11//
sham agnayaH samiddhA A rabhantAM prAjApatyaM medhyaM jAtavedasaH /
shR^itaM kR^iNvanta iha mAva chikShipan //12//
yaj~na eti vitataH kalpamAna IjAnam abhi lokaM svargam /
tam agnayaH sarvahutaM juShantAM prAjApatyaM medhyaM jAtavedasaH //13//
IjAnash chitam ArukShad agniM nAkasya pR^iShThAd divam utpatiShyan /
tasmai pra bhAti nabhaso jyotiShImAnt svargaH panthAH sukR^ite devayAnaH //14//
agnir hotAdhvaryuSh Te bR^ihaspatir indro brahmA dakShiNatas te astu /
huto 'yaM saMsthito yaj~na eti yatra pUrvam ayanaM hutAnAm //15//
apUpavAn kShIravAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //16//
apUpavAn dadhivAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //17//
apUpavAn drapsavAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //18//
apUpavAn ghR^itavAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //19//
apUpavAn mAMsavAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //20// {21}

apUpavAn annavAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //21//
apUpavAn madhumAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //22//
apUpavAn rasavAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //23//
apUpavAn apavAMsh charur eha sIdatu /
lokakR^itaH pathikR^ito yajAmahe ye devAnAM hutabhAgA iha stha //24//
apUpApihitAn kumbhAn yAMs te devA adhArayan /
