te te santu svadhAvanto madhumanto ghR^itashchutah //25//
yAs te dhAnA anukirAmi tilamishrAH svadhAvatIH /
tAs te santUdbhvIH prabhvIs tAs te yamo rAjAnu manyatAm //26//
akShitiM bhUyasIm //27//
drapsash chaskanda pR^ithivIm anu dyAm imaM cha yonim anu yash cha pUrvaH /
samAnaM yonim anu samcharantaM drapsam juhomy anu sapta hotrAH //28//
shatadhAraM vAyum arkaM svarvidaM nR^ichakShasas te abhi chakShate rayim /
ye pR^inanti pra cha yachhanti sarvadA te duhrate daksiNAM saptamAtaram //29//
koshaM duhanti kalashaM chaturbilam iDAM dhenuM madhumatIM svastaye /
UrjaM madantIm aditiM janeShv agne mA hiMsIH parame vyoman //30// {22}

etat te devaH savitA vAso dadAti bhartave /
tat tvaM yamasya rAjye vasAnas tArpyaM chara //31//
dhAnA dhenur abhavad vatso asyAs tilo 'bhavat /
tAM vai yamasya rAjye akShitAm upa jIvati //32//
etAs te asau dhenavaH kAmadughA bhavantu /
enIH shyenIH sarUpA virUpAs tilavatsA upa tiShThantu tvAtra //33//
enIr dhAnA hariNIH shyenIr asya kR^iShNA dhAnA rohiNIr dhenavas te /
tilavatsA Urjam asmai duhAnA vishvAhA santv anapasphurantIH //34//
vaishvAnare havir idaM juhomi sAhasraM shatadhAram utsam /
sa bibharti pitaraM pitAmahAn prapitAmahAn bibharti pinvamAnaH //35//
sahasradhAraM shatadhAram utsam akShitaM vyachyamAnaM salilasya pR^iShThe /
UrjaM duhAnam anapasphurantam upAsate pitaraH svadhAbhih //36//
idaM kasAmbu chayanena chitaM tat sajAtA ava pashyateta /
martyo 'yam amR^itatvam eti tasmai gR^ihAn kR^iNuta yAvatsabandhu //37//
ihaivaidhi dhanasanir ihachitta ihakratuH /
ihaidhi vIryavattaro vayodhA aparAhataH //38//
putraM pautram abhitarpayantIr Apo madhumatIr imAH /
svadhAM pitR^ibhyo amR^itaM duhAnA Apo devIr ubhayAMs tarpayantu //39//
Apo agniM pra hiNuta pitR^Im?r upemaM yaj~naM pitaro me juShantAm /
AsInAm Urjam upa ye sachante te no rayiM sarvavIraM ni yachhAn //40// {23}

sam indhate amartyaM havyavAhaM ghR^itapriyam /
sa veda nihitAn nidhIn pitR^In parAvato gatAn //41//
yaM te manthaM yam odananaM yan mAMsaM nipR^iNAmi te /
te te santu svadhAvanto madhumanto ghR^itashchutaH //42//
yAs te dhAnA anukirAmi tilamishrAH svadhAvatIH /
tAs te santUdbhvIH prabhvIs tAs te yamo rAjAnu manyatAm //43//
idaM pUrvam aparaM niyAnaM yenA te pUrve pitaraH paretAH /
purogavA ye abhisAcho asya te tvA vahanti sukR^itAm u lokam //44//
sarasvatIM devayanto havante sarasvatIm adhvare tAyamAne /
sarasvatIM sukR^ito havante sarasvatI dAshuShe vAryaM dAt //45//
sarasvatIM pitaro havante dakShiNA yaj~nam abhinakShamANAH /
AsadyAsmin barhiShi mAdayadhvam anamIvA iSha A dhehy asme //46//
sarasvati yA sarathaM yayAthokthaiH svadhAbhir devi pitR^ibhir madantI /
sahasrArgham iDo atra bhAgaM rAyas poShaM yajamAnAya dhehi //47//
pR^ithivIM tvA pR^ithivyAm A veshayAmi devo no dhAtA pra tirAty AyuH /
parAparaitA vasuvid vo astv adhA mR^itAH pitR^iShu saM bhavantu //48//
A pra chyavethAm apa tan mR^ijethAM yad vAm abhibhA atrochuH /
asmAd etam aghnyau tad vashIyo dAtuH pitR^iShv ihabhojanau mama //49//
eyam agan dakShiNA bhadrato nA anena dattA sudughA vayodhAH /
yauvane jIvAn upapR^i~nchatI jarA pitR^ibhya upasaMparANayAd imAn //50// {24}

idaM pitR^ibhyaH pra bharAmi barhir jIvaM devebhya uttaraM stR^iNAmi /
tad A roha puruSha medhyo bhavan prati tvA jAnantu pitarah paretam //51//
edaM barhir asado medhyo 'bhUH prati tvA jAnantu pitaraH paretam /
yathAparu tanva1M saM bharasva gAtrANi te brahmaNA kalpayAmi //52//
parNo rAjApidhAnaM charUNAm Urjo balaM saha ojo na Agan /
Ayur jIvebhyo vidadhad dIrghAyutvAya shatashAradAya //53//
Urjo bhAgo ya imaM jajAnAshmAnnAnAm AdhipatyaM jagAma /
tam archata vishvamitrA havirbhiH sa no yamaH prataraM jIvase dhAt //54//
yathA yamAya harmyam avapan pa~ncha mAnavAH /
evA vapAmi harmyaM yathA me bhUrayo 'sata //55//
idaM hiraNyaM bibhR^ihi yat te pitAbibhaH purA /
svargaM yataH pitur hastaM nir mR^iDDhi dakShiNam //56//
ye cha jIvA ye cha mR^itA ye jAtA ye cha yaj~niyAH /
tebhyo ghR^itasya kulyaitu madhudhArA vyundatI //57//
vR^iShA matInAM pavate vichakShaNaH sUro ahnAM pratarItoShasAM divaH /
prANaH sindhUnAM kalashAm? achikradad indrasya hArdim Avishan manIShayA //58//
tveShas te dhUma UrNotu divi ShaM chhukra AtataH /
sUro na hi dyutA tvaM kR^ipA pAvaka rochase //59//
pra vA etIndur indrasya niShkR^itiM sakhA sakhyur na pra minAti saMgiraH /
marya iva yoShAH sam arShase somaH kalashe shatayAmanA pathA //60// {25}

akShann amImadanta hy ava priyA.N adhUShata /
astoShata svabhAnavo viprA yaviShThA Imahe //61//
A yAta pitaraH somyAso gambhIraiH pathibhiH pitR^iyANaiH /
Ayur asmabhyaM dadhataH prajAM cha rAyash cha poShair abhi naH sachadhvam //62//
parA yAta pitaraH somyAso gambhIraiH pathibhiH pUryANaiH /
adhA mAsi punar A yAta no gR^ihAn havir attuM suprajasaH suvIrAH //63//
yad vo agnir ajahAd ekam a~NgaM pitR^ilokaM gamayaM jAtavedAH /
tad va etat punar A pyAyayAmi sA~NgAH svarge pitaro mAdayadhvam //64//
abhUd dUtaH prahito jAtavedAH sAyaM nyahna upavandyo nR^ibhiH /
prAdAH pitR^ibhyaH svadhayA te akShann addhi tvaM deva prayatA havIMShi //65//
asau hA iha te manaH kakutsalam iva jAmayaH /
abhy enaM bhUma UrNuhi //66//
shumbhantAM lokAH pitR^iShadanAH pitR^iShadane tvA loka A sAdayAmi //67//
ye asmAkaM pitaras teShAM barhir asi //68//
ud uttamaM varuNa pAsham asmad avAdhamaM shrathAya /
adhA vayam Aditya vrate tavAnAgaso aditaye syAma //69//
prAsmat pAshAn varuNa mu~ncha sarvAn yaih samAme badhyate yair vyAme /
adhA jIvema sharadaM shatAni tvayA rAjan gupitA rakShamANAH //70// {26}

agnaye kavyavAhanAya svadhA namaH //71//
somAya pitR^imate svadhA namaH //72//
pitR^ibhyaH somavadbhyaH svadhA namaH //73//
yamAya pitR^imate svadhA namaH //74//
etat te pratatAmaha svadhA ye cha tvAm anu //75//
etat te tatAmaha svadhA ye cha tvAm anu //76//
etat te tata svadhA //77//
svadhA pitR^ibhyaH pR^ithiviShadbhyaH //78//
svadhA pitR^ibhyo antarikShasadbhyaH //79//
svadhA pitR^ibhyo diviShadbhyaH //80// {27}

namo vaH pitara Urje namo vaH pitaro rasAya //81//
namo vaH pitaro bhAmAya namo vaH pitaro manyave //82//
namo vaH pitaro yad ghoraM tasmai namo vaH pitaro yat krUraM tasmai //83//
namo vaH pitaro yach chhivaM tasmai namo vaH pitaro yat syonaM tasmai //84//
namo vaH pitaraH svadhA vaH pitaraH //85//
ye 'tra pitaraH pitaro ye 'tra yUyaM stha yuShmAMs te 'nu yUyaM teShAM shreShThA bhUyAstha //86//
ya iha pitaro jIvA iha vayaM smaH /
asmA.Ns te 'nu vayaM teShAM shreShThA bhUyAsma //87//
A tvAgna idhImahi dyumantaM devAjaram /
yad gha sA te panIyasI samid dIdayati dyavi /
iShaM stotR^ibhya A bhara //88//
chandramA apsv a1ntar A suparNo dhAvate divi /
na vo hiraNyanemayaH padaM vindanti vidyuto vittaM me asya rodasI //89// {28}

559
Book:
19

saMsaM sravantu nadya1H saM vAtAH saM patatriNaH /
yaj~nam imaM vardhayatA giraH saMsrAvyeNa haviShA juhomi //1//
imaM homA yaj~nam avatemaM saMsrAvaNA uta yaj~nam imaM vardhayatA giraH saMsrAvyeNa haviShA juhomi //2//
rUpaMrUpaM vayovayaH saMrabhyainaM pari Shvaje /
yaj~nam imaM chatasraH pradisho vardhayantu saMsrAvyeNa haviShA juhomi //3//
560

shaM ta Apo haimavatIH sham u te santUtsyAH /
shaM te saniShyadA ApaH sham u te santu varShyAH //1//
shaM ta Apo dhanvanyA3H shaM te santv anUpyAH /
shaM te khanitrimA ApaH shaM yAH kumbhebhir AbhR^itAH //2//
anabhrayaH khanamAnA viprA gambhIre apasaH /
bhiShagbhyo bhiShaktarA Apo achhA vadAmasi //3//
apAm aha divyAnAm apAM srotasyAnAm /
apAm aha praNejane 'shvA bhavatha vAjinaH //4//
tA apaH shivA apo 'yakShmaMkaraNIr apaH /
yathaiva tR^ipyate mayas tAs ta A datta bhesajIH //5//
561

divas pR^ithivyAH pary antarikShAd vanaspatibhyo adhy oShadhIbhyaH /
yatrayatra vibhR^ito jAtavedAs tata stuto juShamANo na ehi //1//
yas te apsu mahimA yo vaneShu ya oShadhIShu pashuShv apsv antaH /
agne sarvAs tanva1H saM rabhasva tAbhir na ehi draviNodA ajasraH //2//
yas te deveShu mahimA svargo yA te tanuH pitR^iShv Avivesha /
puShTir yA te manuShyeShu paprathe 'gne tayA rayim asmAsu dhehi //3//
shrutkarNAya kavaye vedyAya vachobhir vAkair upa yAmi rAtim /
yato bhayam abhayaM tan no astv ava devAnAM yaja heDo agne //4//
562

yAm AhutiM prathamAm atharvA yA jAtA yA havyam akR^iNoj jAtavedAH /
tAM ta etAM prathamo johavImi tAbhiSh Tupto vahatu havyam agnir agnaye svAha //1//
AkUtiM devIM subhagAM puro dadhe chittasya mAtA suhavA no astu /
yAm AshAm emi kevalI sA me astu videyam enAM manasi praviShTAm //2//
AkUtyA no bR^ihaspata AkUtyA na upA gahi /
atho bhagasya no dhehy atho naH suhavo bhava //3//
bR^ihaspatir ma AkUtim A~NgirasaH prati jAnAtu vAcham etAm /
yasya devA devatAH saMbabhUvuH sa supraNItAH kAmo anv etv asmAn //4//
563

indro rAjA jagatash charShaNInAm adhi kShami viShurUpaM yad asti /
tato dadAti dAshuShe vasUni chodad rAdha upastutash chid arvAk //1//
564

sahasrabAhuH puruShaH sahasrAkShaH sahasrapAt /
sa bhUmiM vishvato vR^itvAty atiShThad dashA~Ngulam //1//
tribhiH padbhir dyAm arohat pAd asyehAbhavat punaH /
tathA vy akrAmad viShva~N ashanAnashane anu //2//
tAvanto asya mahimAnas tato jyAyAMsh cha pUruShaH /
pAdo 'sya vishvA bhUtAni tripAd asyAmR^itaM divi //3//
puruSha evedaM sarvaM yad bhUtaM yach cha bhAvyam /
utAmR^itatvasyeshvaro yad anyenAbhavat saha //4//
yat puruShaM vy adadhuH katidhA vy akalpayan /
mukhaM kim asya kim bAhU kim UrU pAdA uchyate //5//
brAhmaNo 'sya mukham AsId bAhU rAjanyo 'bhavat /
madhyaM tad asya yad vaishyaH padbhyAM shUdro ajAyata //6//
chandramA manaso jAtash chakShoH sUryo ajAyata /
mukhAd indrash chAgnish cha prANAd vAyur ajAyata //7//
nAbhyA AsId antarikShaM shIrShNo dyauH sam avartata /
padbhyAM bhUmir dishaH shrotrAt tathA lokA.N akalpayan //8//
virAD agre sam abhavad virAjo adhi pUruShaH /
sa jAto aty arichyata pashchAd bhUmim atho puraH //9//
yat puruSheNa haviShA devA yaj~nam atanvata /
vasanto asyAsId AjyaM grIShma idhmaH sharad dhaviH //10//
taM yaj~naM prAvR^iShA praukShan puruShaM jAtam agrashaH /
tena devA ayajanta sAdhyA vasavash cha ye //11//
tasmAd ashvA ajAyanta ye cha ke chobhayAdataH /
gAvo ha jaj~nire tasmAt tasmAj jAtA ajAvayaH //12//
tasmAd yaj~nAt sarvahuta R^ichaH sAmAni jaj~nire /
chhando ha jaj~nire tasmAd yajus tasmAd ajAyata //13//
tasmAd yaj~nAt sarvahutaH saMbhR^itaM pR^iShadAjyam /
pashUMs tAMsh chakre vAyavyAn AraNyA grAmyAsh cha ye //14//
saptAsyAsan paridhayas triH sapta samidhaH kR^itAH /
devA yad yaj~naM tanvAnA abadhnan puruShaM pashum //15//
mUrdhno devasya bR^ihato aMshavaH sapta saptatIH /
rAj~naH somasyAjAyanta jAtasya puruShAd adhi //16//
565

chitrANi sAkaM divi rochanAni sarIsR^ipANi bhuvane javAni /
turmishaM sumatim ichhamAno ahAni gIrbhiH saparyAmi nAkam //1//
suhavam agne kR^ittikA rohiNI chAstu bhadraM mR^igashiraH sham ArdrA /
punarvasU sUnR^itA chAru puShyo bhAnur AshleShA ayanaM maghA me //2//
puNyaM pUrvA phalgunyau chAtra hastash chitrA shivA svAti sukho me astu /
rAdhe vishAkhe suhavAnurAdhA jyeShThA sunakShatram ariShTa mUlam //3//
annaM pUrvA rAsatAM me aShADhA UrjaM devy uttarA A vahantu /
abhijin me rAsatAM puNyam eva shravaNaH shraviShThAH kurvatAM supuShTim //4//
A me mahach chhatabhiShag varIya A me dvayA proShThapadA susharma /
A revatI chAshvayujau bhagaM ma A me rayiM bharaNya A vahantu //5//
566

yAni nakShatrANi divy a1ntarikShe apsu bhUmau yAni nageShu dikShu /
prakalpayaMsh chandramA yAny eti sarvANi mamaitAni shivAni santu //1//
aShTAviMshAni shivAni shagmAni saha yogaM bhajantu me /
yogaM pra padye kShemaM cha kShemaM pra padye yogaM cha namo 'horAtrAbhyAm astu //2//
svastitaM me suprAtaH susAyaM sudivaM sumR^igaM sushakunaM me astu /
suhavam agne svasty a1martyaM gatvA punar AyAbhinandan //3//
anuhavaM parihavaM parivAdaM parikShavam /
sarvair me riktakumbhAn parA tAnt savitaH suva //4//
apapApaM parikShavaM puNyaM bhakShImahi kShavam /
shivA te pApa nAsikAM puNyagash chAbhi mehatAm //5//
imA yA brahmaNas pate viShuchIr vAta Irate /
sadhrIchIr indra tAH kR^itvA mahyaM shivatamAs kR^idhi //6//
svasti no astv abhayaM no astu namo 'horatrAbhyAm astu //7//
567

shAntA dyauH shAntA pR^ithivI shAntam idam urv a1ntarikSham /
shAntA udanvatIr ApaH shAntA naH santv oShadhIH //1//
shAntAni pUrvarUpANi shAntaM no astu kR^itAkR^itam /
shAntaM bhUtaM cha bhavyaM cha sarvam eva sham astu naH //2//
iyaM yA parameShThinI vAg devI brahmasaMshitA /
yayaiva sasR^ije ghoraM tayaiva shAntir astu naH //3//
idaM yat parameShThinaM mano vAM brahmasaMshitam /
yenaiva sasR^ije ghoraM tenaiva shAntir astu naH //4//
imAni yAni pa~nchendriyAni manaHShaShThAni me hR^idi brahmaNA saMshitAni /
yair eva sasR^ije ghoraM tair eva shAntir astu naH //5//
shaM no mitraH shaM varuNaH shaM viShNuH shaM prajApatiH /
shaM na indro bR^ihaspatiH shaM no bhavatv aryamA //6//
shaM no mitraH shaM varuNaH shaM vivasvAM chham antakaH /
utpAtAH pArthivAntarikShAH shaM no divicharA grahAH //7//
shaM no bhUmir vepyamAnA sham ulkA nirhataM cha yat /
shaM gAvo lohitakShIrAH shaM bhUmir ava tIryatIH //8//
nakShatram ulkAbhihataM sham astu naH shaM no 'bhichArAH sham u santu kR^ityAH /
shaM no nikhAtA valgAH sham ulkA deshopasargAH sham u no bhavantu //9//
shaM no grahAsh chAndramasAH sham Adityash cha rAhuNA /
shaM no mR^ityur dhUmaketuH shaM rudrAs tigmatejasaH //10//
shaM rudrAH shaM vasavaH sham AdityAH sham agnayaH /
shaM no maharShayo devAH shaM devAH shaM bR^ihaspatiH //11//
brahma prajApatir dhAtA lokA vedAH saptaR^iShayo 'gnayaH /
tair me kR^itaM svastyayanam indro me sharma yachhatu brahmA me sharma yachhatu /
vishve me devAH sharma yachhantu sarve me devAH sharma yachhantu //12//
yAni kAni chich chhAntAni loke saptaR^iShayo viduH /
sarvANi shaM bhavantu me shaM me astv abhayaM me astu //13//
pR^ithivI shAntir antarikShaM shAntir dyauH shAntir ApaH shAntir oShadhayaH shAntir vanaspatayaH shAntir vishve me devAH shAntiH sarve me devAH shAntiH shAntiH shAntiH
shAntibhiH /
yad iha ghoraM yad iha krUraM yad iha pApaM tach chhAntaM tach chhivaM sarvam eva sham astu naH //14//
568

shaM na indrAgnI bhavatAm avobhiH shaM na indrAvaruNA rAtahavyA /
sham indrAsomA suvitAya shaM yoH shaM na indrApUShaNA vAjasAtau //1//
shaM no bhagaH sham u naH shaMso astu shaM naH puraMdhiH sham u santu rAyaH /
shaM naH satyasya suyamasya shaMsaH shaM no aryamA purujAto astu //2//
shaM no dhAtA sham u dhartA no astu shaM na urUchI bhavatu svadhAbhiH /
shaM rodasI bR^ihatI shaM no adriH shaM no devAnAM suhavAni santu //3//
shaM no agnir jyotiranIko astu shaM no mitrAvaruNAv ashvinA sham /
shaM naH sukR^itAM sukR^itAni santu shaM na iShiro abhi vAtu vAtaH //4//
shaM no dyAvApR^ithivI pUrvahUtau sham antarikShaM dR^ishaye no astu /
shaM na oShadhIr vanino bhavantu shaM no rajasas patir astu jiShNuH //5//
shaM na indro vasubhir devo astu sham Adityebhir varuNaH sushaMsaH /
shaM no rudro rudrebhir jalAShaH shaM nas tvaShTA gnAbhir iha shR^iNotu //6//
shaM naH somo bhavatu brahma shaM naH shaM no grAvANaH sham u santu yaj~nAH /
shaM naH svarUnAM mitayo bhavantu shaM naH prasva1H sham v astu vediH //7//
shaM naH sUrya uruchakShA ud etu shaM no bhavantu pradishash chatasraH /
shaM naH parvatA dhruvayo bhavantu shaM naH sindhavaH sham u santv Apah //8//
shaM no aditir bhavatu vratebhiH shaM no bhavantu marutaH svarkAH /
shaM no viShNuH sham u pUShA no astu shaM no bhavitraM sham v astu vAyuH //9//
shaM no devaH savitA trAyamANaH shaM no bhavantUShaso vibhAtIH /
shaM naH parjanyo bhavatu prajAbhyaH shaM naH kShetrasya patir astu shaMbhuH //10//
569

shaM naH satyasya patayo bhavantu shaM no arvantaH sham u santu gAvaH /
shaM na R^ibhavaH sukR^itaH suhastAH shaM no bhavatu pitaro haveShu //1//
shaM no devA vishvadevA bhavantu shaM sarasvatI saha dhIbhir astu /
sham abhiShAchaH sham u rAtiShAchaH shaM no divyAH pArthivAH shaM no apyAH //2//
shaM no aja ekapAd devo astu sham ahir budhnya1H shaM samudraH /
shaM no apAM napAt perur astu shaM naH pR^iShNir bhavatu devagopA //3//
AdityA rudrA vasavo juShantAm idaM brahma kriyamANaM navIyaH /
sR^iNvantu no divyAH pArthivAso gojAtA uta ye yaj~niyAsaH //4//
ye devAnAm R^itvijo yaj~niyAso manor yajatrA amR^itA R^itaj~nAH /
te no rAsantAm urugAyam adya yUyaM pAta svastibhiH sadA naH //5//
tad astu mitrAvaruNA tad agne shaM yor asmabhyam idam astu shastam /
ashImahi gAdham uta pratiShThAM namo dive bR^ihate sAdanAya //6//
570

uShA apa svasus tamaH saM vartayati vartaniM sujAtatA /
ayA vAjaM devahitaM sanema madema shatahimAH suvIrAH //1//
571

indrasya bAhU sthavirau vR^iShANau chitrA imA vR^iShabhau pArayiShNU /
tau yokShe prathamo yoga Agate yAbhyAM jitam asurANAM sva1r yat //1//
AshuH shishAno vR^iShabho na bhImo ghanAghanaH kShobhaNash charShanInAm /
saMkrandano 'nimiSha ekavIraH shataM senA ajayat sAkam indraH //2//
samkrandanenAnimiSheNa jiShNunAyodhyena dushchyavanena dhR^isnunA /
tad indreNa jayata tat sahadhvaM yudho nara iShuhastena vR^iShNA //3//
sa iShuhastaiH sa niSha~Ngibhir vashI saMsraShTA sa yudha indro gaNena /
saMsR^iShTajit somapA bAhushardhy ùgradhanvA pratihitAbhir astA //4//
balavij~nAyaH sthaviraH pravIraH sahasvAn vAjI sahamAna ugraH /
abhivIro abhiShatvA sahojij jaitram indra ratham A tiShTha govidam //5//
imaM vIram anu harShadhvam ugram indraM sakhAyo anu saM rabhadhvam /
grAmajitaM gojitaM vajrabAhuM jayantam ajma pramR^iNantam ojasA //6//
abhi gotrANi sahasA gAhamAno 'dAya ugraH shatamanyur indraH /
dushchyavanaH pR^itanAShAD ayodhyo3 'smAkaM senA avatu pra yutsu //7//
bR^ihaspate pari dIyA rathena rakShohAmitrAm apabAdhamAnaH /
prabha~njaM chhatrUn pramR^iNann amitrAn asmAkam edhy avitA tanUnAm //8//
indra eShAM netA bR^ihaspatir dakShiNA yaj~naH pura etu somaH /
devasenAnAm abhibha~njatInAM jayantInAM maruto yantu madhye //9//
indrasya vR^iShNo varuNasya rAj~no AdityAnAM marutAM shardha ugram /
mahAmanasAM bhuvanachyavAnAM ghoSho devAnAM jayatAm ud asthAt //10//
asmAkam indraH samR^iteShu dhvajeShv asmAkaSh yA iShavas tA jayantu /
asmAkaM vIrA uttare bhavantv asmAn devAso 'vatA haveShu //11//
572

idam uchchhreyo 'vasAnam AgAM shive me dyAvApR^ithivI abhUtAm /
asapatnAH pradisho me bhavantu na vai tvA dviShmo abhayaM no astu //1//
573

yata indra bhayAmahe tato no abhayaM kR^idhi /
maghavaM chhagdhi tava tvaM na Utibhir vi dviSho vi mR^idho jahi //1//
indraM vayam anUrAdhaM havAmahe 'nu rAdhyAsma dvipadA chatuShpadA /
mA naH senA araruShIr upa gur viShUchir indra druho vi nAshaya //2//
indras trAtota vR^itrahA parasphAno vareNyaH /
sa rakShitA charamataH sa madhyataH sa pashchAt sa purastAn no astu //3//
uruM no lokam anu neShi vidvAnt sva1r yaj jyotir abhayaM svasti /
ugrA ta indra sthavirasya bAhU upa kShayema sharaNA bR^ihantA //4//
abhayaM naH karaty antarikSham abhayaM dyAvApR^ithivI ubhe ime /
abhayaM pashchAd abhayaM purastAd uttarAd adharAd abhayaM no astu //5//
abhayaM mitrAd abhayam amitrAd abhayaM j~nAtAd abhayaM puro yaH /
abhayaM naktam abhayaM divA naH sarvA AshA mama mitraM bhavantu //6//
574

asapatnaM purastAt pashchAn no abhayaM kR^itam /
savitA mA dakShiNata uttarAn mA shachIpatiH //1//
divo mAdityA rakShatu bhUmyA rakShantv agnayaH /
indrAgnI rakShatAM mA purastAd ashvinAv abhitaH sharma yachhatAm /
tirashchIn aghnyA rakShatu jAtavedA bhUtakR^ito me sarvataH santu varma //2//
575

agnir mA pAtu vasubhiH purastAt tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //1//
vAyur mAntarikShenaitasyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //2//
somo mA rudrair dakShiNAyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //3//
varuNo mAdityair etasyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnam pari dade svAha //4//
sUryo mA dyAvApR^ithivIbhyAM pratIchyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //5//
Apo mauShadhImatIr etasyA dishaH pAntu tAsu krame tAsu shraye tAM puraM praimi /
tA mA rakShantu tA mA gopAyantu tAbhya AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //6//
vishvakarmA mA saptaR^iShibhir udIchyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //7//
indro mA marutvAn etasyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //8//
prajApatir mA prajananavAnt saha pratiShThayA dhruvAyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //9//
bR^ihaspatir mA vishvair devair UrdhvAyA dishaH pAtu tasmin krame tasmiM chhraye tAM puraM praimi /
sa mA rakShatu sa mA gopAyatu tasmA AtmAnaM pari dade svAha //10//
576

agniM te vasuvantam R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyavaH prAchyA disho 'bhidAsAn //1//
vAyuM te3 'ntarikShavantam R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyavaH etasyA disho 'bhidAsAn //2//
somaM te rudravantam R^ichhantu /
ye mA 'ghAyavo dakShiNAyA disho 'bhidAsAn //3//
varuNaM ta Adityavantam R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyava etasyA disho 'bhidAsAn //4//
sUryaM te dyAvApR^ithivIvantam R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyava pratIchyAH disho 'bhidAsAn //5//
apas ta oShadhImatIr R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyava etasyA disho 'bhidAsAn //6//
vishvakarmANaM te saptaR^iShivantam R^ichhantu /
ye mA 'ghAyava udIchyA disho 'bhidAsAn //7//
indraM te marutvantam R^ichhantu /
ye mA 'ghAyava etasyA disho 'bhidAsAn //8//
prajApatiM te prajananavantam R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyavo dhruvAyA disho 'bhidAsAn //9//
bR^ihaspatiM te vishvadevavantam R^ichhantu /
ye mAghAyava UrdhvAyA disho 'bhidAsAn //10//
577

mitraH pR^ithivyod akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //1//
vAyur antarikSheNod akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //2//
sUryo divod akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //3//
chandramA nakShatrair ud akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //4//
soma oShadhIbhir ud akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //5//
yaj~no dakShiNAbhir ud akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //6//
samudro nadIbhir ud akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //7//
brahma brahmachAribhir ud akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //8//
indro vIrye3Nod akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //9//
devA amR^itenod akrAmaMs tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //10//
prajApatiH prajAbhir ud akrAmat tAM puraM pra NayAmi vaH /
tAm A vishata tAM pra vishata sA vaH sharma cha varma cha yachhatu //11//
578

apa nyadhuH pauruSheyaM vadhaM yam indrAgnI dhAtA savitA bR^ihaspatiH /
somo rAjA varuNo ashvinA yamaH pUShAsmAn pari pAtu mR^ityoH //1//
yAni chakAra bhuvanasya yas patiH prajApatir mAtarishvA prajAbhyaH /
pradisho yAni vasate dishash cha tAni me varmANi bahulAni santu //2//
yat te tanUShv anahyanta devA dyurAjayo dehinaH /
indro yach chakre varma tad asmAn pAtu vishvataH //3//
varma me dyAvApR^ithivI varmAhar varma sUryaH /
varma me vishve devAH kran mA mA prApat pratIchikA //4//
579

gAyatry ù1ShNig anuShTub bR^ihatI pa~Nktis triShTub jagatyai //1//
580

A~NgirasAnAm AdyaiH pa~nchAnuvAkaiH svAhA //1//
ShaShThAya svAhA //2//
saptamAShTamAbhyAM svAhA //3//
nIlanakhebhyaH svAhA //4//
haritebhyaH svAhA //5//
kShudrebhyaH svAhA //6//
paryAyikebhyaH svAhA //7//
prathamebhyaH sha~NkhebhyaH svAhA //8//
dvitIyebhyaH sha~NkhebhyaH svAhA //9//
tR^itIyebhyaH sha~NkhebhyaH svAhA //10//
upottamebhyaH svAhA //11//
uttamebhyaH svAhA //12//
uttarebhyaH svAhA //13//
R^iShibhyaH svAhA //14//
shikhibhyaH svAhA //15//
gaNebhyaH svAhA //16//
mahAgaNebhyaH svAhA //17//
sarvebhyo '~Ngirobhyo vidagaNebhyaH svAhA //18//
pR^ithakShahasrAbhyAM svAhA //19//
brahmaNe svAhA //20//
brahmajyeShThA sambhR^itA viryANi brahmAgre jyeShThaM divam A tatAna /
bhUtAnAM brahmA prathamota jaj~ne tenArhati brahmaNA spardhituM kaH //21//
581

AtharvaNAnaM chaturR^ichebhyaH svAhA //1//
pa~ncharchebhyaH svAhA //2//
Sha?R^ichebhyaH svAhA //3//
saptarchebhyaH svAhA //4//
aShTarchebhyaH svAhA //5//
navarchebhyaH svAhA //6//
dasharchebhyaH svAhA //7//
ekAdasharchebhyaH svAhA //8//
dvAdasharchebhyaH svAhA //9//
trayodasharchebhyaH svAhA //10//
chaturdasharchebhyah svAhA //11//
pa~nchadasharchebhyaH svAhA //12//
ShoDasharchebhyaH svAhA //13//
saptadasharchebhyaH svAhA //14//
aShTAdasharchebhyaH svAhA //15//
ekonaviMshatiH svAhA //16//
viMshatiH svAhA //17//
mahatkANDAya svAhA //18//
tR^ichebhyaH svAhA //19//
ekarchebhyaH svAhA //20//
kShudrebhyaH svAhA //21//
ekAnR^ichebhyaH svAhA //22//
rohitebhyaH svAhA //23//
sUryAbhyAM svAhA //24//
vrAtyAbhyAM svAhA //25//
prAjApatyAbhyAM svAhA //26//
viShAsahyai svAhA //27//
ma~NgalikebhyaH svAhA //28//
brahmaNe svAhA //29//
brahmajyeShThA saMbhR^itA vIryANi brahmAgre jyeShThaM divam A tatAna /
bhUtAnAM brahmA prathamota jaj~ne tenArhati brahmaNA spardhituM kaH //30//
582

yena devaM savitAraM pari devA adhArayan /
tenemaM brahmaNas pate pari rAShTrAya dhattana //1//
parImam indram AyuShe mahe kShatrAya dhattana /
yathainaM jarase nayAj jyok kShatre 'dhi jAgarat //2//
parImam indram AyuShe mahe shrotrAya dhattana /
yathainaM jarase nayAj jyok shrotre 'dhi jAgarat //3//
pari dhatta dhatta no varchasemaM jarAmR^ityuM kR^iNuta dIrgham AyuH /
bR^ihaspatiH prAyachhad vAsa etat somAya rAj~ne paridhAtavA u //4//
jarAM su gachha pari dhatsva vAso bhavA gR^iShTInAm abhishastipA u /
shataM cha jIva sharadaH purUchI rAyash cha poSham upasaMvyayasva //5//
parIdaM vAso adhithAH svastaye 'bhUr vApInAm abhishastipA u /
shataM cha jIva sharadaH purUchIr vasUni chArur vi bhajAsi jIvan //6//
yogeyoge tavastaraM vAjevAje havAmahe /
sakhAya indram Utaye //7//
hiraNyavarNo ajaraH suvIro jarAmR^ityuH prajayA saM vishasva /
tad agnir Aha tad u soma Aha bR^ihaspatiH savitA tad indraH //8//
583

ashrAntasya tvA manasA yunajmi prathamasya cha /
utkUlam udvaho bhavoduhya prati dhAvatAt //1//
584

agneH prajAtaM pari yad dhiraNyam amR^itaM dadhre adhi martyeShu /
ya enad veda sa id enam arhati jarAmR^ityur bhavati yo bibharti //1//
yad dhiraNyaM sUryeNa suvarNam prajAvanto manavaH pUrva IShire /
tat tvA chandraM varchasA saM sR^ijaty AyuShmAn bhavati yo bibharti //2//
AyuShe tvA varchase tvaujase cha balAya cha /
yathA hiraNyatejasA vibhAsAsi janAm? anu //3//
yad veda rAjA varuNo veda devo bR^ihaspatiH /
indro yad vR^itrahA veda tat ta AyuShyaM bhuvat tat te varchasyaM bhuvat //4//
585

gobhiSh TvA pAtv R^iShabho vR^iShA tvA pAtu vAjibhiH /
vAyuSh tvA brahmaNA pAtv indras tvA pAtv indriyaiH //1//
somas tvA pAtv oShadhIbhir nakShatraiH pAtu sUryaH /
mAdbhyas tvA chandro vR^itrahA vAtaH prANena rakShatu //2//
tisro divas tisraH pR^ithivIs trINy antarikShANi chaturaH samudrAn /
trivR^itaM stomaM trivR^ita Apa Ahus tAs tvA rakShantu trivR^itA trivR^idbhiH //3//
trIn nAkAMs trIn samudrAMs trIn bradhnAMs trIn vaiShTapAn /
trIn mAtarishvanas trInt sUryAn goptR^In kalpayAmi //4//
ghR^itena tvA sam ukShAmy agne Ajyena vardhayan /
agnesh chandrasya sUryasya mA prANaM mAyino dabhan //5//
mA vaH prANaM mA vo 'pAnaM mA haro mAyino dabhan /
bhrAjanto vishvavedaso devA daivyena dhAvata //6//
prAnenAgniM saM sR^ijati vAtaH prANena saMhitaH /
prANena vishvatomukhaM suryaM devA ajanayan //7//
AyuShAyuHkR^itAM jIvAyuShmAn jIva mA mR^ithAH /
prANenAtmanvatAm jIva mA mR^ityor ud agA vasham //8//
devAnAM nihitaM nidhiM yam indro 'nvavindat pathibhir devayAnaiH /
Apo hiraNyaM jugupus trivR^idbhis tAs tvA rakShantu trivR^itA trivR^idbhiH //9//
trayastriMshad devatAs trINi cha vIryANi priyAyamAnA jugupur apsv antaH /
asmiMsh chandre adhi yad dhiraNyaM tenAyaM kR^iNavad vIryANi //10//
ye devA divy ekAdasha stha te devAso havir idaM juShadhvam //11//
ye devA antarikSha ekAdasha stha te devAso havir idaM juShadhvam //12//
ye devA pR^ithivyAm ekAdasha stha te devAso havir idaM juShadhvam //13//
asapatnaM purastAt pashchAn no abhayaM kR^itam /
savitA mA dakShiNata uttarAn mA shachIpatiH //14//
divo mAdityA rakShantu bhUmyA rakShantv agnayaH /
indrAgnI rakShatAM mA purastAd ashvinAv abhitaH sharma yachhatAm /
tirashchIn aghnyA rakShatu jAtavedA bhUtakR^ito me sarvataH santu varma //15//
586

imam badhnAmi te maNiM dIrghAyutvAya tejase /
darbhaM sapatnadambhanaM dviShatas tapanaM hR^idaH //1//
dviShatas tApayan hR^idaH shatrUNAM tApayan manaH /
durhArdaH sarvAMs tvaM darbha gharma ivAbhInt saMtApayan //2//
gharma ivAbhitapan darbha dviShato nitapan maNe /
hR^idaH sapatnAnAM bhinddhIndra iva virujaM balam //3//
bhinddhi darbha sapatnAnAM hR^idayaH dviShatAM maNe /
udyan tvacham iva bhUmyAH shira eShAM vi pAtaya //4//
bhinddhi darbha sapatnAn me bhinddhi me pR^itanAyataH /
bhinddhi me sarvAn durhArdo bhinddhi me dviShato maNe //5//
chhinddhi darbha sapatnAn me chhinddhi me pR^itanAyataH /
chhinddhi me sarvAn durhArdo chhinddhi me dviShato maNe //6//
vR^ishcha darbha sapatnAn me vR^ishcha me pR^itanAyataH /
vR^ishcha me sarvAn durhArdo vR^ishcha me dviShato maNe //7//
kR^inta darbha sapatnAn me kR^inta me pR^itanAyataH /
kR^inta me sarvAn durhArdo kR^inta me dviShato maNe //8//
piMsha darbha sapatnAn me piMsha me pR^itanAyataH /
piMsha me sarvAn durhArdo piMsha me dviShato maNe //9//
vidhya darbha sapatnAn me vidhya me pR^itanAyataH /
vidhya me sarvAn durhArdo vidhya me dviShato maNe //10//
587

nikSha darbha sapatnAn me nikSha me pR^itanAyataH /
nikSha me sarvAn durhArdo nikSha me dviShato maNe //1//
tR^inddhi darbha sapatnAn me tR^inddhi me pR^itanAyataH /
tR^inddhi me sarvAn durhArdo tR^inddhi me dviShato maNe //2//
runddhi darbha sapatnAn me runddhi me pR^itanAyataH /
runddhi me sarvAn durhArdo runddhi me dviShato maNe //3//
mR^iNa darbha sapatnAn me mR^iNa me pR^itanAyataH /
mR^iNa me sarvAn durhArdo mR^iNa me dviShato maNe //4//
mantha darbha sapatnAn me mantha me pR^itanAyataH /
mantha me sarvAn durhArdo mantha me dviShato maNe //5//
piNDDhi darbha sapatnAn me piNDDhi me pR^itanAyataH /
piNDDhi me sarvAn durhArdo piNDDhi me dviShato maNe //6//
oSha darbha sapatnAn me oSha me pR^itanAyataH /
oSha me sarvAn durhArdo oSha me dviShato maNe //7//
daha darbha sapatnAn me daha me pR^itanAyataH /
daha me sarvAn durhArdo daha me dviShato maNe //8//
jahi darbha sapatnAn me jahi me pR^itanAyataH /
jahi me sarvAn durhArdo jahi me dviShato maNe //9//
588

yat te darbha jarAmR^ityu shataM varmasu varma te /
tenemaM varmiNaM kR^itvA sapatnAM jahi vIryaìH //1//
shataM te darbha varmANi sahasraM vIryANi te /
tam asmai vishve tvAM devA jarase bhartavA aduH //2//
tvAm Ahur devavarma tvAM darbha brahmaNas patim /
tvAm indrasyAhur varma tvaM rAShTrANi rakShasi //3//
sapatnakShayaNaM darbha dviShatas tapanaM hR^idah /
maNiM kShatrasya vardhanaM tanUpAnaM kR^iNomi te //4//
yat samudro abhyakrandat parjanyo vidyutA saha /
tato hiranyayo bindus tato darbho ajAyata //5//
589

audumbareNa maNinA puShTikAmAya vedhasA /
pashUNAM sarveShAM sphAtiM goShThe me savitA karat //1//
yo no agnir gArhapatyaH pashUnAm adhipA asat /
audumbaro vR^iso maNiH saM mA sR^ijatu puShTyA //2//
karIShiNIM phalavatIM svadhAm irAM cha no gR^ihe /
audumbarasya tejasA dhAtA puShTiM dadhAtu me //3//
yad dvipAch cha chatuShpAch cha yAny annAni ye rasAH /
gR^ihNe3 'haM tv eShAM bhUmAnaM bibhrad audumbaraM maNim //4//
puShTiM pashUnAm pari jagrabhAhaM chatuShpadAM dvipadAM yach cha dhAnyam /
payaH pashUnAM rasam oShadhInAM bR^ihaspatiH savitA me ni yachhAt //5//
ahaM pashUnAm adhipA asAni mayi puShTaM puShTapatir dadhAtu /
mahyam audumbaro maNir draviNAni ni yachhatu //6//
upa maudumbaro maNiH prajayA cha dhanena cha /
indreNa jinvito maNir A mAgant saha varchasA //7//
devo maNiH sapatnahA dhanasA dhanasAtaye /
pashor annasya bhUmAnaM gavAM sphAtiM ni yachhatu //8//
yathAgre tvaM vanaspate puShThyA saha jaj~niShe /
evA dhanasya me sphAtim A dadhAtu sarasvatI //9//
A me dhanaM sarasvatI payasphAtiM cha dhAnyam /
sinIvAly upA vahAd ayaM chaudumbaro maNiH //10//
tvaM maNINAm adhipA vR^iShAsi tvayi puShTaM puShTapatir jajAna /
tvayIme vAjA draviNAni sarvaudumbaraH sa tvam asmat sahasvArAd ArAd arAtim amatiM kShudhaM cha //11//
grAmaNIr asi grAmaNIr utthAya abhiShikto 'bhi mA si~ncha varchasA /
tejo 'si tejo mayi dhArayAdhi rayir asi rayiM me dhehi //12//
puShTir asi puShTyA mA sam a~Ngdhi gR^ihamedhI gR^ihapatiM mA kR^iNu /
audumbaraH sa tvam asmAsu dhehi rayiM cha naH sarvavIraM ni yachha rAyas poShAya prati mu~nche ahaM tvAm //13//
ayam audumbaro maNir vIro vIrAya badhyate /
sa naH saniM madhumatIM kR^iNotu rayiM cha naH sarvavIram ni yachhAt //14//
590

shatakANDo dushchyavanaH sahasraparNa uttiraH /
darbho ya ugra oShadhis taM te badhnAmy AyuShe //1//
nAsya keshAn pra vapanti norasi tADam A ghnate /
yasmA achhinnaparNena darbhena sharma yachhati //2//
divi te tUlam oShadhe pR^ithivyAm asi niShThitaH /
tvayA sahasrakANDenAyuH pra vardhayAmahe //3//
tisro divo aty atR^iNat tisra imAH pR^ithivIr uta /
tvayAhaM durhArdo jihvAM ni tR^iNadmi vachAMsi //4//
tvam asi sahamAno 'ham asmi sahasvAn /
ubhau sahasvantau bhUtvA sapatnAn sahiShIvahi //5//
sahasva no abhimAtiM sahasva pR^itanAyataH /
sahasva sarvAn durhArdaH suhArdo me bahUn kR^idhi //6//
darbheNa devajAtena divi ShTambhena shashvad it /
tenAhaM shashvato janAm? asanaM sanavAni cha //7//
priyaM mA darbha kR^iNu brahmarAjanyAbhyAm shUdrAya chAryAya cha /
yasmai cha kAmayAmahe sarvasmai cha vipashyate //8//
yo jAyamAnaH pR^ithivIm adR^iMhad yo astabhnAd antarikShaM divaM cha /
yaM bibhrataM nanu pApmA viveda sa no 'yaM darbho varuNo divA kaH //9//
sapatnahA shatakANDaH sahasvAn oShadhInAM prathamaH saM babhUva /
sa no 'yaM darbhaH pari pAtu vishvatas tena sAkShIya pR^itanAH pR^itanyataH //10//
591

sahasrArghaH shatakANDaH payasvAn apAm agnir vIrudhAM rAjasUyam /
sa no 'yaM darbhaH pari pAtu vishvato devo maNir AyuShA saM sR^ijAti naH //1//
ghR^itAd ullupto madhumAn payasvAn bhUmidR^iMho 'chyutash chyAvayiShNuH /
nudant sapatnAn adharAMsh cha kR^iNvan darbhA roha mahatAm indriyeNa //2//
tvaM bhUmim aty eShy ojasA tvaM vedyAM sIdasi chArur adhvare /
tvAM pavitram R^iShayo 'bharanta tvaM punIhi duritAny asmat //3//
tIkShNo rAjA viShAsahI rakShohA vishvacharShaNiH /
ojo devAnAM balam ugram etat taM te badhnAmi jarase svastaye //4//
darbheNa tvaM kR^iNavad vIryANi darbhaM bibhrad AtmanA mA vyathiShThAH /
atiShThAya varchasAdhAnyAnt sUrya ivA bhAhi pradishash chatasraH //5//
592

jA~NgiDo 'si ja~NgiDo rakShitAsi ja~NgidaH /
dvipAch chatuShpAd asmAkaM sarvaM rakShatu ja~NgidaH //1//
yA gR^itsyas tripa~nchAshIH shataM kR^ityAkR^itash cha ye /
sarvAn vinaktu tejaso 'rasAM ja~Ngidas karat //2//
arasaM kR^itrimaM nAdam arasAH sapta visrasaH /
apeto ja~NgiDAmatim iShum asteva shAtaya //3//
kR^ityAdUShaNa evAyam atho arAtidUShaNah /
atho sahasvA~n ja~NgiDaH pra na AyumShi tAriShat //4//
sa ja~NgiDasya mahimA pari NaH pAtu vishvataH /
viShkandhaM yena sAsaha saMskandham oja ojasA //5//
triSh TvA devA ajanayan niShThitaM bhUmyAm adhi /
tam u tvA~NgirA iti brAhmaNAH pUrvyA viduH //6//
na tvA pUrvA oShadhayo na tvA taranti yA navAH /
vibAdha ugro ja~NgiDaH paripANaH suma~NgalaH //7//
athopadAna bhagavo jA~NgiDAmitavIrya /
purA ta ugrA grasata upendro vIryaM dadau //8//
ugra it te vanaspata indra ojmAnam A dadhau /
amIvAH sarvAsh chAtayaM jahi rakShAMsy oShadhe //9//
AsharIkaM visharIkaM balAsaM pR^iShTyAmayam /
takmAnaM vishvashAradam arasAM ja~NgiDas karat //10//
593

indrasya nAma gR^ihNanta R^isayo ja~NgidaM daduH /
devA yaM chakrur bheShajam agre viShkandhadUShaNam //1//
sa no rakShatu ja~NgiDo dhanapAlo dhaneva /
devA yaM chakrur brAhmaNAH paripANam arAtiham //2//
durhArdaH saMghoraM chakShuH pApakR^itvAnam Agamam /
tAMs tvaM sahasrachakSho pratIbodhena nAshaya paripANo 'si ja~NgiDaH //3//
pari mA divaH pari mA pR^ithivyAH pary antarikShAt pari mA vIrudbhyaH /
pari mA bhUtAt pari mota bhavyAd dishodisho ja~NgiDaH pAtv asmAn //4//
ya R^iShNavo devakR^itA ya uto vavR^ite 'nyaH /
sarvAM stAn vishvabheShajo 'rasAM ja~NgiDas karat //5//
594

shatavAro anInashad yakShmAn rakShAMsi tejasA /
Arohan varchasA saha maNir durNAmachAtanaH //1//
shR^i~NgAbhyAM rakSho nudate mUlena yAtudhAnyaH /
madhyena yakShmaM bAdhate nainaM pApmAti tatrati //2//
ye yakShmAso arbhakA mahAnto ye cha shabdinaH /
sarvAM durNAmahA maNiH shatavAro anInashat //3//
shataM vIrAn ajanayach chhataM yakShmAn apAvapat /
durNAmnaH sarvAn hatvAva rakShAMsi dhUnute //4//
hiraNyashR^i~Nga R^iShabhaH shAtavAro ayaM maNiH /
durNAmnaH sarvAMs tR^idhvAva rakShAMsy akramIt //5//
shatam ahaM durNAmnInAM gandharvApsarasAM shatam /
shatam shashvanvatInAM shatavAreNa vAraye //6//
595

idaM varcho agninA dattam Agan bhargo yashaH saha ojo vayo balam /
trayastriMshad yAni cha vIryANi tAny agniH pra dadAtu me //1//
varcha A dhehi me tanvA3M saha ojo vayo balam /
indriyAya tvA karmaNe vIryAya prati gR^ihNAmi shatashAradAya //2//
Urje tvA balAya tvaujase sahase tvA /
abhibhUyAya tvA rAShTrabhR^ityAya pary UhAmi shatashAradAya //3//
R^itubhyaSh TvArtavebhyo mAdbhyaH saMvatsarebhyaH /
dhAtre vidhAtre samR^idhe bhUtasya pataye yaje //4//
596

na taM yakShmA arundhate nainaM shapatho ashnute /
yaM bheShajasya gulguloH surabhir gandho ashnute //1//
viShva~nchas tasmAd yakShmA mR^igA ashvA iverate /
yad gulgulu saindhavaM yad vApy asi samudriyam //2//
ubhayor agrabhaM nAmAsmA ariShTatAtaye //3//
597

aitu devas trAyamANaH kuShTho himavatas pari /
takmAnaM sarvaM nAshaya sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyah //1//
trINi te kuShTha nAmAni nadyamAro nadyAriShaH /
nadyAyaM puruso riShat /
yasmai paribravImi tvA sAyaMprAtar atho divA //2//
jIvalA nAma te mAtA jIvanto nAma te pitA /
nadyAyaM puruSho riShat /
yasmai paribravImi tvA sAyaMprAtar atho divA //3//
uttamo asy oShadhInAm anaDvAn jagatAm iva vyAghraH shvapadAm iva /
nadyAyaM puruSho riShat /
yasmai paribravImi tvA sAyaMprAtar atho divA //4//
triH shAmbubhyo a~Ngirebhyas trir Adityebhyas pari /
trir jato vishvadevebhyaH /
sa kuShTho vishvabheShajaH sAkaM somena tiShThati /
takmAnaM sarvaM nAshaya sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyaH //5//
ashvattho devasadanas tR^itIyasyAm ito divi /
tatrAmR^itasya chakShaNaM tataH kuShTho ajAyata /
sa kuShTho vishvabheShajaH sAkaM somena tiShThati /
takmAnaM sarvaM nAshaya sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyaH //6//
hiraNyayI naur acharad dhiraNyabandhanA divi /
tatrAmR^itasya chakShaNaM tataH kuShTho ajAyata /
sa kuShTho vishvabheShajah sAkaM somena tiShThati /
takmAnaM sarvaM nAshaya sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyaH //7//
yatra nAvaprabhraMshanaM yatra himavataH shiraH /
tatrAmR^itasya chakShaNaM tataH kuShTho ajAyata /
sa kuShTho vishvabheShajaH sAkaM somena tiShThati /
takmAnaM sarvaM nAshaya sarvAsh cha yAtudhAnyah //8//
yaM tvA veda pUrva ikShvAko yaM vA tvA kuShTha kAmyaH /
yaM vA vaso yam Atsyas tenAsi vishvabheShajaH //9//
shIrShashokaM tR^itIyakaM sadaMdir yash cha hAyanaH /
takmAnaM vishvadhAvIryAdharA~nchaM parA suva //10//
598

yan me chhidraM manaso yach cha vAchaH sarasvatI manyumantaM jagAma /
vishvais tad devaiH saha saMvidAnaH saM dadhAtu bR^ihaspatih //1//
mA na Apo medhAM mA brahma pra mathiShTana /
suShyadA yUyaM syandadhvam upahUto 'haM sumedhA varchasvI //2//
mA no medhAM mA no dIkShAM mA no hiMsiShTaM yat tapaH /
shivA naH shaM santv AyuShe shivA bhavantu mAtaraH //3//
yA naH pIparad ashvinA jyotiShmatI tamas tiraH /
tAm asme rAsatAm iSham //4//
599

bhadram ichhanta R^iShayaH svarvidas tapo dIkShAm upaniShedur agre /
tato rAShTraM balam ojash cha jAtaM tad asmai devA upasaMnamantu //1//
600

brahma hotA brahma yaj~nA brahmaNA svaravo mitAH /
adhvaryur brahmaNo jAto brahmaNo 'ntarhitaM haviH //1//
brahma srucho ghR^itavatIr brahmaNA vedir uddhitA /
brahma yaj~nasya tattvaM cha R^itvijo ye haviShkR^itaH /
shamitAya svAhA //2//
aMhomuche pra bhare manIShAm A sutrAvNe sumatim AvR^iNAnaH /
imam indra prati havyaM gR^ibhAya satyAH santu yajamAnasya kAmAH //3//
amhomuchaM vrShabhaM yaj~niyAnAM virAjantaM prathamam adhvarANam /
apAM napAtam ashvinA huve dhiya indriyeNa ta indriyaM dattam ojaH //4//
601

yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
agnir mA tatra nayatv agnir medhA dadhAtu me /
agnaye svAhA //1//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
vAyur mA tatra nayatu vAyuH praNAn dadhAtu me vAyave svAhA //2//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
sUryo mA tatra nayatu chakShuH sUryo dadhAtu me /
sUryAya svAhA //3//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
chandro mA tatra nayatu manash chandro dadhAtu me /
chandrAya svAhA //4//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
somo mA tatra nayatu payaH somo dadhAtu me /
somAya svAhA //5//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
indro mA tatra nayatu balam indro dadhAtu me /
indrAya svAhA //6//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
Apo mA tatra nayatv amR^itam mopa tiShThatu /
adbhyaH svAhA //7//
yatra brahmavido yAnti dIkShayA tapasA saha /
brahmA mA tatra nayatu brahmA brahma dadhAtu me /
brahmaNe svAhA //8//
602

AyuSho 'si prataraNaM vipraM bheShajam uchyase /
tad A~njana tvaM shaMtAte sham Apo abhayaM kR^itam //1//
yo harimA jAyAnyo '~Ngabhedo viShalpakaH /
sarvaM te yakShmam a~Ngebhyo bahir nir hantv A~njanam //2//
A~njanaM pR^ithivyAM jAtaM bhadraM puruShajIvanam /
kR^iNotv apramAyukaM rathajUtim anAgasam //3//
prANa prANaM trAyasvAso asave mR^iDa /
nirR^ite nirR^ityA naH pAshebhyo mu~ncha //4//
sindhor garbho 'si vidyutaM puShpam /
vAtaH prANaH sUryash chakShur divas payaH //5//
devA~njana traikakuda pari mA pAhi vishvataH /
na tvA taranty oShadhayo bAhyAH parvatIyA uta //6//
vIdaM madhyam avAsR^ipad rakShohAmIvachAtanaH /
amIvAH sarvAsh chAtayan nAshayad abhibhA itaH //7//
bahv ì3daM rAjan varuNAnR^itam Aha pUruShaH /
tasmAt sahasravIrya mu~ncha naH pary aMhasaH //8//
yad Apo aghnyA iti varuNeti yad Uchima /
tasmAt sahasravIrya mu~ncha naH pary aMhasaH //9//
mitrash cha tvA varuNash chAnupreyatur A~njana /
tau tvAnugatya dUraM bhogAya punar ohatuH //10//
603

R^iNAd R^iNam iva saM naya kR^ityAM kR^ityAkR^ito gR^iham /
chakShurmantrasya durhArdaH pR^iShTIr api shR^iNA~njana //1//
yad asmAsu duShvapnyaM yad goShu yach cha no gR^ihe /
anAmagas taM cha durhArdaH priyaH prati mu~nchatAm //2//
apAm Urja ojaso vAvR^idhAnam agner jAtam adhi jAtavedasaH /
chaturvIraM parvatIyaM yad A~njanaM dishaH pradishaH karad ich chhivAs te //3//
chaturvIraM badhyata A~njanaM te sarvA disho abhayAs te bhavantu /
dhruvas tiShThAsi saviteva chArya imA visho abhi harantu te balim //4//
AkShvaikaM maNim ekaM krNuShva snAhy ekenA pibaikam eShAm /
chaturvIraM nairR^itebhyash chaturbhyo grAhyA bandhebhyaH pari pAtv asmAn //5//
agnir mAgninAvatu prANAyApAnAyAyuShe varchasa ojase /
tejase svastaye subhUtaye svAhA //6//
indro mendriyeNAvatu prANAyApAnAyAyuShe varchasa ojase /
tejase svastaye subhUtaye svAhA //7//
somo mA saumyenAvatu prANAyApAnAyAyuShe varchasa ojase /
tejase svastaye subhUtaye svAhA //8//
bhago ma bhagenAvatu prANAyApAnAyAyuShe varchasa ojase /
tejase svastaye subhUtaye svAhA //9//
maruto mA gaNair avantu prANAyApAnAyuShe varchasa ojase tejase /
svastaye subhUtaye svAhA //10//
604

prajApatiSh TvA badhnAt prathamam astR^itaM vIryAya kam /
tat te badhnAmy AyuShe varchasa ojase cha balAya chAstR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //1//
Urdhvas tiShThatu rakShann apramAdam astR^itemam mA tvA dabhan paNayo yAtudhAnAH /
indra iva dasyUn ava dhUnuShva pR^itanyataH sarvAM chhatrUn vi ShahasvAstR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //2//
shataM cha na praharanto nighnanto na tastire /
tasminn indraH pary adatta chakShuH prANam atho balam astR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //3//
indrasya tvA varmaNA pari dhApayAmo yo devAnAm adhirAjo babhUva /
punas tvA devAH pra Nayantu sarve 'stR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //4//
asmin maNAv ekashataM vIryANi sahasraM prANA asminn astR^ite /
vyAghraH shatrUn abhi tiShTha sarvAn yas tvA pR^itanyAd adharaH so astv astR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //5//
ghR^itAd ullupto madhumAn payasvAnt sahasraprANaH shatayonir vayodhAH /
shambhUsh cha mayobhUsh chorjasvAMsh cha payasvAMsh chAstR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //6//
yathA tvam uttaro 'so asapatnaH sapatnahA /
sajAtAnAm asad vashI tathA tvA savitA karad astR^itas tvAbhi rakShatu //7//
605

A rAtri pArthivaM rajaH pitur aprAyi dhAmabhiH /
divaH sadAMsi bR^ihatI vi tiShThasa A tveShaM vartate tamaH //1//
na yasyAH pAraM dadR^ishe na yoyuvad vishvam asyAM ni vishate yad ejAti /
ariShTAsas ta urvi tamasvati rAtri pAram ashImahi bhadre pAram ashImahi //2//
ye te rAtri nR^ichakShaso draShTAro navatIr nava /
ashItiH santy aShTA uto te sapta saptatiH //3//
ShaShTish cha ShaT cha revati pa~nchAshat pa~ncha sumnayi /
chatvArash chatvAriMshach cha trayas triMshach cha vAjini //4//
dvau cha te viMshatish cha te rAtry ekAdashAvamAH /
tebhir no adya pAyubhir nu pAhi duhitar divaH //5//
rakShA mAkir no adhashaMsa Ishata mA no duHshaMsa Ishata /
mA no adya gavAM steno mAvInAM vR^ika Ishata //6//
mAshvAnAM bhadre taskaro mA nR^iNAM yAtudhAnyaH /
paramebhiH pathibhiH steno dhAvatu taskaraH /
pareNa datvatI rajjuH pareNAghayur arShatu //7//
adha rAtri tR^iShTadhUmam ashIrShANam ahiM kR^iNu /
hanU vR^ikasya jambhayA stenaM drupade jahi //8//
tvayi rAtri vasAmasi svapiShyAmasi jAgR^ihi /
gobhyo naH sharma yachhAshvebhyaH puruShebhyaH //9//
606

atho yAni cha yasmA ha yAni chAntaH parINahi /
tAni te pari dadmasi //1//
rAtri mAtar uShase naH pari dehi /
uSho no ahne pari dadAtv ahas tubhyaM vibhAvari //2//
yat kiM chedaM patayati yat kiM chedaM sarIsR^ipam /
yat kiM cha parvatAyAsatvaM tasmAt tvaM rAtri pAhi naH //3//
sA pashchAt pAhi sA puraH sottarAd adharAd uta /
gopAya no vibhAvari stotAras ta iha smasi //4//
ye rAtrim anutiShThanti ye cha bhUteShu jAgrati /
pashUn ye sarvAn rakShanti te na Atmasu jAgrati te naH pashuShu jAgrati //5//
veda vai rAtri te nAma ghR^itAchI nAma vA asi /
tAM tvAM bharadvAjo veda sA no vitte 'dhi jAgrati //6//
607

iShirA yoShA yuvatir damUnA rAtrI devasya savitur bhagasya /
ashvakShabhA suhavA saMbhR^itashrIr A paprau dyAvApR^ithivI mahitvA //1//
ati vishvAny aruhad gambhiro varShiShTham aruhanta shraviShThAH /
ushatI rAtry anu sA bhadrAbhi tiShThate mitra iva svadhAbhiH //2//
varye vande subhage sujAta Ajagan rAtri sumanA iha syAm /
asmAMs trAyasva naryANi jatA atho yAni gavyAni puShThyA //3//
siMhasya rAtry ushatI pIMShasya vyAghrasya dvIpino varcha A dade /
ashvasya bradhnaM puruShasya mAyuM puru rUpANi kR^iNuShe vibhAtI //4//
shivAM rAtrim anusUryaM cha himasya mAtA suhavA no astu /
asya stomasya subhage ni bodha yena tvA vande vishvAsu dikShu //5//
stomasya no vibhAvari rAtri rAjeva joShase /
asAma sarvavIrA bhavAma sarvavedaso vyuchhantIr anUShasaH //6//
shamyA ha nAma dadhiShe mama dipsanti ye dhanA /
rAtrIhi tAn asutapA ya steno na vidyate yat punar na vidyate //7//
bhadrAsi rAtri chamaso na viShTo viShva~N gorUpaM yuvatir bibharShi /
chakShuShmatI me ushatI vapUmShi prati tvaM divyA na kShAm amukthAH //8//
yo adya stena Ayaty aghAyur martyo ripuH /
rAtrI tasya pratItya pra grIvAH pra shiro hanat //9//
pra pAdau na yathAyati pra hastau na yathAshiShat /
yo malimlur upAyati sa saMpiShTo apAyati /
apAyati svapAyati shuShke sthANAv apAyati //10//
608

adha rAtri tR^iShTadhUmam ashIrShANam ahiM kR^iNu /
akShau vR^ikasya nir jahyAs tena taM drupade jahi //1//
ye te rAtry anaDvAhas tIkShNashR^i~NgAH svAshavaH /
tebhir no adya pArayAti durgANi vishvahA //2//
rAtriMrAtrim ariShyantas tarema tanvA vayam /
gambhIram aplavA iva na tareyur arAtayaH //3//
yathA shAmyAkaH prapatann apavAn nAnuvidyate /
evA rAtri pra pAtaya yo asmA.N abhyaghAyati //4//
apa stenaM vAsayo goajam uta taskaram /
atho yo arvataH shiro 'bhidhAya ninIShati //5//
yad adya rAtri subhage vibhajanty ayo vasu /
yad etad asmAn bhojaya yathed anyAn upAyasi //6//
uShase naH pari dehi sarvAn rAtry anAgasaH /
uShA no ahne A bhajAd ahas tubhyaM vibhAvari //7//
609

ayuto 'ham ayuto ma AtmAyutaM me chakShur ayutaM me shrotram /
ayuto me prANo 'yuto me 'pAno 'yuto me vyAno 'yuto 'haM sarvaH //1//
devasya tvA savituH prasave 'shvinor bAhubhyAM pUShNo hastAbhyAM prasUta A rabhe //2//
610

kAmas tad agre sam avartata manaso retaH prathamaM yad AsIt /
sa kAma kAmena bR^ihatA sayonI rAyas poShaM yajamAnAya dhehi //1//
tvaM kAma sahasAsi pratiShThito vibhur vibhAvA sakha A sakhIyate /
tvam ugraH pR^itanAsu sasahiH saha ojo yajamAnAya dhehi //2//
dUrAch chakamAnAya pratipANAyAkShaye /
AsmA ashR^iNvann AshAH kAmenAjanayant svaH //3//
kAmena mA kAma Agan hR^idayAd dhR^idayaM pari /
yad amIShAm ado manas tad aitUpa mAm iha //4//
yat kAma kAmayamAnA idaM kR^iNmasi te haviH /
tan naH sarvaM sam R^idhyatAm athaitasya haviSho vIhi svAhA //5//
611

kAlo ashvo vahati saptarashmiH sahasrAkSho ajaro bhUriretAH /
tam A rohanti kavayo vipashchitas tasya chakrA bhuvanAni vishvA //1//
sapta chakrAn vahati kAla eSha saptAsya nAbhIr amR^itaM nv akShaH /
sa imA vishvA bhuvanAny a~njat kAlaH sa Iyate prathamo nu devaH //2//
pUrNaH kumbho 'dhi kAla Ahitas taM vai pashyAmo bahudhA nu santam /
sa imA vishvA bhuvanAni pratya~N kAlaM tam AhuH parame vyoman //3//
sa eva saM bhuvanAny Abharat sa eva saM bhuvanAni pary ait /
pitA sann abhavat putra eShAM tasmAd vai nAnyat param asti tejaH //4//
kAlo 'mUM divam ajanayat kAla imAH pR^ithivIr uta /
kAle ha bhUtaM bhavyaM cheShitaM ha vi tiShThate //5//
kAlo bhUtim asR^ijata kAle tapati sUryaH /
kAle ha vishvA bhUtAni kAle chakShur vi pashyati //6//
kAle manaH kAle prANaH kAle nAma samAhitam /
kAlena sarvA nandanty Agatena prajA imAH //7//
kAle tapaH kAle jyeShTham kAle brahma samAhitam /
kAlo ha sarvasyeshvaro yaH pitAsIt prajApateH //8//
teneShitaM tena jAtaM tad u tasmin pratiShThitam /
kAlo ha brahma bhUtvA bibharti parameShThinam //9//
kAlah prajA asR^ijata kAlo agre prajApatim /
svayaMbhUH kashyapaH kAlAt tapaH kAlAd ajAyata //10//
612

kAlAd ApaH sam abhavan kAlAd brahma tapo dishaH /
kAlenod eti sUryaH kAle ni vishate punaH //1//
kAlena vAtaH pavate kAlena pR^ithivI mahI /
dyaur mahI kAla AhitA //2//
kAlo ha bhUtaM bhavyaM cha putro ajanayat purA /
kAlAd R^ichaH sam abhavan yajuH kAlAd ajAyata //3//
kAlo yaj~naM sam airayad devebhyo bhAgam akShitam /
kAle gandharvApsarasaH kAle lokAH pratiShThitAH //4//
kAle 'yam a~NgirA devo 'tharvA chAdhi tiShThataH /
imaM cha lokaM paramaM cha lokaM puNyAMsh cha lokAn vidhR^itIsh cha puNyAH /
sarvAMl lokAn abhijitya brahmaNA kAlaH sa Iyate paramo nu devaH //5//
613

rAtriMrAtrim aprayAtaM bharanto 'shvAyeva tiShThate ghAsam asmai /
rAyas poSheNa sam iShA madanto mA te agne prativeshA riShAma //1//
yA te vasor vAta iShuH sA ta eShA tayA no mR^iDa /
rAyas poSheNa sam iShA madanto mA te agne prativeshA riShAma //2//
sAyaMsAyaM gR^ihapatir no agniH prAtaHprAtaH saumanasasya dAtA /
vasorvasor vasudAna edhi vayaM tvendhAnAs tanvaM puShema //3//
prAtaHprAtar gR^ihapatir no agniH sAyaMsAyaM saumanasasya dAtA /
vasorvasor vasudAna edhIndhAnAs tvA shataMhimA R^idhema //4//
apashchA dagdhAnnasya bhUyAsam /
annAdAyAnnapataye rudrAya namo agnaye /
sabhyaH sabhAM me pAhi ye cha sabhyAH sabhAsadaH //5//
tvAm indrA puruhUta vishvam Ayur vy ashnavan /
aharahar balim it te haranto 'shvAyeva tiShThate ghAsam agne //6//
614

yamasya lokAd adhy A babhUvitha pramadA martyAn pra yunakShi dhIraH /
ekAkinA sarathaM yAsi vidvAnt svapnaM mimAno asurasya yonau //1//
bandhas tvAgre vishvachayA apashyat purA rAtryA janitor eke ahni /
tataH svapnedam adhy A babhUvitha bhiShagbhyo rUpam apagUhamAnaH //2//
bR^ihadgAvAsurebhyo 'dhi devAn upAvartata mahimAnam ichhan /
tasmai svapnAya dadhur AdhipatyaM trayastriMshAsaH svar AnashAnAH //3//
naitAM viduH pitaro nota devA yeShAM jalpish charaty antaredam /
trite svapnam adadhur Aptye nara AdityAso varuNenAnushiShTAH //4//
yasya krUram abhajanta duShkR^ito 'svapnena sukR^itaH puNyam AyuH /
svar madasi parameNa bandhunA tapyamAnasya manaso 'dhi jaj~niShe //5//
vidma te sarvAH parijAH purastAd vidma svapna yo adhipA ihA te /
yashashvino no yashaseha pAhy ArAd dviShebhir apa yAhi dUram //6//
615

yathA kalAM yathA shaphaM yathA rNaM samnayanti /
evA duShvapnyaM sarvam apriye saM nayAmasi //1//
saM rAjAno aguH sam R^iNAmy aguH saM kuShThA aguH saM kalA aguH /
sam asmAsu yad duShvapnyaM nir dviShate duShvapnyaM suvAma //2//
devAnAM patnInAM garbha yamasya kara yo bhadraH svapna /
sa mama yaH pApas tad dviShate pra hiNmaH /
mA tR^iShTAnAm asi kR^iShNashakuner mukham //3//
taM tvA svapna tathA saM vidma sa tvaM svapnAshva iva kAyam ashva iva nInAham /
anAsmAkaM devapIyuM piyAruM vapa yad asmAsu duShvapnyaM yad goShu yach cha no gR^ihe //4//
anAsmAkas tad devapIyuH piyArur niShkam iva prati mu~nchatAm /
navAratnIn apamayA asmAkaM tataH pari /
duShvapnyaM sarvaM dviShate nir dayAmasi //5//
616

ghR^itasya jUtiH samanA sadevA saMvatsaraM haviShA vardhayantI /
shrotraM chakShuH prANo 'chhinno no astv achhinnA vayam AyuSho varchasaH //1//
upAsmAn prANo hvayatAm upa prANaM havAmahe /
varcho jagrAha pR^ithivy a1ntarikShaM varchaH somo bR^ihaspatir vidhattA //2//
varchaso dyAvApR^ithivI saMgrahaNI babhUvathur varcho gR^ihItvA pR^ithivIm anu saM charema /
yashasam gAvo gopatim upa tiShThanty AyatIr yasho gR^ihItvA pR^ithivIm anu saM charema //3//
vrajaM kR^iNudhvaM sa hi vo nR^ipANo varmA sIvyadhvaM bahulA pR^ithUni /
puraH kR^iNudhvam AyasIr adhR^iShTA mA vaH susroch chamaso dR^iMhata tam //4//
yaj~nasya chakShuH prabhR^itir mukhaM cha vAchA shrotreNa manasA juhomi /
imaM yaj~naM vitataM vishvakarmaNA devA yantu sumanasyamAnAH //5//
ye devAnAm R^itvijo ye cha yaj~niyA yebhyo havyaM kriyate bhAgadheyam /
imaM yaj~naM saha patnIbhir etya yAvanto devAs taviShA mAdayantAm //6//
617

tvam agne vratapA asi deva A martyeShv A /
tvaM yaj~neShv IDyaH //1//
yad vo vayaM praminAma vratAni viduShAM devA aviduShTarAsaH /
agniSh Tad vishvAd A pR^iNAtu vidvAnt somasya yo brAhmaNA.N Avivesha //2//
A devAnAm api panthAm aganma yach chhaknavAma tad anupravodum /
agnir vidvAnt sa yajAt sa id dhotA so 'dhvarAnt sa R^itUn kalpayAti //3//
618

vA~N ma Asan nasoH prANash chakShur akShNoH shrotraM karNayoH /
apalitAH keshA ashoNA dantA bahu bAhvor balam //1//
Urvor ojo ja~Nghayor javaH pAdayoH /
pratiShThA ariShTAni me sarvAtmAnibhR^iShTaH //2//
619

tanUs tanvA me sahe dataH sarvam Ayur ashIya /
syonaM me sIda puruH pR^iNasva pavamAnaH svarge //1//
620

priyaM mA kR^iNu deveShu priyaM rAjasu mA kR^iNu /
priyaM sarvasya pashyata uta shUdra utArye //1//
621

ut tiShTha brahmaNas pate devAn yaj~nena bodhaya /
AyuH prANaM prajAM pashUn kIrtiM yajamAnaM cha vardhaya //1//
622

agne samidham AhArShaM bR^ihate jAtavedase /
sa me shraddhAM cha medhAM cha jAtavedAH pra yachhatu //1//
idhmena tvA jAtavedaH samidhA vardhayAmasi /
tathA tvam asmAn vardhaya prajayA cha dhanena cha //2//
yad agne yAni kAni chid A te dArUNi dadhmasi /
sarvaM tad astu me shivaM taj juShasva yaviShThya //3//
etAs te agne samidhas tvam iddhaH samid bhava /
Ayur asmAsu dhehy amR^itatvam AchAryAya //4//
623

hariH suparNo divam Aruho 'rchiShA ye tvA dipsanti divam utpatantam /
ava tAM jahi harasA jAtavedo 'bibhyad ugro 'rchiShA divam A roha sUrya //1//
624

ayojAlA asurA mAyino 'yasmayaiH pAshair a~Nkino ye charanti /
tAMs te randhayAmi harasA jAtavedaH sahasraR^iShTiH sapatnAn pramR^iNan pAhi vajraH //1//
625

pashyema sharadaH shatam //1//
jIvema sharadaH shatam //2//
budhyema sharadaH shatam //3//
rohema sharadaH shatam //4//
pUShema sharadaH shatam //5//
bhavema sharadaH shatam //6//
bhUShema sharadaH shatam //7//
bhUyasIH sharadaH shatam //8//
626

avyasash cha vyachasash cha bilaM vi ShyAmi mAyayA /
tAbhyAm uddhR^itya vedam atha karmANi kR^iNmahe //1//
627

jIvA stha jIvyAsaM sarvam Ayur jIvyAsam //1//
upajIvA sthopa jIvyAsaM sarvam Ayur jIvyAsam //2//
saMjIvA stha saM jIvyAsaM sarvam Ayur jIvyAsam //3//
jIvalA stha jIvyAsaM sarvam Ayur jIvyAsam //4//
628

indra jIva sUrya jIva devA jIvA jIvyAsam aham /
sarvam Ayur jIvyAsam //1//
629

stutA mayA varadA vedamAtA pra chodayantAM pAvamAnI dvijAnAm /
AyuH prANaM prajAM pashuM kIrtiM draviNaM brahmavarchasam /
mahyaM dattvA vrajata brahmalokam //1//
630

yasmAt koshAd udabharAma vedaM tasminn antar ava dadhma enam /
kR^itam iShTaM brahmaNo vIryeNa tena mA devAs tapasAvateha //1//
631
Book:
20

indra tvA vR^iShabhaM vayaM sute some havAmahe /
sa pAhi madhvo andhasaH //1//
maruto yasya hi kShaye pAthA divo vimahasaH /
sa sugopAtamo janaH //2//
ukShAnnAya vashAnnAya somapR^iShThAya vedhase /
stomair vidhemAgnaye //3//
632

marutaH potrAt suShTubhaH svarkAd R^itunA somaM pibantu //1//
agnir AgnIdhrAt suShTubhaH svarkAd R^itunA somaM pibatu //2//
indro brahmA brAhmaNAt suShTubhaH svarkAd R^itunA somaM pibatu //3//
devo draviNodAH potrAt suShTubhaH svarkAd R^itunA somaM pibatu //4//
633

A yAhi suShumA hi ta indra somaM pibA imam /
edaM barhiH sado mama //1//
A tvA brahmayujA harI vahatAm indra keshinA /
upa brahmANi naH shR^iNu //2//
brahmANas tvA vayaM yujA somapAm indra sominaH /
sutAvanto havAmahe //3//
634

A no yAhi sutAvato 'smAkaM suShTutIr upa /
pibA su shiprinn andhasaH //1//
A te si~nchAmi kukShyor anu gAtrA vi dhAvatu /
gR^ibhAya jihvayA madhu //2//
svAduSh Te astu saMsude madhumAn tanve3 tava /
somaH sham astu te hR^ide //3//
635

ayam u tvA vicharShaNe janIr ivAbhi saMvR^itaH /
pra soma indra sarpatu //1//
tuvigrIvo vapodaraH subAhur andhaso sade /
indro vR^itrANi jighnate //2//
indra prehi puras tvaM vishvasyeshAna ojasA /
vR^itrANi vR^itrahaM jahi //3//
dIrghas te astv a~Nkusho yenA vasu prayachhasi /
yajamAnAya sunvate //4//
ayaM ta indra somo nipUto adhi barhiShi /
ehIm asya dravA piba //5//
shAchigo shAchipUjanAyaM raNAya te sutaH /
AkhaNDala pra hUyase //6//
yas te shR^i~NgavR^iSho napAt praNapAt kuNDapAyyaH /
ny asmin dadhra A manaH //7//
636

indra tvA vR^iShabhaM vayaM sute some havAmahe /
sa pAhi madhvo andhasaH //1//
indra kratuvidaM sutaM somaM harya puruShTuta /
pibA vR^iShasva tAtR^ipim //2//
indra pra No dhitAvAnaM yaj~naM vishvebhir devebhir /
tira stavAna vishpate //3//
indra somAH sutA ime tava pra yanti satpate /
kShayaM chandrAsa indavaH //4//
dadhiShvA jaThare sutaM somam indra vareNyam /
tava dyukShAsa indavaH //5//
girvaNaH pAhi naH sutaM madhor dhArAbhir ajyase /
indra tvAdAtam id yashaH //6//
abhi dyumnAni vanina indraM sachante akShitA /
pItvI somasya vAvR^idhe //7//
arvAvato na A gahi parAvatash cha vR^itrahan /
imA juShasva no giraH //8//
yad antarA parAvatam arvAvataM cha hUyase /
indreha tata A gahi //9//
637

ud ghed abhi shrutAmaghaM vR^iShabhaM naryApasam /
astAram eShi sUrya //1//
nava yo navatiM puro bibheda bAhvojasA /
ahiM cha vR^itrahAvadhIt //2//
sa na indraH shivaH sakhAshvAvad gomad yavamat /
urudhAreva dohate //3//
indra kratuvidaM sutaM somaM harya puruShTuta /
pibA vR^iShasva tAtR^ipim //4//
638

evA pAhi pratnathA mandatu tvA shrudhi brahma vAvR^idhasvota gIrbhiH /
AviH sUryaM kR^iNuhi pIpihISho jahi shatrUm?r abhi gA indra tR^indhi //1//
arvA~N ehi somakAmaM tvAhur ayaM sutas tasya pibA madAya /
uruvyachA jaThara A vR^iShasva piteva naH shR^iNuhi hUyamAnaH //2//
ApUrNo asya kalashaH svAhA sekteva koshaM siShiche pibadhyai /
sam u priyA AvavR^itran madAya pradakShiNid abhi somAsa indram //3//
639

taM vo dasmam R^itIShaham vasor mandAnam andhasaH /
abhi vatsaM na svasareShu dhenava indraM gIrbhir navAmahe //1//
dyukShaM sudAnuM taviShIbhir AvR^itaM giriM na purubhojasam /
kShumantaM vAjaM shatinaM sahasriNaM makShU gomantam Imahe //2//
tat tvA yAmi suvIryaM tad brahma pUrvachittaye /
yenA yatibhyo bhR^igave dhane hite yena praskaNvam Avitha //3//
yenA samudram asR^ijo mahIr apas tad indra vR^iShNi te shavaH /
sadyaH so asya mahimA na saMnashe yaM kShoNIr anuchakrade //4//
640

ud u te madhumattamA gira stomAsa Irate /
satrAjito dhanasA akShitotayo vAjayanto rathA iva //1//
kaNvA iva bhR^igavaH sUryA iva vishvam id dhItam AnashuH /
indraM stomebhir mahayanta AyavaH priyamedhAso asvaran //2//
641

indraH pUrbhid Atirad dAsam arkair vidadvasur dayamAno vi shatrUn /
brahmajUtas tanvA vAvR^idhAno bhUridAtra ApR^iNad rodasI ubhe //1//
makhasya te taviShasya pra jUtim iyarmi vAcham amR^itAya bhUShan /
indra kShitInAm asi mAnuShINAM vishAM daivInAm uta pUrvayAvA //2//
indro vR^itram avR^iNoch chhardhanItiH pra mAyinAm aminAd varpaNItiH /
ahan vyaMsam ushadhag vaneShv Avir dhenA akR^iNod rAmyANAm //3//
indraH svarShA janayann ahAni jigAyoshigbhiH pR^itanA abhiShTiH /
prArochayan manave ketum ahnAm avindaj jyotir bR^ihate raNAya //4//
indras tujo barhaNA A vivesha nR^ivad dadhAno naryA purUNi /
achetayad dhiya imA jaritre premaM varNam atirach chhukram AsAm //5//
maho mahAni panayanty asyendrasya karma sukR^itA purUNi /
vR^ijanena vR^ijinAnt saM pipeSha mAyAbhir dasyU.Nr abhibhUtyojAH //6//
yudhendro mahnA varivash chakAra devebhyaH satpatish charShaNiprAH /
vivasvataH sadane asya tAni viprA ukthebhiH kavayo gR^iNanti //7//
satrAsAhaM vareNyaM sahodAM sasavAMsaM svar apash cha devIH /
sasAna yaH pR^ithivIM dyAm utemAm indraM madanty anu dhIraNAsaH //8//
sasAnAtyAm? uta sUryaM sasAnendraH sasAna purubhojasaM gAm /
hiraNyayam uta bhogaM sasAna hatvI dasyUn prAryaM varNam Avat //9//
indra oShadhIr asanod ahAni vanaspatIm?r asanod antarikSham /
bibheda balaM nunude vivAcho 'thAbhavad damitAbhikratUnAm //10//
shunaM huvema maghavAnam indram asmin bhare nR^itamaM vAjasAtau /
shR^iNvantam ugram Utaye samatsu ghnantaM vR^itrANi saMjitaM dhanAnAm //11//
642

ud u brahmANy airata shravasyendraM samarye mahayA vasiShTha /
A yo vishvAni shavasA tatAnopashrotA ma Ivato vachAMsi //1//
ayAmi ghoSha indra devajAmir irajyanta yach chhurudho vivAchi /
nahi svam Ayush chikite janeShu tAnId aMhAMsy ati parShy asmAn //2//
yuje rathaM gaveShaNaM haribhyAm upa brahmANi jujuShANam asthuH /
vi bAdhiShTa sya rodasI mahitvendro vR^itrANy apratI jaghanvAn //3//
Apash chit pipyu staryo3 na gAvo nakShann R^itaM jaritAras ta indra /
yAhi vAyur na niyuto no achhA tvaM hi dhIbhir dayase vi vAjAn //4//
te tvA madA indra mAdayantu shuShmiNaM tuvirAdhasaM jaritre /
eko devatrA dayase hi martAn asmin chhUra savane mAdayasva //5//
eved indraM vR^iShaNaM vajrabAhuM vasiShThAso abhy archanty arkaiH /
sa na stuto vIravad dhAtu gomad yUyaM pAta svastibhiH sadA naH //6//
R^ijIShI vajrI vR^iShabhas turAShAT chhuShmI rAjA vR^itrahA somapAvA /
yuktvA haribhyAm upa yAsad arvA~N mAdhyaMdine savane matsad indraH //7//
643

indrash cha somaM pibataM bR^ihaspate 'smin yaj~ne mandasAnA vR^iShaNvasU /
A vAM vishantv indavaH svAbhuvo 'sme rayiM sarvavIraM ni yachhatam //1//
A vo vahantu saptayo raghuShyado raghupatvAnaH pra jigAta bAhubhiH /
sIdatA barhir uru vaH sadas kR^itaM mAdayadhvaM maruto madhvo andhasaH //2//
imaM stomam arhate jAtavedase ratham iva saM mahemA manIShayA /
bhadrA hi naH pramatir asya saMsady agne sakhye mA riShAmA vayaM tava //3//
aibhir agne sarathaM yAhy arvA~N nAnArathaM vA vibhavo hy ashvAH /
patnIvatas triMshataM trIMsh cha devAn anuShvadham A vaha mAdayasva //4//
644

vayam u tvAm apUrvya sthUraM na kach chid bharanto 'vasyavaH /
vAje chitraM havAmahe //1//
upa tvA karmann Utaye sa no yuvogrash chakrAma yo dhR^iShat /
tvAm id dhy avitAraM vavR^imahe sakhAya indra sAnasim //2//
yo na idamidaM purA pra vasya AninAya tam u va stuShe /
sakhAya indram Utaye //3//
haryashvaM satpatiM charShaNIsahaM sa hi ShmA yo amandata /
A tu naH sa vayati gavyam ashvyaM stotR^ibhyo maghavA shatam //4//
645

pra maMhiShThAya bR^ihate bR^ihadraye satyashuShmAya tavase matiM bhare /
apAm iva pravaNe yasya durdharaM rAdho vishvAyu shavase apAvR^itam //1//
adha te vishvam anu hAsad iShTaya Apo nimneva savanA haviShmataH /
yat parvate na samashIta haryata indrasya vajraH shnathitA hiraNyayaH //2//
asmai bhImAya namasA sam adhvara uSho na shubhra A bharA panIyase /
yasya dhAma shravase nAmendriyaM jyotir akAri harito nAyase //3//
ime ta indra te vayaM puruShTuta ye tvArabhya charAmasi prabhUvaso /
nahi tvad anyo girvaNo giraH sadhat kShoNIr iva prati no harya tad vachaH //4//
bhUri ta indra vIrya1M tava smasy asya stotur maghavan kAmam A pR^iNa /
anu te dyaur bR^ihatI vIryaM mama iyaM cha te pR^ithivI nema ojase //5//
tvaM tam indra parvataM mahAm uruM vajreNa vajrin parvashash chakartitha /
avAsR^ijo nivR^itAH sartavA apaH satrA vishvaM dadhiShe kevalaM sahaH //6//
646

udapruto na vayo rakShamANA vAvadato abhriyasyeva ghoShAH /
giribhrajo normayo madanto bR^ihaspatim abhy arkA anAvan //1//
saM gobhir a~Ngiraso nakShamANo bhaga ived aryamaNaM ninAya /
jane mitro na dampatI anakti bR^ihaspate vAjayAshU.Nr ivAjau //2//
sAdhvaryA atithinIr iShirA spArhAH suvarNA anavadyarUpAH /
bR^ihaspatiH parvatebhyo vitUryA nir gA Upe yavam iva sthivibhyah //3//
ApruShAyan madhunA R^itasya yonim avakShipann arka ulkAm iva dyoH /
bR^ihaspatir uddharann ashmano gA bhUmyA udneva vi tvachaM bibheda //4//
apa jyotiShA tamo antarikShad udnaH shIpAlam iva vAta Ajat /
bR^ihaspatir anumR^ishyA valasyAbhram iva vAta A chakra A gAH //5//
yadA valasya pIyato jasuM bhed bR^ihaspatir agnitapobhir arkaiH /
dadbhir na jihvA pariviShTam Adad Avir nidhI.Nr akR^iNod usriyANAm //6//
bR^ihaspatir amata hi tyad AsAM nAma svarINAM sadane guhA yat /
ANDeva bhitvA shakunasya garbham ud usriyAH parvatasya tmanAjat //7//
ashnApinaddhaM madhu pary apashyan matsyaM na dIna udani kShiyantam /
niSh Taj jabhAra chamasaM na vR^ikShAd bR^ihaspatir viraveNA vikR^itya //8//
soShAm avindat sa sva1H so agniM so arkeNa vi babAdhe tamAMsi /
bR^ihaspatir govapuSho valasya nir majjAnaM na parvaNo jabhAra //9//
himeva parNA muShitA vanAni bR^ihaspatinAkR^ipayad valo gAH /
anAnukR^ityam apunash chakAra yAt sUryAmAsA mitha uchcharAtaH //10//
abhi shyAvaM na kR^ishanebhir ashvaM nakShatrebhiH pitaro dyAm apiMshan /
rAtryAM tamo adadhur jyotir ahan bR^ihaspatir bhinad adriM vidad gAH //11//
idam akarma namo abhriyAya yaH pUrvIr anv AnonavIti /
bR^ihaspatiH sa hi gobhiH so ashvaiH sa vIrebhiH sa nR^ibhir no vayo dhAt //12//
647

achhA ma indraM matayaH svarvidaH sadhrIchIr vishvA ushatIr anUShata /
pari Shvajante janayo yathA patiM maryaM na shundhyuM maghavAnam Utaye //1//
na ghA tvadrig apa veti me manas tve it kAmaM puruhUta shishraya /
rAjeva dasma ni Shado 'dhi barhiShy asmint su some 'vapAnam astu te //2//
viShUvR^id indro amuter uta kShudhaH sa id rAyo maghavA vasva Ishate /
tasyed ime pravaNe sapta sindhavo vayo vardhanti vR^iShabhasya shuShmiNaH //3//
vayo na vR^ikShaM supalAsham Asadant somAsa indraM mandinash chamUShadaH /
praiShAm anIkaM shavasA davidyutad vidat sva1r manave jyotir Aryam //4//
kR^itaM na shvaghnI vi chinoti devane saMvargaM yan maghavA sUryaM jayat /
na tat te anyo anu vIryaM shakan na purANo maghavan nota nUtanaH //5//
vishaMvishaM maghavA pary ashAyata janAnAM dhenA avachAkashad vR^iShA /
yasyAha shakraH savaneShu raNyati sa tIvraiH somaiH sahate pR^itanyataH //6//
Apo na sindhum abhi yat samakSharant somAsa indraM kulyA iva hradam /
vardhanti viprA maho asya sAdane yavaM na vR^iShTir divyena dAnunA //7//
vR^iShA na kruddhaH patayad rajaHsv A yo aryapatnIr akR^iNod imA apaH /
sa sunvate maghavA jIradAnave 'vindaj jyotir manave haviShmate //8//
uj jAyatAM parashu jyotiShA saha bhUyA R^itasya sudughA purANavat /
vi rochatAm aruSho bhAnunA shuchiH sva1r na shukraM shushuchIta satpatiH //9//
gobhiSh TaremAmatiM durevAM yavena kShudhaM puruhUta vishvAm /
vayaM rAjabhiH prathamA dhanAny asmAkena vR^ijanenA jayema //10//
bR^ihaspatir naH pari pAtu pashchAd utottarasmAd adharAd aghAyoH /
indraH purastAd uta madhyato naH sakhA sakhibhyaH varivaH kR^iNotu //11//
bR^ihaspate yuvam indrash cha vasvo divyasyeshAthe uta pArthivasya /
dhattaM rayiM stuvate kIraye chid yUyaM pAta svastibhiH sadA naH //12//
648

vayam u tvA taditarthA indra tvAyantaH sakhAyaH /
kaNvA ukthebhir jarante //1//
na ghem anyad A papana vajrinn apaso naviShTau /
taved u stomaM chiketa //2//
ichhanti devAH sunvantaM na svapnAya spR^ihayanti /
yanti pramAdam atandrAH //3//
vayam indra tvAyavo 'bhi pra Nonumo vR^iShan /
viddhi tv asya no vaso //4//
mA no nide cha vaktave 'ryo randhIr arAvne /
tve api kratur mama //5//
tvaM varmAsi saprathaH puroyodhash cha vR^itrahan /
tvayA prati bruve yujA //6//
649

vArtrahatyAya shavase pR^itanAShAhyAya cha /
indra tvA vartayAmasi //1//
arvAchInaM su te mana uta chakShuH shatakrato /
indra kR^iNvantu vAghataH //2//
nAmAni te shatakrato vishvAbhir gIrbhir Imahe /
indrAbhimAtiShAhye //3//
puruShTutasya dhAmabhiH shatena mahayAmasi /
indrasya charShaNIdhR^itaH //4//
indraM vR^itrAya hantave puruhUtam upa bruve /
bhareShu vAjasAtaye //5//
vAjeShu sAsahir bhava tvAm Imahe shatakrato /
indra vR^itrAya hantave //6//
dyumneShu pR^itanAjye pR^itsutUrShu shravaHsu cha /
indra sAkShvAbhimAtiShu //7//
650

shuShmintamaM na Utaye dyumninaM pAhi jAgR^ivim /
indra somaM shatakrato //1//
indriyANi shatakrato yA te janeShu pa~nchasu /
indra tAni ta A vR^iNe //2//
agann indra shravo bR^ihad dyumnaM dadhiShva duShTaram /
ut te shuShmaM tirAmasi //3//
arvAvato na A gahy atho shakra parAvataH /
u loko yas te adriva indreha tata A gahi //4//
indro a~NgaM mahad bhayam abhi Shad apa chuchyavat /
sa hi sthiro vicharShaNiH //5//
indrash cha mR^ilayAti no na naH pashchAd aghaM nashat /
bhadraM bhavAti naH puraH //6//
indra AshAbhyas pari sarvAbhyo abhayaM karat /
jetA shatrUn vicharShaNiH //7//
651

ny U Shu vAchaM pra mahe bharAmahe gira indrAya sadane vivasvataH /
nU chid dhi ratnaM sasatAm ivAvidan na duShTutir draviNodeShu shasyate //1//
duro ashvasya dura indra gor asi duro yavasya vasuna inas patiH /
shikShAnaraH pradivo akAmakarshanaH sakhA sakhibhyas tam idaM gR^iNImasi //2//
shachIva indra purukR^id dyumattama taved idam abhitash chekite vasu /
ataH saMgR^ibhyAbhibhUta A bhara mA tvAyato jarituH kAmam UnayIH //3//
ebhir dyubhir sumanA ebhir indubhir nirundhAno amatiM gobhir ashvinA /
indreNa dasyuM darayanta indubhir yutadveShasaH sam iShA rabhemahi //4//
sam indra rAyA sam iShA rabhemahi saM vAjebhiH purushchandrair abhidyubhiH /
saM devyA pramatyA vIrashuShmayA goagrayAshvAvatyA rabhemahi //5//
te tvA madA amadan tAni vR^iShNyA te somAso vR^itrahatyeShu satpate /
yat kArave dasha vR^itrANy aprati barhiShmate ni sahasrANi barhayaH //6//
yudhA yudham upa ghed eShi dhR^iShNuyA purA puraM sam idaM haMsy ojasA /
namyA yad indra sakhyA parAvati nibarhayo namuchiM nAma mAyinam //7//
tvaM kara~njam uta parNayaM vadhIs tejiShThayAtithigvasya vartanI /
tvaM shatA va~NgR^idasyAbhinat puro 'nAnudaH pariShUtA R^ijishvanA //8//
tvam etAM janarAj~no dvir dashAbandhunA sushravasopajagmuShaH /
ShaShTiM sahasrA navatiM nava shruto ni chakreNa rathyA duShpadAvR^iNak //9//
tvam Avitha sushravasaM tavotibhis tava trAmabhir indra tUrvayANam /
tva asmai kutsam atithigvam AyuM mahe rAj~ne yUne arandhanAyaH //10//
ya udR^ichIndra devagopAH sakhAyas te shivatamA asAma /
tvAM stoShAma tvayA suvIrA drAghIya AyuH prataraM dadhAnAH //11//
652

abhi tvA vR^iShabhA sute sutaM sR^ijAmi pItaye /
tR^impA vy ashnuhI madam //1//
mA tvA mUrA aviShyavo mopahasvAna A dabhan /
mAkIM brahmadviSho vanaH //2//
iha tvA goparINasA mahe mandantu rAdhase /
saro gauro yathA piba //3//
abhi pra gopatiM girendram archa yathA vide /
sUnuM satyasya satpatim //4//
A harayaH sasR^ijrire 'ruShIr adhi barhiShi /
yatrAbhi saMnavAmahe //5//
indrAya gAva AshiraM duduhre vajriNe madhu /
yat sIm upahvare vidat //6//
653

A tU na indra madryag ghuvAnaH somapItaye /
haribhyAM yAhy adrivaH //1//
satto hotA na R^itviyas tistire barhir AnuShak /
ayujran prAtar adrayaH //2//
imA brahma brahmavAhaH kriyanta A barhiH sIda /
vIhi shUra purolAsham //3//
rArandhi savaneShu Na eShu stomeShu vR^itrahan /
uktheShv indra girvaNaH //4//
matayaH somapAm uruM rihanti shavasas patim /
indraM vatsaM na mAtaraH //5//
sa mandasvA hy andhaso rAdhase tanvA mahe /
na stotAraM nide karaH //6//
vayam indra tvAyavo haviShmanto jarAmahe /
uta tvam asmayur vaso //7//
mAre asmad vi mumucho haripriyArvA~N yAhi /
indra svadhAvo matsveha //8//
arvA~nchaM tvA sukhe rathe vahatAm indra keshinA /
ghR^itasnU barhir Asade //9//
654

upa naH sutam A gahi somam indra gavAshiram /
haribhyAM yas te asmayuH //1//
tam indra madam A gahi barhiShThAM grAvabhiH sutam /
kuvin nv asya tR^ipNavaH //2//
indram itthA giro mamAchhAgur iShitA itaH /
AvR^ite somapItaye //3//
indraM somasya pItaye stomair iha havAmahe /
ukthebhiH kuvid Agamat //4//
indra somAH sutA ime tAn dadhiShva shatakrato /
jathare vAjinIvaso //5//
vidmA hi tvA dhanaMjayaM vAjeShu dadhR^iShaM kave /
adhA te sumnam Imahe //6//
imam indra gavAshiraM yavAshiraM cha naH piba /
AgatyA vR^iShabhiH sutam //7//
tubhyed indra sva okye3 somaM chodAmi pItaye /
eSha rArantu te hR^idi //8//
tvAM sutasya pItaye pratnam indra havAmahe /
kushikAso avasyavaH //9//
655

ashvAvati prathamo goShu gachhati suprAvIr indra martyas tavotibhiH /
tam it pR^iNakShi vasunA bhavIyasA sindhum Apo yathAbhito vichetasaH //1//
Apo na devIr upa yanti hotriyam avah pashyanti vitataM yathA rajaH /
prAchair devAsaH pra Nayanti devayuM brahmapriyaM joShayante varA iva //2//
adhi dvayor adadhA ukthya1M vacho yatasruchA mithunA yA saparyataH /
asaMyatto vrate te kSheti puShyati bhadrA shaktir yajamAnAya sunvate //3//
Ad a~NgirAH prathamaM dadhire vaya iddhAgnayaH shamyA ye sukR^ityayA /
sarvaM paNeH sam avindanta bhojanam ashvAvantaM gomantam A pashuM naraH //4//
yaj~nair atharvA prathamaH pathas tate tataH sUryo vratapA vena Ajani /
A gA Ajad ushanA kAvyaH sachA yamasya jAtam amR^itaM yajAmahe //5//
barhir vA yat svapatyAya vR^ijyate 'rko vA shlokam AghoShate divi /
grAvA yatra vadati karur ukthya1s tasyed indro abhipitveShu raNyati //6//
progrAM pItiM vR^iShNa iyarmi satyAM prayai sutasya haryashva tubhyam /
indra dhenAbhir iha mAdayasva dhIbhir vishvAbhiH shachyA gR^iNAnaH //7//
656

yogeyoge tavastaraM vAjevAje havAmahe /
sakhAya indram Utaye //1//
A ghA gamad yadi shravat sahasriNIbhir UtibhiH /
vAjebhir upa no havam //2//
anu pratnasyaukaso huve tuvipratiM naram /
yaM te pUrvaM pitA huve //3//
yu~njanti bradhnam aruShaM charantaM pari tasthuShaH /
rochante rochanA divi //4//
yu~njanti asya kAmyA harI vipakShasA rathe /
shoNA dhR^iShNU nR^ivAhasA //5//
ketuM kR^iNvann aketave pesho maryA apeshase /
sam uShadbhir ajAyathAH //6//
657

yad indrAhaM yathA tvam IshIya vasva eka it /
stotA me goShakhA syAt //1//
shikSheyam asmai ditseyaM shachIpate manIShiNe /
yad ahaM gopatiH syAm //2//
dhenuSh Ta indra sUnR^itA yajamAnAya sunvate /
gAm ashvaM pipyuShI duhe //3//
na te vartAsti rAdhasa indra devo na martyaH /
yad ditsasi stuto magham //4//
yaj~na indram avardhayad yad bhUmiM vy avartayat /
chakrANa opashaM divi //5//
vAvR^idhAnasya te vayaM vishvA dhanAni jigyuShaH /
Utim indrA vR^iNImahe //6//
658

vy a1ntarikSham atiran made somasya rochanA /
indro yad abhinad valam //1//
ud gA Ajad a~Ngirobhya AviSh krNvan guhA satIH /
arvA~nchaM nunude valam //2//
indreNa rochanA divo dR^ilhAni dR^iMhitAni cha /
sthirANi na parANude //3//
apAm Urmir madann iva stoma indrAjirAyate /
vi te madA arAjiShuH //4//
659

tvaM hi stomavardhana indrAsy ukthavardhanaH /
stotR^INAm uta bhadrakR^it //1//
indram it keshinA harI somapeyAya vakShataH /
upa yaj~naM surAdhasam //2//
apAM phenena namucheH shira indrod avartayaH /
vishvA yad ajaya spR^idhaH //3//
mAyAbhir utsisR^ipsata indra dyAm ArurukShataH /
ava dasyUm?r adhUnuthAH //4//
asunvAm indra saMsadaM viShUchIM vy anAshayaH /
somapA uttaro bhavan //5//
660

pra te mahe vidathe shaMsiShaM harI pra te vanve vanuSho haryataM madam /
ghR^itaM na yo haribhish chAru sechata A tvA vishantu harivarpasaM giraH //1//
hariM hi yonim abhi ye samasvaran hinvanto harI divyaM yathA sadaH /
A yaM pR^iNanti haribhir na dhenava indrAya shUshaM harivantam archata //2//
so asya vajro harito ya Ayaso harir nikAmo harir A gabhastyoH /
dyumnI sushipro harimanyusAyaka indre ni rUpA haritA mimikShire //3//
divi na ketur adhi dhAyi haryato vivyachad vajro harito na raMhyA /
tudad ahiM harishipro ya AyasaH sahasrashokA abhavad dharimbharaH //4//
tvaMtvam aharyathA upastutaH pUrvebhir indra harikesha yajvabhiH /
tvaM haryasi tava vishvam ukthya1m asAmi rAdho harijAta haryatam //5//
661

tA vajriNaM mandinaM stomyaM mada indraM rathe vahato haryatA harI /
purUNy asmai savanAni haryata indrAya somA harayo dadhanvire //1//
araM kAmAya harayo dadhamire sthirAya hinvan harayo harI turA /
arvadbhir yo haribhir joSham Iyate so asya kAmaM harivantam Anashe //2//
harishmashArur harikesha Ayasas turaspeye yo haripA avardhata /
arvadbhir yo haribhir vAjinIvasur ati vishvA duritA pAriShad dharI //3//
shruveva yasya hariNI vipetatuH shipre vAjAya hariNI davidhvataH /
pra yat kR^ite chamase marmR^ijad dharI pItvA madasya haryatasyAndhasaH //4//
uta sma sadna haryatasya pastyo3r atyo na vAjaM harivAm? achikradat /
mahI chid dhi dhiShaNAharyad ojasA bR^ihad vayo dadhiShe haryatas chid A //5//
662

A rodasI haryamANo mahitvA navyaMnavyaM haryasi manma nu priyam /
pra pastyam asura haryataM gor AviSh kR^idhi haraye sUryAya //1//
A tvA haryantaM prayujo janAnAM rathe vahantu harishipram indra /
pibA yathA pratibhR^itasya madhvo haryan yaj~naM sadhamAde dashoNim //2//
apAH pUrveShAM harivaH sutAnAm atho idaM savanaM kevalaM te /
mamaddhi somaM madhumantam indra satrA vR^iShaM jathara A vR^iShasva //3//
663

apsu dhUtasya harivaH pibeha nR^ibhiH sutasya jaTharaM pR^iNasva /
mimikShur yam adraya indra tubhyaM tebhir vardhasva madam ukthavAhaH //1//
progrAM pItiM vR^iShNa iyarmi satyAM prayai sutasya haryashva tubhyam /
indra dhenAbhir iha mAdayasva dhIbhir vishvAbhiH shachyA gR^iNAnaH //2//
UtI shachIvas tava vIryeNa vayo dadhAnA ushija R^itaj~nAH /
prajAvad indra maMso duroNe tasthur gR^iNantaH sadhamAdyAsaH //3//
664

yo jAta eva prathamo manasvAn devo devAn kratunA paryamUShat /
yasya shuShmAd rodasI abhyasetAM nR^imNasya mahnA sa janAsa indraH //1//
yaH pR^ithivIM vyathamAnAm adR^iMhad yaH parvatAn prakupitAm? aramNAt /
yo antarikShaM vimame varIyo yo dyAm astabhnAt sa janAsa indraH //2//
yo hatvAhim ariNAt sapta sindhUn yo gA udAjad apadhA valasya /
yo ashmanor antar agniM jajAna saMvR^ik samatsu sa janAsa indraH //3//
yenemA vishvA chyavanA kR^itAni yo dAsaM varNam adharaM guhAkaH /
shvaghnIva yo jigIvAM lakSham Adad aryaH puShTAni sa janAsa indraH //4//
yaM smA pR^ichhanti kuha seti ghoram utem Ahur naiSho astIty enam /
so aryaH puShTIr vija ivA minAti shrad asmai dhatta sa janAsa indraH //5//
yo radhrasya choditA yaH kR^ishasya yo brahmaNo nAdhamAnasya kIreH /
yuktagrAvNo yo 'vitA sushipraH sutasomasya sa janAsa indraH //6//
yasyAshvAsaH pradishi yasya gAvo yasya grAmA yasya vishve rathAsaH /
yaH sUryaM ya uShasaM jajAna yo apAM netA sa janAsa indraH //7//
yaM krandasI saMyatI vihvayete pare 'vare ubhayA amitrAH /
samAnaM chid ratham AtasthivAMsA nAnA havete sa janAsa indraH //8//
yasmAn na R^ite vijayante janAso yaM yudhyamAnA avase havante /
yo vishvasya pratimAnaM babhUva yo achyutachyut sa janAsa indraH //9//
yaH shasvato mahy eno dadhAnAn amanyamAnAM chharvA jaghAna /
yah shardhate nAnudadAti shR^idhyAM yo dasyor hantA sa janAsa indraH //10//
yaH shambharaM parvateShu kShiyantaM chatvAriMshyAM sharady anvavindat /
