ojAyamAnaM yo ahiM jaghAna dAnuM shayAnaM sa janAsa indraH //11//
yaH shambharaM paryatarat kasIbhir yo 'chArukAsnApibat sutasya /
antar girau yajamAnaM bahuM janaM yasminn AmUrchhat sa janAsa indraH //12//
yaH saptarashmir vR^iShabhas tuviShmAn avAsR^ijat sartave sapta sindhUn /
yo rauhiNam asphurad vajrabAhur dyAm ArohantaM sa janAsa indraH //13//
dyAvA chid asmai pR^ithivI mamete shuShmAch chid asya parvatA bhayante /
yaH somapA nichito vajrabAhur yo vajrahastaH sa janAsa indraH //14//
yaH sunvantam avati yaH pachantaM yaH shaMsantaM yaH shashamAnam UtI /
yasya brahma vardhanaM yasya somo yasyedaM rAdhaH sa janAsa indraH //15//
jAto vy akhyat pitror upasthe bhuvo na veda janituH parasya /
staviShyamANo no yo asmad vratA devAnAM sa janAsa indraH //16//
yaH somakAmo haryashvaH sUrir yasmAd rejante bhuvanAni vishvA /
yo jaghAna shambaraM yash cha shuShNaM ya ekavIraH sa janAsa indraH //17//
yaH sunvate pachate dudhra A chid vAjaM dardarShi sa kilAsi satyaH /
vayaM ta indra vishvaha priyAsaH suvIrAso vidatham A vadema //18//
665

asmA id u pra tavase turAya prayo na harmi stomaM mAhinAya /
R^ichIShamAyAdhrigava oham indrAya brahmANi rAtatamA //1//
asmA id u praya iva pra yaMsi bharAmy AngUShaM bAdhe suvR^ikti /
indrAya hR^idA manasA manIShA pratnAya patye dhiyo marjayanta //2//
asmA id u tyam upamaM svarShAM bharAmy A~NgUSham Asyena /
maMhiShTham achhoktibhir matInAM suvR^iktibhiH sUriM vAvR^idhadhyai //3//
asmA id u stomaM saM hinomi rathaM na taShTeva tatsinAya /
girash cha girvAhase suvR^iktIndrAya vishvaminvaM medhirAya //4//
asmA id u saptim iva shravasyendrAyArkaM juhvA3 sam a~nje /
vIram dAnaukasaM vandadhyai purAM gUrtashravasaM darmANam //5//
asmA id u tvaShTA takShad vajraM svapastamaM svarya1M raNAya /
vR^itrasya chid vidad yena marma tujann IshAnas tujatA kiyedhAH //6//
asyed u mAtuH savaneShu sadyo mahaH pituM papivAM chArv annA /
muShAyad viShNuH pachataM sahIyAn vidhyad varAhaM tiro adrim astA //7//
asmA id u gnAsh chid devapatnIr indrAyArkam ahihatya UvuH /
pari dyAvApR^ithivI jabhra urvI nAsya te mahimAnaM pari ShTaH //8//
asyed eva pra ririche mahitvaM divas pR^ithivyAH pary antarikShAt /
svarAl indro dama A vishvagUrtaH svarir amatro vavakShe raNAya //9//
asyed eva shavasA shushantaM vi vR^ishchad vajreNa vR^itram indraH /
gA na vrANA avanIr amu~nchad abhi shravo dAvane sachetAH //10//
asyed u tveShasA ranta sindhavaH pari yad vajreNa sIm ayachhat /
IshAnakR^id dAshuShe dashasyan turvItaye gAdhaM turvaNiH kaH //11//
asmA id u pra bharA tUtujAno vR^itrAya vajram IshAnaH kiyedhAH /
gor na parva vi radA tirashcheShyann arNAMsy apAM charadhyai //12//
asyed u pra brUhi pUrvyANi turasya karmANi navya ukthaiH /
yudhe yad iShNAna AyudhAny R^ighAyamANo niriNAti shatrUn //13//
asyed u bhiyA girayash cha dR^ilhA dyAvA cha bhUmA januShas tujete /
upo venasya joguvAna oNiM sadyo bhuvad vIryAya nodhAH //14//
asmA id u tyad anu dAyy eShAm eko yad vavne bhUrer IshAnaH /
praitashaM sUrye paspR^idhAnaM sauvashvye suShvim Avad indraH //15//
evA te hAriyojanA suvR^iktIndra brahmANi gotamAso akran /
aiShu vishvapeshasaM dhiyaM dhAH prAtar makShU dhiyAvasur jagamyAt //16//
666

ya eka id dhavyash charShaNInAm indraM taM gIrbhir abhy archa AbhiH /
yaH patyate vR^iShabho vR^iShNyAvAnt satyaH satvA purumAyaH sahasvAn //1//
tam u naH pUrve pitaro navagvAH sapta viprAso abhi vAjayantaH /
nakShaddAbhaM taturiM parvateShThAm adroghavAchaM matibhiH shaviShTham //2//
tam Imahe indram asya rAyaH puruvIrasya nR^ivataH purukShoH /
yo aShkR^idhoyur ajaraH svarvAn tam A bhara harivo mAdayadhyai //3//
tan no vi vocho yadi te purA chij jaritAra AnashuH sumnam indra /
kas te bhAgaH kiM vayo dudhra khiduH puruhUta purUvaso 'suraghnaH //4//
taM pR^ichhantI vajrahastaM ratheShThAm indraM vepI vakvarI yasya nU gIH /
tuvigrAbhaM tuvikUrmiM rabhodAM gAtum iShe nakShate tumram achha //5//
ayA ha tyaM mAyayA vAvR^idhAnaM manojuvA svatavaH parvatena /
achyutA chid vIlitA svojo rujo vi dR^ilhA dhR^iShatA virapshin //6//
tam vo dhiyA navyasyA shaviShTham pratnaM pratnavat paritaMsayadhyai /
sa no vakShad animAnaH suvahnendro vishvAny ati durgahANi //7//
A janAya druhvaNe pArthivAni divyAni dIpayo 'ntarikShA /
tapA vR^iShan vishvataH shochiShA tAn brahmadviShe shochaya kShAm apash cha //8//
bhuvo janasya divyasya rAjA pArthivasya jagatas tveShasaMdR^ik /
dhiShva vajraM dakShiNa indra haste vishvA ajurya dayase vi mAyAH //9//
A saMyatam indra NaH svastiM shatrutUryAya bR^ihatIm amR^idhrAm /
yayA dAsAny AryANi vR^itrA karo vajrint sutukA nAhuShANi //10//
sa no niyudbhiH puruhUta vedho vishvavArAbhir A gahi prayajyo /
na yA adevo varate na deva Abhir yAhi tUyam A madryadrik //11//
667

yas tigmashR^i~Ngo vR^iShabho na bhImaH ekaH kR^iShTIsh chyavayati pra vishvAH /
yaH shashvato adAshuSho gayasya prayantAsi suShvitarAya vedaH //1//
tvaM ha tyad indra kutsam AvaH shushrUShamANas tanvA samarye /
dAsaM yach shuShNam kuyavaM ny asmA arandhaya ArjuneyAya shikShan //2//
tvaM dhR^iShNo dhR^iShatA vItahavyaM prAvo vishvAbhir UtibhiH sudAsam /
pra paurukutsiM trasadasyum AvaH kShetrasAtA vR^itrahatyeShu pUrum //3//
tvaM nR^ibhir nR^imaNo devavItau bhUrINi vR^itrA haryashva haMsi /
tvaM ni dasyuM chumuriM dhuniM chAsvApayo dabhItaye suhantu //4//
tava chyautnAni vajrahasta tAni nava yat puro navatiM cha sadyaH /
niveshane shatatamAviveShIr ahaM cha vR^itraM namuchim utAhan //5//
sanA tA ta indra bhojanAni rAtahavyAya dAshuShe sudAse /
vR^iShNe te harI vR^iShaNA yunajmi vyantu brahmANi purushAka vAjam //6//
mA te asyAM sahasAvan pariShTAv aghAya bhUma harivaH parAdau /
trAyasva no 'vR^ikebhir varUthais tava priyAsaH sUriShu syAma //7//
priyAsa it te maghavann abhiShTau naro madema sharaNe sakhAyaH /
ni turvashaM ni yAdvaM shishIhy atithigvAya shaMsyaM kariShyan //8//
sadyash chin nu te maghavann abhiShTau naraH shaMsanty ukthashAsa ukthA /
ye te havebhir vi paNI.Nr adAshann asmAn vR^iNIShva yujyAya tasmai //9//
ete stomA narAM nR^itama tubhyam asmadrya~ncho dadato maghAni /
teShAm indra vR^itrahatye shivo bhUH sakhA cha shUro 'vitA cha nR^iNAm //10//
nU indra shUra stavamAna UtI brahmajUtas tanvA vAvR^idhasva /
upa no vAjAn mimIhy upa stIn yuyaM pAta svastibhiH sadA naH //11//
668

A yAhi suShumA hi ta indra somaM pibA imam /
edaM barhiH sado mama //1//
A tvA brahmayujA harI vahatAm indra keshinA /
upa brahmANi naH shR^iNu //2//
brahmANas tvA vayaM yujA somapAm indra sominaH /
sutAvanto havAmahe //3//
indram id gAthino bR^ihad indram arkebhir arkiNaH /
indraM vANIr anUShata //4//
indra id dharyoH sachA saMmishla A vachoyujA /
indro vajrI hiraNyayaH //5//
indro dIrghAya chakShasa A sUryaM rohayad divi /
vi gobhir adrim airayat //6//
669

indraM vo vishvatas pari havAmahe janebhyaH /
asmAkam astu kevalaH //1//
vy a1ntarikSham atiran made somasya rochanA /
indro yad abhinad valam //2//
ud gA Ajad a~Ngirobhya AviSh kR^iNvan guhA satIH /
arvA~nchaM nunude valam //3//
indreNa rochanA divo dR^ilhAni dR^iMhitAni cha /
sthirANi na parANude //4//
apAm Urmir madann iva stoma indrAjirAyate /
vi te madA arAjiShuH //5//
670

indreNa saM hi dR^ikShase saMjagmAno abibhyuShA /
mandU samAnavarchasA //1//
anavadyair abhidyubhir makhaH sahasvad archati /
gaNair indrasya kAmyaiH //2//
Ad aha svadhAm anu punar garbhatvam erire /
dadhAnA nAma yaj~niyam //3//
671

indro dadhIcho asthabhir vR^itrANy apratiShkutaH /
jaghAna navatIr nava //1//
ichhan ashvasya yach chhiraH parvateShv apashritam /
tad vidach chharyaNAvati //2//
atrAha gor amanvata nAma tvaShTur apIchyam /
itthA chandramaso gR^ihe //3//
672

vAcham aShTApadIm ahaM navasraktim R^itaspR^isham /
indrAt pari tanvam mame //1//
anu tvA rodasI ubhe krakShamANam akR^ipetAm /
indra yad dasyuhAbhavaH //2//
uttiShThann ojasA saha pItvI shipre avepayaH /
somam indra chamU sutam //3//
673

bhindhi vishvA apa dviShaH bAdho jahI mR^idhaH /
vasu spArhaM tad A bhara //1//
yad vIlAv indra yat sthire yat parshAne parAbhR^itam /
vasu spArhaM tad A bhara //2//
yasya te vishvamAnuSho bhUrer dattasya vedati /
vasu spArhaM tad A bhara //3//
674

pra samrAjaM charShaNInAm indraM stotA navyaM gIrbhiH /
naraM nR^iShAhaM maMhiShTham //1//
yasminn ukthAni raNyanti vishvAni cha shravasya /
apAm avo na samudre //2//
taM suShTutyA vivAse jyeShTharAjaM bhare kR^itnum /
maho vAjinaM sanibhyaH //3//
675

ayam u te sam atasi kapota iva garbhadhim /
vachas tach chin na ohase //1//
stotraM rAdhAnAM pate girvAho vIra yasya te /
vibhUtir astu sUnR^itA //2//
Urdhvas tiShThA na Utaye 'smin vAje shatakrato /
sam anyeShu bravAvahai //3//
676

praNetAram vasyo achhA kartAraM jyotiH samatsu /
sAsahvAMsam yudhAmitrAn //1//
sa naH papriH pArayAti svasti nAvA puruhUtaH /
indro vishvA ati dviShaH //2//
sa tvaM na indra vAjobhir dashasyA cha gAtuyA cha /
achhA cha naH sumnaM neShi //3//
677

tam indraM vAjayAmasi mahe vR^itrAya hantave /
sa vR^iShA vR^iShabho bhuvat //1//
indraH sa dAmane kR^ita ojiShThaH sa made hitaH /
dyumnI shlokI sa somyaH //2//
girA vajro na saMbhR^itaH sabalo anapachyutaH /
vavakSha R^iShvo astR^itaH //3//
indram id gAthino bR^ihad indram arkebhir arkiNaH /
indraM vANIr anUShata //4//
indra id dharyoH sachA saMmishla A vachoyujA /
indro vajrI hiraNyayaH //5//
indro dIrghAya chakShasa A sUryaM rohayad divi /
vi gobhir adrim airayat //6//
A yAhi suShumA hi ta indra somaM pibA imam /
edaM barhiH sado mama //7//
A tvA brahmayujA harI vahatAm indra keshinA /
upa brahmANi naH shR^iNu //8//
brahmANas tvA vayaM yujA somapAm indra sominaH /
sutAvanto havAmahe //9//
yu~njanti bradhnam aruShaM charantaM pari tasthuShaH /
rochante rochanA divi //10//
yu~njanty asya kAmyA harI vipakShasA rathe /
shoNA dhR^iShNU nR^ivAhasA //11//
ketuM kR^iNvann aketave pesho maryA apeshase /
sam uShadbhir ajAyathAH //12//
ud u tyaM jAtavedasaM devaM vahanti ketavaH /
dR^ishe vishvAya sUryam //13//
apa tye tAyavo yathA nakShatrA yanty aktubhiH /
sUrAya vishvachakShase //14//
adR^ishrann asya ketavo vi rashmayo janAm? anu /
bhrAjanto agnayo yathA //15//
taraNir vishvadarshato jyotiShkR^id asi sUrya /
vishvam A bhAsi rochana //16//
pratya~N devAnAM vishaH pratya~N~N ud eShi mAnuShIH /
pratya~N vishvaM svar dR^ishe //17//
yenA pAvaka chakShasA bhuraNyantaM janAm? anu /
tvaM varuNa pashyasi //18//
vi dyAm eShi rajas pR^ithv ahar mimAno aktubhiH /
pashyaM janmAni sUrya //19//
sapta tvA harito rathe vahanti deva sUrya /
shochiShkesham vichakShaNam //20//
ayukta sapta shundhyuvaH sUro rathasya naptyaH /
tAbhir yAti svayuktibhiH //21//
678

abhi tvA varchasA giraH si~nchanty A charaNyuvaH /
abhi vatsaM na dhenavaH //1//
tA arShanti shubhriyaH pR^i~nchatIr varchasA payaH /
jAtaM janir yathA hR^idA //2//
vajrApavasAdhyaH kIrtir mriyamANam Avahan /
mahyam Ayur ghR^itaM payaH //3//
AyaM gauH pR^ishnir akramId asadan mAtaraM puraH /
pitaraM cha prayant svaH //4//
antash charati rochanA asya prANAd apAnataH /
vy akhyan mahiShaH svaH //5//
triMshad dhAmA vi rAjati vAk pata~Ngo ashishriyat /
prati vastor ahar dyubhiH //6//
679

yach chhakrA vAcham Aruhann antarikShaM siShAsathaH /
saM devA amadan vR^iShA //1//
shakro vAcham adhR^iShTAyor uvAcho adhR^iShNuhi /
maMhiShTha A madardivi //2//
shakro vAcham adhR^iShNuhi dhAmadharman vi rAjati /
vimadan barhir Asaran //3//
taM vo dasmam R^itIShahaM vasor mandAnam andhasaH /
abhi vatsaM na svasareShu dhenava indraM gIrbhir navAmahe //4//
dyukShaM sudAnuM taviShIbhir AvR^itam giriM na purubhojasam /
kShumantaM vAjaM shatinaM sahasriNaM makShU gomantam Imahe //5//
tat tvA yAmi suvIryaM tad brahma pUrvachittaye /
yenA yatibhyo bhR^igave dhane hite yena praskaNvam Avitha //6//
yenA samudram asR^ijo mahIr apas tad indra vR^iShNi te shavaH /
sadyaH so asya mahimA na saMnashe yaM kShoNIr anuchakrade //7//
680

kan navyo atasInAM turo gR^iNIta martyaH /
nahI nv asya mahimAnam indriyaM svar gR^iNanta AnashuH //1//
kad u stuvanta R^itayanta devata R^iShiH ko vipra ohate /
kadA havaM maghavann indra sunvataH kad u stuvata A gamaH //2//
681

abhi pra vaH surAdhasam indram archa yathA vide /
yo jaritR^ibhyo maghavA purUvasuH sahasreNeva shikShati //1//
shatAnIkeva pra jigAti dhR^iShNuyA hanti vR^itrANi dAshuShe /
girer iva pra rasA asya pinvire datrANi purubhojasaH //2//
pra su shrutaM surAdhasam archA shakram abhiShTaye /
yaH sunvate stuvate kAmyaM vasu sahasreNeva maMhate //3//
shatAnIkA hetayo asya duShTarA indrasya samiSho mahIH /
girir na bhujmA maghatsu pinvate yad IM sutA amandiShuH //4//
682

vayaM gha tvA sutAvanta Apo na vR^iktabarhiShaH /
pavitrasya prasravaNeShu vR^itrahan pari stotAra Asate //1//
svaranti tvA sute naro vaso nireka ukthinaH /
kadA sutaM tR^iShANa oka A gama indra svabdIva vaMsagaH //2//
kaNvebhir dhR^iShNav A dhR^isad vAjaM darShi sahasriNam /
pisha~NgarUpaM maghavan vicharShaNe maksU gomantam Imahe //3//
683

ka IM veda sute sachA pibantaM kad vayo dadhe /
ayaM yaH puro vibhinatty ojasA mandAnaH shipry andhasaH //1//
dAnA mR^igo na vAraNaH purutrA charathaM dadhe /
nakiSh TvA ni yamad A sute gamo mahAsh charasy ojasA //2//
ya ugraH sann aniShTR^ita sthiro raNAya saMskR^itaH /
yadi stotur maghavA shR^iNavad dhavaM nendro yoShaty A gamat //3//
684

vishvAH pR^itanA abhibhUtaraM naraM sajUs tatakShur indraM jajanush cha rAjase /
kratvA variShThaM vara Amurim utogram ojiShThaM tavasaM tarasvinam //1//
sam IM rebhAso asvarann indraM somasya pItaye /
svarpatiM yad IM vR^idhe dhR^itavrato hy ojasA sam UtibhiH //2//
nemiM namanti chakShasA meShaM viprA abhisvarA /
sudItayo vo adruho 'pi karNe tarasvinaH sam R^ikvabhiH //3//
685

tam indraM johavImi maghavAnam ugraM satrA dadhAnam apratiShkutaM shavAMsi /
maMhiShTho gIrbhir A cha yaj~niyo vavartad rAye no vishvA supathA kR^iNotu vajrI //1//
yA indra bhuja AbharaH svarvAm? asurebhyaH /
stotAram in maghavann asya vardhaya ye cha tve vR^iktabarhiShaH //2//
yam indra dadhiShe tvam ashvaM gAM bhAgam avyayam /
yajamAne sunvati dakShiNAvati tasmin taM dhehi mA paNau //3//
686

indro madAya vAvR^idhe shavase vR^itrahA nR^ibhiH /
tam in mahatsv AjiShUtem arbhe havAmahe sa vAjeShu pra no 'viShat //1//
asi hi vIra senyo 'si bhUri parAdadiH /
asi dabhrasya chid vR^idho yajamAnAya shikShasi sunvate bhUri te vasu //2//
yad udIrata Ajayo dhR^iShNave dhIyate dhanA /
yukShvA madachyutA harI kaM hanaH kaM vasau dadho 'smA.N indra vasau dadhaH //3//
mademade hi no dadir yUthA gavAm R^ijukratuH /
saM gR^ibhAya puru shatobhayAhastyA vasu shishIhi rAya A bhara //4//
mAdayasva sute sachA shavase shUra rAdhase /
vidmA hi tvA purUvasum upa kAmAnt sasR^ijmahe 'thA no 'vitA bhava //5//
ete ta indra jantavo vishvaM puShyanti vAryam /
antar hi khyo janAnAm aryo vedo adAshuShAM teShAM no veda A bhara //6//
687

surUpakR^itnum Utaye sudughAm iva goduhe /
juhUmasi dyavidyavi //1//
upa naH savanA gahi somasya somapAh piba /
godA id revato madaH //2//
athA te antamAnAM vidyAma sumatInAm /
mA no ati khya A gahi //3//
shuShmintamaM na Utaye dyumninaM pAhi jAgR^ivim /
indra somaM shatakrato //4//
indriyANi shatakrato yA te janeShu pa~nchasu /
indra tAni ta A vR^iNe //5//
agann indra shravo bR^ihad dyumnaM dadhiShva duShTaram /
ut te shuShmaM tirAmasi //6//
arvAvato na A gahy atho shakra parAvataH /
u loko yas te adriva indreha tata A gahi //7//
indro a~Nga mahad bhayam abhI Shad apa chuchyavat /
sa hi sthiro vicharShaniH //8//
indrash cha mR^ilayAti no na naH pashchAd aghaM nashat /
bhadraM bhavAti naH puraH //9//
indra AshAbhyas pari sarvAbhyo abhayaM karat /
jetA shatrUn vicharShaNiH //10//
ka IM veda sute sachA pibantaM kad vayo dadhe /
ayaM yaH puro vibhinatty ojasA mandAnaH shipry andhasaH //11//
dAnA mR^igo na vAraNaH purutrA charathaM dadhe /
nakiSh TvA ni yamad A sute gamo mahAMsh charasy ojasA //12//
ya ugraH sann aniShTR^ita sthiro raNAya saMskR^itaH /
yadi stotur maghavA shR^iNavad dhavaM nendro yoShaty A gamat //13//
vayaM gha tvA sutAvanta Apo na vR^iktabarhiShaH /
pavitrasya prasravaNeShu vR^itrahan pari stotAra Asate //14//
svaranti tvA sute naro vaso nireka ukthinaH /
kadA sutaM tR^iShANa oka A gama indra svabdIva vaMsagaH //15//
kaNvebhir dhR^iShNav A dhR^iShad vAjaM darShi sahasriNam /
pisha~NgarUpaM maghavan vicharShaNe makShU gomantam Imahe //16//
688

shrAyanta iva sUryaM vishved indrasya bhakShata /
vasUni jAte janamAna ojasA prati bhAgaM na dIdhima //1//
anarsharAtiM vasudAm upa stuhi bhadrA indrasya rAtayaH /
so asya kAmaM vidhato na roShati mano dAnAya chodayan //2//
baN mahA.N asi sUrya baD Aditya mahA.N asi /
mahas te sato mahimA panasyate 'ddhA deva mahA.N asi //3//
baT sUrya shravasA mahA.N asi satrA deva mahA.N asi /
mahnA devAnAm asuryaH purohito vibhu jyotir adAbhyam //4//
689

ud u tye madhu mattamA gira stomAsa Irate /
satrAjito dhanasA akShitotayo vAjayanto rathA iva //1//
kaNvA iva bhR^igavaH sUrya iva vishvam id dhItam AnashuH /
indraM stomebhir mahayanta AyavaH priyamedhAso asvaran //2//
ud in nv asya richyate 'Msho dhanaM na jigyusaH /
ya indro harivAn na dabhanti taM ripo daksaM dadhAti somini //3//
mantram akharvaM sudhitaM supeshasaM dadhAta yaj~niyeShv A /
pUrvIsh chana prasitayas taranti taM ya indre karmaNA bhuvat //4//
690

evA hy asi vIrayur evA shUra uta sthiraH /
evA te rAdhyaM manaH //1//
evA rAtis tuvImagha vishvebhir dhAyi dhAtR^ibhiH /
aghA chid indra me sachA //2//
mo Shu brahmeva tandrayur bhuvo vAjAnAM pate /
matsvA sutasya gomataH //3//
evA hy asya sUnR^itA virapshI gomatI mahI /
pakvA shAkhA na dAshuShe //4//
evA hi te vibhUtaya Utaya indra mAvate /
sadyash chit santi dAshuShe //5//
evA hy asya kAmyA stoma ukthaM cha shaMsyA /
indrAya somapItaye //6//
691

taM te madaM gR^iNImasi vR^iShaNaM pR^itsu sAsahim /
u lokakR^itnum adrivo harishriyam //1//
yena jyotImShy Ayave manave cha viveditha /
mandAno asya barhiSho vi rAjasi //2//
tad adyA chit ta ukthino 'nu ShTuvanti pUrvathA /
vR^iShapatnIr apo jayA divedive //3//
tam v abhi pra gAyata puruhUtaM puruShTutam /
indraM gIrbhis taviSham A vivAsata //4//
yasya dvibarhaso bR^ihat saho dAdhAra rodasI /
girI.Nr ajrAm? apaH svar vR^iShatvanA //5//
sa rAjasi puruShTutam? eko vR^itrANi jighnase /
indra jaitrA shravasya cha yantave //6//
692

vayam u tvAm apUrvya sthUraM na kach chid bharanto 'vasyavaH /
vAje chitraM havAmahe //1//
upa tvA karmann Utaye sa no yuvogrash chakrAma yo dhR^iShat /
tvAm id dhy avitAraM vavR^imahe sakhAya indra sAnasim //2//
yo na idamidaM purA pra vasya AninAya tam u va stuShe /
sakhAya indram Utaye //3//
haryashvaM satpatiM charShaNIsahaM sa hi ShmA yo amandata /
A tu naH sa vayati gavyam ashvyaM stotR^ibhyo maghavA shatam //4//
indrAya sAma gAyata viprAya bR^ihate bR^ihat /
dharmakR^ite vipashchite panasyave //5//
tvam indrAbhibhUr asi tvaM sUryam arochayaH /
vishvakarmA vishvadevo mahA.N asi //6//
vibhrAjaM jyotiShA sva1r agachho rochanaM divaH /
devAs ta indra sakhyAya yemire //7//
tam v abhi pra gAyata puruhUtaM puruShTutam /
indraM gIrbhis taviSham A vivAsata //8//
yasya dvibarhaso bR^ihat saho dAdhAra rodasI /
girI.Nr ajrAm? apaH svar vR^iShatvanA //9//
sa rAjasi puruShTutam? eko vR^itrANi jighnase /
indra jaitra shravasya cha yantave //10//
693

imA nu kaM bhuvanA sIShadhAmendrash cha vishve cha devAH /
yaj~naM cha nas tanvaM cha prajAM chAdityair indraH saha chIk?pAti //1//
Adityair indraH sagaNo marudbhir asmAkaM bhUtv avitA tanUnAm /
hatvAya devA asurAn yad Ayan devA devatvam abhirakShamANAH //2//
pratya~ncham arkam anayaM chhachIbhir Ad it svadhAm iShirAM pary apashyan /
ayA vAjaM devahitaM sanema madema shatahimAH suvIrAH //3//
ya eka id vidayate vasu martAya dAshuShe /
IshAno apratiShkuta indro a~Nga //4//
kadA martam arAdhasaM padA kShumpam iva sphurat /
kadA naH shushravad gira indro a~Nga //5//
yash chid dhi tvA bahubhya A sutAvAm? AvivAsati /
ugraM tat patyate shava indro a~Nga //6//
ya indra somapAtamo madaH shaviShTha chetati /
yenA haMsi ny a1ttriNaM tam Imahe //7//
yenA dashagvam adhriguM vepayantaM svarNaram /
yenA samudram AvithA tam Imahe //8//
yena sindhuM mahIr apo rathAm? iva prachodayaH /
panthAm R^itasya yAtave tam Imahe //9//
694

endra no gadhi priyaH satrAjid agohyaH /
girir na vishvatas pR^ithuH patir divaH //1//
abhi hi satya somapA ubhe babhUtha rodasI /
indrAsi sunvato vR^idhaH patir divaH //2//
tvaM hi shashvatInAm indra dartA purAm asi /
hantA dasyor manor vR^idhaH patir divaH //3//
ed u madhvo madintaraM si~ncha vAdhvaryo andhasaH /
evA hi vIra stavate sadAvR^idhaH //4//
indra sthAtar harINAM nakiSh te pUrvyastutim /
ud AnaMsha shavasA na bhandanA //5//
taM vo vAjAnAM patim ahUmahi shravasyavaH /
aprAyubhir yaj~nebhir vAvR^idhenyam //6//
695

eto nv indraM stavAma sakhAya stomyaM naram /
kuShTIr yo vishvA abhy asty eka it //1//
agorudhAya gaviShe dyukShAya dasmyaM vachaH /
ghR^itAt svAdIyo madhunash cha vochata //2//
yasyAmitAni vIryA3 na rAdhaH paryetave /
jyotir na vishvam abhy asti dakShiNA //3//
696

stuhIndraM vyashvavad anUrmiM vAjinaM yamam /
aryo gayaM maMhamAnaM vi dAshuShe //1//
evA nUnam upa stuhi vaiyashva dashamaM navam /
suvidvAMsaM charkR^ityaM charaNInAm //2//
vetthA hi nirR^itInAM vajrahasta parivR^ijam /
aharahaH shundhyuH paripadAm iva //3//
697

vanoti hi sunvan kShayaM parINasaH sunvAno hi ShmA yajaty ava dviSho devAnAm ava dviShaH /
sunvAna it siShAsati sahasrA vAjy avR^itaH /
sunvAnAyendro dadAty AbhuvaM rayiM dadAty Abhuvam //1//
mo Shu vo asmad abhi tAni pauMsyA sanA bhUvan dyumnAni mota jAriShur asmat purota jAriShuH /
yad vash chitraM yugeyuge navyaM ghoShAd amartyam /
asmAsu tan maruto yach cha duShTaraM didhR^itA yach cha duShTaram //2//
agniM hotAram manye dAsvantaM vasuM sUnuM sahaso jAtavedasaM vipraM na jAtavedasam /
ya UrdhvayA svadhvaro devo devAchyA kR^ipA /
ghR^itasya vibhrAShTim anu vaShTi shochiShAjuhvAnasya sarpiShaH //3//
yaj~naiH saMmishlAH pR^iShatIbhir R^iShTibhir yAmaM chhubhrAso a~njiShu priyA uta /
AsadyA barhir bharatasya sUnavaH potrAd A somaM pibatA divo naraH //4//
A vakShi devA.N iha vipra yakShi choshan hotar ni ShadA yoniShu triShu /
prati vIhi prasthitaM somyaM madhu pibAgnIdhrAt tava bhAgasya tR^isNuhi //5//
eSha sya te tanvo nR^imNavardhanaH saha ojaH pradivi bAhvor hitaH /
tubhyaM suto maghavan tubhyam AbhR^itas tvam asya brAhmaNAd A tR^ipat piba //6//
yam u pUrvam ahuve tam idaM huve sed u havyo dadir yo nAma patyate /
adhvaryubhiH prasthitaM somyaM madhu potrAt somaM draviNodaH piba R^itubhiH //7//
698

surUpakR^itnum Utaye sudughAm iva goduhe /
juhUmasi dyavidyavi //1//
upa naH savanA gahi somasya somapAH piba /
godA id revato madaH //2//
athA te antamAnAM vidyAma sumatInAm /
mA no ati khya A gahi //3//
parehi vigram astR^itam indraM pR^ichhA vipashchitam /
yas te sakhibhya A varam //4//
uta bruvantu no nido nir anyatash chid Arata /
dadhAnA indra id duvaH //5//
uta naH subhagAm? arir vocheyur dasma kR^iShTayaH /
syAmed indrasya sharmaNi //6//
em Ashum Ashave bhara yaj~nashriyaM nR^imAdanam /
patayan mandayatsakham //7//
asya pItvA shatakrato ghano vR^itrANAm abhavaH /
prAvo vAjeShu vAjinam //8//
taM tvA vAjeShu vAjinaM vAjayAmaH shatakrato /
dhanAnAm indra sAtaye //9//
yo rAyo3 'vanir mahAnt supAraH sunvataH sakhA /
tasmA indrAya gAyata //10//
A tv etA ni ShIdatendram abhi pra gAyata /
sakhAya stomavAhasaH //11//
purUtamaM purUNAm IshAnaM vAryANAm /
indraM some sachA sute //12//
699

sa ghA no yoga A bhuvat sa rAye sa puraMdhyAm /
gamad vAjebhir A sa naH //1//
yasya saMsthe na vR^iNvate harI samatsu shatravaH /
tasmA indrAya gAyata //2//
sutapAvne sutA ime shuchayo yanti vItaye /
somAso dadhyAshiraH //3//
tvaM sutasya pItaye sadyo vR^iddho ajAyathAH /
indra jyaiShThyAya sukrato //4//
A tvA vishantv AshavaH somAsa indra girvaNaH /
shaM te santu prachetase //5//
tvAM stomA avIvR^idhan tvAm ukthA shatakrato /
tvAM vardhantu no giraH //6//
akShitotiH saned imaM vAjam indraH sahasriNam /
yasmin vishvAni pauMsyA //7//
mA no martA abhi druhan tanUnAm indra girvaNaH /
IshAno yavayA vadham //8//
yu~njanti bradhnam aruSham charantaM pari tasthuShaH /
rochante rochanA divi //9//
yu~njanty asya kAmyA harI vipakShasA rathe /
shoNA dhR^iShNU nR^ivAhasA //10//
ketuM kR^iNvann aketave pesho maryA apeshase /
sam uShadbhir ajAyathAH //11//
Ad aha svadhAm anu punar garbhatvam erire /
dadhAnA nAma yaj~niyam //12//
700

vIlu chid Arujatnubhir guhA chid indra vahnibhiH /
avinda usriyA anu //1//
devayanto yathA matim achhA vidadvasuM giraH /
mahAm anUShata shrutam //2//
indreNa saM hi dR^ikShase saMjagmAno abibhyuShA /
mandU samAnavarchasA //3//
anavadyair abhidyubhir makhaH sahasvad archati /
gaNair indrasya kAmyaiH //4//
ataH parijmann A gahi divo vA rochanAd adhi /
sam asminn R^i~njate giraH //5//
ito vA sAtim Imahe divo vA pArthivAd adhi /
indraM maho vA rajasaH //6//
indram id gathino bR^ihad indram arkebhir arkiNaH /
indraM vANIr anUShata //7//
indra id dharyoH sachA saMmishla A vachoyujA /
indro vajrI hiraNyayaH //8//
indro dIrghAya chakShasa A sUryaM rohayad divi /
vi gobhir indram airayat //9//
indra vAjeShu no 'va sahasrapradhaneShu cha /
ugra ugrAbhir UtibhiH //10//
indraM vayaM mahAdhana indram arbhe havAmahe /
yujaM vR^itreShu vajriNam //11//
sa no vR^iShann amuM charuM satrAdAvann apA vR^idhi /
asmabhyam apratiShkutaH //12//
tu~njetu~nje ya uttare stomA indrasya vajriNaH /
na vindhe asya suShTutim //13//
vR^iShA yUtheva vaMsagaH kR^iShTIr iyarty ojasA /
IshAno apratiShkutaH //14//
ya ekash charShaNInAM vasUnAm irajyati /
indraH pa~ncha kShitInAm //15//
indraM vo vishvatas pari havAmahe janebhyaH /
asmAkam astu kevalaH //16//
endra sAnasiM rayiM sajitvAnaM sadAsaham /
varShiShTham Utaye bhara //17//
ni yena muShTihatyayA ni vR^itrA ruNadhAmahai /
tvotAso ny arvatA //18//
indra tvotAso A vayaM vajraM ghanA dadImahi /
jayema saM yudhi spR^idhaH //19//
vayaM shUrebhir astR^ibhir indra tvayA yujA vayam /
sAsahyAma pR^itanyataH //20//
701

mahA.N indraH parash cha nu mahitvam astu vajriNe /
dyaur na prathinA shavaH //1//
samohe vA ya Ashata naras tokasya sanitau /
viprAso vA dhiyAyavaH //2//
yaH kukShiH somapAtamaH samudra iva pinvate /
urvIr Apo na kAkudaH //3//
evA hy asya sUnR^itA virapshI gomatI mahI /
pakvA shAkhA na dAshuShe //4//
evA hi te vibhUtaya Utaya indra mAvate /
sadyash chit santi dAshuShe //5//
evA hy asya kAmyA stoma ukthaM cha shaMsyA /
indrAya somapItaye //6//
indrehi matsy andhaso vishvebhiH somaparvabhiH /
mahA.N abhiShTir ojasA //7//
em enaM sR^ijatA sute mandim indrAya mandine /
chakriM vishvAni chakraye //8//
matsvA sushipra mandibhi stomebhir vishvacharShaNe /
sachaiShu savaneShv A //9//
asR^igram indra te giraH prati tvAm ud ahAsata /
ajoShA vR^iShabhaM patim //10//
sam chodaya chitram arvAg rAdha indra vareNyam /
asad it te vibhu prabhu //11//
asmAnt su tatra chodayendra rAye rabhasvataH /
tuvidyumna yashasvataH //12//
saM gomad indra vAjavad asme pR^ithu shravo bR^ihat /
vishvAyur dhehy akShitam //13//
asme dhehi shravo bR^ihad dyumnaM sahasrasAtamam /
indra tA rathinIr iShaH //14//
vasor indram vasupatiM gIrbhir gR^iNanta R^igmiyam /
homa gantAram Utaye //15//
sutesute nyokase bR^ihad bR^ihata ed ariH /
indrAya shUSham archati //16//
702

vishveShu hi tvA savaneShu tu~njate samAnam ekaM vR^iShamaNyavaH pR^ithak svaH saniShyavaH pR^ithak /
taM tvA nAvaM na parShaNiM shUShasya dhuri dhImahi /
indraM na yaj~naish chatayanta Ayava stomebhir indram AyavaH //1//
vi tvA tatasre mithunA avasyavo vrajasya sAtA gavyasya niHsR^ijaH sakShanta indra niHsR^ijaH /
yad gavyantA dvA janA sva1r yantA samUhasi /
AviSh karikrad vR^iShaNaM sachAbhuvaM vajram indra sachAbhuvam //2//
uto no asyA uShaso juSheta hy a1rkasya bodhi haviSho havImabhiH svarShAtA havImabhiH /
yad indra hantave mR^igho vR^iShA vajriM chiketasi /
A me asya vedhaso navIyaso manma shrudhi navIyasaH //3//
703

tubhyed imA savanA shUra vishvA tubhyaM brahmANi vardhanA kR^iNomi /
tvaM nR^ibhir havyo vishvadhAsi //1//
nU chin nu te manyamAnasya dasmod ashnuvanti mahimAnam ugra /
na vIryam indra te na rAdhaH //2//
pra vo mahe mahivR^idhe bharadhvaM prachetase pra sumatiM kR^iNudhvam /
vishaH pUrvIH pra charA charShaNiprAH //3//
yadA vajraM hiraNyam id athA rathaM harI yamasya vahato vi sUribhiH /
A tiShThati maghavA sanashruta indro vAjasya dIrghashravasas patiH //4//
so chin nu vR^iShTir yUthyA3 svA sachAm? indraH shmashrUNi haritAbhi pruShNute /
ava veti sukShayaM sute madhUd id dhUNoti vAto yathA vanam //5//
yo vAchA vivAcho mR^idhravAchaH purU sahasrAshivA jaghAna /
tattad id asya pauMsyaM gR^iNImasi piteva yas taviShIM vAvR^idhe shavaH //6//
704

yach chid dhi satya somapA anAshastA iva smasi /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //1//
shiprin vAjAnAM pate shachIvas tava daMsanA /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //2//
ni ShvApayA mithUdR^ishA sastAm abudhyamAne /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //3//
sasantu tyA arAtayo bodhantu shUra rAtayaH /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //4//
sam indra gardabhaM mR^iNa nuvantaM papayAmuyA /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //5//
patAti kuNDR^iNAchyA dUraM vAto vanAd adhi /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //6//
sarvaM parikroshaM jahi jambhayA kR^ikadAshvam /
A tU na indra shaMsaya goShv ashveShu shubhriShu sahasreShu tuvImagha //7//
705

vi tvA tatasre mithunA avasyavo vrajasya sAtA gavyasya niHsR^ijaH sakShanta indra niHsR^ijaH /
yad gavyanta dvA janA sva1r yantA samUhasi /
AviSh karikrad vR^iShaNaM sachAbhuvaM vajram indra sachAbhuvam //1//
viduSh Te asya vIryasya pUravaH puro yad indra shAradIr avAtiraH sAsahAno avAtiraH /
shAsas tam indra martyam ayajuM shavasas pate /
mahIm amuShNAH pR^ithivIm imA apo mandasAna imA apaH //2//
Ad it te asya vIryasya charkiran madeShu vR^iShann ushijo yad Avitha sakhIyato yad Avitha /
chakartha kAram ebhyaH pR^itanAsu pravantave /
te anyAmanyAM nadyaM saniShNata shravasyantaH saniShNata //3//
706

vane na vA yo ny adhAyi chAkraM chhuchir vAM stomo bhuraNAv ajIgaH /
yasyed indraH purudineShu hotA nR^iNAM narvo nR^itamaH kShapAvAn //1//
pra te asyA uShasaH prAparasyA nR^itau syAma nR^itam asya nR^iNAm /
anu trishokaH shatam Avahan nR^In kutsena ratho yo asat sasavAn //2//
kas te mada indra rantyo bhUd duro giro abhy ù1gro vi dhAva /
kad vAho arvAg upa mA manIShA A tvA shakyAm upamam rAdho annaiH //3//
kad u dyumnam indra tvAvato nR^In kayA dhiyA karase kan na Agan /
mitro na satya urugAya bhR^ityA anne samasya yad asan manIShAH //4//
preraya sUro arthaM na pAraM ye asya kAmaM janidhA iva gman /
girash cha ye te tuvijAta pUrvIr nara indra pratishikShanty annaiH //5//
mAtre nu te sumite indra pUrvI dyaur majmanA pR^ithivI kAvyena /
varAya te ghR^itavantaH sutAsaH svAdnan bhavantu pItaye madhUni //6//
A madhvo asmA asichann amatram indrAya pUrNaM sa hi satyarAdhAH /
sa vAvR^idhe varimann A pR^ithivyA abhi kratvA naryaH pauMsyaish cha //7//
vy Ana? indraH pR^itanAH svojA Asmai yatante sakhyAya pUrvIH /
A smA rathaM na pR^itanAsu tiShTha yaM bhadrayA sumatyA chodayAse //8//
707

A satyo yAtu maghavAm? R^ijIShI dravantv asya haraya upa naH /
tasmA id andhaH suShumA sudakSham ihAbhipitvaM karate gR^iNAnaH //1//
ava sya shUrAdhvano nAnte 'smin no adya savane mandadhyai /
shaMsAty uktham ushaneva vedhAsh chikituShe asuryAya manma //2//
kavir na niNyaM vidathAni sAdhan vR^iShA yat sekaM vipipAno archAt /
diva itthA jIjanat sapta kArUn ahnA chich chakrur vayunA gR^iNantaH //3//
sva1r yad vedi sudR^ishIkam arkair mahi jyotI ruruchur yad dha vastoH /
andhA tamAMsi dudhitA vichakShe nR^ibhyash chakAra nR^itamo abhiShTau //4//
vavakSha indro amitam R^ijiShy ù1bhe A paprau rodasI mahitvA /
atash chid asya mahimA vi rechy abhi yo vishvA bhuvanA babhUva //5//
vishvAni shakro naryANi vidvAn apo rirecha sakhibhir nikAmaiH /
ashmAnaM chid ye bibhidur vachobhir vrajam gomantam ushijo vi vavruH //6//
apo vR^itraM vavrivAMsaM parAhan prAvat te vajraM pR^ithivI sachetAH /
prArNAMsi samudriyANy ainoH patir bhavaM chhavasA shUra dhR^iShNo //7//
apo yad adriM puruhUta dardar Avir bhuvat saramA pUrvyaM te /
sa no netA vAjam A darShi bhUriM gotrA rujann a~Ngirobhir gR^iNAnaH //8//
708

tad vo gAya sute sachA puruhUtAya satvane /
shaM yad gave na shAkine //1//
na ghA vasur ni yamate dAnaM vAjasya gomataH /
yat sIm upa shravad giraH //2//
kuvitsasya pra hi vrajaM gomantaM dasyuhA gamat /
shachIbhir apa no varat //3//
709

indra kratuM na A bhara pitA putrebhyo yathA /
shikShA No asmin puruhUta yAmani jIvA jyotir ashImahi //1//
mA no aj~nAtA vR^ijanA durAdhyo3 mAshivAso ava kramuH /
tvayA vayaM pravataH shashvatIr apo 'ti shUra tarAmasi //2//
710

indra jyeShThaM na A bharam? ojiShThaM papuri shravaH /
yeneme chitra vajrahasta rodasI obhe sushipra prAH //1//
tvAm ugram avase charShaNIsahaM rAjan deveShu hUmahe /
vishvA su no vithurA pibdanA vaso 'mitrAn suShahAn kR^idhi //2//
711

yad dyAva indra te shataM shataM bhUmir uta syuH /
na tvA vajrint sahasraM sUryA anu na jAtam aShTa rodasI //1//
A paprAtha mahinA kR^iShNyA vR^iShan vishvA shaviShTha shavasA /
asmA.N ava maghavan gomati vraje vajrim chitrAbhir UtibhiH //2//
712

yad indra yAvatas tvam etAvad aham IshIya /
stotAram id didhiSheya radAvaso na pApatvAya rAsIya //1//
shikSheyam in mahayate divedive rAya A kuhachidvide /
nahi tvad anyan maghavan na ApyaM vasyo asti pitA chana //2//
713

indra tridhAtu sharaNaM trivarUthaM svastimat /
chhardir yachha maghavadbhyash cha mahyaM cha yAvayA didyum ebhyaH //1//
ye gavyatA manasA shatrum Adabhur abhipraghnanti dhR^iShNuyA /
agha smA no maghavann indra girvaNas tanUpA antamo bhava //2//
714

indrA yAhi chitrabhAno sutA ime tvAyavaH /
aNvIbhis tanA pUtAsaH //1//
indrA yAhi dhiyeShito viprajutaH sutAvataH /
upa brahmANi vAghataH //2//
indrA yAhi tUtujAna upa brahmANi harivaH /
sute dadhiShva nash chanaH //3//
715

mA chid anyad vi shaMsata sakhAyo mA riShaNyata /
indram it stotA vR^iShaNaM sachA sute muhur ukthA cha shaMsata //1//
avakrakShiNaM vR^iShabhaM yathAjuram gAM na charShaNIsaham /
vidveShaNaM saMvananobhayaMkaraM maMhiShTham ubhayAvinam //2//
yach chid dhi tvA janA ime nAnA havanta Utaye /
asmAkaM brahmedam indra bhUtu te 'hA vishvA cha vardhanam //3//
vi tartUryante maghavan vipashchito 'ryo vipo janAnAm /
upa kramasva pururUpam A bhara vAjaM nediShTham Utaye //4//
716

brahmaNA te brahmayujA yunajmi harI sakhAyA sadhamAda AshU /
sthiraM rathaM sukham indrAdhitiShThan prajAnan vidvA.N upa yAhi somam //1//
717

adhvaryavo 'ruNaM dugdham aMshuM juhotana vR^iShabhAya kShitInAm /
gaurAd vedIyAm? avapAnam indro vishvAhed yAti sutasomam ichhan //1//
yad dadhiShe pradivi chArv annaM divedive pItim id asya vakShi /
uta hR^idota manasA juShANa ushann indra prasthitAn pAhi somAn //2//
jaj~nAnaH somaM sahase papAtha pra te mAtA mahimAnam uvAcha /
endra paprAthorv a1ntarikShaM yudhA devebhyo varivash chakartha //3//
yad yodhayA mahato manyamAnAn sAkShAma tAn bAhubhiH shAshadAnAn /
yad vA nR^ibhir vR^ita indrAbhiyudhyAs taM tvayAjiM saushravasaM jayema //4//
prendrasya vochaM prathamA kR^itAni pra nUtanA maghavA yA chakAra /
yaded adevIr asahiShTa mAyA athAbhavat kevalaH somo asya //5//
tavedaM vishvam abhitaH pashavya1M yat pashyasi chakShasA sUryasya /
gavAm asi gopatir eka indra bhakShImahi te prayatasya vasvaH //6//
bR^ihaspate yuvam indrash cha vasvo divyasyeshAthe uta pArthivasya /
dhattaM rayiM stuvate kIraye chid yUyaM pAta svastibhiH sadA naH //7//
718

yas tastambha sahasA vi jmo antAn bR^ihaspatis triShadhastho raveNa /
taM pratnAsa R^iShayo dIdhyAnAH puro viprA dadhire mandrajihvam //1//
dhunetayaH supraketaM madanto bR^ihaspate abhi ye nas tatasre /
pR^iShantaM sR^ipram adabdham UrvaM bR^ihaspate rakShatAd asya yonim //2//
bR^ihaspate yA paramA parAvad ata A te R^itaspR^isho ni SheduH /
tubhyaM khAtA avatA adridugdhA madhva shchotanty abhito virapsham //3//
bR^ihaspatiH prathamaM jAyamAno maho jyotiShaH parame vyoman /
saptAsyas tuvijAto raveNa vi saptarashmir adhamat tamAMsi //4//
sa suShTubhA sa R^ikvatA gaNena valaM ruroja phaligaM ravena /
bR^ihaspatir usriyA havyasUdaH kanikradad vAvashatIr ud Ajat //5//
evA pitre vishvadevAya vR^iShNe yaj~nair vidhema namasA havirbhiH /
bR^ihaspate suprajA vIravanto vayaM syAma patayo rayINAm //6//
719

asteva su prataraM lAyam asyan bhUShann iva pra bharA stomam asmai /
vAchA viprAs tarata vAcham aryo ni rAmaya jaritaH soma indram //1//
dohena gAm upa shikShA sakhAyaM pra bodhaya jaritar jAram indram /
koshaM na pUrNaM vasunA nyr?ShTam A chyAvaya maghadeyAya shUram //2//
kim a~Nga tvA maghavan bhojam AhuH shishIhi mA shishayaM tvA shR^iNomi /
apnasvatI mama dhIr astu shakra vasuvidaM bhagam indrA bharA naH //3//
tvAM janA mamasatyeShv indra saMtasthAnA vi hvayante samIke /
atrA yujaM kR^iNute yo haviShmAn nAsunvatA sakhyaM vaShTi shUraH //4//
dhanaM na spandraM bahulaM yo asmai tIvrAnt somAm? Asunoti prayasvAn /
tasmai shatrUnt sutukAn prAtar ahno ni svaShTrAn yuvati hanti vR^itram //5//
yasmin vayaM dadhimA shaMsam indre yaH shishrAya maghavA kAmam asme /
ArAch chit san bhayatAm asya shatrur ny asmai dyumnA janyA namantAm //6//
ArAch chhatrum apa bAdhasva dUram ugro yaH shambaH puruhUta tena /
asme dhehi yavamad gomad indra kR^idhI dhiyaM jaritre vAjaratnAm //7//
pra yam antar vR^iShasavAso ajman tIvrAH somA bahulAntAsa indram /
nAha dAmAnaM maghavA ni yaMsan ni sunvate vahati bhUri vAmam //8//
uta prahAm atidIvA jayati kR^itam iva shvaghnI vi chinoti kAle /
yo devakAmo na dhanaM ruNaddhi sam it taM rAyaH sR^ijati svadhAbhiH //9//
gobhiSh TaremAmatiM durevAM yavena vA kShudhaM puruhUta vishve /
vayaM rAjasu prathamA dhanAny ariShTAso vR^ijanIbhir jayema //10//
bR^ihaspatir naH pari pAtu pashchAd utottarasmAd adharAd aghayoH /
indraH purastAd uta madhyato naH sakhA sakhibhyo varIyaH kR^iNotu //11//
720

yo adribhit prathamajA R^itAvA bR^ihaspatir A~Ngiraso haviShmAn /
dvibarhajmA prAgharmasat pitA na A rodasI vR^iShabho roravIti //1//
janAya chid ya Ivate u lokaM bR^ihaspatir devahUtau chakAra /
ghnan vR^itrANi vi puro dardarIti jayaM chhatrUMr amitrAn pR^itsu sAhan //2//
bR^ihaspatiH sam ajayad vasUni maho vrajAn gomate deva eShaH /
apaH siShAsant sva1r apratIto bR^ihaspatir hanty amitram arkaiH //3//
721

imAM dhiyaM saptashIrShNIM pitA na R^itaprajAtAM bR^ihatIm avindat /
turIyaM svij janayad vishvajanyo 'yAsya uktham indrAya shaMsan //1//
R^itaM shaMsanta R^iju dIdhyAnA divas putrAso asurasya vIrAH /
vipraM padam a~Ngiraso dadhAnA yaj~nasya dhAma prathamaM mananta //2//
haMsair iva sakhibhir vAvadadbhir ashmanmayAni nahanA vyasyan /
bR^ihaspatir abhikanikradad gA uta prAstaud uch cha vidvA.N agAyat //3//
avo dvAbhyAM para ekayA gA guhA tiShThantIr anR^itasya setau /
bR^ihaspatis tamasi jyotir ichhan ud usrA Akar vi hi tisra AvaH //4//
vibhidyA puraM shayathem apAchIM nis trINi sAkam udadher akR^intat /
bR^ihaspatir uShasaM sUryaM gAm arkaM viveda stanayann iva dyauH //5//
indro valaM rakShitAraM dughAnAM kareNeva vi chakartA raveNa /
svedA~njibhir Ashiram ichhamAno 'rodayat paNim A gA amuShNAt //6//
sa IM satyebhiH sakhibhiH shuchadbhir godhAyasaM vi dhanasair adardaH /
brahmaNas patir vR^iShabhir varAhair gharmasvedebhir draviNaM vy AnaT //7//
te satyena manasA gopatiM gA iyAnAsa iShaNayanta dhIbhiH /
bR^ihaspatir mithoavadyapebhir ud usriyA asR^ijata svayugbhiH //8//
taM vardhayanto matibhiH shivAbhiH siMham iva nAnadataM sadhasthe /
bR^ihaspatiM vR^iShaNaM shUrasAtau bharebhare anu madema jiShNum //9//
yadA vAjam asanad vishvarUpam A dyAm arukShad uttarANi sadma /
bR^ihaspatiM vR^iShaNaM vardhayanto nAnA santo bibhrato jyotir AsA //10//
satyam AshiShaM kR^iNutA vayodhai kIriM chid dhy avatha svebhir evaiH /
pashchA mR^idho apa bhavantu vishvAs tad rodasI shR^iNutaM vishvaminve //11//
indro mahnA mahato arNavasya vi mUrdhAnam abhinad arbudasya /
ahann ahim ariNAt sapta sindhUn devair dyAvApR^ithivI prAvataM naH //12//
722

abhi pra gopatiM girendram archa yathA vide /
sUtuM satyasya satpatim //1//
A harayaH sasR^ijrire 'ruShIr adhi barhiShi /
yatrAbhi saMnavAmahe //2//
indrAya gAva AshiraM duduhre vajriNe madhu /
yat sIm upahvare vidat //3//
ud yad bradhnasya viShTapaM gR^iham indrash cha ganvahi /
madhvaH pItvA sachevahi triH sapta sakhyuH pade //4//
archata prArchata priyamedhAso archata /
archantu putrakA uta puraM na dhR^iShNv archata //5//
ava svarAti gargaro godhA pari saniShvaNat /
pi~NgA pari chaniShkadad indrAya brahmodyatam //6//
A yat patanty enyaH sudughA anapasphuraH /
apasphuraM gR^ibhAyata somam indrAya pAtave //7//
apAd indro apAd agnir vishve devA amatsata /
varuNa id iha kShayat tam Apo abhy anUShata vatsaM saMshishvarIr iva //8//
sudevo asi varuNa yasya te sapta sindhavaH /
anukSharanti kAkudaM sUryaM suShirAm iva //9//
yo vyatIm?r aphANayat suyuktAm? upa dAshuShe /
takvo netA tad id vapur upamA yo amuchyata //10//
atId u shakra ohata indro vishvA ati dviShaH /
bhinat kanIna odanaM pachyamAnaM paro girA //11//
arbhako na kumArako 'dhi tiShThann avaM ratham /
sa pakShan mahiShaM mR^igaM pitre mAtre vibhukratum //12//
A tU sushipra daMpate rathaM tiShThA hiraNyayam /
adha dyukShaM sachevahi sahasrapAdam aruShaM svastigAm anehasam //13//
tam ghem itthA namasvina upa svarAjam Asate /
arthaM chid asya sudhitaM yad etave Avartayanti dAvane //14//
anu pratnasyaukasaH priyamedhAsa eShAm /
pUrvAm anu prayatiM vR^iktabarhiSho hitaprayasa Ashata //15//
yo rAjA charShaNInAM yAtA rathebhir adhriguH /
vishvAsAM tarutA pR^itanAnAM jyeShTho yo vR^itrahA gR^iNe //16//
indraM taM shumbha puruhanmann avase yasya dvitA vidhartari /
hastAya vajraH prati dhAyi darshato maho dive na sUryaH //17//
nakiSh TaM karmaNA nashad yash chakAra sadAvR^idham /
indraM na yaj~nair vishvagUrtam R^ibhvasam adhR^iShTaM dhR^iShNvojasam //18//
aShAlham ugraM pR^itanAsu sAsahiM yasmin mahIr urujrayaH /
saM dhenavo jAyamAne anonavur dyAvaH kShAmo anonavuH //19//
yad dyAva indra te shataM shataM bhUmIr uta syuH /
na tvA vajrint sahasraM sUryA anu na jAtam aShTa rodasI //20//
A paprAtha mahinA vR^iShNyA vR^iShan vishvA shaviShTha shavasA /
asmA.N ava maghavan gomati vraje vajriM chitrAbhir UtibhiH //21//
723

ut tvA mandantu stomAH kR^iNuShva rAdho adrivaH /
ava brahmadviSho jahi //1//
padA paNI.Nr arAdhaso ni bAdhasva mahA.N asi /
nahi tvA kash chana prati //2//
tvam IshiShe sutAnAm indra tvam asutAnAm /
tvaM rAjA janAnAm //3//
I~NkhayantIr apasyuva indraM jAtam upAsate /
bhejAnAsaH suvIryam //4//
tvam indra balAd adhi sahaso jAta ojasaH /
tvaM vR^iShan vR^iShed asi //5//
tvam indrAsi vR^itrahA vy a1ntarikSham atiraH /
ud dyAm astabhnA ojasA //6//
tvam indra sajoShasam arkaM bibharShi bAhvoH /
vajraM shishAna ojasA //7//
tvam indrAbhibhur asi vishvA jAtAny ojasA /
sa vishvA bhuva Abhavah //8//
724

A yAtv indraH svapatir madAya yo dharmaNA tUtujAnas tuviShmAn /
pratvakShANo ati vishvA sahAMsy apAreNa mahatA vR^iShNyena //1//
suShThAmA rathaH suyamA harI te mimyakSha vajro nR^ipate gabhastau /
shIbhaM rAjan supathA yAhy arvA~N vardhAma te papuso vR^iShNyAni //2//
endravAho nR^ipatiM vajrabAhum ugram ugrAsas taviShAsa enam /
pratvakShasaM vR^iShabhaM satyashuShmam em asmatrA sadhamAdo vahantu //3//
evA patiM droNasAchaM sachetasam Urja skambhaM dharuNa A vR^iShAyase /
ojaH kR^iShva saM gR^ibhAya tve apy aso yathA kenipAnAm ino vR^idhe //4//
gamann asme vasUny A hi shaMsiShaM svAshiShaM bharam A yAhi sominaH /
tvam IshiShe sAsminn A satsi barhiShy anAdhR^iShyA tava pAtrANi dharmaNA //5//
pR^ithak prAyan prathamA devahUtayo 'kR^iNvata shravasyAni duShTarA /
na ye shekur yaj~niyAM nAvam Aruham irmaiva te ny avishanta kepayaH //6//
evaivApAg apare santu dUdhyo 'shvA yeShAM duryuga Ayuyujre /
itthA ye prAg upare santi dAvane purUNi yatra vayunAni bhojanA //7//
girI.Nr ajrAn rejamAnAm? adhArayad dyauH krandad antarikShANi kopayat /
samIchIne dhiShaNe vi ShkabhAyati vR^iShNaH pItvA mada ukthAni shaMsati //8//
imaM bibharmi sukR^itaM te a~NkushaM yenArujAsi maghavaM chhaphArujaH /
asmint su te savane astv oktyaM suta iShTau maghavan bodhy AbhagaH //9//
gobhiSh TaremAmatiM durevAM yavena kShudhaM puruhUta vishvAm /
vayaM rAjabhiH prathamA dhanAny asmAkena vR^ijanenA jayema //10//
bR^ihaspatir naH pari pAtu pashchAd utottarasmAd adharAd aghayoH /
indraH purastAd uta madhyato naH sakhA sakhibhyo varivaH kR^iNotu //11//
725

trikadrukeShu mahiSho yavAshiraM tuvishuShmas tR^ipat somam apibad viShNunA sutaM yathAvashat /
sa IM mamAda mahi karma kartave mahAm uruM sainaM sashchad devo devaM satyam indraM satya induH //1//
pro Shv asmai puroratham indrAya shUSham archata /
abhIke chid u lokakR^it saMge samatsu vR^itrahAsmAkaM bodhi choditA nabhantAm anyakeShAM jyAkA adhi dhanvasu //2//
tvaM sindhUm?r avAsR^ijo 'dharAcho ahann ahim /
ashatrur indra jaj~niShe vishvaM puShyasi vAryaM taM tvA pari ShvajAmahe nabhantAm anyakeShAM jyAkA adhi dhanvasu //3//
vi Shu vishvA arAtayo 'ryo nashanta no dhiyaH /
astAsi shatrave vadhaM yo na indra jighAMsati yA te rAtir dadir vasu /
nabhantAm anyakeShAM jyakA adhi dhanvasu //4//
726

tIvrasyAbhivayaso asya pAhi sarvarathA vi harI iha mu~ncha /
indra mA tvA yajamAnAso anye ni rIraman tubhyam ime sutAsaH //1//
tubhyaM sutAs tubhyam u sotvAsas tvAM giraH shvAtryA A hvayanti /
indredam adya savanaM juShANo vishvasya vidvA.N iha pAhi somam //2//
ya ushatA manasA somam asmai sarvahR^idA devakAmaH sunoti /
na gA indras tasya parA dadAti prashastam ich chArum asmai kR^iNoti //3//
anuspaShTo bhavaty eSho asya yo asmai revAn na sunoti somam /
nir aratnau maghavA taM dadhAti brahmadviSho hanty anAnudiShTaH //4//
ashvAyanto gavyanto vAjayanto havAmahe tvopagantavA u /
AbhUShantas te sumatau navAyAM vayam indra tvA shunaM huvema //5//
mu~nchAmi tvA haviShA jIvanAya kam aj~nAtayakShmAd uta rAjayakShmAt /
grAhir jagrAha yady etad tasyA indrAgnI pra mumuktam enam //6//
yadi kShitAyur yadi vA pareto yadi mR^ityor antikaM nIta eva /
tam A harAmi nirR^iter upasthAd aspArsham enaM shatashAradAya //7//
sahasrAkSheNa shatavIryeNa shatAyuShA haviShAhArSham enam /
indro yathainaM sharado nayAty ati vishvasya duritasya pAram //8//
shataM jIva sharado vardhamAnaH shataM hemantAn chhatam u vasantAn /
shataM ta indro agniH savitA bR^ihaspatiH shatAyuShA haviShAhArSham enam //9//
AhArSham avidaM tvA punar AgAH punarNavaH /
sarvA~Nga sarvaM te chakShuH sarvam Ayush cha te 'vidam //10//
brahmaNAgniH samvidAno rakShohA bAdhatAm itaH /
amIvA yas te garbhaM durNAmA yonim Ashaye //11//
yas te garbham amIvA durNAmA yonim Ashaye /
agniSh TaM brahmaNA saha niSh kravyAdam anInashat //12//
yas te hanti patayantaM niShatsnuM yaH sarIsR^ipam /
jAtaM yas te jighAMsati tam ito nAshayAmasi //13//
yas ta UrU viharaty antarA dampatI shaye /
yoniM yo antar Arelhi tam ito nAshayAmasi //14//
yas tvA bhrAtA patir bhUtvA jAro bhUtvA nipadyate /
prajAM yas te jighAMsati tam ito nAshayAmasi //15//
yas tvA svapnena tamasA mohayitvA nipadyate /
prajAM yas te jighAMsati tam ito nAshayAmasi //16//
akShIbhyAM te nAsikAbhyAM karNAbhyAM chhubukAd adhi /
yakShmaM shIrShaNyaM mastiShkAj jihvAyA vi vR^ihAmi te //17//
grIvAbhyas ta uShNihAbhyaH kIkasAbhyo anUkyAt /
yakShmaM doShaNya1m aMsAbhyAM bAhubhyAM vi vR^ihAmi te //18//
hR^idayAt te pari klomno halIkShNAt pArshvAbhyAm /
yakShmaM matasnAbhyAM plIhno yaknas te vi vR^ihAmasi //19//
 {19}
Antrebhyas te gudAbhyo vaniShThor udarAd adhi /
yakShmaM kukShibhyAM plAsher nAbhyA vi vR^ihAmi te //20//
 {20}
UrubhyAM te aShThIvadbhyAM pArShNibhyAM prapadAbhyAm /
yakShmaM bhasadya1M shroNibhyAM bhAsadaM bhAMsaso vi vR^ihAmi te //21//
mehanAd vanaMkaraNAl lomabhyas te nakhebhyaH /
yaksmaM sarvasmAd Atmanas tam idaM vi vR^ihAmi te //22//
{22}asthibhyas te majjabhyaH snAvabhyo dhamanibhyaH
yaksmaM pANibhyAm a~Ngulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vR^ihAmi te //22//
 {22}
a~Ngea~Nge lomnilomni yas te parvaNiparvaNi /
yakShaM tvachasyaM te vayaM kashyapasya vIbarheNa viShva~nchaM vi vR^ihAmasi //23//
 {23}
apehi manasas pate 'pa kAma parash chara /
paro nirR^ityA A chakShva bahudhA jIvato manaH //24//
727

vayam enam idA hyopIpemeha vajriNam /
tasmA u adya samanA sutaM bharA nUnaM bhUShata shrute //1//
vR^ikash chid asya vAraNa urAmathir A vayuneShu bhUShati /
semaM naH stomaM jujuShANa A gahIndra pra chitrayA dhiyA //2//
kad u nv asyAkR^itam indrasyAsti pauMsyam /
keno nu kaM shromatena na shushruve januShaH pari vR^itrahA //3//
728

tvAm id dhi havAmahe sAtA vAjasya kAravaH /
tvAM vR^itreShv indra satpatiM naras tvAM kAShThAsv arvataH //1//
sa tvaM nash chitra vajrahasta dhR^iShNuyA maha stavAno adrivaH /
gAm ashvaM rathyam indra saM kira satrA vAjaM na jigyuShe //2//
729

abhi tvA pUrvapItaya indra stomebhir AyavaH /
samIchInAsa R^ibhavaH sam asvaran rudrA gR^iNanta pUrvyam //1//
asyed indro vAvR^idhe vR^iShNyaM shavo made sutasya viShNavi /
adyA tam asya mahimAnam Ayavo 'nu ShTuvanti pUrvathA //2//
730

adhA hIndra girvaNa upa tvA kAmAn mahaH sasR^ijmahe /
udeva yanta udabhiH //1//
vAr Na tvA yavyAbhir vardhanti shUra brahmANi /
vAvR^idhvAMsaM chid adrivo divedive //2//
yu~njanti harI iShirasya gAthayorau ratha uruyuge /
indravAhA vachoyujA //3//
731

agniM dUtaM vR^iNImahe hotAraM vishvavedasam /
asya yaj~nasya sukratum //1//
agnimagniM havImabhiH sadA havanta vishpatim /
havyavAhaM purupriyam //2//
agne devA.N ihA vaha jaj~nAno vR^iktabarhiShe /
asi hotA na IDyaH //3//
732

Ilenyo namasyas tiras tamAMsi darshataH /
sam agnir idhyate vR^iShA //1//
vR^iSho agniH sam idhyate 'shvo na devavAhanaH /
taM haviShmantaH Ilate //2//
vR^iShaNaM tvA vayaM vR^iShan vR^iShaNaH sam idhImahi /
agne dIdyataM bR^ihat //3//
733

agnim IliShvAvase gAthAbhiH shIrashochiSham /
agniM rAye purumIlha shrutaM naro 'gniM sudItaye chhardiH //1//
agna A yAhy agnibhir hotAraM tvA vR^iNImahe /
A tvAm anaktu prayatA haviShmatI yajiShThaM barhir Asade //2//
achha hi tvA sahasaH sUno a~NgiraH sruchash charanty adhvare /
Urjo napAtaM ghR^itakesham Imahe 'gniM yaj~neShu pUrvyam //3//
734

imA u tvA purUvaso giro vardhantu yA mama /
pAvakavarNAH shuchayo vipashchito 'bhi stomair anUShata //1//
ayaM sahasram R^iShibhiH sahaskR^itaH samudra iva paprathe /
satyaH so asya mahimA gR^ine shavo yaj~neShu viprarAjye //2//
A no vishvAsu havya indraH samatsu bhUShatu /
upa brahmANi savanAni vR^itrahA paramajyA R^ichIShamaH //3//
tvaM dAtA prathamo rAghasAm asy asi satya IshAnakR^it /
tuvidyumnasya yujyA vR^iNImahe putrasya shavaso mahaH //4//
735

tvam indra pratUrtiShv abhi vishvA asi spR^idhaH /
ashastihA janitA vishvatUr asi tvaM tUrya taruShyataH //1//
anu te shuShmaM turayantam IyatuH kShoNI shishuM na mAtarA /
vishvAs te spR^idhaH shnathayanta manyave vR^itraM yad indra tUrvasi //2//
ita UtI vo ajaraM prahetAram aprahitam /
AshuM jetAraM hetAraM rathItamam atUrtaM tugryAvR^idham //3//
yo rAjA charShaNInAM yAtA rathebhir adhriguH /
vishvAsAM tarutA pR^itanAnAM jyeShTho yo vR^itrahA gR^iNe //4//
indraM taM shumbha puruhanmann avase yasya dvitA vidhartari /
hastAya vajraH prati dhAyi darshato maho dive na sUryaH //5//
736

tava tyad indriyaM bR^ihat tava shuShmam uta kratum /
vajraM shishAti dhiShaNA vareNyam //1//
tava dyaur indra pauMsyaM pR^ithivI vardhati shravaH /
tvAm ApaH parvatAsash cha hinvire //2//
tvAM viShNur bR^ihan kShayo mitro gR^iNAti varuNaH /
tvAM shardho madaty anu mArutam //3//
737

sam asya manyave visho vishvA namanta kuShTayaH /
samudrAyeva sindhavaH //1//
ojas tad asya titviSha ubhe yat samavartayat /
indrash charmeva rodasI //2//
vi chid vR^itrasya dodhato vajreNa shataparvaNA /
shiro bibhed vR^iShNinA //3//
tad id Asa bhuvaneShu jyeShThaM yato jaj~na ugras tveShanR^imNaH /
sadyo jaj~nAno ni riNAti shatrUn anu yad enaM madanti vishva UmAH //4//
vAvR^idhAnaH shavasA bhUryojAH shatrur dAsAya bhiyasaM dadhAti /
avyanach cha vyanach cha sasni saM te navanta prabhR^itA madeShu //5//
tve kratum api pR^i~nchanti bhUri dvir yad ete trir bhavanty UmAH /
svAdoH svAdIyaH svAdunA sR^ijA sam adaH su madhu madhunAbhi yodhIH //6//
yadi chin nu tvA dhanA jayantaM raNeraNe anumadanti viprAH /
ojIyaH shuShmint sthiram A tanuShva mA tvA dabhan durevAsaH kashokAH //7//
tvayA vayaM shAshadmahe raNeShu prapashyanto yudhenyAni bhUri /
chodayAmi ta AyudhA vachobhiH saM te shishAmi brahmaNA vayAMsi //8//
ni tad dadhiShe 'vare pare cha yasminn AvithAvasA duroNe /
A sthApayata mAtaraM jigatnum ata invata karvarANi bhUri //9//
stuShva varShman puruvartmAnaM sam R^ibhvANam inatamam Aptam AptyAnAm /
A darshati shavasA bhUryojAH pra sakShati pratimAnaM pR^ithivyAH //10//
imA brahma bR^ihaddivaH kR^iNavad indrAya shUSham agniyaH svarShAH /
maho gotrasya kShayati svarAjA turash chid vishvam arNavat tapasvAn //11//
evA mahAn bR^ihaddivo atharvAvochat svAM tanva1m indram eva /
svasArau mAtaribhvarI aripre hinvanti chaine shavasA vardhayanti cha //12//
chitraM devAnAM ketur anIkaM jyotiShmAn pradishaH sUrya udyan /
divAkaro 'ti dyumnais tamAMsi vishvAtArId duritAni shukraH //13//
chitraM devAnAm ud agAd anIkaM chakShur mitrasya varuNasyAgneH /
AprAd dyAvApR^ithivI antarikShaM sUrya AtmA jagatas tasthuShash cha //14//
sUryo devIm uShasaM rochamAnAM maryo na yoShAm abhy eti pashchAt /
yatrA naro devayanto yugAni vitanvate prati bhadrAya bhadram //15//
738

tvaM na indrA bharam? ojo nR^imNaM shatakrato vicharShaNe /
A vIraM pR^itanAShaham //1//
tvaM hi naH pitA vaso tvaM mAtA shatakrato babhUvitha /
adhA te sumnam Imahe //2//
tvAM shuShmin puruhUta vAjayantam upa bruve shatakrato /
sa no rAsva suvIryam //3//
739

svAdor itthA viShuvato madhvaH pibanti gauryaH /
yA indreNa sayAvarIr vR^iShNA madanti shobhase vasvIr anu svarAjyam //1//
tA asya pR^ishanAyuvaH somaM shrINanti pR^ishnayaH /
priyA indrasya dhenavo vajraM hinvanti sAyakaM vasvIr anu svarAjyam //2//
tA asya namasA sahaH saparyanti prachetasaH /
vratAny asya sashchire purUNi pUrvachittaye vasvIr anu svarAjyam //3//
740

indrAya madUne sutaM pari ShTobhantu no giraH /
arkam archantu kAravaH //1//
yasmin vishvA adhi shriyo raNanti sapta saMsadaH /
indraM sute havAmahe //2//
trikadrukeShu chetanaM devAso yaj~nam atnata /
tam id vardhantu no giraH //3//
741

yat somam indra viShNavi yad vA gha trita Aptye /
yad vA marutsu mandase sam indubhiH //1//
yad vA shakra parAvati samudre adhi mandase /
asmAkam it sute raNA sam indubhiH //2//
yad vAsi sunvato vR^idho yajamAnasya satpate /
ukthe vA yasya raNyasi sam indubhiH //3//
742

yad adya kach cha vR^itrahann udagA abhi sUrya /
sarvaM tad indra te vashe //1//
yad vA pravR^iddha satpate na marA iti manyase /
uto tat satyam it tava //2//
ye somAsaH parAvati ye arvAvati sunvire /
sarvAMs tA.N indra gachhasi //3//
743

ubhayaM shR^iNavach cha na indro arvAg idaM vachaH /
satrAchyA maghavA somapItaye dhiyA shaviShTha A gamat //1//
taM hi svarAjaM vR^iShabhaM tam ojase dhiShaNe niShTatakShatuH /
utopamAnAM prathamo ni ShIdasi somakAmaM hi te manaH //2//
744

abhrAtR^ivyo'anA tvam anApir indra januShA sanAd asi /
yudhed Apitvam ichhase //1//
nakI revantaM sakhyAya vindase pIyanti te surAshvaH /
yadA kR^iNoShi nadanuM sam Uhasy Ad it piteva hUyase //2//
745

aham id dhi pituSh pari medhAm R^itasya jagrabha /
ahaM sUrya ivAjani //1//
ahaM pratnena manmanA giraH shumbhAmi kaNvavat /
yenendraH shuShmam id dadhe //2//
ye tvAm indra na tuShTuvur R^iShayo ye cha tuShTuvuH /
mamed vardhasva suShTutaH //3//
746

mA bhUma niShTyA ivendra tvad araNA iva /
vanAni ni prajahitAny adrivo duroShAso amanmahi //1//
amanmahId anAshavo 'nugrAsash cha vR^itrahan /
sukR^it su te mahatA shUra rAdhasAnu stomaM mudImahi //2//
747

pibA somam indra mandatu tvA yaM te suShAva haryashvAdriH /
sotur bAhubhyAM suyato nArvA //1//
yas te mado yujas chArur asti yena vR^itrANi haryashva haMsi /
sa tvAm indra prabhUvaso mamattu //2//
bodhA su me maghavan vAcham emAM yAM te vasiShTho archati prashastim /
imA brahma sadhamAde juShasva //3//
748

shagdhy U Shu shachIpata indra vishvAbhir UtibhiH /
bhagaM na hi tvA yashasaM vasuvidam anu shUra charAmasi //1//
pauro ashvasya purukR^id gavAm asy utso deva hiraNyayaH /
nakir hi dAnaM parimardhiShat tve yadyad yAmi tad A bhara //2//
indram id devatAtaye indraM prayaty adhvare /
indraM samIke vanino havAmaha indraM dhanasya sAtaye //3//
indro mahnA rodasI paprathach chhava indraH sUryam arochayat /
indre ha vishvA bhuvanAni yemira indre suvAnAsa indavaH //4//
749

astAvi manma pUrvyaM brahmendrAya vochata /
pUrvIr R^itasya bR^ihatIr anUShata stotur meghA asR^ikShata //1//
turaNyavo madhumantaM ghR^itashchutaM viprAso arkam AnR^ichuH /
asme rayiH paprathe vR^iShNyaM shavo 'sme suvAnAsa indavaH //2//
750

yad indra prAg apAg uda~N nyag vA hUyase nR^ibhiH /
simA purU nR^iShUto asy Anave 'si prashardha turvashe //1//
yad vA rume rushame shyAvake kR^ipa indra mAdayase sachA /
kaNvAsas tvA brahmabhi stomavAhasa indrA yachhanty A gahi //2//
751

abhi tvA shUra nonumo 'dugdhA iva dhenavaH /
IshAnam asya jagataH svardR^isham IshAnam indra tasthuShaH //1//
na tvAvAm? anyo divyo na pArthivo na jAto na janiShyate /
ashvAyanto maghavann indra vAjino gavyantas tvA havAmahe //2//
752

revatIr naH sadhamAda indre santu tuvivAjAH /
kShumanto yAbhir madema //1//
A gha tvAvAn tmanApta stotR^ibhyo dhR^iShNav iyAnaH /
R^iNor akShaM na chakrayoH //2//
A yad duvaH shatakratav A kAmaM jaritR^INAm /
R^iNor akShaM na shachIbhiH //3//
753

tat sUryasya devatvaM tan mahitvaM madhyA kartor vitataM saM jabhAra /
yaded ayukta haritaH sadhasthAd Ad rAtrI vAsas tanute simasmai //1//
tan mitrasya varuNasyAbhichakShe sUryo rUpaM kR^iNute dyor upasthe /
anantam anyad rushad asya prAjaH kR^iShNam anyad dharitaH saM bharanti //2//
754

kayA nash chitra A bhuvad UtI sadAvR^idhaH sakhA /
kayA shachiShThayA vR^itA //1//
kas tvA satyo madAnAM maMhiShTho matsad andhasaH /
dR^ilhA chid Aruje vasu //2//
abhI Shu naH sakhInAm avitA jaritR^INAm /
shataM bhavAsy UtibhiH //3//
imA nu kaM bhuvanA sIShadhAmendrash cha vishve cha devAH /
yaj~naM cha nas tanvaM cha prajAM chAdityair indraH saha chIk?pAti //4//
Adityair indraH sagaNo marudbhir asmAkaM bhUtv avitA tanUnAm /
hatvAya devA asurAn yad Ayan devA devatvam abhirakShamANAH //5//
pratya~ncham arkam anayaM chhachIbhir Ad it svadhAm iShirAm pary apashyan /
ayA vAjaM devahitaM sanema madema shatahimAH suvIrAH //6//
755

apendra prAcho maghavann amitrAn apApAcho abhibhUte nudasva /
apodIcho apa shUrAdharAcha urau yathA tava sharman madema //1//
kuvid a~Nga yavamanto yavaM chid yathA dAnty anupUrvaM viyUya /
ihehaiShAM kR^iNuhi bhojanAni ye barhiSho namovR^iktiM na jagmuH //2//
nahi sthUry R^ituthA yAtam asti nota shravo vivide saMgameShu /
gavyanta indraM sakhyAya viprA ashvAyanto vR^iShaNaM vAjayantaH //3//
yuvaM surAmam ashvinA namuchAv Asure sachA /
vipipAnA shubhas patI indraM karmasv Avatam //4//
putram iva pitarAv ashvinobhendrAvathuH kAvyair daMsanAbhiH /
yat surAmaM vy apibaH shachIbhiH sarasvatI tvA maghavann abhiShNak //5//
indraH sutrAmA svavAm? avobhiH sumR^iDIko bhavatu vishvavedAH /
bAdhatAM dveSho abhayaM naH kR^iNotu suvIryasya patayaH syAma //6//
sa sutrAmA svavAm? indro asmad ArAch chid dveShaH sanutar yuyotu /
tasya vayaM sumatau yaj~niyasyApi bhadre saumanase syAma //7//
756

vi hi sotor asR^ikShata nendraM devam amaMsata /
yatrAmadad vR^iShAkapir aryaH puShTeShu matsakhA vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //1//
parA hIndra dhAvasi vR^iShAkaper ati vyathiH /
no aha pra vindasy anyatra somapItaye vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //2//
kim ayaM tvAM vR^iShAkapish chakAra harito mR^igaH /
yasmA irasyasId u nv a1ryo vA puShTimad vasu vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //3//
yam imaM tvaM vR^iShAkapiM priyam indrAbhirakShasi /
shvA nv asya jambhiShad api karNe varAhayur vishvasmAd indra uttarah //4//
priyA taShTAni me kapir vyaktA vy adUduShat /
shiro nv asya rAviShaM na sugaM duShkR^ite bhuvaM vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //5//
na mat strI subhasattarA na suyAshutarA bhuvat /
na mat pratichyavIyasI na sakthy udyamIyasI vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //6//
uve amba sulAbhike yathevA~NgaM bhaviShyati /
bhasan me amba sakthi me shiro me vIva hR^iShyati vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //7//
kiM subAho sva~Ngure pR^ithuShTo pR^ithujAghane /
kiM shUrapatni nas tvam abhy amIShi vR^iShAkapiM vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //8//
avIrAm iva mAm ayaM sharArur abhi manyate /
utAham asmi vIriNIndrapatnI marutsakhA vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //9//
saMhotraM sma purA nArI samanaM vAva gachhati /
vedhA R^itasya vIriNIndrapatnI mahIyate vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //10//
indrANIm Asu nAriShu subhagAm aham ashravam /
nahy asyA aparaM chana jarasA marate patir vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //11//
nAham indrANi rAraNa sakhyur vR^iShAkaper R^ite /
yasyedam apyaM haviH priyaM deveShu gachhati vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //12//
vR^isAkapAyi revati suputra Ad u susnuShe /
ghasat ta indra ukShaNaH priyaM kAchitkaraM havir vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //13//
ukShNo hi me pa~nchadasha sAkaM pachanti viMsatim /
utAham admi pIva id ubhA kukShI pR^iNanti me vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //14//
vR^iShabho na tigmashR^i~Ngo 'ntar yUtheShu roruvat /
manthas ta indra shaM hR^ide yaM te sunoti bhAvayur vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //15//
na seshe yasya rambate 'ntarA sakthyA3 kapR^it /
sedIshe yasya romashaM niSheduSho vijR^imbhate vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //16//
na seshe yasya romashaM niSheduSho vijR^imbhate /
sedIshe yasya rambate 'ntarA sakthyA3 kapR^it vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //17//
ayam indra vR^iShAkapiH parasvantaM hataM vidat /
asiM sUnAM navaM charum Ad edhasyAna AchitaM vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //18//
ayam emi vichAkashad vichinvan dAsam Aryam /
pibAmi pAkasutvano 'bhi dhIram achAkashaM vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //19//
dhanva cha yat kR^intatraM cha kati svit tA vi yojanA /
nedIyaso vR^iShAkape 'stam ehi gR^ihA.N upa vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //20//
punar ehi vR^iShAkape suvitA kalpayAvahai /
ya eSha svapnanaMshano 'stam eShi pathA punar vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //21//
yad uda~ncho vR^iShAkape gR^iham indrAjagantana /
kva1 sya pulvagho mR^igaH kam agaM janayopano vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //22//
parshur ha nAma mAnavI sAkaM sasUva viMshatim /
bhadraM bhala tyasyA abhUd yasyA udaram Amayad vishvasmAd indra uttaraH //23//
757

idaM janA upa shruta narAshaMsa staviShyate /
ShaShTiM sahasrA navatiM cha kaurama A rushameShu dadmahe //1//
uShTrA yasya pravAhaNo vadhUmanto dvirdasha /
varShmA rathasya ni jihIDate diva IShamANA upaspR^ishaH //2//
eSha iShAya mAmahe shataM niShkAn dasha srajaH /
trINi shatAnyarvatAM sahasrA dasha gonAm //3//
vachyasva rebha vachyasva vR^ikShe na pakve shakunaH /
naShTe jihvA charcharIti kShuro na bhurijoriva //4//
pra rebhAso manIShA vR^iShA gAva iverate /
amotaputrakA eShAmamota gA ivAsate //5//
pra rebha dhIm bharasva govidaM vasuvidam /
devatremAM vAchaM srINIhIShurnAvIrastAram //6//
rAj~no vishvajanInasya yo devomartyAm? ati /
vaishvAnarasya suShTutimA sunotA parikShitaH //7//
parichhinnaH kShemam akarot tama AsanamAcharan /
kulAyan kR^iNvan kauravyaH patir vadati jAyayA //8//
katarat ta A harANi dadhi manthAM pari shrutam /
jAyAh patiM vi pR^ichhati rAShTre rAj~naH parikShitaH //9//
abhIvasvaH pra jihIte yavaH pakvaH patho bilam /
janaH sa bhadram edhati rAShTre rAj~naH parikShitah //10//
indraH kArum abUbudhad uttiShTha vi charA janam /
mamedugrasya charkR^idhi sarva it te pR^iNAdariH //11//
iha gAvaH pra jAyadhvam ihAshvA iha pUruShAH /
iho sahasradakShiNopi pUShA ni ShIdati //12//
nemA indra gAvo riShan mo AsAM gopa rIriShat /
mAsAm amitrayurjana indra mA stena Ishata //13//
upa no na ramasi sUktena vachasA vayaM bhadreNa vachasA vayam /
vanAd adhidhvano giro na riShyema kadA chana //14//
758

yaH sabheyo vidathyaH sutvA yajvAtha pUruShaH /
sUryaM chAmU rishAdasas tad devAH prAg akalpayan //1//
yo jAmyA aprathayas tad yat sakhAyaM dudhUrShati /
jyeShTho yad aprachetAs tad Ahur adharAg iti //2//
yad bhadrasya puruShasya putro bhavati dAdhR^iShiH /
tad vipro abravId u tad gandharvaH kAmyaM vachaH //3//
yashcha paNi raghujiShThyo yashcha devA.N adAshuriH /
dhIrANAM shashvatAm ahaM tad apAg iti shushruma //4//
ye cha devA ayajantAtho ye cha parAdadiH /
sUryo divam iva gatvAya maghavA no vi rapshate //5//
yo'nAktAkSho anabhyakto amaNivo ahiraNyavaH /
abrahmA brahmaNaH putrastotA kalpeShu saMmitA //6//
ya AktAkShaH subhyaktaH sumaNiH suhiraNyavaH /
subrahmA brahmaNaH putrastotA kalpeShu saMmitA //7//
aprapANA cha veshantA revA.N apratidishyayaH /
ayabhyA kanyA kalyANI totA kalpeShu saMmitA //8//
suprapANA cha veshantA revAnt supratidishyayaH /
suyabhyA kanyA kalyANI totA kalpeShu saMmitA //9//
parivR^iktA cha mahiShI svastyA cha yudhiM gamaH /
anAshurashchAyAmI totA kalpeShu saMmitA //10//
vAvAtA cha mahiShI svastyA cha yudhiM gamaH /
shvAshurash chAyAmI totA kalpeShu saMmitA //11//
yad indrAdo dAsharAj~ne mAnuShaM vi gAhathAH /
virUpaH sarvasmA AsIt saha yakShAya kalpate //12//
tvaM vR^iShAkShuM maghavann amraM maryAkaro raviH /
tvaM rauhiNaM vyAsyo vi vR^itrasyAbhinach chhiraH //13//
yaH parvatAn vyadadhAd yo apo vyagAhathAH /
indro yo vR^itrahAnmahaM tasmAd indra namo 'stu te //14//
pR^iShThaM dhAvantaM haryor auchchaiH shravasam abruvan /
svastyashva jaitrAyendram A vaha susrajam //15//
ye tvA shvetA ajaishravaso hAryo yu~njanti dakShiNam /
pUrvA namasya devAnAM bibhrad indra mahIyate //16//
759

etA ashvA A plavante //1//
pratIpaM prAti sutvanam //2//
tAsAm ekA hariknikA //3//
hariknike kim ichhAsi //4//

760

ko arya bahulimA iShUni //1//

atho iyanniyann iti //17//

761

Aminoniti bhadyate //1//
tasya anu nibha~njanam //2//
varuNo yAti vasvabhiH //3//
shataM vA bhAratI shavaH //4//
shatamAshvA hiraNyayAH /
shataM rathyA hiraNyayAH /
shataM kuthA hiraNyayAH /
shataM niShkA hiraNyayAH //5//
ahula kusha varttaka //6//
shaphena iva ohate //7//

vaniShThA nAva gR^ihyanti //9//

pAka baliH //12//

araduparama //15//

762

AdalAbukamekakam //1//
alAbukam nikhAtakam //2//
karkariko nikhAtakaH //3//
tad vAta unmathAyati //4//
kulAyaM kR^iNavAditi //5//
ugraM vaniShadAtatam //6//

ka eShAM karkarI likhat //8//
ka eShAM dundubhiM hanat //9//

devI hanat kuhanat //11//
paryAgAraM punaHpunaH //12//
trINyuShTrasya nAmAni //13//

dvau vA ye shishavaH //15//

763

vitatau kiraNau dvau tAvA pinaShTi pUruShaH /
na vai kumAri tat tathA yathA kumAri manyase //1//

na vai kumAri tat tathA yathA kumAri manyase //2//

na vai kumAri tat tathA yathA kumAri manyase //3//

na vai kumAri tat tathA yathA kumAri manyase //4//
shlakShNAyAM shlakShNikAyAM shlakShNamevAva gUhasi /

avashlaksNamiva bhraMshad antarlomamati hrade /

764

ihettha prAgapAgudagadharAg arAlAgudabhartsatha //1//
ihettha prAgapAgudagadharAg vatsAH puruShanta Asate //2//

ihettha prAgapAgudagadharAg Aste lAhaNi lIshAthI //5//
ihettha prAgapAgudagadharAg akShlilI puchhilIyate //6//
765

bhug ity abhigataH shalityapakrAntaH phalityabhiShThitaH /
dundubhim AhananAbhyAM jaritarothAmo daiva //1//
koshabile rajani granther dhAnam upAnahi pAdam /
uttamAM janimAM janyAnuttamAM janIn vartmanyAt //2//
alAbUni pR^iShAtakAnyashvatthapalAsham /
pipIlikAvatashvaso vidyutsvAparNashapho goshapho jaritarothAmo daiva //3//
vIme devA akraMsatAdhvaryo kShipraM prachara /
susatyam id gavAm asyasi prakhudasi //4//
patnI yadR^ishyate patnI yakShyamANA jaritarothAmo daiva /
hotA viShTImena jaritar othAmo daiva //5//
AdityA ha jaritar a~Ngirobhyo dakShiNAm anayan /
tAM ha jaritaH pratyAyaMs tAm u ha jaritaH pratyAyan //6//
tAM ha jaritar naH pratyagR^ibhNaMs tAm u ha jaritar naH pratyagR^ibhNaH /
ahAnetarasaM na vi chetanAni yaj~nAn etarasaM na purogavAmaH //7//
uta shveta AshupatvA uto padyAbhir yaviShThaH /
utem Ashu mAnaM piparti //8//
AdityA rudrA vasavas tvenu ta idaM rAdhah prati gR^ibhNIhy a~NgiraH /
idaM rAdho vibhu prabhu idaM rAdho bR^ihat pR^ithu //9//
devA dadatvAsuraM tad vo astu suchetanam /
yuShmAm? astu divedive pratyeva gR^ibhAyat //10//
tvam indra sharmariNA havyaM pArAvatebhyaH /
viprAya stuvate vasuvaniM durashravase vaha //11//
tvam indra kapotAya chhinnapakShAya va~nchate /
shyAmAkaM pakvaM pIlu cha vArasmA akR^iNor bahuh //12//
araMgaro vAvadIti tredhA baddho varatrayA /
irAmaha prashaMsaty anirAm apa sedhati //13//
766

yad asyA aMhubhedyAH kR^idhu sthUlam upAtasat /
muShkAvidasyA ejato goshaphe shakulAv iva //1//
yadA sthUlena pasasANau muShkA upAvadhIt /
viShva~nchA vasyA vardhataH sikatAsv eva gardabhau //2//
yad alpikAsvalpikA karkadhUkevaShadyate /
vAsantikam iva tejanaM yanty avAtAya vitpati //3//
yad devAso lalAmaguM praviShTIminam AviShuH /
sakulA dedishyate nArI satyasyAkShibhuvo yathA //4//
mahAnagnyatR^ipnadvi mokradadasthAnAsaran /
shaktikAnanA svachamashakaM saktu padyama //5//
mahAnagnyùlUkhalam atikrAmanty abravIt /
yathA tava vanaspate niraghnanti tathaiveti //6//
mahAnagnyupa brUte bhraShTothApyabhUbhuvaH /
yathaiva te vanaspate pippati tathaiveti //7//
mahAnagnyupa brUte bhraShTothApyabhUbhuvaH /
yathA vayo vidAhya svarge namavadahyate //8//
mahAnagnyupa brUte svasAveshitaM pasaH /
itthaM phalasya vR^ikShasya shUrpe shUrpaM bhajemahi //9//
mahAnagnI kR^ikavAkaM shamyayA pari dhAvati /
ayaM na vidma yo mR^igaH shIrShNA harati dhANikAm //10//
mahAnagnI mahAnagnaM dhAvantam anu dhAvati /
imAs tad asya gA rakSha yabha mAm addhyaudanam //11//
sudevas tvA mahAnagnIr babAdhate mahataH sAdhu khodanam /
kusaM pIvaro navat //12//
vashA dagdhAm imA~NguriM prasR^ijatogrataM pare /
mahAn vai bhadro yabha mAm addhyaudanam //13//
videvas tvA mahAnagnIr vibAdhate mahataH sAdhu khodanam /
kumArIkA pi~NgalikA kArda bhasmA ku dhAvati //14//
mahAn vai bhadro bilvo mahAn bhadra udumbaraH /
mahA.N abhikta bAdhate mahataH sAdhu khodanam //15//
yaH kumArI pi~NgalikA vasantaM pIvarI labhet /
tailakuNdamimA~NguShThaM rodantaM shudam uddharet //16//
767

yad dha prAchIr ajagantoro maNDUradhANikIH /
hatA indrasya shatravaH sarve budbudayAshavaH //1//
kapR^in naraH kapR^itham ud dadhAtana chodayata khudata vAjasAtaye /
niShTigryaH putram A chyAvayotaya indraM sabAdha iha somapItaye //2//
dadhikrAvNo akAriShaM jiShNor ashvasya vAjinaH /
surabhi no mukhA karat pra Na AyUMShi tAriShat //3//
sutAso madhumattamAH somA indrAya mandinaH /
pavitravanto akSharan devAn gachhantu vo madAH //4//
indur indrAya pavata iti devAso abruvan /
vAchas patir makhasyate vishvasyeshAna ojasA //5//
sahasradhAraH pavate samudro vAchamI~NkhayaH /
somaH patI rayINAM sakhendrasya divedive //6//
ava drapso aMshumatIm atiShThad iyAnaH kR^iShNo dashabhiH sahasraiH /
Avat tam indraH shachyA dhamantam apa snehitIr nR^imaNA adhatta //7//
drapsam apashyaM viShuNe charantam upahvare nadyo aMshumatyAH /
nabho na kR^iShNam avatasthivAMsam iShyAmi vo vR^iShaNo yudhyatAjau //8//
adha drapso aMshumatyA upasthe 'dhArayat tanvaM titviShANaH /
visho adevIr abhy AcharantIr bR^ihaspatinA yujendraH sasAhe //9//
tvaM ha tyat saptabhyo jAyamAno 'shatrubhyo abhavaH shatrur indra /
gUlhe dyAvApR^ithivI anv avindo vibhumadbhyo bhuvanebhyo raNaM dhAH //10//
tvaM ha tyad apratimAnam ojo vajreNa vajrin dhR^iShito jaghantha /
tvaM shuShNasyAvAtiro vadhatrais tvaM gA indra shachyed avindaH //11//
tam indraM vAjayAmasi mahe vR^itrAya hantave /
sa vR^iShA vR^iShabho bhuvat //12//
indraH sa dAmane kR^ita ojiShThaH sa made hitaH /
dyumnI shlokI sa somyaH //13//
girA vajro na saMbhR^itaH sabalo anapachyutaH /
vavakSha R^iShvo astR^itaH //14//
768

mahA.N indro ya ojasA parjanyo vR^iShTimA.N iva /
stomair vatsasya vAvR^idhe //1//
prajAm R^itasya piprataH pra yad bharanta vahnayaH /
viprA R^itasya vAhasA //2//
kaNvAH indraM yad akrata stomair yaj~nasya sAdhanam /
jAmi bruvata Ayudham //3//
769

A nUnam ashvinA yuvaM vatsasya gantam avase /
prAsmai yachhatam avR^ikam pR^ithu chhardir yuyutaM yA arAtayaH //1//
yad antarikShe yad divi yat pa~ncha mAnuShAm? anu /
nR^imnaM tad dhattam ashvinA //2//
ye vAm daMsAMsy ashvinA viprAsaH parimAmR^ishuH /
evet kANvasya bodhatam //3//
ayaM vAM gharmo ashvinA stomena pari Shichyate /
ayaM somo madhumAn vAjinIvasU yena vR^itraM chiketathaH //4//
yad apsu yad vanaspatau yad oShadhIShu purudaMsasA kR^itam /
tena mAviShTam ashvinA //5//
770

yan nAsatyA bhuraNyatho yad vA deva bhiShajyathaH /
ayaM vAM vatso matibhir na vindhate haviShmantaM hi gachhathaH //1//
A nUnam ashvinor R^iShi stomaM chiketa vAmayA /
A somaM madhumattamaM gharmaM si~nchAd atharvaNi //2//
A nUnaM raghuvartaniM rathaM tiShThAtho ashvinA /
A vAM stomA ime mama nabho na chuchyavIrata //3//
yad adya vAM nAsatyokthair AchuchyuvImahi /
yad vA vANIbhir ashvinevet kaNvasya bodhatam //4//
yad vAM kakShIvAm? uta yad vyashva R^iShir yad vAM dIrghatamA juhAva /
pR^ithI yad vAM vainyaH sAdaneShv eved ato ashvinA chetayethAm //5//
771

yAtaM chhardiShpA uta paraspA bhUtaM jagatpA uta nas tanUpA /
vartis tokAya tanayAya yAtam //1//
yad indreNa sarathaM yAtho ashvinA yad vA vAyunA bhavathaH samokasA /
yad Adityebhir R^ibhubhiH sajoShasA yad vA viShNor vikramaNeShu tiShThathaH //2//
yad adyAshvinAv ahaM huveya vAjasAtaye /
yat pR^itsu turvaNe sanas tach chhreShTham ashvinor avaH //3//
A nUnaM yAtam ashvinemA havyAni vAM hitA /
ime somAso adhi turvashe yadAv ime kaNveShu vAm atha //4//
yan nAsatyA parAke arvAke asti bheShajam /
tena nUnaM vimadAya prachetasA chhardir vatsAya yachhatam //5//
772

abhutsy u pra devyA sAkaM vAchAham ashvinoh /
vy Avar devy A matiM vi rAtiM martyebhyaH //1//
pra bodhayoSho ashvinA pra devi sUnR^ite mahi /
pra yaj~nahotar AnuShak pra madAya shravo bR^ihat //2//
yad uSho yAsi bhAnunA saM sUryeNa rochase /
A hAyam ashvino ratho vartir yAti nR^ipAyyam //3//
yad ApItAso aMshavo gAvo na duhra UdhabhiH /
yad vA vANIr anuShata pra devayanto ashvinA //4//
pra dyumnAya pra shavase pra nR^iShAhyAya sharmaNe /
pra dakShAya prachetasA //5//
yan nUnaM dhIbhir ashvinA pitur yonA niShIdathaH /
yad vA sumnebhir ukthyA //6//
773

taM vAM rathaM vayam adyA huvema pR^ithujrayam ashvinA saMgatiM goH /
yaH sUryAM vahati vandhurAyur girvAhasaM purutamaM vasUyum //1//
yuvaM shriyam ashvinA devatA tAM divo napAtA vanathaH shachIbhiH /
yuvor vapur abhi pR^ikShaH sachante vahanti yat kakuhAso rathe vAm //2//
ko vAm adyA karate rAtahavya Utaye vA sutapeyAya vArkaiH /
R^itasya vA vanuShe pUrvyAya namo yemAno ashvinA vavartat //3//
hiraNyayena purubhU rathenemaM yaj~naM nAsatyopa yAtam /
pibAtha in madhunaH somyasya dadhatho ratnaM vidhate janAya //4//
A no yAtaM divo achha pR^ithivyA hiraNyayena suvR^itA rathena /
mA vAm anye ni yaman devayantaH saM yad dade nAbhiH pUrvyA vAm //5//
nU no rayiM puruvIraM bR^ihantaM dasrA mimAthAm ubhayeShv asme /
naro yad vAm ashvinA stomam Avant sadhastutim AjamIlhAso agman //6//
iheha yad vAM samanA papR^ikShe seyam asme sumatir vAjaratnA /
uruShyataM jaritAraM yuvaM ha shritaH kAmo nAsatyA yuvadrik //7//
madhumatIr oShadhIr dyAva Apo madhuman no bhavatv antarikSham /
kShetrasya patir madhumAn no astv ariShyanto anv enaM charema //8//
panAyyaM tad ashvinA kR^itaM vAM vR^iShabho divo rajasaH pR^ithivyAH /
sahasraM shaMsA uta ye gaviShTau sarvAm? it tA.N upa yAtA pibadhyai //9//
